# 📘 Chapter 1: Overview of Futures and Derivatives ｜ 📘 第一章 期货及衍生品概述

## Section 1: The Emergence and Development of Derivatives Markets ｜ 第一节 衍生品市场的产生与发展

### 📌 Examination Topic: The Formation of the Modern Futures Market ｜ 📌 考点：现代期货市场的形成

#### I. The Early Development of Futures Markets ｜ 一、期货市场的萌芽

1. Futures trading is generally believed to have first emerged in Europe. ｜ 一般认为，期货交易最早萌芽于欧洲。
2. As early as ancient Greece and ancient Rome, centralised marketplaces and large-scale barter transactions had already appeared in Europe. ｜ 早在古希腊和古罗马时期，欧洲就已经出现了集中的交易场所和大宗易货交易。
3. These activities gradually developed into transactions conducted at fixed locations and predetermined times. ｜ 这些活动逐渐发展为在固定场所和既定时间开展的交易。
4. Forward trading also has a long history in China. ｜ 中国的远期交易同样源远流长。
5. During the Spring and Autumn Period, Fan Li, also known as Tao Zhu Gong, reportedly conducted forward transactions. ｜ 春秋时期，被称为陶朱公的范蠡就曾开展远期交易。
6. Futures trading originated from forward trading. ｜ 期货交易萌芽于远期交易。
7. The centralisation and organisation of forward spot trading laid the foundation for the emergence of futures trading and futures markets. ｜ 远期现货交易的集中化和组织化，为期货交易的产生及期货市场的形成奠定了基础。

#### II. The Formation of the Modern Futures Market ｜ 二、现代期货市场的形成

1. A regulated modern futures market emerged in Chicago in the United States in the mid-nineteenth century. ｜ 规范的现代期货市场于19世纪中期产生于美国芝加哥。
2. In 1848, 82 grain merchants established the Chicago Board of Trade. ｜ 1848年，82位粮食商人在芝加哥发起组建了芝加哥期货交易所。
3. It was the world’s first relatively regulated futures exchange. ｜ 它是世界上第一家较为规范的期货交易所。
4. In 1865, the Chicago Board of Trade introduced standardised contracts and a margin system. ｜ 1865年，芝加哥期货交易所推出了标准化合约和保证金制度。
5. Both parties were required to deposit margin of no more than 10% of the contract value as a guarantee of performance. ｜ 签约双方需要缴纳不超过合约价值10%的保证金，作为履约保证。
6. These institutional innovations contributed to the birth of modern futures trading. ｜ 这些制度创新促成了真正意义上的期货交易的诞生。
7. In 1882, traders were allowed to offset their positions instead of performing the original contract. ｜ 1882年，交易所允许交易者通过对冲方式免除原合约的履约责任。
8. This attracted more speculators and improved market liquidity. ｜ 这一制度吸引了更多投机者，并提高了市场流动性。

------

### 📌 Examination Topic: Development Trends of the International Futures Market ｜ 📌 考点：国际期货市场的发展趋势

#### I. The Development History of the International Futures Market ｜ 一、国际期货市场的发展历程

The international futures market gradually developed from commodity futures to financial futures. ｜ 国际期货市场逐渐由商品期货发展到金融期货。

The number of traded products and the overall trading scale continued to increase. ｜ 交易品种不断增加，交易规模不断扩大。

##### (I) Commodity Futures ｜ （一）商品期货

A commodity futures contract is a futures contract whose underlying asset is a physical commodity. ｜ 商品期货是指标的物为实物商品的期货合约。

Commodity futures mainly include agricultural futures, metal futures, and energy and chemical futures. ｜ 商品期货主要包括农产品期货、金属期货和能源化工期货。

##### (II) Financial Futures ｜ （二）金融期货

Financial futures emerged after the collapse of the Bretton Woods system. ｜ 金融期货产生于布雷顿森林体系解体之后。

The replacement of fixed exchange rates with floating exchange rates increased exchange-rate fluctuations. ｜ 浮动汇率制取代固定汇率制后，汇率波动明显加剧。

The gradual removal of interest-rate controls also increased interest-rate fluctuations. ｜ 利率管制逐渐取消后，利率波动也明显加剧。

Market participants therefore began to use futures as hedging instruments. ｜ 因此，市场参与者开始利用期货工具规避风险。

#### II. Development Trends of the International Futures Market ｜ 二、国际期货市场的发展趋势

##### (I) Futures Trading Centres Are Becoming Increasingly Concentrated ｜ （一）交易中心日益集中

Major international futures trading centres are concentrated in cities such as Chicago, New York, London, and Frankfurt. ｜ 主要的国际期货交易中心集中在芝加哥、纽约、伦敦和法兰克福等城市。

![image-20260726153534679](/Users/tangjun/Library/Application Support/typora-user-images/image-20260726153534679.png)

##### (II) Exchanges Are Shifting from Membership Structures to Corporate Structures ｜ （二）交易所由会员制向公司制发展

Corporate restructuring and public listing have become major development trends among global exchanges. ｜ 公司制改革和公开发行上市已经成为全球交易所的重要发展趋势。

##### (III) Mergers and Restructuring Are Increasing ｜ （三）交易所兼并重组趋势明显

Economic globalisation and intensified competition have encouraged exchanges to merge and restructure. ｜ 经济全球化和竞争加剧推动了交易所的兼并重组。

##### (IV) Financial Futures Have Overtaken Commodity Futures ｜ （四）金融期货后来居上

The trading volume of financial futures has greatly exceeded that of commodity futures. ｜ 金融期货的交易量已经远远超过商品期货。

##### (V) Competition and Service Quality Are Increasing ｜ （五）交易所竞争加剧，服务质量不断提高

Exchanges continue to improve their products, trading systems, and services to attract investors. ｜ 交易所不断改进产品、交易系统和服务，以吸引更多投资者。

## Section 2: Basic ==Derivative== Instruments ｜ 第二节 基础衍生工具



![image-20260726153813881](/Users/tangjun/Library/Application Support/typora-user-images/image-20260726153813881.png)

### 📌 Examination Topic: Basic Derivative Instruments ｜ 📌 考点：基础衍生工具



#### I. Forward Contracts ｜ 一、远期合约



##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

A forward contract is an agreement to buy or sell a specified quantity of an underlying asset at a predetermined price on a specified future date. ｜ 远期合约是指交易双方约定在未来某一确定时间，以事先确定的价格买卖一定数量标的资产的合约。

##### (II) Common Types ｜ （二）常见类型

Common forward transactions include commodity forwards, Forward Rate Agreements, foreign exchange forwards, and Non-Deliverable Forwards. ｜ 常见的远期交易包括商品远期、远期利率协议、外汇远期和无本金交割外汇远期。

##### (III) Differences Between Forward and Spot Transactions ｜ （三）远期交易与即期交易的区别

A spot transaction is normally settled immediately or within several days. ｜ 即期交易通常立即交割或者在几天内完成交割。

A forward transaction is settled on an agreed future date. ｜ 远期交易则在约定的未来日期进行交割。

#### II. Futures ｜ 二、期货

##### (I) The Relationship Between Futures and Spot Trading ｜ （一）期货与现货的关系

Futures are derived from spot trading. ｜ 期货由现货交易衍生而来。

##### (II) Meaning of Futures ｜ （二）期货的含义

Futures are tradable standardised forward contracts based on commodities or financial assets. ｜ 期货是以大宗商品或者金融资产为标的、可以交易的标准化远期合约。

##### (III) Definition of a Futures Contract ｜ （三）期货合约的定义

A futures contract is a standardised contract created by a futures exchange. ｜ 期货合约是由期货交易所统一制定的标准化合约。

##### (IV) Underlying Assets ｜ （四）期货合约的标的物

The underlying asset may be a physical commodity, financial product, or related financial indicator. ｜ 期货合约的标的物可以是实物商品、金融产品或者相关金融指标。

#### III. Options ｜ 三、期权

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

An option gives its buyer the right to buy or sell an underlying asset at a predetermined price. ｜ 期权赋予买方按照事先约定的价格买入或者卖出标的资产的权利。

##### (II) Trading Markets ｜ （二）交易市场

Options may be traded on exchanges or in the over-the-counter market. ｜ 期权既可以在交易所交易，也可以在场外交易市场交易。

##### (III) Common Types ｜ （三）常见类型

Common options include interest-rate options, foreign exchange options, equity options, and commodity options. ｜ 常见期权包括利率期权、外汇期权、股权类期权和商品期权。

#### IV. Swaps ｜ 四、互换

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

A swap is a contract under which two or more parties exchange a series of cash flows. ｜ 互换是两个或两个以上当事人交换一系列现金流的合约。

##### (II) Relationship with Forward Contracts ｜ （二）互换与远期合约的关系

A swap may be regarded as a series of forward contracts. ｜ 互换可以看作一系列远期合约的组合。

##### (III) Common Types ｜ （三）常见类型

Common swaps include interest-rate swaps, currency swaps, commodity swaps, equity swaps, and forward swaps. ｜ 常见互换包括利率互换、货币互换、商品互换、股权类互换和远期互换。

![image-20260726153849166](/Users/tangjun/Library/Application Support/typora-user-images/image-20260726153849166.png)



### 📌 Examination Topic: Main Characteristics of Derivatives Trading ｜ 📌 考点：衍生品交易的主要特征

#### I. Basic Characteristics of Forward Transactions ｜ 一、远期交易的基本特征

##### (I) Forward Transactions Are Conducted in the Over-the-Counter Market ｜ （一）远期交易属于场外交易

Forward transactions are conducted in the over-the-counter market. ｜ 远期交易通常在场外市场进行。

The over-the-counter market is a decentralised market. ｜ 场外市场是一个分散的市场。

The two parties negotiate directly and enter into a forward contract. ｜ 交易双方通过直接谈判签订远期合约。

The terms of a forward contract are generally non-standardised. ｜ 远期合约的内容通常是非标准化的。

When entering into the contract, the parties negotiate the terms according to their individual needs. ｜ 在签订合约时，双方根据各自的需求协商确定合约条款。

These terms may include the maturity date, delivery price, and transaction quantity. ｜ 这些条款可能包括到期时间、交割价格和交易数量等。

Forward contracts are therefore highly flexible. ｜ 因此，远期合约具有较高的灵活性。

They can better satisfy the individual needs of both parties. ｜ 它们能够更好地满足交易双方的个性化需求。

##### (II) Forward Transactions Usually Use Physical Delivery ｜ （二）远期交易通常采用实物交割

The buyer and seller of a forward contract usually settle the contract through physical delivery. ｜ 远期合约的买卖双方通常通过实物交割的方式了结合约。

At the settlement date specified in the contract, the buyer pays the delivery amount. ｜ 在合约规定的结算时间，买方支付交割款项。

At the same time, the seller transfers the underlying asset to the buyer. ｜ 与此同时，卖方向买方转移标的资产。

The underlying asset must meet the quantity and quality requirements specified in the contract. ｜ 标的资产必须符合合约约定的数量和品质要求。

Physical delivery is the most common settlement method for forward contracts. ｜ 实物交割是远期合约最常见的交割方式。

##### (III) Forward Transactions Have Relatively Low Liquidity ｜ （三）远期交易的流动性较差

In theory, one party may terminate a forward contract early by transferring it to a third party. ｜ 理论上，交易一方可以通过将远期合约转让给第三方来提前终止合约。

However, it is usually difficult to find a suitable new counterparty. ｜ 但是，交易者通常很难找到合适的新交易对手。

The new counterparty must be willing to accept the same underlying asset, transaction quantity, maturity date, and other contract terms. ｜ 新的交易对手必须愿意接受相同的标的资产、交易数量、到期期限以及其他合约条款。

It is difficult to find another party whose needs exactly match all the existing contract terms. ｜ 要找到需求与原合约所有条款完全一致的交易对手通常比较困难。

The original counterparty may also refuse to accept a change of counterparty. ｜ 原来的交易对手也可能不同意更换交易对手。

Therefore, forward contracts generally have relatively low liquidity. ｜ 因此，远期合约的流动性通常较差。

It is usually difficult to terminate a forward contract before maturity. ｜ 远期合约通常很难在到期前提前终止。

##### (IV) Forward Transactions Have Relatively High Default Risk ｜ （四）远期交易的违约风险较大

Both parties to a forward contract are exposed to default risk. ｜ 远期合约的交易双方都面临违约风险。

An over-the-counter transaction originally involves only the two contracting parties. ｜ 场外交易通常只涉及签订合约的双方。

The transaction can be concluded as long as both parties reach an agreement. ｜ 只要双方达成一致，就可以签订合约。

Whether the contract can be performed on time mainly depends on the creditworthiness of both parties. ｜ 合约能否按期履行，主要取决于交易双方的信用状况。

Over-the-counter transactions are generally subject to fewer regulatory measures than exchange-traded transactions. ｜ 与场内交易相比，场外交易通常受到的监管措施较少。

They also generally provide fewer performance guarantees. ｜ 场外交易通常也缺乏充分的履约保障。

When market prices move against one party, that party may suffer a substantial loss. ｜ 当市场价格朝着不利于某一方的方向变化时，该方可能遭受较大损失。

Under these circumstances, the probability of default may increase. ｜ 在这种情况下，该方发生违约的可能性可能会增加。

#### II. Basic Characteristics of Futures Trading ｜ 二、期货交易的基本特征

##### (I) Standardised Contracts ｜ （一）合约标准化

Futures contracts are standardised contracts uniformly designed by futures exchanges. The quantity and specifications of the underlying asset, as well as the delivery time, delivery location and other major terms, are determined in advance by the exchange. Traders generally only need to decide the contract, trading direction, quantity and transaction price. ｜ 期货合约是由期货交易所统一制定的标准化合约。合约中标的物的数量、规格、交割时间、交割地点等主要条款均由交易所事先确定，交易者通常只需要选择具体合约、交易方向、交易数量和成交价格。

##### (II) Centralised Auction Trading on Exchanges ｜ （二）场内集中竞价交易

All futures buy and sell orders must be matched through centralised auction trading on a futures exchange. Only exchange members are permitted to trade directly on the exchange, while other traders must appoint an exchange member, such as a futures company, to conduct futures transactions on their behalf. ｜ 所有期货买卖指令都必须在期货交易所内通过集中竞价的方式成交。只有交易所会员能够直接进入交易所进行交易，其他交易者则需要委托交易所会员，例如期货公司，代理其进行期货交易。

##### (III) Margin Trading ｜ （三）保证金交易

When trading futures contracts, traders are required to deposit a certain percentage of the contract value as margin, generally around 5% to 15%, as a guarantee of contract performance. Because traders can control contracts worth several times the amount of margin deposited, futures trading has a leverage effect and is therefore also known as leveraged trading. ｜ 交易者买卖期货合约时，需要按照合约价值的一定比例缴纳保证金，一般约为5%至15%，作为履约保证。由于交易者只需要缴纳少量保证金，就能够进行价值数倍于保证金的期货交易，因此期货交易具有杠杆效应，也被称为“杠杆交易”。

##### (IV) Two-Way Trading ｜ （四）双向交易

Futures traders may establish a position by either buying or selling a futures contract. Buying a contract to establish a position is known as opening a long position, while selling a contract to establish a position is known as opening a short position. Therefore, traders may potentially profit from both rising and falling market prices. ｜ 期货交易者既可以通过买入期货合约建立多头头寸，也可以通过卖出期货合约建立空头头寸。买入建仓通常称为“做多”或“买空”，卖出建仓通常称为“做空”或“卖空”，因此交易者既可以在价格上涨时获利，也可以在价格下跌时寻找获利机会。

##### (V) Closing Positions Through Offset ｜ （五）对冲了结

After opening a long position, a trader may close the position and discharge the obligation to perform the contract by selling the same futures contract. Similarly, after opening a short position, the trader may close the position by buying the same futures contract. Most futures transactions are therefore settled through offsetting positions rather than physical delivery. ｜ 买入建仓后，交易者可以通过卖出相同的期货合约进行平仓，从而解除履约责任；卖出建仓后，交易者也可以通过买入相同的期货合约进行平仓。因此，大多数期货交易通过对冲平仓的方式了结，而不是最终进行实物交割。

##### (VI) Daily Mark-to-Market Settlement ｜ （六）当日无负债结算

Futures trading adopts a daily mark-to-market settlement system, also known as ==daily settlement without debt.== At the end of each trading day, the exchange calculates the profits and losses of each trader according to the daily settlement price and adjusts the trader’s margin account accordingly. If the margin balance falls below the required level, the trader must provide additional margin. ｜ 期货交易实行当日无负债结算制度，也称为==逐日盯市==。每个交易日结束后，交易所按照当日结算价计算交易者的盈亏，并相应调整其保证金账户。如果保证金余额低于规定标准，交易者需要及时追加保证金。

#### III. Basic Characteristics of Options Trading ｜ 三、期权交易的基本特征

##### (I) Asymmetry of Rights ｜ （一）权利不对等

The option buyer has the right to decide whether to exercise the option. After paying the premium, the buyer may buy or sell the underlying asset at the agreed price within the specified period, but is not required to do so. ｜ 期权买方享有决定是否行权的权利。买方支付权利金后，可以在约定期限内按照约定价格买入或卖出标的物，但没有必须行权的义务。

The option seller does not have this choice. Once the buyer decides to exercise the option, the seller must accept the buyer’s decision. ｜ 期权卖方没有这种选择权。一旦买方决定行权，卖方必须接受买方的决定。

##### (II) Asymmetry of Obligations ｜ （二）义务不对等

The buyer may exercise the option or allow it to expire, depending on whether exercising the option is beneficial. Therefore, the buyer has a right but no compulsory obligation to trade. ｜ 买方可以根据行权是否有利，选择行权或者放弃期权。因此，买方享有交易的权利，但没有必须交易的义务。

The seller receives the premium but must perform the contract if the buyer exercises the option. For a call option, the seller must sell the underlying asset; for a put option, the seller must buy the underlying asset. ｜ 卖方收取权利金后，如果买方行权，就必须履行合约。对于看涨期权，卖方必须出售标的物；对于看跌期权，卖方必须购买标的物。

##### (III) Asymmetry of Returns and Risks ｜ （三）收益和风险不对等

If the market moves in the buyer’s favour, the buyer may exercise the option and obtain a profit. If the market moves against the buyer, the buyer may abandon the option, so the buyer’s maximum loss is generally limited to the premium paid. ｜ 如果市场价格朝着有利于买方的方向变化，买方可以行权并获得收益；如果市场价格朝着不利于买方的方向变化，买方可以放弃行权。因此，买方的最大损失通常仅限于已经支付的权利金。

For the seller, the maximum profit is generally limited to the premium received. However, if the market moves strongly in the buyer’s favour, the seller may suffer a substantial loss because the seller must still perform the contract. ｜ 对于卖方而言，最大收益通常仅限于收取的权利金。但是，如果市场价格大幅朝着有利于买方的方向变化，卖方仍然必须履约，因此可能遭受较大损失。

In simple terms, the buyer has limited loss and potentially large profit, while the seller has limited profit and potentially large loss. ｜ 简单来说，买方的损失有限、潜在收益较大；卖方的收益有限、潜在损失较大。

##### (IV) Different Margin Requirements ｜ （四）保证金缴纳情况不同

The option buyer usually does not need to deposit margin because the buyer has already paid the premium and the maximum loss is generally limited to that premium. ｜ 期权买方通常不需要缴纳保证金，因为买方已经支付了权利金，其最大损失通常也仅限于这笔权利金。

The option seller usually needs to deposit margin because the seller must perform the contract when the buyer exercises the option and may face a substantial potential loss. ｜ 期权卖方通常需要缴纳保证金，因为买方行权时卖方必须履约，而且卖方可能面临较大的潜在损失。

##### (V) Non-Linear Profit and Loss Structure ｜ （五）独特的非线性损益结构

The profit and loss of an option do not change proportionally with every movement in the price of the underlying asset. This is because the buyer can choose not to exercise the option when exercising would be unfavourable. ｜ 期权的盈亏不会随着标的物价格的每一次变化而完全按比例变化。这是因为当行权不利时，买方可以选择不行权。

For example, when the market price moves against the buyer, the buyer’s loss normally stops at the premium paid. When the market price moves in the buyer’s favour beyond a certain point, the buyer’s profit begins to increase. ｜ 例如，当市场价格朝着不利于买方的方向变化时，买方的损失通常止于已经支付的权利金；当市场价格朝着有利于买方的方向变化并超过一定水平后，买方的收益才会开始增加。

This produces a non-linear profit and loss structure, which is fundamentally different from the linear profit and loss structure of futures trading. ｜ 这就形成了期权独特的非线性损益结构，与期货交易的线性损益结构存在本质区别。

这次删去解释性和重复文字，只保留可直接复习的知识点，并用 emoji 标出核心区别。

#### IV. Basic Characteristics of Swap Transactions ｜ 四、互换交易的基本特征

Swaps are generally non-standardised contracts. The key point is to distinguish a foreign exchange swap from a currency swap. ｜ 互换通常是非标准化合约。这里的重点是区分**外汇掉期**和**货币互换**。

##### (I) Differences in Transaction Structure ｜ （一）交易结构不同

🔄 **Foreign Exchange Swap**

A foreign exchange swap consists of two opposite transactions involving the same currencies and equal amounts but different settlement dates. It can be divided into one spot transaction and one forward transaction, or two forward transactions with different maturities. ｜ 外汇掉期是针对**相同币种、相同金额**进行的两笔**方向相反、交割日期不同**的交易，可以拆分为“一笔即期交易＋一笔远期交易”，或者“两笔期限不同的远期交易”。

It is mainly a foreign exchange trading method rather than an independent derivative contract. ｜ 它主要是一种外汇交易方式，而不是独立的衍生合约。

💱 **Currency Swap**

A currency swap is a contract in which two parties exchange different currencies, interest payments, or both principal and interest over an agreed period. ｜ 货币互换是交易双方在约定期限内交换**不同货币的本金、利息，或者同时交换本金和利息**的合约。

It is an independent financial derivative with a substantive contract. ｜ 它具有独立、实质性的合约，是一种金融衍生工具。

✅ **Key distinction**

A foreign exchange swap combines two foreign exchange transactions, while a currency swap involves a continuing exchange of cash flows. ｜ 外汇掉期是**两笔外汇交易的组合**，货币互换是**一系列现金流的持续交换**。

##### (II) Differences in Trading Markets ｜ （二）交易市场不同

A foreign exchange swap is conducted directly in the foreign exchange market and has no separate specialised market. ｜ 外汇掉期直接在外汇市场中进行，本身没有独立的专门市场。

A currency swap has developed its own specialised trading market. ｜ 货币互换已经形成了相对独立的专门交易市场。

------

### 🔗 Examination Topic: Relationships and Differences Among Derivatives ｜ 🔗 考点：不同衍生品之间的联系和区别

#### I. Relationships ｜ 一、联系

##### (I) Futures and Forwards ｜ （一）期货与远期

Forward trading is an extension of spot trading into the future. ｜ 远期交易是现货交易在时间上的延伸。

Forward trading is the early form of futures trading, while futures trading developed through the standardisation of forward contracts. ｜ 远期交易是期货交易的雏形，期货是在远期合约标准化的基础上发展形成的。

📌 **Spot → Forward → Futures** ｜ 📌 **现货交易 → 远期交易 → 期货交易**

##### (II) Futures and Options ｜ （二）期货与期权

Futures and exchange-traded options are both standardised contracts traded on exchanges and centrally settled through clearing institutions. ｜ 期货和场内期权都是在交易所交易的标准化合约，并通过结算机构统一结算。

Futures contracts may serve as the underlying assets of futures options. ｜ 期货合约可以作为期货期权的标的资产。

📌 **Futures trading provides the underlying market for futures options.** ｜ 📌 **期货交易是期货期权交易的基础。**

##### (III) Futures, Forwards and Swaps ｜ （三）期货、远期与互换

A swap involves the exchange of cash flows at several future dates and can therefore be regarded as a series of forward contracts. ｜ 互换是在未来多个时间交换现金流，因此可以看作一系列远期合约的组合。

Forward contracts form the common foundation of futures and swaps. ｜ 远期合约是期货和互换的共同基础。

Futures are standardised forward contracts, while swaps are combinations of multiple forward transactions. ｜ 期货是标准化的远期合约，互换则是多笔远期交易的组合。

Forward agreements may also provide a pricing basis for futures and swaps. ｜ 远期协议还可以为期货和互换提供定价基础。

📌 **One standardised forward contract → Futures** ｜ 📌 **一笔标准化远期合约 → 期货**

📌 **A series of forward contracts → Swap** ｜ 📌 **一系列远期合约 → 互换**

------

#### II. Differences ｜ 二、区别

##### (I) Degree of Standardisation ｜ （一）标准化程度

Futures and exchange-traded options are standardised contracts. ｜ 期货和场内期权属于标准化合约。

Forwards and swaps are generally customised, non-standardised contracts. ｜ 远期和互换通常是个性化的非标准化合约。

##### (II) Trading Venue ｜ （二）交易地点

Futures and exchange-traded options are traded on exchanges. ｜ 期货和场内期权主要在交易所交易。

Forwards and swaps are mainly traded in over-the-counter markets. ｜ 远期和互换主要在场外市场交易。

##### (III) Transaction Method ｜ （三）成交方式

Exchange-traded derivatives are normally executed through centralised auction and order matching. ｜ 场内衍生品通常通过集中竞价和撮合方式成交。

Over-the-counter derivatives are normally concluded through quotation and direct negotiation. ｜ 场外衍生品通常通过询价和直接协商成交。

##### (IV) Credit Risk ｜ （四）信用风险

Exchange-traded derivatives generally have relatively low credit risk because a clearing institution acts as the central counterparty. ｜ 场内衍生品通常由结算机构作为中央对手方，因此信用风险相对较低。

Over-the-counter derivatives generally have higher counterparty credit risk because performance depends directly on the creditworthiness of the parties. ｜ 场外衍生品的履约直接依赖交易双方的信用状况，因此交易对手信用风险相对较高。

##### (V) Rights and Obligations ｜ （五）权利和义务

The rights and obligations of both parties to futures and forward contracts are symmetrical, and both parties must perform the contract. ｜ 期货和远期合约双方的权利义务是对称的，双方都必须履约。

The rights and obligations of an option contract are asymmetrical: the buyer has the right to exercise, while the seller has the obligation to perform. ｜ 期权合约双方的权利义务不对称：买方享有行权权利，卖方承担履约义务。

##### (VI) Profit and Loss Structure ｜ （六）盈亏结构

The profits and losses of futures and forward contracts are symmetrical: one party’s profit corresponds to the other party’s loss. ｜ 期货和远期合约的盈亏是对称的，一方的盈利对应另一方的亏损。

The profit and loss of an option contract are asymmetrical: the buyer has limited loss, while the seller may face substantial potential loss. ｜ 期权合约的盈亏不对称：买方损失有限，而卖方可能面临较大的潜在损失。

------

### 🧠 Core Summary ｜ 🧠 核心总结

**Forward:** customised contract for one future transaction. ｜ **远期：**个性化约定的一次未来交易。

**Futures:** standardised forward contract traded on an exchange. ｜ **期货：**在交易所交易的标准化远期合约。

**Option:** the buyer has a right, while the seller has an obligation. ｜ **期权：**买方享有权利，卖方承担义务。

**Swap:** a combination of multiple future cash-flow exchanges. ｜ **互换：**多次未来现金流交换的组合。

后面的内容继续按这种“定义＋核心区别＋一句总结”的密度整理。

### 🏛️ Examination Topic: Exchange-Traded and Over-the-Counter Markets ｜ 🏛️ 考点：场内市场与场外市场

#### I. Definitions ｜ 一、定义

##### (I) Exchange-Traded Market ｜ （一）场内市场

An exchange-traded market, also known as an exchange-traded derivatives market or ETD market, is a market in which buyers and sellers trade through centralised auction and order matching at a fixed exchange. ｜ 场内市场又称交易所市场或ETD市场，是指买卖双方集中在固定的交易所内，通过公开竞价和集中撮合完成交易的市场。

##### (II) Over-the-Counter Market ｜ （二）场外市场

An over-the-counter market, or OTC market, is a decentralised market in which the parties trade directly through private negotiation rather than through a central exchange. Transactions may be conducted face to face, by telephone, through the internet, or through an intermediary. ｜ 场外市场又称柜台交易市场或OTC市场，是指交易双方不通过集中交易所，而是通过私下协商直接达成交易的分散市场。交易可以通过面对面商谈、电话、互联网或者经纪人中介完成。

##### (III) Main Participants in the OTC Derivatives Market ｜ （三）场外衍生品市场的主要参与者

The main participants in the modern OTC derivatives market include investors, dealers, clearing institutions, regulators, and self-regulatory organisations. A central counterparty, or CCP, may stand between the two parties and provide centralised clearing. ｜ 现代场外衍生品市场的主要参与者包括投资者、交易商、清算机构、监管机构和自律组织。中央对手方，即CCP，可以介入交易双方之间并提供集中清算。

The three main clearing methods for OTC derivatives are non-standardised bilateral clearing, standardised bilateral clearing, and central counterparty clearing. ｜ 场外衍生品主要有三种清算方式：非标准化双边清算、标准化双边清算和中央对手方清算。

Following the introduction of stronger financial regulation, central counterparty clearing has become increasingly important. ｜ 随着金融监管不断加强，中央对手方清算模式的重要性不断提高。

------

#### II. Classification of China’s OTC Markets ｜ 二、我国场外市场的分类

##### (I) Interbank OTC Derivatives Market ｜ （一）银行间场外衍生品市场

The interbank OTC derivatives market refers to the Chinese OTC derivatives market under the supervision of the People’s Bank of China. It includes transactions completed through trading systems recognised by the central bank and transactions conducted over the counters of commercial banks. ｜ 银行间场外衍生品市场是指由中国人民银行监管的场外衍生品市场，包括通过人民银行认可的交易系统达成的交易，以及在商业银行柜台开展的场外衍生品交易。

##### (II) Securities and Futures OTC Derivatives Market ｜ （二）证券期货场外衍生品市场

The securities and futures OTC derivatives market refers to the Chinese OTC derivatives market supervised by the China Securities Regulatory Commission, with securities and futures institutions acting as the main market participants. ｜ 证券期货场外衍生品市场是指由中国证监会监管，并以证券期货经营机构为核心参与者的场外衍生品市场。

------

#### III. Comparison Between Exchange-Traded and OTC Markets ｜ 三、场内市场与场外市场的比较

##### (I) Trading Venue and Method ｜ （一）交易场所与成交方式

🏛️ In an exchange-traded market, transactions are concentrated on an exchange and completed through public auction and order matching. ｜ 🏛️ 场内市场的交易集中在交易所内，通过公开竞价和集中撮合成交。

🤝 In an OTC market, transactions are decentralised and completed through direct negotiation between the parties. ｜ 🤝 场外市场的交易较为分散，主要通过交易双方直接协商完成。

##### (II) Information Transparency and Market Efficiency ｜ （二）信息透明度与市场效率

🔍 Exchange-traded markets have centralised and transparent information, open pricing, and relatively high market efficiency. ｜ 🔍 场内市场的信息较为集中和透明，价格形成公开，市场效率相对较高。

🌫️ OTC markets have lower information transparency, may involve information asymmetry, and often require greater effort to find a suitable counterparty. ｜ 🌫️ 场外市场的信息透明度相对较低，容易出现信息不对称，寻找合适交易对手的成本也较高。

##### (III) Degree of Contract Standardisation ｜ （三）合约标准化程度

📄 Exchange-traded derivatives are standardised contracts. The underlying asset, contract size, quotation method, minimum price movement, delivery terms, and other major conditions are determined in advance by the exchange. ｜ 📄 场内衍生品属于标准化合约，标的资产、合约规模、报价方式、最小变动单位和交割条件等主要内容均由交易所事先规定。

📝 OTC derivatives are customised contracts. The parties may negotiate the contract terms according to their own needs. ｜ 📝 场外衍生品属于个性化合约，交易双方可以根据自身需求协商确定具体条款。

##### (IV) Trading and Clearing Mechanisms ｜ （四）交易与结算机制

⚙️ Exchange-traded markets have complete trading, clearing, margin, and risk-control systems. Only exchange members may trade directly, while other investors must trade through exchange members. ｜ ⚙️ 场内市场具有较完整的交易、结算、保证金和风险控制制度。只有交易所会员能够直接交易，其他投资者需要通过交易所会员代理交易。

🔗 OTC transactions rely more heavily on the contractual arrangements and creditworthiness of the two parties. ｜ 🔗 场外交易更加依赖交易双方之间的合约安排和信用状况。

##### (V) Credit Risk ｜ （五）信用风险

🛡️ Exchange-traded markets generally have lower default risk because clearing institutions and central counterparties provide performance guarantees. ｜ 🛡️ 场内市场通常由结算机构和中央对手方提供履约保障，因此违约风险相对较低。

⚠️ OTC markets generally have higher counterparty credit risk because each party must bear the risk that the other party may fail to perform the contract. ｜ ⚠️ 场外市场中，交易双方需要承担对方不能履约的风险，因此交易对手信用风险相对较高。

------

#### IV. Core Comparison ｜ 四、核心对比

🏛️ Exchange-Traded Market: centralised, standardised, transparent, and lower credit risk. ｜ 🏛️ 场内市场：集中交易、合约标准化、信息透明、信用风险较低。

🤝 OTC Market: decentralised, customised, flexible, and higher counterparty risk. ｜ 🤝 场外市场：分散交易、合约个性化、灵活性高、交易对手风险较高。

人 一

## 🎯 Section 4: Functions and Roles of Derivatives Markets ｜ 🎯 第四节 衍生品市场的功能及作用

### 📌 Examination Topic: Functions of Derivatives Markets ｜ 📌 考点：衍生品市场的功能

#### I. Functions of Derivatives Markets ｜ 一、衍生品市场的功能

##### (I) Risk Hedging ｜ （一）规避风险

**1. Concept of Hedging** ｜ **1. 套期保值的概念**

Hedging means taking a futures position that is equal in quantity but opposite in direction to an existing or expected spot position. The futures position is later closed through an offsetting transaction, so that profits in one market can approximately offset losses in the other market. ｜ 套期保值是指在期货市场上建立一个与现货头寸**数量相等、方向相反**的期货头寸，并在未来通过反向交易对冲平仓，使期货市场的盈利与现货市场的亏损大致相抵。

🔑 **Core logic: opposite positions offset price risk.** ｜ 🔑 **核心逻辑：通过方向相反的头寸抵消价格风险。**

**2. Principle of Hedging** ｜ **2. 套期保值的原理**

The prices of the same commodity in the spot and futures markets generally move in the same direction. As the futures contract approaches its delivery date, the futures price and the spot price tend to converge. ｜ 同一种商品在现货市场和期货市场中的价格通常呈现相同的变动趋势。随着期货合约逐渐接近交割日期，期货价格与现货价格会趋于一致。

📈 **Same direction of price movements** ｜ 📈 **现货价格与期货价格同向变动**

📍 **Price convergence near delivery** ｜ 📍 **临近交割时期现价格趋于一致**

**3. The Role of Speculators** ｜ **3. 投机者的作用**

Hedging does not eliminate risk; it transfers price risk from hedgers to market participants who are willing to bear it. Speculators take on this risk in exchange for the opportunity to earn profits from price fluctuations. ｜ 套期保值并不会消灭风险，而是将价格风险从套期保值者转移给愿意承担风险的市场参与者。投机者在承担价格波动风险的同时，也获得赚取投机利润的机会。

🔄 **Hedgers transfer risk; speculators assume risk.** ｜ 🔄 **套期保值者转移风险，投机者承担风险。**

------

##### (II) Price Discovery ｜ （二）价格发现

Price discovery refers to the process through which futures trading brings together market information and the expectations of buyers and sellers to form a relatively fair and representative market price. ｜ 价格发现是指期货市场汇集各类市场信息以及买卖双方的预期，从而形成相对公平、具有代表性的市场价格的过程。

**1. Reasons for Price Discovery** ｜ **1. 价格发现的原因**

① A large number of market participants represent both supply and demand, helping to form a fair market price. ｜ ① 期货市场参与者众多，能够充分反映供需双方的力量，有助于形成公平价格。

② Many participants have professional knowledge, extensive information channels, and analytical and forecasting abilities. ｜ ② 许多交易者熟悉商品行情，具有丰富的专业知识、广泛的信息渠道以及分析和预测能力。

③ Futures trading is relatively transparent, and open competition helps produce a fair and credible price. ｜ ③ 期货交易透明度较高，公开、公平的市场竞争有助于形成公正且可信的价格。

**2. Characteristics of Price Discovery** ｜ **2. 价格发现的特点**

🔮 **Expectational:** Futures prices reflect market expectations about future supply, demand and prices. ｜ 🔮 **预期性：**期货价格能够反映市场对未来供求关系和价格变化的预期。

🔄 **Continuous:** Futures prices are continuously updated as new information enters the market. ｜ 🔄 **连续性：**随着新信息不断进入市场，期货价格会持续调整。

🔍 **Public:** Futures prices are formed through open trading and are publicly available. ｜ 🔍 **公开性：**期货价格通过公开交易形成，并向市场公开。

🏛️ **Authoritative:** Futures prices reflect extensive market information and therefore have strong market influence. ｜ 🏛️ **权威性：**期货价格综合了大量市场信息，因此具有较强的市场影响力。

------

##### (III) Asset Allocation ｜ （三）资产配置

Asset allocation means using futures and other derivatives as part of an investment portfolio to manage risk, preserve asset value and improve the overall risk-return structure. ｜ 资产配置是指将期货等衍生工具纳入投资组合，以管理风险、实现资产保值，并改善投资组合整体的风险收益结构。

**1. Reasons for Using Futures in Asset Allocation** ｜ **1. 期货用于资产配置的原因**

① Futures can hedge the price risk of spot assets and other investments. ｜ ① 期货可以为现货资产和其他投资进行风险对冲。

② The margin system and leverage mechanism make futures trading flexible and capital-efficient. ｜ ② 保证金制度和杠杆机制使期货交易具有较高的灵活性和资金使用效率。

③ Leverage may increase potential returns, but it also increases potential losses and overall investment risk. ｜ ③ 杠杆可能放大潜在收益，但也会放大潜在损失和投资风险。

**2. Principles of Asset Allocation** ｜ **2. 资产配置的原理**

① Futures can hedge risks in spot assets or investment portfolios, helping to stabilise returns and reduce overall risk. ｜ ① 期货可以为现货资产或投资组合进行套期保值，从而稳定收益并降低整体风险。

② Commodity futures may serve as a tool for preserving asset value during inflation or commodity price increases. ｜ ② 在通货膨胀或商品价格上涨时期，商品期货可以发挥一定的资产保值作用。

③ Adding futures to an investment portfolio may improve diversification and produce a better risk-return combination. ｜ ③ 将期货纳入投资组合，有助于分散风险并形成更合理的风险收益组合。

------

### 🧠 Core Summary ｜ 🧠 核心总结

🛡️ **Risk hedging:** Transfer and offset price risk. ｜ 🛡️ **规避风险：**转移并抵消价格波动风险。

💡 **Price discovery:** Form a fair price by combining market information and expectations. ｜ 💡 **价格发现：**汇集市场信息和预期，形成公平价格。

📊 **Asset allocation:** Improve portfolio diversification and the risk-return structure. ｜ 📊 **资产配置：**改善投资组合的分散化程度和风险收益结构。

第二章 期货市场组织结构



# 📘 Chapter 2: Organisational Structure of the Futures Market ｜ 📘 第二章 期货市场组织结构

## Section 1: Futures Exchanges ｜ 第一节 期货交易所

### 📌 Examination Topic: Futures Exchanges ｜ 📌 考点：期货交易所

#### I. Functions of a Futures Exchange ｜ 一、期货交易所的职能

1. A futures exchange provides trading venues, facilities and related services. ｜ 期货交易所为期货交易提供场所、设施和相关服务。
2. It designs futures contracts and arranges for contracts to be listed. ｜ 期货交易所负责设计期货合约并安排合约上市。
3. It establishes and implements market systems and trading rules. ｜ 期货交易所负责制定并实施期货市场制度和交易规则。
4. It organises and supervises futures trading and monitors market risks. ｜ 期货交易所负责组织和监督期货交易，并监控市场风险。
5. It collects and publishes futures market information. ｜ 期货交易所负责收集并发布期货市场信息。

🧠 **Core functions: provide trading services, design contracts, establish rules, control risks and publish information.** ｜ 🧠 **核心职能：提供交易服务、设计合约、制定规则、控制风险和发布信息。**

------

#### II. Organisational Structure of Futures Exchanges ｜ 二、期货交易所的组织结构

##### (I) Membership-Based Futures Exchange ｜ （一）会员制期货交易所

**1. Definition** ｜ **1. 概念**

A membership-based futures exchange is a non-profit legal entity jointly funded and established by its members. Members pay membership fees as registered capital, and the exchange bears limited liability with all its assets. ｜ 会员制期货交易所是由全体会员共同出资组建的非营利性法人。会员缴纳一定的会员资格费作为注册资本，交易所以其全部财产承担有限责任。

**2. Organisational Structure** ｜ **2. 组织架构**

👥 **Members’ General Meeting:** the highest authority of the exchange. ｜ 👥 **会员大会：**交易所的最高权力机构。

🏛️ **Board of Governors:** the permanent decision-making body. ｜ 🏛️ **理事会：**交易所的常设机构。

📋 **Specialised Committees:** responsible for supervision, trading, membership review, finance and technology. ｜ 📋 **专业委员会：**负责监察、交易、会员资格审查、财务和技术等事项。

👔 **Senior Management:** responsible for the daily management of the exchange. ｜ 👔 **高级管理人员：**负责交易所的日常经营和管理。

⚙️ **Business Departments:** carry out specific trading, settlement, technology and administrative work. ｜ ⚙️ **业务部门：**负责具体的交易、结算、技术和行政工作。

**3. Methods of Obtaining Membership** ｜ **3. 会员资格的取得方式**

A person or institution may obtain membership by joining as an original founder of the exchange. ｜ 可以以交易所创办发起人的身份取得会员资格。

Membership may be obtained by accepting a transfer from an exchange founder. ｜ 可以通过接受交易所发起人的会员资格转让加入。

Membership may also be obtained by accepting a transfer from another exchange member. ｜ 可以通过接受其他交易所会员的资格转让加入。

An applicant may also join according to the rules established by the exchange. ｜ 申请者也可以依据交易所规定的条件和程序加入。

**4. Basic Rights and Obligations of Members** ｜ **4. 会员的基本权利与义务**

📌 **Rights of Members** ｜ 📌 **会员的权利**

Members may attend the members’ general meeting and exercise voting and appeal rights. ｜ 会员可以参加会员大会，并行使表决权和申诉权。

Members may trade on the exchange and use its trading facilities, information and services. ｜ 会员可以在交易所内进行期货交易，并使用交易设施、市场信息和相关服务。

Members may transfer their membership qualifications according to the rules. ｜ 会员可以按照规定转让会员资格。

Members may jointly propose the convening of an extraordinary members’ general meeting. ｜ 会员可以联名提议召开临时会员大会。

📌 **Obligations of Members** ｜ 📌 **会员的义务**

Members must comply with relevant laws, regulations, rules and government policies. ｜ 会员必须遵守国家有关法律、法规、规章和政策。

Members must comply with the exchange’s articles of association, business rules and decisions. ｜ 会员必须遵守交易所章程、业务规则和有关决定。

Members must pay relevant fees according to the rules. ｜ 会员必须按照规定缴纳各种费用。

Members must implement the resolutions of the members’ general meeting and the board of governors. ｜ 会员必须执行会员大会和理事会作出的决议。

Members must accept the supervision and administration of the futures exchange. ｜ 会员必须接受期货交易所的业务监管。

------

##### (II) Corporate Futures Exchange ｜ （二）公司制期货交易所

**1. Organisational Structure** ｜ **1. 组织架构**

👥 **Shareholders’ General Meeting:** the highest authority of the exchange. ｜ 👥 **股东大会：**交易所的最高权力机构。

🏛️ **Board of Directors:** the permanent decision-making body. ｜ 🏛️ **董事会：**交易所的常设决策机构。

🔍 **Board of Supervisors or Supervisor:** supervises directors and senior management. ｜ 🔍 **监事会或监事：**负责监督董事和高级管理人员履行职责。

👔 **Senior Management:** the general manager and other managers are responsible for daily operations. ｜ 👔 **高级管理人员：**总经理等高级管理人员负责日常经营管理。

⚙️ **Business Departments:** carry out the exchange’s specific business activities. ｜ ⚙️ **业务部门：**负责执行交易所的各项具体业务。

**2. Membership Qualifications** ｜ **2. 会员资格**

The methods and requirements for obtaining membership in a corporate futures exchange are generally similar to those of a membership-based futures exchange. ｜ 公司制期货交易所会员资格的取得方式和基本要求，与会员制期货交易所大体相似。

------

##### (III) Main Differences Between Membership-Based and Corporate Futures Exchanges ｜ （三）会员制与公司制期货交易所的主要区别

💰 **Business Objective** ｜ 💰 **经营目标**

A corporate futures exchange may operate for profit. ｜ 公司制期货交易所可以以营利为目标。

A membership-based futures exchange is generally a non-profit legal entity. ｜ 会员制期货交易所通常是非营利性法人。

⚖️ **Applicable Law** ｜ ⚖️ **适用法律**

A corporate futures exchange is mainly governed by company law. ｜ 公司制期货交易所主要适用《公司法》。

A membership-based futures exchange is mainly governed by civil law and relevant futures regulations. ｜ 会员制期货交易所主要适用民事法律和期货市场相关规定。

🏛️ **Highest Decision-Making Body** ｜ 🏛️ **最高权力机构**

The highest authority of a corporate futures exchange is the shareholders’ general meeting. ｜ 公司制期货交易所的最高权力机构是股东大会。

The highest authority of a membership-based futures exchange is the members’ general meeting. ｜ 会员制期货交易所的最高权力机构是会员大会。

👤 **Basis of Governance** ｜ 👤 **治理基础**

A corporate futures exchange is governed according to shareholders’ ownership interests. ｜ 公司制期货交易所主要以股东的出资和股权关系为治理基础。

A membership-based futures exchange is governed according to the rights and obligations of its members. ｜ 会员制期货交易所主要以会员的资格、权利和义务为治理基础。

------

#### III. Core Comparison ｜ 三、核心对比

🏢 **Corporate exchange: shareholders invest → shareholders’ general meeting → board of directors → profit-oriented.** ｜ 🏢 **公司制交易所：股东出资 → 股东大会 → 董事会 → 可以营利。**

👥 **Membership-based exchange: members contribute → members’ general meeting → board of governors → non-profit.** ｜ 👥 **会员制交易所：会员出资 → 会员大会 → 理事会 → 非营利。**



原表格中的上市品种存在明显 OCR 错位，而且品种会持续更新。下面改成“**组织形式—会员管理—广期所—主要品种结构—国际化节点**”，只保留可复习的知识，并根据官方资料核对了交易所名称和组织形式。

#### III. Futures Exchanges in Mainland China ｜ 三、我国境内期货交易所

##### (I) Organisational Forms of Futures Exchanges ｜ （一）我国境内期货交易所的组织形式

Futures exchanges may adopt either a membership-based structure or a corporate structure. ｜ 期货交易所可以采取会员制或者公司制两种组织形式。([Edu Shfe](https://edu.shfe.com.cn/TradingAssistant/RulesIntroduction/514.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

👥 **1. Membership-Based Exchanges** ｜ 👥 **1. 会员制期货交易所**

Membership-based exchanges are funded by their members, and the members’ general meeting is the highest authority. ｜ 会员制交易所由会员出资，会员大会是交易所的最高权力机构。

The membership-based futures exchanges in mainland China include: ｜ 我国境内的会员制期货交易所包括：

- Shanghai Futures Exchange, or SHFE ｜ 上海期货交易所，简称上期所
- Dalian Commodity Exchange, or DCE ｜ 大连商品交易所，简称大商所
- Zhengzhou Commodity Exchange, or CZCE ｜ 郑州商品交易所，简称郑商所

The abbreviation of the Shanghai Futures Exchange is **SHFE**, not “CHART.” ｜ 上海期货交易所的英文缩写是 **SHFE**，不是原文识别出的“CHART”。

🏢 **2. Corporate Exchanges** ｜ 🏢 **2. 公司制期货交易所**

Corporate exchanges are established as companies, and the shareholders’ general meeting is the highest authority. ｜ 公司制交易所以公司形式设立，股东大会是交易所的最高权力机构。

The corporate futures exchanges in mainland China include: ｜ 我国境内的公司制期货交易所包括：

- China Financial Futures Exchange, or CFFEX ｜ 中国金融期货交易所，简称中金所
- Guangzhou Futures Exchange, or GFEX ｜ 广州期货交易所，简称广期所

The China Financial Futures Exchange is a corporate exchange specialising in financial futures, options and related financial derivatives. ｜ 中国金融期货交易所是专门从事金融期货、期权等金融衍生品交易与结算的公司制交易所。([Cffex](https://www.cffex.com.cn/gk/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

📌 **Core distinction: membership-based exchanges are governed by members, while corporate exchanges are governed by shareholders.** ｜ 📌 **核心区别：会员制交易所以会员为治理基础，公司制交易所以股东为治理基础。**

------

##### (II) Membership Management of Futures Exchanges ｜ （二）我国境内期货交易所的会员管理

🌍 **1. International Classification of Members** ｜ 🌍 **1. 国际上的会员分类**

International futures exchanges may classify members according to their legal status, business scope and clearing qualifications. ｜ 国际期货交易所可以按照会员的法律身份、业务范围和结算资格进行分类。

Common classifications include: ｜ 常见分类包括：

- Individual members and legal-entity members ｜ 自然人会员与法人会员
- Full members and specialised members ｜ 全权会员与专业会员
- Clearing members and non-clearing members ｜ 结算会员与非结算会员

🏛️ **2. Membership in Mainland China** ｜ 🏛️ **2. 我国境内的会员资格**

Members of futures exchanges in mainland China are generally enterprises or other economic organisations registered within the People’s Republic of China. ｜ 我国境内期货交易所的会员通常是在中华人民共和国境内登记注册的企业法人或者其他经济组织。

Under current Shanghai Futures Exchange rules, members are classified by business scope into futures-company members and non-futures-company members. ｜ 按照现行上海期货交易所规则，会员可以按照业务范围分为期货公司会员和非期货公司会员。([Shfe](https://www.shfe.com.cn/regulation/exchangerules/otherrules/202508/t20250807_828510.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

📌 **Clearing members may settle directly with the exchange, while non-clearing members must complete settlement through clearing members.** ｜ 📌 **结算会员可以直接与交易所进行结算，非结算会员则需要通过结算会员完成结算。**

------

##### (III) Guangzhou Futures Exchange ｜ （三）广州期货交易所概况

🏢 **1. Establishment**

The Guangzhou Futures Exchange was approved by the China Securities Regulatory Commission on 22 January 2021 and was officially inaugurated on 19 April 2021. ｜ 广州期货交易所于2021年1月22日获中国证监会批准设立，并于2021年4月19日正式揭牌。([Cyber Security Review Committee](https://www.csrc.gov.cn/csrc/c100028/c3618ea5cc8974a37a52f624f40f70d3a/content.shtml?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

It is the fifth futures exchange established in mainland China. ｜ 它是我国境内设立的第五家期货交易所。([Guangzhou HR and Social Security](https://rsj.gz.gov.cn/ywzt/rcgz/bsh/pypt/bshkygzzfz/content/post_9137008.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

🤝 **2. Mixed-Ownership Structure**

The Guangzhou Futures Exchange is China’s first mixed-ownership exchange and was jointly established by eight shareholders. ｜ 广州期货交易所是我国第一家混合所有制交易所，由八家股东共同发起设立。

Its shareholders include the four existing futures exchanges, Ping An Insurance, Guangdong Pearl River Investment, Guangzhou Financial Holdings and Hong Kong Exchanges and Clearing. ｜ 其股东包括四家原有期货交易所、中国平安保险、广东珠江投资控股集团、广州金融控股集团以及香港交易及结算所有限公司。([Guangzhou Government](https://www.gz.gov.cn/ysgz/xwdt/ysdt/content/post_7072899.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

🌱 **3. Development Positioning**

The Guangzhou Futures Exchange focuses on serving the real economy and supporting green development. ｜ 广州期货交易所立足于服务实体经济和服务绿色发展。

Its development direction emphasises innovation, market orientation, legal governance and internationalisation. ｜ 其发展方向强调创新化、市场化、法治化和国际化。([Cyber Security Review Committee](https://www.csrc.gov.cn/csrc/c100028/c3618ea5cc8974a37a52f624f40f70d3a/content.shtml?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

📌 **Core positioning: a green, innovative and internationally oriented futures exchange.** ｜ 📌 **核心定位：建设绿色、创新并具有国际化方向的期货交易所。**

------

##### (IV) Main Product Areas of China’s Exchanges ｜ （四）我国境内交易所的主要品种领域

The specific list of futures and options changes over time, so the key point is to understand the main product areas of each exchange rather than memorising an outdated complete list. ｜ 期货和期权的具体上市品种会不断变化，因此重点是掌握各交易所的主要品种领域，而不是死记已经过时的完整清单。

🔩 **1. Shanghai Futures Exchange**

The Shanghai Futures Exchange mainly lists futures and options related to non-ferrous metals, precious metals, ferrous metals, energy and chemical products. ｜ 上海期货交易所的主要品种领域包括有色金属、贵金属、黑色金属以及能源化工产品。

Representative products include copper, aluminium, zinc, nickel, gold, silver, natural rubber, fuel oil and bitumen. ｜ 代表性品种包括铜、铝、锌、镍、黄金、白银、天然橡胶、燃料油和石油沥青等。([Shfe](https://www.shfe.com.cn/?_t_t_t=0.14610074600204825&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

🌾 **2. Zhengzhou Commodity Exchange**

The Zhengzhou Commodity Exchange mainly lists agricultural, chemical, metal and energy-related futures and options. ｜ 郑州商品交易所主要上市农产品、化工、金属和能源类期货及期权。

Representative products include wheat, cotton, sugar, rapeseed oil, rapeseed meal, apples, red dates, PTA, methanol, glass, soda ash, ferrosilicon and silicomanganese. ｜ 代表性品种包括小麦、棉花、白糖、菜籽油、菜籽粕、苹果、红枣、PTA、甲醇、玻璃、纯碱、硅铁和锰硅等。([CZCE](https://www.czce.com.cn/cn/content_file/gyjys/qhscyjcs/cgxc/2026/4/077939e9d8fc4ad088cd76a52ed8d3a0.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

🌽 **3. Dalian Commodity Exchange**

The Dalian Commodity Exchange mainly covers agricultural products, energy and chemical products, and raw materials used by the iron and steel industry. ｜ 大连商品交易所的主要品种领域包括农产品、能源化工产品和钢铁产业原材料。

Representative products include soybeans, soybean meal, soybean oil, corn, palm oil, eggs, live hogs, polyethylene, polypropylene, coke, coking coal and iron ore. ｜ 代表性品种包括大豆、豆粕、豆油、玉米、棕榈油、鸡蛋、生猪、聚乙烯、聚丙烯、焦炭、焦煤和铁矿石等。

📈 **4. China Financial Futures Exchange**

The China Financial Futures Exchange mainly lists stock-index futures, stock-index options and government-bond futures. ｜ 中国金融期货交易所主要上市股指期货、股指期权和国债期货。

Its products include the CSI 300, SSE 50, CSI 500 and CSI 1000 index futures, as well as 2-year, 5-year, 10-year and 30-year government-bond futures. ｜ 其产品包括沪深300、上证50、中证500和中证1000股指期货，以及2年期、5年期、10年期和30年期国债期货等。([Cffex](https://www.cffex.com.cn/cp/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

🌱 **5. Guangzhou Futures Exchange**

The Guangzhou Futures Exchange focuses on innovative products associated with green and low-carbon development. ｜ 广州期货交易所重点发展与绿色低碳相关的创新型品种。

Representative areas include industrial silicon, lithium carbonate, polysilicon and other products related to new energy and strategic industries. ｜ 代表性领域包括工业硅、碳酸锂、多晶硅以及其他与新能源和战略性产业相关的品种。([GFEX](https://www.gfex.com.cn/gfex/tzts/202606/e9acdad20ec940baabf3e42b4b7d7066.shtml?t=aged&updatetime=short&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

📊 **6. Securities Exchanges**

The Shanghai Stock Exchange and Shenzhen Stock Exchange also list exchange-traded options, such as ETF options, but they are securities exchanges rather than futures exchanges. ｜ 上海证券交易所和深圳证券交易所也上市ETF期权等场内期权，但二者属于证券交易所，不属于五家期货交易所。

------

##### (V) Internationalisation of China’s Futures and Options Markets ｜ （五）我国期货和期权市场的国际化

🌍 **1. Crude Oil Futures**

On 26 March 2018, crude oil futures were listed on the Shanghai International Energy Exchange, a subsidiary of the Shanghai Futures Exchange. ｜ 2018年3月26日，原油期货在上海期货交易所子公司上海国际能源交易中心挂牌上市。

They were China’s first internationalised futures product and allowed overseas investors to participate. ｜ 原油期货是我国首个国际化期货品种，并引入了境外投资者。

🌏 **2. Internationalised Options**

On 18 June 2021, palm-oil options were opened to international investors on the Dalian Commodity Exchange, becoming China’s first internationalised options product. ｜ 2021年6月18日，大连商品交易所的棕榈油期权对境外投资者开放，成为我国首个国际化期权产品。

Crude-oil options were listed on 21 June 2021 and were denominated in renminbi and open to overseas investors. ｜ 2021年6月21日，原油期权挂牌上市，采用人民币计价，并向境外投资者开放。

------

#### IV. Core Summary ｜ 四、核心总结

👥 **Membership-based exchanges:** SHFE, DCE and CZCE. ｜ 👥 **会员制交易所：**上期所、大商所和郑商所。

🏢 **Corporate exchanges:** CFFEX and GFEX. ｜ 🏢 **公司制交易所：**中金所和广期所。

🌾 **Commodity futures:** mainly traded on SHFE, DCE, CZCE and GFEX. ｜ 🌾 **商品期货：**主要在上期所、大商所、郑商所和广期所交易。

📈 **Financial futures:** mainly traded on CFFEX. ｜ 📈 **金融期货：**主要在中金所交易。

🌱 **GFEX:** focuses on green, low-carbon and innovative products. ｜ 🌱 **广期所：**重点发展绿色、低碳和创新型品种。

已删除 OCR 杂字并修正明显错字，继续保持“英文在前、中文在后”和原有标题层级。

##### (IV) OTC Trading Platforms of Domestic Futures Exchanges ｜ （四）境内期货交易所场外交易平台

The Shanghai Futures Exchange, Zhengzhou Commodity Exchange and Dalian Commodity Exchange have established over-the-counter trading platforms. These platforms connect futures prices with spot-market transactions and provide additional pricing and risk-management services for enterprises. ｜ 上海期货交易所、郑州商品交易所和大连商品交易所均已建立场外交易平台。这些平台将期货价格与现货交易相结合，为企业提供定价和风险管理服务。

The basis trading platform of the Dalian Commodity Exchange began trial operation on 25 September 2019. It promotes spot transactions using the pricing model of “futures price plus basis.” ｜ 大连商品交易所基差交易平台于2019年9月25日上线试运行，主要推广以“期货价格＋基差”为定价模式的现货贸易。

📌 **Futures price + basis = spot transaction price** ｜ 📌 **期货价格＋基差＝现货交易价格**

------

## 🧾 Section 2: Futures Clearing Institutions ｜ 🧾 第二节 期货结算机构

#### I. Nature and Functions of Futures Clearing Institutions ｜ 一、期货结算机构的性质与职能

##### (I) Guaranteeing Contract Performance ｜ （一）担保交易履约

A futures clearing institution provides settlement guarantees and helps ensure that futures contracts are properly performed by both parties. ｜ 期货结算机构为交易提供结算和履约保障，确保期货合约能够按照规定完成履行。

##### (II) Settling Trading Profits and Losses ｜ （二）结算交易盈亏

A futures clearing institution calculates and settles the profits, losses, margin changes and related funds of traders. ｜ 期货结算机构负责计算和结算交易者的盈亏、保证金变动以及相关资金。

##### (III) Controlling Market Risk ｜ （三）控制市场风险

A futures clearing institution controls settlement and default risks through margin requirements, daily settlement and other risk-management mechanisms. ｜ 期货结算机构通过保证金、每日结算等风险管理机制，控制结算风险和违约风险。

🧠 **Core functions: guarantee performance, settle profits and losses, and control risks.** ｜ 🧠 **核心职能：担保履约、结算盈亏、控制风险。**

------

#### II. Forms of Futures Clearing Institutions ｜ 二、期货结算机构的形式

##### (I) An Internal Department of a Futures Exchange ｜ （一）交易所的内部结算机构

A clearing institution may be an internal department of a futures exchange and provide clearing services only for that exchange. ｜ 结算机构可以作为某一期货交易所的内部机构，仅为该交易所提供结算服务。

##### (II) An Independent Clearing Company ｜ （二）独立的结算公司

A clearing institution may also be established as an independent clearing company that provides clearing services for one or more futures exchanges. ｜ 结算机构也可以作为独立的结算公司，为一家或多家期货交易所提供结算服务。

China currently mainly adopts the first form, under which each futures exchange has its own internal clearing institution. ｜ 目前，我国主要采取第一种形式，即由期货交易所内部的结算机构负责结算。

------

#### III. Futures Clearing Institutions and Clearing Systems in Mainland China ｜ 三、我国境内期货结算机构与结算制度

China’s domestic futures market adopts two clearing systems: the full-member clearing system and the tiered-member clearing system. ｜ 我国境内期货市场实行两种结算制度：**全员结算制度**和**会员分级结算制度**。

------

##### (I) Full-Member Clearing System ｜ （一）全员结算制度

Under the full-member clearing system, every member of a futures exchange is qualified to settle directly with the exchange. Each member is both a trading member and a clearing member, so there is no distinction between clearing members and non-clearing members. ｜ 在全员结算制度下，期货交易所的所有会员都具有直接与交易所进行结算的资格。每个会员既是交易会员，也是结算会员，因此不区分结算会员与非结算会员。

The exchange settles with its members, and the members then settle with the clients that have appointed them to trade. ｜ 结算关系为：**交易所对会员结算，会员对其受托客户结算。**

🔄 **Exchange → Member → Client** ｜ 🔄 **交易所 → 会员 → 客户**

The Zhengzhou Commodity Exchange, Dalian Commodity Exchange, Shanghai Futures Exchange and Guangzhou Futures Exchange adopt the full-member clearing system. ｜ 郑州商品交易所、大连商品交易所、上海期货交易所和广州期货交易所实行全员结算制度。

------

##### (II) Tiered-Member Clearing System ｜ （二）会员分级结算制度

Under the tiered-member clearing system, exchange members are divided into clearing members and non-clearing members according to whether they are qualified to settle directly with the exchange. ｜ 在会员分级结算制度下，交易所会员按照能否直接与交易所进行结算，被划分为结算会员和非结算会员。

Clearing members may settle directly with the exchange, while non-clearing members must complete settlement through clearing members. ｜ 结算会员具有直接与交易所结算的资格，非结算会员则必须通过结算会员完成结算。

The exchange settles with clearing members, clearing members settle with non-clearing members, and non-clearing members settle with their clients. ｜ 结算关系为：**交易所对结算会员结算，结算会员对非结算会员结算，非结算会员对客户结算。**

🔄 **Exchange → Clearing Member → Non-Clearing Member → Client** ｜ 🔄 **交易所 → 结算会员 → 非结算会员 → 客户**

------

##### (III) Member Types of the China Financial Futures Exchange ｜ （三）中国金融期货交易所的会员类型

The China Financial Futures Exchange adopts the tiered-member clearing system. Its members are divided into trading members, trading-clearing members, comprehensive clearing members and special clearing members. ｜ 中国金融期货交易所实行会员分级结算制度。按照业务范围，会员分为交易会员、交易结算会员、全面结算会员和特别结算会员四类。

**1. Trading Member** ｜ **1. 交易会员**

A trading member may conduct futures trading but is not qualified to settle directly with the exchange. It is therefore a non-clearing member. ｜ 交易会员可以进行期货交易，但不能直接与交易所结算，因此属于非结算会员。

**2. Trading-Clearing Member** ｜ **2. 交易结算会员**

A trading-clearing member may settle and arrange delivery only for its own clients. ｜ 交易结算会员只能为其受托客户办理结算和交割业务。

**3. Comprehensive Clearing Member** ｜ **3. 全面结算会员**

A comprehensive clearing member may settle and arrange delivery for its own clients. It may also provide clearing and delivery services for trading members that have entered into clearing agreements with it. ｜ 全面结算会员既可以为自己的受托客户办理结算和交割，也可以为与其签订结算协议的交易会员办理结算和交割。

**4. Special Clearing Member** ｜ **4. 特别结算会员**

A special clearing member may provide clearing and delivery services only for trading members that have entered into clearing agreements with it. ｜ 特别结算会员只能为与其签订结算协议的交易会员办理结算和交割业务。

📌 **Non-clearing member: trading member** ｜ 📌 **非结算会员：交易会员**

📌 **Clearing members: trading-clearing, comprehensive clearing and special clearing members** ｜ 📌 **结算会员：交易结算会员、全面结算会员和特别结算会员**

------

##### (IV) Clearing Guarantee Fund System ｜ （四）结算担保金制度

A futures exchange that adopts the tiered-member clearing system must also establish a clearing guarantee fund system. ｜ 实行会员分级结算制度的期货交易所，必须配套建立结算担保金制度。

The clearing guarantee fund is paid to the futures exchange by clearing members using their own funds. It remains the property of the clearing members and is used to deal with the risk of a clearing member’s default. ｜ 结算担保金由结算会员使用自有资金向期货交易所缴纳，所有权仍属于结算会员，主要用于应对结算会员的违约风险。

🛡️ **Purpose: protect the clearing system against member default.** ｜ 🛡️ **作用：防范结算会员违约对整个结算体系造成影响。**



下面删除 OCR 杂字和重复标题，只保留可复习的知识点，并保持原来的层级。

## 🏢 Section 3: Futures Companies and Service Institutions ｜ 🏢 第三节 期货公司与服务机构

### 📌 Examination Topic: Futures Companies ｜ 📌 考点：期货公司

#### I. Introduction to Futures Companies ｜ 一、期货公司的简介

A futures company is an intermediary institution that conducts futures trading on behalf of clients and charges commissions for its services. ｜ 期货公司是代理客户进行期货交易并收取交易佣金的中介机构。

A futures company is classified as a non-bank financial institution. ｜ 期货公司属于非银行金融机构。

------

#### II. Main Business Types of Futures Companies ｜ 二、期货公司的业务类型

A futures company may legally engage in commodity futures brokerage. It must obtain the relevant qualifications before conducting financial futures brokerage, overseas futures brokerage or futures investment consulting. Asset management business must be registered and filed according to law. ｜ 期货公司可以依法从事商品期货经纪业务。从事金融期货经纪、境外期货经纪或者期货投资咨询业务时，应当取得相应业务资格；从事资产管理业务时，应当依法登记备案。

##### (I) Futures Brokerage Business ｜ （一）期货经纪业务

Futures brokerage refers to the business in which a futures company uses its own trading channels to execute futures transactions on behalf of clients and charges commissions. The futures company accepts the client’s instruction and conducts the transaction in its own name, while the resulting profits and losses are borne by the client. ｜ 期货经纪业务是指期货公司通过自身交易通道代理客户进行期货交易，并收取交易佣金的业务。期货公司接受客户委托，以自己的名义进行交易，但交易产生的盈亏由客户承担。

📌 **Core relationship: the futures company executes the trade, while the client bears the result.** ｜ 📌 **核心关系：期货公司负责代理交易，客户承担交易结果。**

------

##### (II) Futures Investment Consulting Business ｜ （二）期货投资咨询业务

A qualified futures company may provide risk-management advice, market research, information analysis and trading consultation to clients and charge corresponding consulting fees. ｜ 取得期货投资咨询资格的期货公司，可以根据客户委托提供风险管理顾问、市场研究分析和交易咨询等服务，并收取相应的咨询费用。

Its main services include: ｜ 其主要服务包括：

1. **Risk-management advisory services** ｜ **风险管理顾问服务**

   The futures company may assist clients in establishing risk-management systems and operating procedures, and may provide specialised training and risk-management consultation. ｜ 期货公司可以协助客户建立风险管理制度和操作流程，并提供风险管理咨询及专项培训。

2. **Research and analysis services** ｜ **研究分析服务**

   The futures company may collect and organise futures-market information and related economic data, analyse prices in futures and spot markets, and provide research reports or market information. ｜ 期货公司可以收集整理期货市场信息及相关经济信息，分析期货市场和现货市场的价格及其影响因素，并提供研究报告或者资讯信息。

3. **Trading consultation services** ｜ **交易咨询服务**

   The futures company may design hedging and arbitrage plans for clients and formulate futures trading strategies. ｜ 期货公司可以为客户设计套期保值、套利等方案，并拟定期货交易策略。

------

##### (III) Asset Management Business ｜ （三）资产管理业务

A futures company must sign an asset-management contract with the client and provide services through a dedicated account. It may manage assets for a single client or for a specified group of clients according to law. ｜ 期货公司从事资产管理业务时，应当与客户签订资产管理合同，并通过专门账户提供服务。期货公司既可以为单一客户办理资产管理业务，也可以依法为特定的多个客户办理资产管理业务。

The permitted investment scope mainly includes: ｜ 资产管理业务的投资范围主要包括：

1. Futures, options and other financial derivatives ｜ 期货、期权及其他金融衍生品
2. Stocks, bonds, securities investment funds, collective asset-management plans, central-bank bills, short-term financing bills and asset-backed securities ｜ 股票、债券、证券投资基金、集合资产管理计划、央行票据、短期融资券和资产支持证券等
3. Other investment products recognised by the China Securities Regulatory Commission ｜ 中国证监会认可的其他投资品种

The actual investment scope must comply with the asset-management contract and must match the client’s risk awareness and risk-bearing capacity. ｜ 实际投资范围必须符合资产管理合同的约定，不得超出规定范围，并应当与客户的风险认知和风险承受能力相匹配。

📌 **Core requirements: a dedicated account, a written contract and suitability matching.** ｜ 📌 **核心要求：专门账户、书面合同和风险适当性匹配。**

------

##### (IV) Risk Management Business ｜ （四）风险管理业务

A futures company may establish a risk-management subsidiary to provide suitable risk-management services and products to real-sector enterprises, financial institutions, investment institutions, other legal persons or organisations, and high-net-worth individual clients. ｜ 期货公司可以设立风险管理子公司，为实体企业、金融机构、投资机构等法人或组织以及高净值自然人客户，提供适当的风险管理服务和产品。

The main purpose of this business is to help clients manage risks related to commodity prices, financial markets and business operations. ｜ 风险管理业务的主要目的是帮助客户管理商品价格、金融市场以及经营活动中的相关风险。

------

#### III. Core Summary ｜ 三、核心总结

💼 **Brokerage:** execute trades for clients and charge commissions. ｜ 💼 **经纪业务：**代理客户交易并收取佣金。

📊 **Investment consulting:** provide research, risk-management advice and trading strategies. ｜ 📊 **投资咨询：**提供研究分析、风险管理建议和交易策略。

💰 **Asset management:** manage client assets through dedicated accounts. ｜ 💰 **资产管理：**通过专门账户管理客户资产。

🛡️ **Risk management:** provide risk-management products and services through subsidiaries. ｜ 🛡️ **风险管理：**通过风险管理子公司提供风险管理产品和服务。

#### III. Functions and Roles of Futures Companies ｜ 三、期货公司的功能与作用

##### (I) Connecting Traders with Futures Exchanges ｜ （一）衔接交易者与期货交易所

Futures companies act as intermediaries between traders and futures exchanges. They provide account opening, order execution, settlement and other professional services, allowing non-exchange members to participate in futures trading and reducing overall transaction costs. ｜ 期货公司是期货交易者与期货交易所之间的中介。它们通过提供开户、交易执行、结算等专业服务，使非交易所会员能够参与期货交易，并降低市场整体的交易成本。

🔗 **Core role: connecting clients with futures exchanges.** ｜ 🔗 **核心作用：连接客户与期货交易所。**

##### (II) Reducing Information Asymmetry ｜ （二）降低信息不对称

Futures companies provide clients with market information, research reports, risk disclosures and professional consultation, helping clients better understand market conditions, trading rules and investment risks. ｜ 期货公司通过提供市场信息、研究报告、风险提示和专业咨询，帮助客户了解市场行情、交易规则和投资风险，从而降低期货交易中的信息不对称程度。

📊 **Core role: providing information and professional analysis.** ｜ 📊 **核心作用：提供信息和专业分析。**

##### (III) Transferring Risk and Preventing Systemic Risk ｜ （三）转移风险并防范系统性风险

Futures companies help clients use futures, options and other derivatives to transfer and manage price risks. They also reduce settlement and default risks through margin management, daily settlement, risk monitoring and other controls. ｜ 期货公司帮助客户利用期货、期权等衍生工具转移和管理价格风险，同时通过保证金管理、每日结算和风险监控等措施，降低结算风险和违约风险，防范系统性风险。

🛡️ **Core role: transferring market risk and controlling settlement risk.** ｜ 🛡️ **核心作用：转移市场风险并控制结算风险。**

##### (IV) Providing Professional Asset-Management Services ｜ （四）通过专业服务实现资产管理

Futures companies may provide asset-management services through professional investment research, portfolio construction and risk control, helping clients manage assets and improve their risk-return structure. ｜ 期货公司可以通过专业的投资研究、资产组合设计和风险控制，为客户提供资产管理服务，帮助客户管理资产并改善风险收益结构。

💼 **Core role: professional asset management.** ｜ 💼 **核心作用：提供专业资产管理服务。**

------

#### IV. Risk-Management Systems and Related Requirements of Futures Companies ｜ 四、期货公司的风险管理制度与相关要求

##### (I) Improving the Corporate Governance Structure ｜ （一）完善期货公司治理结构

A futures company must establish a sound governance structure and maintain independence from its controlling shareholders and actual controllers in business operations, management and services. ｜ 期货公司应当建立完善的治理结构，并在经营、管理和服务等方面与控股股东、实际控制人保持独立。

When shareholders or actual controllers experience major events, they must notify the futures company within the required period. When the futures company itself experiences a major event, it must immediately notify all shareholders in writing and report to the local office of the China Securities Regulatory Commission. ｜ 当期货公司的股东或者实际控制人发生重大事项时，应当在规定期限内通知期货公司；当期货公司发生重大事项时，应当立即书面通知全体股东，并向公司住所地的中国证监会派出机构报告。

A futures company must establish a board of directors and a board of supervisors, or supervisors, and appoint a chief risk officer. The chief risk officer supervises and inspects the legality, compliance and risk management of the company’s operations. ｜ 期货公司应当设立董事会和监事会或者监事，并设立首席风险官。首席风险官负责监督和检查期货公司经营管理行为的合法合规性以及风险管理情况。

🏛️ **Governance focus: independence, internal supervision and compliance.** ｜ 🏛️ **治理重点：保持独立、加强内部监督、确保合法合规。**

##### (II) Establishing an Effective Risk-Management System ｜ （二）建立有效的风险管理制度

A futures company must establish internal-control systems that match regulatory risk indicators. It must also establish a dynamic risk-monitoring and capital-replenishment mechanism centred on net capital, ensuring that net capital and other regulatory indicators continuously meet the required standards. ｜ 期货公司应当建立与风险监管指标相适应的内部控制制度，并建立以净资本为核心的动态风险监控和资本补充机制，确保净资本等风险监管指标持续符合规定标准。

A futures company must strictly implement the margin system. Client margin must be fully deposited in designated futures margin accounts and special settlement accounts of futures exchanges, and accurate information must be reported to the futures margin monitoring institution as required. ｜ 期货公司应当严格执行保证金制度。客户保证金必须全额存放在期货保证金账户和期货交易所专用结算账户中，并按照保证金存管监控要求，及时报送真实、准确的信息。

A futures company must also establish an independent risk-management system, standardised business procedures and comprehensive risk-control rules. ｜ 期货公司还应当建立独立的风险管理系统、规范完善的业务操作流程和风险管理制度。

📌 **Core requirements: net-capital control, margin security and independent risk management.** ｜ 📌 **核心要求：净资本监管、保证金安全和独立风险管理。**

##### (III) Management of Branch Offices ｜ （三）对分支机构的管理

Futures companies must exercise centralised and unified management over their branches and business departments. They may not jointly operate branches with other parties or contract, lease or entrust branches to others for management. ｜ 期货公司应当对分公司、营业部等分支机构实行集中统一管理，不得与他人合资或者合作经营分支机构，也不得将分支机构承包、租赁或者委托给他人经营管理。

The business conducted by a branch must not exceed the authorised business scope of the futures company and must comply with the relevant requirements of the China Securities Regulatory Commission. ｜ 分支机构开展的业务不得超出期货公司的业务范围，并应当符合中国证监会对相关业务的规定。

Futures companies must apply the “four unified controls” to their business departments: unified settlement, unified risk management, unified fund allocation, and unified financial management and accounting. ｜ 期货公司应当对营业部实行“四统一”，即统一结算、统一风险管理、统一资金调拨、统一财务管理和会计核算。

🔒 **Core principle: branches are managed centrally and cannot operate independently.** ｜ 🔒 **核心原则：分支机构必须集中管理，不得独立经营。**

------

#### V. Current Development of Futures Companies ｜ 五、期货公司的发展现状

##### (I) Increasing Capital Strength and Trading Scale ｜ （一）资本实力和代理交易规模不断扩大

The capital strength of futures companies continues to increase, while the scale of client trading conducted through futures companies has gradually expanded. ｜ 期货公司的资本实力不断增强，通过期货公司代理进行的客户交易规模也在逐步扩大。

##### (II) Improving Revenue and Profitability ｜ （二）收入和盈利能力明显改善

The revenue and profitability of futures companies have improved, and the previous situation in which trading volume increased without a corresponding increase in revenue has gradually changed. ｜ 期货公司的收入和盈利状况明显改善，过去长期存在的“增产不增收”现象有所缓解。

##### (III) Increasing Business Diversification ｜ （三）业务更加多元化

Futures companies have expanded from traditional brokerage services into investment consulting, asset management, risk management and other diversified businesses. ｜ 期货公司的业务已经从传统经纪业务逐步扩展到投资咨询、资产管理和风险管理等多元化领域。

##### (IV) Stronger Ability to Serve the Real Economy ｜ （四）服务实体经济的能力不断增强

Futures companies increasingly provide hedging, pricing and risk-management services to enterprises, helping them manage commodity-price and operational risks. ｜ 期货公司越来越多地为实体企业提供套期保值、定价和风险管理服务，帮助企业管理商品价格风险和经营风险。

------

#### VI. Core Summary ｜ 六、核心总结

🔗 **Market intermediary:** connects traders with futures exchanges. ｜ 🔗 **市场中介：**连接交易者与期货交易所。

📊 **Information service:** reduces information asymmetry. ｜ 📊 **信息服务：**降低市场信息不对称。

🛡️ **Risk management:** transfers risks and protects margin funds. ｜ 🛡️ **风险管理：**转移风险并保障保证金安全。

💼 **Professional services:** provides consulting, asset management and risk-management services. ｜ 💼 **专业服务：**提供咨询、资产管理和风险管理业务。



下面删去 OCR 杂字，只保留核心知识点，并继续保持原有层级、英文在前、中文在后。

### 🏢 Examination Topic: Subsidiaries of Futures Companies ｜ 🏢 考点：期货公司子公司

#### I. Overview of Risk Management Companies ｜ 一、风险管理公司概述

To better serve the real economy, futures companies may establish subsidiaries known as risk management companies. These subsidiaries mainly provide customised risk-management services to enterprises and other clients. ｜ 为满足期货市场服务实体经济的需要，期货公司可以设立子公司，即风险管理公司。风险管理公司主要为实体企业及其他客户提供个性化的风险管理服务。

------

#### II. Business Types of Risk Management Companies ｜ 二、风险管理公司的业务类型

Risk management companies may conduct basis trading, warehouse receipt services, cooperative hedging, over-the-counter derivatives business, market-making business and other risk-management-related activities. ｜ 风险管理公司可以开展基差贸易、仓单服务、合作套保、场外衍生品业务、做市业务以及其他与风险管理服务相关的业务。

------

##### (I) Basis Trading ｜ （一）基差贸易

Basis trading refers to spot transactions in which a risk management company provides quotations based on a fixed price, price fixing, average pricing or other pricing methods and trades directly with clients. ｜ 基差贸易是指风险管理公司采用确定价格、点价、均价等方式向客户提供报价，并与客户直接进行现货交易的业务。

📌 **Core idea: futures price or agreed pricing method + basis = spot transaction price.** ｜ 📌 **核心逻辑：期货价格或约定价格方式＋基差＝现货交易价格。**

------

##### (II) Warehouse Receipt Services ｜ （二）仓单服务

Warehouse receipt services refer to services provided through warehouse receipt exchange, warehouse receipt pledge, agreed repurchase and other methods. These services help clients obtain financing, improve commodity circulation and manage inventory. ｜ 仓单服务是指风险管理公司通过仓单串换、仓单质押、约定购回等方式，为客户提供融资、货物流转和库存管理服务。

A warehouse receipt is a document representing the right to collect or own physical commodities. It may include a standard warehouse receipt, an ordinary warehouse receipt issued by a warehousing or logistics company, or a transferable bill of lading. ｜ 仓单是代表实物商品提货权或者货物所有权的凭证，包括标准仓单、仓储物流企业出具的普通仓单以及可转让提单等。

📦 **Warehouse receipt exchange:** replacing one warehouse receipt with another to meet delivery or operational needs. ｜ 📦 **仓单串换：**将一种仓单转换为另一种仓单，以满足交割或经营需求。

💰 **Warehouse receipt pledge:** using warehouse receipts as collateral to obtain financing. ｜ 💰 **仓单质押：**以仓单作为担保获得融资。

🔄 **Agreed repurchase:** transferring warehouse receipts while agreeing to repurchase them in the future. ｜ 🔄 **约定购回：**先转让仓单，同时约定未来重新购回。

------

##### (III) Cooperative Hedging ｜ （三）合作套保

Cooperative hedging refers to a service in which a risk management company designs and implements hedging arrangements for clients to reduce the market risks arising from their spot production and business activities. ｜ 合作套保是指风险管理公司为降低客户现货生产经营中的市场风险，为客户设计并实施套期保值方案，以抵销被套期项目全部或部分价格风险。

In simple terms, the client has spot-market exposure, while the risk management company uses futures or options to help offset the corresponding price risk. ｜ 简单来说，客户在现货经营中面临价格风险，风险管理公司利用期货或期权帮助客户抵销相应风险。

🛡️ **Core idea: spot-market risk + derivatives hedge = reduced price exposure.** ｜ 🛡️ **核心逻辑：现货风险＋衍生品对冲＝降低价格风险。**

------

##### (IV) Over-the-Counter Derivatives Business ｜ （四）场外衍生品业务

Over-the-counter derivatives business refers to transactions in which a risk management company enters directly into customised derivatives agreements with counterparties. ｜ 场外衍生品业务是指风险管理公司根据与交易对手达成的协议，直接开展个性化场外衍生品交易。

These agreements can be customised according to the client’s commodity, quantity, maturity, price and risk-management needs. They help solve the mismatch between standardised exchange-traded contracts and the individual needs of real-sector enterprises. ｜ 场外协议可以根据客户的商品、数量、期限、价格以及风险管理需求进行个性化设计，从而解决场内标准化合约与实体企业个性化需求不匹配的问题。

Clients may also achieve similar risk-management effects with lower initial capital requirements by using OTC derivatives. ｜ 企业还可以通过场外衍生品，以相对较低的初始资金成本实现风险管理。

------

###### 🌾 “Insurance + Futures” Model ｜ 🌾 “保险＋期货”模式

“Insurance + futures” is an important application of over-the-counter derivatives, especially in agricultural risk management. ｜ “保险＋期货”是场外衍生品业务的重要应用形式，主要用于农业价格风险管理。

The basic process is: ｜ 基本流程如下：

**Farmer or cooperative → Insurance company → Risk management company → Futures market** ｜ **农户或合作社 → 保险公司 → 风险管理公司 → 期货市场**

1. Farmers or agricultural cooperatives purchase agricultural price insurance from an insurance company. ｜ 农户或者合作社向保险公司购买农产品价格保险。
2. The insurance company purchases an over-the-counter option from a risk management company to hedge its insurance liability. ｜ 保险公司向风险管理公司购买场外期权，对冲保险赔付风险。
3. The risk management company transfers and hedges the risk through futures or options traded on the exchange. ｜ 风险管理公司再通过期货或者场内期权转移和对冲风险。

This creates a closed risk-management chain in which agricultural producers receive insurance protection, insurers transfer their risk and risk management companies hedge through the futures market. ｜ 由此形成完整的风险管理闭环：农业经营者获得保险保障，保险公司转移赔付风险，风险管理公司通过期货市场完成风险对冲。

📌 **Core chain: insurance protects farmers, OTC options protect insurers, and futures hedge the final market risk.** ｜ 📌 **核心链条：保险保障农户，场外期权保障保险公司，期货市场对冲最终风险。**

------

##### (V) Market-Making Business ｜ （五）做市业务

Market-making refers to the provision of continuous quotations or responses to quotation requests for specified futures, options and other derivatives contracts according to exchange rules. ｜ 做市业务是指风险管理公司按照交易所规则，为特定的期货、期权等衍生品合约提供连续报价或者回应报价的服务。

A market maker normally provides both a bid price and an ask price, allowing other traders to buy or sell more easily. ｜ 做市商通常同时提供买入价和卖出价，使其他交易者能够更方便地完成买卖。

Market-making helps improve market liquidity, price continuity and trading efficiency. ｜ 做市业务有助于提高市场流动性、价格连续性和交易效率。

💧 **Core role: continuously provide prices and improve liquidity.** ｜ 💧 **核心作用：持续提供报价，提高市场流动性。**

------

#### III. Core Summary ｜ 三、核心总结

📊 **Basis trading:** links futures pricing with spot transactions. ｜ 📊 **基差贸易：**将期货定价与现货贸易相结合。

📦 **Warehouse receipt services:** support financing, circulation and inventory management. ｜ 📦 **仓单服务：**支持融资、货物流转和库存管理。

🛡️ **Cooperative hedging:** helps enterprises offset spot price risks. ｜ 🛡️ **合作套保：**帮助企业对冲现货价格风险。

🤝 **OTC derivatives:** provide customised risk-management contracts. ｜ 🤝 **场外衍生品：**提供个性化风险管理合约。

🌾 **Insurance + futures:** transfers agricultural price risk through insurance, OTC options and futures. ｜ 🌾 **保险＋期货：**通过保险、场外期权和期货逐层转移农业价格风险。

💧 **Market making:** provides continuous quotations and improves liquidity. ｜ 💧 **做市业务：**提供连续报价并提高市场流动性。

下面删除 OCR 杂字，只保留知识点，并继续保持“英文在前、中文在后”和原有标题层级。

### 🤝 Examination Topic: Futures Intermediaries ｜ 🤝 考点：居间人

#### I. Definition ｜ 一、概念

A futures intermediary is an institution or individual entrusted by a futures company to provide intermediary services for the conclusion of futures brokerage contracts. The intermediary independently bears the civil liabilities arising from its services, while the futures company pays remuneration according to the agreement. ｜ 居间人是指受期货公司委托，为期货公司订立期货经纪合同提供中介服务的机构或者自然人。居间人独立承担因中介服务产生的民事责任，期货公司按照约定向其支付报酬。

📌 **Core role: introducing investors to futures companies and facilitating the conclusion of brokerage contracts.** ｜ 📌 **核心作用：向期货公司介绍投资者，并促成双方签订期货经纪合同。**

------

#### II. Classification ｜ 二、分类

Futures intermediaries mainly include securities companies acting as introducing brokers and natural persons providing intermediary services. ｜ 期货居间人主要分为两类：作为介绍经纪商的证券公司，以及提供居间服务的自然人。

##### (I) Securities Company as an Introducing Broker ｜ （一）券商IB

###### 1. Definition ｜ 1. 定义

An Introducing Broker, or IB, is a securities company entrusted by a futures company to introduce clients and provide certain supporting services for futures trading. The futures company pays the securities company a commission for these services. ｜ 券商IB是指受期货公司委托，将客户介绍给期货公司，并为客户参与期货交易提供一定辅助服务的证券公司。期货公司按照约定向证券公司支付佣金。

📌 **IB = Introducing Broker** ｜ 📌 **IB＝介绍经纪商**

###### 2. Services Provided ｜ 2. 提供的服务

A securities company acting as an IB may provide the following services: ｜ 证券公司从事中间介绍业务，可以提供以下服务：

1. Assist clients in completing futures account-opening procedures. ｜ 协助客户办理期货账户开户手续。
2. Provide futures market information and trading facilities. ｜ 向客户提供期货行情信息和交易设施。
3. Provide other services permitted by the China Securities Regulatory Commission. ｜ 提供中国证监会规定的其他服务。

###### 3. Prohibited Activities ｜ 3. 禁止事项

A securities company acting as an IB may only introduce clients and provide supporting services. It may not directly handle futures trading, settlement, delivery or margin funds. ｜ 券商IB只能提供客户介绍和辅助服务，不得直接办理期货交易、结算、交割或者保证金收付。

Specifically, it may not: ｜ 具体而言，证券公司不得：

1. Execute futures transactions on behalf of clients. ｜ 代理客户进行期货交易。
2. Conduct futures settlement or delivery on behalf of clients. ｜ 代理客户办理期货结算或者交割。
3. Receive or pay futures margin on behalf of a futures company or client. ｜ 代期货公司或者客户收付期货保证金。
4. Use securities accounts to deposit, withdraw or transfer futures margin. ｜ 利用证券资金账户为客户存取或者划转期货保证金。

🚫 **Core boundary: an IB may introduce and assist, but may not trade or handle client funds.** ｜ 🚫 **核心边界：券商IB可以介绍和协助，但不能代理交易或者经手客户资金。**

###### 4. Written Entrustment Agreement ｜ 4. 书面委托协议

A securities company must sign a written entrustment agreement with the futures company before conducting IB business. ｜ 证券公司从事IB业务前，应当与期货公司签订书面委托协议。

The agreement should specify: ｜ 委托协议应当载明：

1. The scope of the introducing business. ｜ 中间介绍业务的范围。
2. Measures for implementing the futures margin custody system. ｜ 执行期货保证金安全存管制度的措施。
3. Rules for cooperation and business coordination. ｜ 中间介绍业务的对接规则。
4. Procedures for receiving and handling client complaints. ｜ 客户投诉的接待和处理方式。
5. The payment of remuneration and allocation of related expenses. ｜ 报酬支付及相关费用的分担方式。
6. Liability for breach of contract. ｜ 违约责任。
7. Other matters required by the China Securities Regulatory Commission. ｜ 中国证监会规定的其他事项。

###### 5. Business Qualification ｜ 5. 业务资格

According to the supplied material, administrative approval for securities-company IB qualifications was cancelled in October 2012, and securities companies conducting IB business are subject to filing requirements. ｜ 根据原材料，2012年10月，我国取消了券商IB业务资格的行政审批，证券公司开展中间介绍业务改为依法备案。

The arrangement allows a securities company to act as an IB for a futures company and also allows a futures company to provide introducing services for a securities company. ｜ 这种制度既允许证券公司成为期货公司的IB，也允许期货公司为证券公司提供相应的中间介绍服务。

------

##### (II) Natural Persons Providing Intermediary Services ｜ （二）开展居间服务的自然人

###### 1. Basic Requirements ｜ 1. 基本条件

A natural person providing futures intermediary services should satisfy the following basic requirements: ｜ 自然人从事期货居间服务，应当满足以下基本条件：

1. Be at least 18 years old and have full capacity for civil conduct. ｜ 年满18周岁，并具有完全民事行为能力。
2. Have good personal conduct and professional ethics. ｜ 品行端正，具有良好的职业道德。
3. Undertake to comply with relevant laws, regulations and intermediary-management rules. ｜ 承诺遵守相关法律法规以及居间人管理规定。
4. Hold a certificate showing a passing result in the futures practitioner qualification examination. ｜ 取得期货从业人员资格考试成绩合格证明。
5. Complete the training courses required by the China Futures Association. ｜ 完成中国期货业协会要求的培训课程。
6. Meet other conditions required by the association. ｜ 符合中国期货业协会规定的其他条件。

A natural-person intermediary may simultaneously sign intermediary contracts with no more than three futures companies. ｜ 自然人居间人可以同时与不超过三家期货公司签订居间合同。

###### 2. Disqualifying Circumstances ｜ 2. 不得从事居间服务的情形

A natural person may not conduct futures intermediary services under any of the following circumstances: ｜ 自然人存在下列情形之一的，不得开展期货居间服务：

1. The person has received criminal punishment within the previous three years. ｜ 最近三年内受到过刑事处罚。
2. The person is subject to a market-entry prohibition imposed by financial regulators and the prohibition period has not expired. ｜ 被中国证监会等金融监管部门采取市场禁入措施，且禁入期限尚未届满。
3. The person has received an administrative penalty or administrative regulatory measure from the China Securities Regulatory Commission or its local offices within the previous three years. ｜ 最近三年内受到中国证监会或者其派出机构的行政处罚或行政监管措施。
4. The person is subject to a qualification restriction imposed by the China Futures Association and the restriction period has not expired. ｜ 被中国期货业协会采取资格限制纪律处分，且限制期限尚未届满。
5. The person has been included on the association’s list of dishonest intermediaries. ｜ 被中国期货业协会列入居间人失信名单。
6. The person falls under other circumstances determined by regulators or the association. ｜ 存在中国证监会或者中国期货业协会认定的其他情形。

###### 3. Important Notes ｜ 3. 注意事项

A futures intermediary is independent from the futures company and does not have an employment or subordinate relationship with it. ｜ 居间人与期货公司之间不存在劳动关系或者隶属关系。

The intermediary is not a party to the futures brokerage contract signed between the investor and the futures company. ｜ 居间人不是投资者与期货公司所签期货经纪合同的当事人。

Employees of a futures company and their immediate family members may not act as intermediaries for that company or another futures company. ｜ 期货公司的在职人员及其直系亲属，不得成为本公司或者其他期货公司的居间人。

📌 **Core relationship: the intermediary introduces the contract but is not a party to the contract.** ｜ 📌 **核心关系：居间人促成合同，但不是期货经纪合同的当事人。**

###### 4. Scope of Permitted Activities ｜ 4. 合作范围

A natural-person intermediary may conduct the following activities: ｜ 自然人居间人可以开展以下活动：

1. Introduce investors to a futures company and facilitate the signing of futures brokerage contracts. ｜ 向期货公司介绍投资者，并促成投资者与期货公司签订期货经纪合同。
2. Introduce the basic information of the futures company to investors. ｜ 向投资者介绍期货公司的基本情况。
3. Explain basic futures-investment knowledge, including account opening, trading, additional margin, forced liquidation and fund transfers. ｜ 向投资者介绍期货投资的基本常识，包括开户、交易、追加保证金、强行平仓和资金存取等。
