4. Introduce relevant laws, regulations, departmental rules, self-regulatory rules and futures-company regulations. ｜ 向投资者介绍与期货投资相关的法律法规、部门规章、自律规则以及期货公司的相关制度。
5. Deliver promotional materials uniformly prepared by the futures company. ｜ 向投资者传递由期货公司统一提供的宣传推介材料。
6. Conduct other activities recognised by the China Futures Association. ｜ 开展中国期货业协会认定的其他活动。

------

#### III. Core Summary ｜ 三、核心总结

🏢 **Securities-company IB:** introduces clients and assists with account opening, but cannot trade or handle margin. ｜ 🏢 **券商IB：**负责介绍客户和协助开户，但不能代理交易或者经手保证金。

👤 **Natural-person intermediary:** introduces investors and explains basic information, but is independent from the futures company. ｜ 👤 **自然人居间人：**负责介绍投资者和说明基本信息，但与期货公司不存在隶属关系。

🚫 **Common restriction:** intermediaries may not promise returns, trade for clients or directly handle client funds. ｜ 🚫 **共同边界：**居间人只能提供介绍和辅助服务，不应代客交易或者直接经手客户资金。



### 🧩 Examination Topic: Other Service Institutions in the Futures Market ｜ 🧩 考点：期货市场其他服务机构

#### I. China Futures Market Monitoring Center ｜ 一、中国期货市场监控中心

The China Futures Market Monitoring Center provides unified account-opening services for the futures market, allows investors to access trading and settlement information, compiles and publishes commodity and other indices, provides information services to regulators and futures exchanges, and conducts macroeconomic and industry research. ｜ 中国期货市场监控中心负责期货市场统一开户，为投资者提供交易结算信息查询，编制和发布商品及其他指数，为监管机构和期货交易所提供信息服务，并开展宏观经济和产业分析研究。

It also monitors the safety of futures margin, supervises the operation of the futures market and futures intermediaries, conducts market investigations, assists in handling market risks, and manages the Futures Investor Protection Fund. ｜ 此外，中国期货市场监控中心还负责监控期货保证金安全、监测期货市场运行和期货中介机构、开展期货市场调查、协助处置市场风险，并代管期货投资者保障基金。

🔍 **Core functions: information services, margin monitoring, market supervision and investor protection.** ｜ 🔍 **核心职能：信息服务、保证金监控、市场监测和投资者保护。**

------

#### II. Futures Margin Depository Banks ｜ 二、期货保证金存管银行

A futures margin depository bank is a bank designated by a futures exchange to assist with the settlement of futures transactions and the custody and transfer of futures margin. ｜ 期货保证金存管银行是由期货交易所指定，协助交易所办理期货交易结算以及保证金存管和划转业务的银行。

Its main role is to ensure that client margin is securely deposited, transferred and settled through designated accounts. ｜ 其主要作用是确保客户保证金通过专用账户安全存放、划转和结算。

🏦 **Core function: safeguarding and transferring futures margin.** ｜ 🏦 **核心职能：安全存管和划转期货保证金。**

------

#### III. Delivery Warehouses ｜ 三、交割仓库

A delivery warehouse is a service institution designated by a futures exchange to provide physical delivery services and issue standard warehouse receipts when a futures contract enters the delivery stage. ｜ 交割仓库是由期货交易所指定，在期货合约进入实物交割阶段时提供交割服务并生成标准仓单的期货服务机构。

Its daily operations mainly include three stages: receiving commodities into storage, storing and safeguarding commodities, and releasing commodities from storage. ｜ 交割仓库的日常业务主要分为三个阶段：商品入库、商品保管和商品出库。

📦 **Core process: receipt into storage → custody → release from storage.** ｜ 📦 **核心流程：商品入库 → 商品保管 → 商品出库。**

------

#### IV. China Securities Commodity Index Company ｜ 四、中证商品指数公司

China Securities Commodity Index Co., Ltd. was jointly established in December 2020 by the Shanghai Futures Exchange, Zhengzhou Commodity Exchange, Dalian Commodity Exchange and China Financial Futures Exchange. ｜ 中证商品指数有限责任公司于2020年12月由上海期货交易所、郑州商品交易所、大连商品交易所和中国金融期货交易所共同出资设立。

It is China’s first national platform specialising in the compilation and operation of commodity indices. ｜ 它是我国首家全国性商品指数编制与运营服务平台。

Its main functions include compiling, maintaining, publishing and licensing commodity indices, macroeconomic indices, government bond indices and other financial indices, as well as providing related data and technical services. ｜ 其主要职能包括商品指数、宏观指数、国债指数等金融指数的编制、维护、发布和授权，并提供相关数据与技术服务。

📊 **Core function: compiling and operating commodity and financial indices.** ｜ 📊 **核心职能：编制和运营商品及金融指数。**

------

#### V. Futures Information Technology Service Institutions ｜ 五、期货信息技术服务机构

Futures information technology service institutions provide futures quotation software, trading systems and other related information technology services. ｜ 期货信息技术服务机构主要提供期货行情软件、交易系统以及其他相关信息技术服务。

They provide the technical infrastructure required for market-data transmission, order execution and trading-system operation. ｜ 它们为行情传输、交易指令执行以及交易系统运行提供技术基础设施。

💻 **Core function: providing market-data and trading-system technology.** ｜ 💻 **核心职能：提供行情和交易系统技术服务。**

------

#### VI. Core Summary ｜ 六、核心总结

🔍 **Monitoring Center:** supervises margin, market operations and investor information. ｜ 🔍 **监控中心：**监控保证金、市场运行和投资者信息。

🏦 **Depository Bank:** safeguards and transfers futures margin. ｜ 🏦 **存管银行：**存管并划转期货保证金。

📦 **Delivery Warehouse:** completes physical delivery and issues warehouse receipts. ｜ 📦 **交割仓库：**完成实物交割并出具仓单。

📊 **Commodity Index Company:** compiles and operates commodity and financial indices. ｜ 📊 **商品指数公司：**编制和运营商品及金融指数。

💻 **IT Service Institution:** provides quotation software and trading systems. ｜ 💻 **信息技术机构：**提供行情软件和交易系统。

## 👥 Section 4: Futures Traders ｜ 👥 第四节 期货交易者

### 📌 Examination Topic: Classification and Requirements of Futures Traders ｜ 📌 考点：期货交易者的分类与参与条件



#### I. Classification of Futures Traders ｜ 一、期货交易者的分类

##### (I) Classification by Trading Purpose ｜ （一）按照进入期货市场的目的分类

Futures traders may be divided into hedgers and speculators according to their trading purposes. ｜ 按照进入期货市场的目的，期货交易者可以分为套期保值者和投机者。

🛡️ **Hedgers**

Hedgers use futures contracts to reduce the risk of price fluctuations arising from their spot-market production, operation or investment activities. ｜ 套期保值者通过买卖期货合约，降低现货生产经营或投资活动中面临的价格波动风险。

📈 **Speculators**

Speculators use their own funds to trade futures contracts in order to earn profits from price movements. They buy when they expect prices to rise and sell when they expect prices to fall. ｜ 投机者通过投入资金参与期货交易，利用价格波动获取收益。预期价格上涨时买入，预期价格下跌时卖出。

📌 **Hedgers reduce existing risks, while speculators actively assume price risks to seek profits.** ｜ 📌 **套期保值者主要减少已有风险，投机者则主动承担价格风险以获取收益。**

##### (II) Classification by Legal Status ｜ （二）按照交易者的法律身份分类

Futures traders may be divided into individual investors and institutional investors. ｜ 按照交易者是自然人还是法人，可以分为个人投资者和机构投资者。

- **Individual investors:** natural persons participating in futures trading ｜ **个人投资者：**以自然人身份参与期货交易的投资者
- **Institutional investors:** legal persons or other organisations participating in futures trading ｜ **机构投资者：**以法人或者其他组织身份参与期货交易的投资者

##### (III) Classification by Account Identity and Trading Characteristics ｜ （三）按照开户身份和交易特征分类

According to account identity and trading characteristics, traders may be classified as natural-person clients, industrial clients, special legal-person clients and futures asset-management clients. ｜ 按照开户身份和交易特征，交易者可以分为自然人客户、产业客户、特殊法人客户和期货资产管理客户等。

🏭 **Industrial clients** participate mainly to manage risks arising from production, processing, procurement or sales. ｜ 🏭 **产业客户**主要为了管理生产、加工、采购或者销售过程中的价格风险。

💼 **Futures asset-management clients** participate through futures asset-management products or accounts. ｜ 💼 **期货资管客户**通过期货资产管理产品或者账户参与市场。

##### (IV) Classification by Investor Suitability ｜ （四）按照投资者适当性分类

According to factors such as financial condition, financial assets, investment knowledge, experience and professional capability, traders may be divided into ordinary traders and professional traders. ｜ 根据财产状况、金融资产状况、投资知识、投资经验和专业能力等因素，交易者可以分为普通交易者和专业交易者。

👤 **Ordinary traders** generally have relatively limited professional knowledge and risk-bearing capacity and therefore require stronger investor protection. ｜ 👤 **普通交易者**的专业知识和风险承受能力通常相对有限，因此需要受到更充分的投资者保护。

🎓 **Professional traders** generally possess stronger financial resources, professional knowledge, investment experience and risk-identification能力.
🎓 **专业交易者**通常具有更强的资金实力、专业知识、投资经验和风险识别能力。

#### II. Individual Investors ｜ 二、个人投资者

##### (I) Requirements for Opening a Financial Futures Trading Code ｜ （一）申请开立金融期货交易编码的条件

An individual investor applying for a financial futures trading code must satisfy the following conditions. ｜ 个人投资者申请开立金融期货交易编码，应当满足以下条件。

1. The available balance in the margin account must be no less than RMB 500,000. ｜ 保证金账户可用资金余额不得低于人民币50万元。
2. The investor must possess basic knowledge of financial futures and pass the relevant test. ｜ 投资者应当具备金融期货基础知识，并通过相关测试。
3. The investor must have either at least 20 financial-futures simulation trades over a cumulative period of 10 trading days, or at least 10 futures trades during the previous three years. ｜ 投资者应当具有累计10个交易日、20笔以上的金融期货仿真交易记录，或者最近三年内具有10笔以上期货交易记录。
4. The investor must not have any serious adverse credit records. ｜ 投资者不得存在严重不良诚信记录。
5. The investor must not be prohibited or restricted from financial futures trading by laws, administrative regulations, rules or exchange business rules. ｜ 投资者不得存在法律、行政法规、规章或者交易所业务规则禁止或限制从事金融期货交易的情形。

🧠 **Memory aid: funds + knowledge + experience + credit + legal eligibility.** ｜ 🧠 **记忆要点：资金、知识、经历、诚信、合规。**

##### (II) Requirements for Participating in Options Trading ｜ （二）参与期权交易的条件

The source lists the following requirements for individual investors participating in options trading. ｜ 原材料列出了个人投资者参与期权交易的以下条件。

1. At the end of the previous trading day, the total value of securities and available funds must be no less than RMB 500,000. ｜ 上一交易日日终，证券市值与资金可用余额合计不得低于人民币50万元。
2. The investor must have maintained a futures margin account with a futures company for at least six months and have experience in financial futures trading. ｜ 投资者应当在期货公司开立期货保证金账户六个月以上，并具有金融期货交易经历。
3. The investor must possess basic knowledge of options and pass a test recognised by the exchange. ｜ 投资者应当具备期权基础知识，并通过交易所认可的相关测试。
4. The investor must have options simulation trading experience recognised by the exchange. ｜ 投资者应当具有交易所认可的期权模拟交易经历。
5. The investor must possess the corresponding risk-bearing capacity. ｜ 投资者应当具有与期权交易相匹配的风险承受能力。
6. The investor must not have serious adverse credit records or be prohibited or restricted from options trading by laws, regulations, rules or exchange business rules. ｜ 投资者不得存在严重不良诚信记录，也不得存在法律、法规、规章或者交易所业务规则禁止或限制从事期权交易的情形。
7. The investor must satisfy other conditions prescribed by the Shanghai Stock Exchange. ｜ 投资者还应当符合上海证券交易所规定的其他条件。

🧠 **Memory aid: assets + account experience + knowledge + simulation + risk capacity + credit + other requirements.** ｜ 🧠 **记忆要点：资产、账户经历、知识、模拟交易、风险能力、诚信、其他条件。**

#### III. Core Summary ｜ 三、核心总结

🛡️ **Hedger:** uses futures to reduce spot-market risk. ｜ 🛡️ **套期保值者：**利用期货降低现货市场风险。

📈 **Speculator:** assumes price risk to obtain trading profits. ｜ 📈 **投机者：**承担价格风险以获取交易收益。

👤 **Individual investor:** participates as a natural person. ｜ 👤 **个人投资者：**以自然人身份参与交易。

🏢 **Institutional investor:** participates as a legal person or organisation. ｜ 🏢 **机构投资者：**以法人或者其他组织身份参与交易。

📋 **Participation requirements:** financial strength, knowledge, trading experience, risk capacity, good credit and legal eligibility. ｜ 📋 **参与条件：**具备资金实力、专业知识、交易经历、风险承受能力、良好诚信和合法资格。

下面继续按你前面的格式整理，删除 OCR 杂字和重复内容，只保留核心知识，并把商品投资基金的参与主体关系理清。

#### III. Institutional Investors ｜ 三、机构投资者

##### (I) Special Institutional Clients and General Institutional Clients ｜ （一）特殊单位客户和一般单位客户

🏦 **1. Special Institutional Clients** ｜ 🏦 **1. 特殊单位客户**

Special institutional clients are financial institutions or other institutions with specialised investment qualifications and risk-management capabilities. ｜ 特殊单位客户是具有专业投资资格和较强风险管理能力的金融机构或者其他机构。

They mainly include: ｜ 主要包括：

1. Securities companies ｜ 证券公司
2. Fund management companies ｜ 基金管理公司
3. Trust companies ｜ 信托公司
4. Banks and other financial institutions ｜ 银行及其他金融机构
5. Social security institutions ｜ 社会保障类机构
6. Qualified Foreign Institutional Investors, or QFIIs ｜ 合格境外机构投资者，即QFII

🏢 **2. General Institutional Clients** ｜ 🏢 **2. 一般单位客户**

General institutional clients refer to institutional investors other than special institutional clients. ｜ 一般单位客户是指特殊单位客户以外的其他机构投资者。

📌 **Core distinction: special institutional clients have professional financial qualifications, while general institutional clients are other ordinary organisations.** ｜ 📌 **核心区别：特殊单位客户通常具有专业金融资质，一般单位客户则是其他普通机构。**

------

##### (II) Professional and Ordinary Institutional Investors ｜ （二）专业机构投资者和普通机构投资者

This classification is mainly used in China’s stock options market. ｜ 这种分类主要适用于我国股票期权市场。

🎓 **1. Professional Institutional Investors** ｜ 🎓 **1. 专业机构投资者**

Professional institutional investors mainly include the following three categories. ｜ 专业机构投资者主要包括以下三类。

**① Professional financial institutions and their branches** ｜ **① 专业金融机构及其分支机构**

These include commercial banks, options business institutions, insurance institutions, trust companies, fund management companies, finance companies and Qualified Foreign Institutional Investors. ｜ 主要包括商业银行、期权经营机构、保险机构、信托公司、基金管理公司、财务公司和合格境外机构投资者等。

**② Professional investment funds and asset-management products** ｜ **② 专业投资基金和资产管理产品**

These include securities investment funds, social security funds, pension funds, enterprise annuities, trust plans, asset-management plans, bank wealth-management products and insurance wealth-management products. ｜ 主要包括证券投资基金、社保基金、养老基金、企业年金、信托计划、资产管理计划、银行理财产品和保险理财产品等。

Other funds or entrusted assets managed by professional financial institutions also fall into this category. ｜ 由专业金融机构担任管理人的其他基金或者受托投资资产，也属于专业机构投资者。

**③ Other professional institutional investors** ｜ **③ 其他专业机构投资者**

These include other professional institutional investors recognised by regulators or the relevant exchange. ｜ 主要包括监管机构或者交易所规定的其他专业机构投资者。

👥 **2. Ordinary Institutional Investors** ｜ 👥 **2. 普通机构投资者**

Ordinary institutional investors refer to institutional clients that do not meet the definition of professional institutional investors. ｜ 普通机构投资者是指专业机构投资者以外的其他机构客户。

📌 **Core distinction: professional institutions have stronger professional capabilities and investment experience; other institutional clients are classified as ordinary institutional investors.** ｜ 📌 **核心区别：专业机构具有较强的专业能力和投资经验，其他机构客户则属于普通机构投资者。**

------

##### (III) Commodity Investment Funds ｜ （三）商品投资基金的相关概念

**1. Definition of a Commodity Investment Fund** ｜ **1. 商品投资基金的定义**

A commodity investment fund is a collective investment arrangement in which investors pool their funds and entrust professional institutions to conduct futures and options trading through Commodity Trading Advisors. ｜ 商品投资基金是指投资者集中资金，并委托专业投资机构通过商品交易顾问进行期货和期权交易的一种集合投资方式。

Investors bear the investment risks and receive the corresponding investment returns. ｜ 投资者承担投资风险，并享有相应的投资收益。

💰 **Investors provide funds → professional institutions manage the fund → futures and options are traded.** ｜ 💰 **投资者提供资金 → 专业机构管理基金 → 进行期货和期权交易。**

------

**2. Organisational Structure of a US Commodity Investment Fund** ｜ **2. 美国商品投资基金的组织结构**

The main participants include the Commodity Pool Operator, Commodity Trading Advisor, Trading Manager, Futures Commission Merchant and Custodian. ｜ 主要参与者包括商品基金经理、商品交易顾问、交易经理、期货佣金商和托管人。

🔹 **Commodity Pool Operator, CPO** ｜ 🔹 **商品基金经理，CPO**

The CPO is the principal manager, designer and decision-maker of the fund. ｜ CPO是基金的主要管理人、设计者和经营决策者。

It determines the method of fund issuance, selects the principal participants and decides the overall investment direction. ｜ 其主要职责包括决定基金发行方式、选择基金主要参与者以及确定基金的总体投资方向。

📌 **CPO decides how the fund is organised and managed.** ｜ 📌 **CPO负责决定基金如何设立和管理。**

------

🔹 **Commodity Trading Advisor, CTA** ｜ 🔹 **商品交易顾问，CTA**

A CTA may provide advice on buying and selling futures and options or conduct transactions on behalf of clients. ｜ CTA可以向客户提供期货和期权交易建议，也可以根据授权以客户名义进行交易。

The CTA is normally appointed by the CPO and is responsible for formulating and implementing specific futures and options trading strategies. ｜ CTA通常由CPO聘请，负责制定并执行具体的期货和期权交易策略。

📌 **CPO manages the fund, while CTA conducts the actual investment trading.** ｜ 📌 **CPO负责管理基金，CTA负责具体投资交易。**

------

🔹 **Trading Manager, TM** ｜ 🔹 **交易经理，TM**

The trading manager is appointed by the CPO. ｜ 交易经理由CPO聘请。

Its main duties are to help select CTAs, monitor their trading activities, control risks and allocate fund assets among different CTAs. ｜ 其主要职责包括协助CPO选择CTA、监督CTA的交易活动、控制风险，并在不同CTA之间分配基金资产。

📌 **TM selects, monitors and coordinates CTAs.** ｜ 📌 **TM负责选择、监督和协调CTA。**

------

🔹 **Futures Commission Merchant, FCM** ｜ 🔹 **期货佣金商，FCM**

The FCM is a major futures intermediary in the United States. ｜ FCM是美国期货市场中的主要中介机构。

It executes the trading instructions issued by CTAs and manages the margin associated with futures positions. ｜ 其主要职责是执行CTA发出的交易指令，并管理期货头寸所需要的保证金。

📌 **CTA makes the trading decision; FCM executes the order and manages margin.** ｜ 📌 **CTA作出交易决策，FCM执行交易指令并管理保证金。**

------

🔹 **Custodian** ｜ 🔹 **托管人**

The custodian safeguards the fund’s assets and supervises the operation of the fund. ｜ 托管人负责保管基金资产，并监督基金的运作。

Custodians are generally independent financial institutions, such as commercial banks, savings banks or large investment companies. ｜ 托管人通常是商业银行、储蓄银行或者大型投资公司等独立金融机构。

📌 **The custodian holds the assets but does not make investment decisions.** ｜ 📌 **托管人负责保管资产，但不负责作出投资决策。**

------

🧠 **Organisational Relationship** ｜ 🧠 **组织关系**

**Investors → CPO → TM and CTA → FCM → Futures and Options Markets** ｜ **投资者 → CPO → TM和CTA → FCM → 期货和期权市场**

- CPO manages the entire fund. ｜ CPO负责基金的整体管理。
- TM selects and supervises CTAs. ｜ TM负责选择和监督CTA。
- CTA formulates trading strategies. ｜ CTA负责制定交易策略。
- FCM executes orders and manages margin. ｜ FCM负责执行交易指令和管理保证金。
- The custodian independently safeguards fund assets. ｜ 托管人独立保管基金资产。

------

**3. Hedge Funds** ｜ **3. 对冲基金**

A hedge fund, also known as a hedged fund, originally referred to a fund that used hedging strategies to reduce investment risks. ｜ 对冲基金又称避险基金，最初是指通过对冲操作降低投资风险的基金。

Modern hedge funds often make extensive use of futures, options and other financial derivatives, as well as leverage, to pursue relatively high returns while assuming relatively high risks. ｜ 现代对冲基金通常会大量使用期货、期权等金融衍生品和杠杆工具，在承担较高风险的同时追求较高收益。

📌 **Original purpose: hedge risks.** ｜ 📌 **最初目的：对冲和降低风险。**

📈 **Modern model: use leverage and derivatives to pursue high returns.** ｜ 📈 **现代模式：利用杠杆和衍生品追求较高收益。**

------

#### IV. Core Summary ｜ 四、核心总结

🏦 **Special institutional clients:** professional financial institutions and qualified investors. ｜ 🏦 **特殊单位客户：**专业金融机构和具有特定资格的投资者。

🏢 **General institutional clients:** institutional investors other than special institutional clients. ｜ 🏢 **一般单位客户：**特殊单位客户以外的其他机构投资者。

🎓 **Professional institutional investors:** professional institutions, investment funds and asset-management products. ｜ 🎓 **专业机构投资者：**专业金融机构、投资基金和资产管理产品。

💰 **Commodity investment fund:** investors pool funds for professional futures and options investment. ｜ 💰 **商品投资基金：**投资者集中资金，由专业机构进行期货和期权投资。

🧑‍💼 **CPO:** manages the entire fund. ｜ 🧑‍💼 **CPO：**负责基金整体管理。

📊 **CTA:** formulates and executes trading strategies. ｜ 📊 **CTA：**负责制定和执行交易策略。

🔍 **TM:** selects and monitors CTAs. ｜ 🔍 **TM：**负责选择和监督CTA。

⚙️ **FCM:** executes orders and manages margin. ｜ ⚙️ **FCM：**负责执行交易指令和管理保证金。

🏦 **Custodian:** safeguards fund assets. ｜ 🏦 **托管人：**负责保管基金资产。

#### IV. Market Makers ｜ 四、做市商

##### (I) Definition of a Market Maker ｜ （一）做市商的定义

A market maker is a legal person or an unincorporated organisation recognised by an exchange to provide two-way quotations and other services for designated futures or options contracts. It continuously publishes bid and ask prices and uses its own funds to trade with investors at those quoted prices. ｜ 做市商是指经交易所认可，为指定期货或期权合约提供双边报价等服务的法人或者非法人组织。做市商持续向市场报出买入价和卖出价，并使用自有资金按照报价与投资者进行交易。

💧 **Core function: continuously provide bid and ask prices to improve market liquidity.** ｜ 💧 **核心作用：持续提供买卖报价，提高市场流动性。**

------

##### (II) Definition of the Market-Making System ｜ （二）做市商制度的定义

The market-making system is a trading mechanism in which market makers provide quotations, facilitate transactions and support the formation of market prices. ｜ 做市商制度是指由做市商持续提供报价，以报价促进交易并推动市场价格形成的交易机制。

📌 **Market maker quotes → investors trade → market liquidity and price continuity improve.** ｜ 📌 **做市商报价 → 投资者成交 → 提高市场流动性和价格连续性。**

------

##### (III) Requirements for Market Makers ｜ （三）做市商应当具备的条件

An applicant for market-maker status should satisfy the following conditions: ｜ 申请成为做市商，应当具备以下条件：

1. Net assets of no less than RMB 50 million. ｜ 净资产不得低于人民币5000万元。
2. A specialised department and qualified personnel responsible for market-making activities. ｜ 具有专门负责做市交易的机构和人员，相关人员应熟悉法律法规及交易所业务规则。
3. A comprehensive market-making plan, internal-control system and risk-management system. ｜ 具有健全的做市交易实施方案、内部控制制度和风险管理制度。
4. No major violations of laws or regulations during the previous three years. ｜ 最近三年内不存在重大违法违规记录。
5. A stable and reliable market-making technology system. ｜ 具有稳定、可靠的做市交易技术系统。
6. Trading, market-making or simulated market-making experience recognised by the exchange. ｜ 具有交易所认可的交易、做市或者仿真做市经历。
7. Other conditions prescribed by the exchange. ｜ 符合交易所规定的其他条件。

🧠 **Memory aid: capital + personnel + systems + compliance + technology + experience.** ｜ 🧠 **记忆要点：资金、人员、制度、合规、技术、经验。**

------

##### (IV) Exemptions from Quotation Obligations for Futures Market Makers ｜ （四）期货合约做市商报价义务的免除情形

A futures market maker may be exempted from quotation obligations under the following circumstances: ｜ 期货合约做市商在以下情形下可以免除报价义务：

1. During the opening call auction, quotation obligations for all designated contracts are suspended. ｜ 在期货合约开盘集合竞价期间，免除所有做市合约的报价义务。
2. When the principal contract specified in the agreement reaches its daily price limit, quotation obligations for all designated contracts of that product are suspended. ｜ 当协议约定的主力合约达到涨跌停板价格时，免除该品种所有做市合约的报价义务。
3. When a specific market-making contract reaches its daily price limit, quotation obligations for that contract are suspended. ｜ 当某一做市合约达到涨跌停板价格时，免除该合约的报价义务。
4. The exchange may grant an exemption under other circumstances recognised by the exchange or specified in the agreement. ｜ 出现交易所认定或者协议约定的其他情形时，可以免除相应的报价义务。

📌 **Core principle: quotation obligations may be suspended when normal pricing or trading becomes difficult.** ｜ 📌 **核心原则：当市场无法正常定价或交易时，可以暂时免除报价义务。**

------

##### (V) Exemptions from Quotation Obligations for Options Market Makers ｜ （五）期权合约做市商报价义务的免除情形

An options market maker may be exempted from quotation obligations under the following circumstances: ｜ 期权合约做市商在以下情形下可以免除报价义务：

1. During the opening call auction, quotation obligations for all designated options contracts are suspended. ｜ 在期权合约开盘集合竞价期间，免除所有做市合约的报价义务。
2. When the underlying futures contract reaches its daily price limit, quotation obligations for all options based on that futures contract are suspended. ｜ 当标的期货合约达到涨跌停板价格时，免除以该期货合约为标的的所有期权合约的报价义务。
3. When a specific options contract reaches its daily price limit, quotation obligations for that contract are suspended. ｜ 当某一期权合约达到涨跌停板价格时，免除该期权合约的报价义务。
4. When the market maker’s ask quotation for an options contract is below the level specified in the agreement, its bid quotation obligation for that contract may be suspended. ｜ 当做市商对某一期权合约的卖出报价低于协议规定的标准时，可以免除其对该合约的买入报价义务。
5. The exchange may grant an exemption under other circumstances recognised by the exchange or specified in the agreement. ｜ 出现交易所认定或者协议约定的其他情形时，可以免除相应的报价义务。

------

##### (VI) Trading Strategies and Risk Management of Derivatives Market Makers ｜ （六）衍生品做市商的交易策略与风险管理

Derivatives market makers require sufficient capital, professional personnel, reliable trading systems and appropriate pricing models. Options market makers commonly use the Black–Scholes–Merton model, or BSM model, to estimate the theoretical value of options and determine bid and ask quotations. ｜ 衍生品做市商需要具备充足的资金、专业人员、可靠的交易系统和适当的定价模型。期权做市商通常使用Black–Scholes–Merton模型，即BSM模型，计算期权理论价值并确定买卖报价。

⚖️ **1. Balanced Quotation Strategy** ｜ ⚖️ **1. 仓位均衡报价策略**

Market makers adjust bid and ask quotations to maintain a relatively balanced position and avoid excessive exposure in one direction. ｜ 做市商通过调整买入价和卖出价，使买卖仓位保持相对均衡，避免形成过大的单方向风险敞口。

📉 **2. Delta Hedging** ｜ 📉 **2. Delta对冲**

Delta measures how much an option’s price changes when the price of the underlying asset changes. Market makers dynamically buy or sell the underlying futures contract to reduce the directional price risk of their options positions. ｜ Delta反映标的资产价格变化时，期权价格的变动程度。做市商通过动态买卖标的期货合约，降低期权头寸面临的方向性价格风险。

📊 **3. Vega Hedging** ｜ 📊 **3. Vega对冲**

Vega measures how sensitive an option’s value is to changes in implied volatility. Market makers adjust their options portfolios to control the risk caused by volatility changes. ｜ Vega反映期权价值对隐含波动率变化的敏感程度。做市商通过调整期权组合，控制波动率变化带来的风险。

💰 **4. Edge Management** ｜ 💰 **4. Edge管理**

Edge is the difference between the market price and the theoretical price calculated by the market maker. ｜ Edge是市场价格与做市商计算的理论价格之间的差额。

When the market price is lower than the theoretical price, the market maker may buy because the contract appears undervalued. The buying edge equals the theoretical price minus the market price. ｜ 当市场价格低于理论价格时，做市商可以考虑买入，因为该合约可能被低估。买入Edge等于理论价格减去市场价格。

When the market price is higher than the theoretical price, the market maker may sell because the contract appears overvalued. The selling edge equals the market price minus the theoretical price. ｜ 当市场价格高于理论价格时，做市商可以考虑卖出，因为该合约可能被高估。卖出Edge等于市场价格减去理论价格。

📌 **Buy below theoretical value and sell above theoretical value to obtain a positive edge.** ｜ 📌 **低于理论价值时买入，高于理论价值时卖出，以获取正Edge。**

🛡️ **5. Quotation Risk Monitoring** ｜ 🛡️ **5. 报价风险监控**

Market makers must continuously monitor quotation spreads, quotation quantities and position risks. Warning mechanisms should be activated when quotations fail to meet exchange or agreement requirements. ｜ 做市商应当实时监控买卖价差、报价数量和持仓风险。当报价不符合交易所或者协议要求时，应及时触发风险预警。

------

#### V. Core Summary ｜ 五、核心总结

💧 **Market making:** continuously quote bid and ask prices to provide liquidity. ｜ 💧 **做市：**持续提供买卖报价，为市场提供流动性。

⚖️ **Balanced positions:** prevent excessive one-way exposure. ｜ ⚖️ **仓位均衡：**避免单方向风险敞口过大。

📉 **Delta hedging:** control the risk caused by movements in the underlying price. ｜ 📉 **Delta对冲：**控制标的资产价格变化风险。

📊 **Vega hedging:** control the risk caused by volatility changes. ｜ 📊 **Vega对冲：**控制波动率变化风险。

💰 **Edge management:** trade based on the difference between theoretical and market prices. ｜ 💰 **Edge管理：**根据理论价格与市场价格的差异进行交易。

🛡️ **Risk monitoring:** monitor spreads, quotation volumes and positions in real time. ｜ 🛡️ **风险监控：**实时监控价差、报价数量和持仓风险。

## 🧠 Chapter 2 Summary ｜ 🧠 第二章总结

Chapter 2 introduces the main participants and organisational structure of the futures market. ｜ 第二章主要介绍期货市场的组织结构和主要参与机构。

The chapter covers futures exchanges, clearing institutions, futures companies and related service institutions, intermediaries, and futures traders. ｜ 本章包括期货交易所、期货结算机构、期货公司与相关服务机构、居间人以及期货交易者。

📌 **Core structure:** exchanges organise trading, clearing institutions settle transactions, futures companies connect clients with exchanges, service institutions support market operations, and traders participate in the market. ｜ 📌 **核心结构：**交易所组织交易，结算机构负责结算，期货公司连接客户与交易所，服务机构支持市场运行，交易者参与市场交易。

# 📘 Chapter 3: Futures Contracts and Futures Trading Systems ｜ 📘 第三章 期货合约与期货交易制度

This chapter accounts for approximately 10% of the examination score. ｜ 本章分值占比约为10%。

## Section 1: Futures Contracts ｜ 第一节 期货合约

### 📌 Examination Topic: Futures Contracts ｜ 📌 考点：期货合约

#### I. Definition of a Futures Contract ｜ 一、期货合约的概念

A futures contract is a standardised contract uniformly designed by a futures exchange, requiring a specified quantity and quality of an underlying asset to be delivered at a specified time and place in the future. ｜ 期货合约是由期货交易所统一制定的标准化合约，规定在未来某一特定时间和地点，交割一定数量和质量的标的物。

📌 **Core features: exchange-designed, standardised and settled in the future.** ｜ 📌 **核心特征：交易所制定、合约标准化、未来交割。**

#### II. Factors in Selecting an Underlying Asset for a Futures Contract ｜ 二、选择期货合约标的物需要考虑的因素

##### (I) Specifications or Quality Must Be Easy to Quantify and Grade ｜ （一）规格或质量易于量化和评级

A futures contract must specify a standard delivery grade. Therefore, the specifications or quality of the underlying asset must be measurable and capable of being classified into different grades. This allows the exchange to determine the standard product and calculate price premiums or discounts between the standard product and permitted substitute products. ｜ 期货合约必须规定标准交割等级，因此标的物的规格或者质量必须能够量化和分级。这样，交易所才能确定标准品，并计算标准品与允许交割的替代品之间的升贴水。

📏 **Core requirement: the quality must be measurable and standardisable.** ｜ 📏 **核心要求：标的物质量必须能够量化和标准化。**

##### (II) Prices Must Fluctuate Frequently and Significantly ｜ （二）价格波动应当较为频繁和明显

Frequent price fluctuations create demand for both hedging and speculation. Hedgers enter the futures market to manage spot-price risks, while speculators seek profits from price movements. If prices remain stable for a long period, the futures market will lack sufficient trading demand and liquidity. ｜ 价格频繁波动会产生套期保值和投机需求。套期保值者利用期货管理现货价格风险，投机者则通过价格波动获取收益。如果价格长期缺乏波动，期货市场就难以形成足够的交易需求和流动性。

📈 **Price volatility creates demand for futures trading.** ｜ 📈 **价格波动是期货交易需求产生的重要条件。**

##### (III) Supply Must Be Large and Difficult to Control ｜ （三）供应量较大且不易被少数主体控制

The underlying asset should have a sufficiently large supply in the spot market and should not be easily controlled or monopolised by a small number of market participants. Otherwise, its price may be manipulated, affecting the fairness and stability of futures trading. ｜ 期货标的物在现货市场中应当具有较大的供应量，并且不容易被少数市场参与者控制或者垄断。否则，标的物价格容易受到操纵，影响期货市场的公平性和稳定性。

🛡️ **Core requirement: sufficient supply reduces the risk of price manipulation.** ｜ 🛡️ **核心要求：充足的供应量可以降低价格操纵风险。**

#### III. Main Terms of a Futures Contract ｜ 三、期货合约的主要条款

##### (I) Contract Name ｜ （一）合约名称

The name of a futures contract normally consists of the name of the underlying product and the name of the exchange on which it is listed. ｜ 期货合约的名称通常由期货品种名称和上市交易所名称组成。

For example, “Shanghai Futures Exchange Cathode Copper Futures Contract” identifies both the product and the exchange. ｜ 例如，“上海期货交易所阴极铜期货合约”同时说明了交易品种和上市交易所。

##### (II) Trading Unit and Contract Value ｜ （二）交易单位与合约价值

**1. Trading Unit** ｜ **1. 交易单位**

The trading unit refers to the quantity of the underlying asset represented by one futures contract. ｜ 交易单位是指每手期货合约所代表的标的物数量。

For example, the trading unit of the soybean meal futures contract listed on the Dalian Commodity Exchange is 10 tonnes per contract. ｜ 例如，大连商品交易所豆粕期货合约的交易单位为10吨/手。

**2. Contract Value** ｜ **2. 合约价值**

The contract value is the total value of the underlying asset represented by one futures contract. ｜ 合约价值是指每手期货合约所代表的标的物总价值。

For a commodity futures contract: ｜ 对于商品期货合约：

**Contract value = futures price × trading unit** ｜ **合约价值＝期货价格×交易单位**

For the CSI 300 stock-index futures contract: ｜ 对于沪深300股指期货合约：

**Contract value = index points × RMB 300 per point** ｜ **合约价值＝沪深300指数点数×每点300元**

**3. Factors Affecting the Trading Unit** ｜ **3. 确定交易单位的主要因素**

The trading unit is mainly affected by the market size of the underlying asset, the financial capacity of traders, the membership structure of the exchange and spot-market trading practices. ｜ 交易单位的大小主要受到标的物市场规模、交易者资金规模、交易所会员结构以及商品现货交易习惯等因素的影响。

Generally, a larger market and greater trader capital allow the exchange to establish a larger trading unit. ｜ 通常，标的物市场规模越大、交易者资金实力越强，交易单位可以相应设置得更大。

##### (III) Quotation Unit ｜ （三）报价单位

The quotation unit is the monetary price used to quote each measurement unit of the underlying asset during public trading. ｜ 报价单位是指公开竞价过程中，对每一计量单位的标的物进行报价时使用的货币价格单位。

For example, commodity futures may be quoted in “RMB per tonne.” ｜ 例如，商品期货可以采用“人民币元/吨”作为报价单位。

##### (IV) Minimum Price Movement ｜ （四）最小变动价位

The minimum price movement is the smallest permitted change in the quoted price of each measurement unit of a futures contract. ｜ 最小变动价位是指期货合约每一计量单位报价允许发生的最小价格变动。

**Minimum change in contract value = minimum price movement × trading unit** ｜ **合约价值的最小变动额＝最小变动价位×交易单位**

A relatively small minimum price movement may increase pricing precision and market liquidity, but an excessively small value may increase quotation and transaction costs. ｜ 较小的最小变动价位有利于提高报价精度和市场流动性，但如果设置得过小，也可能增加报价和交易成本。

A relatively large minimum price movement may reduce the number of available prices, decrease trading volume and weaken market activity. ｜ 较大的最小变动价位会减少可选择的报价档位，可能降低交易量和市场活跃程度。

##### (V) Daily Price Limit ｜ （五）每日价格最大波动限制

The daily price limit restricts the maximum amount by which the price of a futures contract may rise or fall during one trading day. ｜ 每日价格最大波动限制是指期货合约价格在一个交易日内允许上涨或者下跌的最大幅度。

The limit is generally calculated on the basis of the settlement price from the previous trading day. ｜ 每日涨跌幅限制通常以上一交易日的结算价为基准确定。

The upper limit is called the limit-up price, while the lower limit is called the limit-down price. ｜ 价格上涨的最高限制称为涨停板，价格下跌的最低限制称为跌停板。

The more frequently and sharply the underlying asset fluctuates, the larger the daily price limit may need to be. ｜ 标的物价格波动越频繁、越剧烈，每日价格最大波动幅度通常需要相应设置得更大。

##### (VI) Delivery Month ｜ （六）合约交割月份

The delivery month, also known as the contract month, is the month in which a futures contract expires and enters delivery. ｜ 合约交割月份又称合约月份，是指期货合约到期并进行交割的月份。

The selection of delivery months is mainly influenced by the production, use, storage and circulation characteristics of the underlying commodity. ｜ 交割月份的设置主要受到商品生产、使用、储藏和流通特点的影响。

##### (VII) Trading Hours ｜ （七）交易时间

Trading hours refer to the periods during which a futures contract may be traded on the exchange. ｜ 交易时间是指期货合约可以在交易所进行买卖的具体时间段。

The exchange determines the trading hours according to the characteristics of the product and market needs. ｜ 交易所会根据期货品种特点和市场需求确定具体交易时间。

##### (VIII) Last Trading Day ｜ （八）最后交易日

The last trading day is the final date on which a futures contract may be traded. ｜ 最后交易日是指某一期货合约可以进行交易的最后日期。

After the last trading day, open positions generally enter the delivery or final settlement process. ｜ 最后交易日结束后，未平仓合约通常进入交割或者最终结算程序。

##### (IX) Delivery Date ｜ （九）交割日期

The delivery date is the date or period during which the buyer and seller complete the delivery of the underlying asset or cash settlement. ｜ 交割日期是指期货合约买卖双方完成标的物交割或者现金结算的日期或期间。

##### (X) Delivery Grade ｜ （十）交割等级

The delivery grade is the quality standard prescribed by the futures exchange for the underlying asset that may be delivered under a futures contract. ｜ 交割等级是指期货交易所统一规定的、允许用于期货合约实物交割的标的物质量等级。

The exchange normally specifies a standard product and may also permit substitute products of different grades. ｜ 交易所通常会规定一个标准品，同时允许符合条件的其他等级商品作为替代品交割。

If the actual delivery product differs from the standard grade, the recipient may not refuse delivery as long as it meets the exchange’s requirements. ｜ 如果实际交割商品与标准交割等级存在差异，只要符合交易所规定，收货人通常不得拒收。

The exchange establishes and adjusts the premiums or discounts between the standard product and substitute products. ｜ 交易所统一规定并适时调整替代品与标准品之间的升贴水标准。

📌 **Premium:** the substitute product is valued above the standard product. ｜ 📌 **升水：**替代品的交割价值高于标准品。

📌 **Discount:** the substitute product is valued below the standard product. ｜ 📌 **贴水：**替代品的交割价值低于标准品。

##### (XI) Delivery Location ｜ （十一）交割地点

The delivery location is the designated place prescribed by the futures exchange for physical delivery. ｜ 交割地点是指期货交易所统一规定的进行实物交割的指定地点。

Commodity futures exchanges normally designate delivery warehouses. ｜ 商品期货交易所通常会指定交割仓库。

Financial futures generally do not require physical delivery warehouses, but the exchange may designate settlement or delivery banks. ｜ 金融期货通常不需要指定实物交割仓库，但交易所可能指定结算银行或者交割银行。

##### (XII) Trading Fees ｜ （十二）交易手续费

Trading fees are charges collected by a futures exchange according to the value or number of futures contracts traded. ｜ 交易手续费是指期货交易所按照成交合约金额的一定比例或者成交手数收取的费用。

Excessively high trading fees increase transaction costs, widen the no-arbitrage range and may reduce market liquidity and trading volume. ｜ 过高的交易手续费会增加交易成本，扩大无套利区间，并可能降低市场流动性和交易量。

However, appropriately higher fees may help restrain excessive speculation. ｜ 但是，适当提高交易手续费也可以在一定程度上抑制过度投机。

##### (XIII) Delivery Method ｜ （十三）交割方式

Futures contracts may use physical delivery or cash settlement. ｜ 期货合约可以采用实物交割或者现金交割。

Commodity futures, stock futures, foreign exchange futures and medium- or long-term interest-rate futures commonly use physical delivery. ｜ 商品期货、股票期货、外汇期货以及中长期利率期货通常采用实物交割。

Stock-index futures and short-term interest-rate futures commonly use cash settlement. ｜ 股票指数期货和短期利率期货通常采用现金交割。

📦 **Physical delivery:** the underlying asset is actually transferred. ｜ 📦 **实物交割：**实际转移合约标的物。

💵 **Cash settlement:** only the price difference is settled in cash. ｜ 💵 **现金交割：**不转移标的物，只结算价格差额。

##### (XIV) Trading Code ｜ （十四）交易代码

A trading code is the abbreviated symbol used to identify a futures product in the trading system. ｜ 交易代码是指在交易系统中用于识别某一期货品种的简称。

Common examples include: ｜ 常见示例包括：

- CSI 300 stock-index futures: **IF** ｜ 沪深300股指期货：**IF**
- Zhengzhou Commodity Exchange sugar futures: **SR** ｜ 郑州商品交易所白糖期货：**SR**
- Dalian Commodity Exchange soybean-oil futures: **Y** ｜ 大连商品交易所豆油期货：**Y**
- Shanghai Futures Exchange copper futures: **CU** ｜ 上海期货交易所铜期货：**CU**

#### IV. Core Summary ｜ 四、核心总结

📄 **Futures contracts are standardised contracts designed by exchanges.** ｜ 📄 **期货合约是由交易所统一设计的标准化合约。**

✅ **A suitable underlying asset should be measurable, volatile and difficult to manipulate.** ｜ ✅ **适合作为期货标的物的商品应当易于标准化、价格具有波动性且不易被操纵。**

📊 **The main contract terms determine what is traded, how it is priced, when it is traded and how it is delivered.** ｜ 📊 **期货合约的主要条款规定交易什么、如何报价、何时交易以及如何交割。**

🧠 **Key contract terms:** name, trading unit, quotation unit, minimum price movement, price limit, delivery month, trading hours, last trading day, delivery date, delivery grade, delivery location, fees, delivery method and trading code. ｜ 🧠 **核心条款：**合约名称、交易单位、报价单位、最小变动价位、涨跌停板、交割月份、交易时间、最后交易日、交割日期、交割等级、交割地点、手续费、交割方式和交易代码。

## ⚙️ Section 2: Basic Systems of the Futures Market ｜ ⚙️ 第二节 期货市场基本制度

### 📌 Examination Topic: Basic Futures Market Systems ｜ 📌 考点：期货市场基本制度

#### I. Margin System ｜ 一、保证金制度

##### (I) Definition of the Margin System ｜ （一）保证金制度的概念

In futures trading, both buyers and sellers must deposit a certain percentage of the contract value, usually 5%–15%, as margin. The margin is used for daily settlement and as a guarantee that the parties will perform their contractual obligations. ｜ 在期货交易中，买方和卖方都必须按照期货合约价值的一定比例缴纳保证金，通常为5%～15%。保证金主要用于每日结算，并作为交易双方履行合约的担保。

📌 **Margin allows traders to control a larger contract value with a smaller amount of capital, creating leverage.** ｜ 📌 **保证金使交易者能够用较少资金控制较大价值的合约，因此形成杠杆效应。**

##### (II) General Features of International Margin Systems ｜ （二）国际期货市场保证金制度的一般特点

1. Margin requirements correspond to the level of risk faced by traders. ｜ 保证金水平与交易者承担的风险相对应，风险越高，保证金要求通常越高。
2. Exchanges establish minimum margin standards according to the characteristics of each contract and may adjust them when market risk changes. ｜ 交易所根据合约特点设定最低保证金标准，并可以根据市场风险变化进行调整。
3. Margin is collected through different levels of the futures clearing system. ｜ 保证金按照期货市场的分级结算关系逐级收取。

🔄 **Client → Futures company or member → Exchange** ｜ 🔄 **客户 → 期货公司或会员 → 交易所**

##### (III) Features of China’s Futures Margin System ｜ （三）我国境内期货交易保证金制度的特点

1. Different margin ratios may apply at different stages of a futures contract’s life. ｜ 期货合约在上市运行的不同阶段，可以适用不同的交易保证金比例。
2. As the open interest of a contract increases, the exchange may gradually increase its margin ratio. ｜ 随着某一期货合约持仓量增大，交易所可以逐步提高该合约的保证金比例。
3. When a contract continuously reaches its daily price limit, the exchange may increase the required margin. ｜ 当某一期货合约连续出现涨停或跌停时，交易所可以相应提高保证金比例。
4. When cumulative price movements reach a specified level over several consecutive trading days, the exchange may adopt one or more risk-control measures. ｜ 当某合约连续若干交易日的累计涨跌幅达到规定程度时，交易所可以采取一种或多种风险控制措施。

These measures include: ｜ 相关措施包括：

- Increasing margin requirements for some or all members’ long or short positions ｜ 对部分或全部会员的单边或双边持仓提高保证金水平
- Restricting the withdrawal or transfer of funds ｜ 限制部分或全部会员划出资金
- Suspending the opening of new positions ｜ 暂停部分或全部会员开立新仓
- Adjusting the daily price limit ｜ 调整涨跌停板幅度
- Requiring positions to be closed within a specified period or conducting forced liquidation ｜ 采取限期平仓或者强行平仓措施

1. When abnormal trading occurs, the exchange may adjust the margin ratio according to prescribed procedures. ｜ 当期货合约交易出现异常情况时，交易所可以按照规定程序调整交易保证金比例。

🧠 **Core rule: greater market risk → higher margin requirements.** ｜ 🧠 **核心规律：市场风险越高，保证金要求通常越高。**

------

#### II. Daily Mark-to-Market Settlement System ｜ 二、当日无负债结算制度

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

The daily mark-to-market settlement system means that, after the end of each trading day, the futures clearing institution calculates the profits and losses of every margin account and transfers funds according to the settlement results. ｜ 当日无负债结算制度是指每个交易日结束后，期货结算机构对各保证金账户当日的盈亏进行结算，并根据结算结果进行资金划转。

Profits are credited to the account, while losses are deducted from the account. If the remaining margin is insufficient, the trader must provide additional margin. ｜ 盈利会计入账户，亏损会从账户中扣除；如果保证金余额不足，交易者需要追加保证金。

📌 **Daily settlement prevents losses from accumulating over a long period.** ｜ 📌 **每日结算可以防止亏损长期累积。**

##### (II) Main Features ｜ （二）特点

1. Contracts of different products and delivery months are settled separately. ｜ 不同品种、不同交割月份的合约分别进行结算。
2. Both realised profits and losses from closed positions and unrealised profits and losses from open positions are settled. ｜ 已平仓头寸的实际盈亏和未平仓头寸的浮动盈亏都需要结算。
3. The margin occupied by open positions is recalculated every trading day. ｜ 交易头寸占用的保证金需要逐日重新计算。
4. Settlement is implemented through the tiered clearing structure of the futures market. ｜ 当日结算通过期货市场的分级结算体系实施。

🔄 **Exchange → Member → Client** ｜ 🔄 **交易所 → 会员 → 客户**

------

#### III. Daily Price Limit System ｜ 三、涨跌停板制度

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

The daily price limit system restricts the maximum amount by which a futures contract may rise or fall during one trading day. Quotations beyond the permitted range are invalid and cannot be executed. ｜ 涨跌停板制度又称每日价格最大波动限制制度，是指期货合约价格在一个交易日内不得超过规定的上涨或下跌幅度。超过涨跌停板范围的报价属于无效报价，不能成交。

📈 **Upper limit: limit-up price** ｜ 📈 **上涨上限：涨停板价格**

📉 **Lower limit: limit-down price** ｜ 📉 **下跌下限：跌停板价格**

##### (II) Features of China’s Daily Price Limit System ｜ （二）我国境内涨跌停板制度的特点

1. The price-limit range for a newly listed product may be two or three times the normal limit specified in the contract. ｜ 新上市品种的涨跌停板幅度，通常可以设置为合约正常涨跌停板幅度的两倍或三倍。
2. The exchange may adjust the price-limit range when continuous price limits occur, before or after national holidays, or when market risk changes significantly. ｜ 当合约连续出现涨跌停板、遇到国家法定长假或者市场风险明显变化时，交易所可以调整涨跌停板幅度。
3. If two or more price-limit adjustment rules apply simultaneously, the highest applicable limit is generally used. ｜ 当同一期货合约同时适用两种或两种以上涨跌停板规定时，通常按照其中最高的涨跌停板幅度执行。
4. When a contract reaches its price limit, the exchange may apply special trading and risk-control measures. ｜ 当期货合约出现涨跌停板时，交易所可以采取特殊的成交和风险控制措施。

###### 1. Matching Priority at the Price Limit ｜ 1. 停板价格下的撮合原则

When transactions occur at the limit-up or limit-down price, orders are generally matched according to the principles of closing-position priority and time priority. ｜ 当期货合约以涨停板或者跌停板价格成交时，通常按照平仓优先和时间优先的原则进行撮合。

However, closing a position opened on the same trading day generally does not receive closing-position priority. ｜ 但是，平掉当日新开仓位通常不适用平仓优先原则。

###### 2. Forced Position Reduction ｜ 2. 强制减仓

When a contract experiences consecutive one-sided markets without continuous quotations, the exchange may implement forced position reduction. ｜ 当某一期货合约连续出现单边市且缺乏连续报价时，交易所可以实行强制减仓。

##### (III) One-Sided Market ｜ （三）单边市

A one-sided market generally occurs when, during the final five minutes before the close, there are only buy orders at the limit-up price but no sell orders, or only sell orders at the limit-down price but no buy orders. ｜ 单边市通常是指在交易日收盘前5分钟内，市场只有涨停价格的买入申报而没有卖出申报，或者只有跌停价格的卖出申报而没有买入申报。

It may also include a situation in which opposite orders appear and are immediately executed, but the contract remains locked at the price limit. ｜ 如果相反方向的申报一出现就立即成交，但价格仍未打开涨跌停板，也可以视为单边市。

🧠 **Core purpose: limit extreme daily price movements and control market risk.** ｜ 🧠 **核心目的：限制单日极端价格波动，控制市场风险。**

------

#### IV. Position Limit and Large-Position Reporting Systems ｜ 四、持仓限额及大户报告制度

##### (I) Position Limit System ｜ （一）持仓限额制度

The position limit system specifies the maximum speculative position that a member or client may hold in a particular contract, generally calculated on a one-sided basis. ｜ 持仓限额制度是指交易所规定会员或者客户在某一期货合约中可以持有的最大投机头寸，通常按照单边持仓计算。

Its purpose is to prevent excessive concentration of positions, reduce market manipulation and control trading risk. ｜ 其主要目的是防止持仓过度集中，降低市场操纵风险，并控制交易风险。

📌 **Position limit = maximum permitted speculative position.** ｜ 📌 **持仓限额＝允许持有的最大投机头寸。**

##### (II) Large-Position Reporting System ｜ （二）大户报告制度

When a member’s or client’s position in a particular product or contract reaches the reporting threshold prescribed by the exchange, the member or client must report the position and related information to the exchange. ｜ 当会员或者客户在某一品种或合约中的持仓达到交易所规定的报告标准时，应当向交易所报告持仓及相关信息。

The exchange uses these reports to monitor large positions, identify concentrated risks and prevent market manipulation. ｜ 交易所通过大户报告掌握大额持仓情况，识别集中风险，并防范市场操纵。

📌 **Position limits control how much may be held; large-position reporting reveals who holds a large position.** ｜ 📌 **持仓限额控制“最多能持有多少”，大户报告关注“谁已经持有较大头寸”。**

------

#### V. Core Summary ｜ 五、核心总结

💰 **Margin system:** requires funds as a performance guarantee and adjusts them according to risk. ｜ 💰 **保证金制度：**缴纳履约担保，并根据风险变化调整比例。

🧾 **Daily settlement:** settles profits, losses and margin every trading day. ｜ 🧾 **当日无负债结算：**每日结算盈亏和保证金。

🚧 **Daily price limit:** restricts the maximum daily price movement. ｜ 🚧 **涨跌停板制度：**限制单日价格最大波动幅度。

📊 **Position limit:** restricts the maximum speculative position. ｜ 📊 **持仓限额制度：**限制最大投机持仓量。

🔍 **Large-position reporting:** requires large position holders to report to the exchange. ｜ 🔍 **大户报告制度：**大额持仓达到标准时必须向交易所报告。



##### (II) Account-Opening Process ｜ （二）开户流程

###### 1. Account-Opening Application ｜ 1. 开户申请

An individual client must personally complete the account-opening procedures and sign the account-opening documents. The client may not authorise another person to complete the procedures on their behalf. ｜ 个人客户应当亲自办理开户手续并签署开户资料，不得委托代理人代为办理。

An institutional client may appoint an agent to complete the account-opening procedures. The client must provide a letter of authorisation, the agent’s identification document and other required account-opening documents. ｜ 单位客户可以委托代理人办理开户手续，但应当提供授权委托书、代理人的身份证明以及其他开户证件。

The futures company must review the client’s account-opening documents to ensure that they are compliant, authentic, accurate and complete. ｜ 期货公司应当对客户的开户资料进行审核，确保开户资料合规、真实、准确和完整。

------

###### 2. Reading and Signing the Futures Trading Risk Disclosure Statement ｜ 2. 阅读并签署《期货交易风险说明书》

Before opening an account, the client must read the Futures Trading Risk Disclosure Statement and sign it to confirm their understanding of the risks associated with futures trading. ｜ 开户前，客户应当阅读《期货交易风险说明书》，并签字确认已经了解期货交易风险。

An individual client must personally sign the risk disclosure statement. ｜ 个人客户应当由本人签字确认。

For an institutional client, the authorised agent must sign the statement and affix the institution’s official seal. ｜ 单位客户应当由授权代理人签字并加盖单位公章。

------

###### 3. Signing the Futures Brokerage Contract ｜ 3. 签署《期货经纪合同书》

The client and the futures company must sign a Futures Brokerage Contract to establish their futures brokerage relationship. ｜ 客户与期货公司应当签署《期货经纪合同书》，正式建立期货经纪关系。

------

###### 4. Applying for a Trading Code and Confirming the Fund Account ｜ 4. 申请交易编码并确认资金账号

The China Futures Market Monitoring Center is responsible for processing futures account-opening information and allocating client trading codes. ｜ 中国期货市场监控中心负责处理期货开户信息，并为客户分配交易编码。

A client trading code allocated on the current day may be used from the next trading day. ｜ 当日分配的客户交易编码，从下一交易日起可以使用。

A trading code consists of 12 digits. The first four digits represent the member number, and the last eight digits represent the client number. ｜ 交易编码由12位数字组成，其中前4位为会员号，后8位为客户号。

📌 **Account-opening process: application → risk disclosure → brokerage contract → trading code and fund account.** ｜ 📌 **开户流程：开户申请 → 风险揭示 → 签署经纪合同 → 申请交易编码并确认资金账号。**

#### II. Order Placement ｜ 二、下单

##### (I) Definition of Order Placement ｜ （一）下单的概念

Order placement refers to the act of a client issuing a trading instruction to a futures company before each transaction. The instruction specifies the contract type, trading direction, quantity, price and other relevant details. ｜ 下单是指客户在每笔交易前向期货公司下达交易指令，说明拟买卖合约的种类、买卖方向、数量、价格等内容。

📌 **Core elements: contract + direction + quantity + price.** ｜ 📌 **核心要素：合约＋方向＋数量＋价格。**

##### (II) Common Orders in International Futures Markets ｜ （二）国际期货市场常用交易指令

###### 1. Market Order ｜ 1. 市价指令

A market order requires the transaction to be executed immediately at the best price currently available in the market. ｜ 市价指令是指按照市场当前可以获得的最优价格立即成交的指令。

It prioritises execution speed but does not guarantee the final execution price. ｜ 市价指令优先保证成交速度，但不能保证最终成交价格。

###### 2. Limit Order ｜ 2. 限价指令

A limit order specifies the highest price at which the client is willing to buy or the lowest price at which the client is willing to sell. ｜ 限价指令是指客户规定最高买入价格或者最低卖出价格的交易指令。

The order can be executed only at the specified price or at a more favourable price. ｜ 该指令只能按照指定价格或者对客户更有利的价格成交。

📌 **Market order prioritises execution; limit order prioritises price.** ｜ 📌 **市价指令优先成交，限价指令优先价格。**

###### 3. Stop Order ｜ 3. 止损指令

A stop order becomes a market order when the market price reaches the trigger price predetermined by the client. ｜ 止损指令是指市场价格达到客户预先设定的触发价格时，立即转变为市价指令并予以执行。

Stop orders are generally used to close existing positions and prevent losses from continuing to increase. ｜ 止损指令通常用于平仓，以防止已有持仓的亏损继续扩大。

###### 4. Stop-Limit Order ｜ 4. 停止限价指令

A stop-limit order becomes a limit order when the market price reaches the trigger price predetermined by the client. ｜ 停止限价指令是指市场价格达到客户预先设定的触发价格时，转变为限价指令并予以执行。

After being triggered, the order can be executed only at the specified limit price or at a more favourable price. Therefore, the execution price is controlled, but execution is not guaranteed. ｜ 指令触发后，只能按照指定的限价或者更有利的价格成交。因此，它可以控制成交价格，但不能保证一定成交。

###### 5. Market-if-Touched Order ｜ 5. 触价指令

A market-if-touched order becomes a market order when the market price reaches the specified trigger price. ｜ 触价指令是指市场价格达到预先指定的触发价位时，转变为市价指令并予以执行。

The difference between a market-if-touched order and a stop order lies mainly in the position of the trigger price relative to the current market price. ｜ 触价指令与止损指令的主要区别，在于触发价格与当前市场价格之间的位置关系不同。

For a sell order, the trigger price of a sell stop order is below the current market price, while the trigger price of a sell market-if-touched order is above the current market price. ｜ 对于卖出指令，卖出止损指令的触发价格低于当前市场价格，而卖出触价指令的触发价格高于当前市场价格。

For a buy order, the relationship is reversed: the trigger price of a buy stop order is above the current market price, while that of a buy market-if-touched order is below the current market price. ｜ 对于买入指令，情况相反：买入止损指令的触发价格高于当前市场价格，而买入触价指令的触发价格低于当前市场价格。

Stop orders are generally used to close positions, whereas market-if-touched orders are usually used to open new positions. ｜ 止损指令通常用于平仓，触价指令一般用于开立新仓。

| Order                   | Sell Trigger Price  | Buy Trigger Price   | Common Purpose                    |
| ----------------------- | ------------------- | ------------------- | --------------------------------- |
| Stop order              | Below current price | Above current price | Close a position and limit losses |
| Market-if-touched order | Above current price | Below current price | Open a new position               |

| 指令     | 卖出触发价   | 买入触发价   | 常见用途 |
| -------- | ------------ | ------------ | -------- |
| 止损指令 | 低于当前价格 | 高于当前价格 | 平仓止损 |
| 触价指令 | 高于当前价格 | 低于当前价格 | 开立新仓 |

###### 6. Time-Limit Order ｜ 6. 限时指令

A time-limit order remains valid only during a period specified by the client. If it is not executed within that period, it automatically becomes invalid. ｜ 限时指令仅在客户指定的时间范围内有效；如果未在规定时间内成交，指令自动失效。

###### 7. Good-Till-Cancelled Order ｜ 7. 长效指令

A good-till-cancelled order remains valid until it is executed or cancelled by the client. ｜ 长效指令在成交或者被客户取消之前持续有效。

###### 8. Spread Order ｜ 8. 套利指令

A spread order is an instruction to simultaneously buy and sell two or more related futures contracts in order to trade the price difference between them. ｜ 套利指令是指同时买入和卖出两个或者多个相关期货合约，以交易合约之间价差的指令。

###### 9. Cancel Order ｜ 9. 取消指令

A cancel order instructs the trading system to cancel an order that has already been submitted but has not yet been executed. ｜ 取消指令是指撤销已经下达但尚未成交的交易指令。

##### (III) Common Orders in China ｜ （三）我国常用交易指令

The supplied material provides only this heading and does not list the specific types of orders commonly used in China. ｜ 原材料仅列出了“我国常用交易指令”这一标题，没有提供具体的交易指令类型。

##### (IV) Methods of Placing Orders ｜ （四）指令下达方式

###### 1. Written Orders ｜ 1. 书面下单

The client completes and signs a written trading instruction form and submits it to the futures company. The futures company then transmits the instruction to the exchange for matching and execution. ｜ 客户填写书面交易指令单并签字后，将其交给期货公司，再由期货公司将指令发送至交易所进行撮合成交。

###### 2. Telephone Orders ｜ 2. 电话下单

The client issues a trading instruction to the futures company by telephone, and the futures company then transmits the instruction to the exchange. ｜ 客户通过电话向期货公司下达交易指令，再由期货公司将指令发送至交易所参与交易。

The futures company must record the client’s telephone instruction simultaneously for subsequent verification. ｜ 期货公司必须对客户的电话交易指令进行同步录音，以备后续查询和核实。

###### 3. Internet Orders ｜ 3. 互联网下单

The client uses the online order-placement system provided by the futures company through the internet or a local-area network. ｜ 客户通过互联网或者局域网，使用期货公司提供的网上交易系统下单。

After entering the system, the client must input the client number and password. Once the identity is verified, the client may enter the trading instruction. ｜ 进入交易系统后，客户需要输入客户号和密码，身份验证通过后即可输入交易指令。

The instruction is transmitted to the futures company through the internet or local-area network and then sent through a dedicated line to the exchange’s host computer for order matching. ｜ 交易指令通过互联网或者局域网传送至期货公司，再通过专线发送至交易所主机进行撮合成交。

🔄 **Client terminal → futures company → exchange host → order matching** ｜ 🔄 **客户终端 → 期货公司 → 交易所主机 → 撮合成交**

#### III. Core Summary ｜ 三、核心总结

⚡ **Market order:** prioritises immediate execution. ｜ ⚡ **市价指令：**优先保证快速成交。

🎯 **Limit order:** controls the execution price. ｜ 🎯 **限价指令：**重点控制成交价格。

🛑 **Stop order:** becomes a market order after being triggered and is commonly used to limit losses. ｜ 🛑 **止损指令：**触发后转为市价指令，通常用于平仓止损。

🔒 **Stop-limit order:** becomes a limit order after being triggered. ｜ 🔒 **停止限价指令：**触发后转为限价指令。

🔔 **Market-if-touched order:** is generally triggered at a more favourable price and is commonly used to open a new position. ｜ 🔔 **触价指令：**通常在更有利的价格触发，主要用于开立新仓。

📝 **Order methods:** written orders, telephone orders and internet orders. ｜ 📝 **下单方式：**书面下单、电话下单和互联网下单。

#### III. Auction and Order Matching ｜ 三、竞价

##### (I) Auction Methods ｜ （一）竞价方式

###### 1. Open Outcry ｜ 1. 公开喊价

Open outcry is a traditional trading method in which traders publicly communicate buying and selling intentions on the trading floor. ｜ 公开喊价是指交易者在交易场所公开表达买卖意愿并进行成交的传统交易方式。

It mainly includes continuous auction and single-price auction. ｜ 公开喊价主要包括连续竞价制和一节一价制。

**Continuous auction** allows transactions to be concluded continuously as matching buy and sell orders appear. ｜ **连续竞价制**是指买卖申报在交易过程中连续进行，并在符合条件时不断成交。

**Single-price auction** determines one transaction price for all trades during a particular trading session. ｜ **一节一价制**是指在某一交易时段内，以统一价格完成交易。

###### 2. Computerised Order Matching ｜ 2. 计算机撮合成交

Computerised order matching mainly includes continuous auction and call auction. ｜ 计算机撮合成交主要包括连续竞价和集合竞价。

###### (1) Continuous Auction ｜ （1）连续竞价

Continuous auction follows the principles of price priority and time priority. ｜ 连续竞价遵循价格优先、时间优先原则。

**Price priority** means that a higher buy price has priority over a lower buy price, while a lower sell price has priority over a higher sell price. ｜ **价格优先**是指较高的买入价格优先于较低的买入价格，较低的卖出价格优先于较高的卖出价格。

**Time priority** means that when orders have the same price, the order submitted earlier has priority. ｜ **时间优先**是指在申报价格相同的情况下，较早提交的指令优先成交。

The matching price is the middle value among the buy price, the sell price and the previous transaction price. ｜ 撮合成交价等于买入申报价、卖出申报价和前一成交价三者中的居中价格。

**Transaction price = median of BP, SP and CP** ｜ **成交价＝买入价BP、卖出价SP和前一成交价CP三者的中间值**

For example, if the buy price is 101, the sell price is 99 and the previous transaction price is 100, the matching price is 100. ｜ 例如，买入价为101、卖出价为99、前一成交价为100时，三者的中间值为100，因此成交价为100。

###### (2) Call Auction ｜ （2）集合竞价

A call auction is mainly used to determine the opening price of a futures contract. ｜ 集合竞价主要用于产生期货合约的开盘价。

The first four minutes of the five-minute call-auction period are used for submitting buy and sell orders, while the final minute is used for centralised order matching. ｜ 集合竞价的前4分钟为买卖指令申报时间，后1分钟为集中撮合时间。

For contracts with night trading, the opening call auction is conducted during the five minutes before the night session begins. No separate opening call auction is conducted during the following daytime session. ｜ 对于设有夜盘交易的合约，开盘集合竞价在每个交易日夜盘开市前5分钟进行，随后日盘不再单独进行集合竞价。

The call auction follows the principle of maximising transaction volume. ｜ 集合竞价采用最大成交量原则确定成交价格。

All buy orders above the call-auction price are executed, and all sell orders below the call-auction price are executed. ｜ 高于集合竞价价格的买入申报全部成交，低于集合竞价价格的卖出申报全部成交。

For orders placed exactly at the call-auction price, the executed quantity is determined according to the smaller quantity between buy orders and sell orders. ｜ 对于等于集合竞价价格的买入申报和卖出申报，按照买卖双方申报数量较少的一方确定成交数量。

📌 **Core principle: select the price that produces the maximum trading volume.** ｜ 📌 **核心原则：选择能够实现最大成交量的价格。**

##### (II) Trade Confirmation ｜ （二）成交回报与确认

After the computerised matching system confirms that an order has been executed, the client can immediately receive the trade confirmation through the futures company’s order-placement system. ｜ 当计算机撮合系统显示交易指令已经成交后，客户可以立即通过期货公司的下单系统获得成交回报。

The trade confirmation generally shows information such as the contract, trading direction, transaction price and executed quantity. ｜ 成交回报通常会显示成交合约、买卖方向、成交价格和成交数量等信息。

#### IV. Concepts Related to Settlement ｜ 四、结算的相关概念

##### (I) Definition of Settlement ｜ （一）结算的概念

Settlement refers to the calculation, clearing and transfer of funds between trading parties according to the settlement price published by the futures exchange. ｜ 结算是指根据期货交易所公布的结算价格，对交易双方的交易结果进行资金清算和划转。

📌 **Settlement determines daily profits, losses and margin balances.** ｜ 📌 **结算用于确定每日盈亏和保证金余额。**

##### (II) Settlement Systems ｜ （二）结算制度

###### 1. All-Member Settlement ｜ 1. 全员结算

Under the all-member settlement system, the exchange directly settles transactions with all of its clearing members. ｜ 在全员结算制度下，交易所直接与所有结算会员进行结算。

According to the supplied material, the following exchanges adopt the all-member settlement system: ｜ 根据原材料，实行全员结算的交易所包括：

1. Dalian Commodity Exchange ｜ 大连商品交易所
2. Zhengzhou Commodity Exchange ｜ 郑州商品交易所
3. Shanghai Futures Exchange ｜ 上海期货交易所

###### 2. Tiered Settlement ｜ 2. 分级结算

Under the tiered settlement system, the exchange settles with clearing members, while clearing members settle with non-clearing members or clients at the next level. ｜ 在分级结算制度下，交易所先与结算会员进行结算，再由结算会员与下一级非结算会员或者客户进行结算。

According to the supplied material, the China Financial Futures Exchange adopts the tiered settlement system. ｜ 根据原材料，中国金融期货交易所实行分级结算制度。

🔄 **Exchange → clearing member → non-clearing member or client** ｜ 🔄 **交易所 → 结算会员 → 非结算会员或客户**

##### (III) Classification of Margin ｜ （三）保证金分类

###### 1. Settlement Reserve ｜ 1. 结算准备金

Settlement reserve refers to funds in the margin account that are not occupied by open futures positions. ｜ 结算准备金是指保证金账户中尚未被期货合约持仓占用的资金。

It may be used to cover trading losses, fees or additional margin requirements. ｜ 结算准备金可以用于弥补交易亏损、支付相关费用或者满足追加保证金要求。

###### 2. Trading Margin ｜ 2. 交易保证金

Trading margin refers to the portion of margin funds occupied by open futures positions. ｜ 交易保证金是指已经被期货合约持仓占用的保证金。

📌 **Settlement reserve = unoccupied funds; trading margin = funds occupied by positions.** ｜ 📌 **结算准备金＝未被持仓占用的资金；交易保证金＝已被持仓占用的资金。**

##### (IV) Settlement Procedure ｜ （四）结算程序

Settlement is conducted at two main levels. ｜ 期货结算主要分为两个层次。

1. The exchange settles with its members. ｜ 交易所对会员进行结算。
2. The futures company settles with its clients. ｜ 期货公司对客户进行结算。

🔄 **Exchange settlement → member settlement → client settlement** ｜ 🔄 **交易所结算 → 会员结算 → 客户结算**

##### (V) Opening, Closing, Holding Positions and Settlement Price ｜ （五）期货开仓、平仓、持仓与结算价

###### 1. Opening, Closing and Holding Futures Positions ｜ 1. 期货的开仓、平仓与持仓

Opening a position means buying or selling a futures contract to establish a new position. ｜ 开仓是指通过买入或者卖出期货合约建立新的交易头寸。

Closing a position means conducting an opposite transaction to offset an existing position. ｜ 平仓是指通过进行方向相反的交易，了结原有期货头寸。

An open position refers to a futures position that has not yet been closed or delivered. ｜ 持仓是指尚未通过平仓或者交割方式了结的期货头寸。

###### 2. Futures Settlement Price ｜ 2. 期货结算价

According to the supplied material, the daily settlement price of the Zhengzhou Commodity Exchange, Dalian Commodity Exchange and Shanghai Futures Exchange is the volume-weighted average price of all transactions in a futures contract during that trading day. ｜ 根据原材料，郑州商品交易所、大连商品交易所和上海期货交易所的当日结算价，为某一期货合约当日全部成交价格按照成交量计算的加权平均价。

If no transaction occurs on that day, the settlement price from the previous trading day is used as the current day’s settlement price. ｜ 如果该合约当日没有成交，则以上一交易日的结算价作为当日结算价。

**Daily settlement price = sum of transaction price × transaction volume ÷ total transaction volume** ｜ **当日结算价＝各成交价格与相应成交量乘积之和÷总成交量**

According to the supplied material, the China Financial Futures Exchange defines the daily settlement price as the volume-weighted average price of transactions during the final hour of trading. ｜ 根据原材料，中国金融期货交易所的当日结算价，是指某一期货合约最后一小时成交价格按照成交量计算的加权平均价。

📌 **The settlement price is used for daily profit-and-loss settlement and is not necessarily the same as the closing price.** ｜ 📌 **结算价用于每日盈亏结算，不一定等于收盘价。**

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

⚖️ **Continuous auction:** follows price priority and time priority. ｜ ⚖️ **连续竞价：**遵循价格优先和时间优先原则。

🎯 **Matching price:** the middle value among the buy price, sell price and previous transaction price. ｜ 🎯 **撮合成交价：**买入价、卖出价和前一成交价三者的中间值。

🔔 **Call auction:** determines the opening price according to the maximum-volume principle. ｜ 🔔 **集合竞价：**按照最大成交量原则确定开盘价。

🧾 **Settlement:** calculates and transfers daily profits, losses and margin. ｜ 🧾 **结算：**每日计算并划转盈亏和保证金。

💰 **Settlement reserve:** funds not occupied by positions. ｜ 💰 **结算准备金：**未被持仓占用的资金。

🔒 **Trading margin:** funds occupied by open positions. ｜ 🔒 **交易保证金：**已被持仓占用的资金。

📊 **Settlement price:** usually calculated using a volume-weighted average price. ｜ 📊 **结算价：**通常采用成交量加权平均价格计算。

#### V. Settlement Between a Futures Exchange and Its Members ｜ 五、期货交易所对会员的结算

##### (I) Formulas ｜ （一）公式

###### 1. Daily Settlement Reserve Balance ｜ 1. 当日结算准备金余额

**Daily settlement reserve balance**
$$
\begin{aligned}
=&\ \text{Previous trading day’s settlement reserve balance}\\
&+\text{Previous trading day’s trading margin}\\
&-\text{Current trading day’s trading margin}\\
&+\text{Current available value of assets used as margin}\\
&-\text{Previous available value of assets used as margin}\\
&+\text{Daily profit or loss}\\
&+\text{Funds deposited}\\
&-\text{Funds withdrawn}\\
&-\text{Transaction fees}
\end{aligned}
$$
**当日结算准备金余额**
$$
\begin{aligned}
=&\ \text{上一交易日结算准备金余额}\\
&+\text{上一交易日交易保证金}\\
&-\text{当日交易保证金}\\
&+\text{当日作为保证金的资产实际可用金额}\\
&-\text{上一交易日作为保证金的资产实际可用金额}\\
&+\text{当日盈亏}\\
&+\text{入金}\\
&-\text{出金}\\
&-\text{手续费}
\end{aligned}
$$
The settlement reserve increases when trading margin is released, profits are earned or funds are deposited. It decreases when additional margin is occupied, losses occur, funds are withdrawn or transaction fees are deducted. ｜ 交易保证金被释放、交易产生盈利或者账户入金时，结算准备金增加；新的持仓占用保证金、交易产生亏损、账户出金或者扣除手续费时，结算准备金减少。

------

###### 2. Calculation of Daily Profit or Loss ｜ 2. 当日盈亏计算

##### (1) Commodity Futures ｜ （1）商品期货

$$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Daily P/L}
=&\sum[(\text{Selling price}-\text{Daily settlement price})
\times \text{Selling quantity}]\\
&+\sum[(\text{Daily settlement price}-\text{Buying price})
\times \text{Buying quantity}]\\
&+(\text{Previous settlement price}-\text{Daily settlement price})\\
&\quad\times(\text{Previous short position}
-\text{Previous long position})
\end{aligned}
$$



**当日结算准备金余额**
$$
\begin{aligned}
=&\ \text{上一交易日结算准备金余额}\\
&+\text{上一交易日交易保证金}\\
&-\text{当日交易保证金}\\
&+\text{当日作为保证金的资产实际可用金额}\\
&-\text{上一交易日作为保证金的资产实际可用金额}\\
&+\text{当日盈亏}\\
&+\text{入金}\\
&-\text{出金}\\
&-\text{手续费}
\end{aligned}
$$
The first two parts calculate the profit or loss generated by the current day’s sell and buy transactions. The final part calculates the mark-to-market profit or loss on positions carried forward from the previous trading day. ｜ 前两部分计算当日卖出和买入交易产生的盈亏，最后一部分计算上一交易日持仓因结算价变化产生的盯市盈亏。

**Daily P/L = current-day transaction P/L + previous position P/L** ｜ **当日盈亏＝当日成交盈亏＋上一交易日持仓盈亏**

------

##### (2) Stock Index Futures ｜ （2）股票指数期货

$$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Daily P/L}
=&\sum[(\text{Selling price}-\text{Daily settlement price})\\
&\quad\times\text{Number of contracts sold}
\times\text{Contract multiplier}]\\
&+\sum[(\text{Daily settlement price}-\text{Buying price})\\
&\quad\times\text{Number of contracts bought}
\times\text{Contract multiplier}]\\
&+(\text{Previous settlement price}-\text{Daily settlement price})\\
&\quad\times(\text{Previous short contracts}
-\text{Previous long contracts})\\
&\quad\times\text{Contract multiplier}
\end{aligned}

$$



当日盈亏=∑[(卖出成交价−当日结算价)×卖出量]+∑[(当日结算价−买入成交价)×买入量]+(上一交易日结算价−当日结算价)×(上一交易日卖出持仓量−上一交易日买入持仓量)



The calculation principle is the same as that for commodity futures. However, stock index futures prices are expressed in index points, so the price difference must be multiplied by the contract multiplier to obtain the monetary profit or loss. ｜ 股指期货与商品期货的盈亏计算逻辑相同，但股指期货的成交价和结算价以指数点数表示，因此需要乘以合约乘数，才能计算实际的货币盈亏。

**Stock index futures P/L = change in index points × number of contracts × contract multiplier** ｜ **股指期货盈亏＝指数点数变化×合约手数×合约乘数**

------

###### 3. Daily Trading Margin ｜ 3. 当日交易保证金

##### (1) Commodity Futures ｜ （1）商品期货

$$
\text{Daily trading margin}
=
\text{Daily settlement price}
\times
\text{Total open positions at the end of the day}
\times
\text{Margin ratio}
$$



​           当日交易保证金=当日结算价×当日交易结束后的持仓总量×交易保证金比例

The settlement price is normally expressed as the price per unit of the commodity, while the position quantity represents the total quantity of the commodity covered by the contracts. ｜ 结算价通常按照每一计量单位的商品价格表示，持仓总量则表示期货合约所代表的商品总数量。

------

##### (2) Stock Index Futures ｜ （2）股指期货

$$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Daily trading margin}
=&\ \text{Daily settlement price}\\
&\times\text{Contract multiplier}\\
&\times\text{Total open contracts at the end of the day}\\
&\times\text{Margin ratio}
\end{aligned}
$$



当日交易保证金= 当日结算价×合约乘数×当日交易结束后的持仓总手数×交易保证金比例





In stock index futures calculations, both the transaction price and settlement price are expressed in index points. The contract multiplier converts index points into the monetary value of the contract. ｜ 在股指期货计算中，成交价和结算价都以指数点数表示。合约乘数用于将指数点数转换为期货合约的货币价值。

**Contract value = index points × contract multiplier** ｜ **合约价值＝指数点数×合约乘数**

**Trading margin = contract value × open positions × margin ratio** ｜ **交易保证金＝合约价值×持仓量×保证金比例**

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

💰 **Settlement reserve balance:** available funds after adjusting margin, profit and loss, deposits, withdrawals and fees. ｜ 💰 **结算准备金余额：**保证金、盈亏、出入金和手续费调整后的可用资金。

📊 **Daily profit or loss:** includes the current day’s transaction results and the mark-to-market result of previous positions. ｜ 📊 **当日盈亏：**包括当日成交盈亏和上一交易日持仓的盯市盈亏。

📦 **Commodity futures margin:** settlement price × position quantity × margin ratio. ｜ 📦 **商品期货保证金：**结算价×持仓数量×保证金比例。

📈 **Stock index futures margin:** settlement points × contract multiplier × number of contracts × margin ratio. ｜ 📈 **股指期货保证金：**结算点数×合约乘数×持仓手数×保证金比例。

#### VI. Settlement Between a Futures Company and Its Clients ｜ 六、期货公司对客户的结算

##### (I) Profit-and-Loss Calculation Methods ｜ （一）盈亏计算方式

Futures companies may calculate clients’ profits and losses using either the daily mark-to-market method or the trade-by-trade offset method. ｜ 期货公司可以采用逐日盯市或者逐笔对冲两种方式，计算客户的交易盈亏。

###### 1. Daily Mark-to-Market ｜ 1. 逐日盯市

Under the daily mark-to-market method, profits and losses are calculated every trading day according to the daily mark-to-market settlement system. ｜ 逐日盯市是依据当日无负债结算制度，在每个交易日结束后计算客户的当日盈亏。

The profit or loss on an open contract is treated as mark-to-market profit or loss and is included in the client’s daily account balance. ｜ 对于尚未平仓的合约，其盈亏作为持仓盯市盈亏，并累计计入当日结存。

For positions carried forward from the previous trading day: ｜ 对于上一交易日结转的历史持仓：

- Closing profit or loss is calculated using the previous settlement price and the closing price. ｜ 平仓盈亏按照上一交易日结算价与平仓价计算。
- Holding profit or loss is calculated using the previous settlement price and the current settlement price. ｜ 持仓盯市盈亏按照上一交易日结算价与当日结算价计算。

------

###### 2. Trade-by-Trade Offset ｜ 2. 逐笔对冲

Under the trade-by-trade offset method, the cumulative profit or loss from the opening date to the current trading day is calculated each day. ｜ 逐笔对冲是指每日计算自开仓之日起至当日的累计盈亏，最终反映该笔交易从开仓到当前的总体盈亏。

The profit or loss on an open contract is treated as floating profit or loss and is not included in the daily account balance. ｜ 对于尚未平仓的合约，其盈亏作为浮动盈亏，不计入当日结存。

Once the contract is closed, the floating profit or loss becomes realised closing profit or loss, and the floating profit or loss on that contract becomes zero. ｜ 当合约平仓后，原来的浮动盈亏转为平仓盈亏，该合约的浮动盈亏归零。

Under this method: ｜ 在逐笔对冲方式下：

- Closing profit or loss is calculated using the opening price and the closing price. ｜ 平仓盈亏按照开仓价与平仓价计算。
- Floating profit or loss is calculated using the opening price and the current settlement price. ｜ 浮动盈亏按照开仓价与当日结算价计算。

------

###### Comparison of the Two Methods ｜ 两种方式的区别

| Item                               | Daily Mark-to-Market                                  | Trade-by-Trade Offset                     |
| ---------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- |
| Calculation basis                  | Daily profit or loss                                  | Cumulative profit or loss from opening    |
| Open-position P/L                  | Mark-to-market P/L                                    | Floating P/L                              |
| Included in daily balance          | Yes                                                   | No                                        |
| Closing P/L for previous positions | Previous settlement price vs closing price            | Opening price vs closing price            |
| Open-position P/L                  | Previous settlement price vs current settlement price | Opening price vs current settlement price |

| 项目             | 逐日盯市                   | 逐笔对冲                 |
| ---------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------ |
| 计算基础         | 每日计算当日盈亏           | 计算自开仓以来的累计盈亏 |
| 未平仓合约盈亏   | 持仓盯市盈亏               | 浮动盈亏                 |
| 是否计入当日结存 | 计入                       | 不计入                   |
| 历史持仓平仓盈亏 | 上日结算价与平仓价之差     | 开仓价与平仓价之差       |
| 未平仓持仓盈亏   | 当日结算价与上日结算价之差 | 当日结算价与开仓价之差   |

Although the presentation of profit and loss differs, the two methods produce no difference in margin occupation, daily deposits and withdrawals, transaction fees, client equity, collateral, available funds, additional margin or risk ratio. ｜ 虽然两种方法对盈亏的展示方式不同，但保证金占用、当日出入金、手续费、客户权益、质押金、可用资金、追加保证金和风险度等指标的结果没有差别。

For contracts opened and closed on the same trading day, the profit-or-loss calculation is also the same under both methods. ｜ 对于当日开仓并在当日平仓的合约，两种方法计算出的盈亏也相同。

------

##### (II) Risk Ratio ｜ （二）风险度的相关概念

###### Risk Ratio Formula ｜ 风险度计算公式

$$
\text{Risk ratio}
=
\frac{\text{Margin occupied}}{\text{Client equity}}
\times 100\%
$$


$$
\text{风险度}
=
\frac{\text{保证金占用}}{\text{客户权益}}
\times 100\%
$$
The risk ratio reflects the proportion of client equity that has been occupied by trading margin. ｜ 风险度反映客户权益中已经被交易保证金占用的比例。

The closer the risk ratio is to 100%, the greater the client’s account risk. ｜ 风险度越接近100%，客户账户的风险越大。

When the risk ratio equals 100%, the client’s available funds are zero. ｜ 当风险度等于100%时，客户的可用资金为零。

A futures company generally does not permit the client’s risk ratio to exceed 100%. Otherwise, it will issue a margin call requiring the client to deposit additional funds or reduce positions. ｜ 期货公司通常不允许客户的风险度超过100%。否则，期货公司会发出《追加保证金通知书》，要求客户追加资金或者减少持仓。

📌 **Higher risk ratio → lower available funds → greater probability of forced liquidation.** ｜ 📌 **风险度越高 → 可用资金越少 → 被强行平仓的可能性越大。**

------

#### VII. Delivery ｜ 七、交割

##### (I) Definition of Delivery ｜ （一）交割的概念

Delivery is the process of closing an expired open futures contract according to the rules and procedures of the futures exchange, either by transferring ownership of the underlying asset or by settling the cash price difference. ｜ 交割是指期货合约到期时，按照期货交易所规定的规则和程序，通过转移合约标的物的所有权，或者按照结算价格进行现金差价结算，了结到期未平仓合约的过程。

------

##### (II) Function of Delivery ｜ （二）交割的作用

The delivery system provides an institutional mechanism that causes futures prices and spot prices to converge as the contract approaches expiration. ｜ 交割制度是促使期货价格与现货价格在合约临近到期时趋于一致的重要制度保障。

If futures and spot prices differ significantly near expiration, traders may conduct delivery or arbitrage transactions, causing the two prices to move closer together. ｜ 如果临近到期时期货价格与现货价格差异较大，交易者可以通过交割或者套利进行交易，从而促使两种价格逐渐接近。

📌 **Delivery links the futures market with the spot market.** ｜ 📌 **交割是连接期货市场和现货市场的重要环节。**

------

##### (III) Classification of Delivery ｜ （三）交割的分类

###### 1. Physical Delivery ｜ 1. 实物交割

Physical delivery closes an expired futures contract through the actual transfer of ownership of the underlying commodity. ｜ 实物交割是通过实际转移标的商品所有权，了结到期未平仓合约的交割方式。

**Centralised delivery** ｜ **集中交割**

Centralised delivery is completed at one time after the last trading day of the delivery month. ｜ 集中交割是指在交割月份最后交易日结束后，一次性集中办理交割。

**Rolling delivery** ｜ **滚动交割**

Rolling delivery may be conducted from the first trading day of the delivery month to the trading day immediately before the last trading day. ｜ 滚动交割是指从交割月第一个交易日起，到最后交易日前一个交易日之间，可以办理交割。

------

###### 2. Cash Settlement ｜ 2. 现金交割

Cash settlement closes an expired open contract by calculating and paying the cash price difference according to the exchange’s rules, procedures and published final settlement price. ｜ 现金交割是指合约到期时，交易双方按照交易所规定的规则、程序和公布的交割结算价，通过结算现金差额了结到期未平仓合约。

No actual underlying asset is transferred under cash settlement. ｜ 现金交割不发生标的物所有权的实际转移。

According to the supplied material, the final settlement price of stock index futures is the arithmetic average of the underlying index during the final two hours of the last trading day. ｜ 根据原材料，股票指数期货的交割结算价为最后交易日标的指数最后两小时的算术平均价。

------

###### 3. Delivery Methods for Different Futures Products ｜ 3. 不同期货品种的交割方式

Stock index futures and short-term interest-rate futures generally use cash settlement. ｜ 股指期货和短期利率期货通常采用现金交割。

According to the supplied material, the Shanghai Futures Exchange uses centralised delivery. ｜ 根据原材料，上海期货交易所采用集中交割。

The Zhengzhou Commodity Exchange uses a combination of rolling delivery and centralised delivery. ｜ 郑州商品交易所采用滚动交割与集中交割相结合的方式。

For No. 1 soybeans, No. 2 soybeans, soybean meal, soybean oil and corn contracts, the Dalian Commodity Exchange uses a combination of rolling delivery and centralised delivery. ｜ 大连商品交易所对黄大豆1号、黄大豆2号、豆粕、豆油和玉米合约，采用滚动交割与集中交割相结合的方式。

原文在大连商品交易所其他品种的交割方式处出现截断，因此这里不补充未显示的内容。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

📊 **Daily mark-to-market:** calculates daily profit or loss and includes open-position profit or loss in the daily account balance. ｜ 📊 **逐日盯市：**每日计算盈亏，并将未平仓合约的盯市盈亏计入当日结存。

📈 **Trade-by-trade offset:** calculates cumulative profit or loss from the opening price, while open-position profit or loss remains floating. ｜ 📈 **逐笔对冲：**以开仓价为基础计算累计盈亏，未平仓盈亏作为浮动盈亏。

⚠️ **Risk ratio:** margin occupied divided by client equity. A ratio closer to 100% indicates greater risk. ｜ ⚠️ **风险度：**保证金占用除以客户权益，越接近100%，账户风险越高。

📦 **Physical delivery:** transfers ownership of the underlying asset. ｜ 📦 **实物交割：**通过转移标的物所有权完成交割。

💵 **Cash settlement:** closes the contract by settling the cash price difference. ｜ 💵 **现金交割：**通过结算现金差价了结合约。

🔗 **Delivery function:** promotes convergence between futures prices and spot prices. ｜ 🔗 **交割作用：**促使期货价格与现货价格趋于一致。



### 📌 Examination Topic: Exchange of Futures for Physicals ｜ 📌 考点：期转现

#### I. Exchange of Futures for Physicals ｜ 一、期转现交易

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

An Exchange of Futures for Physicals, or EFP, refers to a transaction in which two members or clients holding opposite positions in contracts of the same product and delivery month reach an agreement and apply to the futures exchange. ｜ 期货转现货交易，简称期转现，是指持有同一品种、同一交割月份但方向相反期货头寸的会员或者客户，经协商一致后向期货交易所提出申请。

After approval by the exchange, the exchange closes both parties’ futures positions at their agreed futures price. This price should generally fall within the price fluctuation range prescribed by the exchange. ｜ 交易所批准后，按照双方商定的期货价格代为平仓。该价格一般应当处于交易所规定的价格波动范围内。

At the same time, the two parties complete the transfer of the corresponding physical commodity or warehouse receipts at the agreed spot price. ｜ 与此同时，双方按照协议确定的现货价格，完成与期货合约标的物品种和数量相对应的现货或者仓单交收。

📌 **Core idea: futures positions are closed and the corresponding spot transaction is completed simultaneously.** ｜ 📌 **核心逻辑：期货头寸平仓与相应的现货交易同时完成。**

------

##### (II) Application of EFP in China ｜ （二）期转现在我国的应用

According to the supplied material, the Dalian Commodity Exchange, Shanghai Futures Exchange and Zhengzhou Commodity Exchange have introduced EFP transactions. ｜ 根据原材料，大连商品交易所、上海期货交易所和郑州商品交易所已经推出期转现交易。

------

##### (III) Advantages of EFP ｜ （三）期转现交易的优越性

EFP can help processing enterprises and production or trading enterprises reduce the costs associated with standard futures delivery. ｜ 加工企业和生产经营企业可以利用期转现，降低期货实物交割成本。

It is more convenient than first closing futures positions and then separately purchasing or selling the physical commodity. ｜ 与“先平仓、再单独购销现货”相比，期转现可以将期货平仓与现货交易结合起来，操作更加便捷。

Compared with ordinary forward contracts and standard futures delivery, EFP allows the parties to negotiate the spot price, delivery arrangements and other terms more flexibly. ｜ 与普通远期合同交易和标准化期货实物交割相比，期转现可以由双方协商现货价格和交收安排，具有更强的灵活性。

📌 **Main advantages: lower delivery costs, simpler procedures and more flexible terms.** ｜ 📌 **主要优势：降低交割成本、简化交易流程、提高交收灵活性。**

------

##### (IV) Basic Process of EFP ｜ （四）期转现交易的基本流程

###### Finding a Counterparty ｜ 寻找交易对手

The parties first identify a suitable counterparty holding an opposite futures position in the same product and delivery month. ｜ 交易双方首先寻找持有同一品种、同一交割月份相反方向头寸的交易对手。

###### Negotiating Prices ｜ 协商交易价格

The parties negotiate two prices: ｜ 双方需要协商两个价格：

- The futures closing price ｜ 期货平仓价格
- The spot delivery price ｜ 现货交收价格

The futures closing price is used to close the futures positions, while the spot delivery price is used for the transfer of the physical commodity or warehouse receipts. ｜ 期货平仓价格用于了结期货头寸，现货交收价格用于完成现货或者仓单交收。
