###### Submitting an Application ｜ 向交易所提出申请

After reaching an agreement, the two parties jointly submit an EFP application to the futures exchange. ｜ 双方协商一致后，共同向期货交易所提交期转现申请。

###### Exchange Approval ｜ 交易所核准

The exchange reviews the application and determines whether it complies with the relevant rules. ｜ 交易所对申请进行审核，确认其是否符合相关规定。

###### Completing the Procedures ｜ 办理相关手续

After approval, the exchange closes the futures positions, while the two parties complete the payment, transfer of goods or warehouse receipts and other delivery procedures. ｜ 交易所核准后，由交易所代为平仓，双方办理货款支付、货物或者仓单转移等交收手续。

###### Paying Taxes ｜ 纳税

The parties pay the relevant taxes according to the requirements applicable to the spot transaction. ｜ 交易双方按照现货交易的相关规定缴纳税费。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🔄 **EFP combines futures position closing with spot delivery.** ｜ 🔄 **期转现将期货平仓与现货交收结合起来。**

🤝 **Both parties must hold opposite positions in the same product and delivery month.** ｜ 🤝 **交易双方应当持有同品种、同月份的相反期货头寸。**

💰 **The parties separately agree on the futures closing price and the spot delivery price.** ｜ 💰 **双方需要分别商定期货平仓价和现货交收价。**

🏛️ **The transaction must be submitted to and approved by the futures exchange.** ｜ 🏛️ **期转现交易需要向期货交易所申请并获得核准。**

📦 **Its advantages include lower costs, simpler procedures and more flexible delivery arrangements.** ｜ 📦 **其优势包括降低成本、简化程序以及提高交收灵活性。**

# Hedging ｜ 套期保值

This chapter accounts for approximately 10% of the examination score. ｜ 本章分值占比约为10%。

## Section 1: Concept and Principles of Hedging ｜ 第一节 套期保值的概念与原理

#### I. Risks Faced by Enterprises and Risk-Management Methods ｜ 一、企业面临的风险与管理方式

##### (I) Risks Faced in Business Operations ｜ （一）企业经营面临的风险

Enterprises may face price risk, political risk, legal risk, operational risk and credit risk during their production and business activities. ｜ 企业在生产经营过程中可能面临价格风险、政治风险、法律风险、操作风险和信用风险。

Enterprise risk management refers to identifying, measuring, analysing and evaluating these risks, and then adopting timely and effective measures to prevent and control them. Its objective is to manage risks in the most economical and reasonable way and provide the greatest possible protection for the enterprise. ｜ 企业风险管理是指对各种风险进行识别、衡量、分析和评价，并及时采取有效措施进行防范和控制，以最经济、合理的方式综合处理风险，从而最大程度保障企业经营安全。

##### (II) Methods of Managing Risk ｜ （二）企业管理风险的手段

Enterprises may manage risks through avoidance, prevention, diversification and transfer. ｜ 企业可以通过消极躲避、预防、分散和转移等方式管理风险。

Hedging belongs to the category of risk transfer. It transfers the adverse effects of price movements through transactions in derivatives markets. ｜ 套期保值属于风险转移方式，即通过衍生品市场交易，转移价格不利变动可能带来的影响。

##### (III) Types of Risk Transferred Through Hedging ｜ （三）套期保值转移的风险类型

Hedging may be used to transfer different types of price risk, including commodity-price risk, interest-rate risk, exchange-rate risk and stock-price risk. ｜ 套期保值可以用于转移不同类型的价格风险，包括商品价格风险、利率风险、汇率风险和股票价格风险。

Derivatives may also be used to manage certain forms of credit risk. ｜ 衍生工具也可以用于管理特定形式的信用风险。

------

#### II. Principles of Hedging ｜ 二、套期保值的原理

##### (I) Definition of Hedging ｜ （一）套期保值的概念

Hedging, also called risk hedging or offsetting, broadly refers to an enterprise using one or more financial instruments to offset all or part of the price risks arising from its production and business activities. ｜ 套期保值又称避险或者对冲。广义上的套期保值，是指企业利用一种或者多种金融工具进行交易，预期抵销其生产经营中面临的全部或者部分价格风险。

The purpose of hedging is generally not to earn additional speculative profits, but to reduce the uncertainty caused by future price movements. ｜ 套期保值的主要目的通常不是获取额外的投机收益，而是降低未来价格变化带来的不确定性。

##### (II) Hedging Instruments ｜ （二）套期保值工具

Common hedging instruments include futures, options, forwards and swaps. ｜ 常见的套期保值工具包括期货、期权、远期和互换。

##### (III) Basic Principle ｜ （三）套期保值的基本原理

Futures prices and spot prices are affected by similar supply, demand and market factors, so their movements generally tend to be in the same direction. ｜ 期货价格和现货价格受到相似的供求关系和市场因素影响，因此二者的变动趋势通常趋于一致。

An enterprise establishes a position in the futures market that offsets its spot-market exposure. Whether prices rise or fall, the profit in one market can offset the loss in the other market. ｜ 企业在期货市场建立与现货风险相抵销的头寸。无论价格上涨还是下跌，一个市场的盈利都可以抵销另一个市场的亏损。

📌 **Core principle: opposite exposures in the spot and futures markets offset price risk.** ｜ 📌 **核心原理：通过现货市场与期货市场的相反风险敞口抵销价格风险。**

------

#### III. Conditions for Effective Risk Hedging ｜ 三、实现风险对冲应满足的条件

##### (I) Futures and Spot Values Should Be Approximately Equal ｜ （一）期货与现货的价值变动应当大体相当

The quantity of futures contracts should be selected so that changes in the value of the futures position are approximately equal to changes in the value of the spot position. ｜ 套期保值者应当合理选择期货合约数量，使期货头寸与现货头寸的价值变动大体相当。

The hedging ratio is the ratio between the quantity represented by the futures contracts and the quantity of the spot position being hedged. ｜ 套期保值比率是期货合约代表的数量与被套期保值现货数量之间的比率。
$$
\text{Hedge ratio}
=
\frac{\text{Quantity represented by futures contracts}}
{\text{Quantity of the spot position}}
$$
In a simple full hedge, the hedging ratio is usually approximately 1. ｜ 在简单的完全套期保值中，套期保值比率通常约为1。

##### (II) The Futures Position Should Offset the Spot Exposure ｜ （二）期货头寸方向应当与现货风险相反

A spot long position exists when an enterprise currently owns a commodity or asset, or has agreed to purchase it at a fixed price in the future. Its main risk is a decline in price. ｜ 当企业已经持有某种商品或资产，或者已经按照固定价格约定在未来购买该商品或资产时，企业处于现货多头状态，主要担心价格下跌。

An enterprise with a spot long position normally establishes a short position in the futures market. ｜ 企业处于现货多头时，通常应当在期货市场建立空头头寸，即卖出期货进行套期保值。

A spot short position exists when an enterprise has agreed to sell a commodity or asset at a fixed price in the future but does not yet own it. Its main risk is a rise in the purchase price. ｜ 当企业已经按照固定价格约定未来出售某种商品或资产，但目前尚未持有该商品或资产时，企业处于现货空头状态，主要担心未来采购价格上涨。

An enterprise with a spot short position normally establishes a long position in the futures market. ｜ 企业处于现货空头时，通常应当在期货市场建立多头头寸，即买入期货进行套期保值。

📌 **Spot long → sell futures.** ｜ 📌 **现货多头 → 卖出期货。**

📌 **Spot short → buy futures.** ｜ 📌 **现货空头 → 买入期货。**

##### (III) The Hedging Period Should Correspond to the Risk Period ｜ （三）期货持仓时间应当与现货风险期间对应

The period during which the futures position is held should correspond to the period during which the enterprise faces spot-market risk. ｜ 期货头寸的持有期间应当与企业承担现货市场风险的期间相对应。

When the spot transaction is completed or the spot position is closed, the enterprise should normally close the futures position or complete both positions through delivery. ｜ 当现货交易完成或者现货头寸了结时，企业通常应当平掉套期保值的期货头寸，或者通过到期交割同时了结期货和现货头寸。

The futures contract month does not have to correspond exactly to the spot risk period. It is normally selected to mature at or after the end of that risk period. ｜ 期货合约月份不一定必须与现货风险期间完全一致，通常应选择到期时间等于或者晚于现货风险结束时间的合约。

------

## Section 2: Types of Hedging ｜ 第二节 套期保值的种类

#### I. Short Hedging ｜ 一、卖出套期保值

##### (I) Applicable Circumstances ｜ （一）卖出套期保值适用的情形

Short hedging is used when an enterprise is exposed to the risk of falling prices. ｜ 卖出套期保值适用于企业担心未来价格下跌的情形。

It is commonly used when: ｜ 常见适用情形包括：

- The enterprise currently owns a commodity or asset and is concerned that its market value or future sales revenue may decline. ｜ 企业目前持有某种商品或资产，担心市场价格下跌导致资产价值或者销售收入下降。
- The enterprise has purchased a commodity or asset for future delivery at a fixed price and is concerned that its value may decline. ｜ 企业已经按照固定价格买入未来交收的商品或资产，担心市场价格下跌。
- The enterprise expects to sell a commodity or asset in the future, but the sales price has not yet been determined. ｜ 企业预计未来将销售某种商品或资产，但销售价格尚未确定，担心未来价格下跌。

The enterprise sells futures contracts now and later buys them back when the spot sale occurs. ｜ 企业现在卖出期货合约，待未来进行现货销售时再买入期货平仓。

📌 **Concerned about falling prices → sell futures.** ｜ 📌 **担心价格下跌 → 卖出期货。**

------

#### II. Long Hedging ｜ 二、买入套期保值

##### (I) Applicable Circumstances ｜ （一）买入套期保值适用的情形

Long hedging is used when an enterprise is exposed to the risk of rising prices. ｜ 买入套期保值适用于企业担心未来价格上涨的情形。

It is commonly used when: ｜ 常见适用情形包括：

- The enterprise expects to purchase a commodity or asset in the future, but the purchase price has not yet been determined. ｜ 企业预计未来需要购买某种商品或资产，但采购价格尚未确定，担心价格上涨导致采购成本增加。
- The enterprise has already agreed to sell a commodity or asset at a fixed price but does not currently own it. ｜ 企业尚未持有某种商品或资产，但已经按照固定价格将其出售，担心未来采购价格上涨。

The enterprise buys futures contracts now and later sells them when it purchases the spot commodity. ｜ 企业现在买入期货合约，待未来采购现货时再卖出期货平仓。

📌 **Concerned about rising prices → buy futures.** ｜ 📌 **担心价格上涨 → 买入期货。**

------

## Section 3: Basis and Hedging Effectiveness ｜ 第三节 基差与套期保值效果

#### I. Causes of Imperfect Hedging ｜ 一、导致不完全套期保值的原因

A hedge may be imperfect because futures prices and spot prices do not always change by exactly the same amount. ｜ 期货价格和现货价格的变动幅度并不总是完全一致，因此套期保值可能无法完全抵销风险。

The grade or quality of the futures contract’s underlying commodity may differ from the commodity traded by the hedger in the spot market. ｜ 期货合约标的物的等级或者质量，可能与套期保值者在现货市场交易的商品存在差异。

The quantity of futures positions may differ from the quantity of the spot position being hedged. ｜ 期货市场建立的头寸数量，可能与被套期保值的现货数量存在差异。

Some enterprises may also lack a futures product that corresponds directly to the finished product they produce or trade. ｜ 部分企业缺少与其产成品直接对应的期货品种，只能使用相关品种进行交叉套期保值。

------

#### II. Definition of Basis ｜ 二、基差的定义

Basis is the difference between the spot price of a commodity or asset at a particular location and the price of a specified futures contract for the same or related commodity or asset. ｜ 基差是某一特定地点的商品或者资产现货价格，与相同或者相关商品某一特定期货合约价格之间的差额。
$$
\text{Basis}=\text{Spot price}-\text{Futures price}
$$
If the spot price is higher than the futures price, the basis is positive. ｜ 当现货价格高于期货价格时，基差为正。

If the spot price is lower than the futures price, the basis is negative. ｜ 当现货价格低于期货价格时，基差为负。

------

#### III. Strengthening and Weakening of Basis ｜ 三、基差走强与基差走弱

##### (I) Strengthening Basis ｜ （一）基差走强

A strengthening basis means that the numerical value of the basis increases. ｜ 基差走强是指基差的数值变大。

This includes: ｜ 具体包括：

- A positive basis becoming more positive ｜ 基差为正且数值继续增大
- A negative basis becoming less negative ｜ 基差为负但绝对值减小
- A basis changing from negative to positive ｜ 基差由负数变为正数

Examples: ｜ 例如：
$$
-300\rightarrow-200
$$

##### (II) Weakening Basis ｜ （二）基差走弱

A weakening basis means that the numerical value of the basis decreases. ｜ 基差走弱是指基差的数值变小。

This includes: ｜ 具体包括：

- A positive basis becoming less positive ｜ 基差为正但数值减小
- A negative basis becoming more negative ｜ 基差为负且绝对值增大
- A basis changing from positive to negative ｜ 基差由正数变为负数

Examples: ｜ 例如：
$$
300\rightarrow200
$$
📌 **Do not judge basis changes by absolute value alone. Compare the actual numerical value.** ｜ 📌 **判断基差走强或走弱时，应比较基差数值本身，不能只比较绝对值。**

------

#### IV. Basis Changes and Hedging Effectiveness ｜ 四、基差变动与套期保值效果

##### (I) Basis Changes and Short Hedging ｜ （一）基差变动与卖出套期保值

###### Case 1: Strengthening Basis ｜ 案例一：基差走强

At the beginning of May, a sugar factory agreed to sell 100 tonnes of sugar in early August at the market price prevailing at that time. The current spot price was RMB 5,500 per tonne. ｜ 5月初，某糖厂约定在8月初按照届时市场价格销售100吨白糖，当时现货价格为5500元/吨。

To protect against falling prices, the factory sold 10 September sugar futures contracts, each representing 10 tonnes, at RMB 5,800 per tonne. ｜ 为了防范价格下跌，该糖厂卖出10手9月份白糖期货合约，每手10吨，成交价格为5800元/吨。

By early August, the spot price had fallen to RMB 5,000 per tonne and the futures price had fallen to RMB 5,200 per tonne. ｜ 到8月初，白糖现货价格下跌至5000元/吨，期货价格下跌至5200元/吨。

| Stage   | Spot market | Futures market      | Basis               |
| ------- | ----------- | ------------------- | ------------------- |
| Opening | 5,500       | 5,800               | -300                |
| Closing | 5,000       | 5,200               | -200                |
| Change  | -500        | +600 futures profit | Strengthened by 100 |

| 阶段 | 现货市场 | 期货市场    | 基差    |
| ---- | -------- | ----------- | ------- |
| 开始 | 5500     | 5800        | -300    |
| 结束 | 5000     | 5200        | -200    |
| 盈亏 | -500     | 期货盈利600 | 走强100 |

$$
\text{Effective selling price}
=
5,000+600
=
5,600
$$

The effective selling price was RMB 100 higher than the original spot price of RMB 5,500. ｜ 实际销售价格比最初的现货价格5500元/吨高100元。

📌 **For a short hedge, a strengthening basis produces a net gain.** ｜ 📌 **卖出套期保值中，基差走强产生净盈利。**

------

###### Case 2: Weakening Basis ｜ 案例二：基差走弱

At the beginning of May, a dealer held 5,000 tonnes of steel with a spot price of RMB 4,380 per tonne. ｜ 5月初，某经销商持有5000吨钢材，当时现货价格为4380元/吨。

The dealer sold 500 November rebar futures contracts at RMB 4,800 per tonne. Each contract represented 10 tonnes. ｜ 为防范钢材价格下跌，该经销商卖出500手11月份螺纹钢期货合约，每手10吨，成交价格为4800元/吨。

In August, the spot steel was sold at RMB 4,850 per tonne, while the futures contracts were closed at RMB 5,330 per tonne. ｜ 到8月，该经销商以4850元/吨出售现货，同时以5330元/吨买入期货平仓。

| Stage   | Spot market | Futures market | Basis          |
| ------- | ----------- | -------------- | -------------- |
| Opening | 4,380       | 4,800          | -420           |
| Closing | 4,850       | 5,330          | -480           |
| Change  | +470        | -530           | Weakened by 60 |

| 阶段 | 现货市场 | 期货市场 | 基差   |
| ---- | -------- | -------- | ------ |
| 开始 | 4380     | 4800     | -420   |
| 结束 | 4850     | 5330     | -480   |
| 盈亏 | +470     | -530     | 走弱60 |

$$
\text{Effective selling price}
=
4,850-530
=
4,320
$$

The effective selling price was RMB 60 lower than the original spot price. ｜ 实际销售价格比最初的现货价格低60元/吨。

📌 **For a short hedge, a weakening basis produces a net loss.** ｜ 📌 **卖出套期保值中，基差走弱产生净亏损。**

------

##### (II) Basis Changes and Long Hedging ｜ （二）基差变动与买入套期保值

###### Case 1: Strengthening Basis ｜ 案例一：基差走强

At the beginning of May, a feed company expected to purchase 3,000 tonnes of soybean meal three months later. ｜ 5月初，某饲料公司预计三个月后需要购买3000吨豆粕。

To protect against rising prices, it bought 300 September soybean-meal futures contracts at RMB 2,910 per tonne. The current spot price was RMB 3,160 per tonne. ｜ 为了防范价格上涨，该公司以2910元/吨买入300手9月份豆粕期货合约，当时现货价格为3160元/吨。

In August, the spot price increased to RMB 3,600 per tonne and the futures position was closed at RMB 3,280 per tonne. ｜ 到8月，豆粕现货价格上涨至3600元/吨，期货平仓价格为3280元/吨。

| Stage   | Spot market                    | Futures market     | Basis              |
| ------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------ | ------------------ |
| Opening | 3,160                          | 2,910              | 250                |
| Closing | 3,600                          | 3,280              | 320                |
| Change  | Purchase cost increased by 440 | Futures profit 370 | Strengthened by 70 |

| 阶段 | 现货市场        | 期货市场    | 基差   |
| ---- | --------------- | ----------- | ------ |
| 开始 | 3160            | 2910        | 250    |
| 结束 | 3600            | 3280        | 320    |
| 盈亏 | 采购成本增加440 | 期货盈利370 | 走强70 |

$$
\text{Effective purchase price}
=
3,600-370
=
3,230
$$

The effective purchase price was RMB 70 higher than the initial spot price. ｜ 实际采购价格比最初现货价格高70元/吨。

📌 **For a long hedge, a strengthening basis produces a net loss.** ｜ 📌 **买入套期保值中，基差走强产生净亏损。**

------

###### Case 2: Weakening Basis ｜ 案例二：基差走弱

At the beginning of March, a tyre manufacturer bought 100 July natural-rubber futures contracts at RMB 24,000 per tonne to hedge the purchase of 1,000 tonnes of rubber. The current spot price was RMB 23,000 per tonne. ｜ 3月初，某轮胎企业为未来采购1000吨天然橡胶进行套期保值，以24000元/吨买入100手7月份天然橡胶期货合约，当时现货价格为23000元/吨。

By early June, the spot price had fallen to RMB 20,000 per tonne and the futures price had fallen to RMB 21,200 per tonne. ｜ 到6月初，天然橡胶现货价格下跌至20000元/吨，期货价格下跌至21200元/吨。

| Stage   | Spot market                      | Futures market     | Basis           |
| ------- | -------------------------------- | ------------------ | --------------- |
| Opening | 23,000                           | 24,000             | -1,000          |
| Closing | 20,000                           | 21,200             | -1,200          |
| Change  | Purchase cost decreased by 3,000 | Futures loss 2,800 | Weakened by 200 |

| 阶段 | 现货市场         | 期货市场     | 基差    |
| ---- | ---------------- | ------------ | ------- |
| 开始 | 23000            | 24000        | -1000   |
| 结束 | 20000            | 21200        | -1200   |
| 盈亏 | 采购成本降低3000 | 期货亏损2800 | 走弱200 |

$$
\text{Effective purchase price}
=
20,000+2,800
=
22,800
$$

The effective purchase price was RMB 200 lower than the initial spot price. ｜ 实际采购价格比最初的现货价格低200元/吨。

📌 **For a long hedge, a weakening basis produces a net gain.** ｜ 📌 **买入套期保值中，基差走弱产生净盈利。**

------

##### (III) Effect of Basis Changes ｜ （三）基差变动对套期保值的影响

| Basis change      | Long hedge     | Short hedge    |
| ----------------- | -------------- | -------------- |
| Basis unchanged   | Complete hedge | Complete hedge |
| Basis strengthens | Net loss       | Net gain       |
| Basis weakens     | Net gain       | Net loss       |

| 基差变动 | 买入套期保值 | 卖出套期保值 |
| -------- | ------------ | ------------ |
| 基差不变 | 完全套期保值 | 完全套期保值 |
| 基差走强 | 净亏损       | 净盈利       |
| 基差走弱 | 净盈利       | 净亏损       |

🧠 **Memory aid: buy benefits from weakening; sell benefits from strengthening.** ｜ 🧠 **记忆口诀：买入套保喜欢基差走弱，卖出套保喜欢基差走强。**

------

#### V. Factors Affecting Basis ｜ 五、影响基差的因素

##### (I) Carrying Costs ｜ （一）持仓费

Carrying costs, also called costs of carry, refer to the total costs of owning or holding a commodity or asset, including storage expenses, insurance expenses and interest costs. ｜ 持仓费又称持仓成本，是指拥有或者保留某种商品或资产所需要支付的仓储费、保险费和利息等费用的总和。

Carrying costs generally increase as the time remaining until the futures contract expires becomes longer. ｜ 距离期货合约到期时间越长，持仓费通常越高。

📌 **Longer time to maturity → higher carrying costs.** ｜ 📌 **距离到期时间越长 → 持仓费通常越高。**

##### (II) Normal and Inverted Markets ｜ （二）正向市场与反向市场

###### Normal Market ｜ 正向市场

A normal market exists when the futures price is higher than the spot price, or when a distant futures contract is priced higher than a nearby futures contract. ｜ 当期货价格高于现货价格，或者远月期货合约价格高于近月合约价格时，市场处于正向市场状态。

In a normal market, the basis is normally negative. ｜ 正向市场中，基差通常为负值。

The price difference mainly reflects carrying costs. ｜ 这种价差主要反映商品的持仓成本。
$$
F>S,\qquad \text{Basis}<0
$$

###### Inverted Market ｜ 反向市场

An inverted market, also called a backwardation market or spot-premium market, exists when the spot price is higher than the futures price, or when a nearby futures contract is priced higher than a distant contract. ｜ 当现货价格高于期货价格，或者近月期货合约价格高于远月合约价格时，市场处于反向市场状态，也称逆转市场或者现货溢价。

In an inverted market, the basis is normally positive. ｜ 反向市场中，基差通常为正值。
$$
S>F,\qquad \text{Basis}>0
$$
An inverted market may arise because current demand is very strong relative to current production and inventories, causing spot prices to rise sharply. ｜ 如果近期需求远高于当前产量和库存，现货供应紧张，现货价格可能显著高于期货价格，从而形成反向市场。

It may also arise because the market expects future supply to increase substantially, causing distant futures prices to fall below current spot prices. ｜ 如果市场预计未来供给将大幅增加，远期期货价格可能下降至现货价格以下，也会形成反向市场。

------

## Section 4: Expansion of Hedging Business ｜ 第四节 套期保值的业务拓展

### 📌 Examination Topic: Price-Fixing Transactions ｜ 📌 考点：点价交易

#### I. Methods for Avoiding Basis Risk ｜ 一、规避基差风险的操作方式

##### (I) Price-Fixing Transactions ｜ （一）点价交易

A price-fixing transaction is a spot-pricing method in which the price of a specified futures contract is used as the pricing benchmark, and the final spot price is determined by adding or subtracting a premium or discount agreed by the parties. ｜ 点价交易是指以某一月份期货合约的价格为计价基础，再加上或者减去交易双方协商确定的升贴水，从而确定现货商品交易价格的定价方式。
$$
\text{Spot transaction price}
=
\text{Futures benchmark price}
\pm
\text{Agreed premium or discount}
$$
Price fixing is essentially a method of determining the price of a spot transaction. The parties do not necessarily need to participate directly in futures trading. ｜ 点价交易本质上是一种现货贸易定价方式，交易双方并不一定需要直接参与期货交易。

##### (II) Classification ｜ （二）分类

According to which party has the right to determine the specific futures price used for settlement, price-fixing transactions may be divided into buyer’s pricing and seller’s pricing. ｜ 根据确定具体期货计价价格的权利归属，点价交易可以分为买方叫价和卖方叫价。

Under buyer’s pricing, the buyer selects the time and corresponding futures price used as the pricing benchmark. ｜ 在买方叫价中，由买方选择点价时间和作为计价基础的期货价格。

Under seller’s pricing, the seller selects the time and corresponding futures price. ｜ 在卖方叫价中，由卖方选择点价时间和相应的期货价格。

##### (III) Combining Price Fixing with Hedging ｜ （三）点价交易与套期保值的结合

Before the final price is fixed, the agreed futures benchmark price continues to fluctuate, so the party responsible for pricing still faces price risk. ｜ 在正式点价之前，双方约定作为计价基础的期货价格仍会不断变化，因此相关交易者仍然面临价格波动风险。

To manage this risk, the enterprise may combine the price-fixing transaction with futures hedging. This arrangement is called basis trading. ｜ 为了管理这一风险，企业可以将点价交易与期货套期保值结合起来，这种交易方式称为基差交易。

📌 **Price fixing determines the spot price; hedging manages the price risk before the fixing occurs.** ｜ 📌 **点价交易用于确定现货价格，套期保值用于管理正式点价前的价格风险。**

------

#### II. Matters Requiring Attention in Corporate Hedging ｜ 二、企业开展套期保值业务需注意的事项

Before participating in futures hedging, an enterprise should evaluate its own production, operating activities, risk exposure, financial capacity and hedging needs. ｜ 企业参与期货套期保值前，应当结合自身生产经营情况、风险敞口、资金能力和套期保值需求进行评估。

The enterprise should establish an appropriate organisational structure and clarify the responsibilities of decision-making, trading, settlement and risk-control personnel. ｜ 企业应当完善套期保值的组织机构设置，明确决策、交易、结算和风险控制等岗位的职责。

It should establish sound internal-control and risk-management systems. ｜ 企业需要建立健全的内部控制制度和风险管理制度。

The enterprise should also strengthen the management of cash-flow risk, liquidity risk and operational risk arising from hedging transactions. ｜ 企业还应当加强对套期保值交易中现金流风险、流动性风险和操作风险的管理。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🛡️ **Hedging transfers price risk by establishing an offsetting derivatives position.** ｜ 🛡️ **套期保值通过建立相反方向的衍生品头寸转移价格风险。**

📉 **Short hedging is used when an enterprise is concerned about falling prices.** ｜ 📉 **担心价格下跌时，采用卖出套期保值。**

📈 **Long hedging is used when an enterprise is concerned about rising prices.** ｜ 📈 **担心价格上涨时，采用买入套期保值。**

⚖️ **Basis equals the spot price minus the futures price.** ｜ ⚖️ **基差等于现货价格减去期货价格。**

💰 **A strengthening basis benefits a short hedge but harms a long hedge.** ｜ 💰 **基差走强有利于卖出套期保值，不利于买入套期保值。**

💰 **A weakening basis benefits a long hedge but harms a short hedge.** ｜ 💰 **基差走弱有利于买入套期保值，不利于卖出套期保值。**

🧠 **Memory aid: buy benefits from weakening; sell benefits from strengthening.** ｜ 🧠 **记忆口诀：买走弱，卖走强。**

📦 **A normal market usually has a negative basis, while an inverted market usually has a positive basis.** ｜ 📦 **正向市场的基差通常为负，反向市场的基差通常为正。**

🔗 **Price-fixing transactions use a futures price plus or minus an agreed premium or discount to determine the spot price.** ｜ 🔗 **点价交易以期货价格加减约定升贴水确定现货交易价格。**

## Section 4: Business Expansion of Hedging ｜ 第四节 套期保值的业务拓展

#### I. Cooperative Hedging ｜ 一、合作套保

##### (I) Background ｜ （一）背景

In practice, some enterprises, especially small and medium-sized enterprises, have hedging needs but find it difficult to use the futures market independently because of limited professional personnel and operational capabilities. ｜ 现实中，一些企业，尤其是中小型企业，虽然具有套期保值需求，但由于缺乏专业人才、操作能力不足等限制，难以独立利用期货市场进行套期保值。

##### (II) Operating Method ｜ （二）操作方法

The risk management subsidiary of a futures company provides services according to the enterprise’s actual production and operational needs. It participates in different stages of the enterprise’s operations, helps identify and accurately measure risk exposures, and provides support throughout the process, from risk-management planning and hedging-strategy design to the implementation of specific hedging transactions. ｜ 期货公司的风险管理子公司根据企业实际生产经营需求，深入企业生产经营的各个环节，帮助企业提高风险识别能力和风险敞口的准确计量能力，并从风险管理预案策划、套期保值方案制定到具体交易实施，全程参与企业的风险管理。

##### (III) Cooperative Hedging Models ｜ （三）合作套保模式

**Guided operation** ｜ **指导操作模式**

Small and medium-sized enterprises conduct spot-related procurement, sales, logistics and warehousing activities under the guidance of the futures company. The futures company is responsible for designing hedging strategies and providing risk-management services. ｜ 中小企业客户在期货公司的指导下开展现货采购、销售、物流和仓储等业务，期货公司负责制定套期保值策略并提供风险管理服务。

**Outsourced hedging** ｜ **套保外包模式**

Small and medium-sized enterprises outsource hedging operations to the risk management subsidiary of a futures company. According to the specific hedging objectives proposed by the client, the risk management company uses futures or spot instruments to help the enterprise achieve its risk-management objectives. ｜ 中小企业客户将套期保值操作外包给期货公司的风险管理子公司。风险管理公司根据客户提出的具体保值目标，利用期货或者现货工具开展风险管理，帮助企业完成保值目标。

##### (IV) Prohibited Conduct in Managing Cooperative Hedging Funds ｜ （四）合作套保资金管理和使用中的禁止行为

A risk management company and its client may not conduct cooperative hedging business with financial institutions, asset-management products, private fund managers and their products, or natural persons. ｜ 风险管理公司与客户不得同金融机构、资产管理产品、私募基金管理人及其产品或者自然人签订合同或者开展合作套保业务。

The risk management company may not provide cooperative hedging funds to the client. ｜ 风险管理公司不得为客户提供合作套保资金。

The risk management company may not misappropriate the client’s cooperative hedging funds. ｜ 风险管理公司不得挪用客户的合作套保资金。

Other conduct prohibited by the China Securities Regulatory Commission or the relevant industry association is also not permitted. ｜ 中国证监会及相关行业协会禁止的其他行为同样不得实施。

------

#### II. Basis Trading ｜ 二、基差贸易

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

Basis trading conducted by risk management companies refers to spot transactions in which the company provides quotations using a fixed price, price fixing, average pricing or other agreed methods and trades physical commodities directly with clients. ｜ 风险管理公司开展的基差贸易，是指风险管理公司采用确定价格、点价、均价等方式向客户提供报价，并与客户直接进行现货交易的业务。

In a typical basis transaction, the spot transaction price is linked to a specified futures price and an agreed basis. ｜ 典型的基差贸易中，现货交易价格与指定期货价格及双方约定的基差相联系。
$$
\text{Spot transaction price}
=
\text{Futures benchmark price}
\pm
\text{Agreed basis}
$$

##### (II) Standard Warehouse Receipt Basis-Trading Case ｜ （二）标准仓单基差贸易案例

**Background** ｜ **背景**

A lead smelter had sold December lead futures contracts and planned to transport a batch of lead ingots to a delivery warehouse in early November for physical delivery. ｜ 某铅冶炼厂已经卖出12月份交割的铅期货合约，计划在11月初将一批铅锭运送到交割仓库，准备进行实物交割。

Because of funding needs, the smelter wanted to sell the goods in advance. However, it believed that the current market price of lead ingots was too low. ｜ 后来由于资金需求，该冶炼厂希望提前出售这批货物，但认为当时的铅锭市场价格偏低。

**Operating method** ｜ **操作方法**

The smelter signed a basis sale contract with a risk management company and sold the goods through the pricing formula **PB1812 − 100**. ｜ 该冶炼厂与风险管理公司签订基差销售合同，并按照 **PB1812－100** 的基差点价方式销售该批货物。

This means that the final selling price was determined using the PB1812 lead futures price minus RMB 100 per tonne. ｜ 这表示最终销售价格以PB1812铅期货价格减去100元/吨确定。

The risk management company used the average price of No. 1 lead published by the relevant non-ferrous metals information platform as the provisional settlement price and paid 80% of the provisional payment. ｜ 风险管理公司以相关有色金属信息平台公布的1号铅平均价格作为临时结算价，先支付80%的临时货款。

The smelter was given a one-week pricing period, while the remaining 20% of the provisional payment served as pricing margin. ｜ 风险管理公司给予冶炼厂一周的点价期限，剩余20%的临时货款作为冶炼厂的点价保证金。

After the smelter completed the price fixing, the risk management company calculated the final payment and paid the remaining amount. ｜ 冶炼厂完成点价后，风险管理公司按照实际成交价格进行结算，并支付剩余货款。

**Results** ｜ **结果**

The risk management company purchased the lead ingots through basis pricing and used them for physical delivery, obtaining income from the standard warehouse receipt delivery business. ｜ 风险管理公司通过基差点价方式采购铅锭，并将其用于期货实物交割，从标准仓单卖出交割业务中获得收益。

The smelter quickly obtained funds while retaining control over the final selling price. ｜ 铅冶炼厂既快速回笼了资金，又保留了对最终销售价格的控制权。

📌 **The enterprise receives most of the payment first and determines the final price later through futures-based price fixing.** ｜ 📌 **企业先取得大部分货款，再通过期货点价确定最终销售价格。**

------

#### III. “Insurance + Futures” ｜ 三、“保险＋期货”

##### (I) Basic Structure ｜ （一）模式逻辑

The “insurance + futures” model transfers agricultural risks through several stages. ｜ “保险＋期货”模式通过多个环节逐层转移农业经营风险。
$$
\text{Farmer or enterprise}
\rightarrow
\text{Insurance company}
\rightarrow
\text{Futures company}
\rightarrow
\text{Futures market}
$$
The farmer or enterprise first transfers risk to the insurance company by purchasing insurance. ｜ 农户或者企业首先通过购买保险，将风险转移给保险公司。

The insurance company then purchases an over-the-counter option from the futures company and transfers the price risk again. ｜ 保险公司再向期货公司购买场外期权，将相关价格风险进行第二次转移。

The futures company finally hedges the risk through futures or options transactions. ｜ 期货公司最终通过期货或者期权交易完成风险对冲。

------

##### (II) Classification by the Type of Risk Protected ｜ （二）按照规避风险类型分类

###### Price-Protection Model ｜ 价格保护型模式

The objective of the price-protection model is to protect farmers against declines in agricultural-product prices. ｜ 价格保护型模式的目标是帮助农户规避农产品价格下跌风险。

The farmer purchases insurance from an insurance company. The policy specifies a minimum protected price for the agricultural product. ｜ 农户向保险公司购买农产品价格保险，保险合同约定一个最低保障价格。

If the market price during the harvest season falls below the protected price, the insurance company compensates the farmer for the price difference. ｜ 如果收获季节的市场价格低于最低保障价格，保险公司按照约定向农户赔付价格差额。

The insurance company pays an option premium and purchases an over-the-counter put option from the futures company, transferring the risk of falling prices to the futures company. ｜ 保险公司支付期权费，向期货公司买入场外看跌期权，从而将价格下跌风险转移给期货公司。

The futures company may replicate the put option in the exchange-traded market or trade an equivalent quantity of futures contracts in the opposite direction to hedge the risk. ｜ 期货公司可以在场内复制相应的看跌期权，或者通过买卖数量相当、风险方向相反的期货合约进行对冲。

📌 **Price falls → insurer compensates the farmer → put option compensates the insurer.** ｜ 📌 **价格下跌 → 保险公司赔付农户 → 看跌期权补偿保险公司。**

------

###### Income-Protection Model ｜ 收入保障型模式

The objective of the income-protection model is to provide farmers with relatively stable agricultural sales revenue. ｜ 收入保障型模式的目标是保障农户获得相对稳定的农产品销售收入。

The farmer pays an insurance premium and obtains an income insurance policy. ｜ 农户支付保险费并取得一份收入保险。

If the actual output value during the harvest season is lower than the target output value specified in the policy, the insurance company compensates the farmer for the income difference. ｜ 如果收获季节的实际产值低于保险合同约定的目标产值，保险公司向农户赔付相应的收入差额。

Agricultural revenue is jointly determined by price and output. ｜ 农产品收入由价格和产量共同决定。
$$
\text{Revenue}
=
\text{Price}
\times
\text{Output}
$$
The insurance company transfers price risk to the futures company in the same way as under the price-protection model. ｜ 保险公司按照价格保护型模式，将价格风险转移给期货公司。

It also purchases crop-yield insurance from one or more other insurers or uses reinsurance to share the potential production risk. ｜ 保险公司还可以向其他一家或者多家保险公司购买农作物产量保险，或者通过再保险共同承担潜在的产量风险。

📌 **Income risk consists of both price risk and output risk.** ｜ 📌 **收入风险同时包括价格风险和产量风险。**

------

###### Two-Way “Insurance + Futures” Model ｜ 双向“保险＋期货”模式

This model applies when farmers and agricultural enterprises sign agricultural-product purchase contracts. ｜ 双向“保险＋期货”模式适用于农户与农业企业签订农产品收购合同的情形。

Farmers normally hope that market prices will rise, while agricultural enterprises normally hope that purchasing prices will fall. ｜ 农户通常希望市场价格上涨，而采购农产品的农业企业通常希望价格下降。

**Farmer’s arrangement** ｜ **农户方的操作**

The farmer purchases price insurance from an insurance company. ｜ 农户向保险公司购买一份价格保险。

According to the supplied material, if the market price at harvest is higher than the contract price, the insurance company compensates the farmer according to an agreed proportion of the price increase. ｜ 根据原材料，如果收获时农产品市场价格高于合同价格，保险公司按照价格上涨幅度的一定比例向农户赔付。

To avoid excessive liability when prices rise sharply, the insurance company normally establishes a maximum compensation amount. ｜ 为了避免农产品价格大幅上涨造成过高赔付，保险公司通常会设定最高赔付金额。

**Agricultural enterprise’s arrangement** ｜ **农业企业方的操作**

The agricultural enterprise also purchases price insurance from the insurance company. ｜ 农业企业也向保险公司购买一份价格保险。

If the market price at harvest is lower than the contract price agreed with the farmer, the insurance company compensates the enterprise according to an agreed proportion of the price decline. ｜ 如果收获时农产品市场价格低于企业与农户约定的合同价格，保险公司按照价格下跌幅度的一定比例向农业企业赔付。

**Risks faced by the insurance company** ｜ **保险公司面临的风险**

The insurance company faces the risk of rising agricultural prices arising from the farmer’s policy. ｜ 保险公司面临来自农户一方的农产品价格上涨风险。

It also faces the risk of falling agricultural prices arising from the agricultural enterprise’s policy. ｜ 保险公司还面临来自农业企业一方的农产品价格下跌风险。

**Insurance company’s operation** ｜ **保险公司的操作**

The insurance company purchases an over-the-counter combination option from the futures company to manage the two-way price risk. ｜ 保险公司向期货公司购买场外组合期权，以规避双向价格风险。

**Futures company’s operation** ｜ **期货公司的操作**

The futures company uses option-replication strategies in the OTC options market or futures market to hedge the resulting risk exposure. ｜ 期货公司在场外期权市场或者期货市场采用复制期权策略，实现风险对冲。

📌 **The two-way model provides protection to both the farmer and the purchasing enterprise.** ｜ 📌 **双向模式同时为农户和采购企业提供价格保障。**

------

#### Chapter Summary ｜ 本章总结

🛡️ **Hedging transfers price risks arising from production and business activities.** ｜ 🛡️ **套期保值用于转移生产经营活动中的价格风险。**

📉 **Short hedging protects against falling prices, while long hedging protects against rising prices.** ｜ 📉 **卖出套期保值防范价格下跌，买入套期保值防范价格上涨。**

⚖️ **Basis equals the spot price minus the futures price, and basis changes affect the final hedging result.** ｜ ⚖️ **基差等于现货价格减去期货价格，基差变化会影响最终套期保值效果。**

🧠 **A strengthening basis benefits short hedging, while a weakening basis benefits long hedging.** ｜ 🧠 **基差走强有利于卖出套期保值，基差走弱有利于买入套期保值。**

🤝 **Cooperative hedging provides professional support to enterprises that cannot hedge independently.** ｜ 🤝 **合作套保为难以独立开展套期保值的企业提供专业支持。**

📦 **Basis trading combines futures-based pricing with spot transactions.** ｜ 📦 **基差贸易将期货定价与现货交易结合起来。**

🌾 **“Insurance + futures” transfers agricultural risks through insurance, OTC options and futures hedging.** ｜ 🌾 **“保险＋期货”通过保险、场外期权和期货对冲逐层转移农业风险。**

------

# Chapter 5: Speculation and Arbitrage ｜ 第五章 投机与套利

This chapter accounts for approximately 10% of the examination score. ｜ 本章分值占比约为10%。

## Section 1: Futures Speculation ｜ 第一节 期货投机交易

## Section 2: Futures Arbitrage ｜ 第二节 期货套利交易

## Section 3: Basic Futures Arbitrage Strategies ｜ 第三节 期货套利的基本策略

## Section 4: Futures Spread Orders ｜ 第四节 期货价差套利指令

------

## Section 1: Futures Speculation ｜ 第一节 期货投机交易

#### I. Definition of Futures Speculation ｜ 一、期货投机的概念

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

Futures speculation refers to a trading activity in which traders predict future changes in futures contract prices and conduct transactions for the purpose of earning profits from price differences. ｜ 期货投机是指交易者通过预测期货合约未来价格的变化，在期货市场进行买卖，以获取价差收益为目的的交易行为。

A trader buys futures contracts when expecting prices to rise and sells futures contracts when expecting prices to fall. ｜ 交易者预期价格上涨时买入期货合约，预期价格下跌时卖出期货合约。

##### (II) Difference Between Speculation and Hedging ｜ （二）期货投机与套期保值的区别

The original comparison table is seriously damaged by OCR, so only the information that can be confirmed from the supplied material is retained. ｜ 原文中的对比表受到严重的OCR损坏，因此这里只保留能够从材料中确认的内容。

| Item              | Futures Speculation                          | Hedging                                                |
| ----------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |
| Purpose           | Earn profits from price changes              | Manage price risks                                     |
| Risk              | Actively assumes price risk                  | Transfers or offsets price risk                        |
| Spot position     | Does not necessarily require a spot position | Usually based on an existing or expected spot exposure |
| Futures direction | Based on price predictions                   | Opposite to the spot risk                              |

| 项目     | 期货投机               | 套期保值                         |
| -------- | ---------------------- | -------------------------------- |
| 目的     | 利用价格变化获取收益   | 管理价格风险                     |
| 风险     | 主动承担价格风险       | 转移或者抵销价格风险             |
| 现货头寸 | 不一定具有对应现货头寸 | 通常以已有或预期的现货风险为基础 |
| 期货方向 | 根据价格预测确定       | 与现货风险方向相反               |

------

#### II. Types of Futures Speculators ｜ 二、期货投机者的类型

##### (I) Classification by Trading Entity ｜ （一）按照交易主体分类

Institutional speculators are institutional investors that conduct speculative futures trading. ｜ 机构投机者是指以机构身份开展期货投机交易的投资者。

Individual speculators are natural persons who use their own funds to conduct speculative futures trading. ｜ 个人投机者是指以自然人身份，使用自有资金开展期货投机交易的投资者。

##### (II) Classification by Position Direction ｜ （二）按照持仓方向分类

A long speculator expects futures prices to rise and therefore buys futures contracts. ｜ 多头投机者预期期货价格上涨，因此买入期货合约。

A short speculator expects futures prices to fall and therefore sells futures contracts. ｜ 空头投机者预期期货价格下跌，因此卖出期货合约。

📈 **Expected price increase → long speculation.** ｜ 📈 **预期价格上涨 → 多头投机。**

📉 **Expected price decrease → short speculation.** ｜ 📉 **预期价格下跌 → 空头投机。**

------

#### III. Functions of Futures Speculation ｜ 三、期货投机的作用

##### Assuming Price Risk ｜ 承担价格风险

Speculators assume the price risks transferred by hedgers. ｜ 投机者承担套期保值者转移出来的价格风险。

Without speculators willing to take opposite positions, hedgers may find it difficult to complete their transactions. ｜ 如果没有投机者愿意建立相反方向的头寸，套期保值者可能难以完成交易。

##### Promoting Price Discovery ｜ 促进价格发现

Speculators analyse supply, demand and other market information and express their expectations through futures trading. ｜ 投机者分析供求关系和其他市场信息，并通过期货交易表达对未来价格的判断。

Their trading activities help incorporate market information into futures prices and promote price discovery. ｜ 投机者的交易有助于将市场信息反映在期货价格中，从而促进价格发现。

##### Moderating Price Fluctuations ｜ 缓解价格波动

Rational and moderate speculation can increase the number of counterparties and reduce short-term imbalances between buy and sell orders. ｜ 理性且适度的投机可以增加交易对手数量，缓解短期买卖力量失衡。

This function requires speculators to trade rationally and speculation to remain at an appropriate level. ｜ 发挥这一作用的前提是投机者进行理性操作，并且市场中的投机程度保持适度。

Excessive or irrational speculation may instead increase price volatility. ｜ 过度或者非理性投机反而可能加剧价格波动。

##### Improving Market Liquidity ｜ 提高市场流动性

Speculators frequently participate in buying and selling, increasing trading volume and providing counterparties for other market participants. ｜ 投机者频繁参与市场买卖，可以增加成交量，并为其他市场参与者提供交易对手。

This makes it easier for traders to establish or close positions. ｜ 这使交易者能够更加方便地建立或者平掉期货头寸。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

📊 **Futures speculation seeks profits by predicting changes in futures prices.** ｜ 📊 **期货投机通过预测期货价格变化获取价差收益。**

📈 **Long speculators expect prices to rise, while short speculators expect prices to fall.** ｜ 📈 **多头投机者预期价格上涨，空头投机者预期价格下跌。**

🛡️ **Speculators assume the price risks transferred by hedgers.** ｜ 🛡️ **投机者承担套期保值者转移出来的价格风险。**

🔍 **Speculation helps market information become reflected in futures prices.** ｜ 🔍 **投机有助于市场信息反映到期货价格中。**

💧 **Appropriate speculation improves liquidity and may moderate price fluctuations.** ｜ 💧 **适度投机可以提高市场流动性，并可能缓解价格波动。**

⚠️ **Excessive or irrational speculation may increase market volatility.** ｜ ⚠️ **过度或者非理性投机可能加剧市场价格波动。**

## Section 4: Business Expansion of Hedging ｜ 第四节 套期保值的业务拓展

#### I. Cooperative Hedging ｜ 一、合作套保

##### (I) Background ｜ （一）背景

In practice, some enterprises, especially small and medium-sized enterprises, have hedging needs but find it difficult to use the futures market independently because of limited professional personnel and operational capabilities. ｜ 现实中，一些企业，尤其是中小型企业，虽然具有套期保值需求，但由于缺乏专业人才、操作能力不足等限制，难以独立利用期货市场进行套期保值。

##### (II) Operating Method ｜ （二）操作方法

The risk management subsidiary of a futures company provides services according to the enterprise’s actual production and operational needs. It participates in different stages of the enterprise’s operations, helps identify and accurately measure risk exposures, and provides support throughout the process, from risk-management planning and hedging-strategy design to the implementation of specific hedging transactions. ｜ 期货公司的风险管理子公司根据企业实际生产经营需求，深入企业生产经营的各个环节，帮助企业提高风险识别能力和风险敞口的准确计量能力，并从风险管理预案策划、套期保值方案制定到具体交易实施，全程参与企业的风险管理。

##### (III) Cooperative Hedging Models ｜ （三）合作套保模式

**Guided operation** ｜ **指导操作模式**

Small and medium-sized enterprises conduct spot-related procurement, sales, logistics and warehousing activities under the guidance of the futures company. The futures company is responsible for designing hedging strategies and providing risk-management services. ｜ 中小企业客户在期货公司的指导下开展现货采购、销售、物流和仓储等业务，期货公司负责制定套期保值策略并提供风险管理服务。

**Outsourced hedging** ｜ **套保外包模式**

Small and medium-sized enterprises outsource hedging operations to the risk management subsidiary of a futures company. According to the specific hedging objectives proposed by the client, the risk management company uses futures or spot instruments to help the enterprise achieve its risk-management objectives. ｜ 中小企业客户将套期保值操作外包给期货公司的风险管理子公司。风险管理公司根据客户提出的具体保值目标，利用期货或者现货工具开展风险管理，帮助企业完成保值目标。

##### (IV) Prohibited Conduct in Managing Cooperative Hedging Funds ｜ （四）合作套保资金管理和使用中的禁止行为

A risk management company and its client may not conduct cooperative hedging business with financial institutions, asset-management products, private fund managers and their products, or natural persons. ｜ 风险管理公司与客户不得同金融机构、资产管理产品、私募基金管理人及其产品或者自然人签订合同或者开展合作套保业务。

The risk management company may not provide cooperative hedging funds to the client. ｜ 风险管理公司不得为客户提供合作套保资金。

The risk management company may not misappropriate the client’s cooperative hedging funds. ｜ 风险管理公司不得挪用客户的合作套保资金。

Other conduct prohibited by the China Securities Regulatory Commission or the relevant industry association is also not permitted. ｜ 中国证监会及相关行业协会禁止的其他行为同样不得实施。

------

#### II. Basis Trading ｜ 二、基差贸易

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

Basis trading conducted by risk management companies refers to spot transactions in which the company provides quotations using a fixed price, price fixing, average pricing or other agreed methods and trades physical commodities directly with clients. ｜ 风险管理公司开展的基差贸易，是指风险管理公司采用确定价格、点价、均价等方式向客户提供报价，并与客户直接进行现货交易的业务。

In a typical basis transaction, the spot transaction price is linked to a specified futures price and an agreed basis. ｜ 典型的基差贸易中，现货交易价格与指定期货价格及双方约定的基差相联系。
$$
\text{Spot transaction price}
=
\text{Futures benchmark price}
\pm
\text{Agreed basis}
$$

##### (II) Standard Warehouse Receipt Basis-Trading Case ｜ （二）标准仓单基差贸易案例

**Background** ｜ **背景**

A lead smelter had sold December lead futures contracts and planned to transport a batch of lead ingots to a delivery warehouse in early November for physical delivery. ｜ 某铅冶炼厂已经卖出12月份交割的铅期货合约，计划在11月初将一批铅锭运送到交割仓库，准备进行实物交割。

Because of funding needs, the smelter wanted to sell the goods in advance. However, it believed that the current market price of lead ingots was too low. ｜ 后来由于资金需求，该冶炼厂希望提前出售这批货物，但认为当时的铅锭市场价格偏低。

**Operating method** ｜ **操作方法**

The smelter signed a basis sale contract with a risk management company and sold the goods through the pricing formula **PB1812 − 100**. ｜ 该冶炼厂与风险管理公司签订基差销售合同，并按照 **PB1812－100** 的基差点价方式销售该批货物。

This means that the final selling price was determined using the PB1812 lead futures price minus RMB 100 per tonne. ｜ 这表示最终销售价格以PB1812铅期货价格减去100元/吨确定。

The risk management company used the average price of No. 1 lead published by the relevant non-ferrous metals information platform as the provisional settlement price and paid 80% of the provisional payment. ｜ 风险管理公司以相关有色金属信息平台公布的1号铅平均价格作为临时结算价，先支付80%的临时货款。

The smelter was given a one-week pricing period, while the remaining 20% of the provisional payment served as pricing margin. ｜ 风险管理公司给予冶炼厂一周的点价期限，剩余20%的临时货款作为冶炼厂的点价保证金。

After the smelter completed the price fixing, the risk management company calculated the final payment and paid the remaining amount. ｜ 冶炼厂完成点价后，风险管理公司按照实际成交价格进行结算，并支付剩余货款。

**Results** ｜ **结果**

The risk management company purchased the lead ingots through basis pricing and used them for physical delivery, obtaining income from the standard warehouse receipt delivery business. ｜ 风险管理公司通过基差点价方式采购铅锭，并将其用于期货实物交割，从标准仓单卖出交割业务中获得收益。

The smelter quickly obtained funds while retaining control over the final selling price. ｜ 铅冶炼厂既快速回笼了资金，又保留了对最终销售价格的控制权。

📌 **The enterprise receives most of the payment first and determines the final price later through futures-based price fixing.** ｜ 📌 **企业先取得大部分货款，再通过期货点价确定最终销售价格。**

------

#### III. “Insurance + Futures” ｜ 三、“保险＋期货”

##### (I) Basic Structure ｜ （一）模式逻辑

The “insurance + futures” model transfers agricultural risks through several stages. ｜ “保险＋期货”模式通过多个环节逐层转移农业经营风险。
$$
\text{Farmer or enterprise}
\rightarrow
\text{Insurance company}
\rightarrow
\text{Futures company}
\rightarrow
\text{Futures market}
$$
The farmer or enterprise first transfers risk to the insurance company by purchasing insurance. ｜ 农户或者企业首先通过购买保险，将风险转移给保险公司。

The insurance company then purchases an over-the-counter option from the futures company and transfers the price risk again. ｜ 保险公司再向期货公司购买场外期权，将相关价格风险进行第二次转移。

The futures company finally hedges the risk through futures or options transactions. ｜ 期货公司最终通过期货或者期权交易完成风险对冲。

------

##### (II) Classification by the Type of Risk Protected ｜ （二）按照规避风险类型分类

###### Price-Protection Model ｜ 价格保护型模式

The objective of the price-protection model is to protect farmers against declines in agricultural-product prices. ｜ 价格保护型模式的目标是帮助农户规避农产品价格下跌风险。

The farmer purchases insurance from an insurance company. The policy specifies a minimum protected price for the agricultural product. ｜ 农户向保险公司购买农产品价格保险，保险合同约定一个最低保障价格。

If the market price during the harvest season falls below the protected price, the insurance company compensates the farmer for the price difference. ｜ 如果收获季节的市场价格低于最低保障价格，保险公司按照约定向农户赔付价格差额。

The insurance company pays an option premium and purchases an over-the-counter put option from the futures company, transferring the risk of falling prices to the futures company. ｜ 保险公司支付期权费，向期货公司买入场外看跌期权，从而将价格下跌风险转移给期货公司。

The futures company may replicate the put option in the exchange-traded market or trade an equivalent quantity of futures contracts in the opposite direction to hedge the risk. ｜ 期货公司可以在场内复制相应的看跌期权，或者通过买卖数量相当、风险方向相反的期货合约进行对冲。

📌 **Price falls → insurer compensates the farmer → put option compensates the insurer.** ｜ 📌 **价格下跌 → 保险公司赔付农户 → 看跌期权补偿保险公司。**

------

###### Income-Protection Model ｜ 收入保障型模式

The objective of the income-protection model is to provide farmers with relatively stable agricultural sales revenue. ｜ 收入保障型模式的目标是保障农户获得相对稳定的农产品销售收入。

The farmer pays an insurance premium and obtains an income insurance policy. ｜ 农户支付保险费并取得一份收入保险。

If the actual output value during the harvest season is lower than the target output value specified in the policy, the insurance company compensates the farmer for the income difference. ｜ 如果收获季节的实际产值低于保险合同约定的目标产值，保险公司向农户赔付相应的收入差额。

Agricultural revenue is jointly determined by price and output. ｜ 农产品收入由价格和产量共同决定。
$$
\text{Revenue}
=
\text{Price}
\times
\text{Output}
$$
The insurance company transfers price risk to the futures company in the same way as under the price-protection model. ｜ 保险公司按照价格保护型模式，将价格风险转移给期货公司。

It also purchases crop-yield insurance from one or more other insurers or uses reinsurance to share the potential production risk. ｜ 保险公司还可以向其他一家或者多家保险公司购买农作物产量保险，或者通过再保险共同承担潜在的产量风险。

📌 **Income risk consists of both price risk and output risk.** ｜ 📌 **收入风险同时包括价格风险和产量风险。**

------

###### Two-Way “Insurance + Futures” Model ｜ 双向“保险＋期货”模式

This model applies when farmers and agricultural enterprises sign agricultural-product purchase contracts. ｜ 双向“保险＋期货”模式适用于农户与农业企业签订农产品收购合同的情形。

Farmers normally hope that market prices will rise, while agricultural enterprises normally hope that purchasing prices will fall. ｜ 农户通常希望市场价格上涨，而采购农产品的农业企业通常希望价格下降。

**Farmer’s arrangement** ｜ **农户方的操作**

The farmer purchases price insurance from an insurance company. ｜ 农户向保险公司购买一份价格保险。

According to the supplied material, if the market price at harvest is higher than the contract price, the insurance company compensates the farmer according to an agreed proportion of the price increase. ｜ 根据原材料，如果收获时农产品市场价格高于合同价格，保险公司按照价格上涨幅度的一定比例向农户赔付。

To avoid excessive liability when prices rise sharply, the insurance company normally establishes a maximum compensation amount. ｜ 为了避免农产品价格大幅上涨造成过高赔付，保险公司通常会设定最高赔付金额。

**Agricultural enterprise’s arrangement** ｜ **农业企业方的操作**

The agricultural enterprise also purchases price insurance from the insurance company. ｜ 农业企业也向保险公司购买一份价格保险。

If the market price at harvest is lower than the contract price agreed with the farmer, the insurance company compensates the enterprise according to an agreed proportion of the price decline. ｜ 如果收获时农产品市场价格低于企业与农户约定的合同价格，保险公司按照价格下跌幅度的一定比例向农业企业赔付。

**Risks faced by the insurance company** ｜ **保险公司面临的风险**

The insurance company faces the risk of rising agricultural prices arising from the farmer’s policy. ｜ 保险公司面临来自农户一方的农产品价格上涨风险。

It also faces the risk of falling agricultural prices arising from the agricultural enterprise’s policy. ｜ 保险公司还面临来自农业企业一方的农产品价格下跌风险。

**Insurance company’s operation** ｜ **保险公司的操作**

The insurance company purchases an over-the-counter combination option from the futures company to manage the two-way price risk. ｜ 保险公司向期货公司购买场外组合期权，以规避双向价格风险。

**Futures company’s operation** ｜ **期货公司的操作**

The futures company uses option-replication strategies in the OTC options market or futures market to hedge the resulting risk exposure. ｜ 期货公司在场外期权市场或者期货市场采用复制期权策略，实现风险对冲。

📌 **The two-way model provides protection to both the farmer and the purchasing enterprise.** ｜ 📌 **双向模式同时为农户和采购企业提供价格保障。**

------

#### Chapter Summary ｜ 本章总结

🛡️ **Hedging transfers price risks arising from production and business activities.** ｜ 🛡️ **套期保值用于转移生产经营活动中的价格风险。**

📉 **Short hedging protects against falling prices, while long hedging protects against rising prices.** ｜ 📉 **卖出套期保值防范价格下跌，买入套期保值防范价格上涨。**

⚖️ **Basis equals the spot price minus the futures price, and basis changes affect the final hedging result.** ｜ ⚖️ **基差等于现货价格减去期货价格，基差变化会影响最终套期保值效果。**

🧠 **A strengthening basis benefits short hedging, while a weakening basis benefits long hedging.** ｜ 🧠 **基差走强有利于卖出套期保值，基差走弱有利于买入套期保值。**

🤝 **Cooperative hedging provides professional support to enterprises that cannot hedge independently.** ｜ 🤝 **合作套保为难以独立开展套期保值的企业提供专业支持。**

📦 **Basis trading combines futures-based pricing with spot transactions.** ｜ 📦 **基差贸易将期货定价与现货交易结合起来。**

🌾 **“Insurance + futures” transfers agricultural risks through insurance, OTC options and futures hedging.** ｜ 🌾 **“保险＋期货”通过保险、场外期权和期货对冲逐层转移农业风险。**

# Chapter 5: Speculation and Arbitrage ｜ 第五章 投机与套利

------

## Section 1: Futures Speculation ｜ 第一节 期货投机交易

#### I. Definition of Futures Speculation ｜ 一、期货投机的概念

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

Futures speculation refers to a trading activity in which traders predict future changes in futures contract prices and conduct transactions for the purpose of earning profits from price differences. ｜ 期货投机是指交易者通过预测期货合约未来价格的变化，在期货市场进行买卖，以获取价差收益为目的的交易行为。

A trader buys futures contracts when expecting prices to rise and sells futures contracts when expecting prices to fall. ｜ 交易者预期价格上涨时买入期货合约，预期价格下跌时卖出期货合约。

##### (II) Difference Between Speculation and Hedging ｜ （二）期货投机与套期保值的区别

The original comparison table is seriously damaged by OCR, so only the information that can be confirmed from the supplied material is retained. ｜ 原文中的对比表受到严重的OCR损坏，因此这里只保留能够从材料中确认的内容。

| Item              | Futures Speculation                          | Hedging                                                |
| ----------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |
| Purpose           | Earn profits from price changes              | Manage price risks                                     |
| Risk              | Actively assumes price risk                  | Transfers or offsets price risk                        |
| Spot position     | Does not necessarily require a spot position | Usually based on an existing or expected spot exposure |
| Futures direction | Based on price predictions                   | Opposite to the spot risk                              |

| 项目     | 期货投机               | 套期保值                         |
| -------- | ---------------------- | -------------------------------- |
| 目的     | 利用价格变化获取收益   | 管理价格风险                     |
| 风险     | 主动承担价格风险       | 转移或者抵销价格风险             |
| 现货头寸 | 不一定具有对应现货头寸 | 通常以已有或预期的现货风险为基础 |
| 期货方向 | 根据价格预测确定       | 与现货风险方向相反               |

------

#### II. Types of Futures Speculators ｜ 二、期货投机者的类型

##### (I) Classification by Trading Entity ｜ （一）按照交易主体分类

Institutional speculators are institutional investors that conduct speculative futures trading. ｜ 机构投机者是指以机构身份开展期货投机交易的投资者。

Individual speculators are natural persons who use their own funds to conduct speculative futures trading. ｜ 个人投机者是指以自然人身份，使用自有资金开展期货投机交易的投资者。

##### (II) Classification by Position Direction ｜ （二）按照持仓方向分类

A long speculator expects futures prices to rise and therefore buys futures contracts. ｜ 多头投机者预期期货价格上涨，因此买入期货合约。

A short speculator expects futures prices to fall and therefore sells futures contracts. ｜ 空头投机者预期期货价格下跌，因此卖出期货合约。

📈 **Expected price increase → long speculation.** ｜ 📈 **预期价格上涨 → 多头投机。**

📉 **Expected price decrease → short speculation.** ｜ 📉 **预期价格下跌 → 空头投机。**

------

#### III. Functions of Futures Speculation ｜ 三、期货投机的作用

##### Assuming Price Risk ｜ 承担价格风险

Speculators assume the price risks transferred by hedgers. ｜ 投机者承担套期保值者转移出来的价格风险。

Without speculators willing to take opposite positions, hedgers may find it difficult to complete their transactions. ｜ 如果没有投机者愿意建立相反方向的头寸，套期保值者可能难以完成交易。

##### Promoting Price Discovery ｜ 促进价格发现

Speculators analyse supply, demand and other market information and express their expectations through futures trading. ｜ 投机者分析供求关系和其他市场信息，并通过期货交易表达对未来价格的判断。

Their trading activities help incorporate market information into futures prices and promote price discovery. ｜ 投机者的交易有助于将市场信息反映在期货价格中，从而促进价格发现。

##### Moderating Price Fluctuations ｜ 缓解价格波动

Rational and moderate speculation can increase the number of counterparties and reduce short-term imbalances between buy and sell orders. ｜ 理性且适度的投机可以增加交易对手数量，缓解短期买卖力量失衡。

This function requires speculators to trade rationally and speculation to remain at an appropriate level. ｜ 发挥这一作用的前提是投机者进行理性操作，并且市场中的投机程度保持适度。

Excessive or irrational speculation may instead increase price volatility. ｜ 过度或者非理性投机反而可能加剧价格波动。

##### Improving Market Liquidity ｜ 提高市场流动性

Speculators frequently participate in buying and selling, increasing trading volume and providing counterparties for other market participants. ｜ 投机者频繁参与市场买卖，可以增加成交量，并为其他市场参与者提供交易对手。

This makes it easier for traders to establish or close positions. ｜ 这使交易者能够更加方便地建立或者平掉期货头寸。

------

#### IV. Common Operating Methods of Futures Speculation ｜ 四、期货投机交易的常见操作方法

##### (I) Position-Opening Stage ｜ （一）开仓阶段

###### 1. Selecting the Time to Enter the Market ｜ 1. 入市时机选择

Traders first use fundamental analysis to determine whether the price of a particular futures contract is likely to rise or fall. ｜ 交易者首先通过基本面分析，判断某一期货合约的价格未来可能上涨还是下跌。

They then evaluate the potential risks and expected returns before deciding the specific time to enter the market. ｜ 随后，交易者需要权衡潜在风险和获利前景，并确定具体的入市时间。

📌 **Basic process: determine the price direction → evaluate risk and return → choose the entry time.** ｜ 📌 **基本流程：判断价格方向 → 权衡风险收益 → 选择入市时机。**

------

###### 2. Pyramid Position Building ｜ 2. 金字塔式建仓

Pyramid position building means gradually increasing a position when the market continues to move in the expected direction, while reducing the quantity added each time. ｜ 金字塔式建仓是指市场价格继续按照预期方向发展时，逐步增加持仓，同时使每次增仓的数量逐渐减少。

The main principles are: ｜ 主要原则是：

- A position may be increased only when the existing position is already profitable. ｜ 只有在现有持仓已经盈利的情况下，才能继续增仓。
- The quantity added each time should gradually decrease. ｜ 每次增加的持仓数量应当逐渐减少。

📌 **Add only to profitable positions, and make each additional position smaller.** ｜ 📌 **只有盈利后才能增仓，并且每次增仓数量递减。**

###### Pyramid Position-Building Case ｜ 金字塔式建仓案例

A speculator expected the price of the September soybean futures contract to rise and initially bought 7 contracts at RMB 4,310 per tonne. Each contract represented 10 tonnes. ｜ 某投机者预计9月份大豆期货合约价格将上涨，因此以4310元/吨买入7手合约，每手10吨。

When the price rose to RMB 4,350 per tonne, the initial position generated the following floating profit: ｜ 当价格上涨至4350元/吨时，最初7手合约产生的浮动盈利为：
$$
(4350-4310)\times10\times7=2800\text{元}
$$
Because the existing position was profitable, the trader bought another 5 contracts at RMB 4,350 per tonne. ｜ 由于原有持仓已经盈利，投机者又以4350元/吨买入5手合约。

The total position increased to 12 contracts, and the average purchase price became: ｜ 总持仓增加至12手，平均买入价格为：
$$
\frac{4310\times7+4350\times5}{12}
=4326.7\text{元/吨}
$$
When the price rose to RMB 4,385 per tonne, the trader bought another 3 contracts. The total position became 15 contracts, with an average purchase price of RMB 4,338.3 per tonne. ｜ 当价格上涨至4385元/吨时，投机者又买入3手，总持仓增加至15手，平均买入价格为4338.3元/吨。

When the price rose to RMB 4,405 per tonne, the trader bought another 2 contracts. The total position became 17 contracts, with an average purchase price of RMB 4,346.2 per tonne. ｜ 当价格上涨至4405元/吨时，投机者又买入2手，总持仓增加至17手，平均买入价格为4346.2元/吨。

When the price rose to RMB 4,425 per tonne, the trader bought another contract. The total position became 18 contracts, with an average purchase price of RMB 4,350.6 per tonne. ｜ 当价格上涨至4425元/吨时，投机者再买入1手，总持仓增加至18手，平均买入价格为4350.6元/吨。

| Purchase price | Additional contracts | Total position | Average price |
| -------------- | -------------------- | -------------- | ------------- |
| 4,310          | 7                    | 7              | 4,310.0       |
| 4,350          | 5                    | 12             | 4,326.7       |
| 4,385          | 3                    | 15             | 4,338.3       |
| 4,405          | 2                    | 17             | 4,346.2       |
| 4,425          | 1                    | 18             | 4,350.6       |

| 买入价格 | 本次增仓 | 总持仓 | 平均买入价格 |
| -------- | -------- | ------ | ------------ |
| 4310     | 7手      | 7手    | 4310.0       |
| 4350     | 5手      | 12手   | 4326.7       |
| 4385     | 3手      | 15手   | 4338.3       |
| 4405     | 2手      | 17手   | 4346.2       |
| 4425     | 1手      | 18手   | 4350.6       |

The quantities added form a decreasing sequence: ｜ 每次增加的持仓数量逐渐递减：
$$
7\rightarrow5\rightarrow3\rightarrow2\rightarrow1
$$
This method allows the trader to increase exposure when the market moves favourably without causing the average purchase price to rise too quickly. ｜ 这种方法可以使投机者在行情有利时增加持仓，同时避免平均买入成本上升过快。



![image-20260728201159663](/Users/tangjun/Library/Application Support/typora-user-images/image-20260728201159663.png)

------

###### 3. Selecting the Contract Month ｜ 3. 合约交割月份的选择

Contract liquidity should be considered when selecting a delivery month. ｜ 选择期货合约月份时，应当考虑合约流动性。

Traders should generally select actively traded contracts and avoid inactive contracts. ｜ 交易者通常应当选择交易活跃的合约，并避开成交不活跃的合约。

The relationship between nearby and distant futures prices should also be considered. ｜ 同时，还需要考虑近月合约与远月合约之间的价格关系。

In a normal market, long speculators should generally buy nearby contracts, while short speculators should generally sell distant contracts. ｜ 在正向市场中，多头投机者通常应买入近月合约，空头投机者通常应卖出远月合约。

In an inverted market, long speculators should generally buy distant contracts, while short speculators should generally sell nearby contracts. ｜ 在反向市场中，多头投机者通常宜买入远月合约，空头投机者通常宜卖出近月合约。

| Market structure | Long speculator       | Short speculator       |
| ---------------- | --------------------- | ---------------------- |
| Normal market    | Buy nearby contracts  | Sell distant contracts |
| Inverted market  | Buy distant contracts | Sell nearby contracts  |

| 市场结构 | 多头投机者   | 空头投机者   |
| -------- | ------------ | ------------ |
| 正向市场 | 买入近月合约 | 卖出远月合约 |
| 反向市场 | 买入远月合约 | 卖出近月合约 |

------

##### (II) Position-Closing Stage ｜ （二）平仓阶段

###### 1. Application of Stop Orders ｜ 1. 止损指令的应用

After establishing a position, a speculator should closely monitor market movements and close the position at an appropriate time. ｜ 投机者建仓后，应当密切关注市场行情变化，并在适当时机进行平仓。

A stop order is an important tool for limiting losses and protecting accumulated profits. ｜ 止损指令是实现“限制损失、保护盈利”的重要工具。

The stop price should not be set too close to the current market price, because ordinary short-term fluctuations may trigger the order prematurely. ｜ 止损价格不能设置得离当前市场价格过近，否则正常的短期波动就可能过早触发止损。

The stop price should not be set too far from the current market price either, because losses may become too large before the order is triggered. ｜ 止损价格也不能距离当前市场价格过远，否则在止损指令触发前，亏损可能已经过大。

📌 **Too close → easily triggered by ordinary fluctuations.** ｜ 📌 **止损价过近 → 容易被正常波动触发。**

📌 **Too far → unable to control losses promptly.** ｜ 📌 **止损价过远 → 无法及时控制亏损。**

------

###### 2. Stop-Order Cases ｜ 2. 止损案例

###### Case 1: Limiting Potential Losses ｜ 案例一：限制潜在亏损

A speculator decided to trade wheat futures and set the maximum acceptable loss at RMB 50 per tonne. ｜ 某投机者决定进行小麦期货交易，并将最大可接受损失设定为50元/吨。

After buying 20 contracts at RMB 2,550 per tonne, the trader placed a sell stop order at RMB 2,500 per tonne. ｜ 投机者以2550元/吨买入20手合约后，又下达了一份卖出止损指令，止损价格为2500元/吨。

If the market price fell to the trigger price, the long position would be closed. ｜ 如果市场价格下跌至止损触发价格，多头持仓将被平仓。

The loss would therefore be limited to approximately RMB 50 per tonne, excluding possible price slippage and transaction costs. ｜ 因此，在不考虑成交滑点和交易费用的情况下，损失可以被控制在约50元/吨。
$$
2550-2500=50\text{元/吨}
$$

------

###### Case 2: Protecting Existing Profits ｜ 案例二：保护已有盈利

A speculator bought 20 wheat futures contracts at RMB 2,550 per tonne. The price subsequently rose to RMB 2,610 per tonne. ｜ 某投机者以2550元/吨买入20手小麦期货合约，随后市场价格上涨至2610元/吨。

The trader expected the price to continue rising and therefore decided to retain the position. ｜ 由于预期价格仍将上涨，投机者决定继续持有该头寸。

To prevent a market decline from eliminating the existing profit, the trader placed a sell stop order at RMB 2,590 per tonne. ｜ 为了防止市场价格下跌侵蚀已有盈利，投机者将卖出止损价格设定为2590元/吨。

If the market price fell to RMB 2,590 per tonne, the position would be closed, preserving a profit of approximately RMB 40 per tonne. ｜ 如果市场价格下跌至2590元/吨，持仓将被平掉，并保留约40元/吨的盈利。
$$
2590-2550=40\text{元/吨}
$$
If the price continued to rise without reaching the stop price, the position could continue to generate additional profits. ｜ 如果价格继续上涨且未触及止损价格，该头寸仍可以继续扩大盈利。

📌 **A stop order can both limit losses and protect profits already obtained.** ｜ 📌 **止损指令既可以限制亏损，也可以保护已经获得的盈利。**

------

##### (III) Capital and Risk Management ｜ （三）资金和风险管理

###### Definition ｜ 概念

Capital management refers to how a trader allocates and uses trading funds. ｜ 资金管理是指交易者对交易资金进行配置和运用。

Its purpose is to control overall risk and prevent one or several unsuccessful transactions from causing excessive losses to the trading account. ｜ 其目的在于控制总体风险，防止一次或者少数几次交易失败对账户造成过大损失。

###### Main Components ｜ 主要内容

Capital and risk management mainly includes: ｜ 资金和风险管理主要包括：

- Designing the investment portfolio ｜ 设计投资组合
- Allocating capital among different markets ｜ 在不同市场之间分配投资资金
- Setting stop-loss points ｜ 设计止损点
- Balancing potential returns against risks ｜ 权衡报偿与风险的比例
- Determining appropriate measures after successful or unsuccessful trading periods ｜ 决定在连续盈利或者遭遇挫折后采取何种措施
- Choosing between conservative and aggressive trading methods ｜ 选择保守稳健或者积极大胆的交易方式

------

###### General Capital-Management Guidelines ｜ 一般性的资金管理要领

The total amount invested in futures trading should generally be limited to between one-third and one-half of the trader’s total capital. ｜ 投入期货交易的资金通常应当控制在全部资本的三分之一至二分之一以内。

Depending on the amount of capital, the maximum capital allocated to a single product should generally be limited to 10%–20% of total capital. ｜ 根据资金规模不同，投入单个品种的最大资金通常应控制在总资本的10%～20%。

The maximum total loss in a single market should generally be limited to approximately 5% of total capital. ｜ 单个市场中的最大总亏损金额通常宜控制在总资本的5%以内。

The total margin invested in any one group of related markets should generally be limited to 20%–30% of total capital. ｜ 投入任何一个相关市场群的保证金总额，通常宜控制在总资本的20%～30%。

###### Capital-Management Example ｜ 资金管理案例

Suppose a speculator has total capital of RMB 100,000 and decides to allocate 10% of the capital to one transaction. ｜ 假设某投机者拥有总资本10万元，并决定将总资本的10%投入某一笔交易。

The amount available for this transaction is: ｜ 该笔交易可以投入的资金为：
$$
100000\times10\%=10000\text{元}
$$
If the margin requirement for one corn futures contract is approximately RMB 2,000, the maximum number of contracts is: ｜ 如果每手玉米期货合约需要约2000元保证金，则可以持有的合约数量为：
$$
\frac{10000}{2000}=5\text{手}
$$
The trader may therefore hold up to approximately 5 corn futures contracts under this allocation rule. ｜ 因此，按照这一资金分配规则，该投机者最多可以持有约5手玉米期货合约。

------

##### (IV) Diversified and Concentrated Investment ｜ （四）分散投资与集中投资

###### Vertical Diversification ｜ 纵向投资分散化

Vertical diversification means selecting a small number of familiar products and investing funds in different stages rather than entering the entire position at one time. ｜ 纵向投资分散化是指选择少数几个熟悉的品种，并在不同阶段分批投入资金，而不是一次性建立全部持仓。

Pyramid position building is an example of investing funds in stages. ｜ 金字塔式建仓就是一种分阶段投入资金的方式。

###### Horizontal Diversification ｜ 横向投资多元化

The source text is incomplete, but its confirmed core meaning is to allocate funds among different products or markets in order to diversify investment risk. ｜ 原文在此处存在缺失，但可以确认的核心含义是：将资金分配到不同品种或者市场，以达到分散投资风险的目的。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🎯 **Market entry requires judging the direction, evaluating risk and return, and selecting the appropriate timing.** ｜ 🎯 **入市前需要判断价格方向、权衡风险收益并选择合适时机。**

🔺 **Pyramid position building adds only to profitable positions, with each additional position becoming smaller.** ｜ 🔺 **金字塔式建仓只在已有持仓盈利时增仓，并且每次增仓数量逐渐减少。**

📅 **Contract-month selection should consider liquidity and the relationship between nearby and distant prices.** ｜ 📅 **选择合约月份时，应考虑流动性以及近月、远月合约的价格关系。**

🛑 **Stop orders can limit losses and protect existing profits.** ｜ 🛑 **止损指令既可以限制亏损，也可以保护已有盈利。**

💰 **Capital management controls the allocation of funds, position size and maximum acceptable losses.** ｜ 💰 **资金管理用于控制资金分配、持仓规模和最大可接受损失。**

⚖️ **Diversification reduces the risk of concentrating all capital in one product, market or entry point.** ｜ ⚖️ **分散投资可以减少资金过度集中于单一品种、市场或者入场时点的风险。**





### Examination Topic: Calendar Spread Arbitrage ｜ 考点：跨期套利

#### I. Definition and Types of Calendar Spread Arbitrage ｜ 一、跨期套利的概念与种类

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

Calendar spread arbitrage refers to simultaneously buying and selling futures contracts on the same underlying product but with different delivery months in the same market or exchange. The positions are later closed simultaneously when the spread moves in a favourable direction. ｜ 跨期套利是指在同一市场或者交易所，同时买入和卖出同一期货品种、不同交割月份的期货合约，并在价差向有利方向变化时，同时将两个合约对冲平仓以获取收益。

##### (II) Types ｜ （二）分类

Calendar spread arbitrage mainly includes bull spread arbitrage, bear spread arbitrage and butterfly spread arbitrage. ｜ 跨期套利主要包括牛市套利、熊市套利和蝶式套利。

------

#### II. Bull Spread Arbitrage ｜ 二、牛市套利

##### (I) Operating Conditions ｜ （一）操作前提

Bull spread arbitrage is generally used when market supply is insufficient, demand is strong, or the trader expects nearby contracts to perform more strongly than distant contracts. ｜ 牛市套利通常适用于市场供给不足、需求旺盛，或者交易者预计近月合约的价格表现将强于远月合约的情形。

##### (II) Operating Method ｜ （二）操作方式

A bull spread is established by buying a nearby-month contract and simultaneously selling a distant-month contract. ｜ 牛市套利的操作方式是买入较近月份合约，同时卖出较远月份合约。

📌 **Bull spread: buy nearby and sell distant.** ｜ 📌 **牛市套利：买近月、卖远月。**

------

##### (III) Cases ｜ （三）案例

###### Case 1: Prices Rise and the Spread Contracts ｜ 案例一：价格上涨、价差缩小

On 26 October, the price of the May cotton futures contract for the following year was RMB 12,075 per tonne, while the September contract was RMB 12,725 per tonne. ｜ 10月26日，次年5月份棉花期货合约价格为12075元/吨，次年9月份合约价格为12725元/吨。

The opening spread was: ｜ 建仓时的价差为：
$$
12725-12075=650\text{元/吨}
$$
The trader expected cotton prices to rise and the spread between the May and September contracts to contract. The trader therefore bought 50 May contracts and simultaneously sold 50 September contracts. ｜ 交易者预计棉花价格将上涨，并且5月份与9月份合约之间的价差可能缩小，因此买入50手5月份合约，同时卖出50手9月份合约。

On 26 December, the May and September prices increased to RMB 12,555 and RMB 13,060 per tonne respectively. ｜ 12月26日，5月份和9月份合约价格分别上涨至12555元/吨和13060元/吨。

The closing spread was: ｜ 平仓时的价差为：
$$
13060-12555=505\text{元/吨}
$$
The spread contracted by: ｜ 价差缩小了：
$$
650-505=145\text{元/吨}
$$

| Contract  | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss |
| --------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------- |
| May       | Buy at 12,075    | Sell at 12,555   | +480           |
| September | Sell at 12,725   | Buy at 13,060    | −335           |

| 合约  | 建仓      | 平仓      | 盈亏 |
| ----- | --------- | --------- | ---- |
| 5月份 | 买入12075 | 卖出12555 | +480 |
| 9月份 | 卖出12725 | 买入13060 | −335 |

Each contract represents 5 tonnes, and the trader held 50 contracts. ｜ 每手合约代表5吨，交易者共持有50手。
$$
(480-335)\times5\times50
=
36250\text{元}
$$
The total arbitrage profit was RMB 36,250. ｜ 该套利交易总盈利为36250元。

------

###### Case 2: Prices Fall but the Spread Still Contracts ｜ 案例二：价格下跌、价差仍然缩小

Assume that cotton prices did not rise as expected. On 26 December, the May and September contract prices instead fell to RMB 11,985 and RMB 12,480 per tonne. ｜ 假设棉花价格并未按照预期上涨。到12月26日，5月份和9月份合约价格反而分别下跌至11985元/吨和12480元/吨。

The closing spread was: ｜ 平仓时的价差为：
$$
12480-11985=495\text{元/吨}
$$
The spread contracted by: ｜ 价差缩小了：
$$
650-495=155\text{元/吨}
$$

| Contract  | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss |
| --------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------- |
| May       | Buy at 12,075    | Sell at 11,985   | −90            |
| September | Sell at 12,725   | Buy at 12,480    | +245           |

| 合约  | 建仓      | 平仓      | 盈亏 |
| ----- | --------- | --------- | ---- |
| 5月份 | 买入12075 | 卖出11985 | −90  |
| 9月份 | 卖出12725 | 买入12480 | +245 |

$$
(-90+245)\times5\times50
=
38750\text{元}
$$

The total arbitrage profit was RMB 38,750. ｜ 该套利交易总盈利为38750元。

------

##### (IV) Case Conclusion ｜ （四）案例结论

In a normal market, a bull spread generates a profit as long as the spread between the nearby and distant contracts contracts. The direction of the overall futures price movement is not decisive. ｜ 在正向市场中进行牛市套利，只要近月与远月合约之间的价差缩小，交易者就可以盈利，期货价格整体上涨还是下跌并不是决定性因素。

📌 **The key is the change in the spread, not the absolute direction of prices.** ｜ 📌 **套利盈亏的关键是价差变化，而不是价格整体涨跌。**

##### (V) Special Note ｜ （五）特别注意

In a normal market, the distant contract is generally priced higher than the nearby contract. Buying the nearby lower-priced contract and selling the distant higher-priced contract is essentially sell spread arbitrage. ｜ 在正向市场中，远月合约价格通常高于近月合约。买入低价的近月合约，同时卖出高价的远月合约，本质上属于卖出套利。

Sell spread arbitrage earns a profit when the spread contracts. ｜ 卖出套利在价差缩小时获利。

The potential loss of a bull spread in a normal market is relatively limited because expansion of the spread is constrained by carrying costs. If the spread becomes excessively large, arbitrage activity may push it back toward a reasonable level. ｜ 正向市场中，牛市套利的潜在损失相对有限，因为价差扩大的幅度会受到持仓费的制约。如果价差过度扩大，套利交易会促使其回到合理范围。

However, the spread may contract substantially. The nearby contract may rise much more sharply than the distant contract, and the market may even change from a normal market to an inverted market. ｜ 但价差缩小的幅度可能较大。近月合约价格可能远远强于远月合约，正向市场甚至可能转变为反向市场，使价差由正数变为负数。

------

#### III. Bear Spread Arbitrage ｜ 三、熊市套利

##### (I) Operating Conditions ｜ （一）操作前提

Bear spread arbitrage is generally used when supply is relatively sufficient, demand is weak, or the trader expects distant contracts to perform more strongly than nearby contracts. ｜ 熊市套利通常适用于市场供给相对充足、需求不足，或者交易者预计远月合约的价格表现将强于近月合约的情形。

##### (II) Operating Method ｜ （二）操作方式

A bear spread is established by selling a nearby-month contract and simultaneously buying a distant-month contract. ｜ 熊市套利的操作方式是卖出较近月份合约，同时买入较远月份合约。

📌 **Bear spread: sell nearby and buy distant.** ｜ 📌 **熊市套利：卖近月、买远月。**

------

##### (III) Cases ｜ （三）案例

###### Case 1: Prices Fall and the Spread Expands ｜ 案例一：价格下跌、价差扩大

On 8 July, the November natural-rubber futures contract was priced at RMB 12,955 per tonne, while the January contract for the following year was priced at RMB 13,420 per tonne. ｜ 7月8日，上海期货交易所11月份天然橡胶期货合约价格为12955元/吨，次年1月份合约价格为13420元/吨。

The opening spread was: ｜ 建仓时的价差为：
$$
13420-12955=465\text{元/吨}
$$
The trader expected natural-rubber prices to decline and the spread between the November and January contracts to expand. The trader sold 60 November contracts and bought 60 January contracts. Each contract represented 5 tonnes. ｜ 交易者预计天然橡胶价格将下跌，并且11月份与次年1月份合约之间的价差将扩大，因此卖出60手11月份合约，同时买入60手次年1月份合约，每手5吨。

On 8 September, the November and January contract prices declined to RMB 12,215 and RMB 12,775 per tonne respectively. ｜ 到9月8日，11月份和次年1月份合约价格分别下跌至12215元/吨和12775元/吨。

The closing spread was: ｜ 平仓时的价差为：
$$
12775-12215=560\text{元/吨}
$$
The spread expanded by: ｜ 价差扩大了：
$$
560-465=95\text{元/吨}
$$

| Contract | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss |
| -------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------- |
| November | Sell at 12,955   | Buy at 12,215    | +740           |
| January  | Buy at 13,420    | Sell at 12,775   | −645           |

| 合约      | 建仓      | 平仓      | 盈亏 |
| --------- | --------- | --------- | ---- |
| 11月份    | 卖出12955 | 买入12215 | +740 |
| 次年1月份 | 买入13420 | 卖出12775 | −645 |

$$
(740-645)\times5\times60
=
28500\text{元}
$$

The total arbitrage profit was RMB 28,500. ｜ 该套利交易总盈利为28500元。

------

###### Case 2: Prices Rise but the Spread Still Expands ｜ 案例二：价格上涨、价差仍然扩大

Assume that natural-rubber prices did not decline as expected. Instead, the November and January contract prices rose to RMB 13,075 and RMB 13,625 per tonne. ｜ 假设天然橡胶价格并未按照预期下跌，11月份和次年1月份合约价格反而分别上涨至13075元/吨和13625元/吨。

The closing spread was: ｜ 平仓时的价差为：
$$
13625-13075=550\text{元/吨}
$$
The spread expanded by: ｜ 价差扩大了：
$$
550-465=85\text{元/吨}
$$

| Contract | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss |
| -------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------- |
| November | Sell at 12,955   | Buy at 13,075    | −120           |
| January  | Buy at 13,420    | Sell at 13,625   | +205           |

| 合约      | 建仓      | 平仓      | 盈亏 |
| --------- | --------- | --------- | ---- |
| 11月份    | 卖出12955 | 买入13075 | −120 |
| 次年1月份 | 买入13420 | 卖出13625 | +205 |

$$
(-120+205)\times5\times60
=
25500\text{元}
$$

The total arbitrage profit was RMB 25,500. ｜ 该套利交易总盈利为25500元。

------

##### (IV) Case Conclusion ｜ （四）案例结论

In a normal market, a bear spread generates a profit as long as the spread between the distant and nearby contracts expands. The overall direction of natural-rubber prices does not determine the result. ｜ 在正向市场中进行熊市套利，只要远月与近月合约之间的价差扩大，交易者就可以盈利，与天然橡胶期货价格整体上涨还是下跌无关。

In a normal market, the distant contract is the higher-priced contract. Therefore, selling the nearby lower-priced contract and buying the distant higher-priced contract is essentially buy spread arbitrage, which benefits from spread expansion. ｜ 在正向市场中，远月合约是价格较高的合约。因此，卖出低价近月合约并买入高价远月合约，本质上属于买入套利，在价差扩大时获利。

##### (V) Special Note for an Inverted Market ｜ （五）反向市场中的特别说明

In an inverted market, the nearby contract is generally priced higher than the distant contract. ｜ 在反向市场中，近月合约价格通常高于远月合约。

A bear spread still involves selling the nearby contract and buying the distant contract. However, this now means selling the higher-priced contract and buying the lower-priced contract, which is sell spread arbitrage. ｜ 熊市套利仍然是卖出近月合约并买入远月合约，但此时相当于卖出高价合约、买入低价合约，属于卖出套利。

Therefore, in an inverted market, this bear spread earns a profit when the spread contracts. ｜ 因此，在反向市场中，这种熊市套利需要价差缩小才能盈利。

------

#### IV. Butterfly Spread Arbitrage ｜ 四、蝶式套利

##### (I) Operating Method ｜ （一）操作方法

A butterfly spread involves three contracts with different delivery months. ｜ 蝶式套利同时涉及三个不同交割月份的期货合约。

The trader buys the nearby and distant contracts while selling the middle-month contract, or takes the exact opposite positions. ｜ 交易者可以买入近月和远月合约，同时卖出居中月份合约；也可以采取完全相反的操作。

The number of middle-month contracts must equal the combined number of nearby and distant contracts. ｜ 居中月份合约的数量应当等于近月合约与远月合约数量之和。
$$
\text{Middle-month quantity}
=
\text{Nearby-month quantity}
+
\text{Distant-month quantity}
$$
A butterfly spread is essentially a combination of two calendar spreads with opposite characteristics. ｜ 蝶式套利本质上是两个方向相互配合的跨期套利组合。

It may be understood as: ｜ 可以理解为：

- One bull spread plus one bear spread ｜ 一个牛市套利加一个熊市套利
- Or one bear spread plus one bull spread ｜ 或者一个熊市套利加一个牛市套利

------

##### (II) Case ｜ （二）案例

On 1 February, the March, May and July soybean futures contracts were priced at RMB 4,450, RMB 4,530 and RMB 4,575 per tonne respectively. ｜ 2月1日，3月份、5月份和7月份大豆期货合约价格分别为4450元/吨、4530元/吨和4575元/吨。

The trader believed that the spread between March and May was too large, while the spread between May and July was too small. ｜ 交易者认为3月份与5月份合约之间的价差过大，而5月份与7月份合约之间的价差过小。

The trader expected the March–May spread to contract and the May–July spread to expand. ｜ 交易者预计3月份与5月份合约之间的价差将缩小，而5月份与7月份合约之间的价差将扩大。

The trader therefore: ｜ 因此，交易者同时进行以下操作：

- Bought 150 March contracts at RMB 4,450 per tonne ｜ 以4450元/吨买入150手3月份合约
- Sold 350 May contracts at RMB 4,530 per tonne ｜ 以4530元/吨卖出350手5月份合约
- Bought 200 July contracts at RMB 4,575 per tonne ｜ 以4575元/吨买入200手7月份合约

The quantity relationship satisfied the butterfly requirement: ｜ 合约数量符合蝶式套利的要求：
$$
350=150+200
$$
On 18 February, the March, May and July prices fell to RMB 4,250, RMB 4,310 and RMB 4,370 per tonne respectively. ｜ 到2月18日，3月份、5月份和7月份合约价格分别下跌至4250元/吨、4310元/吨和4370元/吨。

| Contract | Quantity | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss per tonne |
| -------- | -------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | ------------------------ |
| March    | 150      | Buy at 4,450     | Sell at 4,250    | −200                     |
| May      | 350      | Sell at 4,530    | Buy at 4,310     | +220                     |
| July     | 200      | Buy at 4,575     | Sell at 4,370    | −205                     |

| 合约  | 数量  | 建仓     | 平仓     | 每吨盈亏 |
| ----- | ----- | -------- | -------- | -------- |
| 3月份 | 150手 | 买入4450 | 卖出4250 | −200     |
| 5月份 | 350手 | 卖出4530 | 买入4310 | +220     |
| 7月份 | 200手 | 买入4575 | 卖出4370 | −205     |

Each soybean futures contract represented 10 tonnes. ｜ 每手大豆期货合约代表10吨。
$$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total P/L}
=&(-200)\times10\times150\\
&+220\times10\times350\\
&+(-205)\times10\times200\\
=&60000\text{元}
\end{aligned}
$$
The total profit from the butterfly spread was RMB 60,000. ｜ 该蝶式套利交易总盈利为60000元。

------

##### (III) Case Conclusion ｜ （三）案例结论

A butterfly spread is a balanced combination of two calendar spreads and may be described as “arbitrage on arbitrage.” ｜ 蝶式套利是两个跨期套利相互补充、相互平衡的组合，也可以称为“套利的套利”。

Compared with an ordinary calendar spread, a butterfly spread theoretically has lower risk and lower expected return. ｜ 与普通跨期套利相比，蝶式套利在理论上的风险和预期收益通常都相对较小。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结
