📅 **Calendar spread arbitrage trades different delivery months of the same futures product on the same exchange.** ｜ 📅 **跨期套利是在同一交易所交易同一期货品种的不同交割月份合约。**

🐂 **Bull spread: buy the nearby contract and sell the distant contract.** ｜ 🐂 **牛市套利：买入近月合约，卖出远月合约。**

🐻 **Bear spread: sell the nearby contract and buy the distant contract.** ｜ 🐻 **熊市套利：卖出近月合约，买入远月合约。**

⚖️ **In a normal market, a bull spread benefits from spread contraction, while a bear spread benefits from spread expansion.** ｜ ⚖️ **在正向市场中，牛市套利在价差缩小时获利，熊市套利在价差扩大时获利。**

🔍 **The direction of the overall price movement is less important than the change in the spread.** ｜ 🔍 **价格整体上涨还是下跌并不关键，关键是价差如何变化。**

🦋 **A butterfly spread uses three delivery months, and the middle-month quantity equals the sum of the nearby- and distant-month quantities.** ｜ 🦋 **蝶式套利使用三个交割月份，居中月份合约数量等于近月和远月合约数量之和。**

📉 **Butterfly spreads generally have lower risk and lower expected return than ordinary calendar spreads.** ｜ 📉 **蝶式套利的风险和预期收益通常低于普通跨期套利。**



### Examination Topic: Inter-Commodity Spread Arbitrage ｜ 考点：跨品种套利

#### I. Definition ｜ 一、概念

Inter-commodity spread arbitrage refers to arbitrage based on price differences among futures contracts on two or three different but economically related commodities. ｜ 跨品种套利是指利用两种或者三种不同但相互关联的商品期货合约之间的价格差异进行套利。

The commodities involved are different, but their prices are connected through substitution, complementarity, production or processing relationships. ｜ 参与套利的商品虽然不同，但它们之间通常存在替代、互补、生产或者加工关系，因此价格变化具有一定关联性。

------

#### II. Types ｜ 二、分类

##### (I) Arbitrage Between Related Commodities ｜ （一）相关商品间的套利

###### Types of Related Commodities ｜ 相关商品的类型

Related commodities may include: ｜ 相关商品主要包括：

- Demand substitutes ｜ 需求替代品
- Demand complements ｜ 需求互补品
- Production substitutes ｜ 生产替代品
- Production complements ｜ 生产互补品

Because the prices of these commodities are economically related, an abnormal change in their price difference may create an arbitrage opportunity. ｜ 由于这些商品的价格存在经济联系，当它们之间的价差偏离合理水平时，可能产生套利机会。

------

###### Case: Copper–Aluminium Spread Arbitrage ｜ 案例：铜与铝的跨品种套利

On 1 June, the March copper futures contract for the following year on the Shanghai Futures Exchange was priced at RMB 54,390 per tonne, while the March aluminium futures contract was priced at RMB 15,700 per tonne. ｜ 6月1日，上海期货交易所次年3月份铜期货合约价格为54390元/吨，次年3月份铝期货合约价格为15700元/吨。

The opening spread was: ｜ 建仓时的价差为：
$$
54390-15700=38690\text{元/吨}
$$
The arbitrageur believed that the spread between copper and aluminium was below its reasonable level and expected it to return to normal by expanding. ｜ 套利者认为铜与铝之间的价差低于合理水平，预计未来价差将扩大并恢复正常。

The trader therefore bought 30 March copper futures contracts and simultaneously sold 30 March aluminium futures contracts. Each contract represented 5 tonnes. ｜ 因此，套利者买入30手次年3月份铜期货合约，同时卖出30手次年3月份铝期货合约，每手为5吨。

On 28 June, the copper and aluminium futures prices fell to RMB 54,020 and RMB 15,265 per tonne respectively. ｜ 到6月28日，铜期货和铝期货价格分别下跌至54020元/吨和15265元/吨。

The closing spread was: ｜ 平仓时的价差为：
$$
54020-15265=38755\text{元/吨}
$$
The spread expanded by: ｜ 价差扩大了：
$$
38755-38690=65\text{元/吨}
$$

| Contract  | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss |
| --------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------- |
| Copper    | Buy at 54,390    | Sell at 54,020   | −370           |
| Aluminium | Sell at 15,700   | Buy at 15,265    | +435           |

| 合约 | 建仓      | 平仓      | 每吨盈亏 |
| ---- | --------- | --------- | -------- |
| 铜   | 买入54390 | 卖出54020 | −370     |
| 铝   | 卖出15700 | 买入15265 | +435     |

The total profit was: ｜ 总盈利为：
$$
(-370+435)\times5\times30
=
9750\text{元}
$$
Although the prices of both commodities fell, the arbitrage was profitable because the copper–aluminium spread expanded as expected. ｜ 虽然铜和铝的价格都出现下跌，但由于铜铝价差按照预期扩大，该套利交易仍然获得了盈利。

📌 **The key is the relative price change between the two commodities, not whether both prices rise or fall.** ｜ 📌 **关键在于两种商品的相对价格变化，而不是它们整体上涨还是下跌。**

------

##### (II) Arbitrage Between Raw Materials and Finished Products ｜ （二）原材料与制成品之间的套利

Arbitrage between raw materials and finished products uses the price relationship between a raw material and the products processed from it. ｜ 原材料与制成品之间的套利，是指利用原材料商品与其加工制成品之间的价格关系进行套利。

A typical example is the relationship among soybeans, soybean oil and soybean meal. ｜ 典型案例是大豆、豆油和豆粕之间的价格关系。

Soybeans are processed into soybean oil and soybean meal. Therefore, changes in the prices of these three products affect the theoretical processing margin. ｜ 大豆经过加工可以生产豆油和豆粕，因此三者价格变化会影响理论加工利润。

###### Soybean Crush Arbitrage ｜ 大豆提油套利

A soybean crush arbitrage consists of: ｜ 大豆提油套利的操作是：

- Buying soybean futures contracts ｜ 买入大豆期货合约
- Selling soybean oil and soybean meal futures contracts ｜ 卖出豆油和豆粕期货合约

This strategy is generally used when the trader expects the processing margin between soybeans and their products to expand. ｜ 这种策略通常适用于交易者预期大豆加工利润扩大的情形。

###### Reverse Soybean Crush Arbitrage ｜ 反向大豆提油套利

A reverse soybean crush arbitrage consists of: ｜ 反向大豆提油套利的操作是：

- Selling soybean futures contracts ｜ 卖出大豆期货合约
- Buying soybean oil and soybean meal futures contracts ｜ 买入豆油和豆粕期货合约

This strategy is generally used when the trader expects the processing margin to contract. ｜ 这种策略通常适用于交易者预期大豆加工利润缩小的情形。

📌 **Soybean crush arbitrage: buy raw materials and sell finished products.** ｜ 📌 **大豆提油套利：买入原材料，卖出制成品。**

📌 **Reverse crush arbitrage: sell raw materials and buy finished products.** ｜ 📌 **反向提油套利：卖出原材料，买入制成品。**

------

### Examination Topic: Inter-Market Arbitrage ｜ 考点：跨市套利

#### I. Definition ｜ 一、概念

Inter-market arbitrage, also called market-to-market arbitrage, refers to simultaneously buying a futures contract for a particular commodity and delivery month on one exchange and selling the same commodity and delivery-month contract on another exchange. ｜ 跨市套利又称市场间套利，是指在某一交易所买入某种商品某一交割月份的期货合约，同时在另一交易所卖出同一商品、同一交割月份的期货合约。

The two positions are later closed simultaneously when the price difference between the exchanges moves in a favourable direction. ｜ 当两个交易所之间的价差向有利方向变化时，交易者同时将两个市场中的合约对冲平仓以获取收益。

The underlying commodity and delivery month are generally the same, while the trading exchanges are different. ｜ 跨市套利中，期货品种和交割月份通常相同，但交易场所不同。

------

#### II. Case ｜ 二、案例

On 1 July, the December wheat futures contract on the Kansas City Board of Trade was priced at 730 cents per bushel. On the same day, the December wheat futures contract on the Chicago Board of Trade was priced at 740 cents per bushel. ｜ 7月1日，堪萨斯期货交易所12月份小麦期货合约价格为730美分/蒲式耳。同日，芝加哥期货交易所12月份小麦期货合约价格为740美分/蒲式耳。

The opening spread was: ｜ 建仓时的价差为：
$$
740-730=10\text{美分/蒲式耳}
$$
The arbitrageur believed that the Kansas contract was still relatively expensive compared with its normal relationship with the Chicago contract and expected the spread to expand. ｜ 套利者认为，虽然堪萨斯合约的绝对价格较低，但相对于两地正常的价格关系而言仍然偏高，因此预计两个交易所之间的价差将扩大。

The trader sold 20 Kansas December wheat contracts and simultaneously bought 20 Chicago December wheat contracts. Each contract represented 5,000 bushels. ｜ 因此，套利者卖出20手堪萨斯交易所12月份小麦期货合约，同时买入20手芝加哥交易所12月份小麦期货合约，每手为5000蒲式耳。

Later, the Kansas contract fell to 720 cents per bushel, while the Chicago contract fell to 735 cents per bushel. ｜ 随后，堪萨斯合约价格下跌至720美分/蒲式耳，芝加哥合约价格下跌至735美分/蒲式耳。

The closing spread was: ｜ 平仓时的价差为：
$$
735-720=15\text{美分/蒲式耳}
$$
The spread expanded by: ｜ 价差扩大了：
$$
15-10=5\text{美分/蒲式耳}
$$

| Exchange | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss |
| -------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------- |
| Kansas   | Sell at 730      | Buy at 720       | +10 cents      |
| Chicago  | Buy at 740       | Sell at 735      | −5 cents       |

| 交易所 | 建仓    | 平仓    | 每蒲式耳盈亏 |
| ------ | ------- | ------- | ------------ |
| 堪萨斯 | 卖出730 | 买入720 | +10美分      |
| 芝加哥 | 买入740 | 卖出735 | −5美分       |

The net profit per bushel was: ｜ 每蒲式耳净盈利为：
$$
10-5=5\text{美分}
$$
The total profit was: ｜ 总盈利为：
$$
5\times5000\times20
=
500000\text{美分}
$$
Because 100 cents equals 1 US dollar: ｜ 由于100美分等于1美元：
$$
500000\div100
=
5000\text{美元}
$$
The total arbitrage profit was USD 5,000. ｜ 该跨市套利交易总盈利为5000美元。

📌 **When the unit is cents, the final result must be converted into dollars.** ｜ 📌 **题目以美分计价时，最终结果不要忘记换算成美元。**

------

### Examination Topic: Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage ｜ 考点：期现套利

#### I. Classification ｜ 一、分类

##### (I) Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage ｜ （一）正向套利

Cash-and-carry arbitrage is used when the spread between the futures price and the spot price is significantly greater than the carrying cost. ｜ 当期货价格与现货价格之间的价差明显高于持仓费时，可以进行正向套利。

The trader buys the spot commodity and simultaneously sells the related futures contract. ｜ 具体操作是买入现货，同时卖出相关期货合约。
$$
\text{Buy spot}+\text{Sell futures}
$$

##### (II) Reverse Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage ｜ （二）反向套利

Reverse cash-and-carry arbitrage is used when the spread between the futures price and the spot price is significantly lower than the carrying cost. ｜ 当期货价格与现货价格之间的价差明显低于持仓费时，可以进行反向套利。

The trader sells the spot commodity and simultaneously buys the related futures contract. ｜ 具体操作是卖出现货，同时买入相关期货合约。
$$
\text{Sell spot}+\text{Buy futures}
$$

------

#### II. Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage Case ｜ 二、正向期现套利案例

On 30 June, the September sugar futures price on the Zhengzhou Commodity Exchange was RMB 5,200 per tonne, while the spot price of sugar in Zhengzhou was RMB 5,100 per tonne. ｜ 6月30日，郑州商品交易所9月份白糖期货价格为5200元/吨，郑州现货市场白糖价格为5100元/吨。

The futures price was RMB 100 per tonne higher than the spot price. ｜ 期货价格比现货价格高100元/吨。
$$
5200-5100=100\text{元/吨}
$$
The arbitrageur estimated that the total carrying cost was approximately RMB 30 per tonne and concluded that a cash-and-carry arbitrage opportunity existed. ｜ 套利者估算持仓费约为30元/吨，认为期货与现货之间的价差高于持仓费，因此存在正向期现套利机会。

The trader bought spot sugar at RMB 5,100 per tonne and simultaneously sold the September sugar futures contract at RMB 5,200 per tonne. ｜ 因此，套利者以5100元/吨买入白糖现货，同时以5200元/吨卖出9月份白糖期货合约。

If the trader held the positions until maturity and completed delivery, the gross price difference would be RMB 100 per tonne. ｜ 如果套利者将头寸持有至到期并完成交割，获得的毛价差为100元/吨。

After deducting the carrying cost of RMB 30 per tonne, the net profit would be: ｜ 扣除30元/吨的持仓费后，净盈利为：
$$
100-30=70\text{元/吨}
$$
The arbitrageur therefore earned RMB 70 per tonne. ｜ 因此，该期现套利交易每吨盈利70元。

A general expression for the profit is: ｜ 其基本盈利关系可以表示为：
$$
\text{Arbitrage profit}
=
\text{Futures price}
-
\text{Spot price}
-
\text{Carrying cost}
$$

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🔗 **Inter-commodity arbitrage uses price relationships among different but economically related commodities.** ｜ 🔗 **跨品种套利利用不同但具有经济关联的商品之间的价格关系。**

📊 **Related commodities may be substitutes, complements, raw materials or processed products.** ｜ 📊 **相关商品可能属于替代品、互补品，或者存在原材料与制成品关系。**

🌱 **Soybean crush arbitrage buys soybean futures and sells soybean oil and soybean meal futures.** ｜ 🌱 **大豆提油套利买入大豆期货，同时卖出豆油和豆粕期货。**

🔄 **Inter-market arbitrage trades the same commodity and delivery month on different exchanges.** ｜ 🔄 **跨市套利在不同交易所交易同一品种、同一交割月份的期货合约。**

📏 **Inter-market arbitrage profits from favourable changes in the price difference between exchanges.** ｜ 📏 **跨市套利通过两个交易所之间价差的有利变化获利。**

💵 **When prices are quoted in cents, the final profit must be converted into dollars.** ｜ 💵 **价格单位为美分时，最终盈利需要换算成美元。**

📦 **Cash-and-carry arbitrage buys spot and sells futures when the futures premium exceeds carrying costs.** ｜ 📦 **当期货升水明显高于持仓费时，正向套利买入现货并卖出期货。**

↩️ **Reverse cash-and-carry arbitrage sells spot and buys futures when the futures–spot spread is too low.** ｜ ↩️ **当期现价差明显低于持仓费时，反向套利卖出现货并买入期货。**



## Section 4: Futures Spread Orders ｜ 第四节 期货价差套利指令

#### I. Concept and Types of Spread Orders ｜ 一、套利指令的概念与种类

##### (I) Concept ｜ （一）概念

To facilitate spread trading, futures exchanges design special spread orders that combine the buy and sell instructions for two or more futures contracts into a single order. ｜ 为了便利套利交易，期货交易所会设计专门的套利指令，将两个或者两个以上期货合约的买卖指令合并为一个组合指令。

By submitting one spread order, an arbitrageur can simultaneously complete transactions in multiple related contracts. ｜ 套利者只需要提交一份套利指令，就可以同时完成多个相关期货合约的买卖操作。

A spread order normally does not require the trader to specify the exact transaction price of each individual contract. Instead, the trader generally specifies the required spread between the contracts. ｜ 套利指令通常不要求交易者分别标明每个期货合约的具体成交价格，而只需要标明相关合约之间的目标价差。

##### (II) Types ｜ （二）种类

Spread orders mainly include: ｜ 套利指令主要包括：

- Market spread orders ｜ 套利市价指令
- Limit spread orders ｜ 套利限价指令
- Calendar spread orders ｜ 跨期套利指令
- Inter-commodity spread orders ｜ 跨品种套利指令

------

#### II. Quotation of Spread Orders ｜ 二、套利指令的报价

When using a spread order, the arbitrageur must understand how the exchange defines the calculation of the spread. ｜ 使用套利指令时，套利者必须了解交易所对价差计算方式的具体规定。

Because the order concerns only the price difference between related contracts, the exchange specifies in advance which contract price should be subtracted from which other contract price. ｜ 由于套利指令只涉及相关合约之间的价差，交易所会事先规定用“哪一个合约价格”减去“哪一个合约价格”。

The general form is: ｜ 一般形式为：
$$
\text{Quoted spread}
=
\text{Price of the first specified leg}
-
\text{Price of the second specified leg}
$$
The trader must follow the exchange’s quotation convention when opening and closing the position. Otherwise, the direction of spread expansion or contraction may be misunderstood. ｜ 交易者在建仓和平仓时，都必须遵循交易所规定的报价顺序，否则可能误判价差扩大或者缩小的方向。

The specific quotation table in the source is severely damaged by OCR, so its detailed formulas are not reproduced here. ｜ 原材料中的具体报价表受到严重的OCR损坏，因此不补充其中无法确认的详细公式。

📌 **Buy spread arbitrage profits when the quoted spread expands.** ｜ 📌 **买入套利在报价价差扩大时获利。**

📌 **Sell spread arbitrage profits when the quoted spread contracts.** ｜ 📌 **卖出套利在报价价差缩小时获利。**

------

#### III. Application of Market and Limit Spread Orders ｜ 三、套利市价指令和套利限价指令的应用

##### (I) Application of a Market Spread Order ｜ （一）套利市价指令的应用

If an arbitrageur wishes to complete the transaction as quickly as possible at the current spread level, a market spread order may be used. ｜ 如果套利者希望按照当前市场的价差水平尽快成交，可以使用套利市价指令。

When submitting a market spread order, the trader does not need to specify the exact spread. The trader only needs to state the types and delivery months of the contracts to be bought and sold. ｜ 使用套利市价指令时，交易者不需要注明具体价差，只需要注明买入和卖出的期货品种及交割月份。

The actual execution spread depends on market conditions at the time the order is executed. ｜ 最终成交价差取决于指令执行时的市场行情。

The main advantage of a market spread order is rapid execution. ｜ 套利市价指令的主要优点是成交速度快。

Its disadvantage is that when market prices change rapidly, the actual execution spread may differ significantly from the trader’s expectation. ｜ 其缺点是，当市场行情发生较大变化时，实际成交价差可能与交易者的预期存在较大差异。

| Feature             | Market spread order           |
| ------------------- | ----------------------------- |
| Spread specified    | No                            |
| Execution speed     | Relatively fast               |
| Execution certainty | Relatively high               |
| Spread certainty    | Relatively low                |
| Main risk           | Unfavourable execution spread |

| 特征         | 套利市价指令       |
| ------------ | ------------------ |
| 是否指定价差 | 不需要             |
| 成交速度     | 较快               |
| 成交确定性   | 较高               |
| 价差确定性   | 较低               |
| 主要风险     | 实际成交价差不理想 |

------

##### (II) Application of a Limit Spread Order ｜ （二）套利限价指令的应用

If an arbitrageur wishes to trade only at an ideal spread, a limit spread order may be used. ｜ 如果套利者希望在某个理想价差水平成交，可以使用套利限价指令。

When using a limit spread order, the trader must specify: ｜ 使用套利限价指令时，交易者需要注明：

- The specific spread ｜ 具体价差
- The contracts to be bought ｜ 买入合约的品种和月份
- The contracts to be sold ｜ 卖出合约的品种和月份

A limit spread order ensures that the transaction will be executed only at the specified spread or a more favourable spread. ｜ 套利限价指令可以保证交易只按照指定价差或者更有利的价差成交。

Its advantage is that the trader can control the execution spread. ｜ 其优点是交易者可以控制实际成交价差。

Its disadvantage is that the order can be executed only when the market spread reaches the specified condition, so immediate execution cannot be guaranteed. ｜ 其缺点是，只有当市场价差达到设定条件时才能成交，因此不能保证立即成交。

| Feature             | Limit spread order            |
| ------------------- | ----------------------------- |
| Spread specified    | Yes                           |
| Execution speed     | Uncertain                     |
| Execution certainty | Relatively low                |
| Spread certainty    | Relatively high               |
| Main risk           | The order may not be executed |

| 特征         | 套利限价指令     |
| ------------ | ---------------- |
| 是否指定价差 | 需要             |
| 成交速度     | 不确定           |
| 成交确定性   | 较低             |
| 价差确定性   | 较高             |
| 主要风险     | 指令可能无法成交 |

📌 **Market order: prioritises execution speed.** ｜ 📌 **市价指令：优先保证成交速度。**

📌 **Limit order: prioritises the execution spread.** ｜ 📌 **限价指令：优先保证成交价差。**

------

#### IV. Futures Quantitative Trading ｜ 四、期货量化交易

##### (I) Classification of Investment Strategies ｜ （一）投资策略分类

###### Passive Investment Strategies ｜ 被动型投资策略

Passive investment strategies mainly refer to strategies that track various market indices. ｜ 被动型投资策略主要是指以追踪各类市场指数为主要投资手段的策略。

These strategies generally follow predetermined portfolio rules rather than actively predicting short-term market movements. ｜ 这类策略通常按照预先确定的组合规则运行，而不是主动预测短期市场行情。

###### Active Investment Strategies ｜ 主动型投资策略

Active investment strategies may include traditional discretionary strategies based on traders’ subjective analysis of futures-market conditions. ｜ 主动型投资策略可以包括依靠交易者对期货行情进行主观分析的传统主观交易策略。

They may also include modern quantitative strategies that rely on computers, data and algorithmic models to conduct systematic trading. ｜ 主动型投资策略还可以包括依靠计算机、数据和算法模型进行程序化交易的现代量化投资策略。

------

##### (II) Definition of Quantitative Trading ｜ （二）量化交易的定义

Quantitative trading uses mathematical and statistical analysis to examine price-movement patterns. ｜ 量化交易通过数学和统计分析研究价格运动规律。

It models data such as: ｜ 量化交易可以对以下数据进行建模：

- Macroeconomic indicators ｜ 宏观经济指标
- Financial data ｜ 财务数据
- Price–volume relationships ｜ 量价关系
- Capital flows and transaction data ｜ 资金流向和交易数据

The purpose is to identify internal relationships among data, continuously generate quantitative investment signals and execute trading instructions strictly through computer systems. ｜ 其目的是寻找不同数据之间的内在关联，持续生成定量化投资信号，并通过计算机系统严格执行交易指令。

The ultimate objective is to obtain relatively stable trading returns. ｜ 其最终目标是获得相对稳定的交易收益。

------

##### (III) Advantages of Futures Quantitative Trading ｜ （三）期货量化交易的优势

###### Scientific and Systematic Methods ｜ 方法科学

Trading decisions generated by quantitative systems are based on theoretical models and supported by data. ｜ 量化交易系统产生的交易决策具有一定的理论依据，并能够得到数据支持。

A quantitative strategy normally needs to be tested using substantial historical or practical data before it is formally applied. ｜ 量化交易策略通常需要经过大量历史数据或者实际数据的检验，才能正式应用。

###### Reducing Cognitive Bias ｜ 避免认知偏差

Quantitative trading decisions are automatically generated by algorithmic models and are less affected by human emotions and subjective judgement. ｜ 量化交易决策由算法模型自动生成，较少受到交易者情绪和主观判断的影响。

It can therefore reduce cognitive biases that commonly occur in discretionary trading. ｜ 因此，量化交易能够在一定程度上克服主观交易中常见的认知偏差。

###### Rapid Response and Strong Execution ｜ 响应迅速、执行力强

Computer systems can rapidly process large amounts of market data and identify potential trading opportunities. ｜ 计算机系统可以快速处理大量市场数据，并及时发现潜在的交易机会。

Once a trading signal is generated, the system can promptly execute the corresponding order. ｜ 交易信号生成后，系统可以及时执行相应的交易指令。

📌 **Quantitative trading does not merely analyse faster; it also executes more consistently.** ｜ 📌 **量化交易不仅分析速度更快，而且执行过程更加稳定一致。**

------

##### (IV) Main Modelling Methods in Futures Quantitative Trading ｜ （四）期货量化交易的主要建模方法

###### Artificial Intelligence ｜ 人工智能

Futures quantitative trading is a complex application involving various fields of knowledge and technology, so it requires substantial intelligent-processing capabilities. ｜ 期货量化交易是一项综合多种知识与技术的复杂应用，因此对智能分析和处理能力具有较高要求。

Artificial intelligence techniques such as machine learning, neural networks and genetic algorithms may be used to design and implement futures quantitative-trading strategies. ｜ 机器学习、神经网络和遗传算法等人工智能技术，都可以用于期货量化交易策略的设计和应用。

------

###### Data Mining ｜ 数据挖掘

Data mining in quantitative trading generally contains three stages. ｜ 量化交易中的数据挖掘通常包括三个阶段。

**Data preparation** ｜ **数据准备**

Required data are selected from relevant sources and integrated into a usable dataset. ｜ 从相关数据源中选取所需数据，并将其整合为可以使用的数据集。

**Pattern discovery** ｜ **规律寻找**

Statistical, computational or algorithmic methods are used to identify patterns hidden in the dataset. ｜ 采用统计、计算或者算法方法，寻找数据集中隐藏的规律。

**Pattern representation** ｜ **规律表示**

The discovered patterns are presented in a form that people can understand, including tables, models or visualisations. ｜ 通过人们可以理解的方式呈现数据规律，例如使用表格、模型或者可视化结果。
$$
\text{Data preparation}
\rightarrow
\text{Pattern discovery}
\rightarrow
\text{Pattern representation}
$$

------

###### Wavelet Analysis ｜ 小波分析

The main function of wavelet analysis in quantitative investment is to process the waveforms of trading signals. ｜ 小波分析在量化投资中的主要作用是对交易信号的波形进行处理。

The price and volume movements of an investment product can be treated as a waveform containing both useful signals and substantial noise. ｜ 投资产品的价格和成交量走势可以被看作一种波形，其中既包含有效信号，也不可避免地包含大量噪声。

Wavelet analysis can perform: ｜ 小波分析可以进行：

- Denoising ｜ 去噪
- Reconstruction ｜ 重构
- Diagnosis ｜ 诊断
- Identification ｜ 识别

These processes may help traders analyse and judge future market movements. ｜ 这些处理有助于交易者分析并判断未来市场走势。

------

###### Support Vector Machines ｜ 支持向量机

A support vector machine maps samples from the original space into a high-dimensional or even infinite-dimensional feature space through a nonlinear mapping. ｜ 支持向量机通过非线性映射，将原始样本空间映射到高维甚至无穷维的特征空间。

This transformation converts a problem that is nonlinearly separable in the original low-dimensional space into a linearly separable problem in the higher-dimensional feature space. ｜ 通过这种升维处理，可以把原低维空间中的非线性可分问题，转化为高维特征空间中的线性可分问题。

Its basic idea can therefore be summarised as: ｜ 其基本思想可以概括为：
$$
\text{Nonlinear low-dimensional problem}
\rightarrow
\text{High-dimensional mapping}
\rightarrow
\text{Linear separation}
$$

------

##### (V) Applications of Futures Quantitative Trading ｜ （五）期货量化交易的应用

###### Statistical Arbitrage ｜ 统计套利

Statistical arbitrage uses historical statistical relationships among asset prices to identify and trade temporary pricing deviations. ｜ 统计套利利用资产价格之间的历史统计规律，识别并交易暂时性的价格偏离。

However, statistical arbitrage is not risk-free arbitrage. ｜ 但是，统计套利并不是无风险套利。

Its main risk is that a statistical relationship observed in historical data may weaken, change or disappear in the future. ｜ 其主要风险在于，根据历史数据得到的统计规律在未来可能减弱、改变甚至消失。

📌 **Historical correlation does not guarantee future continuation.** ｜ 📌 **历史统计关系并不保证未来仍然持续。**

------

###### Algorithmic Trading ｜ 算法交易

Algorithmic trading is also called automated trading, black-box trading or machine trading. ｜ 算法交易又称自动交易、黑盒交易或者机器交易。

It is a trading method in which computer programs generate and execute orders according to predetermined algorithms. ｜ 算法交易是指计算机程序按照预先设计的算法生成并执行交易指令的交易方法。

Depending on the algorithm, the system may determine: ｜ 根据算法设计，系统可以决定：

- Whether to trade ｜ 是否进行交易
- Trading time ｜ 交易时间
- Transaction price ｜ 交易价格
- Transaction quantity ｜ 交易数量

------

###### Passive Algorithmic Trading ｜ 被动型算法交易

Passive algorithmic trading is also called structured algorithmic trading. ｜ 被动型算法交易也称结构型算法交易。

Except for using historical data to estimate the main parameters of the trading model, this type of algorithm generally does not actively select transaction timing and quantity according to real-time market conditions. ｜ 除了利用历史数据估计交易模型的核心参数外，这类算法通常不会根据实时行情主动选择交易时机和交易数量。

It normally executes transactions according to predetermined trading rules. ｜ 它通常按照既定的交易方针和执行规则完成交易。

------

###### Active Algorithmic Trading ｜ 主动型算法交易

Active algorithmic trading is also called opportunistic algorithmic trading. ｜ 主动型算法交易也称机会型算法交易。

Its decisions are primarily made in real time according to current market conditions. ｜ 这类算法主要根据实时市场状况作出交易决策。

It determines whether to trade, how much to trade and at what price to trade. ｜ 系统会根据市场变化判断是否交易、交易数量以及交易价格。

------

###### Hybrid Algorithmic Trading ｜ 综合型算法交易

Hybrid algorithmic trading combines the characteristics of passive and active algorithmic trading. ｜ 综合型算法交易是被动型算法交易与主动型算法交易的有机结合。

It follows predetermined execution rules while also making certain adjustments according to real-time market conditions. ｜ 它既遵循预先设定的执行规则，也会根据实时市场情况作出一定调整。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🧾 **A spread order combines transactions in two or more futures contracts into one instruction.** ｜ 🧾 **套利指令将两个或者多个期货合约的买卖操作合并为一份指令。**

📏 **Spread quotation depends on the subtraction order specified by the exchange.** ｜ 📏 **套利价差的报价取决于交易所规定的合约相减顺序。**

📈 **Buy spread arbitrage benefits from spread expansion, while sell spread arbitrage benefits from spread contraction.** ｜ 📈 **买入套利在价差扩大时获利，卖出套利在价差缩小时获利。**

⚡ **A market spread order prioritises rapid execution but cannot guarantee the exact spread.** ｜ ⚡ **套利市价指令优先保证快速成交，但不能保证具体成交价差。**

🎯 **A limit spread order controls the execution spread but cannot guarantee immediate execution.** ｜ 🎯 **套利限价指令可以控制成交价差，但不能保证立即成交。**

💻 **Quantitative trading uses mathematical models, data and computer systems to generate and execute trading signals.** ｜ 💻 **量化交易利用数学模型、数据和计算机系统生成并执行交易信号。**

🧠 **Its main modelling tools include artificial intelligence, data mining, wavelet analysis and support vector machines.** ｜ 🧠 **其主要建模方法包括人工智能、数据挖掘、小波分析和支持向量机。**

⚠️ **Statistical arbitrage is not risk-free because historical statistical relationships may fail in the future.** ｜ ⚠️ **统计套利并非无风险套利，因为历史统计规律未来可能失效。**

🤖 **Algorithmic trading may be passive, active or hybrid.** ｜ 🤖 **算法交易可以分为被动型、主动型和综合型。**



# Chapter 6: Interest Rate Futures ｜ 第六章 利率期货

This chapter accounts for approximately 8% of the examination score. ｜ 本章分值占比约为8%。

## Section 1: Interest Rate Futures and Their Price Determinants ｜ 第一节 利率期货及其价格影响因素

### Examination Topic: Interest Rate Futures and Their Quotations ｜ 考点：利率期货及其报价

#### I. Definition of Interest Rate Futures ｜ 一、利率期货的概念

Interest rate futures are futures contracts whose underlying instruments are interest-rate-related financial instruments, such as certificates of deposit, interbank funds, short-term government bonds and medium- or long-term government bonds. ｜ 利率期货是指以定期存单、同业拆借资金、短期国债以及中长期国债等利率类金融工具为标的物的期货品种。

------

#### II. Classification of Interest Rate Futures ｜ 二、利率期货的分类

##### (I) Classification by Contract Term ｜ （一）按照合约期限分类

###### Short-Term Interest Rate Futures ｜ 短期利率期货

The underlying interest rate generally has a maturity of one year or less. ｜ 短期利率期货的交易标的主要是期限在一年以内，含一年的短期利率。

###### Medium- and Long-Term Interest Rate Futures ｜ 中长期利率期货

The underlying instruments are mainly medium- and long-term government bonds with maturities of more than one year. ｜ 中长期利率期货的交易标的主要是期限在一年以上的中长期国债。

##### (II) Classification of Medium- and Long-Term Government Bond Futures ｜ （二）中长期国债期货的分类

Medium-term government bond futures generally have maturities of 1–10 years. ｜ 中期国债期货的期限通常为1～10年。

Long-term government bond futures generally have maturities of 10–30 years. ｜ 长期国债期货的期限通常为10～30年。

Ultra-long-term government bond futures generally have maturities of more than 30 years. ｜ 超长期国债期货的期限通常为30年以上。

##### (III) Domestic Products Listed in the Source ｜ （三）原材料列出的国内品种

The source lists the following government bond futures products: ｜ 原材料列出的国内国债期货品种包括：

- Two-year government bond futures ｜ 2年期国债期货
- Five-year government bond futures ｜ 5年期国债期货
- Ten-year government bond futures ｜ 10年期国债期货

------

#### III. Quotations of Short-Term Interest Rate Futures ｜ 三、短期利率期货的报价

##### (I) Typical Futures Contracts ｜ （一）典型期货合约

Typical short-term interest rate futures contracts include: ｜ 典型的短期利率期货合约包括：

- Three-month Eurodollar futures ｜ 3个月欧洲美元期货
- Three-month euro interbank interest rate futures ｜ 3个月欧元拆借利率期货

------

##### (II) Quotation Method ｜ （二）报价方式

Short-term interest rate futures generally use an index quotation method. ｜ 短期利率期货通常采用指数报价方式。

The quoted index equals 100 minus the annual interest rate expressed without the percentage sign. ｜ 期货报价等于100减去不带百分号的年利率数值。
$$
\text{Futures quotation}
=
100-\text{Annual interest rate}
$$
For example, if the annual interest rate is 2.5%, the futures quotation is: ｜ 例如，当年利率为2.5%时，期货报价为：
$$
100-2.5=97.500
$$
📌 **Interest rate rises → futures quotation falls.** ｜ 📌 **利率上升 → 期货报价下降。**

📌 **Interest rate falls → futures quotation rises.** ｜ 📌 **利率下降 → 期货报价上升。**

------

##### (III) CME Three-Month Eurodollar Futures ｜ （三）CME三个月欧洲美元期货

###### Quotation Basis ｜ 报价基础

The contract quotation is based on a three-month Eurodollar interbank interest rate index. ｜ 该合约采用3个月欧洲美元拆借利率指数进行报价。

The quotation equals 100 minus the annual interest rate calculated on a 360-day basis. ｜ 期货报价等于100减去按照一年360天计算的不带百分号的年利率。

For example: ｜ 例如：
$$
\text{Annual interest rate}=2.5\%
$$

------

###### Contract Size ｜ 合约规模

According to the textbook example, the notional principal of one contract is USD 1,000,000, and the term of the underlying deposit is three months. ｜ 根据教材案例，一手合约的名义本金为100万美元，标的是期限为3个月的欧洲美元定期存款。

------

###### Value of One Basis Point ｜ 一个基点的合约价值

One basis point equals 0.01 percentage points in the annual interest rate and corresponds to a change of 0.01 in the futures quotation. ｜ 一个基点等于年利率的0.01个百分点，对应期货报价变动0.01。

The value of one basis point is: ｜ 一个基点对应的合约价值为：
$$
1{,}000{,}000
\times0.01\%
\times\frac{3}{12}
=
25\text{美元}
$$
Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
0.01\text{ quotation point}
=
1\text{ basis point}
=
25\text{美元}
$$

------

###### Minimum Price Fluctuation ｜ 最小变动价位

For the nearest-expiry contract, the minimum price fluctuation is one-quarter of a basis point, or 0.0025 quotation points. ｜ 最近到期合约的最小变动价位为四分之一个基点，即报价变动0.0025。

Its corresponding contract value is: ｜ 其对应的合约价值为：
$$
25\times\frac14=6.25\text{美元}
$$
For other listed contracts, the minimum price fluctuation is one-half of a basis point, or 0.005 quotation points. ｜ 其他挂牌合约的最小变动价位为二分之一个基点，即报价变动0.005。

Its corresponding contract value is: ｜ 其对应的合约价值为：
$$
25\times\frac12=12.50\text{美元}
$$

| Quotation change | Basis-point change | Contract-value change |
| ---------------- | ------------------ | --------------------- |
| 0.0100           | 1 basis point      | USD 25.00             |
| 0.0050           | 1/2 basis point    | USD 12.50             |
| 0.0025           | 1/4 basis point    | USD 6.25              |

| 报价变动 | 基点变动  | 合约价值变动 |
| -------- | --------- | ------------ |
| 0.0100   | 1个基点   | 25美元       |
| 0.0050   | 1/2个基点 | 12.50美元    |
| 0.0025   | 1/4个基点 | 6.25美元     |

------

##### (IV) Conversion Between Quotation and Discounted Value ｜ （四）成交报价与贴现值的换算

Suppose the futures quotation is 98.580. ｜ 假设期货成交报价为98.580。

The corresponding annual discount rate is: ｜ 对应的年贴现率为：
$$
(100-98.580)\%
=
1.420\%
$$
For a three-month deposit with a notional principal of USD 1,000,000, the theoretical discounted value is: ｜ 对于名义本金为100万美元、期限为3个月的存款，其理论贴现值为：
$$
1{,}000{,}000
\times
\left(
1-1.420\%\times\frac{3}{12}
\right)
$$
原材料中的“1.429%”和“994650美元”与报价98.580不一致。按照其给出的公式，正确结果应为 **1.420%和996450美元**。

------

##### (V) Relationship Between Interest Rates and Futures Prices ｜ （五）市场利率与期货价格的关系

When market interest rates rise, the quotation of three-month Eurodollar futures generally falls. ｜ 当市场利率上升时，3个月欧洲美元期货价格通常下降。

When market interest rates fall, the futures quotation generally rises. ｜ 当市场利率下降时，3个月欧洲美元期货价格通常上涨。
$$
\text{Interest rate}\uparrow
\quad\Rightarrow\quad
\text{Futures quotation}\downarrow
$$
📌 **Interest rate and short-term interest rate futures quotation move in opposite directions.** ｜ 📌 **市场利率与短期利率期货报价呈反向变动关系。**

------

##### (VI) Profit-and-Loss Case ｜ （六）盈亏计算案例

Suppose an investor buys one three-month Eurodollar futures contract at 98.580 and later sells it at 99.000. ｜ 假设投资者以98.580买入一手3个月欧洲美元期货，之后以99.000卖出平仓。

The quotation increased by: ｜ 报价上涨了：
$$
99.000-98.580=0.420
$$
A quotation change of 0.01 is worth USD 25, so a change of 0.420 is equivalent to 42 basis points. ｜ 报价每变动0.01对应25美元，因此报价变动0.420相当于42个基点。
$$
42\times25
=
1050\text{美元}
$$
It can also be calculated using the shortcut given in the source: ｜ 也可以使用原材料给出的简便方法：
$$
(99.000-98.580)\times2500
=
1050\text{美元}
$$
The investor earned USD 1,050. ｜ 该投资者盈利1050美元。

📌 **One full quotation point is worth USD 2,500.** ｜ 📌 **报价每变动完整的1点，对应合约价值变动2500美元。**

------

##### (VII) Settlement of Short-Term Interest Rate Futures ｜ （七）短期利率期货的交割

The buyer and seller do not physically deliver cash deposits or certificates of deposit. ｜ 买卖双方并不实际交付现金或者定期存单。

Instead, the difference between the final settlement price and the relevant previous settlement price is transferred in cash. ｜ 交易双方根据交割结算价与相关结算价格之间的差额进行现金划转。

Therefore, short-term interest rate futures generally use cash settlement. ｜ 因此，短期利率期货通常采用现金交割。

------

#### IV. Quotations of Government Bond Futures ｜ 四、国债期货的报价

##### (I) Quotation in China ｜ （一）中国国债期货的报价

Government bond futures in China use a net-price quotation based on a government bond with a face value of RMB 100. ｜ 我国国债期货采用面值100元国债的净价报价方式，报价中不包含持有期间的应计利息。

Prices after the decimal point are expressed using the decimal system. ｜ 价格小数点以后采用十进位制。

For example, a quotation of 97.335 means that the futures price corresponding to a government bond with a face value of RMB 100 is RMB 97.335. ｜ 例如，报价97.335表示面值100元的国债期货价格为97.335元。

------

##### (II) Quotation of US Treasury Futures ｜ （二）美国国债期货的报价

A US Treasury futures quotation may be written as: ｜ 美国国债期货报价可以表示为：
$$
118'222
$$
or: ｜ 或者：
$$
118\text{-}222
$$

###### First Part: Full Points ｜ 第一部分：整数点

The number 118 represents 118 full price points. ｜ 数字118表示118个完整的价格点。

For a USD 100,000 contract, one full point is worth: ｜ 对于面值10万美元的一手合约，完整的1点价值为：
$$
\frac{100{,}000}{100}
=
1000\text{美元}
$$

------

###### Second Part: Thirty-Seconds ｜ 第二部分：三十二分位

The next two digits range from 00 to 31 and use a base-32 quotation system. ｜ 后两位数字的取值范围为00～31，采用三十二进位制。

For example: ｜ 例如：
$$
22=\frac{22}{32}\text{点}
$$

------

###### Third Part: Fractions of One Thirty-Second ｜ 第三部分：三十二分之一的细分

The final digit generally uses 0, 2, 5 or 7. ｜ 最后一位通常使用0、2、5、7四个数字。

| Final digit | Meaning         |
| ----------- | --------------- |
| 0           | $0$ of $1/32$   |
| 2           | $1/4$ of $1/32$ |
| 5           | $2/4$ of $1/32$ |
| 7           | $3/4$ of $1/32$ |

| 最后一位 | 含义                     |
| -------- | ------------------------ |
| 0        | $1/32$点的0              |
| 2        | $1/32$点的$1/4$          |
| 5        | $1/32$点的$2/4$，即$1/2$ |
| 7        | $1/32$点的$3/4$          |

Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
118\text{-}222
=
118+\frac{22}{32}+\frac{1}{4}\times\frac{1}{32}
$$

------

##### (III) Profit-and-Loss Case for US Treasury Futures ｜ （三）美国国债期货盈亏案例

Suppose an investor buys one ten-year US Treasury futures contract at 118-215 and later sells it at 119-237. ｜ 假设投资者以118-215买入一手10年期美国国债期货，之后以119-237卖出平仓。

The change in full points is: ｜ 整数点的变化为：
$$
119-118=1
$$
The change in thirty-seconds is: ｜ 三十二分位的变化为：
$$
\frac{23-21}{32}
=
\frac{2}{32}
$$
The last digit changes from 5 to 7. The digit 5 represents $2/4$ of $1/32$, while 7 represents $3/4$ of $1/32$. ｜ 最后一位由5变为7。其中5代表$1/32$点的$2/4$，7代表$1/32$点的$3/4$。

Therefore, the additional change is: ｜ 因此，这一部分的变化为：
$$
\left(\frac34-\frac24\right)\times\frac1{32}
=
\frac1{128}
$$
The total profit is: ｜ 总盈利为：
$$
\left[
(119-118)
+
\frac{23-21}{32}
+
\left(\frac34-\frac24\right)\frac1{32}
\right]
\times1000
$$
The investor earned approximately USD 1,070.31. ｜ 该投资者盈利约1070.31美元。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

📊 **Interest rate futures use interest-rate-related financial instruments as their underlying instruments.** ｜ 📊 **利率期货以利率类金融工具作为交易标的。**

⏳ **Short-term interest rate futures generally involve maturities of one year or less, while medium- and long-term futures mainly use government bonds with maturities of more than one year.** ｜ ⏳ **短期利率期货的期限通常不超过一年，中长期利率期货主要以一年以上的国债为标的。**

🔢 **Short-term interest rate futures quotation equals 100 minus the annual interest rate.** ｜ 🔢 **短期利率期货报价等于100减去年利率数值。**

↔️ **Market interest rates and short-term interest rate futures quotations move in opposite directions.** ｜ ↔️ **市场利率与短期利率期货报价呈反向变动关系。**

💵 **For the textbook’s three-month Eurodollar contract, one basis point is worth USD 25.** ｜ 💵 **在教材的3个月欧洲美元合约中，一个基点对应25美元。**

🧮 **A quotation change of one full point corresponds to USD 2,500.** ｜ 🧮 **报价完整变动1点，对应合约价值变动2500美元。**

💳 **Short-term interest rate futures generally use cash settlement rather than physical delivery of deposits.** ｜ 💳 **短期利率期货通常采用现金交割，不实际交付存款。**

🇨🇳 **Chinese government bond futures use decimal net-price quotations per RMB 100 face value.** ｜ 🇨🇳 **我国国债期货采用每100元面值的十进制净价报价。**

🇺🇸 **US Treasury futures quotations use full points, thirty-seconds and fractions of one thirty-second.** ｜ 🇺🇸 **美国国债期货报价由整数点、三十二分位及其细分组成。**

### Examination Topic: Inter-Commodity Spread Arbitrage ｜ 考点：跨品种套利

#### I. Definition ｜ 一、概念

Inter-commodity spread arbitrage refers to arbitrage based on price differences among futures contracts on two or three different but economically related commodities. ｜ 跨品种套利是指利用两种或者三种不同但相互关联的商品期货合约之间的价格差异进行套利。

The commodities involved are different, but their prices are connected through substitution, complementarity, production or processing relationships. ｜ 参与套利的商品虽然不同，但它们之间通常存在替代、互补、生产或者加工关系，因此价格变化具有一定关联性。

------

#### II. Types ｜ 二、分类

##### (I) Arbitrage Between Related Commodities ｜ （一）相关商品间的套利

###### Types of Related Commodities ｜ 相关商品的类型

Related commodities may include: ｜ 相关商品主要包括：

- Demand substitutes ｜ 需求替代品
- Demand complements ｜ 需求互补品
- Production substitutes ｜ 生产替代品
- Production complements ｜ 生产互补品

Because the prices of these commodities are economically related, an abnormal change in their price difference may create an arbitrage opportunity. ｜ 由于这些商品的价格存在经济联系，当它们之间的价差偏离合理水平时，可能产生套利机会。

------

###### Case: Copper–Aluminium Spread Arbitrage ｜ 案例：铜与铝的跨品种套利

On 1 June, the March copper futures contract for the following year on the Shanghai Futures Exchange was priced at RMB 54,390 per tonne, while the March aluminium futures contract was priced at RMB 15,700 per tonne. ｜ 6月1日，上海期货交易所次年3月份铜期货合约价格为54390元/吨，次年3月份铝期货合约价格为15700元/吨。

The opening spread was: ｜ 建仓时的价差为：
$$
54390-15700=38690\text{元/吨}
$$
The arbitrageur believed that the spread between copper and aluminium was below its reasonable level and expected it to return to normal by expanding. ｜ 套利者认为铜与铝之间的价差低于合理水平，预计未来价差将扩大并恢复正常。

The trader therefore bought 30 March copper futures contracts and simultaneously sold 30 March aluminium futures contracts. Each contract represented 5 tonnes. ｜ 因此，套利者买入30手次年3月份铜期货合约，同时卖出30手次年3月份铝期货合约，每手为5吨。

On 28 June, the copper and aluminium futures prices fell to RMB 54,020 and RMB 15,265 per tonne respectively. ｜ 到6月28日，铜期货和铝期货价格分别下跌至54020元/吨和15265元/吨。

The closing spread was: ｜ 平仓时的价差为：
$$
54020-15265=38755\text{元/吨}
$$
The spread expanded by: ｜ 价差扩大了：
$$
38755-38690=65\text{元/吨}
$$

| Contract  | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss |
| --------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------- |
| Copper    | Buy at 54,390    | Sell at 54,020   | −370           |
| Aluminium | Sell at 15,700   | Buy at 15,265    | +435           |

| 合约 | 建仓      | 平仓      | 每吨盈亏 |
| ---- | --------- | --------- | -------- |
| 铜   | 买入54390 | 卖出54020 | −370     |
| 铝   | 卖出15700 | 买入15265 | +435     |

The total profit was: ｜ 总盈利为：
$$
(-370+435)\times5\times30
=
9750\text{元}
$$
Although the prices of both commodities fell, the arbitrage was profitable because the copper–aluminium spread expanded as expected. ｜ 虽然铜和铝的价格都出现下跌，但由于铜铝价差按照预期扩大，该套利交易仍然获得了盈利。

📌 **The key is the relative price change between the two commodities, not whether both prices rise or fall.** ｜ 📌 **关键在于两种商品的相对价格变化，而不是它们整体上涨还是下跌。**

------

##### (II) Arbitrage Between Raw Materials and Finished Products ｜ （二）原材料与制成品之间的套利

Arbitrage between raw materials and finished products uses the price relationship between a raw material and the products processed from it. ｜ 原材料与制成品之间的套利，是指利用原材料商品与其加工制成品之间的价格关系进行套利。

A typical example is the relationship among soybeans, soybean oil and soybean meal. ｜ 典型案例是大豆、豆油和豆粕之间的价格关系。

Soybeans are processed into soybean oil and soybean meal. Therefore, changes in the prices of these three products affect the theoretical processing margin. ｜ 大豆经过加工可以生产豆油和豆粕，因此三者价格变化会影响理论加工利润。

###### Soybean Crush Arbitrage ｜ 大豆提油套利

A soybean crush arbitrage consists of: ｜ 大豆提油套利的操作是：

- Buying soybean futures contracts ｜ 买入大豆期货合约
- Selling soybean oil and soybean meal futures contracts ｜ 卖出豆油和豆粕期货合约

This strategy is generally used when the trader expects the processing margin between soybeans and their products to expand. ｜ 这种策略通常适用于交易者预期大豆加工利润扩大的情形。

###### Reverse Soybean Crush Arbitrage ｜ 反向大豆提油套利

A reverse soybean crush arbitrage consists of: ｜ 反向大豆提油套利的操作是：

- Selling soybean futures contracts ｜ 卖出大豆期货合约
- Buying soybean oil and soybean meal futures contracts ｜ 买入豆油和豆粕期货合约

This strategy is generally used when the trader expects the processing margin to contract. ｜ 这种策略通常适用于交易者预期大豆加工利润缩小的情形。

📌 **Soybean crush arbitrage: buy raw materials and sell finished products.** ｜ 📌 **大豆提油套利：买入原材料，卖出制成品。**

📌 **Reverse crush arbitrage: sell raw materials and buy finished products.** ｜ 📌 **反向提油套利：卖出原材料，买入制成品。**

------

### Examination Topic: Inter-Market Arbitrage ｜ 考点：跨市套利

#### I. Definition ｜ 一、概念

Inter-market arbitrage, also called market-to-market arbitrage, refers to simultaneously buying a futures contract for a particular commodity and delivery month on one exchange and selling the same commodity and delivery-month contract on another exchange. ｜ 跨市套利又称市场间套利，是指在某一交易所买入某种商品某一交割月份的期货合约，同时在另一交易所卖出同一商品、同一交割月份的期货合约。

The two positions are later closed simultaneously when the price difference between the exchanges moves in a favourable direction. ｜ 当两个交易所之间的价差向有利方向变化时，交易者同时将两个市场中的合约对冲平仓以获取收益。

The underlying commodity and delivery month are generally the same, while the trading exchanges are different. ｜ 跨市套利中，期货品种和交割月份通常相同，但交易场所不同。

------

#### II. Case ｜ 二、案例

On 1 July, the December wheat futures contract on the Kansas City Board of Trade was priced at 730 cents per bushel. On the same day, the December wheat futures contract on the Chicago Board of Trade was priced at 740 cents per bushel. ｜ 7月1日，堪萨斯期货交易所12月份小麦期货合约价格为730美分/蒲式耳。同日，芝加哥期货交易所12月份小麦期货合约价格为740美分/蒲式耳。

The opening spread was: ｜ 建仓时的价差为：
$$
740-730=10\text{美分/蒲式耳}
$$
The arbitrageur believed that the Kansas contract was still relatively expensive compared with its normal relationship with the Chicago contract and expected the spread to expand. ｜ 套利者认为，虽然堪萨斯合约的绝对价格较低，但相对于两地正常的价格关系而言仍然偏高，因此预计两个交易所之间的价差将扩大。

The trader sold 20 Kansas December wheat contracts and simultaneously bought 20 Chicago December wheat contracts. Each contract represented 5,000 bushels. ｜ 因此，套利者卖出20手堪萨斯交易所12月份小麦期货合约，同时买入20手芝加哥交易所12月份小麦期货合约，每手为5000蒲式耳。

Later, the Kansas contract fell to 720 cents per bushel, while the Chicago contract fell to 735 cents per bushel. ｜ 随后，堪萨斯合约价格下跌至720美分/蒲式耳，芝加哥合约价格下跌至735美分/蒲式耳。

The closing spread was: ｜ 平仓时的价差为：
$$
735-720=15\text{美分/蒲式耳}
$$
The spread expanded by: ｜ 价差扩大了：
$$
15-10=5\text{美分/蒲式耳}
$$

| Exchange | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss |
| -------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------- |
| Kansas   | Sell at 730      | Buy at 720       | +10 cents      |
| Chicago  | Buy at 740       | Sell at 735      | −5 cents       |

| 交易所 | 建仓    | 平仓    | 每蒲式耳盈亏 |
| ------ | ------- | ------- | ------------ |
| 堪萨斯 | 卖出730 | 买入720 | +10美分      |
| 芝加哥 | 买入740 | 卖出735 | −5美分       |

The net profit per bushel was: ｜ 每蒲式耳净盈利为：
$$
10-5=5\text{美分}
$$
The total profit was: ｜ 总盈利为：
$$
5\times5000\times20
=
500000\text{美分}
$$
Because 100 cents equals 1 US dollar: ｜ 由于100美分等于1美元：
$$
500000\div100
=
5000\text{美元}
$$
The total arbitrage profit was USD 5,000. ｜ 该跨市套利交易总盈利为5000美元。

📌 **When the unit is cents, the final result must be converted into dollars.** ｜ 📌 **题目以美分计价时，最终结果不要忘记换算成美元。**

------

### Examination Topic: Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage ｜ 考点：期现套利

#### I. Classification ｜ 一、分类

##### (I) Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage ｜ （一）正向套利

Cash-and-carry arbitrage is used when the spread between the futures price and the spot price is significantly greater than the carrying cost. ｜ 当期货价格与现货价格之间的价差明显高于持仓费时，可以进行正向套利。

The trader buys the spot commodity and simultaneously sells the related futures contract. ｜ 具体操作是买入现货，同时卖出相关期货合约。
$$
\text{Buy spot}+\text{Sell futures}
$$

##### (II) Reverse Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage ｜ （二）反向套利

Reverse cash-and-carry arbitrage is used when the spread between the futures price and the spot price is significantly lower than the carrying cost. ｜ 当期货价格与现货价格之间的价差明显低于持仓费时，可以进行反向套利。

The trader sells the spot commodity and simultaneously buys the related futures contract. ｜ 具体操作是卖出现货，同时买入相关期货合约。
$$
\text{Sell spot}+\text{Buy futures}
$$

------

#### II. Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage Case ｜ 二、正向期现套利案例

On 30 June, the September sugar futures price on the Zhengzhou Commodity Exchange was RMB 5,200 per tonne, while the spot price of sugar in Zhengzhou was RMB 5,100 per tonne. ｜ 6月30日，郑州商品交易所9月份白糖期货价格为5200元/吨，郑州现货市场白糖价格为5100元/吨。

The futures price was RMB 100 per tonne higher than the spot price. ｜ 期货价格比现货价格高100元/吨。
$$
5200-5100=100\text{元/吨}
$$
The arbitrageur estimated that the total carrying cost was approximately RMB 30 per tonne and concluded that a cash-and-carry arbitrage opportunity existed. ｜ 套利者估算持仓费约为30元/吨，认为期货与现货之间的价差高于持仓费，因此存在正向期现套利机会。

The trader bought spot sugar at RMB 5,100 per tonne and simultaneously sold the September sugar futures contract at RMB 5,200 per tonne. ｜ 因此，套利者以5100元/吨买入白糖现货，同时以5200元/吨卖出9月份白糖期货合约。

If the trader held the positions until maturity and completed delivery, the gross price difference would be RMB 100 per tonne. ｜ 如果套利者将头寸持有至到期并完成交割，获得的毛价差为100元/吨。

After deducting the carrying cost of RMB 30 per tonne, the net profit would be: ｜ 扣除30元/吨的持仓费后，净盈利为：
$$
100-30=70\text{元/吨}
$$
The arbitrageur therefore earned RMB 70 per tonne. ｜ 因此，该期现套利交易每吨盈利70元。

A general expression for the profit is: ｜ 其基本盈利关系可以表示为：
$$
\text{Arbitrage profit}
=
\text{Futures price}
-
\text{Spot price}
-
\text{Carrying cost}
$$

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🔗 **Inter-commodity arbitrage uses price relationships among different but economically related commodities.** ｜ 🔗 **跨品种套利利用不同但具有经济关联的商品之间的价格关系。**

📊 **Related commodities may be substitutes, complements, raw materials or processed products.** ｜ 📊 **相关商品可能属于替代品、互补品，或者存在原材料与制成品关系。**

🌱 **Soybean crush arbitrage buys soybean futures and sells soybean oil and soybean meal futures.** ｜ 🌱 **大豆提油套利买入大豆期货，同时卖出豆油和豆粕期货。**

🔄 **Inter-market arbitrage trades the same commodity and delivery month on different exchanges.** ｜ 🔄 **跨市套利在不同交易所交易同一品种、同一交割月份的期货合约。**

📏 **Inter-market arbitrage profits from favourable changes in the price difference between exchanges.** ｜ 📏 **跨市套利通过两个交易所之间价差的有利变化获利。**

💵 **When prices are quoted in cents, the final profit must be converted into dollars.** ｜ 💵 **价格单位为美分时，最终盈利需要换算成美元。**

📦 **Cash-and-carry arbitrage buys spot and sells futures when the futures premium exceeds carrying costs.** ｜ 📦 **当期货升水明显高于持仓费时，正向套利买入现货并卖出期货。**

↩️ **Reverse cash-and-carry arbitrage sells spot and buys futures when the futures–spot spread is too low.** ｜ ↩️ **当期现价差明显低于持仓费时，反向套利卖出现货并买入期货。**

Worked for 23s

## Section 4: Futures Spread Orders ｜ 第四节 期货价差套利指令

#### I. Concept and Types of Spread Orders ｜ 一、套利指令的概念与种类

##### (I) Concept ｜ （一）概念

To facilitate spread trading, futures exchanges design special spread orders that combine the buy and sell instructions for two or more futures contracts into a single order. ｜ 为了便利套利交易，期货交易所会设计专门的套利指令，将两个或者两个以上期货合约的买卖指令合并为一个组合指令。

By submitting one spread order, an arbitrageur can simultaneously complete transactions in multiple related contracts. ｜ 套利者只需要提交一份套利指令，就可以同时完成多个相关期货合约的买卖操作。

A spread order normally does not require the trader to specify the exact transaction price of each individual contract. Instead, the trader generally specifies the required spread between the contracts. ｜ 套利指令通常不要求交易者分别标明每个期货合约的具体成交价格，而只需要标明相关合约之间的目标价差。

##### (II) Types ｜ （二）种类

Spread orders mainly include: ｜ 套利指令主要包括：

- Market spread orders ｜ 套利市价指令
- Limit spread orders ｜ 套利限价指令
- Calendar spread orders ｜ 跨期套利指令
- Inter-commodity spread orders ｜ 跨品种套利指令

------

#### II. Quotation of Spread Orders ｜ 二、套利指令的报价

When using a spread order, the arbitrageur must understand how the exchange defines the calculation of the spread. ｜ 使用套利指令时，套利者必须了解交易所对价差计算方式的具体规定。

Because the order concerns only the price difference between related contracts, the exchange specifies in advance which contract price should be subtracted from which other contract price. ｜ 由于套利指令只涉及相关合约之间的价差，交易所会事先规定用“哪一个合约价格”减去“哪一个合约价格”。

The general form is: ｜ 一般形式为：
$$
\text{Quoted spread}
=
\text{Price of the first specified leg}
-
\text{Price of the second specified leg}
$$
The trader must follow the exchange’s quotation convention when opening and closing the position. Otherwise, the direction of spread expansion or contraction may be misunderstood. ｜ 交易者在建仓和平仓时，都必须遵循交易所规定的报价顺序，否则可能误判价差扩大或者缩小的方向。

The specific quotation table in the source is severely damaged by OCR, so its detailed formulas are not reproduced here. ｜ 原材料中的具体报价表受到严重的OCR损坏，因此不补充其中无法确认的详细公式。

📌 **Buy spread arbitrage profits when the quoted spread expands.** ｜ 📌 **买入套利在报价价差扩大时获利。**

📌 **Sell spread arbitrage profits when the quoted spread contracts.** ｜ 📌 **卖出套利在报价价差缩小时获利。**

------

#### III. Application of Market and Limit Spread Orders ｜ 三、套利市价指令和套利限价指令的应用

##### (I) Application of a Market Spread Order ｜ （一）套利市价指令的应用

If an arbitrageur wishes to complete the transaction as quickly as possible at the current spread level, a market spread order may be used. ｜ 如果套利者希望按照当前市场的价差水平尽快成交，可以使用套利市价指令。

When submitting a market spread order, the trader does not need to specify the exact spread. The trader only needs to state the types and delivery months of the contracts to be bought and sold. ｜ 使用套利市价指令时，交易者不需要注明具体价差，只需要注明买入和卖出的期货品种及交割月份。

The actual execution spread depends on market conditions at the time the order is executed. ｜ 最终成交价差取决于指令执行时的市场行情。

The main advantage of a market spread order is rapid execution. ｜ 套利市价指令的主要优点是成交速度快。

Its disadvantage is that when market prices change rapidly, the actual execution spread may differ significantly from the trader’s expectation. ｜ 其缺点是，当市场行情发生较大变化时，实际成交价差可能与交易者的预期存在较大差异。

| Feature             | Market spread order           |
| ------------------- | ----------------------------- |
| Spread specified    | No                            |
| Execution speed     | Relatively fast               |
| Execution certainty | Relatively high               |
| Spread certainty    | Relatively low                |
| Main risk           | Unfavourable execution spread |

| 特征         | 套利市价指令       |
| ------------ | ------------------ |
| 是否指定价差 | 不需要             |
| 成交速度     | 较快               |
| 成交确定性   | 较高               |
| 价差确定性   | 较低               |
| 主要风险     | 实际成交价差不理想 |

------

##### (II) Application of a Limit Spread Order ｜ （二）套利限价指令的应用

If an arbitrageur wishes to trade only at an ideal spread, a limit spread order may be used. ｜ 如果套利者希望在某个理想价差水平成交，可以使用套利限价指令。

When using a limit spread order, the trader must specify: ｜ 使用套利限价指令时，交易者需要注明：

- The specific spread ｜ 具体价差
- The contracts to be bought ｜ 买入合约的品种和月份
- The contracts to be sold ｜ 卖出合约的品种和月份

A limit spread order ensures that the transaction will be executed only at the specified spread or a more favourable spread. ｜ 套利限价指令可以保证交易只按照指定价差或者更有利的价差成交。

Its advantage is that the trader can control the execution spread. ｜ 其优点是交易者可以控制实际成交价差。

Its disadvantage is that the order can be executed only when the market spread reaches the specified condition, so immediate execution cannot be guaranteed. ｜ 其缺点是，只有当市场价差达到设定条件时才能成交，因此不能保证立即成交。

| Feature             | Limit spread order            |
| ------------------- | ----------------------------- |
| Spread specified    | Yes                           |
| Execution speed     | Uncertain                     |
| Execution certainty | Relatively low                |
| Spread certainty    | Relatively high               |
| Main risk           | The order may not be executed |

| 特征         | 套利限价指令     |
| ------------ | ---------------- |
| 是否指定价差 | 需要             |
| 成交速度     | 不确定           |
| 成交确定性   | 较低             |
| 价差确定性   | 较高             |
| 主要风险     | 指令可能无法成交 |

📌 **Market order: prioritises execution speed.** ｜ 📌 **市价指令：优先保证成交速度。**

📌 **Limit order: prioritises the execution spread.** ｜ 📌 **限价指令：优先保证成交价差。**

------

#### IV. Futures Quantitative Trading ｜ 四、期货量化交易

##### (I) Classification of Investment Strategies ｜ （一）投资策略分类

###### Passive Investment Strategies ｜ 被动型投资策略

Passive investment strategies mainly refer to strategies that track various market indices. ｜ 被动型投资策略主要是指以追踪各类市场指数为主要投资手段的策略。

These strategies generally follow predetermined portfolio rules rather than actively predicting short-term market movements. ｜ 这类策略通常按照预先确定的组合规则运行，而不是主动预测短期市场行情。

###### Active Investment Strategies ｜ 主动型投资策略

Active investment strategies may include traditional discretionary strategies based on traders’ subjective analysis of futures-market conditions. ｜ 主动型投资策略可以包括依靠交易者对期货行情进行主观分析的传统主观交易策略。

They may also include modern quantitative strategies that rely on computers, data and algorithmic models to conduct systematic trading. ｜ 主动型投资策略还可以包括依靠计算机、数据和算法模型进行程序化交易的现代量化投资策略。

------

##### (II) Definition of Quantitative Trading ｜ （二）量化交易的定义

Quantitative trading uses mathematical and statistical analysis to examine price-movement patterns. ｜ 量化交易通过数学和统计分析研究价格运动规律。

It models data such as: ｜ 量化交易可以对以下数据进行建模：

- Macroeconomic indicators ｜ 宏观经济指标
- Financial data ｜ 财务数据
- Price–volume relationships ｜ 量价关系
- Capital flows and transaction data ｜ 资金流向和交易数据

The purpose is to identify internal relationships among data, continuously generate quantitative investment signals and execute trading instructions strictly through computer systems. ｜ 其目的是寻找不同数据之间的内在关联，持续生成定量化投资信号，并通过计算机系统严格执行交易指令。

The ultimate objective is to obtain relatively stable trading returns. ｜ 其最终目标是获得相对稳定的交易收益。

------

##### (III) Advantages of Futures Quantitative Trading ｜ （三）期货量化交易的优势

###### Scientific and Systematic Methods ｜ 方法科学

Trading decisions generated by quantitative systems are based on theoretical models and supported by data. ｜ 量化交易系统产生的交易决策具有一定的理论依据，并能够得到数据支持。

A quantitative strategy normally needs to be tested using substantial historical or practical data before it is formally applied. ｜ 量化交易策略通常需要经过大量历史数据或者实际数据的检验，才能正式应用。

###### Reducing Cognitive Bias ｜ 避免认知偏差

Quantitative trading decisions are automatically generated by algorithmic models and are less affected by human emotions and subjective judgement. ｜ 量化交易决策由算法模型自动生成，较少受到交易者情绪和主观判断的影响。

It can therefore reduce cognitive biases that commonly occur in discretionary trading. ｜ 因此，量化交易能够在一定程度上克服主观交易中常见的认知偏差。

###### Rapid Response and Strong Execution ｜ 响应迅速、执行力强

Computer systems can rapidly process large amounts of market data and identify potential trading opportunities. ｜ 计算机系统可以快速处理大量市场数据，并及时发现潜在的交易机会。

Once a trading signal is generated, the system can promptly execute the corresponding order. ｜ 交易信号生成后，系统可以及时执行相应的交易指令。

📌 **Quantitative trading does not merely analyse faster; it also executes more consistently.** ｜ 📌 **量化交易不仅分析速度更快，而且执行过程更加稳定一致。**

------

##### (IV) Main Modelling Methods in Futures Quantitative Trading ｜ （四）期货量化交易的主要建模方法

###### Artificial Intelligence ｜ 人工智能

Futures quantitative trading is a complex application involving various fields of knowledge and technology, so it requires substantial intelligent-processing capabilities. ｜ 期货量化交易是一项综合多种知识与技术的复杂应用，因此对智能分析和处理能力具有较高要求。

Artificial intelligence techniques such as machine learning, neural networks and genetic algorithms may be used to design and implement futures quantitative-trading strategies. ｜ 机器学习、神经网络和遗传算法等人工智能技术，都可以用于期货量化交易策略的设计和应用。

------

###### Data Mining ｜ 数据挖掘

Data mining in quantitative trading generally contains three stages. ｜ 量化交易中的数据挖掘通常包括三个阶段。

**Data preparation** ｜ **数据准备**

Required data are selected from relevant sources and integrated into a usable dataset. ｜ 从相关数据源中选取所需数据，并将其整合为可以使用的数据集。

**Pattern discovery** ｜ **规律寻找**

Statistical, computational or algorithmic methods are used to identify patterns hidden in the dataset. ｜ 采用统计、计算或者算法方法，寻找数据集中隐藏的规律。

**Pattern representation** ｜ **规律表示**

The discovered patterns are presented in a form that people can understand, including tables, models or visualisations. ｜ 通过人们可以理解的方式呈现数据规律，例如使用表格、模型或者可视化结果。
$$
\text{Data preparation}
\rightarrow
\text{Pattern discovery}
\rightarrow
\text{Pattern representation}
$$

------

###### Wavelet Analysis ｜ 小波分析

The main function of wavelet analysis in quantitative investment is to process the waveforms of trading signals. ｜ 小波分析在量化投资中的主要作用是对交易信号的波形进行处理。

The price and volume movements of an investment product can be treated as a waveform containing both useful signals and substantial noise. ｜ 投资产品的价格和成交量走势可以被看作一种波形，其中既包含有效信号，也不可避免地包含大量噪声。

Wavelet analysis can perform: ｜ 小波分析可以进行：

- Denoising ｜ 去噪
- Reconstruction ｜ 重构
- Diagnosis ｜ 诊断
- Identification ｜ 识别

These processes may help traders analyse and judge future market movements. ｜ 这些处理有助于交易者分析并判断未来市场走势。

------

###### Support Vector Machines ｜ 支持向量机

A support vector machine maps samples from the original space into a high-dimensional or even infinite-dimensional feature space through a nonlinear mapping. ｜ 支持向量机通过非线性映射，将原始样本空间映射到高维甚至无穷维的特征空间。

This transformation converts a problem that is nonlinearly separable in the original low-dimensional space into a linearly separable problem in the higher-dimensional feature space. ｜ 通过这种升维处理，可以把原低维空间中的非线性可分问题，转化为高维特征空间中的线性可分问题。

Its basic idea can therefore be summarised as: ｜ 其基本思想可以概括为：
$$
\text{Nonlinear low-dimensional problem}
\rightarrow
\text{High-dimensional mapping}
\rightarrow
\text{Linear separation}
$$

------

##### (V) Applications of Futures Quantitative Trading ｜ （五）期货量化交易的应用

###### Statistical Arbitrage ｜ 统计套利

Statistical arbitrage uses historical statistical relationships among asset prices to identify and trade temporary pricing deviations. ｜ 统计套利利用资产价格之间的历史统计规律，识别并交易暂时性的价格偏离。

However, statistical arbitrage is not risk-free arbitrage. ｜ 但是，统计套利并不是无风险套利。

Its main risk is that a statistical relationship observed in historical data may weaken, change or disappear in the future. ｜ 其主要风险在于，根据历史数据得到的统计规律在未来可能减弱、改变甚至消失。

📌 **Historical correlation does not guarantee future continuation.** ｜ 📌 **历史统计关系并不保证未来仍然持续。**

------

###### Algorithmic Trading ｜ 算法交易

Algorithmic trading is also called automated trading, black-box trading or machine trading. ｜ 算法交易又称自动交易、黑盒交易或者机器交易。

It is a trading method in which computer programs generate and execute orders according to predetermined algorithms. ｜ 算法交易是指计算机程序按照预先设计的算法生成并执行交易指令的交易方法。

Depending on the algorithm, the system may determine: ｜ 根据算法设计，系统可以决定：

- Whether to trade ｜ 是否进行交易
- Trading time ｜ 交易时间
- Transaction price ｜ 交易价格
- Transaction quantity ｜ 交易数量

------

###### Passive Algorithmic Trading ｜ 被动型算法交易

Passive algorithmic trading is also called structured algorithmic trading. ｜ 被动型算法交易也称结构型算法交易。

Except for using historical data to estimate the main parameters of the trading model, this type of algorithm generally does not actively select transaction timing and quantity according to real-time market conditions. ｜ 除了利用历史数据估计交易模型的核心参数外，这类算法通常不会根据实时行情主动选择交易时机和交易数量。

It normally executes transactions according to predetermined trading rules. ｜ 它通常按照既定的交易方针和执行规则完成交易。

------

###### Active Algorithmic Trading ｜ 主动型算法交易

Active algorithmic trading is also called opportunistic algorithmic trading. ｜ 主动型算法交易也称机会型算法交易。

Its decisions are primarily made in real time according to current market conditions. ｜ 这类算法主要根据实时市场状况作出交易决策。

It determines whether to trade, how much to trade and at what price to trade. ｜ 系统会根据市场变化判断是否交易、交易数量以及交易价格。

------

###### Hybrid Algorithmic Trading ｜ 综合型算法交易

Hybrid algorithmic trading combines the characteristics of passive and active algorithmic trading. ｜ 综合型算法交易是被动型算法交易与主动型算法交易的有机结合。

It follows predetermined execution rules while also making certain adjustments according to real-time market conditions. ｜ 它既遵循预先设定的执行规则，也会根据实时市场情况作出一定调整。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🧾 **A spread order combines transactions in two or more futures contracts into one instruction.** ｜ 🧾 **套利指令将两个或者多个期货合约的买卖操作合并为一份指令。**

📏 **Spread quotation depends on the subtraction order specified by the exchange.** ｜ 📏 **套利价差的报价取决于交易所规定的合约相减顺序。**

📈 **Buy spread arbitrage benefits from spread expansion, while sell spread arbitrage benefits from spread contraction.** ｜ 📈 **买入套利在价差扩大时获利，卖出套利在价差缩小时获利。**

⚡ **A market spread order prioritises rapid execution but cannot guarantee the exact spread.** ｜ ⚡ **套利市价指令优先保证快速成交，但不能保证具体成交价差。**

🎯 **A limit spread order controls the execution spread but cannot guarantee immediate execution.** ｜ 🎯 **套利限价指令可以控制成交价差，但不能保证立即成交。**

💻 **Quantitative trading uses mathematical models, data and computer systems to generate and execute trading signals.** ｜ 💻 **量化交易利用数学模型、数据和计算机系统生成并执行交易信号。**

🧠 **Its main modelling tools include artificial intelligence, data mining, wavelet analysis and support vector machines.** ｜ 🧠 **其主要建模方法包括人工智能、数据挖掘、小波分析和支持向量机。**

⚠️ **Statistical arbitrage is not risk-free because historical statistical relationships may fail in the future.** ｜ ⚠️ **统计套利并非无风险套利，因为历史统计规律未来可能失效。**

🤖 **Algorithmic trading may be passive, active or hybrid.** ｜ 🤖 **算法交易可以分为被动型、主动型和综合型。**

# Chapter 6: Interest Rate Futures ｜ 第六章 利率期货

This chapter accounts for approximately 8% of the examination score. ｜ 本章分值占比约为8%。

## Section 1: Interest Rate Futures and Their Price Determinants ｜ 第一节 利率期货及其价格影响因素

### Examination Topic: Interest Rate Futures and Their Quotations ｜ 考点：利率期货及其报价

#### I. Definition of Interest Rate Futures ｜ 一、利率期货的概念

Interest rate futures are futures contracts whose underlying instruments are interest-rate-related financial instruments, such as certificates of deposit, interbank funds, short-term government bonds and medium- or long-term government bonds. ｜ 利率期货是指以定期存单、同业拆借资金、短期国债以及中长期国债等利率类金融工具为标的物的期货品种。

------

#### II. Classification of Interest Rate Futures ｜ 二、利率期货的分类

##### (I) Classification by Contract Term ｜ （一）按照合约期限分类

###### Short-Term Interest Rate Futures ｜ 短期利率期货

The underlying interest rate generally has a maturity of one year or less. ｜ 短期利率期货的交易标的主要是期限在一年以内，含一年的短期利率。

###### Medium- and Long-Term Interest Rate Futures ｜ 中长期利率期货

The underlying instruments are mainly medium- and long-term government bonds with maturities of more than one year. ｜ 中长期利率期货的交易标的主要是期限在一年以上的中长期国债。

##### (II) Classification of Medium- and Long-Term Government Bond Futures ｜ （二）中长期国债期货的分类

Medium-term government bond futures generally have maturities of 1–10 years. ｜ 中期国债期货的期限通常为1～10年。

Long-term government bond futures generally have maturities of 10–30 years. ｜ 长期国债期货的期限通常为10～30年。

Ultra-long-term government bond futures generally have maturities of more than 30 years. ｜ 超长期国债期货的期限通常为30年以上。

##### (III) Domestic Products Listed in the Source ｜ （三）原材料列出的国内品种

The source lists the following government bond futures products: ｜ 原材料列出的国内国债期货品种包括：

- Two-year government bond futures ｜ 2年期国债期货
- Five-year government bond futures ｜ 5年期国债期货
- Ten-year government bond futures ｜ 10年期国债期货

------

#### III. Quotations of Short-Term Interest Rate Futures ｜ 三、短期利率期货的报价

##### (I) Typical Futures Contracts ｜ （一）典型期货合约

Typical short-term interest rate futures contracts include: ｜ 典型的短期利率期货合约包括：

- Three-month Eurodollar futures ｜ 3个月欧洲美元期货
- Three-month euro interbank interest rate futures ｜ 3个月欧元拆借利率期货

------

##### (II) Quotation Method ｜ （二）报价方式

Short-term interest rate futures generally use an index quotation method. ｜ 短期利率期货通常采用指数报价方式。

The quoted index equals 100 minus the annual interest rate expressed without the percentage sign. ｜ 期货报价等于100减去不带百分号的年利率数值。
$$
\text{Futures quotation}
=
100-\text{Annual interest rate}
$$
For example, if the annual interest rate is 2.5%, the futures quotation is: ｜ 例如，当年利率为2.5%时，期货报价为：
$$
100-2.5=97.500
$$
📌 **Interest rate rises → futures quotation falls.** ｜ 📌 **利率上升 → 期货报价下降。**

📌 **Interest rate falls → futures quotation rises.** ｜ 📌 **利率下降 → 期货报价上升。**

------

##### (III) CME Three-Month Eurodollar Futures ｜ （三）CME三个月欧洲美元期货

###### Quotation Basis ｜ 报价基础

The contract quotation is based on a three-month Eurodollar interbank interest rate index. ｜ 该合约采用3个月欧洲美元拆借利率指数进行报价。

The quotation equals 100 minus the annual interest rate calculated on a 360-day basis. ｜ 期货报价等于100减去按照一年360天计算的不带百分号的年利率。

For example: ｜ 例如：
$$
\text{Annual interest rate}=2.5\%
$$

------

###### Contract Size ｜ 合约规模

According to the textbook example, the notional principal of one contract is USD 1,000,000, and the term of the underlying deposit is three months. ｜ 根据教材案例，一手合约的名义本金为100万美元，标的是期限为3个月的欧洲美元定期存款。

------

###### Value of One Basis Point ｜ 一个基点的合约价值

One basis point equals 0.01 percentage points in the annual interest rate and corresponds to a change of 0.01 in the futures quotation. ｜ 一个基点等于年利率的0.01个百分点，对应期货报价变动0.01。

The value of one basis point is: ｜ 一个基点对应的合约价值为：
$$
1{,}000{,}000
\times0.01\%
\times\frac{3}{12}
=
25\text{美元}
$$
Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
0.01\text{ quotation point}
=
1\text{ basis point}
=
25\text{美元}
$$

------

###### Minimum Price Fluctuation ｜ 最小变动价位

For the nearest-expiry contract, the minimum price fluctuation is one-quarter of a basis point, or 0.0025 quotation points. ｜ 最近到期合约的最小变动价位为四分之一个基点，即报价变动0.0025。

Its corresponding contract value is: ｜ 其对应的合约价值为：
$$
25\times\frac14=6.25\text{美元}
$$
For other listed contracts, the minimum price fluctuation is one-half of a basis point, or 0.005 quotation points. ｜ 其他挂牌合约的最小变动价位为二分之一个基点，即报价变动0.005。

Its corresponding contract value is: ｜ 其对应的合约价值为：
$$
25\times\frac12=12.50\text{美元}
$$

| Quotation change | Basis-point change | Contract-value change |
| ---------------- | ------------------ | --------------------- |
| 0.0100           | 1 basis point      | USD 25.00             |
| 0.0050           | 1/2 basis point    | USD 12.50             |
| 0.0025           | 1/4 basis point    | USD 6.25              |

| 报价变动 | 基点变动  | 合约价值变动 |
| -------- | --------- | ------------ |
| 0.0100   | 1个基点   | 25美元       |
| 0.0050   | 1/2个基点 | 12.50美元    |
| 0.0025   | 1/4个基点 | 6.25美元     |

------

##### (IV) Conversion Between Quotation and Discounted Value ｜ （四）成交报价与贴现值的换算

Suppose the futures quotation is 98.580. ｜ 假设期货成交报价为98.580。

The corresponding annual discount rate is: ｜ 对应的年贴现率为：
$$
(100-98.580)\%
=
1.420\%
$$
For a three-month deposit with a notional principal of USD 1,000,000, the theoretical discounted value is: ｜ 对于名义本金为100万美元、期限为3个月的存款，其理论贴现值为：
$$
1{,}000{,}000
\times
\left(
1-1.420\%\times\frac{3}{12}
\right)
$$
原材料中的“1.429%”和“994650美元”与报价98.580不一致。按照其给出的公式，正确结果应为 **1.420%和996450美元**。

------

##### (V) Relationship Between Interest Rates and Futures Prices ｜ （五）市场利率与期货价格的关系

When market interest rates rise, the quotation of three-month Eurodollar futures generally falls. ｜ 当市场利率上升时，3个月欧洲美元期货价格通常下降。

When market interest rates fall, the futures quotation generally rises. ｜ 当市场利率下降时，3个月欧洲美元期货价格通常上涨。
$$
\text{Interest rate}\uparrow
\quad\Rightarrow\quad
\text{Futures quotation}\downarrow
$$
📌 **Interest rate and short-term interest rate futures quotation move in opposite directions.** ｜ 📌 **市场利率与短期利率期货报价呈反向变动关系。**

------

##### (VI) Profit-and-Loss Case ｜ （六）盈亏计算案例

Suppose an investor buys one three-month Eurodollar futures contract at 98.580 and later sells it at 99.000. ｜ 假设投资者以98.580买入一手3个月欧洲美元期货，之后以99.000卖出平仓。

The quotation increased by: ｜ 报价上涨了：
$$
99.000-98.580=0.420
$$
A quotation change of 0.01 is worth USD 25, so a change of 0.420 is equivalent to 42 basis points. ｜ 报价每变动0.01对应25美元，因此报价变动0.420相当于42个基点。
$$
42\times25
=
1050\text{美元}
$$
It can also be calculated using the shortcut given in the source: ｜ 也可以使用原材料给出的简便方法：
$$
(99.000-98.580)\times2500
=
1050\text{美元}
$$
The investor earned USD 1,050. ｜ 该投资者盈利1050美元。

📌 **One full quotation point is worth USD 2,500.** ｜ 📌 **报价每变动完整的1点，对应合约价值变动2500美元。**

------

##### (VII) Settlement of Short-Term Interest Rate Futures ｜ （七）短期利率期货的交割

The buyer and seller do not physically deliver cash deposits or certificates of deposit. ｜ 买卖双方并不实际交付现金或者定期存单。

Instead, the difference between the final settlement price and the relevant previous settlement price is transferred in cash. ｜ 交易双方根据交割结算价与相关结算价格之间的差额进行现金划转。

Therefore, short-term interest rate futures generally use cash settlement. ｜ 因此，短期利率期货通常采用现金交割。

------

#### IV. Quotations of Government Bond Futures ｜ 四、国债期货的报价

##### (I) Quotation in China ｜ （一）中国国债期货的报价

Government bond futures in China use a net-price quotation based on a government bond with a face value of RMB 100. ｜ 我国国债期货采用面值100元国债的净价报价方式，报价中不包含持有期间的应计利息。

Prices after the decimal point are expressed using the decimal system. ｜ 价格小数点以后采用十进位制。

For example, a quotation of 97.335 means that the futures price corresponding to a government bond with a face value of RMB 100 is RMB 97.335. ｜ 例如，报价97.335表示面值100元的国债期货价格为97.335元。

------

##### (II) Quotation of US Treasury Futures ｜ （二）美国国债期货的报价

A US Treasury futures quotation may be written as: ｜ 美国国债期货报价可以表示为：
$$
118'222
$$
or: ｜ 或者：
$$
118\text{-}222
$$

###### First Part: Full Points ｜ 第一部分：整数点

The number 118 represents 118 full price points. ｜ 数字118表示118个完整的价格点。

For a USD 100,000 contract, one full point is worth: ｜ 对于面值10万美元的一手合约，完整的1点价值为：
$$
\frac{100{,}000}{100}
=
1000\text{美元}
$$

------

###### Second Part: Thirty-Seconds ｜ 第二部分：三十二分位

The next two digits range from 00 to 31 and use a base-32 quotation system. ｜ 后两位数字的取值范围为00～31，采用三十二进位制。

For example: ｜ 例如：
$$
22=\frac{22}{32}\text{点}
$$

------

###### Third Part: Fractions of One Thirty-Second ｜ 第三部分：三十二分之一的细分

The final digit generally uses 0, 2, 5 or 7. ｜ 最后一位通常使用0、2、5、7四个数字。

| Final digit | Meaning         |
| ----------- | --------------- |
| 0           | $0$ of $1/32$   |
| 2           | $1/4$ of $1/32$ |
| 5           | $2/4$ of $1/32$ |
| 7           | $3/4$ of $1/32$ |

| 最后一位 | 含义                     |
| -------- | ------------------------ |
| 0        | $1/32$点的0              |
| 2        | $1/32$点的$1/4$          |
| 5        | $1/32$点的$2/4$，即$1/2$ |
| 7        | $1/32$点的$3/4$          |

Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
118\text{-}222
=
118+\frac{22}{32}+\frac{1}{4}\times\frac{1}{32}
$$

------

##### (III) Profit-and-Loss Case for US Treasury Futures ｜ （三）美国国债期货盈亏案例

Suppose an investor buys one ten-year US Treasury futures contract at 118-215 and later sells it at 119-237. ｜ 假设投资者以118-215买入一手10年期美国国债期货，之后以119-237卖出平仓。

The change in full points is: ｜ 整数点的变化为：
$$
119-118=1
$$
The change in thirty-seconds is: ｜ 三十二分位的变化为：
$$
\frac{23-21}{32}
=
\frac{2}{32}
$$
The last digit changes from 5 to 7. The digit 5 represents $2/4$ of $1/32$, while 7 represents $3/4$ of $1/32$. ｜ 最后一位由5变为7。其中5代表$1/32$点的$2/4$，7代表$1/32$点的$3/4$。

Therefore, the additional change is: ｜ 因此，这一部分的变化为：
$$
\left(\frac34-\frac24\right)\times\frac1{32}
=
\frac1{128}
$$
The total profit is: ｜ 总盈利为：
$$
\left[
(119-118)
+
\frac{23-21}{32}
+
\left(\frac34-\frac24\right)\frac1{32}
\right]
\times1000
$$
The investor earned approximately USD 1,070.31. ｜ 该投资者盈利约1070.31美元。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

📊 **Interest rate futures use interest-rate-related financial instruments as their underlying instruments.** ｜ 📊 **利率期货以利率类金融工具作为交易标的。**

⏳ **Short-term interest rate futures generally involve maturities of one year or less, while medium- and long-term futures mainly use government bonds with maturities of more than one year.** ｜ ⏳ **短期利率期货的期限通常不超过一年，中长期利率期货主要以一年以上的国债为标的。**

🔢 **Short-term interest rate futures quotation equals 100 minus the annual interest rate.** ｜ 🔢 **短期利率期货报价等于100减去年利率数值。**

↔️ **Market interest rates and short-term interest rate futures quotations move in opposite directions.** ｜ ↔️ **市场利率与短期利率期货报价呈反向变动关系。**

💵 **For the textbook’s three-month Eurodollar contract, one basis point is worth USD 25.** ｜ 💵 **在教材的3个月欧洲美元合约中，一个基点对应25美元。**

🧮 **A quotation change of one full point corresponds to USD 2,500.** ｜ 🧮 **报价完整变动1点，对应合约价值变动2500美元。**

💳 **Short-term interest rate futures generally use cash settlement rather than physical delivery of deposits.** ｜ 💳 **短期利率期货通常采用现金交割，不实际交付存款。**

🇨🇳 **Chinese government bond futures use decimal net-price quotations per RMB 100 face value.** ｜ 🇨🇳 **我国国债期货采用每100元面值的十进制净价报价。**

🇺🇸 **US Treasury futures quotations use full points, thirty-seconds and fractions of one thirty-second.** ｜ 🇺🇸 **美国国债期货报价由整数点、三十二分位及其细分组成。**
