- 2. 可交割国债票面利率等于国债期货合约标的票面利率，转换因子等于1 ; <!-- PDF 第 69 页 --> (= - 3. 可交割国债票面利率低于国债期货合约标的票面利率，转换因子小于1。 #### 四、发票价格 ##### (一) 概念 用可交割国债的转换因子乘以期货交割结算价可得到转换后该国债的交割价格 (净价)，用国债交割净价加上持有国债期间的应计利息收入即可得到国债期货交割时卖方出让可交割国债时应得到的实际现金价格 〈全价)，该价格为可交割国债的出让价格，即国债交庆全价，也称为发票价格。 ##### (二) 发票价格计算公式 发票价格 〈国债交割全价) =国债期货交割结算价x交割国债转换因子+应计利息 ##### (三) 发票价格计算公式 中金所规定, 国债期货交割时, 应计利息的日计数基准为“实际持有天数/实际计乱天数”，每100元可交割国债的应计利息计算公式如下: 可交割国债票面利率x100 第二交割日-上一付息日每年付息次数 当天付息周期实际天数注意: 应计利息的计算结果应进行四舍五入至小数点后七位。 #### 五、最便宜可交割债券 (CTD) 的相关概念 ##### (一) 概念 在一篮子可交割国债的交割制度下，发行期限和剩余期限在一定范围内的国债都可以参与交割。由于期货合约的卖方拥有可交割国债的选择权，卖方一般会选择最便宜、交割成本最低、对己方最有利的可交割国债进行交割，该债券就是最便宜可交割国债，通常称为最便宜可交齐券 (CTD)。 (=) 寻找最便宜可交割国债 隐含回购利率最高的国债就是最便宜可交割国债。 mn oe gp (OE RT RET) ARMIES 365国债购买价格 SH CATHY式中，国债购买价格为市场价格 OMT) 加上应计利息的全价。显然二隐含回购利率越高的国债价格越便宜。

### Development of Interest Rate Futures ｜ 利率期货的发展历程

#### I. Birth of Interest Rate Futures ｜ 一、利率期货的诞生

On 20 October 1975, the Chicago Board of Trade launched the first interest rate futures contract in history—the Government National Mortgage Association mortgage-backed certificate futures contract. This marked the birth of interest rate futures. ｜ 1975年10月20日，芝加哥期货交易所推出了历史上第一份利率期货合约——政府国民抵押协会抵押支持凭证期货合约，标志着利率期货正式诞生。

#### II. Launch of US Treasury Bill Futures ｜ 二、美国短期国债期货的推出

In January 1976, the International Monetary Market of the Chicago Mercantile Exchange launched futures based on three-month US Treasury bills, with a term of 13 weeks or 91 days. ｜ 1976年1月，芝加哥商业交易所国际货币市场推出了以3个月期美国短期国债为标的的期货品种，其期限为13周或者91天。

#### III. Launch of Long-Term US Treasury Bond Futures ｜ 三、美国长期国债期货的推出

In August 1977, the Chicago Board of Trade launched long-term US Treasury bond futures. ｜ 1977年8月，芝加哥期货交易所推出了美国长期国债期货合约。

#### IV. Launch of Eurodollar Futures ｜ 四、欧洲美元期货的推出

In December 1981, the International Monetary Market of the Chicago Mercantile Exchange launched three-month Eurodollar futures. According to the source, this product was among the first interest rate futures to adopt cash settlement. ｜ 1981年12月，芝加哥商业交易所国际货币市场推出了3个月期欧洲美元期货合约。根据原材料，该品种率先采用现金交割方式。

------

### Examination Topic: Factors Affecting Interest Rate Futures Prices ｜ 考点：利率期货价格的影响因素

#### I. Direction of Price Changes ｜ 一、变动方向

Both short-term interest rate futures and government bond futures generally move in the opposite direction to market interest rates. ｜ 短期利率期货和国债期货的价格通常都与市场利率呈反向变动关系。
$$
\text{Market interest rate}\uparrow
\Rightarrow
\text{Interest rate futures price}\downarrow
$$
The basic reason is that when market interest rates rise, the present value of the fixed future cash flows of existing bonds falls. When market interest rates decline, the present value of those cash flows rises. ｜ 其基本原因是：市场利率上升时，现有债券固定未来现金流的现值下降；市场利率下降时，固定现金流的现值上升。

------

#### II. Main Influencing Factors ｜ 二、主要影响因素

##### (I) Policy Factors ｜ （一）政策因素

###### Fiscal Policy and Monetary Policy ｜ 财政政策与货币政策

Expansionary fiscal policy generally increases aggregate demand and the demand for funds, which tends to push market interest rates upward. ｜ 扩张性财政政策通常会增加社会总需求和资金需求，从而推动市场利率上升。

Contractionary fiscal policy generally reduces aggregate demand and the demand for funds, which tends to push market interest rates downward. ｜ 紧缩性财政政策通常会减少社会总需求和资金需求，从而推动市场利率下降。

Expansionary monetary policy increases the supply of money and liquidity, which generally causes market interest rates to decline. ｜ 扩张性货币政策增加货币和流动性供给，通常会使市场利率下降。

Contractionary monetary policy reduces the supply of money and liquidity, which generally causes market interest rates to rise. ｜ 紧缩性货币政策减少货币和流动性供给，通常会使市场利率上升。

| Policy          | Expansionary policy         | Contractionary policy       |
| --------------- | --------------------------- | --------------------------- |
| Fiscal policy   | Interest rates tend to rise | Interest rates tend to fall |
| Monetary policy | Interest rates tend to fall | Interest rates tend to rise |

| 政策类型 | 扩张政策         | 紧缩政策         |
| -------- | ---------------- | ---------------- |
| 财政政策 | 市场利率趋于上升 | 市场利率趋于下降 |
| 货币政策 | 市场利率趋于下降 | 市场利率趋于上升 |

📌 **Fiscal expansion and monetary expansion have different effects on interest rates.** ｜ 📌 **扩张性财政政策与扩张性货币政策对市场利率的影响方向不同。**

------

##### (II) Economic Factors ｜ （二）经济因素

###### Economic Cycle ｜ 经济周期

Changes in the economic cycle affect investment, consumption and the demand for funds, thereby influencing market interest rates. ｜ 经济周期变化会影响投资、消费和社会资金需求，进而影响市场利率。

During an economic expansion, investment and financing demand normally increase, which tends to push interest rates upward. ｜ 在经济扩张阶段，投资和融资需求通常增加，市场利率趋于上升。

During an economic slowdown or recession, the demand for funds normally decreases, which tends to push interest rates downward. ｜ 在经济放缓或者衰退阶段，社会资金需求通常减少，市场利率趋于下降。

###### Economic Growth Rate ｜ 经济增长速度

When the economy grows rapidly, demand for funds is strong and market interest rates tend to rise. ｜ 经济增长速度较快时，社会资金需求旺盛，市场利率水平通常上升。

When economic growth is slow, demand for funds is relatively weak and market interest rates tend to decline. ｜ 经济增长速度缓慢时，社会资金需求相对减少，市场利率水平通常下降。

###### Inflation Rate ｜ 通货膨胀率

When the inflation rate rises, market interest rates generally rise as well. ｜ 通货膨胀率上升时，市场利率通常也会上升。

When the inflation rate falls, market interest rates generally decline. ｜ 通货膨胀率下降时，市场利率通常也会下降。
$$
\text{Inflation}\uparrow
\Rightarrow
\text{Market interest rate}\uparrow
\Rightarrow
\text{Interest rate futures price}\downarrow
$$

------

##### (III) Interest Rate Levels in Major Global Economies ｜ （三）全球主要经济体的利率水平

Interest rates in major global economies affect international capital flows, exchange rates and domestic monetary conditions, thereby influencing domestic market interest rates. ｜ 全球主要经济体的利率水平会影响国际资本流动、汇率以及国内货币环境，进而影响本国市场利率。

For example, a substantial rise in interest rates in a major economy may attract international capital and exert pressure on interest rates in other markets. ｜ 例如，主要经济体利率大幅上升，可能吸引国际资本流入，并对其他市场的利率水平形成影响。

------

##### (IV) Other Factors ｜ （四）其他因素

Expectations regarding future economic conditions, consumer income, consumer credit and other market factors may also affect changes in market interest rates. ｜ 人们对未来经济形势的预期、消费者收入水平、消费者信贷状况以及其他市场因素，也会在一定程度上影响市场利率变化。

------

## Section 2: Government Bond Futures and Their Applications ｜ 第二节 国债期货及其应用

### Examination Topic: Government Bond Futures ｜ 考点：国债期货

#### I. Definition of Government Bond Futures ｜ 一、国债期货的概念

Government bond futures are futures contracts whose underlying instruments are government bonds issued by sovereign states. ｜ 国债期货是指以主权国家发行的国债作为期货合约标的物的期货品种。

Government bond futures prices are mainly affected by changes in market interest rates. Because bond prices and interest rates generally move in opposite directions, government bond futures prices also normally move inversely to market interest rates. ｜ 国债期货价格主要受到市场利率变化的影响。由于债券价格与市场利率通常呈反向关系，因此国债期货价格一般也与市场利率反向变动。

------

#### II. Main Government Bond Futures Contracts in China ｜ 二、我国主要国债期货合约

The source lists two-year, five-year and ten-year government bond futures. Some figures in the original table are damaged by OCR, so only clearly identifiable information is retained below. ｜ 原材料列出了2年期、5年期和10年期国债期货。由于原表中的部分数值受到OCR损坏，下面只保留能够明确辨认的内容。

Quarterly contract months follow the March, June, September and December cycle. ｜ 季月合约按照3月、6月、9月和12月循环。

According to the source, normal trading hours are 9:30–11:30 and 13:00–15:15, while trading on the last trading day ends at 11:30. ｜ 根据原材料，正常交易时间为9:30—11:30和13:00—15:15，最后交易日的交易时间为9:30—11:30。

The last trading day is the second Friday of the contract’s expiry month, and delivery is conducted through physical delivery. ｜ 最后交易日为合约到期月份的第二个星期五，国债期货采用实物交割方式。

| -------- | ------------ | ----------- |
| 2年期    | 不超过5年    | 1.5～2.25年 |

| 10年期   | 不超过10年   | 不低于6.5年 |

------

#### III. Conversion Factor ｜ 三、转换因子

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

Government bond futures adopt a basket-delivery system, meaning that multiple government bonds meeting specified conditions may be used for physical delivery. ｜ 国债期货采用一揽子可交割国债制度，即一系列符合规定条件的国债都可以用于期货实物交割。

These deliverable bonds may have different remaining maturities and coupon rates. Therefore, a conversion ratio is needed to compare each deliverable bond with the notional standard government bond underlying the futures contract. ｜ 由于各可交割国债的剩余期限和票面利率可能不同，因此需要确定每种可交割国债与期货合约名义标准国债之间的转换比例。

This ratio is called the conversion factor. ｜ 这一转换比例就是转换因子。

The conversion factor is essentially the present value of all remaining cash flows from a deliverable government bond with a face value of RMB 1, discounted using the nominal coupon rate specified in the futures contract. ｜ 转换因子实质上是：将面值1元的可交割国债在剩余期限内的全部现金流，按照国债期货合约标的票面利率进行贴现后得到的现值。

------

##### (II) Determination of the Conversion Factor ｜ （二）转换因子的判断

If the coupon rate of the deliverable government bond is higher than the nominal coupon rate of the futures contract, the conversion factor is greater than 1. ｜ 如果可交割国债的票面利率高于国债期货合约标的票面利率，则转换因子大于1。
$$
\text{Deliverable bond coupon rate}
>
\text{Contract coupon rate}
\Rightarrow
\text{Conversion factor}>1
$$
If the coupon rate of the deliverable bond equals the nominal coupon rate of the futures contract, the conversion factor equals 1. ｜ 如果可交割国债的票面利率等于国债期货合约标的票面利率，则转换因子等于1。
$$
\text{Deliverable bond coupon rate}
=
\text{Contract coupon rate}
\Rightarrow
\text{Conversion factor}=1
$$
If the coupon rate of the deliverable bond is lower than the nominal coupon rate of the futures contract, the conversion factor is less than 1. ｜ 如果可交割国债的票面利率低于国债期货合约标的票面利率，则转换因子小于1。
$$
\text{Deliverable bond coupon rate}
<
\text{Contract coupon rate}
\Rightarrow
\text{Conversion factor}<1
$$

| Coupon-rate comparison                    | Conversion factor |
| ----------------------------------------- | ----------------- |
| Deliverable bond coupon > contract coupon | $>1$              |
| Deliverable bond coupon = contract coupon | $=1$              |
| Deliverable bond coupon < contract coupon | $<1$              |

| 票面利率比较                         | 转换因子 |
| ------------------------------------ | -------- |
| 可交割国债票面利率＞合约标的票面利率 | $>1$     |
| 可交割国债票面利率＝合约标的票面利率 | $=1$     |
| 可交割国债票面利率＜合约标的票面利率 | $<1$     |

------

#### IV. Invoice Price ｜ 四、发票价格

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

The futures delivery settlement price multiplied by the conversion factor gives the converted delivery price, which is the clean price of the deliverable government bond. ｜ 国债期货交割结算价乘以可交割国债的转换因子，可以得到经过转换后的国债交割价格，即交割净价。

The accrued interest on the bond is then added to the clean delivery price to obtain the actual cash amount received by the seller at delivery. ｜ 在交割净价的基础上加上国债应计利息，即可得到卖方交付国债时实际收到的现金金额。

This total price is called the delivery full price or invoice price. ｜ 这一国债交割全价也称为发票价格。

##### (II) Formula ｜ （二）计算公式

$$
\text{Invoice price}
=
\text{Futures delivery settlement price}
\times
\text{Conversion factor}
+
\text{Accrued interest}
$$

📌 **Futures settlement price × conversion factor gives the clean price; adding accrued interest gives the full price.** ｜ 📌 **交割结算价乘以转换因子得到净价，加上应计利息得到全价。**

------

##### (III) Accrued Interest ｜ （三）应计利息

According to the source, the day-count convention for accrued interest in government bond futures delivery is actual days held divided by the actual number of days in the coupon period. ｜ 根据原材料，国债期货交割时应计利息采用“实际持有天数／实际计息天数”的日计数基准。

For each RMB 100 face value of a deliverable government bond, accrued interest may be expressed as: ｜ 对于每100元面值的可交割国债，应计利息可以表示为：
$$
\text{Accrued interest}
=
\frac{\text{Coupon rate}\times100}
{\text{Number of coupon payments per year}}
\times
\frac{
\text{Second delivery date}-\text{Previous coupon date}
}{
\text{Actual days in the current coupon period}
}
$$
The source states that the result should be rounded to seven decimal places. ｜ 根据原材料，应计利息的计算结果需要四舍五入至小数点后七位。

------

#### V. Cheapest-to-Deliver Bond ｜ 五、最便宜可交割国债

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

Under the basket-delivery system, various government bonds with eligible issue maturities and remaining maturities may be used for delivery. ｜ 在一篮子可交割国债制度下，只要发行期限和剩余期限符合规定，多种国债都可以参与期货交割。

The seller of a government bond futures contract has the right to choose which eligible bond to deliver. ｜ 国债期货合约的卖方拥有选择具体交割国债的权利。

The seller normally chooses the bond with the lowest delivery cost and the greatest economic advantage to the seller. ｜ 卖方通常会选择交割成本最低、对自身最有利的可交割国债进行交割。

This bond is called the cheapest-to-deliver bond, or CTD. ｜ 这一债券称为最便宜可交割国债，简称最便宜可交割券或者CTD。

------

##### (II) Identifying the Cheapest-to-Deliver Bond ｜ （二）寻找最便宜可交割国债

The deliverable government bond with the highest implied repo rate is generally the cheapest-to-deliver bond. ｜ 隐含回购利率最高的可交割国债，通常就是最便宜可交割国债。
$$
\text{Highest implied repo rate}
\Rightarrow
\text{Cheapest-to-deliver bond}
$$
The bond purchase price used in the calculation is the full market price, which equals the quoted clean price plus accrued interest. ｜ 隐含回购利率计算中的国债购买价格采用市场全价，即市场净价加上应计利息。
$$
\text{Bond full price}
=
\text{Market clean price}
+
\text{Accrued interest}
$$
The detailed implied repo rate formula in the source is seriously damaged by OCR, so it is not reconstructed here. ｜ 原材料中的隐含回购利率具体公式受到严重的OCR损坏，因此不自行补造。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

📅 **The first interest rate futures contract was launched by the CBOT in 1975.** ｜ 📅 **历史上第一份利率期货合约由芝加哥期货交易所于1975年推出。**

↔️ **Short-term interest rate futures and government bond futures generally move inversely to market interest rates.** ｜ ↔️ **短期利率期货和国债期货价格通常都与市场利率反向变动。**

🏛️ **Expansionary fiscal policy tends to raise interest rates, while expansionary monetary policy tends to lower them.** ｜ 🏛️ **扩张性财政政策通常推动利率上升，扩张性货币政策通常推动利率下降。**

📈 **Faster economic growth and higher inflation generally place upward pressure on market interest rates.** ｜ 📈 **经济增长加快和通货膨胀率上升，通常会对市场利率形成上行压力。**

📄 **Government bond futures use sovereign government bonds as their underlying instruments.** ｜ 📄 **国债期货以主权国家发行的国债作为标的物。**

🔄 **A conversion factor standardises deliverable bonds with different coupons and maturities.** ｜ 🔄 **转换因子用于统一不同票面利率和剩余期限可交割国债的交割价值。**

🧾 **Invoice price equals the futures delivery settlement price multiplied by the conversion factor, plus accrued interest.** ｜ 🧾 **发票价格等于交割结算价乘以转换因子，再加上应计利息。**

🏷️ **The seller normally delivers the eligible bond with the lowest delivery cost.** ｜ 🏷️ **卖方通常会选择交割成本最低的合格国债进行交割。**

💹 **The deliverable bond with the highest implied repo rate is generally the cheapest-to-deliver bond.** ｜ 💹 **隐含回购利率最高的可交割国债通常就是最便宜可交割国债。**

#### VI. Calculation of the Theoretical Price of Government Bond Futures ｜ 六、国债期货理论价格计算

##### (I) Basic Formula ｜ （一）基本公式

The theoretical price of government bond futures is generally calculated using the cost-of-carry model. ｜ 国债期货的理论价格通常可以运用持有成本模型计算。
$$
\text{Theoretical futures price}
=
\text{Spot bond price}
+
\text{Cost of carrying the bond}
$$
The cost of carrying a government bond equals the financing cost of holding the bond minus the interest income received during the holding period. ｜ 国债的持有成本等于持有国债的资金占用成本减去持有期间获得的利息收入。
$$
\text{Cost of carry}
=
\text{Financing cost}
-
\text{Interest income}
$$
Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
\text{Theoretical futures price}
=
\text{Spot bond price}
+
\text{Financing cost}
-
\text{Interest income}
$$

------

##### (II) Formula Using the Cheapest-to-Deliver Bond ｜ （二）使用最便宜可交割国债计算

In practice, the clean price of the cheapest-to-deliver bond is generally used as a substitute for the spot bond price. ｜ 在实际计算中，通常使用最便宜可交割国债的净价代替国债现货价格。

Assuming that the bond does not make a coupon payment between the valuation date and the delivery date, the theoretical futures price may be expressed as: ｜ 假设该国债从计算日至交割日期间不发生付息，国债期货理论价格可以表示为：
$$
\text{Theoretical futures price}
=
\frac{
\text{CTD clean price}
+
\text{Financing cost}
-
\text{Interest income}
}{
\text{Conversion factor}
}
$$
The financing cost should be calculated on the full price of the deliverable government bond rather than only on its clean price. ｜ 持有国债的资金占用成本应当按照可交割国债的全价计算，而不是仅按照净价计算。
$$
\text{Bond full price}
=
\text{Bond clean price}
+
\text{Accrued interest}
$$
According to the source, the interest income during the holding period refers to the accrued interest from the previous coupon-payment date to the second delivery date. ｜ 根据原材料，持有国债期间的利息收入，是指可交割国债从上一付息日至第二交割日之间形成的应计利息。

📌 **Financing cost is calculated using the bond’s full price, while the futures price is adjusted by the conversion factor.** ｜ 📌 **资金占用成本按照国债全价计算，期货理论价格则需要除以转换因子进行调整。**

------

### Examination Topic: Government Bond Futures Speculation and Arbitrage ｜ 考点：国债期货投机与套利

#### I. Long Strategy ｜ 一、多头策略

If an investor expects market interest rates to decline, bond prices are expected to rise. ｜ 如果投资者预期市场利率将下降，则债券价格通常会上涨。

The investor may therefore buy government bond futures and profit if the futures price subsequently rises. ｜ 因此，投资者可以采取多头策略，买入国债期货合约，并在期货价格上涨后获利。
$$
\text{Expected interest rate}\downarrow
\Rightarrow
\text{Expected bond price}\uparrow
\Rightarrow
\text{Buy government bond futures}
$$

------

#### II. Short Strategy ｜ 二、空头策略

If an investor expects market interest rates to rise, bond prices are expected to fall. ｜ 如果投资者预期市场利率将上升，则债券价格通常会下跌。

The investor may therefore sell government bond futures and profit if the futures price subsequently falls. ｜ 因此，投资者可以采取空头策略，卖出国债期货合约，并在期货价格下跌后获利。
$$
\text{Expected interest rate}\uparrow
\Rightarrow
\text{Expected bond price}\downarrow
\Rightarrow
\text{Sell government bond futures}
$$

------

#### III. Government Bond Cash-Futures Arbitrage ｜ 三、国债期现套利策略

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

Government bond cash-futures arbitrage uses a deviation between government bond futures prices and spot bond prices. ｜ 国债期现套利是指利用国债期货价格与现货国债价格之间的偏离进行交易。

The investor simultaneously buys or sells spot government bonds and takes the opposite position in government bond futures, expecting to earn a profit when the price relationship returns to a reasonable level. ｜ 投资者同时买入或者卖出现货国债，并在国债期货市场建立相反方向的头寸，待期现价格关系恢复合理后获取套利收益。

##### (II) Classification ｜ （二）分类

Government bond cash-futures arbitrage mainly includes: ｜ 国债期现套利主要包括：

- Basis trading strategies ｜ 基差交易策略
- Implied repo rate arbitrage strategies ｜ IRR套利策略

------

#### IV. Government Bond Basis and Net Basis ｜ 四、国债基差与净基差

##### (I) Government Bond Basis ｜ （一）国债基差

The government bond basis is the difference between the clean price of a deliverable government bond and the government bond futures price adjusted by the conversion factor. ｜ 国债基差是指可交割国债现货净价与经过转换因子调整后的国债期货价格之间的差额。
$$
\text{Bond basis}
=
\text{Spot bond clean price}
-
\text{Futures price}
\times
\text{Conversion factor}
$$

------

##### (II) Government Bond Net Basis ｜ （二）国债净基差

The government bond net basis is the bond basis after deducting the carry income. It is commonly abbreviated as BNOC. ｜ 国债净基差是扣除持有收益后的国债基差，通常用BNOC表示。
$$
\text{Net basis}
=
\text{Basis}
-
\text{Carry}
$$
Carry equals the interest income earned from holding the bond until delivery minus the financing cost incurred during the same period. ｜ 持有收益等于持有国债至交割期间获得的利息收入，减去持有国债期间发生的资金成本。
$$
\text{Carry}
=
\text{Interest income}
-
\text{Financing cost}
$$
According to the source, both the government bond basis and the net basis converge to zero when the futures contract reaches delivery. ｜ 根据原材料，国债期货合约到期交割时，国债基差和净基差都将趋于零。

------

#### V. Basis Trading Strategies ｜ 五、基差交易策略

##### (I) Buying the Basis ｜ （一）买入基差策略

Buying the basis is also called taking a long basis position. ｜ 买入基差也称为建立基差多头头寸。

The investor buys a deliverable government bond in the spot market and simultaneously sells an amount of government bond futures adjusted according to the conversion factor. ｜ 投资者买入可交割国债现货，同时卖出按照转换因子调整数量的国债期货合约。

The investor later closes both positions when the basis changes favourably. ｜ 随后，当基差向有利方向变化时，投资者同时平仓获利。

A long basis position profits when the basis strengthens. ｜ 基差多头在基差走强时获利。
$$
\text{Basis strengthens}
\Rightarrow
\text{Long basis profits}
$$
If the carry on the spot government bond is positive, the investor may also receive positive carry income. ｜ 如果国债现券的持有收益为正，投资者还可以获得相应的持有收益。

📌 **Buy basis: buy the bond and sell futures.** ｜ 📌 **买入基差：买入现券、卖出期货。**

------

##### (II) Selling the Basis ｜ （二）卖出基差策略

Selling the basis is also called taking a short basis position. ｜ 卖出基差也称为建立基差空头头寸。

The investor sells a deliverable government bond in the spot market and simultaneously buys an amount of government bond futures adjusted according to the conversion factor. ｜ 投资者卖出可交割国债现货，同时买入按照转换因子调整数量的国债期货合约。

A short basis position profits when the basis weakens. ｜ 基差空头在基差走弱时获利。
$$
\text{Basis weakens}
\Rightarrow
\text{Short basis profits}
$$
However, if the carry on the spot government bond is positive, the short-basis investor loses this positive carry and must take that loss into account. ｜ 但是，如果国债现券的持有收益为正，卖出基差的投资者会损失这部分持有收益，因此需要综合考虑。

📌 **Sell basis: sell the bond and buy futures.** ｜ 📌 **卖出基差：卖出现券、买入期货。**

------

#### VI. Implied Repo Rate Arbitrage ｜ 六、IRR套利策略

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

IRR stands for implied repo rate. ｜ IRR是隐含回购利率的英文缩写。

An IRR arbitrage strategy compares the implied repo rate of a deliverable government bond with the actual market financing rate and establishes corresponding spot and futures positions. ｜ IRR套利策略是将可交割国债的隐含回购利率与市场融资利率进行比较，并据此建立相应的国债现货和期货头寸。

------

##### (II) Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage ｜ （二）正向套利

If the market financing rate is lower than the implied repo rate, the return from holding the deliverable bond until delivery is expected to exceed the financing cost. ｜ 如果市场融资利率低于隐含回购利率，意味着持有可交割国债至交割所获得的收益预计能够覆盖融资成本。

The investor should therefore buy the deliverable government bond and simultaneously sell government bond futures. ｜ 因此，投资者适宜采用正向套利策略，即买入可交割国债现货，同时卖出国债期货。
$$
\text{Market financing rate}<\text{IRR}
$$
The approximate arbitrage return comes from the difference between the implied repo rate and the market financing rate. ｜ 套利收益主要来源于隐含回购利率高于市场融资利率的部分。

------

##### (III) Reverse Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage ｜ （三）反向套利

If the implied repo rate is lower than the market financing rate, holding the bond through financing is relatively unattractive. ｜ 如果隐含回购利率低于市场融资利率，通过融资持有国债的收益不足以覆盖较高的资金成本。

The investor should therefore sell the spot government bond and simultaneously buy government bond futures. ｜ 因此，投资者适宜采用反向套利策略，即卖出现货国债，同时买入国债期货。
$$
\text{IRR}<\text{Market financing rate}
$$

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🧮 **The theoretical price of government bond futures is based on the spot bond price plus financing cost minus interest income.** ｜ 🧮 **国债期货理论价格以国债现货价格加资金成本、减利息收入为基础。**

🔄 **When a specific deliverable bond is used, the result must be adjusted by its conversion factor.** ｜ 🔄 **使用具体可交割国债计算时，需要利用转换因子进行调整。**

📉 **Expected interest-rate decline → buy government bond futures.** ｜ 📉 **预期市场利率下降 → 买入国债期货。**

📈 **Expected interest-rate increase → sell government bond futures.** ｜ 📈 **预期市场利率上升 → 卖出国债期货。**

⚖️ **Bond basis equals the spot clean price minus the futures price multiplied by the conversion factor.** ｜ ⚖️ **国债基差等于现货净价减去期货价格与转换因子的乘积。**

💰 **Net basis equals basis minus carry, while carry equals interest income minus financing cost.** ｜ 💰 **净基差等于基差减持有收益，持有收益等于利息收入减资金成本。**

⬆️ **A long basis position profits when the basis strengthens.** ｜ ⬆️ **买入基差在基差走强时获利。**

⬇️ **A short basis position profits when the basis weakens.** ｜ ⬇️ **卖出基差在基差走弱时获利。**

🔁 **Market financing rate below IRR → buy spot bonds and sell futures.** ｜ 🔁 **市场融资利率低于IRR → 买入现券、卖出期货。**

↩️ **IRR below the market financing rate → sell spot bonds and buy futures.** ｜ ↩️ **IRR低于市场融资利率 → 卖出现券、买入期货。**

#### IV. Arbitrage Between Government Bond Futures Contracts ｜ 四、国债期货合约间套利

##### (I) Calendar Spread Arbitrage ｜ （一）跨期套利

Calendar spread arbitrage in government bond futures uses changes in the price difference between government bond futures contracts with different delivery months. ｜ 国债期货跨期套利是利用不同交割月份的国债期货合约之间的价差变化进行套利。

###### Buy Spread Arbitrage ｜ 买入价差套利

Buy spread arbitrage is suitable when the spread between two government bond futures contracts is considered undervalued. ｜ 当国债期货合约之间的价差被低估时，可以采用买入价差套利。

The trader buys the higher-priced contract and simultaneously sells the lower-priced contract. The positions are closed after the spread expands and returns to a reasonable level. ｜ 交易者买入价格较高的合约，同时卖出价格较低的合约，待价差扩大并恢复至合理水平后同时平仓获利。

📌 **Spread undervalued → expect expansion → buy the higher-priced contract and sell the lower-priced contract.** ｜ 📌 **价差被低估 → 预期价差扩大 → 买入高价合约、卖出低价合约。**

###### Sell Spread Arbitrage ｜ 卖出价差套利

Sell spread arbitrage is suitable when the spread between two government bond futures contracts is considered overvalued. ｜ 当国债期货合约之间的价差被高估时，可以采用卖出价差套利。

The trader sells the higher-priced contract and simultaneously buys the lower-priced contract. The positions are closed after the spread contracts and returns to a reasonable level. ｜ 交易者卖出价格较高的合约，同时买入价格较低的合约，待价差缩小并恢复至合理水平后同时平仓获利。

📌 **Spread overvalued → expect contraction → sell the higher-priced contract and buy the lower-priced contract.** ｜ 📌 **价差被高估 → 预期价差缩小 → 卖出高价合约、买入低价合约。**

------

##### (II) Inter-Commodity Arbitrage ｜ （二）跨品种套利

Inter-commodity arbitrage in government bond futures is mainly based on the different sensitivities of bonds with different maturities to changes in market interest rates. ｜ 国债期货跨品种套利主要利用不同期限债券对市场利率变化的敏感程度不同进行交易。

Generally, longer-term bonds are more sensitive to changes in market interest rates than shorter-term bonds. ｜ 一般情况下，长期债券对市场利率变动的敏感程度高于短期债券。

When market interest rates rise, the price decline of long-term bonds is generally greater than that of short-term bonds. ｜ 当市场利率上升时，长期债券价格的下跌幅度通常大于短期债券。

When market interest rates fall, the price increase of long-term bonds is generally greater than that of short-term bonds. ｜ 当市场利率下降时，长期债券价格的上涨幅度通常大于短期债券。

Investors may therefore select government bond futures with different maturities according to their expectations regarding future interest-rate movements. ｜ 因此，投资者可以根据对未来市场利率走势的判断，选择不同期限的国债期货合约进行跨品种套利。

| Expected interest-rate movement | Long-term bond reaction | Short-term bond reaction |
| ------------------------------- | ----------------------- | ------------------------ |
| Interest rates rise             | Larger price decline    | Smaller price decline    |
| Interest rates fall             | Larger price increase   | Smaller price increase   |

| 预期利率变化 | 长期债券反应     | 短期债券反应     |
| ------------ | ---------------- | ---------------- |
| 利率上升     | 价格下跌幅度较大 | 价格下跌幅度较小 |
| 利率下降     | 价格上涨幅度较大 | 价格上涨幅度较小 |

------

### Examination Topic: Government Bond Futures Hedging ｜ 考点：国债期货套期保值

#### I. Short Hedging Strategy ｜ 一、空头套期保值策略

An investor who currently holds bond assets may be concerned that market interest rates will rise and bond prices will fall. ｜ 已经持有债券资产的投资者，可能担心市场利率上升导致债券价格下跌。

The investor may sell government bond futures to offset the potential loss in the spot bond portfolio. ｜ 投资者可以卖出国债期货，通过空头套期保值对冲现货债券的跌价风险。
$$
\text{Hold bonds}
+
\text{Fear interest rates will rise}
\Rightarrow
\text{Sell government bond futures}
$$
📌 **Existing bond holdings → use a short hedge.** ｜ 📌 **已经持有债券 → 采用空头套期保值。**

------

#### II. Long Hedging Strategy ｜ 二、多头套期保值策略

An investor who plans to buy bonds in the future may be concerned that market interest rates will fall and bond prices will rise before the purchase is completed. ｜ 计划在未来购买债券的投资者，可能担心市场利率下降，导致未来买入债券的价格上涨。

The investor may buy government bond futures to offset the increase in the future purchase cost. ｜ 投资者可以买入国债期货，通过多头套期保值对冲未来购债成本上升的风险。
$$
\text{Plan to buy bonds}
+
\text{Fear interest rates will fall}
\Rightarrow
\text{Buy government bond futures}
$$
📌 **Future bond purchase → use a long hedge.** ｜ 📌 **未来需要购买债券 → 采用多头套期保值。**

------

#### III. Relevant Considerations ｜ 三、相关注意事项

##### (I) Selection of the Futures Contract ｜ （一）期货合约的选择

In practice, the bonds held or planned to be held by an investor may differ from the deliverable bonds of the futures contract in terms of original maturity, remaining maturity or other characteristics. ｜ 实践中，投资者持有或者计划持有的债券，可能在发行期限、剩余期限等方面与国债期货可交割券不完全一致。

If the bond is outside the range of deliverable securities, the investor should select a futures contract with good liquidity and a strong price correlation with the hedged bond or bond portfolio. ｜ 如果被套期保值债券不属于可交割国债，投资者应选择流动性较好、与现货债券价格相关性较强的国债期货合约。

------

##### (II) Hedge Ratio ｜ （二）套期保值比率

The hedger should compare the change in the value of the bond portfolio caused by an interest-rate movement with the corresponding change in the value of the government bond futures contract. ｜ 套期保值者需要比较利率变化引起的债券组合价值变动与国债期货合约价值变动。

The number of futures contracts should then be determined so that the futures position can effectively offset the interest-rate risk of the spot bond or bond portfolio. ｜ 在此基础上确定所需国债期货合约数量，使期货头寸能够有效对冲现货债券或者债券组合的利率风险。

Common methods for determining the hedge ratio include the duration method and the basis-point-value method. ｜ 常见的国债期货套期保值比率确定方法包括久期法和基点价值法。

------

###### Duration Method ｜ 久期法

**Macaulay duration** ｜ **麦考利久期**

Macaulay duration is the weighted average time required to recover the cash flows from a bond investment. ｜ 麦考利久期是债券投资各期现金流回收时间的加权平均值，单位为年。

The weight assigned to each cash flow is the proportion of its present value in the bond’s total price. ｜ 各期现金流的权重，是该期现金流现值占债券总价格的比重。

**Modified duration** ｜ **修正久期**

Modified duration is developed from Macaulay duration and measures the sensitivity of a bond’s price to a small change in its yield to maturity. ｜ 修正久期是在麦考利久期基础上发展而来的，用于衡量债券价格对到期收益率变化的敏感程度。

For a bond paying interest once a year: ｜ 对于每年付息一次的债券：
$$
D_m=\frac{D}{1+y}
$$
where: ｜ 其中：

- $D_m$ is modified duration ｜ $D_m$为修正久期
- $D$ is Macaulay duration ｜ $D$为麦考利久期
- $y$ is the yield to maturity ｜ $y$为到期收益率

The modified duration is approximately the percentage change in the bond price when the yield to maturity changes by one percentage point, or 100 basis points. ｜ 修正久期近似表示到期收益率变化1个百分点，即100个基点时，债券价格变化的百分比。

The duration of a bond portfolio is calculated as the weighted average duration of the bonds in the portfolio, with weights based on their proportions of the portfolio’s market value. ｜ 债券组合久期可以通过组合中各债券久期的加权平均计算，权重为各债券市值占组合总市值的比例。

The number of futures contracts required for hedging is: ｜ 久期法下所需国债期货合约数量为：
$$
N=
\frac{
\text{Bond portfolio market value}
\times
\text{Bond portfolio duration}
}{
\text{Futures contract market value}
\times
\text{Futures contract duration}
}
$$

------

###### Basis Point Value Method ｜ 基点价值法

Basis point value, or BPV, is the amount by which the value of an asset changes when its yield to maturity changes by one basis point. ｜ 基点价值简称BPV，是指到期收益率每变化一个基点，即0.01个百分点时，资产价值发生的变动额。

The hedge ratio is determined by comparing the BPV of the bond or bond portfolio with the BPV of one government bond futures contract. ｜ 该方法通过比较现货债券或者债券组合的基点价值与一手国债期货合约的基点价值，确定所需期货合约数量。

------

#### IV. Hedging Cases ｜ 四、套期保值案例

##### (I) Long Hedging ｜ （一）多头套期保值

In March, an institutional investor planned to purchase government bond A with a face value of RMB 8 million two months later. ｜ 3月份，某机构投资者计划在两个月后购买面值800万元的国债A。

Assume that bond A is the cheapest-to-deliver bond for the five-year government bond futures contract. Its conversion factor is 1.25, and its current price is RMB 118.50 per RMB 100 face value. ｜ 假设国债A是中金所5年期国债期货的最便宜可交割券，转换因子为1.25，价格为每100元面值118.50元。

To lock in the future investment cost and protect against an increase in the bond price, the investor establishes a long hedge by buying government bond futures. ｜ 为了锁定未来投资成本并防范国债价格上涨，投资者买入国债期货进行多头套期保值。

For a CTD bond, the source states that the optimal hedge ratio calculated using the duration method or BPV method equals its conversion factor. ｜ 根据原材料，对于CTD券，利用久期法或者基点价值法计算的最优套期保值比率等于其转换因子。

The required number of futures contracts is: ｜ 需要买入的期货合约数量为：
$$
\frac{800}{100}\times1.25=10\text{手}
$$

------

###### Case 1: Bond Prices Rise ｜ 情形一：国债价格上涨

On 6 March: ｜ 3月6日：

- Bond A was priced at RMB 118.50. ｜ 国债A价格为118.50元。
- The investor bought 10 five-year government bond futures contracts at 94.550. ｜ 投资者以94.550买入10手5年期国债期货。

On 6 May: ｜ 5月6日：

- Bond A increased to RMB 119.70. ｜ 国债A价格上涨至119.70元。
- The futures price increased to 95.550, and the investor closed the 10 long contracts. ｜ 国债期货价格上涨至95.550，投资者将10手多头合约平仓。

The spot purchase cost increased by: ｜ 现货购债成本增加：
$$
(119.70-118.50)\times\frac{8{,}000{,}000}{100}
=
96{,}000\text{元}
$$
The futures position earned: ｜ 期货头寸盈利：
$$
(95.550-94.550)\times10{,}000\times10
=
100{,}000\text{元}
$$
The futures profit exceeded the increase in the spot purchase cost by RMB 4,000. ｜ 期货盈利比现货购债成本的增加额多4000元。

Therefore, ignoring hedging costs, the total purchase cost was RMB 4,000 lower than the cost locked in March. ｜ 因此，在不考虑套期保值成本的情况下，最终总购债成本比3月份锁定的成本低4000元，即低0.4万元。

------

###### Case 2: Bond Prices Fall ｜ 情形二：国债价格下跌

Assume that bond A falls from RMB 118.50 to RMB 117.30, while the futures price falls from 94.550 to 93.550. ｜ 假设国债A价格由118.50元下跌至117.30元，期货价格由94.550下跌至93.550。

The spot purchase cost decreases by: ｜ 现货购债成本减少：
$$
(118.50-117.30)\times\frac{8{,}000{,}000}{100}
=
96{,}000\text{元}
$$
The long futures position loses: ｜ 期货多头亏损：
$$
(94.550-93.550)\times10{,}000\times10
=
100{,}000\text{元}
$$
The futures loss exceeds the reduction in the spot purchase cost by RMB 4,000. ｜ 期货亏损比现货购债成本的下降额多4000元。

Therefore, ignoring hedging costs, the total purchase cost was RMB 4,000 higher than the cost locked in March. ｜ 因此，在不考虑套期保值成本的情况下，最终总购债成本比3月份锁定的成本高4000元，即高0.4万元。

📌 **The long hedge largely locks in the future bond purchase cost, regardless of whether bond prices rise or fall.** ｜ 📌 **无论债券价格上涨还是下跌，多头套期保值都基本锁定了未来购债成本。**

------

##### (II) Short Hedging ｜ （二）空头套期保值

On 6 March, a company held government bond B with a face value of RMB 8 million and planned to sell it two months later to pay for goods. ｜ 3月6日，某公司持有面值800万元的国债B，并计划在两个月后将其卖出用于支付货款。

Assume that bond B is the cheapest-to-deliver bond for the five-year government bond futures contract. Its conversion factor is 1.125, and its price is RMB 107.50. ｜ 假设国债B是5年期国债期货的最便宜可交割券，转换因子为1.125，价格为107.50元。

To protect against a decline in the bond price, the company establishes a short hedge by selling government bond futures. ｜ 为了防范国债价格下跌风险，该公司卖出国债期货进行空头套期保值。

The required number of futures contracts is: ｜ 需要卖出的国债期货合约数量为：
$$
\frac{800}{100}\times1.125=9\text{手}
$$

------

###### Case 1: Bond Prices Fall ｜ 情形一：国债价格下跌

On 6 March: ｜ 3月6日：

- Bond B was priced at RMB 107.50. ｜ 国债B价格为107.50元。
- The company sold 9 five-year government bond futures contracts at 94.550. ｜ 公司以94.550卖出9手5年期国债期货。

On 6 May: ｜ 5月6日：

- Bond B fell to RMB 106.30. ｜ 国债B价格下跌至106.30元。
- The futures price fell to 93.550, and the company closed the 9 short contracts. ｜ 国债期货价格下跌至93.550，公司将9手空头合约平仓。

The spot sale proceeds decreased by: ｜ 现货出售收入减少：
$$
(107.50-106.30)\times\frac{8{,}000{,}000}{100}
=
96{,}000\text{元}
$$
The futures position earned: ｜ 期货头寸盈利：
$$
(94.550-93.550)\times10{,}000\times9
=
90{,}000\text{元}
$$
The remaining net reduction in total proceeds was: ｜ 最终总收入净减少：
$$
96{,}000-90{,}000
=
6{,}000\text{元}
$$
Therefore, total proceeds were only RMB 6,000, or RMB 0.6万元, below the amount that could have been obtained in March.

因此，最终总收入仅比3月份可以获得的收入低6000元，即低0.6万元。

------

###### Case 2: Bond Prices Rise ｜ 情形二：国债价格上涨

Assume that bond B rises from RMB 107.50 to RMB 108.60, while the futures price rises from 94.550 to 95.550. ｜ 假设国债B价格由107.50元上涨至108.60元，国债期货价格由94.550上涨至95.550。

The spot sale proceeds increase by: ｜ 现货出售收入增加：
$$
(108.60-107.50)\times\frac{8{,}000{,}000}{100}
=
88{,}000\text{元}
$$
The short futures position loses: ｜ 期货空头亏损：
$$
(95.550-94.550)\times10{,}000\times9
=
90{,}000\text{元}
$$
The remaining net reduction in total proceeds is: ｜ 最终总收入净减少：
$$
90{,}000-88{,}000
=
2{,}000\text{元}
$$
Therefore, total proceeds were RMB 2,000, or RMB 0.2万元, below the amount locked in March.

因此，最终总收入比3月份锁定的收入低2000元，即低0.2万元。

📌 **The short hedge largely locks in the future proceeds from selling the bond.** ｜ 📌 **空头套期保值可以基本锁定未来出售债券所获得的收入。**

------

#### V. Adjustment of the Hedge Ratio ｜ 五、套期保值比率的调整

##### (I) Reasons for Adjustment ｜ （一）调整原因

The duration method and BPV method generally provide good hedging results when the hedged bond is a deliverable bond. ｜ 当被套期保值债券属于国债期货可交割券时，久期法和基点价值法通常具有较好的套期保值效果。

However, the hedged asset may instead be another government bond, a policy-bank bond or a credit bond. ｜ 但是，被套期保值的资产也可能是其他国债、政策性金融债或者信用债。

In such cases, the maturity, interest-rate sensitivity and credit risk of the hedged bond may differ from those of the CTD bond. ｜ 此时，被保值债券的期限、利率敏感性和信用风险等特征，可能与最便宜可交割国债不同。

The yield movements of the hedged bond and the CTD bond may therefore not be identical, creating cross-hedging risk. ｜ 因此，被保值债券与CTD券的收益率变动幅度可能不完全一致，从而产生交叉套期保值风险。

------

##### (II) Yield Beta Adjustment ｜ （二）收益率β系数法

In practice, the hedge ratio calculated using the duration method or BPV method may be adjusted according to the characteristics of the hedged bond. ｜ 实际应用中，需要根据被保值债券的具体特征，对久期法或者基点价值法计算出的套期保值比率进行适当调整。

A commonly used method is the yield beta method. ｜ 常用的调整方法是收益率β系数法。

Historical data are used to establish the following regression relationship: ｜ 利用历史数据建立被保值债券收益率与CTD券收益率之间的回归关系：
$$
r_p=\alpha+\beta r_{CTD}+\varepsilon
$$
where: ｜ 其中：

- $r_p$: yield of the hedged bond ｜ $r_p$：被保值债券的收益率
- $r_{CTD}$: yield of the CTD bond ｜ $r_{CTD}$：国债期货对应CTD券的收益率
- $\alpha$: intercept ｜ $\alpha$：截距项
- $\beta$: regression coefficient ｜ $\beta$：回归系数
- $\varepsilon$: error term ｜ $\varepsilon$：误差项

The estimated $\beta$ is called the yield beta. It represents the relative movement between the yield of the hedged bond and that of the CTD bond. ｜ 回归分析得到的$\beta$估计值称为收益率β，表示被保值债券收益率相对于CTD券收益率的变动幅度。

For example, when the CTD yield changes by 1%, the hedged bond yield is expected to change by approximately $\beta\%$. ｜ 例如，当CTD券收益率变动1%时，被保值债券收益率预计变动约$\beta\%$。

The adjusted hedge ratio is: ｜ 调整后的套期保值比率为：
$$
h_{\beta}=h\times\beta
$$
where $h$ is the original optimal hedge ratio calculated using the duration method or BPV method. ｜ 其中，$h$为利用修正久期法或者基点价值法计算出的原始最优套期保值比率。

------

### Examination Topic: Applications in Risk and Portfolio Management ｜ 考点：在风险管理和投资组合管理中的应用

#### I. Application in Duration Management ｜ 一、在久期管理中的应用

##### (I) Adjusting Target Duration ｜ （一）目标久期调整

When market interest rates are expected to rise, an investor may increase short positions in government bond futures to reduce the duration of the portfolio. ｜ 预期市场利率将上升时，投资者可以增加国债期货空头持仓，降低投资组合的久期。

When market interest rates are expected to fall, an investor may increase long positions in government bond futures to increase the duration of the portfolio. ｜ 预期市场利率将下降时，投资者可以增加国债期货多头持仓，提高投资组合的久期。
$$
\text{Expected rates}\uparrow
\Rightarrow
\text{Increase futures shorts}
\Rightarrow
\text{Reduce portfolio duration}
$$

------

##### (II) Constructing a Long-Duration Portfolio ｜ （二）构建长久期组合

Institutional investors may use government bond futures to construct a long-duration portfolio that would be difficult to achieve using spot bonds alone. ｜ 机构投资者可以利用国债期货构建仅依靠现券难以实现的长久期组合。

This can reduce the effect of limited bond supply and insufficient liquidity in the spot market and allows more flexible adjustment toward the portfolio’s target duration. ｜ 这种方式可以减少现券供给不足和流动性限制的影响，更灵活地实现目标久期调整。

------

#### II. Application in Asset Allocation ｜ 二、在资产配置中的应用

##### (I) Long-Substitution Strategy ｜ （一）多头替代策略

If an investor expects bond yields to decline but temporarily lacks sufficient funds, or funds have not yet arrived, the investor may use a long government bond futures position as a substitute for a long spot-bond position. ｜ 如果投资者预期债券收益率将下降，但短期资金不足或者资金尚未到账，可以买入国债期货，暂时代替买入现券。

This protects the investor against the risk that spot bond prices may rise before the intended allocation is completed. ｜ 这种策略可以防范在现券配置完成前债券价格上涨的风险。

As the spot bond allocation is gradually completed, the corresponding futures long positions may be closed. ｜ 后期随着现券配置逐步完成，投资者可以相应平掉国债期货多头头寸。

If the risk of rising yields is limited and government bond futures trade at a substantial discount to spot bonds, buying futures instead of spot bonds may also generate additional convergence returns. ｜ 在收益率上升风险有限的情况下，如果国债期货相对于现券出现大幅折价，投资者还可以通过买入国债期货替代现券，获得期货价格向现货价格收敛的增强收益。

------

##### (II) Substituting for the Sale of Spot Bonds ｜ （二）替代卖出现券

If an investor is bearish on the bond market in the short term or expects market conditions to weaken, the investor may sell government bond futures instead of immediately selling the spot bond portfolio. ｜ 如果投资者短期看空债券市场，或者预计市场行情可能转弱，可以卖出国债期货，而不必立即出售所持现券。

The short futures position offsets potential declines in the value of the bond portfolio and stabilises portfolio returns. ｜ 期货空头可以对冲债券资产的短期跌价损失，稳定投资组合收益。

By holding spot government bonds and selling government bond futures, the investor may reduce portfolio risk exposure while continuing to receive the investment income generated by the spot bonds. ｜ 从资产组合管理角度看，持有国债现券并卖出国债期货，可以在降低组合风险敞口的同时，继续获得持有国债现券的投资收益。

------

#### III. Applications for Commercial Banks and Insurance Institutions ｜ 三、商业银行和保险机构的应用选择

##### (I) Commercial Banks ｜ （一）商业银行

###### Asset–Liability Management ｜ 资产负债管理

In European and US markets, government bond futures have become an important interest-rate risk-management instrument in the risk-control systems of commercial banks. ｜ 在欧美市场，国债期货已经成为商业银行风险控制体系中重要的利率风险管理工具。

They may compensate for some limitations of direct spot-bond transactions and improve the flexibility of asset–liability management. ｜ 国债期货可以弥补部分债券现券交易的不足，提高资产负债管理的灵活性。

###### Bond Investment Business ｜ 债券投资业务

Commercial banks may use government bond futures to manage the risk of bond investments and enhance investment returns. ｜ 商业银行可以利用国债期货管理债券投资业务风险，并增强投资收益。

When the net basis is abnormally low, a bank may adopt cash-and-carry arbitrage by buying spot bonds and selling futures. ｜ 当净基差异常偏低时，商业银行可以采取买入现券、卖出期货的正向套利策略。

When the net basis is abnormally high, a bank may adopt reverse cash-and-carry arbitrage by selling spot bonds and buying futures. ｜ 当净基差异常偏高时，商业银行可以采取卖出现券、买入期货的反向套利策略。

###### Market-Making Business ｜ 利率债做市业务

Commercial banks generally need to hold a certain inventory of bonds to conduct market-making activities. ｜ 商业银行开展利率债做市业务时，通常需要持有一定规模的债券底仓。

This inventory is exposed to the risk that rising market interest rates will cause bond prices to decline. ｜ 债券底仓会面临市场利率上升导致债券价格下跌的风险。

Banks may use government bond futures to hedge the interest-rate risk of their bond inventories, improve returns and reduce market-making costs. ｜ 商业银行可以利用国债期货对冲现券底仓的利率风险，在控制风险的同时提高收益并降低做市成本。

------

##### (II) Insurance Institutions ｜ （二）保险机构

###### Life Insurance Companies ｜ 寿险公司

Life insurance products generally have relatively long average maturities. ｜ 寿险公司保险产品的平均期限通常较长。

Their bond investments are therefore concentrated mainly in long-term bonds with stable returns, helping them achieve asset–liability matching. ｜ 因此，寿险公司的债券投资通常更多配置于收益稳定的长期债券，以实现资产与负债期限匹配。

###### Property and Casualty Insurance Companies ｜ 财险公司

Property and casualty insurance products generally have shorter average maturities, and the available funds also tend to have shorter durations. ｜ 财险公司保险产品的平均期限通常较短，可运用资金的期限也相对较短。

To match assets with liabilities, these companies generally have greater demand for shorter-term bond investments. ｜ 为了实现资产负债期限匹配，财险公司通常对较短期限债券的需求和配置比例较高。

###### Main Uses of Government Bond Futures ｜ 国债期货的主要用途

Insurance institutions may use hedging and long-substitution strategies to lock in investment costs and improve capital efficiency. ｜ 保险机构可以通过套期保值策略和现券替代策略锁定投资成本，提高资金使用效率。

They may use target-duration management and short hedging to manage risks arising from bond investments and holdings. ｜ 保险机构可以通过目标久期管理和空头套期保值，管理债券投资和持有过程中的风险。

They may also use government bond futures to manage and adjust the duration gap between assets and liabilities. ｜ 保险机构还可以利用国债期货管理和调整资产与负债之间的久期缺口。

The final sentence in the supplied material is truncated after “reduce interest-rate…”. Its complete wording cannot be confirmed from the source. ｜ 原材料最后一句在“降低利率……”处被截断，无法确认其完整表述。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🔄 **Government bond futures calendar spread arbitrage profits from changes in the spread between different delivery months.** ｜ 🔄 **国债期货跨期套利利用不同交割月份合约之间的价差变化获利。**

⏳ **Longer-term bonds are generally more sensitive to interest-rate changes than shorter-term bonds.** ｜ ⏳ **长期债券对利率变化的敏感程度通常高于短期债券。**

📉 **Existing bond holders use short hedging to protect against rising interest rates and falling bond prices.** ｜ 📉 **已经持有债券的投资者通过空头套期保值防范利率上升和债券价格下跌。**

📈 **Investors planning to buy bonds use long hedging to protect against falling interest rates and rising bond prices.** ｜ 📈 **计划购买债券的投资者通过多头套期保值防范利率下降和债券价格上涨。**

⏱️ **Modified duration measures the sensitivity of bond prices to small changes in yields.** ｜ ⏱️ **修正久期用于衡量债券价格对收益率小幅变化的敏感程度。**

💵 **BPV measures the change in asset value caused by a one-basis-point change in yield.** ｜ 💵 **基点价值衡量收益率变动一个基点所引起的资产价值变化。**

🧮 **The number of hedging contracts depends on the relative price sensitivity of the bond portfolio and the futures contract.** ｜ 🧮 **套期保值合约数量取决于债券组合与期货合约价格敏感程度的相对关系。**

β **Yield beta adjusts the hedge ratio when the hedged bond and the CTD bond do not move identically.** ｜ β **当被保值债券与CTD券收益率变化不一致时，可以使用收益率β调整套期保值比率。**

🎯 **Government bond futures can be used to raise or lower portfolio duration.** ｜ 🎯 **国债期货可以用于提高或者降低投资组合久期。**

🏦 **Commercial banks use government bond futures in asset–liability management, investment, arbitrage and market-making.** ｜ 🏦 **商业银行可以将国债期货用于资产负债管理、债券投资、套利和做市业务。**

🛡️ **Insurance institutions use government bond futures for cost locking, hedging and asset–liability duration-gap management.** ｜ 🛡️ **保险机构可以利用国债期货锁定投资成本、对冲风险并管理资产负债久期缺口。**

# Chapter 7: Foreign Exchange Futures ｜ 第七章 外汇期货

This chapter accounts for approximately 5% of the examination score. ｜ 本章分值占比约为5%。

## Section 1: Foreign Exchange and Exchange Rates ｜ 第一节 外汇与汇率

### Examination Topic: Foreign Exchange and Exchange Rates ｜ 考点：外汇与汇率

#### I. Definition of Foreign Exchange ｜ 一、外汇的定义

##### (I) Concept ｜ （一）概念

Foreign exchange refers to payment instruments and assets denominated in foreign currencies that can be used for international settlement. Foreign exchange developed from international trade. ｜ 外汇是指以外币表示、可以用于国际清偿的支付手段和资产。外汇是国际贸易发展的产物。

##### (II) Classification by Degree of Convertibility ｜ （二）按照兑换自由程度分类

###### Freely Convertible Foreign Exchange ｜ 自由兑换外汇

Freely convertible foreign exchange can be freely used and traded in international settlement and international financial markets. ｜ 自由兑换外汇是指可以在国际结算和国际金融市场中自由使用和买卖的外汇。

###### Partially Convertible Foreign Exchange ｜ 有限自由兑换外汇

Partially convertible foreign exchange cannot be freely converted into other currencies or freely used to make payments to third countries. ｜ 有限自由兑换外汇是指不能自由兑换成其他货币，或者不能自由用于向第三国支付的外汇。

According to the classification used in the source material, the renminbi is included in this category. ｜ 按照原材料采用的分类，人民币被列入有限自由兑换外汇。

###### Book-Entry Foreign Exchange ｜ 记账外汇

Book-entry foreign exchange is also called clearing foreign exchange or bilateral foreign exchange. ｜ 记账外汇又称清算外汇或者双边外汇。

It is recorded in bank accounts designated by the two countries or parties. It cannot be converted into other currencies or used to make payments to third countries. ｜ 这种外汇只在双方指定的银行账户中进行记账，不能兑换成其他货币，也不能用于向第三国支付。

------

#### II. Exchange Rates and Exchange-Rate Quotations ｜ 二、汇率与汇率标价

##### (I) Meaning of an Exchange Rate ｜ （一）汇率的含义

An exchange rate, also called a foreign exchange rate or foreign exchange quotation, is the ratio at which the currency of one country can be exchanged for the currency of another country. ｜ 汇率又称外汇行市或者汇价，是一个国家的货币兑换成另一个国家货币的比率。

In other words, an exchange rate expresses the value of one currency in terms of another currency. ｜ 换言之，汇率是使用一种货币表示另一种货币的价值。

------

##### (II) Exchange-Rate Quotation Methods ｜ （二）汇率的标价方法

###### Direct Quotation ｜ 直接标价法

Direct quotation expresses the price of a foreign currency in units of the domestic currency. ｜ 直接标价法是以本国货币表示外国货币的价格。

A fixed amount of foreign currency, such as 1, 100 or 1,000 units, is used as the standard and converted into a corresponding amount of domestic currency. ｜ 具体方法是以1个、100个或者1000个单位的外国货币为标准，折算为一定数量的本国货币。

For example: ｜ 例如：
$$
100\text{ USD}=615.33\text{ CNY}
$$
This means that USD 100 can be exchanged for RMB 615.33. ｜ 这表示100美元可以兑换615.33元人民币。

Under direct quotation: ｜ 在直接标价法下：
$$
\text{Foreign currency appreciates}
\Rightarrow
\text{Quotation rises}
$$

------

###### Indirect Quotation ｜ 间接标价法

Indirect quotation expresses the price of the domestic currency in units of a foreign currency. ｜ 间接标价法是以外国货币表示本国货币的价格。

A fixed amount of domestic currency is used as the standard and converted into a corresponding amount of foreign currency. ｜ 具体方法是以一定单位的本国货币为标准，折算为一定数量的外国货币。

For example, in New York: ｜ 例如，在纽约市场：
$$
1\text{ USD}=0.6605\text{ GBP}
$$
This means that one US dollar can be exchanged for 0.6605 pounds sterling. ｜ 这表示1美元可以兑换0.6605英镑。

According to the source, currencies such as the euro, pound sterling and Australian dollar are commonly quoted indirectly against the US dollar in the international foreign exchange market. ｜ 根据原材料，在国际外汇市场上，欧元、英镑和澳元等货币通常采用以美元为参照的间接标价方式。

------

###### US Dollar Quotation ｜ 美元标价法

Under the US dollar quotation method, countries use a fixed amount of US dollars as the standard and calculate how much of another currency it can be exchanged for. ｜ 美元标价法是以一定单位的美元为标准，计算可以兑换多少其他国家的货币。

When two non-US-dollar currencies are exchanged, their exchange rate can be calculated using their respective exchange rates against the US dollar. ｜ 在进行两种非美元货币之间的交易时，可以根据这两种货币各自对美元的汇率套算出交叉汇率。

------

#### III. Trading-Time Characteristics of the Foreign Exchange Market ｜ 三、外汇交易时间特征

The global foreign exchange market operates continuously for approximately 24 hours each trading day. ｜ 全球外汇市场在每个交易日基本上可以实现24小时连续交易。

This is one of the main characteristics distinguishing the foreign exchange market from stock and futures exchanges with fixed trading sessions. ｜ 这是外汇市场区别于具有固定交易时段的股票市场和期货市场的重要特点之一。

Major foreign exchange markets are centred on financial cities and extend their influence to surrounding countries and regions. ｜ 全球主要外汇市场通常以金融城市为中心，并辐射周边国家和地区。

Periods with greater liquidity and volatility are generally more suitable for active trading. ｜ 市场流动性和波动性越大，通常越适合进行活跃交易。

According to the source, Beijing time from approximately 20:30 to 24:00 is an overlap between the London and New York trading sessions. ｜ 根据原材料，北京时间约20:30—24:00是伦敦市场与纽约市场的重叠交易时段。

During this period, international banks trade intensively, large transactions are concentrated and market volatility is relatively high. It is therefore often called a “golden trading period” in the foreign exchange market. ｜ 这一时段是国际银行外汇交易较为集中的时期，大宗交易较多，市场波动通常也较大，因此常被称为外汇市场的“黄金交易时段”。

------

#### IV. Factors Affecting Exchange Rates ｜ 四、汇率的影响因素

##### (I) Balance of Payments ｜ （一）国际收支状况

A country’s balance-of-payments position is an important foundation of its exchange rate. ｜ 国际收支状况是一国汇率形成的重要基础。

When a country has a balance-of-payments surplus, the supply of foreign exchange is greater than the demand for foreign exchange. ｜ 当一国出现国际收支顺差时，外汇供给大于外汇需求。

The foreign currency tends to depreciate relative to the domestic currency, meaning that the domestic currency tends to appreciate. ｜ 此时外币相对于本币趋于贬值，即本国货币对外币趋于升值。
$$
\text{Balance-of-payments surplus}
\Rightarrow
\text{Domestic currency tends to appreciate}
$$
When a country has a balance-of-payments deficit, the demand for foreign exchange is greater than its supply. ｜ 当一国出现国际收支逆差时，外汇需求大于外汇供给。

The foreign currency tends to appreciate, meaning that the domestic currency tends to depreciate. ｜ 此时外币趋于升值，即本国货币对外币趋于贬值。

------

##### (II) Relative Inflation Rates ｜ （二）两国通货膨胀程度的比较

A country with a relatively high rate of price increases will generally experience a decline in the external value of its currency. ｜ 物价上涨幅度相对较大的国家，其货币的对外价值通常会下降。

A country with a relatively low rate of price increases will generally experience an increase in the external value of its currency. ｜ 物价上涨幅度相对较小的国家，其货币的对外价值通常会上升。
$$
\text{Higher relative inflation}
\Rightarrow
\text{Currency tends to depreciate}
$$

------

##### (III) Interest-Rate Differences Between Countries ｜ （三）两国间的利率差异

When international interest-rate differences exist, capital tends to flow from countries with lower interest rates to countries with higher interest rates. ｜ 当不同国家之间存在利率差异时，资本通常会从利率较低的国家流向利率较高的国家。

These capital flows change the supply and demand for currencies and therefore affect exchange rates. ｜ 国际资本流动会改变不同货币的供求关系，进而影响汇率。

------

##### (IV) Market Expectations ｜ （四）市场预期的心理影响

If market participants generally expect a country’s currency to depreciate, they may sell that currency in large quantities. ｜ 如果市场参与者普遍预期某国货币将贬值，就可能集中抛售该国货币。

This selling pressure may itself cause the currency to depreciate significantly. ｜ 这种集中抛售行为本身可能进一步推动该国货币汇率下跌。

The opposite may occur when market participants expect a currency to appreciate. ｜ 如果市场参与者普遍预期某种货币升值，则可能出现相反的市场反应。

------

##### (V) Government Policies and Central-Bank Intervention ｜ （五）政府政策和中央银行直接干预

Government fiscal and monetary policies can affect the supply of money, interest rates, capital flows and market expectations, thereby influencing exchange rates. ｜ 政府的财政政策和货币政策会影响货币供应量、市场利率、资本流动和市场预期，进而影响汇率。

An expansionary monetary policy generally increases the money supply and may create depreciation pressure on the domestic currency. ｜ 扩张性货币政策通常会增加货币供应量，并可能使本国货币面临贬值压力。

A contractionary monetary policy generally reduces the money supply and may create appreciation pressure on the domestic currency. ｜ 紧缩性货币政策通常会减少货币供应量，并可能使本国货币面临升值压力。

Central banks may also directly buy or sell currencies in the foreign exchange market to influence exchange-rate movements. ｜ 中央银行还可以直接在外汇市场买卖本币或者外币，以干预汇率变化。

------

#### V. Foreign Exchange Risk ｜ 五、外汇风险

##### (I) Transaction Risk ｜ （一）交易风险

Transaction risk is the possibility that an economic entity will suffer a loss because exchange rates change between the time a foreign-currency transaction is agreed and the time payment or settlement occurs. ｜ 交易风险是指在使用外币计价和收付的交易中，由于外币与本币之间或者不同外币之间的汇率发生变化，使经济主体遭受损失的可能性。

###### Import and Export Transactions ｜ 进出口交易

If an importer must pay foreign currency in the future and the foreign currency appreciates after the contract is signed, the importer must use more domestic currency to make the payment. ｜ 如果进口商需要在未来支付外币，而外币汇率在合同签订后上涨，进口商就需要支付更多本币才能兑换足够的外币。

The effect on an exporter is generally the opposite. ｜ 出口商面临的情况通常与进口商相反。

###### Foreign-Currency Claims and Liabilities ｜ 外币债权债务

If the exchange rate of a foreign currency falls between the creation and settlement of a foreign-currency claim, the creditor receives less domestic currency after conversion. ｜ 如果外币债权形成后，该外币在债权清偿前贬值，债权人将外币兑换成本币时能够收回的本币金额会减少。

The situation for a foreign-currency debtor is generally the opposite. ｜ 外币债务人的情况通常与债权人相反。

###### Foreign Exchange Positions Held by Banks ｜ 银行持有的外汇头寸

Banks acting as intermediaries in foreign exchange transactions may hold long or short foreign currency positions. ｜ 外汇银行在开展中介性外汇交易时，可能持有外汇多头或者空头头寸。

Changes in exchange rates may cause gains or losses on these positions. ｜ 汇率变化可能使这些外汇头寸产生盈利或者亏损。

------

##### (II) Accounting Risk ｜ （二）会计风险

Accounting risk is also called translation risk. ｜ 会计风险又称折算风险。

It arises when an enterprise converts assets, liabilities, income or expenses denominated in a functional currency into the reporting currency used in its financial statements. ｜ 当企业将以功能货币计量的资产、负债、收入或者费用，折算为编制会计报表所使用的记账货币时，就可能产生会计风险。

Exchange-rate changes may produce accounting gains or losses even if no actual cash transaction occurs. ｜ 即使没有发生实际现金收付，汇率变化也可能造成账面上的汇兑损益。

------

##### (III) Operating Risk ｜ （三）经营风险

Operating risk refers to the possibility that unexpected exchange-rate changes will reduce an enterprise’s future profits or cash flows by affecting sales volume, product prices and production costs. ｜ 经营风险是指意料之外的汇率变化，通过影响企业的销售数量、销售价格和生产成本，导致企业未来一定期间的收益或者现金流量减少的可能性。

Unlike transaction risk, operating risk may affect an enterprise’s long-term competitiveness and future business performance. ｜ 与交易风险相比，经营风险还可能影响企业的长期竞争能力和未来经营表现。

------

## Section 2: Foreign Exchange Futures and Their Applications ｜ 第二节 外汇期货及其应用

### Examination Topic: Foreign Exchange Futures Hedging ｜ 考点：外汇期货套期保值

#### I. Definition of Foreign Exchange Futures ｜ 一、外汇期货的概念

Foreign exchange futures are standardised contracts under which the parties agree to deliver a specified amount of a specified currency at an agreed exchange rate on a future date. ｜ 外汇期货是指交易双方约定在未来某一时间，按照约定的币种、金额和汇率进行交割的标准化期货合约。

------

#### II. Short Hedging with Foreign Exchange Futures ｜ 二、外汇期货卖出套期保值

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

A short foreign exchange futures hedge is used by a trader who holds or expects to receive foreign currency and is therefore in a long position in the spot foreign exchange market. ｜ 外汇期货卖出套期保值又称外汇期货空头套期保值，适用于在现汇市场持有或者即将收到外币、处于现汇多头地位的交易者。

To protect against depreciation of the foreign currency, the trader sells foreign exchange futures. ｜ 为了防止该外币贬值，交易者在外汇期货市场卖出相应的期货合约。
$$
\text{Hold or expect to receive foreign currency}
\Rightarrow
\text{Sell foreign exchange futures}
$$

##### (II) Suitable Situations ｜ （二）适用情形

A short hedge is suitable for holders of foreign-currency assets who are concerned that the currency may depreciate. ｜ 持有外币资产并担心该货币未来贬值的投资者，适合采用卖出套期保值。

It is also suitable for exporters and banks engaged in international business that expect to receive foreign currency in the future. ｜ 预计未来将收到一笔外汇的出口商或者从事国际业务的银行，也适合采用卖出套期保值。

------

##### (III) Case ｜ （三）案例

A US investor found that Chinese interest rates were higher than US dollar interest rates and decided to purchase RMB 6.3 million for a three-month investment. ｜ 某美国投资者发现中国利率高于美元利率，因此决定购买630万元人民币并投资3个月。

The investor was concerned that the renminbi might depreciate against the US dollar during the investment period. ｜ 该投资者担心投资期间人民币相对于美元贬值。

The investor therefore sold renminbi futures on the Chicago Mercantile Exchange. According to the source, ten futures contracts represented approximately USD 1 million. ｜ 因此，该投资者在芝加哥商业交易所卖出人民币期货。根据原材料，10手期货合约代表约100万美元。

| Market                  | Opening position | Closing position | Stated result       |
| ----------------------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | ------------------- |
| Spot market, USD/CNY    | 6.2974           | 6.2985           | Loss of USD 2,000   |
| Futures market, CNY/USD | Sell at 0.1493   | Buy at 0.1471    | Profit of USD 2,200 |

| 市场              | 建仓       | 平仓       | 原材料列示结果 |
| ----------------- | ---------- | ---------- | -------------- |
| 即期市场，USD/CNY | 6.2974     | 6.2985     | 亏损0.2万美元  |
| 期货市场，CNY/USD | 卖出0.1493 | 买入0.1471 | 盈利0.22万美元 |

According to the source, the futures profit approximately offset the loss caused by the depreciation of the renminbi. ｜ 根据原材料，期货市场的盈利基本抵销了人民币贬值造成的即期市场损失。

> 原表列示的即期市场盈亏，与所给汇率及金额进行精确换算时并不完全一致，因此这里保留教材结果，不重新推算。

------

#### III. Long Hedging with Foreign Exchange Futures ｜ 三、外汇期货买入套期保值

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

A long foreign exchange futures hedge is used by a trader who must purchase or pay foreign currency in the future and is therefore in a short position in the spot foreign exchange market. ｜ 外汇期货买入套期保值又称外汇期货多头套期保值，适用于未来需要购买或者支付外币、在现汇市场处于空头地位的交易者。

To protect against an appreciation of the foreign currency, the trader buys foreign exchange futures. ｜ 为了防止该外币升值，交易者在外汇期货市场买入相应的外汇期货合约。
$$
\text{Future foreign-currency payment}
\Rightarrow
\text{Buy foreign exchange futures}
$$

##### (II) Suitable Situations ｜ （二）适用情形

A long hedge is suitable for traders with short-term foreign-currency liabilities who are concerned that the relevant currency may appreciate. ｜ 持有短期外币负债并担心该外币未来升值的交易者，适合采用买入套期保值。

It is also suitable for importers who are concerned that the foreign currency they must pay may appreciate before the payment date. ｜ 担心付汇时外币汇率上涨的进口商，也适合采用买入套期保值。

------

##### (III) Case ｜ （三）案例

On 1 June, a US importer expected to pay JPY 250 million for imported goods three months later. ｜ 6月1日，某美国进口商预计在3个月后支付2.5亿日元的进口货款。

The current spot exchange rate was: ｜ 当时的即期汇率为：
$$
\text{USD/JPY}=119.65
$$
This means that one US dollar could be exchanged for 119.65 Japanese yen. ｜ 这表示1美元可以兑换119.65日元。

The importer was concerned that the yen might appreciate, requiring more US dollars to purchase JPY 250 million. ｜ 该进口商担心日元升值，导致未来需要支付更多美元才能兑换2.5亿日元。

The importer therefore bought 20 September Japanese yen futures contracts. Each contract represented JPY 12.5 million. ｜ 因此，进口商买入20手9月份到期的日元期货合约，每手合约代表1250万日元。
$$
20\times12.5\text{ million JPY}
=
250\text{ million JPY}
$$

| Market                  | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss       |
| ----------------------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------------- |
| Spot market, USD/JPY    | 119.65           | 117.25           | Loss of USD 42,769   |
| Futures market, JPY/USD | Buy at 0.007957  | Sell at 0.008126 | Profit of USD 42,250 |

| 市场              | 建仓         | 平仓         | 盈亏          |
| ----------------- | ------------ | ------------ | ------------- |
| 即期市场，USD/JPY | 119.65       | 117.25       | 亏损42769美元 |
| 期货市场，JPY/USD | 买入0.007957 | 卖出0.008126 | 盈利42250美元 |

The net result was: ｜ 最终净结果为：
$$
42250-42769=-519\text{美元}
$$
Ignoring transaction costs and other basis effects, the futures profit offset most of the additional spot-market payment caused by the appreciation of the yen. ｜ 在不考虑交易费用和其他基差影响的情况下，期货市场盈利抵销了日元升值导致的大部分现汇市场损失。

------

#### IV. Cross Hedging with Foreign Exchange Futures ｜ 四、外汇期货交叉套期保值

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

Cross hedging is used when a trader faces exchange-rate risk involving a currency pair for which no directly corresponding futures contract is available. ｜ 当交易者面临某一货币对的汇率风险，但市场中没有直接对应的外汇期货合约时，可以采用交叉套期保值。

The trader uses one or more closely related foreign exchange futures contracts to hedge the original currency exposure. ｜ 交易者利用一种或者多种相关性较高的外汇期货合约，对原有的外汇风险进行套期保值。

When the risk involves two non-US-dollar currencies, futures contracts against the US dollar may be combined to construct the hedge. ｜ 如果风险涉及两种非美元货币，可以将这两种货币各自对美元的期货合约组合起来进行保值。

------

##### (II) Important Considerations ｜ （二）注意事项

The futures contract used for cross hedging should have a strong correlation with the currency exposure being hedged. ｜ 承担保值任务的外汇期货合约，应当与被保值货币具有较高的相关性。

Only a highly correlated futures contract can effectively offset changes in the value of the spot foreign currency. ｜ 只有价格变动相关程度较高的期货品种，才适合作为现汇风险的保值工具。

The number of futures contracts must also be adjusted correctly so that the futures exposure matches the size of the hedged position. ｜ 还需要正确调整期货合约数量，使期货头寸规模与被保值对象相匹配。

------

##### (III) Case ｜ （三）案例

On 1 March, a Chinese exporter sold clothing worth NOK 20 million to a Norwegian importer. ｜ 3月1日，国内某出口商向挪威进口商销售了一批价值2000万挪威克朗的服装。

Payment would be received in Norwegian kroner three months later. ｜ 货款将在3个月后以挪威克朗结算。

The current spot exchange rate was: ｜ 当时的即期汇率为：
$$
\text{NOK/CNY}=0.7998
$$
The exporter was concerned that the Norwegian krone might depreciate against the renminbi. ｜ 出口商担心3个月后挪威克朗相对于人民币贬值，从而使收到货款后能够兑换的人民币减少。

Because no direct NOK/CNY futures contract was available, the exporter used CNY/USD and NOK/USD futures on the CME. ｜ 由于市场中没有直接对应的NOK/CNY期货合约，出口商使用CME的CNY/USD和NOK/USD期货进行交叉套期保值。

The exporter: ｜ 出口商采取以下操作：

- Bought 16 CNY/USD futures contracts ｜ 买入16手CNY/USD期货合约
- Sold 10 NOK/USD futures contracts ｜ 卖出10手NOK/USD期货合约

The contract sizes were: ｜ 合约规模为：

- CNY/USD: RMB 1 million per contract ｜ CNY/USD：每手100万元人民币
- NOK/USD: NOK 2 million per contract ｜ NOK/USD：每手200万挪威克朗

The NOK futures position therefore matched the NOK 20 million receivable: ｜ NOK期货头寸与2000万挪威克朗应收款相匹配：
$$
10\times2\text{ million NOK}
=
20\text{ million NOK}
$$

------

###### Opening Positions on 1 March ｜ 3月1日建仓

The spot value of the receivable was: ｜ 应收款对应的人民币价值为：
$$
20{,}000{,}000\times0.7998
=
15{,}996{,}000\text{ CNY}
$$
The CNY/USD futures price was 0.1618, and the exporter bought 16 contracts. ｜ CNY/USD期货价格为0.1618，出口商买入16手。

The NOK/USD futures price was 0.1292, and the exporter sold 10 contracts. ｜ NOK/USD期货价格为0.1292，出口商卖出10手。

------

###### Closing Positions on 1 June ｜ 6月1日平仓

The NOK/CNY spot exchange rate declined to 0.7958. ｜ NOK/CNY即期汇率下降至0.7958。

The value of the NOK 20 million receivable became: ｜ 2000万挪威克朗应收款对应的人民币价值变为：
$$
20{,}000{,}000\times0.7958
=
15{,}916{,}000\text{ CNY}
$$
The spot-market loss was: ｜ 现货市场损失为：
$$
1599.6-1591.6
=
8\text{万元人民币}
$$
The CNY/USD futures price declined from 0.1618 to 0.1613. ｜ CNY/USD期货价格由0.1618下降至0.1613。

Because the exporter had bought these futures, the loss was: ｜ 由于出口商持有CNY/USD期货多头，因此产生亏损：
$$
(0.1613-0.1618)
\times1{,}000{,}000
\times16
=
-8000\text{美元}
$$
The NOK/USD futures price declined from 0.1292 to 0.1281. ｜ NOK/USD期货价格由0.1292下降至0.1281。

Because the exporter had sold these futures, the profit was: ｜ 由于出口商持有NOK/USD期货空头，因此获得盈利：
$$
(0.1292-0.1281)
\times2{,}000{,}000
\times10
=
22000\text{美元}
$$
The total futures profit was: ｜ 期货市场净盈利为：
$$
22000-8000
=
14000\text{美元}
$$
Using the exchange rate stated in the source: ｜ 按照原材料给出的汇率：
$$
\text{USD/CNY}=6.21
$$
The futures profit was equivalent to: ｜ 期货净盈利折合人民币为：
$$
14000\times6.21
=
86940\text{元}
$$
The futures profit of RMB 86,940 offset the spot-market loss of RMB 80,000. ｜ 期货市场盈利8.694万元，抵销了现货市场8万元的损失。

The remaining net gain was approximately: ｜ 最终净收益约为：
$$
86940-80000
=
6940\text{元}
$$
This difference arises because cross hedging cannot normally achieve a perfect match between the spot exposure and the futures positions. ｜ 出现这一差额，是因为交叉套期保值中现货风险与期货头寸通常无法做到完全匹配。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

💱 **Foreign exchange consists of foreign-currency payment instruments and assets used for international settlement.** ｜ 💱 **外汇是以外币表示、可以用于国际清偿的支付手段和资产。**

🔢 **Direct quotation expresses foreign currency in domestic currency, while indirect quotation expresses domestic currency in foreign currency.** ｜ 🔢 **直接标价法以本币表示外币价格，间接标价法以外币表示本币价格。**

🌍 **The foreign exchange market operates across global financial centres and can trade continuously for approximately 24 hours.** ｜ 🌍 **外汇市场连接全球金融中心，基本可以实现24小时连续交易。**

📊 **Exchange rates are affected by the balance of payments, inflation, interest-rate differences, expectations and government policies.** ｜ 📊 **汇率受到国际收支、通货膨胀、利率差异、市场预期和政府政策等因素影响。**

⚠️ **Foreign exchange risk includes transaction risk, accounting risk and operating risk.** ｜ ⚠️ **外汇风险主要包括交易风险、会计风险和经营风险。**

📉 **Holding or expecting to receive foreign currency → sell foreign exchange futures.** ｜ 📉 **持有或者即将收到外币 → 卖出外汇期货进行套期保值。**

📈 **Needing to purchase or pay foreign currency in the future → buy foreign exchange futures.** ｜ 📈 **未来需要购买或者支付外币 → 买入外汇期货进行套期保值。**

🔄 **Cross hedging uses related futures contracts when no direct futures contract exists for the original currency exposure.** ｜ 🔄 **当市场中没有直接对应的外汇期货合约时，可以使用相关期货合约进行交叉套期保值。**

🎯 **Effective cross hedging requires both high correlation and an appropriate number of futures contracts.** ｜ 🎯 **有效的交叉套期保值要求期货品种相关性较高，并且合约数量与被保值风险相匹配。**



### Examination Topic: Foreign Exchange Futures Arbitrage ｜ 考点：外汇期货套利交易

#### I. Definition and Types of Foreign Exchange Futures Arbitrage ｜ 一、外汇期货套利的概念与种类

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）外汇期货套利的概念

Foreign exchange futures arbitrage refers to a trading strategy in which a trader identifies an abnormal deviation from the theoretical price relationship among different markets, delivery months or currencies and simultaneously buys and sells related foreign exchange futures contracts. ｜ 外汇期货套利是指交易者根据不同市场、不同交割期限或者不同币种之间理论价差的异常变化，同时买入和卖出相关的外汇期货合约。

The trader closes the related positions simultaneously after the spread moves in a favourable direction and earns a profit from the change in the spread. ｜ 当相关合约之间的价差向有利方向变化后，交易者同时对冲平仓，并从价差变化中获取收益。

##### (II) Types ｜ （二）外汇期货套利的种类

Foreign exchange futures arbitrage mainly includes: ｜ 外汇期货套利主要包括：

- Foreign exchange cash-futures arbitrage ｜ 外汇期现套利
- Foreign exchange futures calendar spread arbitrage ｜ 外汇期货跨期套利

It may also include cross-currency and inter-market arbitrage. ｜ 此外，还包括跨币种套利和跨市场套利。

------

#### II. Foreign Exchange Cash-Futures Arbitrage ｜ 二、外汇期现套利

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

Foreign exchange cash-futures arbitrage involves simultaneously establishing opposite positions in the foreign exchange spot market and futures market. ｜ 外汇期现套利是指在外汇现货市场与外汇期货市场同时建立方向相反的交易头寸。

The trader sells the overvalued spot currency or futures contract and simultaneously buys the undervalued spot currency or futures contract. ｜ 具体操作是卖出被高估的外汇现货或者期货合约，同时买入被低估的外汇现货或者期货合约。

The trader earns a profit when the difference between the spot and futures prices returns to a reasonable level. ｜ 当期现价差恢复至合理水平时，交易者通过同时平仓获取收益。

------

##### (II) Foreign Exchange Futures Transaction Costs ｜ （二）外汇期货交易成本

Foreign exchange futures transaction costs mainly include: ｜ 外汇期货交易成本主要包括：

- Commissions charged by the futures exchange and futures broker ｜ 交易所和期货经纪商收取的佣金
- Transfer fees charged by the central clearing institution ｜ 中央结算机构收取的过户费

When determining whether an arbitrage opportunity exists, the trader must compare the expected spread profit with total transaction costs. ｜ 判断是否存在套利机会时，交易者需要将预期价差收益与全部交易成本进行比较。

------

##### (III) Case ｜ （三）案例

On 1 March, the spot exchange rate of the US dollar against the renminbi was: ｜ 3月1日，美元兑人民币的即期汇率为：
$$
\text{USD/CNY}=6.1130
$$
The June USD/CNY futures price was 6.1190. ｜ 6月份美元兑人民币期货价格为6.1190。

The cash-futures spread was: ｜ 期现价差为：
$$
6.1190-6.1130=0.0060
$$
Because one point equals 0.0001, the spread was 60 points. ｜ 由于一个点等于0.0001，因此期现价差为60个点。

The trader estimated that the theoretical June futures price was 6.1160 and that the no-arbitrage range was approximately 6.1150–6.1170. ｜ 交易者估算6月份外汇期货的理论价格为6.1160，无套利区间约为6.1150～6.1170。

Because the actual futures price of 6.1190 was above the no-arbitrage range, the trader concluded that the futures contract was overvalued and expected the cash-futures spread to contract. ｜ 由于实际期货价格6.1190高于无套利区间，交易者判断期货合约被高估，并预计期现价差将缩小。

The trader therefore: ｜ 因此，交易者采取以下操作：

- Bought the corresponding amount of US dollars in the spot market at 6.1130 ｜ 在现货市场以6.1130买入相应金额的美元
- Sold 10 June USD/CNY futures contracts at 6.1190 ｜ 在期货市场以6.1190卖出10手6月份美元兑人民币期货合约

On 1 April, the spot and futures prices changed to 6.1140 and 6.1180 respectively. ｜ 4月1日，现货价格和期货价格分别变为6.1140和6.1180。

The new spread was: ｜ 新的期现价差为：
$$
6.1180-6.1140=0.0040
$$
The spread contracted by: ｜ 价差缩小了：
$$
60-40=20\text{个点}
$$

| Market  | Opening position | Closing position | Profit    |
| ------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | --------- |
| Spot    | Buy at 6.1130    | Sell at 6.1140   | 10 points |
| Futures | Sell at 6.1190   | Buy at 6.1180    | 10 points |

| 市场     | 建仓       | 平仓       | 盈利   |
| -------- | ---------- | ---------- | ------ |
| 现货市场 | 买入6.1130 | 卖出6.1140 | 10个点 |
| 期货市场 | 卖出6.1190 | 买入6.1180 | 10个点 |

According to the contract-value relationship given in the source, the total profit was: ｜ 按照原材料给出的合约价值关系，总盈利为：
$$
10\times10\times20
=
2000\text{美元}
$$
The trader earned USD 2,000 before deducting transaction costs. ｜ 在扣除交易成本之前，该交易者盈利2000美元。

------

#### III. Foreign Exchange Futures Calendar Spread Arbitrage ｜ 三、外汇期货跨期套利

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

Foreign exchange futures calendar spread arbitrage refers to simultaneously buying and selling futures contracts on the same currency but with different delivery months. ｜ 外汇期货跨期套利是指同时买入和卖出同一币种、不同交割月份的外汇期货合约。

The trader closes both positions after the spread between the contracts moves in a favourable direction. ｜ 当两个合约之间的价差向有利方向变化后，交易者同时平仓获取收益。

------

##### (II) Bull Spread Arbitrage ｜ （二）牛市套利

A foreign exchange futures bull spread generally involves buying the nearby-month contract and selling the distant-month contract. ｜ 外汇期货牛市套利通常是买入近月合约，同时卖出远月合约。

The strategy earns a profit when the spread between the distant and nearby contracts contracts. ｜ 当远月合约与近月合约之间的价差缩小时，该策略获得盈利。

###### Case ｜ 案例

On 15 January, the March EUR/USD futures price was 1.1138, while the June futures price was 1.1226. ｜ 1月15日，3月份欧元兑美元期货价格为1.1138，6月份合约价格为1.1226。

The opening spread was: ｜ 建仓时的价差为：
$$
1.1226-1.1138=0.0088
$$
The trader expected the EUR/USD exchange rate to rise and the spread between the March and June contracts to contract. ｜ 交易者预计欧元兑美元汇率将上涨，同时3月份与6月份合约之间的价差将缩小。

The trader therefore bought 10 March contracts and simultaneously sold 10 June contracts. ｜ 因此，交易者买入10手3月份欧元兑美元期货合约，同时卖出10手6月份合约。

On 30 January, the March and June contract prices rose to 1.1181 and 1.1236 respectively. ｜ 到1月30日，3月份和6月份合约价格分别上涨至1.1181和1.1236。

The closing spread was: ｜ 平仓时的价差为：
$$
1.1236-1.1181=0.0055
$$
The spread contracted by: ｜ 价差缩小了：
$$
88-55=33\text{个点}
$$

| Contract | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss |
| -------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------- |
| March    | Buy at 1.1138    | Sell at 1.1181   | +43 points     |
| June     | Sell at 1.1226   | Buy at 1.1236    | −10 points     |

| 合约  | 建仓       | 平仓       | 盈亏       |
| ----- | ---------- | ---------- | ---------- |
| 3月份 | 买入1.1138 | 卖出1.1181 | 盈利43个点 |
| 6月份 | 卖出1.1226 | 买入1.1236 | 亏损10个点 |

The net profit was 33 points. ｜ 净盈利为33个点。

According to the point value stated in the source: ｜ 按照原材料给出的点值：
$$
12.50\times10\times33
=
4125\text{美元}
$$
The total arbitrage profit was USD 4,125. ｜ 该套利交易总盈利为4125美元。

------

##### (III) Bear Spread Arbitrage ｜ （三）熊市套利

A foreign exchange futures bear spread generally involves selling the nearby-month contract and buying the distant-month contract. ｜ 外汇期货熊市套利通常是卖出近月合约，同时买入远月合约。

The strategy earns a profit when the spread between the distant and nearby contracts expands. ｜ 当远月合约与近月合约之间的价差扩大时，该策略获得盈利。

###### Case ｜ 案例

On 15 January, the March GBP/USD futures price was 1.5017, while the June futures price was 1.5094. ｜ 1月15日，3月份英镑兑美元期货价格为1.5017，6月份合约价格为1.5094。

The opening spread was: ｜ 建仓时的价差为：
$$
1.5094-1.5017=0.0077
$$
The trader expected the GBP/USD exchange rate to rise and the spread between the March and June contracts to expand. ｜ 交易者预计英镑兑美元汇率将上涨，同时3月份与6月份合约之间的价差将扩大。

The trader therefore sold 50 March contracts and simultaneously bought 50 June contracts. ｜ 因此，交易者卖出50手3月份英镑兑美元期货合约，同时买入50手6月份合约。

On 30 January, the March and June contract prices rose to 1.5043 and 1.5135 respectively. ｜ 到1月30日，3月份和6月份合约价格分别上涨至1.5043和1.5135。

The closing spread was: ｜ 平仓时的价差为：
$$
1.5135-1.5043=0.0092
$$
The spread expanded by: ｜ 价差扩大了：
$$
92-77=15\text{个点}
$$

| Contract | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss |
| -------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------- |
| March    | Sell at 1.5017   | Buy at 1.5043    | −26 points     |
| June     | Buy at 1.5094    | Sell at 1.5135   | +41 points     |

| 合约  | 建仓       | 平仓       | 盈亏       |
| ----- | ---------- | ---------- | ---------- |
| 3月份 | 卖出1.5017 | 买入1.5043 | 亏损26个点 |
| 6月份 | 买入1.5094 | 卖出1.5135 | 盈利41个点 |

The net profit was: ｜ 净盈利为：
$$
-26+41=15\text{个点}
$$
According to the point value given in the source: ｜ 按照原材料给出的点值：
$$
6.25\times50\times15
=
4687.5\text{美元}
$$
The total arbitrage profit was USD 4,687.50. ｜ 该套利交易总盈利为4687.50美元。

------

##### (IV) Butterfly Spread Arbitrage ｜ （四）蝶式套利

A butterfly spread combines three futures contracts with different delivery months. ｜ 蝶式套利同时使用三个不同交割月份的外汇期货合约。

The number of middle-month contracts generally equals the combined number of nearby- and distant-month contracts. ｜ 居中月份合约的数量通常等于近月合约和远月合约数量之和。

###### Case ｜ 案例

On 15 January, the March, June and September USD/CNY futures prices were 6.2849, 6.2832 and 6.2821 respectively. ｜ 1月15日，3月份、6月份和9月份美元兑人民币期货价格分别为6.2849、6.2832和6.2821。

The trader expected the spread between March and June to contract and the spread between June and September to expand. ｜ 交易者预计3月份与6月份合约之间的价差将缩小，同时6月份与9月份合约之间的价差将扩大。

The trader therefore: ｜ 因此，交易者进行以下操作：

- Sold 50 March contracts at 6.2849 ｜ 以6.2849卖出50手3月份合约
- Bought 100 June contracts at 6.2832 ｜ 以6.2832买入100手6月份合约
- Sold 50 September contracts at 6.2821 ｜ 以6.2821卖出50手9月份合约

The quantity relationship was: ｜ 合约数量关系为：
$$
100=50+50
$$
On 30 January, the March, June and September prices fell to 6.2829, 6.2822 and 6.2807 respectively. ｜ 到1月30日，3月份、6月份和9月份合约价格分别下跌至6.2829、6.2822和6.2807。

| Contract  | Quantity | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss |
| --------- | -------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------- |
| March     | 50       | Sell at 6.2849   | Buy at 6.2829    | +20 points     |
| June      | 100      | Buy at 6.2832    | Sell at 6.2822   | −10 points     |
| September | 50       | Sell at 6.2821   | Buy at 6.2807    | +14 points     |

| 合约  | 数量  | 建仓       | 平仓       | 盈亏       |
| ----- | ----- | ---------- | ---------- | ---------- |
| 3月份 | 50手  | 卖出6.2849 | 买入6.2829 | 盈利20个点 |
| 6月份 | 100手 | 买入6.2832 | 卖出6.2822 | 亏损10个点 |
| 9月份 | 50手  | 卖出6.2821 | 买入6.2807 | 盈利14个点 |

According to the source, each point per contract was worth USD 10. ｜ 根据原材料，每手合约每个点对应10美元。

The total profit was: ｜ 总盈利为：
$$
10\times50\times20
-
10\times100\times10
+
10\times50\times14
$$
The butterfly spread generated a total profit of USD 7,000. ｜ 该蝶式套利交易总盈利为7000美元。

------

#### IV. Other Foreign Exchange Futures Arbitrage Strategies ｜ 四、外汇期货其他套利

##### (I) Cross-Currency Arbitrage ｜ （一）跨币种套利

Cross-currency futures arbitrage is based on the expected relative price movements of futures contracts with the same delivery month but different underlying currencies on the same exchange. ｜ 外汇期货跨币种套利，是指交易者根据同一交易所内交割月份相同、但币种不同的外汇期货合约之间的相对价格走势进行套利。

The trader buys the futures contract on one currency and simultaneously sells the futures contract on another currency with the same delivery month. ｜ 交易者买入一种货币的期货合约，同时卖出另一种货币相同交割月份的期货合约。

The profit depends on the favourable change in the relative price relationship between the two currencies. ｜ 套利收益取决于两种货币期货价格关系是否按照预期发生有利变化。

------

##### (II) Inter-Market Arbitrage ｜ （二）跨市场套利

Foreign exchange futures inter-market arbitrage is based on price differences for the same foreign exchange futures contract traded on different exchanges. ｜ 外汇期货跨市场套利，是指利用同一种外汇期货合约在不同交易所之间的价格差异进行套利。

The trader buys the contract on one exchange and simultaneously sells the same contract on another exchange. ｜ 交易者在一个交易所买入某种外汇期货合约，同时在另一个交易所卖出同一种外汇期货合约。

The positions are later closed when the price difference between the exchanges changes in a favourable direction. ｜ 当两个交易所之间的价差向有利方向变化后，交易者同时平仓获利。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

⚖️ **Foreign exchange futures arbitrage profits from abnormal price relationships among markets, delivery months or currencies.** ｜ ⚖️ **外汇期货套利利用不同市场、期限或者币种之间异常的价格关系获利。**

🔄 **Cash-futures arbitrage buys the undervalued side and sells the overvalued side.** ｜ 🔄 **外汇期现套利买入被低估的一方，同时卖出被高估的一方。**

📏 **The existence of an arbitrage opportunity must be judged after considering transaction costs and the no-arbitrage range.** ｜ 📏 **判断套利机会时，需要考虑交易成本和无套利区间。**

🐂 **Foreign exchange futures bull spread: buy the nearby contract and sell the distant contract, benefiting from spread contraction.** ｜ 🐂 **外汇期货牛市套利：买入近月、卖出远月，在价差缩小时获利。**

🐻 **Foreign exchange futures bear spread: sell the nearby contract and buy the distant contract, benefiting from spread expansion.** ｜ 🐻 **外汇期货熊市套利：卖出近月、买入远月，在价差扩大时获利。**

🦋 **A butterfly spread uses three delivery months, with the middle-month quantity equal to the sum of the other two quantities.** ｜ 🦋 **蝶式套利使用三个交割月份，居中月份合约数量等于另外两个合约数量之和。**

💱 **Cross-currency arbitrage trades different currencies with the same delivery month on the same exchange.** ｜ 💱 **跨币种套利在同一交易所交易相同交割月份、不同币种的期货合约。**

🌐 **Inter-market arbitrage trades the same foreign exchange futures contract on different exchanges.** ｜ 🌐 **跨市场套利在不同交易所交易同一种外汇期货合约。**

### Examination Topic: Delivery of Foreign Exchange Futures and Their Application in Portfolios ｜ 考点：外汇期货交割及其在资产组合中的应用

#### I. Delivery of Foreign Exchange Futures ｜ 一、外汇期货交割
