Foreign exchange futures may use either physical delivery or cash settlement. ｜ 外汇期货合约既可以采用实物交割，也可以采用现金交割。

According to the source, most foreign exchange futures contracts traded on the Chicago Mercantile Exchange use physical delivery, while only a smaller number use cash settlement. ｜ 根据原材料，芝加哥商业交易所的大部分外汇期货合约采用实物交割方式，只有少部分合约采用现金交割方式。

Under physical delivery, the seller delivers the specified foreign currency and the buyer pays the corresponding settlement amount according to the contract terms. ｜ 实物交割是指卖方按照合约规定交付相应的外币，买方支付相应的结算金额。

Under cash settlement, the parties do not actually exchange currencies. Instead, the profit or loss is settled in cash according to the difference between the contract price and the final settlement price. ｜ 现金交割不实际交换外币，而是根据合约价格与最终结算价格之间的差额进行现金结算。

------

#### II. Application of Foreign Exchange Futures in Portfolios ｜ 二、外汇期货在资产组合中的应用

##### (I) Alternative Investment and Risk Diversification ｜ （一）另类投资与风险分散

Foreign exchange futures and other exchange-rate products may be treated as alternative investments. ｜ 外汇期货及其他汇率类产品可以被视为一种另类投资工具。

Over the long term, their returns may have relatively low correlations with common investment assets such as equities and bonds. ｜ 从长期来看，外汇和汇率类产品与股票、债券等常见投资标的之间的相关性通常相对较低。

Institutional investors may therefore include foreign exchange products in their portfolios to diversify risk and reduce the portfolio’s overall exposure to systematic market movements. ｜ 因此，投资机构可以在资产组合中配置外汇或者汇率类产品，以分散风险并降低组合整体的系统性风险敞口。

##### (II) Macroeconomic Asset Allocation ｜ （二）宏观经济资产配置

Exchange rates are affected by a country’s economic growth, inflation, interest rates, monetary policy and balance-of-payments conditions. ｜ 汇率会受到一国经济增长、通货膨胀、利率、货币政策和国际收支等宏观因素影响。

After analysing the macroeconomic conditions of a particular country or the global economy, investors may allocate foreign exchange or exchange-rate products according to their views. ｜ 投资者在研究某一国家或者全球宏观经济形势后，可以根据对经济和汇率走势的判断配置外汇或者汇率类产品。

📌 **Foreign exchange products can be used both for diversification and for expressing macroeconomic views.** ｜ 📌 **外汇产品既可以用于分散投资风险，也可以用于表达对宏观经济走势的判断。**

------

### Examination Topic: Foreign Exchange Margin Trading ｜ 考点：外汇保证金交易

#### I. Definition of Foreign Exchange Margin Trading ｜ 一、外汇保证金交易的定义

Foreign exchange spot trading may be divided into fully funded trading and margin trading. ｜ 外汇现货交易可以分为实盘交易和保证金交易。

Margin trading is also called notional or leveraged foreign exchange trading. ｜ 外汇保证金交易又称虚盘交易或者杠杆外汇交易。

##### (I) Fully Funded Foreign Exchange Trading ｜ （一）实盘交易

Fully funded foreign exchange trading refers to the exchange of currencies or currency equivalents actually held by both parties. ｜ 实盘交易是指交易双方以实际持有的货币或者货币等价物进行兑换。

For example, exchanging renminbi for foreign currency at a bank is a form of fully funded foreign exchange trading. ｜ 例如，个人使用人民币在银行兑换外币，就是一种实盘交易。

Institutional fully funded transactions mainly occur between banks or between banks and large clients represented by banks. ｜ 机构之间的实盘交易，主要发生在银行之间，或者银行与其代理的大型客户之间。

According to the source, settlement is generally completed within two business days after the transaction. ｜ 根据原材料，这类交易通常在成交后的两个营业日内完成资金交割。

------

##### (II) Foreign Exchange Margin Trading ｜ （二）外汇保证金交易

Foreign exchange margin trading developed from the margin system used in futures trading. ｜ 外汇保证金交易是在期货保证金交易制度基础上发展而来的。

The trader signs a foreign exchange trading agreement with a bank, foreign exchange dealer or broker and deposits a specified percentage of the contract amount as margin. ｜ 交易者与银行、外汇交易商或者经纪商签订外汇买卖协议，并按照合同金额的一定比例缴纳保证金。

According to the source, the margin is generally no more than 10% of the transaction amount. ｜ 根据原材料，保证金比例通常不超过交易金额的10%。

The trader can therefore control a foreign exchange position several or even dozens of times larger than the deposited margin. ｜ 因此，交易者可以利用杠杆，控制数倍甚至数十倍于保证金金额的外汇头寸。
$$
\text{Leverage}
=
\frac{\text{Total position value}}
{\text{Margin deposited}}
$$
Margin trading can magnify both profits and losses. ｜ 保证金交易既会放大盈利，也会放大亏损。

------

#### II. Differences Between Foreign Exchange Margin Trading and Foreign Exchange Futures ｜ 二、外汇保证金交易与外汇期货交易的区别

| Item                   | Foreign exchange spot margin trading           | Foreign exchange futures                                     |
| ---------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Trading venue          | Over-the-counter market                        | Exchange-traded market                                       |
| Counterparty structure | Market-maker trading                           | Central clearing institution                                 |
| Contract form          | More flexible                                  | Standardised contracts                                       |
| Trading quantity       | May allow non-integer lot sizes                | Usually based on standard contract units                     |
| Trading method         | Margin trading                                 | Margin trading                                               |
| Expiry date            | Usually no fixed expiry date                   | Multiple specified expiry dates                              |
| Settlement             | Daily settlement                               | Daily settlement                                             |
| Position termination   | Mainly cash settlement or closing the position | Closing, cash settlement or physical delivery depending on contract |
| Tradable currencies    | A wide range of currencies                     | Mainly major listed currencies                               |
| Trading hours          | Approximately 24 hours on business days        | Exchange trading hours                                       |

| 项目         | 外汇现货保证金交易           | 外汇期货交易                         |
| ------------ | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------ |
| 交易场所     | 场外市场                     | 交易所市场                           |
| 交易对手结构 | 做市商交易                   | 中央结算机构负责结算                 |
| 合约形式     | 合约较灵活                   | 标准化合约                           |
| 交易数量     | 可以允许非整数手数           | 通常按照标准合约单位交易             |
| 交易方式     | 保证金交易                   | 保证金交易                           |
| 到期日       | 通常没有固定到期日           | 设有多个明确到期日                   |
| 结算方式     | 每日结算                     | 每日结算                             |
| 合约结束方式 | 主要通过现金结算或者反向平仓 | 根据合约采用平仓、现金交割或实物交割 |
| 交易币种     | 可以交易较多国家的货币       | 主要是交易所挂牌的少数主要货币       |
| 交易时间     | 工作日接近24小时             | 按照交易所营业时间交易               |

📌 **Margin trading is an OTC product, while foreign exchange futures are standardised exchange-traded contracts.** ｜ 📌 **外汇保证金交易属于场外交易，外汇期货属于交易所内的标准化交易。**

------

### Examination Topic: Foreign Exchange Arbitrage ｜ 考点：外汇套汇交易

#### I. Definition of Foreign Exchange Arbitrage ｜ 一、套汇交易的定义

Foreign exchange arbitrage uses price differences for the same currency in different foreign exchange markets or among different dealers. ｜ 套汇交易是指利用同一种货币在不同外汇市场或者不同外汇交易商之间的报价差异进行交易。

The trader buys the currency in the lower-priced market and simultaneously sells it in the higher-priced market. ｜ 交易者在报价较低的市场买入外汇，同时在报价较高的市场卖出外汇。

The difference between the two prices becomes the arbitrage profit. ｜ 两个市场之间的价格差额就是套汇收益。
$$
\text{Arbitrage profit}
=
\text{Higher selling price}
-
\text{Lower buying price}
$$

------

#### II. Case ｜ 二、案例

Suppose one foreign exchange dealer quotes a EUR/USD ask price of 1.3200, while another dealer quotes a EUR/USD bid price of 1.3201. ｜ 假设一家外汇交易商对欧元兑美元的卖出价报价为1.3200，另一家交易商的买入价报价为1.3201。

The trader can buy euros from the first dealer at 1.3200 and simultaneously sell euros to the second dealer at 1.3201. ｜ 交易者可以在第一家交易商处以1.3200买入欧元，同时在第二家交易商处以1.3201卖出欧元。

The profit is: ｜ 盈利为：
$$
1.3201-1.3200=0.0001
$$
That is one point. ｜ 即盈利1个点。

Ignoring transaction costs and execution risk, this is a risk-free arbitrage profit. ｜ 在不考虑交易费用和执行风险的情况下，这属于无风险套汇收益。

------

#### III. Conditions for Foreign Exchange Arbitrage Opportunities ｜ 三、外汇套汇机会

##### (I) Different Market Quotations ｜ （一）市场报价存在差异

The existence of price differences among markets or dealers is the basic condition for direct or indirect foreign exchange arbitrage. ｜ 无论是直接套汇还是间接套汇，不同市场或者交易商之间存在汇率报价差异，都是套汇机会产生的基本前提。

However, modern foreign exchange dealers have mature quotation systems and can rapidly adjust prices according to market conditions. ｜ 但是，现代外汇交易商通常拥有较成熟的报价系统，可以根据市场行情迅速调整报价。

Therefore, obvious arbitrage opportunities occur relatively infrequently. ｜ 因此，明显的套汇机会出现概率通常较低。

##### (II) Professional Knowledge and Rapid Execution ｜ （二）专业知识与快速执行能力

Arbitrage traders need sufficient professional knowledge and technical experience to identify price differences and execute correct transactions immediately. ｜ 套汇交易者需要具备一定的专业知识和技术经验，才能及时识别汇率差异并迅速完成正确的交易操作。

Once an arbitrage opportunity appears, other traders usually enter the market quickly. ｜ 套汇机会一旦出现，其他套汇交易者通常也会迅速进入市场。

Their transactions cause the price difference to disappear rapidly. ｜ 这些交易会推动不同市场价格迅速趋于一致，使套汇机会很快消失。

Therefore, foreign exchange arbitrage opportunities are usually short-lived. ｜ 因此，外汇套汇机会通常稍纵即逝。

For ordinary retail traders, the required speed, information access and execution capabilities may be difficult to achieve. ｜ 对于普通散户交易者而言，所需的信息获取速度、技术能力和执行条件通常较难具备。

------

#### IV. Differences Between Arbitrage Trading and Foreign Exchange Arbitrage ｜ 四、外汇套利交易与套汇交易的区别

##### (I) Different Trading Objects ｜ （一）交易对象不同

Arbitrage trading generally occurs between two different contracts, such as futures contracts with different delivery months or different currencies. ｜ 套利交易通常在两个不同合约之间进行，例如不同交割月份或者不同币种的期货合约。

Foreign exchange arbitrage generally occurs between the same currency quoted in different markets or between three currencies connected by a triangular exchange-rate relationship. ｜ 套汇交易通常发生在不同市场中的同一种外汇之间，或者发生在存在三角汇率关系的三种不同货币之间。

------

##### (II) Different Transaction Timing ｜ （二）交易时间不同

Arbitrage trading involves opening positions when a spread is considered abnormal and closing them after the spread returns to a reasonable level. ｜ 套利交易是在价差偏离合理水平时建仓，经过一段时间后，待价差恢复正常再平仓。

Foreign exchange arbitrage involves almost simultaneous buying and selling. ｜ 套汇交易中的买入和卖出通常是瞬时且同时发生的。

------

##### (III) Different Sources of Profit and Risk ｜ （三）盈利来源与风险不同

Arbitrage trading depends on the trader’s prediction of how the spread will change in the future. ｜ 套利交易需要交易者判断未来价差的变化方向。

A profit is earned only if the spread subsequently moves as expected. ｜ 只有未来价差走势与交易者判断一致，套利交易才能获得盈利。

Foreign exchange arbitrage uses an already existing quotation difference. ｜ 套汇交易利用的是已经存在的报价差异。

If the transactions can be executed simultaneously at the observed prices, the market risk is relatively low. ｜ 如果交易者能够按照观察到的价格同时完成交易，套汇交易面临的市场风险相对较小。

However, actual trading may still involve execution delays, transaction costs and counterparty risk. ｜ 但实际交易中仍可能存在成交延迟、交易成本和交易对手风险。

------

##### (IV) Different Frequency of Opportunities ｜ （四）交易机会数量不同

According to the source, arbitrage trading opportunities generally occur more frequently than pure foreign exchange arbitrage opportunities. ｜ 根据原材料，套利交易机会通常多于单纯的外汇套汇机会。

This is because abnormal spreads between related contracts may remain for a period of time, while quotation differences between dealers are normally eliminated very quickly. ｜ 这是因为相关合约之间的异常价差可能持续一段时间，而不同交易商之间的报价差异通常会被迅速消除。

| Item                  | Arbitrage trading                      | Foreign exchange arbitrage                             |
| --------------------- | -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |
| Trading object        | Different related contracts            | Same currency across markets, or triangular currencies |
| Timing                | Open first and close later             | Buy and sell almost simultaneously                     |
| Profit source         | Future convergence or change in spread | Existing price difference                              |
| Market risk           | Depends on future spread movement      | Relatively low if executed simultaneously              |
| Opportunity frequency | Relatively greater                     | Relatively scarce and short-lived                      |

| 项目     | 套利交易                     | 套汇交易                                   |
| -------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ |
| 交易对象 | 不同但相关的合约             | 不同市场中的同种外汇，或具有三角关系的货币 |
| 交易时间 | 先建仓，之后再平仓           | 买卖几乎同时完成                           |
| 盈利来源 | 未来价差回归或变化           | 当前已经存在的报价差异                     |
| 市场风险 | 取决于未来价差是否按预期变化 | 同时成交时市场风险相对较小                 |
| 机会数量 | 相对较多                     | 较少且持续时间很短                         |

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

📦 **Foreign exchange futures may use physical delivery or cash settlement.** ｜ 📦 **外汇期货可以采用实物交割或者现金交割。**

🌐 **Foreign exchange products may diversify portfolios because their long-term correlation with equities and bonds may be relatively low.** ｜ 🌐 **外汇产品与股票、债券的长期相关性可能较低，可以用于分散投资组合风险。**

💹 **Foreign exchange margin trading uses leverage and may magnify both profits and losses.** ｜ 💹 **外汇保证金交易使用杠杆，会同时放大盈利和亏损。**

🏦 **Foreign exchange margin trading is conducted mainly in the OTC market, while foreign exchange futures are traded on exchanges.** ｜ 🏦 **外汇保证金交易主要在场外市场进行，外汇期货则在交易所内交易。**

🔄 **Foreign exchange arbitrage buys at a lower quotation and simultaneously sells at a higher quotation.** ｜ 🔄 **外汇套汇是在低价市场买入，同时在高价市场卖出。**

⚡ **Arbitrage opportunities require quotation differences and very rapid execution.** ｜ ⚡ **套汇机会要求市场存在报价差异，并且交易者能够快速完成交易。**

⏱️ **Ordinary arbitrage holds positions until a spread changes, while foreign exchange arbitrage completes buying and selling almost simultaneously.** ｜ ⏱️ **普通套利需要持仓等待价差变化，套汇交易的买卖则几乎同时完成。**

⚠️ **Although foreign exchange arbitrage has relatively low market risk, transaction costs and execution risk may still exist.** ｜ ⚠️ **外汇套汇的市场风险相对较低，但仍可能存在交易费用和执行风险。**

。Chapter 8: Stock Index Futures

## Section 1: Stocks and Stock Price Indices ｜ 第一节 股票与股票价格指数

### Examination Topic: Stocks and Stock Price Indices ｜ 考点：股票与股票价格指数

#### I. Stocks and Stock Prices ｜ 一、股票与股票价格

##### (I) Stocks ｜ （一）股票

###### Definition ｜ 定义

A stock is a type of negotiable security issued by a joint-stock company to certify an investor’s shareholder status and rights. ｜ 股票是有价证券的一种，是股份有限公司发行的、用于证明投资者股东身份和股东权益的凭证。

A shareholder may receive dividends or profit distributions based on the shares held. ｜ 投资者可以根据持有的股票获得股息或者红利。

Stocks are typical equity financial assets. ｜ 股票是典型的权益类金融资产。

------

###### Classification ｜ 分类

Stocks mainly include common stocks and preferred stocks. ｜ 股票主要包括普通股和优先股。

**Common stock** ｜ **普通股**

Common stock is the most typical form of equity security in a modern economy. ｜ 普通股是现代经济中最典型的权益证券。

Investors holding common stock nominally enjoy rights such as: ｜ 普通股股东在名义上享有以下权利：

- Participation in the company’s business decisions ｜ 参与公司经营决策的权利
- Participation in the distribution of company profits ｜ 参与公司利益分配的权利

At the same time, common shareholders must bear corresponding responsibilities and the operating risks of the company. ｜ 与此同时，普通股股东还需要承担相应责任以及公司的经营风险。

**Preferred stock** ｜ **优先股**

The supplied material only lists preferred stock as a category and does not provide a detailed explanation. ｜ 原材料只列出了“优先股”这一类别，没有提供进一步的定义或者特征说明。

------

##### (II) Stock Prices ｜ （二）股票价格

###### Definition ｜ 定义

A stock price is a random variable that changes continuously over time. ｜ 股票价格是一个随着时间不断变化的随机变量。

The source indicates that greater stock-price volatility is associated with greater investment risk, but the original sentence is incomplete. ｜ 原材料表明，股票价格波动越大，投资者承担的投资风险通常越高，但原句在此处存在缺失。

------

###### Main Determinants of Stock Prices ｜ 股票价格的主要决定因素

A stock’s price mainly depends on two factors. ｜ 股票价格主要取决于两个方面。

**Expected future returns from holding the stock** ｜ **持有股票未来可以获得的收益**

Investors value a stock partly according to the future economic benefits expected from holding it. ｜ 投资者会根据未来持有股票可能获得的经济收益对股票进行估值。

**Risk involved in obtaining those returns** ｜ **获得这些收益所承担的风险**

The greater the uncertainty surrounding future returns, the greater the risk borne by the investor. ｜ 未来收益的不确定性越大，投资者承担的风险也越大。

Therefore, the value of a stock is affected by both expected returns and risk. ｜ 因此，股票价值同时受到预期收益与风险的影响。

------

#### II. Stock Investment Returns and Risks ｜ 二、股票投资的收益与风险

##### (I) Sources of Returns from Holding Stocks ｜ （一）持有股票的收益来源

Stock investment returns mainly come from dividends and capital gains. ｜ 股票投资收益主要来源于股息红利和资本利得。

###### Dividend Income ｜ 股息或红利收入

If the company earns operating profits, it may distribute part of those profits to shareholders in the form of dividends. ｜ 当公司取得经营利润时，可以将部分利润以股息或者红利的形式分配给股东。

###### Capital Gains ｜ 股票价差收入

If a stock is favoured by the market and its market price rises above the investor’s purchase cost, the investor may earn a capital gain. ｜ 如果股票受到市场看好，市场价格上涨并高于投资者的购买成本，投资者就可以获得股票买卖价差收入。
$$
\text{Stock return}
=
\text{Dividend income}
+
\text{Capital gain or loss}
$$

------

##### (II) Systematic Risk ｜ （二）系统性风险

Systematic risk is caused by factors that affect the returns of all or most stocks in the market. ｜ 系统性风险是由对市场上全部或者大部分股票收益产生影响的因素所引起的风险。

Examples listed in the source include: ｜ 原材料列举的系统性风险因素包括：

- Macroeconomic cycles ｜ 宏观经济周期
- Inflation rates ｜ 通货膨胀率
- Interest rates ｜ 利率
- Exchange rates ｜ 汇率

These factors may affect different companies in different directions and to different degrees, but almost all stocks are influenced to some extent. ｜ 这些因素对不同公司股票的影响方向和程度可能不同，但几乎所有股票都会在一定程度上受到影响。

Systematic risk generally cannot be eliminated simply through portfolio diversification. ｜ 系统性风险通常不能单纯通过分散化投资予以消除。

------

##### (III) Unsystematic Risk ｜ （三）非系统性风险

Unsystematic risk is also called company-specific risk. ｜ 非系统性风险又称公司特有风险。

It arises from specific factors that affect only an individual company or a small number of companies. ｜ 这类风险由只影响个别公司或者少数公司的特定因素引起。

Unlike systematic risk, unsystematic risk can be reduced or even substantially eliminated through diversification. ｜ 与系统性风险不同，非系统性风险可以通过分散化投资减少，甚至在较大程度上消除。

| Type of risk      | Main source                  | Can diversification reduce it? |
| ----------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------ |
| Systematic risk   | Market-wide economic factors | Generally no                   |
| Unsystematic risk | Company-specific factors     | Yes                            |

| 风险类型     | 主要来源               | 能否通过分散投资降低 |
| ------------ | ---------------------- | -------------------- |
| 系统性风险   | 影响整个市场的宏观因素 | 通常不能             |
| 非系统性风险 | 个别公司的特定因素     | 可以                 |

------

##### (IV) Capital Asset Pricing Model ｜ （四）资本资产定价模型

The Capital Asset Pricing Model describes the relationship between the expected return of a risky security and the expected return of the overall market. ｜ 资本资产定价模型反映了特定风险证券的期望收益率与整体市场平均期望收益率之间的关系。

The model indicates that investors require an expected return above the risk-free return because they bear risk. ｜ 该模型表明，风险资产由于承担了风险，因此应当具有超过无风险资产收益率的预期收益。

According to the source, this excess return is related only to the systematic risk borne by the asset. ｜ 根据原材料，风险资产的超额收益只与其承担的系统性风险有关。

The systematic risk of a risky asset depends on the correlation between its return and the average return of the overall market. ｜ 风险资产的系统性风险取决于其收益率与市场整体平均收益率之间的相关程度。

This relationship is measured by the asset’s beta coefficient. ｜ 这一系统性风险通常使用资产的贝塔系数，即β系数进行衡量。

A larger beta coefficient indicates greater systematic risk. ｜ β系数越大，说明该资产承担的系统性风险越大。

The beta coefficient of the overall market portfolio is equal to 1. ｜ 市场整体平均收益率对应的市场组合β系数等于1。

| Beta coefficient | General meaning                                    |
| ---------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| $\beta>1$        | Systematic risk is greater than the market average |
| $\beta=1$        | Systematic risk is equal to the market average     |
| $\beta<1$        | Systematic risk is lower than the market average   |

| β系数     | 一般含义                   |
| --------- | -------------------------- |
| $\beta>1$ | 系统性风险高于市场平均水平 |
| $\beta=1$ | 系统性风险等于市场平均水平 |
| $\beta<1$ | 系统性风险低于市场平均水平 |

> 原材料没有给出资本资产定价模型的具体数学公式，因此这里不额外补充公式。

------

#### III. Stock Price Indices ｜ 三、股票价格指数

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

A stock price index is an indicator used to measure the average change in the prices of a group of stocks. ｜ 股票价格指数是反映一组股票价格平均变动情况的指标。

It converts the price movements of selected sample stocks into an index value, allowing investors to observe the overall movement of the stock market or a specific market segment. ｜ 股票价格指数将选定样本股票的价格变化转化为指数数值，用于反映股票市场整体或者某一特定市场板块的价格走势。

------

##### (II) Steps in Constructing a Stock Price Index ｜ （二）股票价格指数的编制步骤

###### Selecting Sample Stocks ｜ 选取样本股票

A certain number of representative sample stocks are selected from all listed stocks. ｜ 首先从全部上市股票中选取一定数量、具有代表性的样本股票。

The selected stocks should be capable of reflecting the overall condition of the market or the target market segment. ｜ 所选股票应当能够反映整个市场或者目标市场板块的总体状况。

------

###### Selecting a Calculation Formula ｜ 选择计算公式

A calculation formula should be selected that is: ｜ 编制指数时，应当选择具有以下特点的计算公式：

- Relatively simple to calculate ｜ 计算相对简便
- Easy to adjust when necessary ｜ 便于在必要时进行修正
- Capable of maintaining consistent statistical standards ｜ 能够保持统计口径一致
- Capable of maintaining index continuity ｜ 能够保持指数的连续性

------

###### Determining the Base Date ｜ 确定基期日

A specific date is selected as the base date of the stock price index. ｜ 需要选择某一个特定日期作为股票价格指数的基期日。

Stock prices on subsequent dates are compared with prices on this base date. ｜ 以后各期的股票价格通常以基期日价格作为比较基础。

------

###### Determining the Base Index Value ｜ 确定基期指数值

An integer such as 10, 50 or 100 is selected as the index value for the base period. ｜ 选取某一个整数作为基期的股票指数，例如10、50或者100。

Subsequent stock-price changes are then expressed through changes in the index relative to this base value. ｜ 以后股票价格的变化通过相对于该基期数值的指数变化表示。



选取样本股票→选择计算公式→确定基期日→确定基期指数
$$
\text{Select samples}
\rightarrow
\text{Select formula}
\rightarrow
\text{Determine base date}
\rightarrow
\text{Set base index}
$$

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

📄 **Stocks are equity securities issued by joint-stock companies to certify shareholders’ rights.** ｜ 📄 **股票是股份有限公司发行的、用于证明股东身份和权益的权益类证券。**

💰 **Stock returns mainly come from dividends and changes in stock prices.** ｜ 💰 **股票投资收益主要来源于股息红利和股票价格变动形成的价差收益。**

⚠️ **Stock prices depend on expected future returns and the risks involved in obtaining those returns.** ｜ ⚠️ **股票价格取决于未来预期收益以及获得这些收益所承担的风险。**

🌐 **Systematic risk affects most stocks and generally cannot be eliminated through diversification.** ｜ 🌐 **系统性风险会影响大部分股票，通常不能通过分散化投资消除。**

🏢 **Unsystematic risk affects individual companies and can be reduced through diversification.** ｜ 🏢 **非系统性风险主要影响个别公司，可以通过分散化投资降低。**

β **Beta measures the systematic risk of an asset relative to the overall market, whose beta is 1.** ｜ β **β系数衡量资产相对于整个市场的系统性风险，市场组合的β系数为1。**

📊 **A stock price index measures the average price movement of selected stocks.** ｜ 📊 **股票价格指数用于反映选定股票价格的平均变动情况。**

🧮 **Index construction includes selecting sample stocks, choosing a calculation formula, setting a base date and determining a base index value.** ｜ 🧮 **股票价格指数的编制包括选择样本股票、确定计算公式、确定基期日和设定基期指数值。**





##### (III) Major Stock Price Indices in the World ｜ （三）世界主要股票价格指数

###### United States ｜ 美国

Major US stock price indices include: ｜ 美国主要股票价格指数包括：

- Dow Jones Industrial Average ｜ 道琼斯工业平均指数
- Standard & Poor’s 500 Index ｜ 标准普尔500指数
- New York Stock Exchange Composite Index ｜ 纽约证券交易所综合股票指数

###### Japan ｜ 日本

The major Japanese stock price index listed in the source is: ｜ 原材料列出的日本主要股票价格指数为：

- Nikkei 225 Stock Average ｜ 日经225股价指数

###### China ｜ 我国

Major stock price indices listed in the source include: ｜ 原材料列出的主要股票价格指数包括：

- Hang Seng Index of Hong Kong, China ｜ 中国香港恒生指数
- CSI 300 Index ｜ 沪深300指数
- SSE 50 Index ｜ 上证50指数
- CSI 500 Index ｜ 中证500指数

- SZSE Composite Index ｜ 深证综合指数

## Section 2: Single-Stock Futures and Stock Index Futures ｜ 第二节 个股期货与股指期货

#### I. Concepts and Applications of Single-Stock Futures and Stock Index Futures ｜ 一、个股期货与股指期货的概念及应用领域

##### (I) Single-Stock Futures ｜ （一）个股期货

Single-stock futures are futures contracts whose underlying assets are individual stocks or indices containing a relatively small number of stocks, also called narrow-based indices. ｜ 个股期货是指以单只股票，或者由较少数量股票组成的窄基指数作为标的资产的期货合约。

------

##### (II) Stock Index Futures ｜ （二）股指期货

Stock index futures are standardised futures contracts whose underlying assets are stock price indices. ｜ 股指期货是以股票价格指数作为标的资产的标准化期货合约。

The parties agree to settle the contract at a specified future date according to a predetermined stock index level. ｜ 交易双方约定在未来某一特定时间，按照事先确定的股票价格指数水平进行结算。

The underlying object of stock index futures trading is the stock price index rather than individual physical shares. ｜ 股指期货交易的标的物是股票价格指数，而不是具体的单只股票。

------

##### (III) Development of Stock Index Futures ｜ （三）股指期货的发展

In February 1982, the Kansas City Board of Trade launched futures based on the Value Line Composite Average Index. ｜ 1982年2月，美国堪萨斯期货交易所推出价值线综合平均指数期货。

Since then, stock index futures have received increasing attention from various investors. ｜ 此后，股指期货逐渐受到各类投资者的重视。

Their trading volume expanded rapidly, and the number of listed products continued to increase. ｜ 股指期货交易规模迅速扩大，交易品种不断增加。

The final sentence in the source is incomplete after stating that stock index futures and interest rate futures are among the major products in global futures markets. ｜ 原材料最后一句在“股指期货与利率期货并列……”之后出现缺失，因此不补充其未显示的内容。

------

##### (IV) Special Characteristics of Stock Index Futures ｜ （四）股指期货的特殊性

###### Quotation in Index Points ｜ 以指数点报价

Stock index futures are quoted in index points. ｜ 股指期货采用指数点报价。

The contract value equals the quoted index level multiplied by the monetary value represented by each index point. ｜ 股指期货合约价值等于指数点数乘以每个指数点所代表的金额。
$$
\text{Contract value}
=
\text{Index level}
\times
\text{Contract multiplier}
$$

------

###### Cash Settlement ｜ 现金交割

A stock index is composed of multiple stocks and is not itself a physical asset that can be delivered. ｜ 股票指数由多只股票构成，本身不是可以实际交付的资产。

Because it is not practicable to deliver all component stocks of the index, stock index futures can only use cash settlement. ｜ 由于无法在交割时将指数包含的全部股票进行实际交付，因此股指期货只能采用现金交割。

------

###### Relatively High Price Volatility ｜ 价格波动相对较大

Compared with bonds, stock price indices generally have greater volatility. ｜ 与债券相比，股票价格指数的波动通常更大。

Because futures prices are closely related to the prices of their underlying assets, stock index futures prices generally fluctuate more than interest rate futures prices. ｜ 由于期货价格与标的资产价格密切相关，股指期货价格的波动通常大于利率期货价格的波动。

------

##### (V) Applications of Stock Index Futures ｜ （五）股指期货的应用

Stock index futures may be used for: ｜ 股指期货可以用于：

- Hedging ｜ 套期保值
- Speculation and arbitrage ｜ 投机与套利
- Portfolio management ｜ 投资组合管理
- Assisting enterprises in issuing or repurchasing shares ｜ 辅助企业进行股票发行或者股票回购

------

#### II. Main Stock Index Futures Products in China ｜ 二、国内主要股指期货品种

The source lists three major stock index futures contracts: ｜ 原材料列出了三种主要股指期货合约：

- CSI 300 stock index futures ｜ 沪深300股指期货
- SSE 50 stock index futures ｜ 上证50股指期货
- CSI 500 stock index futures ｜ 中证500股指期货

| Contract item             | CSI 300                                                      | SSE 50          | CSI 500         |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------- | --------------- |
| Underlying index          | CSI 300 Index                                                | SSE 50 Index    | CSI 500 Index   |

| ------------ | -------------------------------------- | ---------- | ----------- |

The OCR text for the contract multipliers is incomplete. It clearly shows RMB 300 and RMB 200 but does not completely preserve the three-column layout. ｜ 原材料中的合约乘数一栏受到OCR损坏，只能清楚辨认出“300元”和“200元”，无法从扫描文字中完整确认三列的对应关系，因此不自行补齐。

The source also lists a minimum trading margin of 8%. ｜ 原材料列示的最低交易保证金为8%。

> 以上为教材所列合约规则，不代表当前交易所最新规则。

------

#### III. Contract Terms and Trading Rules ｜ 三、合约条款及交易规则

##### (I) Contract Multiplier ｜ （一）合约乘数

The contract multiplier determines the monetary value represented by each index point. ｜ 合约乘数表示每一个指数点所对应的货币金额。

For example, when the CSI 300 futures index level is 2,300 points and the multiplier is RMB 300 per point, the contract value is: ｜ 例如，当沪深300股指期货指数为2300点、合约乘数为每点300元时，合约价值为：
$$
2300\times300
=
690000\text{元}
$$
Therefore, the contract value is RMB 690,000. ｜ 因此，该股指期货合约价值为69万元。

------

##### (II) Quotation and Minimum Price Fluctuation ｜ （二）报价方式与最小变动价位

Stock index futures are quoted in index points. ｜ 股指期货采用指数点报价。

According to the source, the minimum price fluctuation is 0.2 index points. ｜ 根据原材料，股指期货的最小变动价位为0.2点。

The monetary value of one minimum price movement is: ｜ 一个最小变动价位对应的合约价值变化为：
$$
\text{Minimum price fluctuation}
\times
\text{Contract multiplier}
$$
For a contract with a multiplier of RMB 300 per point: ｜ 对于合约乘数为每点300元的合约：
$$
0.2\times300=60\text{元}
$$
For a contract with a multiplier of RMB 200 per point: ｜ 对于合约乘数为每点200元的合约：
$$
0.2\times200=40\text{元}
$$

------

##### (III) Contract Months ｜ （三）合约月份

The source states that listed contract months include: ｜ 原材料规定的挂牌合约月份包括：

- The current month ｜ 当月
- The next month ｜ 下月
- The following two quarterly months ｜ 随后两个季月

Quarterly months generally refer to March, June, September and December. ｜ 季月通常是指3月、6月、9月和12月。

------

##### (IV) Daily Price Limits ｜ （四）每日价格最大变动限制

According to the source, the daily price limit for stock index futures is 10% above or below the previous trading day’s settlement price. ｜ 根据原材料，股指期货每日价格波动限制为上一交易日结算价的上下10%。

The source also states that the price limit on the last trading day is 20% above or below the previous trading day’s settlement price. ｜ 原材料还指出，合约最后交易日的涨跌停板幅度为上一交易日结算价的上下20%。

It additionally mentions a circuit-breaker system with thresholds of 5% and 7%. ｜ 原材料同时提到设置5%和7%两档熔断幅度的熔断制度。

> 本段按照教材原文整理。相关制度可能属于教材编写时的历史规则，不应直接视为当前交易规则。

------

##### (V) Margin ｜ （五）保证金

Stock index futures use margin trading. ｜ 股指期货实行保证金交易制度。

The trader only needs to deposit a specified proportion of the contract value as margin rather than paying the entire contract value. ｜ 交易者不需要支付合约全部价值，只需要按照规定比例缴纳保证金。

According to the source table, the minimum trading margin is 8% of the contract value. ｜ 根据原材料表格，最低交易保证金为合约价值的8%。
$$
\text{Required margin}
=
\text{Contract value}
\times
\text{Margin rate}
$$

------

##### (VI) Position Limits ｜ （六）持仓限额

The China Financial Futures Exchange determines specific position limits for members and clients holding stock index futures contracts. ｜ 会员和客户的股指期货合约持仓限额，由中国金融期货交易所制定具体规定。

Position limits are designed to control excessive speculation, market manipulation and concentration risk. ｜ 持仓限额制度用于控制过度投机、市场操纵以及持仓过度集中的风险。

------

##### (VII) Investor Suitability Requirements ｜ （七）投资者适当性制度

###### Natural-Person Investors ｜ 自然人投资者

According to the source, a natural-person investor applying to open a stock index futures account must satisfy the following conditions: ｜ 根据原材料，自然人投资者申请开立股指期货交易账户，需要满足以下条件：

- The available balance in the margin account must be no less than RMB 500,000 at the time of application. ｜ 申请开户时，保证金账户可用资金余额不低于人民币50万元。
- The investor must possess basic knowledge of stock index futures and obtain a score of at least 80 in the account-opening test. ｜ 具备股指期货基础知识，开户测试成绩不低于80分。
- The investor must have at least 20 simulated stock index futures transactions over a cumulative period of 10 trading days, or at least 10 commodity futures transactions during the most recent three years. ｜ 具有累计10个交易日、20笔以上的股指期货仿真交易记录，或者最近3年内具有10笔以上商品期货交易记录。

------

###### General Corporate and Special Corporate Investors ｜ 一般法人和特殊法人投资者

According to the source, general corporate investors must satisfy additional requirements. ｜ 根据原材料，一般法人投资者申请开户还需要满足额外条件。

- Net assets must be no less than RMB 1 million. ｜ 净资产不低于人民币100万元。
- The entity must have corresponding decision-making mechanisms and operating procedures. ｜ 应当具备相应的决策机制和操作流程。

Special corporate investors must provide approval documents or certificates issued by the relevant regulatory or competent authorities. ｜ 特殊法人投资者申请开户时，应当提供相关监管机构或者主管机构出具的批准文件或证明文件。

> 以上投资者适当性条件按照教材内容整理，不代表当前开户制度。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🌍 **Major global stock indices include the Dow Jones Industrial Average, S&P 500, Nikkei 225, Hang Seng Index and major mainland Chinese indices.** ｜ 🌍 **世界主要股票指数包括道琼斯工业平均指数、标准普尔500指数、日经225指数、恒生指数以及我国主要股票指数。**

📄 **Single-stock futures use individual stocks or narrow-based indices as their underlying assets.** ｜ 📄 **个股期货以单只股票或者窄基指数作为标的资产。**

📊 **Stock index futures are standardised futures contracts based on stock price indices.** ｜ 📊 **股指期货是以股票价格指数为标的的标准化期货合约。**

💵 **Contract value equals the index level multiplied by the contract multiplier.** ｜ 💵 **股指期货合约价值等于指数点数乘以合约乘数。**

💳 **Because a stock index cannot be physically delivered, stock index futures use cash settlement.** ｜ 💳 **由于股票指数无法进行实物交付，股指期货采用现金交割。**

📈 **Stock index futures are used for hedging, speculation, arbitrage and portfolio management.** ｜ 📈 **股指期货可以用于套期保值、投机套利和投资组合管理。**

⚙️ **The main contract rules include the multiplier, minimum price movement, contract months, price limits, margin and position limits.** ｜ ⚙️ **股指期货主要交易规则包括合约乘数、最小变动价位、合约月份、涨跌幅限制、保证金和持仓限额。**

🛡️ **The investor suitability system sets requirements for capital, knowledge, trading experience and internal controls.** ｜ 🛡️ **投资者适当性制度对资金、知识、交易经历和内部管理制度提出要求。**

## Section 3: Stock Index Futures Hedging ｜ 第三节 股指期货套期保值交易

#### I. Optimal Hedge Ratio and the Beta Coefficient ｜ 一、最优套期保值比率与β系数

##### (I) Definition of the Hedge Ratio ｜ （一）套期保值比率的概念

The hedge ratio is the ratio between the position in futures contracts used for hedging and the position in the asset being hedged. ｜ 套期保值比率是指用于套期保值的期货合约头寸与被套期保值资产头寸之间的比例。

The hedge ratio that can most effectively and extensively eliminate the price risk of the hedged asset is called the optimal hedge ratio. ｜ 能够最有效、最大限度地消除被保值对象价格变动风险的套期保值比率，称为最优套期保值比率。

------

##### (II) Reasons for Introducing the Beta Coefficient ｜ （二）引入β系数的原因

Most stock-market investors do not construct their portfolios strictly according to the constituent stocks and weights of a stock index. ｜ 绝大多数股票投资者不会完全按照股票指数的成分股及其权重构建投资组合。

Therefore, the price movement of an investor’s stock portfolio will normally differ from the movement of the stock index underlying the futures contract. ｜ 因此，投资者股票组合的价格变动幅度，通常与股指期货标的指数的变动幅度不完全相同。

To hedge a stock portfolio effectively, the investor must determine an appropriate ratio between the value of the spot portfolio and the value of the stock index futures position. ｜ 为了有效对冲股票组合风险，投资者必须确定股票现货组合与股指期货头寸之间的合理比例。

The beta coefficient is therefore introduced to measure the sensitivity of the stock or stock portfolio to changes in the overall market. ｜ 因此，需要引入β系数，用来衡量股票或者股票组合相对于整体市场变动的敏感程度。

------

##### (III) Economic Meaning of the Beta Coefficient ｜ （三）β系数的经济含义

In the Capital Asset Pricing Model, beta represents the systematic risk of a particular stock relative to the overall market. ｜ 在资本资产定价模型中，β系数表示某只股票相对于整体市场所承担的系统性风险。

It may also be understood as the relative volatility of the stock compared with the market portfolio. ｜ β系数也可以理解为股票相对于市场组合的相对波动率。

If the beta coefficient is greater than 1, the stock fluctuates more strongly than the overall market and bears above-average systematic risk. ｜ 如果β系数大于1，说明该股票的价格波动幅度大于整体市场，其系统性风险高于市场平均水平。

If the beta coefficient is less than 1, the stock fluctuates less strongly than the overall market and bears below-average systematic risk. ｜ 如果β系数小于1，说明该股票的价格波动幅度小于整体市场，其系统性风险低于市场平均水平。

If the beta coefficient equals 1, the stock generally moves to the same extent as the overall market. ｜ 如果β系数等于1，说明该股票的波动幅度通常与整体市场基本一致。

| Beta coefficient | Meaning                             |
| ---------------- | ----------------------------------- |
| $\beta>1$        | More volatile than the market       |
| $\beta=1$        | Moves approximately with the market |
| $\beta<1$        | Less volatile than the market       |

| β系数     | 含义                   |
| --------- | ---------------------- |
| $\beta>1$ | 波动幅度大于市场       |
| $\beta=1$ | 波动幅度与市场大致一致 |
| $\beta<1$ | 波动幅度小于市场       |

------

##### (IV) Beta of a Stock Portfolio ｜ （四）股票组合的β系数

Assume that a portfolio $P$ consists of $n$ stocks. ｜ 假设股票组合 $P$ 由 $n$ 只股票组成。

The proportion of funds invested in stock $i$ is $X_i$, and the beta coefficient of stock $i$ is $\beta_i$. ｜ 第 $i$ 只股票的资金权重为 $X_i$，其β系数为 $\beta_i$。

The sum of the portfolio weights is: ｜ 组合中各股票权重之和为：
$$
X_1+X_2+\cdots+X_n=1
$$
The beta coefficient of the portfolio is the weighted average of the beta coefficients of its component stocks: ｜ 股票组合的β系数等于各成分股票β系数的加权平均：
$$
\beta_P
=
X_1\beta_1+X_2\beta_2+\cdots+X_n\beta_n
$$

------

##### (V) Determining the Number of Futures Contracts ｜ （五）确定期货合约数量

The number of stock index futures contracts required for hedging is: ｜ 套期保值所需股指期货合约数量为：
$$
N
=
\frac{
\beta_P\times V_P
}{
F\times M
}
$$
where: ｜ 其中：

- $N$: number of futures contracts ｜ $N$：所需期货合约数量
- $\beta_P$: beta coefficient of the stock portfolio ｜ $\beta_P$：股票组合的β系数
- $V_P$: current market value of the stock portfolio ｜ $V_P$：股票组合当前市值
- $F$: stock index futures level ｜ $F$：股指期货指数点数
- $M$: contract multiplier ｜ $M$：每点对应的合约乘数

The product $F\times M$ is the value of one stock index futures contract. ｜ 其中，$F\times M$就是一手股指期货合约的价值。

Generally, the larger the portfolio beta, the more futures contracts are required. ｜ 通常，股票组合β系数越大，所需期货合约数量越多。

------

#### II. Short Hedging with Stock Index Futures ｜ 二、股指期货卖出套期保值

A short hedge is suitable for an investor who currently holds a stock portfolio and is concerned that the stock market may decline. ｜ 股指期货卖出套期保值适用于已经持有股票组合，并担心未来股票市场下跌的投资者。

The investor sells stock index futures so that losses in the stock portfolio may be offset by profits from the short futures position. ｜ 投资者卖出股指期货，使股票组合的损失可以由期货空头头寸的盈利进行抵销。
$$
\text{Hold stocks}
+
\text{Fear market decline}
\Rightarrow
\text{Sell stock index futures}
$$

------

##### Case ｜ 案例

On 2 September, a domestic securities investment fund had already achieved a return of 26%. ｜ 某年9月2日，国内某证券投资基金的收益率已经达到26%。

Because the future market outlook was uncertain and the probability of a decline was considered relatively high, the fund decided to use CSI 300 index futures to preserve its performance until December. ｜ 由于后市走势不明朗，且下跌可能性较大，该基金决定利用沪深300股指期货，将现有投资业绩尽量保持至12月。

The current value of the stock portfolio was RMB 224 million, and its beta coefficient relative to the CSI 300 Index was 0.9. ｜ 该股票组合现值为2.24亿元，与沪深300指数之间的β系数为0.9。

The December futures contract was quoted at 3,650 points, and the contract multiplier was RMB 300 per point. ｜ 12月份到期的股指期货合约价格为3650点，合约乘数为每点300元。

The required number of short futures contracts was: ｜ 需要卖出的期货合约数量为：
$$
N
=
\frac{
0.9\times224{,}000{,}000
}{
3650\times300
}
$$
Therefore, the fund sold approximately 184 futures contracts. ｜ 因此，该基金应当卖出约184手股指期货合约。

------

##### Hedging Result ｜ 套期保值结果

According to the source, the market index subsequently declined by 35.29%. ｜ 根据原材料，随后股票市场指数下跌了35.29%。

Because the portfolio beta was 0.9, the estimated percentage decline in the portfolio was: ｜ 由于股票组合的β系数为0.9，其估计跌幅为：
$$
35.29\%\times0.9
=
31.761\%
$$
The reduction in the portfolio value was approximately: ｜ 股票组合市值减少约为：
$$
224{,}000{,}000
\times31.761\%
=
71{,}144{,}640\text{元}
$$
The remaining portfolio value was approximately: ｜ 股票组合剩余市值约为：
$$
224{,}000{,}000-71{,}144{,}640
=
152{,}855{,}360\text{元}
$$
According to the source, the futures price declined by 1,360 points. ｜ 根据原材料，股指期货价格下跌了1360点。

The profit from the short futures position was: ｜ 期货空头头寸盈利为：
$$
184\times1360\times300
=
75{,}072{,}000\text{元}
$$
The futures profit substantially offset the decline in the stock portfolio. ｜ 期货市场的盈利基本抵销了股票组合市值下降造成的损失，实现了套期保值目的。

The difference between the futures profit and the estimated spot loss was: ｜ 期货盈利与估计现货损失之间的差额为：
$$
75{,}072{,}000-71{,}144{,}640
=
3{,}927{,}360\text{元}
$$
The hedge was not perfectly matched because of factors such as rounding the number of contracts, changes in beta and basis risk. ｜ 套期保值结果并非完全一致，可能受到合约数量取整、β系数变化以及基差风险等因素影响。

------

#### III. Long Hedging with Stock Index Futures ｜ 三、股指期货买入套期保值

A long hedge is suitable for an investor who expects to receive funds and purchase stocks in the future but is concerned that stock prices may rise before the funds become available. ｜ 股指期货买入套期保值适用于未来将获得资金并购买股票，但担心资金到账前股票价格上涨的投资者。

The investor buys stock index futures. If stock prices rise, the profit from the futures position can offset the increased cost of purchasing the stocks. ｜ 投资者买入股指期货。如果股票价格上涨，期货多头盈利可以抵销未来购买股票增加的成本。
$$
\text{Plan to buy stocks}
+
\text{Fear market rise}
\Rightarrow
\text{Buy stock index futures}
$$

------

##### Case ｜ 案例

On 15 April, an institution received a commitment that RMB 3 million would become available on 10 June. ｜ 4月15日，某机构得到承诺，将于6月10日获得300万元资金。

The institution planned to invest RMB 1 million in each of stocks A, B and C. ｜ 该机构计划分别向A、B、C三只股票投入100万元。

Their current prices were RMB 20, RMB 25 and RMB 50 respectively. ｜ 三只股票当前价格分别为20元、25元和50元。

If the institution had the funds immediately, it could purchase: ｜ 如果资金当时已经到账，机构可以分别购买：

- 50,000 shares of stock A ｜ A股票5万股
- 40,000 shares of stock B ｜ B股票4万股
- 20,000 shares of stock C ｜ C股票2万股

Because the market was expected to rise, the institution was concerned that the same quantities of shares would cost more by 10 June. ｜ 由于市场处于看涨阶段，该机构担心到6月10日时股票价格已经上涨，300万元将不足以购买原定数量的股票。

The institution therefore decided to buy stock index futures to lock in the future investment cost. ｜ 因此，该机构决定买入股指期货，锁定未来的股票购买成本。

------

##### Calculating the Portfolio Beta ｜ 计算股票组合β系数

The beta coefficients of stocks A, B and C were 1.5, 1.3 and 0.8. ｜ A、B、C三只股票的β系数分别为1.5、1.3和0.8。

Because one-third of the funds would be invested in each stock, the portfolio beta was: ｜ 由于每只股票的投资金额均占组合的三分之一，组合β系数为：
$$
\beta_P
=
1.5\times\frac13
+
1.3\times\frac13
+
0.8\times\frac13
$$

------

##### Calculating the Number of Futures Contracts ｜ 计算期货合约数量

The June futures contract was quoted at 2,500 points, and the contract multiplier was RMB 100 per point. ｜ 6月份到期的股指期货价格为2500点，每点乘数为100元。

The required number of futures contracts was: ｜ 所需期货合约数量为：
$$
N
=
\frac{
1.2\times3{,}000{,}000
}{
2500\times100
}
$$
Because futures contracts must be traded in whole units, the institution bought 15 contracts. ｜ 由于期货合约只能按照整数手交易，因此该机构买入15手股指期货。

> 原材料公式中写成了“300000”，但根据案例总资金300万元以及最终15手的结果，正确数值应为3000000元。

------

##### Hedging Result ｜ 套期保值结果

On 10 June, the institution received the RMB 3 million as scheduled. ｜ 6月10日，该机构如期收到300万元资金。

Both the spot index and futures index had risen by 10%. The futures price increased from 2,500 points to 2,750 points. ｜ 现货指数和期货指数均上涨了10%，股指期货价格由2500点上涨至2750点。

The prices of the three stocks changed as follows: ｜ 三只股票的价格变化如下：

| Stock | Initial price | Later price | Increase |
| ----- | ------------- | ----------- | -------- |
| A     | RMB 20.00     | RMB 23.00   | 15%      |
| B     | RMB 25.00     | RMB 28.25   | 13%      |
| C     | RMB 50.00     | RMB 54.00   | 8%       |

| 股票 | 初始价格 | 后期价格 | 涨幅 |
| ---- | -------- | -------- | ---- |
| A    | 20元     | 23元     | 15%  |
| B    | 25元     | 28.25元  | 13%  |
| C    | 50元     | 54元     | 8%   |

To purchase the originally planned quantities, the institution now required: ｜ 按照原定数量购买三只股票，所需资金变为：
$$
23\times50{,}000
+
28.25\times40{,}000
+
54\times20{,}000
$$
The funding shortfall was: ｜ 资金缺口为：
$$
3{,}360{,}000-3{,}000{,}000
=
360{,}000\text{元}
$$
The profit from the long futures position was: ｜ 股指期货多头头寸盈利为：
$$
15\times(2750-2500)\times100
$$
After covering the RMB 360,000 increase in the purchase cost, RMB 15,000 remained. ｜ 期货盈利抵销36万元股票购买成本增加后，还剩余1.5万元。
$$
375{,}000-360{,}000
=
15{,}000\text{元}
$$
Therefore, the long hedge effectively locked in the future cost of purchasing the stocks. ｜ 因此，该多头套期保值策略基本锁定了未来购买股票的成本。

------

## Section 4: Stock Index Futures Speculation and Arbitrage ｜ 第四节 股指期货投机与套利交易

#### I. Stock Index Futures Speculation ｜ 一、股指期货投机

Stock index futures speculation refers to trading based on predictions about future movements in stock index futures prices. ｜ 股指期货投机是指交易者根据对股指期货合约价格变动趋势的预测进行交易。

A trader who expects the index futures price to rise buys futures contracts. ｜ 交易者预期股指期货价格上涨时，买入股指期货合约。

A trader who expects the index futures price to fall sells futures contracts. ｜ 交易者预期股指期货价格下跌时，卖出股指期货合约。

The trader earns a speculative profit if the futures price subsequently moves in the predicted direction. ｜ 如果股指期货价格按照交易者预测的方向变化，交易者就可以获取价差收益。
$$
\text{Bullish expectation}
\Rightarrow
\text{Buy futures}
$$

------

#### II. Theoretical Basis of Stock Index Cash-Futures Arbitrage ｜ 二、股指期货期现套利的理论依据

In practice, both the spot stock index and stock index futures prices change continuously. ｜ 现实中，股票现货指数和股指期货价格都在不断变化。

The actual relationship between futures and spot prices may deviate from its theoretically reasonable level because of market supply and demand, transaction costs, financing conditions and other factors. ｜ 由于市场供求、交易成本、融资条件等因素，股指期货与现货指数之间的价格关系可能偏离理论上的合理水平。

When the deviation exceeds a certain range, a cash-futures arbitrage opportunity may arise. ｜ 当这种偏离超过一定范围时，就可能产生股指期现套利机会。

The trader may simultaneously establish opposite positions in the spot stock portfolio and stock index futures and earn a profit when the price relationship returns to normal. ｜ 交易者可以在股票现货组合和股指期货市场同时建立相反方向的头寸，并在期现价格关系恢复正常时获利。

The theoretical futures price calculated from the spot index is therefore essential for determining whether an arbitrage opportunity exists. ｜ 因此，根据现货指数计算股指期货理论价格，是判断是否存在期现套利机会的重要基础。

An arbitrage opportunity may exist only when the actual futures price is sufficiently above or below the theoretical futures price. ｜ 只有当实际股指期货价格明显高于或者低于理论价格时，才可能产生套利机会。

------

#### III. Theoretical Price of a Stock Index Futures Contract ｜ 三、股指期货合约的理论价格

##### (I) Components of the Cost of Carry ｜ （一）股指期货持有成本的构成

The cost of carrying a stock portfolio mainly contains two elements. ｜ 持有股票组合的成本主要包括两个方面。

###### Financing Cost ｜ 资金占用成本

Financing cost is measured according to the market interest rate. ｜ 资金占用成本按照市场资金利率计算。

Holding a stock portfolio requires capital, and the capital has an opportunity or financing cost. ｜ 持有股票组合需要占用资金，因此会产生相应的机会成本或者融资成本。

###### Dividend Income ｜ 股票分红收入

During the holding period, the investor may receive dividends from the component stocks. ｜ 在持有期内，投资者可能获得成分股票支付的股息和红利。

Dividend income is an economic benefit of holding the spot stock portfolio and is therefore treated as a negative carrying cost. ｜ 股息红利属于持有股票现货组合取得的收益，因此在持有成本中应当作为负成本处理。

###### Net Cost of Carry ｜ 净持有成本

$$
\text{Net cost of carry}
=
\text{Financing cost}
-
\text{Dividend income}
$$

Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
\text{Theoretical futures price}
=
\text{Spot index value}
+
\text{Net cost of carry}
$$

------

##### (II) Case ｜ （二）案例

A buyer and seller enter into a forward contract for delivery of a stock portfolio three months later. ｜ 买卖双方签订一份在3个月后交割一揽子股票组合的远期合约。

The portfolio exactly corresponds to the composition of the CSI 300 Index. ｜ 该股票组合与沪深300指数的构成完全对应。

Its current market value is RMB 990,000, corresponding to a CSI 300 Index level of 3,300 points. ｜ 该股票组合当前市场价值为99万元，对应沪深300指数3300点。

The contract multiplier is RMB 300 per index point. ｜ 合约乘数为每个指数点300元。
$$
3300\times300
=
990{,}000\text{元}
$$
The annual market interest rate is 6%, and a cash dividend of RMB 6,600 is expected to be received one month later. ｜ 假设市场年利率为6%，并预计一个月后可以收到6600元现金红利。

------

###### Financing Cost ｜ 资金占用成本

The financing cost of holding RMB 990,000 for three months is: ｜ 持有99万元股票组合3个月的资金成本为：
$$
990{,}000\times6\%\times\frac{3}{12}
=
14{,}850\text{元}
$$

------

###### Future Value of Dividend Income ｜ 股息收入的终值

The investor receives a dividend of RMB 6,600 after one month. ｜ 投资者在持有一个月后收到6600元股息。

The dividend can earn interest for the remaining two months: ｜ 该股息在剩余两个月内还可以获得利息：
$$
6600\times6\%\times\frac{2}{12}
=
66\text{元}
$$
Therefore, the value of the dividend income at the delivery date is: ｜ 因此，交割日时股息收入的本利和为：
$$
6600+66
=
6666\text{元}
$$

------

###### Net Cost of Carry ｜ 净持有成本

$$
14{,}850-6{,}666
=
8{,}184\text{元}
$$

The net cost of carry is RMB 8,184. ｜ 净持有成本为8184元。

------

###### Theoretical Contract Value ｜ 理论合约价值

$$
990{,}000+8{,}184
=
998{,}184\text{元}
$$

Therefore, the reasonable theoretical value of the forward contract is RMB 998,184. ｜ 因此，该远期合约的合理理论价值为998184元。

------

##### (III) Calculation in Index Points ｜ （三）按照指数点计算

The current portfolio value of RMB 990,000 corresponds to 3,300 index points. ｜ 99万元的股票组合价值对应3300个指数点。

The three-month financing cost expressed in index points is: ｜ 3个月资金占用成本折算为指数点为：
$$
\frac{14{,}850}{300}
=
49.5\text{点}
$$
The RMB 6,600 dividend is equivalent to: ｜ 6600元股息折算为指数点为：
$$
\frac{6600}{300}
=
22\text{点}
$$
The interest earned on the dividend is equivalent to: ｜ 股息在剩余两个月获得的利息折算为指数点为：
$$
\frac{66}{300}
=
0.22\text{点}
$$
The total value of the dividend income at delivery is: ｜ 股息收入在交割日的总价值为：
$$
22+0.22
=
22.22\text{点}
$$
The net cost of carry is: ｜ 净持有成本为：
$$
49.5-22.22
=
27.28\text{点}
$$
Therefore, the theoretical index futures price is: ｜ 因此，股指期货理论价格为：
$$
3300+27.28
=
3327.28\text{点}
$$

------

##### (IV) General Formula ｜ （四）一般计算公式

Under a simple-interest assumption, the theoretical price of stock index futures may be expressed as: ｜ 在采用单利计算的情况下，股指期货理论价格可以表示为：
$$
F(t,T)
=
S(t)
+
S(t)(r-d)\frac{T-t}{365}
$$
or: ｜ 或者写成：
$$
F(t,T)
=
S(t)
\left[
1+(r-d)\frac{T-t}{365}
\right]
$$
where: ｜ 其中：

- $F(t,T)$: theoretical futures price at time $t$ for delivery at time $T$ ｜ $F(t,T)$：在 $t$ 时点计算的、于 $T$ 时点交割的期货理论价格
- $S(t)$: current spot index ｜ $S(t)$：当前现货指数
- $r$: annual financing interest rate ｜ $r$：年化资金利率
- $d$: annualised dividend yield ｜ $d$：年化股息收益率
- $T-t$: number of days remaining until delivery ｜ $T-t$：距离交割日的剩余天数

The OCR source repeats $S(t)$ in the formula. The expression above preserves the intended cost-of-carry relationship while correcting that duplication. ｜ 原材料公式中出现了重复的 $S(t)$。上式按照原文表达的持有成本关系，对该重复项进行了整理。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

β **The hedge ratio determines the size of the futures position relative to the stock portfolio being hedged.** ｜ β **套期保值比率决定期货头寸相对于被保值股票组合的规模。**

📊 **Portfolio beta is the weighted average beta of the stocks in the portfolio.** ｜ 📊 **股票组合β系数等于组合中各股票β系数的加权平均。**

🧮 **Required futures contracts equal portfolio beta multiplied by portfolio value, divided by the value of one futures contract.** ｜ 🧮 **所需期货合约数量等于组合β乘以组合市值，再除以一手期货合约价值。**

📉 **Holding a stock portfolio and fearing a decline → sell stock index futures.** ｜ 📉 **已经持有股票并担心市场下跌 → 卖出股指期货。**

📈 **Planning to buy stocks and fearing a rise → buy stock index futures.** ｜ 📈 **未来计划购买股票并担心市场上涨 → 买入股指期货。**

💹 **Stock index futures speculation buys when bullish and sells when bearish.** ｜ 💹 **股指期货投机在看涨时买入，在看跌时卖出。**

⚖️ **Cash-futures arbitrage opportunities arise when the actual futures price deviates sufficiently from its theoretical value.** ｜ ⚖️ **实际股指期货价格明显偏离理论价格时，可能产生期现套利机会。**

💰 **Net cost of carry equals financing cost minus dividend income.** ｜ 💰 **净持有成本等于资金占用成本减去股息红利收入。**

📐 **Theoretical futures price equals the spot index plus the net cost of carry.** ｜ 📐 **股指期货理论价格等于现货指数加净持有成本。**

#### IV. Stock Index Futures Cash-Futures Arbitrage Operations ｜ 四、股指期货期现套利操作

##### (I) Overvalued Futures and Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage ｜ （一）期价高估与正向套利

When the actual stock index futures price is sufficiently higher than its theoretical price, the futures contract is considered overvalued. ｜ 当股指期货的实际价格明显高于其理论价格时，可以认为期货价格被高估。

The trader may conduct cash-and-carry arbitrage by: ｜ 交易者可以采取正向套利：

- Selling the overvalued stock index futures contract ｜ 卖出被高估的股指期货合约
- Buying the corresponding spot stock portfolio ｜ 买入与指数相对应的现货股票组合

$$
\text{Futures overvalued}
\Rightarrow
\text{Buy spot stocks}
+
\text{Sell index futures}
$$

The trader earns a profit when the futures price converges toward the spot price or theoretical value. ｜ 当期货价格向现货价格或者理论价格收敛时，交易者可以获得套利收益。

------

##### (II) Undervalued Futures and Reverse Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage ｜ （二）期价低估与反向套利

When the actual stock index futures price is sufficiently lower than its theoretical price, the futures contract is considered undervalued. ｜ 当股指期货的实际价格明显低于其理论价格时，可以认为期货价格被低估。

The trader may conduct reverse cash-and-carry arbitrage by: ｜ 交易者可以采取反向套利：

- Buying the undervalued stock index futures contract ｜ 买入被低估的股指期货合约
- Selling the corresponding spot stock portfolio ｜ 卖出与指数相对应的现货股票组合

$$
\text{Futures undervalued}
\Rightarrow
\text{Sell spot stocks}
+
\text{Buy index futures}
$$

> In practice, reverse arbitrage may be restricted by the difficulty and cost of short-selling the component stocks. ｜ 实际交易中，反向套利可能受到现货股票卖空难度和成本的限制。

------

#### V. Transaction Costs and the No-Arbitrage Band ｜ 五、交易成本与无套利区间

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

The no-arbitrage band is a price range formed by adjusting the theoretical stock index futures price upward and downward by total transaction costs. ｜ 无套利区间是指在股指期货理论价格的基础上，分别加上和减去交易成本后形成的价格区间。
$$
\text{Upper boundary}
=
\text{Theoretical futures price}
+
\text{Transaction costs}
$$
When the actual futures price lies inside this band, the potential price difference is insufficient to cover transaction costs. ｜ 当实际股指期货价格处于无套利区间以内时，期现价差不足以覆盖交易成本。

An arbitrage transaction would therefore generate no profit and might even result in a loss. ｜ 因此，此时进行套利不仅无法获利，反而可能产生亏损。

| Actual futures price     | Arbitrage decision                               |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------ |
| Above the upper boundary | Cash-and-carry arbitrage may be possible         |
| Within the band          | No effective arbitrage opportunity               |
| Below the lower boundary | Reverse cash-and-carry arbitrage may be possible |

| 实际期货价格 | 套利判断             |
| ------------ | -------------------- |
| 高于上界     | 可能存在正向套利机会 |
| 位于区间内   | 不存在有效套利机会   |
| 低于下界     | 可能存在反向套利机会 |

------

##### (II) Case: Calculating Theoretical Futures Prices ｜ （二）案例：计算股指期货理论价格

Assume: ｜ 假设：
$$
r=5\%
$$
The delivery date of the June futures contract is 30 June. ｜ 6月份股指期货合约的交割日为6月30日。

The net annual cost-of-carry rate is: ｜ 年化净持有成本率为：
$$
r-d=5\%-1.5\%=3.5\%
$$
The spot index levels on 1 April, 1 May, 1 June and 30 June are respectively: ｜ 4月1日、5月1日、6月1日和6月30日的现货指数分别为：
$$
1400,\quad1420,\quad1465,\quad1440
$$
The theoretical futures price is calculated using: ｜ 股指期货理论价格采用以下公式计算：
$$
F(t,T)
=
S(t)
\left[
1+(r-d)\times\text{holding period}
\right]
$$

------

###### Theoretical Price on 1 April ｜ 4月1日的理论价格

The holding period from 1 April to 30 June is three months: ｜ 4月1日至6月30日的持有期为3个月：
$$
\frac{3}{12}\text{ year}
$$
Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
F
=
1400
\times
\left[
1+(5\%-1.5\%)\times\frac{3}{12}
\right]
$$

------

###### Theoretical Price on 1 May ｜ 5月1日的理论价格

The holding period from 1 May to 30 June is two months: ｜ 5月1日至6月30日的持有期为2个月：
$$
\frac{2}{12}\text{ year}
$$
Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
F
=
1420
\times
\left[
1+(5\%-1.5\%)\times\frac{2}{12}
\right]
$$

------

###### Theoretical Price on 1 June ｜ 6月1日的理论价格

The holding period from 1 June to 30 June is one month: ｜ 6月1日至6月30日的持有期为1个月：
$$
\frac{1}{12}\text{ year}
$$
Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
F
=
1465
\times
\left[
1+(5\%-1.5\%)\times\frac{1}{12}
\right]
$$

------

###### Theoretical Price at Delivery ｜ 交割日的理论价格

On 30 June, the remaining holding period is zero. ｜ 到6月30日交割日，剩余持有期为零。

Therefore, the futures price should converge to the spot index: ｜ 因此，股指期货价格应当向现货指数收敛：
$$
F(T,T)=S(T)=1440\text{ points}
$$

------

##### (III) Transaction-Cost Components ｜ （三）交易成本的构成

According to the case, the transaction costs include: ｜ 根据案例，交易成本主要包括：

- Round-trip stock transaction fees and market-impact costs ｜ 股票买卖双边手续费及市场冲击成本
- Round-trip futures transaction fees and market-impact costs ｜ 股指期货买卖双边手续费及市场冲击成本
- Cost caused by the difference between borrowing and lending rates ｜ 借贷利率差成本

The round-trip stock transaction cost is 1.2% of the transaction amount. ｜ 股票买卖双边手续费和市场冲击成本合计为成交金额的1.2%。

Expressed in index points: ｜ 折算为指数点：
$$
\text{Stock transaction cost}
=
1.2\%\times S(t)
$$
The original OCR text contains “1.29%,” but the subsequent calculation uses 1.2%, so 1.2% is retained here. ｜ 原材料OCR中出现了“1.29%”，但后续计算明确使用1.2%，因此此处按1.2%整理。

------

##### (IV) No-Arbitrage Band on 1 April ｜ （四）4月1日的无套利区间

###### Stock Transaction Costs ｜ 股票交易成本

$$
1400\times1.2\%
=
16.8\text{ points}
$$

###### Futures Transaction Costs ｜ 期货交易成本

According to the source, the round-trip futures transaction fees and market-impact costs equal 0.4 index points. ｜ 根据原材料，股指期货买卖双边手续费和市场冲击成本合计为0.4个指数点。

###### Borrowing–Lending Rate Difference ｜ 借贷利率差成本

The borrowing–lending interest-rate difference is 0.5% per year. ｜ 借贷利率差为年化0.5%。

For a three-month holding period: ｜ 持有期为3个月时：
$$
1400\times0.5\%\times\frac{3}{12}
=
1.75\text{ points}
$$

###### Total Transaction Costs ｜ 总交易成本

$$
TC
=
16.8+0.4+1.75
$$

###### Upper Boundary ｜ 无套利区间上界

$$
1412.25+18.95
=
1431.20\text{ points}
$$

###### Lower Boundary ｜ 无套利区间下界

$$
1412.25-18.95
=
1393.30\text{ points}
$$

Therefore, the no-arbitrage band on 1 April is: ｜ 因此，4月1日的无套利区间为：
$$
[1393.30,\ 1431.20]
$$
The width of the no-arbitrage band is: ｜ 无套利区间的宽度为：
$$
1431.20-1393.30
=
37.90\text{ points}
$$
📌 **Actual futures price above 1431.20 → consider cash-and-carry arbitrage.** ｜ 📌 **实际期价高于1431.20点 → 可以考虑正向套利。**

📌 **Actual futures price below 1393.30 → consider reverse arbitrage.** ｜ 📌 **实际期价低于1393.30点 → 可以考虑反向套利。**

📌 **Actual futures price within the band → no effective arbitrage opportunity.** ｜ 📌 **实际期价位于区间内 → 不存在有效套利机会。**

> The source mentions calculating the no-arbitrage bands for both 1 April and 1 June, but only the complete calculation for 1 April is provided in the supplied text. ｜ 原材料提到计算4月1日和6月1日的无套利区间，但当前提供的内容只完整显示了4月1日的计算过程。

------

#### VI. Tracking Error in Arbitrage Trading ｜ 六、套利交易中的模拟误差

In actual arbitrage trading, the spot stock portfolio constructed by the trader is rarely identical to the constituent-stock portfolio of the index. ｜ 在实际套利交易中，交易者构建的现货股票组合很少能够与指数成分股组合完全一致。

The return or price movement of the actual portfolio may therefore differ from that of the index. ｜ 因此，实际现货组合与股票指数未来的走势或者收益率可能不完全一致。

The resulting difference is called tracking error or replication error. ｜ 这种差异通常称为模拟误差或者跟踪误差。

##### Main Sources of Tracking Error ｜ 模拟误差的主要来源

One source is that a stock index may contain a large number of constituent stocks. ｜ 一方面，股票指数所包含的成分股数量可能很多。

Purchasing every component stock in the exact index weight may require substantial capital and involve high transaction costs. ｜ 严格按照指数权重买入全部成分股，可能需要大量资金并产生较高交易成本。

Another source is that most indices are constructed using market-capitalisation weights. ｜ 另一方面，大部分股票指数按照市值权重编制。

Because stocks have minimum trading units, it may be impossible to replicate every index weight precisely. ｜ 由于股票交易存在最小交易单位，投资者可能无法完全按照指数权重进行精确复制。
$$
\text{Actual spot portfolio return}
\neq
\text{Index return}
$$

------

#### VII. Stock Index Futures Calendar Spread Arbitrage ｜ 七、股指期货跨期套利

Stock index futures calendar spread arbitrage uses an abnormal spread between futures contracts with different delivery months. ｜ 股指期货跨期套利是利用不同交割月份股指期货合约之间的异常价差进行交易。

The theoretical spread between two futures contracts can be estimated from the net cost of carry during the difference between their delivery periods. ｜ 两个不同到期月份合约之间的理论价差，可以根据两个交割日之间的净持有成本进行估算。
$$
\text{Theoretical calendar spread}
=
S(r-d)\frac{T_2-T_1}{365}
$$

------

##### Case ｜ 案例

Assume that the market interest rate exceeds the stock dividend yield by 3%. ｜ 假设市场利率比股票股息收益率高3%。
$$
r-d=3\%
$$
At 10:00 a.m. on 1 May, the CSI 300 spot index was 3,000 points. ｜ 5月1日上午10点，沪深300现货指数为3000点。

The June futures contract was quoted at 3,050 points, while the September contract was quoted at 3,100 points. ｜ 6月份股指期货合约价格为3050点，9月份合约价格为3100点。

The actual spread was: ｜ 实际价差为：
$$
3100-3050
=
50\text{ points}
$$
The difference between the two delivery months was approximately three months. ｜ 两个交割月份之间相差约3个月。

The theoretical spread was: ｜ 理论价差为：
$$
3000\times3\%\times\frac{3}{12}
$$
Because the actual spread of 50 points was much greater than the theoretical spread of 22.5 points, the spread was considered overvalued. ｜ 由于实际价差50点明显高于理论价差22.5点，说明两个合约之间的价差被高估。

The trader expected the spread to contract and therefore conducted sell spread arbitrage: ｜ 交易者预计价差将缩小，因此进行卖出套利：

- Bought the June contract at 3,050 points ｜ 以3050点买入6月份合约
- Sold the September contract at 3,100 points ｜ 以3100点卖出9月份合约

At 2:00 p.m. on the same day, the June contract rose to 3,120 points and the September contract rose to 3,150 points. ｜ 当日下午2点，6月份合约上涨至3120点，9月份合约上涨至3150点。

The new spread was: ｜ 新的价差为：
$$
3150-3120
=
30\text{ points}
$$
The spread contracted by: ｜ 价差缩小了：
$$
50-30
=
20\text{ points}
$$

| Contract  | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss |
| --------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------- |
| June      | Buy at 3,050     | Sell at 3,120    | +70 points     |
| September | Sell at 3,100    | Buy at 3,150     | −50 points     |

| 合约  | 建仓     | 平仓     | 盈亏     |
| ----- | -------- | -------- | -------- |
| 6月份 | 买入3050 | 卖出3120 | 盈利70点 |
| 9月份 | 卖出3100 | 买入3150 | 亏损50点 |

The net profit was: ｜ 净盈利为：
$$
70-50
=
20\text{ points}
$$
If the contract multiplier is RMB 300 per point, the profit per spread position is: ｜ 如果合约乘数为每点300元，则每组套利头寸的盈利为：
$$
20\times300
=
6000\text{元}
$$
Ignoring transaction costs, the trader earned RMB 6,000 per spread position. ｜ 在不考虑交易成本的情况下，交易者每组套利头寸盈利6000元。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

📈 **Futures overvalued → buy the spot stock portfolio and sell index futures.** ｜ 📈 **期价高估 → 买入现货股票组合、卖出股指期货。**

📉 **Futures undervalued → sell the spot stock portfolio and buy index futures.** ｜ 📉 **期价低估 → 卖出现货股票组合、买入股指期货。**

⚖️ **The no-arbitrage band equals the theoretical futures price plus or minus total transaction costs.** ｜ ⚖️ **无套利区间等于股指期货理论价格加减全部交易成本。**

🚫 **When the actual futures price lies within the no-arbitrage band, arbitrage cannot cover transaction costs.** ｜ 🚫 **实际期价位于无套利区间以内时，套利收益不足以覆盖交易成本。**

📊 **Tracking error arises when the actual stock portfolio cannot perfectly replicate the index portfolio.** ｜ 📊 **实际股票组合无法完全复制指数成分组合时，会产生模拟误差。**

🔢 **Too many constituent stocks, market-capitalisation weights and minimum trading units can all cause tracking error.** ｜ 🔢 **成分股数量过多、市值加权以及最小交易单位限制都会导致模拟误差。**

📅 **Calendar spread arbitrage compares the actual spread between different delivery months with its theoretical spread.** ｜ 📅 **股指期货跨期套利需要比较不同月份合约的实际价差与理论价差。**

↘️ **If the actual calendar spread is excessively large, buy the nearby contract and sell the distant contract to profit from spread contraction.** ｜ ↘️ **实际跨期价差过大时，可以买入近月合约、卖出远月合约，等待价差缩小获利。**





#### IV. Stock Index Futures Cash-Futures Arbitrage Operations ｜ 四、股指期货期现套利操作

##### (I) Overvalued Futures and Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage ｜ （一）期价高估与正向套利

When the actual stock index futures price is sufficiently higher than its theoretical price, the futures contract is considered overvalued. ｜ 当股指期货的实际价格明显高于其理论价格时，可以认为期货价格被高估。

The trader may conduct cash-and-carry arbitrage by: ｜ 交易者可以采取正向套利：

- Selling the overvalued stock index futures contract ｜ 卖出被高估的股指期货合约
- Buying the corresponding spot stock portfolio ｜ 买入与指数相对应的现货股票组合

$$
\text{Futures overvalued}
\Rightarrow
\text{Buy spot stocks}
+
\text{Sell index futures}
$$

The trader earns a profit when the futures price converges toward the spot price or theoretical value. ｜ 当期货价格向现货价格或者理论价格收敛时，交易者可以获得套利收益。

------

##### (II) Undervalued Futures and Reverse Cash-and-Carry Arbitrage ｜ （二）期价低估与反向套利

When the actual stock index futures price is sufficiently lower than its theoretical price, the futures contract is considered undervalued. ｜ 当股指期货的实际价格明显低于其理论价格时，可以认为期货价格被低估。

The trader may conduct reverse cash-and-carry arbitrage by: ｜ 交易者可以采取反向套利：

- Buying the undervalued stock index futures contract ｜ 买入被低估的股指期货合约
- Selling the corresponding spot stock portfolio ｜ 卖出与指数相对应的现货股票组合

$$
\text{Futures undervalued}
\Rightarrow
\text{Sell spot stocks}
+
\text{Buy index futures}
$$

> In practice, reverse arbitrage may be restricted by the difficulty and cost of short-selling the component stocks. ｜ 实际交易中，反向套利可能受到现货股票卖空难度和成本的限制。

------

#### V. Transaction Costs and the No-Arbitrage Band ｜ 五、交易成本与无套利区间

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）概念

The no-arbitrage band is a price range formed by adjusting the theoretical stock index futures price upward and downward by total transaction costs. ｜ 无套利区间是指在股指期货理论价格的基础上，分别加上和减去交易成本后形成的价格区间。
$$
\text{Upper boundary}
=
\text{Theoretical futures price}
+
\text{Transaction costs}
$$
When the actual futures price lies inside this band, the potential price difference is insufficient to cover transaction costs. ｜ 当实际股指期货价格处于无套利区间以内时，期现价差不足以覆盖交易成本。

An arbitrage transaction would therefore generate no profit and might even result in a loss. ｜ 因此，此时进行套利不仅无法获利，反而可能产生亏损。

| Actual futures price     | Arbitrage decision                               |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------ |
| Above the upper boundary | Cash-and-carry arbitrage may be possible         |
| Within the band          | No effective arbitrage opportunity               |
| Below the lower boundary | Reverse cash-and-carry arbitrage may be possible |

| 实际期货价格 | 套利判断             |
| ------------ | -------------------- |
| 高于上界     | 可能存在正向套利机会 |
| 位于区间内   | 不存在有效套利机会   |
| 低于下界     | 可能存在反向套利机会 |

------

##### (II) Case: Calculating Theoretical Futures Prices ｜ （二）案例：计算股指期货理论价格

Assume: ｜ 假设：
$$
r=5\%
$$
The delivery date of the June futures contract is 30 June. ｜ 6月份股指期货合约的交割日为6月30日。

The net annual cost-of-carry rate is: ｜ 年化净持有成本率为：
$$
r-d=5\%-1.5\%=3.5\%
$$
The spot index levels on 1 April, 1 May, 1 June and 30 June are respectively: ｜ 4月1日、5月1日、6月1日和6月30日的现货指数分别为：
$$
1400,\quad1420,\quad1465,\quad1440
$$
The theoretical futures price is calculated using: ｜ 股指期货理论价格采用以下公式计算：
$$
F(t,T)
=
S(t)
\left[
1+(r-d)\times\text{holding period}
\right]
$$

------

###### Theoretical Price on 1 April ｜ 4月1日的理论价格

The holding period from 1 April to 30 June is three months: ｜ 4月1日至6月30日的持有期为3个月：
$$
\frac{3}{12}\text{ year}
$$
Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
F
=
1400
\times
\left[
1+(5\%-1.5\%)\times\frac{3}{12}
\right]
$$

------

###### Theoretical Price on 1 May ｜ 5月1日的理论价格

The holding period from 1 May to 30 June is two months: ｜ 5月1日至6月30日的持有期为2个月：
$$
\frac{2}{12}\text{ year}
$$
Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
F
=
1420
\times
\left[
1+(5\%-1.5\%)\times\frac{2}{12}
\right]
$$

------

###### Theoretical Price on 1 June ｜ 6月1日的理论价格

The holding period from 1 June to 30 June is one month: ｜ 6月1日至6月30日的持有期为1个月：
$$
\frac{1}{12}\text{ year}
$$
Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
F
=
1465
\times
\left[
1+(5\%-1.5\%)\times\frac{1}{12}
\right]
$$

------

###### Theoretical Price at Delivery ｜ 交割日的理论价格

On 30 June, the remaining holding period is zero. ｜ 到6月30日交割日，剩余持有期为零。

Therefore, the futures price should converge to the spot index: ｜ 因此，股指期货价格应当向现货指数收敛：
$$
F(T,T)=S(T)=1440\text{ points}
$$

------

##### (III) Transaction-Cost Components ｜ （三）交易成本的构成

According to the case, the transaction costs include: ｜ 根据案例，交易成本主要包括：

- Round-trip stock transaction fees and market-impact costs ｜ 股票买卖双边手续费及市场冲击成本
- Round-trip futures transaction fees and market-impact costs ｜ 股指期货买卖双边手续费及市场冲击成本
- Cost caused by the difference between borrowing and lending rates ｜ 借贷利率差成本

The round-trip stock transaction cost is 1.2% of the transaction amount. ｜ 股票买卖双边手续费和市场冲击成本合计为成交金额的1.2%。

Expressed in index points: ｜ 折算为指数点：
$$
\text{Stock transaction cost}
=
1.2\%\times S(t)
$$
The original OCR text contains “1.29%,” but the subsequent calculation uses 1.2%, so 1.2% is retained here. ｜ 原材料OCR中出现了“1.29%”，但后续计算明确使用1.2%，因此此处按1.2%整理。

------

##### (IV) No-Arbitrage Band on 1 April ｜ （四）4月1日的无套利区间

###### Stock Transaction Costs ｜ 股票交易成本

$$
1400\times1.2\%
=
16.8\text{ points}
$$

###### Futures Transaction Costs ｜ 期货交易成本

According to the source, the round-trip futures transaction fees and market-impact costs equal 0.4 index points. ｜ 根据原材料，股指期货买卖双边手续费和市场冲击成本合计为0.4个指数点。

###### Borrowing–Lending Rate Difference ｜ 借贷利率差成本

The borrowing–lending interest-rate difference is 0.5% per year. ｜ 借贷利率差为年化0.5%。

For a three-month holding period: ｜ 持有期为3个月时：
$$
1400\times0.5\%\times\frac{3}{12}
=
1.75\text{ points}
$$

###### Total Transaction Costs ｜ 总交易成本

$$
TC
=
16.8+0.4+1.75
$$

###### Upper Boundary ｜ 无套利区间上界

$$
1412.25+18.95
=
1431.20\text{ points}
$$

###### Lower Boundary ｜ 无套利区间下界

$$
1412.25-18.95
=
1393.30\text{ points}
$$

Therefore, the no-arbitrage band on 1 April is: ｜ 因此，4月1日的无套利区间为：
$$
[1393.30,\ 1431.20]
$$
The width of the no-arbitrage band is: ｜ 无套利区间的宽度为：
$$
1431.20-1393.30
=
37.90\text{ points}
$$
📌 **Actual futures price above 1431.20 → consider cash-and-carry arbitrage.** ｜ 📌 **实际期价高于1431.20点 → 可以考虑正向套利。**

📌 **Actual futures price below 1393.30 → consider reverse arbitrage.** ｜ 📌 **实际期价低于1393.30点 → 可以考虑反向套利。**

📌 **Actual futures price within the band → no effective arbitrage opportunity.** ｜ 📌 **实际期价位于区间内 → 不存在有效套利机会。**

> The source mentions calculating the no-arbitrage bands for both 1 April and 1 June, but only the complete calculation for 1 April is provided in the supplied text. ｜ 原材料提到计算4月1日和6月1日的无套利区间，但当前提供的内容只完整显示了4月1日的计算过程。

------

#### VI. Tracking Error in Arbitrage Trading ｜ 六、套利交易中的模拟误差

In actual arbitrage trading, the spot stock portfolio constructed by the trader is rarely identical to the constituent-stock portfolio of the index. ｜ 在实际套利交易中，交易者构建的现货股票组合很少能够与指数成分股组合完全一致。

The return or price movement of the actual portfolio may therefore differ from that of the index. ｜ 因此，实际现货组合与股票指数未来的走势或者收益率可能不完全一致。

The resulting difference is called tracking error or replication error. ｜ 这种差异通常称为模拟误差或者跟踪误差。

##### Main Sources of Tracking Error ｜ 模拟误差的主要来源

One source is that a stock index may contain a large number of constituent stocks. ｜ 一方面，股票指数所包含的成分股数量可能很多。

Purchasing every component stock in the exact index weight may require substantial capital and involve high transaction costs. ｜ 严格按照指数权重买入全部成分股，可能需要大量资金并产生较高交易成本。

Another source is that most indices are constructed using market-capitalisation weights. ｜ 另一方面，大部分股票指数按照市值权重编制。

Because stocks have minimum trading units, it may be impossible to replicate every index weight precisely. ｜ 由于股票交易存在最小交易单位，投资者可能无法完全按照指数权重进行精确复制。
$$
\text{Actual spot portfolio return}
\neq
\text{Index return}
$$

------

#### VII. Stock Index Futures Calendar Spread Arbitrage ｜ 七、股指期货跨期套利

Stock index futures calendar spread arbitrage uses an abnormal spread between futures contracts with different delivery months. ｜ 股指期货跨期套利是利用不同交割月份股指期货合约之间的异常价差进行交易。

The theoretical spread between two futures contracts can be estimated from the net cost of carry during the difference between their delivery periods. ｜ 两个不同到期月份合约之间的理论价差，可以根据两个交割日之间的净持有成本进行估算。
$$
\text{Theoretical calendar spread}
=
S(r-d)\frac{T_2-T_1}{365}
$$

------

##### Case ｜ 案例

Assume that the market interest rate exceeds the stock dividend yield by 3%. ｜ 假设市场利率比股票股息收益率高3%。
$$
r-d=3\%
$$
At 10:00 a.m. on 1 May, the CSI 300 spot index was 3,000 points. ｜ 5月1日上午10点，沪深300现货指数为3000点。

The June futures contract was quoted at 3,050 points, while the September contract was quoted at 3,100 points. ｜ 6月份股指期货合约价格为3050点，9月份合约价格为3100点。

The actual spread was: ｜ 实际价差为：
$$
3100-3050
=
50\text{ points}
$$
The difference between the two delivery months was approximately three months. ｜ 两个交割月份之间相差约3个月。

The theoretical spread was: ｜ 理论价差为：
$$
3000\times3\%\times\frac{3}{12}
$$
Because the actual spread of 50 points was much greater than the theoretical spread of 22.5 points, the spread was considered overvalued. ｜ 由于实际价差50点明显高于理论价差22.5点，说明两个合约之间的价差被高估。

The trader expected the spread to contract and therefore conducted sell spread arbitrage: ｜ 交易者预计价差将缩小，因此进行卖出套利：

- Bought the June contract at 3,050 points ｜ 以3050点买入6月份合约
- Sold the September contract at 3,100 points ｜ 以3100点卖出9月份合约

At 2:00 p.m. on the same day, the June contract rose to 3,120 points and the September contract rose to 3,150 points. ｜ 当日下午2点，6月份合约上涨至3120点，9月份合约上涨至3150点。

The new spread was: ｜ 新的价差为：
$$
3150-3120
=
30\text{ points}
$$
The spread contracted by: ｜ 价差缩小了：
$$
50-30
=
20\text{ points}
$$

| Contract  | Opening position | Closing position | Profit or loss |
| --------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------- |
| June      | Buy at 3,050     | Sell at 3,120    | +70 points     |
| September | Sell at 3,100    | Buy at 3,150     | −50 points     |

| 合约  | 建仓     | 平仓     | 盈亏     |
| ----- | -------- | -------- | -------- |
| 6月份 | 买入3050 | 卖出3120 | 盈利70点 |
| 9月份 | 卖出3100 | 买入3150 | 亏损50点 |

The net profit was: ｜ 净盈利为：
$$
70-50
=
20\text{ points}
$$
If the contract multiplier is RMB 300 per point, the profit per spread position is: ｜ 如果合约乘数为每点300元，则每组套利头寸的盈利为：
$$
20\times300
=
6000\text{元}
$$
Ignoring transaction costs, the trader earned RMB 6,000 per spread position. ｜ 在不考虑交易成本的情况下，交易者每组套利头寸盈利6000元。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

📈 **Futures overvalued → buy the spot stock portfolio and sell index futures.** ｜ 📈 **期价高估 → 买入现货股票组合、卖出股指期货。**

📉 **Futures undervalued → sell the spot stock portfolio and buy index futures.** ｜ 📉 **期价低估 → 卖出现货股票组合、买入股指期货。**

⚖️ **The no-arbitrage band equals the theoretical futures price plus or minus total transaction costs.** ｜ ⚖️ **无套利区间等于股指期货理论价格加减全部交易成本。**

🚫 **When the actual futures price lies within the no-arbitrage band, arbitrage cannot cover transaction costs.** ｜ 🚫 **实际期价位于无套利区间以内时，套利收益不足以覆盖交易成本。**

📊 **Tracking error arises when the actual stock portfolio cannot perfectly replicate the index portfolio.** ｜ 📊 **实际股票组合无法完全复制指数成分组合时，会产生模拟误差。**

🔢 **Too many constituent stocks, market-capitalisation weights and minimum trading units can all cause tracking error.** ｜ 🔢 **成分股数量过多、市值加权以及最小交易单位限制都会导致模拟误差。**

📅 **Calendar spread arbitrage compares the actual spread between different delivery months with its theoretical spread.** ｜ 📅 **股指期货跨期套利需要比较不同月份合约的实际价差与理论价差。**

↘️ **If the actual calendar spread is excessively large, buy the nearby contract and sell the distant contract to profit from spread contraction.** ｜ ↘️ **实际跨期价差过大时，可以买入近月合约、卖出远月合约，等待价差缩小获利。**



# Chapter 9: Options ｜ 第九章 期权

This chapter accounts for approximately 12% of the examination score. ｜ 本章分值占比约为12%。

## Chapter Structure ｜ 本章结构

- Section 1: Options and Options Trading ｜ 第一节：期权及期权交易
- Section 2: Option Prices and Influencing Factors ｜ 第二节：期权价格及影响因素
- Section 3: Option Trading Strategies ｜ 第三节：期权交易策略
- Section 4: Exotic Options ｜ 第四节：奇异期权

------

## Section 1: Options and Options Trading ｜ 第一节 期权及期权交易

### Examination Topic: Overview of Options ｜ 考点：期权概述

#### I. Definition of an Option ｜ 一、期权的定义

An option, also called a right of choice, gives its buyer the right to buy or sell a specified quantity of an underlying asset at a predetermined price within an agreed period. ｜ 期权又称选择权，是指期权买方有权在约定期限内，按照事先确定的价格，买入或者卖出一定数量某种标的资产的权利。

The buyer has a **right rather than an obligation**. The buyer may exercise the option when doing so is favourable or abandon it when exercising would be unfavourable. ｜ 期权买方拥有的是一种**权利而不是义务**。当行权对自己有利时，买方可以行权；当行权不利时，买方可以放弃行权。

The option seller receives the option premium and must fulfil the corresponding obligation if the buyer exercises the option. ｜ 期权卖方收取权利金。如果买方决定行权，卖方必须履行相应义务。

------

#### II. Basic Elements of an Option ｜ 二、期权的基本要素

##### (I) Option Price ｜ （一）期权价格

The option price is the amount paid by the buyer to obtain the option right. ｜ 期权价格是期权买方为了获得期权权利而向卖方支付的费用。

It is also called: ｜ 期权价格又称为：

- Option premium ｜ 权利金
- Option fee ｜ 期权费
- Insurance premium ｜ 保险费

The buyer pays the option premium when purchasing the option. Generally, this premium is also the buyer’s maximum possible loss. ｜ 期权买方在购买期权时支付权利金。一般情况下，该权利金也是期权买方可能遭受的最大损失。

------

##### (II) Underlying Asset ｜ （二）标的资产

The underlying asset is the asset to which the option right relates. ｜ 标的资产是期权买卖权利所指向的资产。

An option’s underlying asset may be: ｜ 期权的标的资产可以是：

- A spot asset ｜ 现货资产
- A futures asset ｜ 期货资产
- A physical asset ｜ 实物资产
- A financial asset ｜ 金融资产
- A financial indicator ｜ 金融指标

------

##### (III) Exercise Direction ｜ （三）行权方向

The exercise direction determines whether the option buyer has the right to buy or sell the underlying asset. ｜ 行权方向决定期权买方获得的是买入标的资产的权利，还是卖出标的资产的权利。

Accordingly, options may be divided into: ｜ 根据行权方向，期权可以分为：

- Call options ｜ 看涨期权
- Put options ｜ 看跌期权

------

##### (IV) Exercise Price ｜ （四）执行价格

The exercise price is the price at which the option buyer may buy or sell the underlying asset when exercising the option. ｜ 执行价格是期权买方行使权利时，按照约定买入或者卖出标的资产的价格。

It is also called: ｜ 执行价格又称：

- Strike price ｜ 履约价格
- Exercise price ｜ 行权价格

------

##### (V) Validity Period ｜ （五）有效期限

The validity period is the period during which the option buyer may exercise the option. ｜ 有效期限是指期权买方可以行使期权权利的期限。

Once the option expires, the buyer’s right becomes invalid if the option has not been exercised. ｜ 期权到期后，如果买方没有行权，其期权权利随之失效。

------

### Examination Topic: Classification of Options ｜ 考点：期权的分类

#### I. Classification by the Buyer’s Exercise Direction ｜ 一、按照买方行权方向分类

##### (I) Call Option ｜ （一）看涨期权

A call option gives the buyer the right to buy a specified asset at the agreed exercise price in the future. ｜ 看涨期权赋予期权买方在未来按照约定的执行价格买入某种标的资产的权利。

If the market price of the underlying asset rises above the exercise price, exercising the call option may be favourable to the buyer. ｜ 如果未来标的资产市场价格上涨至执行价格以上，看涨期权买方可以选择行权。

If the market price falls below the exercise price, the buyer may abandon the option. ｜ 如果未来标的资产价格低于执行价格，买方可以放弃行权。

The premium is the price paid by the buyer to obtain this right. ｜ 期权费是买方为了取得这一买入权利所支付的费用。
$$
\text{Call option}
=
\text{Right to buy the underlying asset}
$$

------

###### Call Option Case ｜ 看涨期权案例

The source provides a quotation for a General Electric stock call option obtained from the CBOE website around 1 August 2017. ｜ 原材料提供了一份2017年8月1日前后从CBOE网站获取的通用电气股票看涨期权报价。

The main contract information was: ｜ 主要合约信息如下：

| Item                | Quotation        |
| ------------------- | ---------------- |
| Underlying stock    | General Electric |
| Option type         | Call option      |
| Expiry date         | 4 August 2017    |
| Exercise price      | USD 25.50        |
| Stock market price  | USD 25.52        |
| Latest option price | USD 0.24         |
| Previous close      | USD 0.21         |
| Opening price       | USD 0.22         |
| Highest price       | USD 0.24         |
| Lowest price        | USD 0.18         |
| Bid price           | USD 0.22         |
| Ask price           | USD 0.28         |
| Trading volume      | 207              |
| Open interest       | 1,383            |

| 项目           | 报价         |
| -------------- | ------------ |
| 标的股票       | 通用电气     |
| 期权类型       | 看涨期权     |
| 到期日         | 2017年8月4日 |
| 执行价格       | 25.50美元    |
| 股票市场价格   | 25.52美元    |
| 期权最新成交价 | 0.24美元     |
| 前收盘价       | 0.21美元     |
| 开盘价         | 0.22美元     |
| 最高价         | 0.24美元     |
| 最低价         | 0.18美元     |
| 买入价         | 0.22美元     |
| 卖出价         | 0.28美元     |
| 成交量         | 207          |
| 持仓量         | 1383         |

According to the example, an investor who paid USD 0.24 for the call option obtained the right to buy one share of General Electric stock at USD 25.50 before 4 August 2017. ｜ 按照教材案例，投资者支付0.24美元购买该看涨期权后，有权在2017年8月4日之前，以25.50美元的价格买入1股通用电气股票。

> 原材料按照“1份期权对应1股”进行说明，下面沿用教材案例口径。

------

###### Result When the Stock Price Is Above the Exercise Price ｜ 到期股票价格高于执行价格
