If the stock price at expiry is higher than USD 25.50, the buyer should exercise the option. ｜ 如果到期时股票价格高于25.50美元，期权买方应当行权。

Suppose the stock price at expiry is $S_T$. The exercise gain is: ｜ 假设到期股票价格为 $S_T$，行权收益为：
$$
S_T-25.50
$$
After deducting the premium of USD 0.24, the buyer’s net profit is: ｜ 扣除0.24美元权利金后，期权买方的净收益为：
$$
S_T-25.50-0.24
$$
However, merely having a stock price above the exercise price does not necessarily mean that the buyer has earned a net profit. The price must exceed the exercise price plus the premium. ｜ 但是，股票价格仅仅高于执行价格，并不一定意味着期权买方已经获得净利润。股票价格还需要高于执行价格与权利金之和。

The break-even stock price is: ｜ 盈亏平衡点为：
$$
25.50+0.24
=
25.74\text{美元}
$$

------

###### Result When the Stock Price Is Below the Exercise Price ｜ 到期股票价格低于执行价格

If the stock price at expiry is below USD 25.50, the buyer should abandon the option. ｜ 如果到期时股票价格低于25.50美元，期权买方应当放弃行权。

The buyer’s loss is limited to the option premium: ｜ 买方的损失仅限于已经支付的权利金：
$$
\text{Maximum loss}
=
0.24\text{美元}
$$
This is the maximum possible loss for the call-option buyer. ｜ 这也是看涨期权买方可能遭受的最大损失。

------

###### Long Call Payoff and Profit ｜ 看涨期权多头的收益与损益

The payoff at expiry is: ｜ 看涨期权买方的到期行权收益为：
$$
\max(S_T-K,0)
$$
The net profit is: ｜ 扣除权利金后的净损益为：
$$
\max(S_T-K,0)-C
$$
where: ｜ 其中：

- $S_T$: underlying asset price at expiry ｜ $S_T$：到期时标的资产价格
- $K$: exercise price ｜ $K$：执行价格
- $C$: call-option premium ｜ $C$：看涨期权权利金

For this case: ｜ 本案例中：
$$
K=25.50,\qquad C=0.24
$$

| Stock price at expiry | Exercise decision | Buyer’s result   |
| --------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------- |
| $S_T\leq25.50$        | Abandon           | Loss of USD 0.24 |
| $25.50                | Exercise          | Partial loss     |
| $S_T=25.74$           | Exercise          | Break-even       |
| $S_T>25.74$           | Exercise          | Net profit       |

| 到期股票价格   | 行权决策 | 买方结果     |
| -------------- | -------- | ------------ |
| $S_T\leq25.50$ | 放弃行权 | 亏损0.24美元 |
| $25.50         | 行权     | 仍然部分亏损 |
| $S_T=25.74$    | 行权     | 盈亏平衡     |
| $S_T>25.74$    | 行权     | 获得净利润   |

📌 **A call-option buyer expects the underlying asset price to rise.** ｜ 📌 **看涨期权买方预期标的资产价格上涨。**

📌 **Maximum loss is limited to the premium, while potential profit increases as the asset price rises.** ｜ 📌 **看涨期权买方最大损失为权利金，潜在收益会随着标的资产价格上涨而增加。**

------

##### (II) Put Option ｜ （二）看跌期权

A put option gives the buyer the right to sell a specified asset at the agreed exercise price in the future. ｜ 看跌期权赋予期权买方在未来按照约定的执行价格卖出某种标的资产的权利。

If the market price of the underlying asset falls below the exercise price, exercising the put option may be favourable. ｜ 如果未来标的资产价格下跌至执行价格以下，看跌期权买方可以选择行权。

If the market price rises above the exercise price, the buyer may abandon the option. ｜ 如果未来标的资产价格高于执行价格，买方可以放弃行权。
$$
\text{Put option}
=
\text{Right to sell the underlying asset}
$$

------

###### Put Option Case ｜ 看跌期权案例

The source provides a quotation for a General Electric stock put option obtained from the CBOE website on 1 August 2017. ｜ 原材料提供了一份2017年8月1日从CBOE网站获取的通用电气股票看跌期权报价。

The option expired on 4 August 2017 and had an exercise price of USD 25.50. ｜ 该期权于2017年8月4日到期，执行价格为25.50美元。

The main quotation information was: ｜ 主要报价信息如下：

| Item                | Quotation        |
| ------------------- | ---------------- |
| Underlying stock    | General Electric |
| Option type         | Put option       |
| Expiry date         | 4 August 2017    |
| Exercise price      | USD 25.50        |
| Latest option price | USD 0.15         |
| Previous close      | USD 0.09         |
| Opening price       | USD 0.10         |
| Highest price       | USD 0.15         |
| Lowest price        | USD 0.10         |
| Bid price           | USD 0.11         |
| Ask price           | USD 0.17         |
| Trading volume      | 20               |
| Open interest       | 1,537            |

| 项目           | 报价         |
| -------------- | ------------ |
| 标的股票       | 通用电气     |
| 期权类型       | 看跌期权     |
| 到期日         | 2017年8月4日 |
| 执行价格       | 25.50美元    |
| 期权最新成交价 | 0.15美元     |
| 前收盘价       | 0.09美元     |
| 开盘价         | 0.10美元     |
| 最高价         | 0.15美元     |
| 最低价         | 0.10美元     |
| 买入价         | 0.11美元     |
| 卖出价         | 0.17美元     |
| 成交量         | 20           |
| 持仓量         | 1537         |

According to the case, an investor who paid USD 0.15 for the put option obtained the right to sell one share of General Electric stock at USD 25.50 before 4 August 2017. ｜ 按照教材案例，投资者支付0.15美元购买该看跌期权后，有权在2017年8月4日之前，以25.50美元的价格卖出1股通用电气股票。

------

###### Result When the Stock Price Is Below the Exercise Price ｜ 到期股票价格低于执行价格

If the stock price at expiry is below USD 25.50, the buyer should exercise the put option. ｜ 如果到期时股票价格低于25.50美元，看跌期权买方应当行权。

Suppose the stock price at expiry is $S_T$. The exercise gain is: ｜ 假设到期股票价格为 $S_T$，行权收益为：
$$
25.50-S_T
$$
After deducting the premium of USD 0.15, the net profit is: ｜ 扣除0.15美元权利金后，净收益为：
$$
25.50-S_T-0.15
$$
The break-even stock price is: ｜ 盈亏平衡点为：
$$
25.50-0.15
=
25.35\text{美元}
$$

------

###### Result When the Stock Price Is Above the Exercise Price ｜ 到期股票价格高于执行价格

If the stock price at expiry is above USD 25.50, the buyer should abandon the option. ｜ 如果到期时股票价格高于25.50美元，看跌期权买方应当放弃行权。

The buyer loses the entire premium: ｜ 买方损失全部权利金：
$$
\text{Maximum loss}
=
0.15\text{美元}
$$
This is the maximum possible loss for the put-option buyer. ｜ 这也是看跌期权买方可能遭受的最大损失。

------

###### Long Put Payoff and Profit ｜ 看跌期权多头的收益与损益

The payoff at expiry is: ｜ 看跌期权买方的到期行权收益为：
$$
\max(K-S_T,0)
$$
The net profit is: ｜ 扣除权利金后的净损益为：
$$
\max(K-S_T,0)-P
$$
where: ｜ 其中：

- $S_T$: underlying asset price at expiry ｜ $S_T$：到期时标的资产价格
- $K$: exercise price ｜ $K$：执行价格
- $P$: put-option premium ｜ $P$：看跌期权权利金

For this case: ｜ 本案例中：
$$
K=25.50,\qquad P=0.15
$$

| Stock price at expiry | Exercise decision | Buyer’s result   |
| --------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------- |
| $S_T\geq25.50$        | Abandon           | Loss of USD 0.15 |
| $25.35                | Exercise          | Partial loss     |
| $S_T=25.35$           | Exercise          | Break-even       |
| $S_T<25.35$           | Exercise          | Net profit       |

| 到期股票价格   | 行权决策 | 买方结果     |
| -------------- | -------- | ------------ |
| $S_T\geq25.50$ | 放弃行权 | 亏损0.15美元 |
| $25.35         | 行权     | 仍然部分亏损 |
| $S_T=25.35$    | 行权     | 盈亏平衡     |
| $S_T<25.35$    | 行权     | 获得净利润   |

📌 **A put-option buyer expects the underlying asset price to fall.** ｜ 📌 **看跌期权买方预期标的资产价格下跌。**

📌 **Maximum loss is limited to the premium, while the option becomes more valuable as the asset price falls.** ｜ 📌 **看跌期权买方最大损失为权利金，标的资产价格越低，期权价值通常越高。**

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🎟️ **An option gives the buyer a right, not an obligation.** ｜ 🎟️ **期权赋予买方的是权利，而不是必须履行的义务。**

💰 **The buyer pays the option premium, which is generally the buyer’s maximum possible loss.** ｜ 💰 **期权买方支付权利金，权利金通常是买方可能遭受的最大损失。**

📦 **The underlying asset may be a spot asset, futures asset, physical asset, financial asset or financial indicator.** ｜ 📦 **期权标的可以是现货、期货、实物资产、金融资产或者金融指标。**

📈 **A call option gives the buyer the right to buy the underlying asset at the exercise price.** ｜ 📈 **看涨期权赋予买方按照执行价格买入标的资产的权利。**

📉 **A put option gives the buyer the right to sell the underlying asset at the exercise price.** ｜ 📉 **看跌期权赋予买方按照执行价格卖出标的资产的权利。**

⚖️ **Call break-even price equals the exercise price plus the premium.** ｜ ⚖️ **看涨期权盈亏平衡点等于执行价格加权利金。**

⚖️ **Put break-even price equals the exercise price minus the premium.** ｜ ⚖️ **看跌期权盈亏平衡点等于执行价格减权利金。**

⏳ **If the option is not exercised before expiry, the buyer’s right expires.** ｜ ⏳ **期权到期前未行权，买方的权利将随期权到期而失效。**

#### II. Classification by the Time at Which the Buyer May Exercise ｜ 二、按照买方行权时间分类

##### (I) American Options ｜ （一）美式期权

An American option allows the buyer to exercise the option at any time on or before the expiry date. ｜ 美式期权允许期权买方在到期日之前的任何时间行权，包括到期日当天。
$$
\text{Exercise time}
=
\text{Any time before or on expiry}
$$
The source gives options on stock index futures listed by the CME Group as an example. ｜ 原材料以CME集团上市的股指期货期权等作为例子。

##### (II) European Options ｜ （二）欧式期权

A European option allows the buyer to exercise the option only on the expiry date. ｜ 欧式期权只允许期权买方在期权到期日行权。
$$
\text{Exercise time}
=
\text{Expiry date only}
$$
The source lists stock price index options and stock options traded on exchanges in Hong Kong and mainland China as examples. ｜ 原材料列举了香港交易所、中国金融期货交易所和上海证券交易所的部分股票指数期权或者股票期权作为例子。

| Type            | Exercise time                   |
| --------------- | ------------------------------- |
| American option | At any time before or on expiry |
| European option | Only on the expiry date         |

| 类型     | 行权时间                 |
| -------- | ------------------------ |
| 美式期权 | 到期日前任何时间均可行权 |
| 欧式期权 | 只能在到期日行权         |

📌 **“American” and “European” describe exercise rules, not the location where the option is traded.** ｜ 📌 **“美式”和“欧式”表示的是行权时间规则，不是期权的交易地区。**

------

#### III. Classification by Underlying Asset ｜ 三、按照期权标的资产分类

##### (I) Commodity Options ｜ （一）商品期权

Commodity options are options whose underlying assets are commodities or commodity futures contracts. ｜ 商品期权是指以商品现货或者商品期货合约作为标的资产的期权。

Examples may involve agricultural products, metals and energy products. ｜ 其标的通常涉及农产品、金属或者能源等商品。

##### (II) Financial Options ｜ （二）金融期权

Financial options are options whose underlying assets are financial assets or financial indicators. ｜ 金融期权是指以金融资产或者金融指标作为标的资产的期权。

Their underlying assets may include: ｜ 其标的资产可以包括：

- Stocks ｜ 股票
- Stock indices ｜ 股票价格指数
- Interest rates or bonds ｜ 利率或者债券
- Foreign currencies ｜ 外汇
- Financial futures contracts ｜ 金融期货合约

------

#### IV. Classification by Trading Venue ｜ 四、按照是否在集中化市场交易分类

Options may be divided into exchange-traded options and over-the-counter options according to the type of market in which they are traded. ｜ 按照交易市场类型的不同，期权可以分为场内期权和场外期权。

##### (I) Exchange-Traded Options ｜ （一）场内期权

Exchange-traded options are options listed and traded on organised exchanges. ｜ 场内期权是指在集中化、规范化的交易所市场挂牌交易的期权。

Examples given in the source include commodity futures options and financial futures options listed by the CME Group. ｜ 原材料列举了CME集团上市的商品期货期权和金融期货期权等。

Exchange-traded options may be divided into: ｜ 场内期权可以进一步分为：

###### Spot Options ｜ 现货期权

A spot option has a spot asset as its underlying asset. ｜ 现货期权以现货资产作为期权标的。

###### Futures Options ｜ 期货期权

A futures option has a futures contract as its underlying asset. ｜ 期货期权以期货合约作为期权标的。

------

##### (II) Over-the-Counter Options ｜ （二）场外期权

Over-the-counter options are options negotiated and traded outside organised exchanges. ｜ 场外期权是指在集中化交易所之外，由交易双方协商达成的期权。

The source gives renminbi foreign exchange options traded in China’s interbank market as an example. ｜ 原材料以我国银行间市场交易的人民币外汇期权作为例子。

Options therefore exist in both standardised exchange markets and flexible over-the-counter markets. ｜ 因此，期权既可以在标准化、集中化的交易所市场进行交易，也可以在场外市场进行交易。

------

##### (III) Characteristics of Over-the-Counter Options ｜ （三）场外期权的特点

###### Non-Standardised Contracts ｜ 合约非标准化

The terms of an over-the-counter option can be negotiated according to the specific needs of the parties. ｜ 场外期权的合约条款可以根据交易双方的具体需求进行协商。

The underlying asset, quantity, exercise price, expiry date and settlement method may all be customised. ｜ 标的资产、交易数量、执行价格、到期时间和结算方式等内容都可以进行定制。

###### Diverse Products and Flexible Forms ｜ 交易品种多样、形式灵活

Over-the-counter options can be designed in many forms and may cover a wide range of underlying assets. ｜ 场外期权可以设计成多种形式，并覆盖范围较广的标的资产。

The market may also have a relatively large transaction scale. ｜ 其市场交易规模也可能较大。

###### Institutional Counterparties ｜ 交易对手机构化

Participants in over-the-counter options are mainly financial institutions, enterprises and other institutional investors. ｜ 场外期权的交易对手通常以金融机构、企业和其他机构投资者为主。

###### Greater Liquidity and Credit Risks ｜ 流动性风险和信用风险较大

Because over-the-counter contracts are non-standardised, it may be difficult to transfer or close a position before expiry. ｜ 由于场外期权合约缺乏标准化，交易者可能难以在到期前转让或者平仓。

In addition, transactions depend on the counterparty’s ability to perform its obligations, resulting in counterparty credit risk. ｜ 此外，场外交易依赖交易对手履行合约义务，因此存在交易对手信用风险。

| Item           | Exchange-traded options        | Over-the-counter options            |
| -------------- | ------------------------------ | ----------------------------------- |
| Contract terms | Standardised                   | Customised                          |
| Trading venue  | Organised exchange             | OTC market                          |
| Clearing       | Generally centralised clearing | Depends on contractual arrangements |
| Flexibility    | Relatively low                 | Relatively high                     |
| Liquidity      | Generally higher               | May be lower                        |
| Credit risk    | Relatively controlled          | Relatively greater                  |

| 项目     | 场内期权     | 场外期权       |
| -------- | ------------ | -------------- |
| 合约条款 | 标准化       | 可以定制       |
| 交易场所 | 集中化交易所 | 场外市场       |
| 结算方式 | 通常集中清算 | 取决于双方协议 |
| 灵活性   | 相对较低     | 相对较高       |
| 流动性   | 通常较高     | 可能较低       |
| 信用风险 | 相对可控     | 相对较大       |

------

##### (IV) Classification of Certain Domestic Options ｜ （四）部分国内期权的类别

According to the source, the options listed on the Shanghai Stock Exchange and Shenzhen Stock Exchange have exchange-traded funds tracking stock indices as their underlying assets. ｜ 根据原材料，上海证券交易所和深圳证券交易所上市的期权，其标的资产均为跟踪股票指数的交易型开放式指数基金，即ETF。

These products are classified as stock options and financial spot options. ｜ 这些期权属于股票期权，同时属于金融现货期权。

The CSI 300 stock index option listed on the China Financial Futures Exchange uses the stock price index itself rather than a stock index futures contract as its underlying asset. ｜ 中国金融期货交易所上市的沪深300股指期权，以股票价格指数本身而不是股指期货合约作为标的资产。

It is therefore also classified as a financial spot option. ｜ 因此，该期权也属于金融现货期权。

📌 **An option on a stock index is not necessarily an option on stock index futures.** ｜ 📌 **股指期权的标的是股票指数，并不等同于股指期货期权。**

------

#### V. Exotic Options ｜ 五、奇异期权

The options market is one of the most active areas of financial innovation. ｜ 期权市场是金融创新较为活跃的市场之一。

Since the 1990s, many non-standardised options with risk and return characteristics different from those of traditional options have appeared. ｜ 20世纪90年代以来，市场中出现了许多收益和风险特征区别于传统期权的非标准化期权。

These options are collectively called exotic options. ｜ 这些非标准化期权统称为奇异期权。

Exotic options are mainly created through innovations such as: ｜ 奇异期权主要通过以下方式进行创新：

- Changing the characteristics of the option’s payoff at expiry ｜ 改变期权到期收益的特征
- Adding activation mechanisms ｜ 附加期权激活机制
- Adding termination mechanisms ｜ 附加期权终止机制
- Changing one underlying asset into multiple underlying assets ｜ 将单一标的资产扩展为多种标的资产

Compared with traditional call and put options, exotic options generally have more complex payoff conditions. ｜ 与传统看涨期权和看跌期权相比，奇异期权的收益条件通常更加复杂。

------

### Examination Topic: Trading of Exchange-Traded Options ｜ 考点：场内期权的交易

#### I. Standardised Contracts ｜ 一、标准化合约

Exchange-traded option contracts have standardised terms determined by the exchange. ｜ 场内期权合约具有由交易所统一规定的标准化条款。

These terms mainly include the contract unit, exercise price, expiry arrangements and delivery rules. ｜ 这些条款主要包括交易单位、执行价格、到期安排和交割规定等。

------

##### (I) Trading Unit ｜ （一）交易单位

The trading unit, also called the contract size, is the quantity of the underlying asset represented by one option contract. ｜ 交易单位又称合约规模，是指一张期权合约所代表的标的资产数量。

For example, one stock option contract commonly represents 100 shares of the underlying stock. ｜ 例如，一张股票期权合约通常代表100股标的股票。
$$
\text{Total underlying quantity}
=
\text{Number of option contracts}
\times
\text{Contract size}
$$

------

##### (II) Exercise Price ｜ （二）执行价格

The exercise prices of exchange-traded option contracts are selected in advance by the exchange. ｜ 场内期权合约的执行价格由交易所事先设定。

When an exchange prepares to list a new option, it generally first determines a central exercise price according to the recent closing price of the underlying asset. ｜ 当交易所准备上市某种期权时，通常先根据标的资产的最近收盘价确定一个中心执行价格。

The exchange then sets several higher and lower exercise prices at predetermined intervals. ｜ 之后，交易所再按照规定的执行价格间距，在中心执行价格上下设置若干执行价格。

Therefore, option contract specifications generally prescribe the interval between exercise prices rather than fixing only one exercise price. ｜ 因此，期权合约规则通常规定执行价格的级距，而不是只规定一个固定的执行价格。

------

###### CBOE Example ｜ CBOE案例

According to the source, the exercise-price intervals for certain CBOE stock options were: ｜ 根据原材料，CBOE部分股票期权的执行价格级距为：

| Stock price                    | Exercise-price interval |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- |
| USD 5–25                       | USD 2.50                |
| Above USD 25 but below USD 200 | USD 5                   |
| Above USD 200                  | USD 10                  |

| 股票价格                | 执行价格级距 |
| ----------------------- | ------------ |
| 5～25美元               | 2.5美元      |
| 高于25美元但低于200美元 | 5美元        |
| 高于200美元             | 10美元       |

------

###### SSE 50 ETF Option Example ｜ 上证50ETF期权案例

According to the source, on the first listing day of an option for each expiry month, the exchange lists five different exercise prices. ｜ 根据原材料，上证50ETF期权每个到期月份的期权在上市首日会挂牌五个不同的执行价格。

These include: ｜ 其中包括：

- One at-the-money or near-the-money option ｜ 一个平值或者接近平值期权
- Two in-the-money options ｜ 两个实值期权
- Two out-of-the-money options ｜ 两个虚值期权

As the price of the SSE 50 ETF changes, the exchange may list additional options with new exercise prices. ｜ 随着上证50ETF价格发生变化，交易所会适时增加新的执行价格。

------

##### (III) Expiry Cycle, Expiry Month, Expiry Date, Last Trading Day and Exercise Date ｜ （三）到期循环、到期月、到期日、最后交易日和执行日

###### Expiry Cycle ｜ 到期循环

An expiry cycle is a system used to arrange the months in which option contracts expire. ｜ 到期循环是用于安排期权合约到期月份的一种制度。

Assume that option contracts are organised around January, February and March cycles. ｜ 假设期权按照1月、2月和3月三个基础月份进行循环。

Options in the January cycle may have expiry months of January, April, July and October. ｜ 属于1月循环的期权，其到期月份可以包括1月、4月、7月和10月。

The other cycles are determined in a similar manner. ｜ 其他到期循环以此类推。

------

###### Expiry Month ｜ 到期月

The expiry month is the month in which the option contract expires. ｜ 到期月是期权合约到期所在的月份。

For example, on 1 December, an option belonging to the January cycle may include the following four expiry months: ｜ 例如，在12月1日，一个属于1月循环的期权可能包括以下四个到期月份：
$$
\text{December, January, April and July}
$$
After the December option expires, the listed expiry months may become: ｜ 12月份期权到期后，挂牌到期月份可能变为：
$$
\text{January, February, April and July}
$$

------

###### Expiry Date ｜ 到期日

The expiry date is the final date on which the buyer retains the rights granted by the option contract. ｜ 到期日是期权买方享有期权权利的最后日期。

According to the historical example in the source, the expiry date of a CBOE stock option was the Saturday immediately following the third Friday of the expiry month. ｜ 根据原材料中的历史案例，CBOE股票期权的到期日为到期月份第三个星期五之后紧接的星期六。

------

###### Last Trading Day ｜ 最后交易日

The last trading day is the final date on which investors may trade the option contract. ｜ 最后交易日是期权交易者可以买卖该期权合约的最后日期。

It is closely related to the expiry date. ｜ 最后交易日与到期日密切相关。

According to the source’s CBOE example, the last trading day was the third Friday of the expiry month. ｜ 根据原材料中的CBOE案例，股票期权的最后交易日为到期月份的第三个星期五。

------

###### Exercise Date ｜ 执行日

The exercise date is the date prescribed by the exchange on which the option buyer may actually exercise the option. ｜ 执行日是交易所规定的、期权买方可以实际行使期权权利的日期。

The exercise date, expiry date and last trading day may be the same date or different dates, depending on the contract rules. ｜ 根据具体合约规则，执行日、到期日和最后交易日可能相同，也可能不同。

------

###### SSE 50 ETF Option Arrangements ｜ 上证50ETF期权的相关安排

According to the source, the expiry months of SSE 50 ETF options include: ｜ 根据原材料，上证50ETF期权的到期月份包括：

- The current month ｜ 当月
- The next month ｜ 下月
- The following two quarterly months ｜ 随后的两个季月

The last trading day and expiry date are the same day—the fourth Wednesday of the contract month. ｜ 最后交易日和到期日为同一天，均为合约月份的第四个星期三。

The exercise settlement date is the next trading day. ｜ 行权交收日为下一交易日。

------

##### (IV) Delivery and Settlement Rules ｜ （四）交割规定

###### Spot Options ｜ 现货期权

When a spot option is exercised, the parties directly deliver the underlying asset at the exercise price. ｜ 现货期权行权时，交易双方按照执行价格对标的资产进行实际交收。

For a call option, the buyer purchases the underlying asset from the seller at the exercise price. ｜ 对于看涨期权，买方按照执行价格从卖方处买入标的资产。

For a put option, the buyer sells the underlying asset to the seller at the exercise price. ｜ 对于看跌期权，买方按照执行价格向卖方出售标的资产。

------

###### Index Options ｜ 指数期权

Because an index cannot be physically delivered, index options generally use cash settlement. ｜ 由于股票指数本身无法进行实物交付，指数期权通常采用现金结算。

The cash settlement amount is determined according to the difference between the exercise price and the market or settlement value of the index on the exercise date. ｜ 结算金额根据执行价格与执行日指数市场价格或者结算价格之间的差额计算。

For a call option: ｜ 对于看涨期权：
$$
\text{Cash settlement payoff}
=
\max(S_T-K,0)\times\text{Contract multiplier}
$$
For a put option: ｜ 对于看跌期权：
$$
\text{Cash settlement payoff}
=
\max(K-S_T,0)\times\text{Contract multiplier}
$$

------

###### Futures Options ｜ 期货期权

When the buyer exercises a futures option, the buyer receives a corresponding position in the underlying futures contract from the option seller. ｜ 期货期权买方行权时，将从期权卖方处获得标的期货合约的相应头寸。

Depending on the option type: ｜ 根据期权类型：

- Exercising a call option generally creates a long futures position for the buyer. ｜ 看涨期权买方行权后，通常取得期货多头头寸。
- Exercising a put option generally creates a short futures position for the buyer. ｜ 看跌期权买方行权后，通常取得期货空头头寸。

The source also states that the settlement includes the difference between the exercise price and the relevant futures price. ｜ 原材料同时指出，行权结算还涉及执行价格与相关期货价格之间的差额。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🇺🇸 **American options may be exercised at any time before or on expiry.** ｜ 🇺🇸 **美式期权可以在到期日前的任何时间行权。**

🇪🇺 **European options may be exercised only on the expiry date.** ｜ 🇪🇺 **欧式期权只能在到期日行权。**

📦 **Options may be classified as commodity options or financial options according to their underlying assets.** ｜ 📦 **按照标的资产不同，期权可以分为商品期权和金融期权。**

🏛️ **Exchange-traded options are standardised, while OTC options are flexible but carry greater liquidity and credit risks.** ｜ 🏛️ **场内期权具有标准化特征，场外期权更加灵活，但流动性风险和信用风险较大。**

✨ **Exotic options are non-traditional options created by changing payoff structures, activation conditions or underlying assets.** ｜ ✨ **奇异期权通过改变收益结构、激活条件或者标的资产等方式形成。**

📏 **The trading unit is the quantity of the underlying asset represented by one option contract.** ｜ 📏 **交易单位是一张期权合约所代表的标的资产数量。**

🎯 **Exchange-traded option exercise prices are set at predetermined intervals around a central exercise price.** ｜ 🎯 **场内期权的执行价格通常围绕中心执行价格按照规定级距设置。**

📅 **Expiry month, expiry date, last trading day and exercise date are different contract concepts.** ｜ 📅 **到期月、到期日、最后交易日和执行日属于不同的合约概念。**

💵 **Spot options may involve delivery of the underlying asset, while index options generally use cash settlement.** ｜ 💵 **现货期权可能进行标的资产交收，指数期权通常采用现金结算。**

📈 **Exercising a futures call generally creates a long futures position, while exercising a futures put generally creates a short futures position.** ｜ 📈 **行使期货看涨期权通常获得期货多头，行使期货看跌期权通常获得期货空头。**

#### II. Trading Instructions for Exchange-Traded Options ｜ 二、场内期权的交易指令

The source lists four basic option trading instructions: ｜ 原材料列出了四种基本的期权交易指令：

##### (I) Buy to Open ｜ （一）买入开仓

Buy to open means buying an option contract to establish a new long option position. ｜ 买入开仓是指买入期权合约，建立新的期权多头头寸。

The buyer pays the option premium and obtains the corresponding option right. ｜ 买方支付权利金，并取得相应的期权权利。

- Buying a call to open gives the right to buy the underlying asset. ｜ 买入看涨期权开仓，获得买入标的资产的权利。
- Buying a put to open gives the right to sell the underlying asset. ｜ 买入看跌期权开仓，获得卖出标的资产的权利。

> 原文第一项受到OCR损坏。根据其与“卖出开仓、买入平仓、卖出平仓”的对应关系，整理为“买入开仓”。

------

##### (II) Sell to Open ｜ （二）卖出开仓

Sell to open means selling an option contract to establish a new short option position. ｜ 卖出开仓是指卖出期权合约，建立新的期权空头头寸。

The seller receives the option premium but assumes the obligation to perform if the buyer exercises the option. ｜ 卖方收取权利金，但如果期权买方行权，卖方必须履行相应义务。

------

##### (III) Buy to Close ｜ （三）买入平仓

Buy to close means that an investor who previously sold an option buys an identical option contract to close the existing short position. ｜ 买入平仓是指原来持有期权空头头寸的交易者，买入相同的期权合约以结束原有空头头寸。

The closing contract normally needs to have the same underlying asset, option type, exercise price and expiry month. ｜ 用于平仓的期权通常应当具有相同的标的资产、期权类型、执行价格和到期月份。

------

##### (IV) Sell to Close ｜ （四）卖出平仓

Sell to close means that an investor who previously bought an option sells an identical option contract to close the existing long position. ｜ 卖出平仓是指原来持有期权多头头寸的交易者，卖出相同的期权合约以结束原有多头头寸。

| Instruction   | Position before transaction | Result                     |
| ------------- | --------------------------- | -------------------------- |
| Buy to open   | No existing position        | Establish a long position  |
| Sell to open  | No existing position        | Establish a short position |
| Buy to close  | Existing short position     | Close the short position   |
| Sell to close | Existing long position      | Close the long position    |

| 交易指令 | 交易前头寸   | 交易结果     |
| -------- | ------------ | ------------ |
| 买入开仓 | 原来没有头寸 | 建立多头头寸 |
| 卖出开仓 | 原来没有头寸 | 建立空头头寸 |
| 买入平仓 | 原来持有空头 | 结束空头头寸 |
| 卖出平仓 | 原来持有多头 | 结束多头头寸 |

------

#### III. Margin System ｜ 三、保证金制度

##### (I) Requirements for Option Buyers ｜ （一）期权买方的缴款要求

The option buyer must pay the full option premium within the prescribed period after the transaction. ｜ 期权买方必须在成交后的规定时间内支付全部权利金。

After paying the premium, the buyer generally does not need to deposit additional margin. ｜ 支付权利金后，期权买方通常不需要再缴纳保证金。

The reason is that the buyer has a right rather than an obligation, and the buyer’s maximum loss is generally limited to the premium already paid. ｜ 这是因为期权买方拥有的是权利而不是义务，其最大损失通常限于已经支付的权利金。

##### (II) Requirements for Option Sellers ｜ （二）期权卖方的保证金要求

The option seller must deposit a specified amount of margin. ｜ 期权卖方必须按照规定缴纳一定数量的保证金。

The seller may be required to perform the contract when the buyer exercises the option, and the seller’s potential loss may substantially exceed the option premium received. ｜ 如果期权买方行权，卖方必须履约，而且卖方的潜在损失可能远远超过已经收取的权利金。

| Party         | Premium              | Margin                         |
| ------------- | -------------------- | ------------------------------ |
| Option buyer  | Pays the premium     | Generally no additional margin |
| Option seller | Receives the premium | Must deposit margin            |

| 交易方   | 权利金     | 保证金             |
| -------- | ---------- | ------------------ |
| 期权买方 | 支付权利金 | 通常不再缴纳保证金 |
| 期权卖方 | 收取权利金 | 必须缴纳保证金     |

------

### Examination Topic: Characteristics of Options ｜ 考点：期权的特点

#### I. Different Rights and Obligations of Buyers and Sellers ｜ 一、买卖双方的权利和义务不同

The option buyer obtains a right after paying the premium. ｜ 期权买方支付权利金后获得相应权利。

The buyer may exercise the option when exercise is favourable and may abandon the option when exercise is unfavourable. ｜ 行权有利时，买方可以选择行权；行权不利时，买方可以放弃行权。

The option seller receives the premium but assumes an obligation. ｜ 期权卖方收取权利金，同时承担履约义务。

If the buyer exercises the option, the seller generally cannot refuse to perform. ｜ 如果买方决定行权，卖方通常不能拒绝履约。

------

#### II. Different Return and Risk Characteristics ｜ 二、买卖双方的收益和风险特征不同

The option buyer’s maximum loss is generally limited to the premium paid, while the potential profit may be relatively large. ｜ 期权买方的最大损失通常仅限于已经支付的权利金，而潜在盈利可能较大。

The option seller’s maximum income is generally limited to the premium received, while the potential loss may be much larger. ｜ 期权卖方的最大收入通常仅限于收取的权利金，而潜在亏损可能很大。

| Party         | Maximum income         | Maximum loss           |
| ------------- | ---------------------- | ---------------------- |
| Option buyer  | May be large           | Limited to the premium |
| Option seller | Limited to the premium | May be large           |

| 交易方   | 最大收益   | 最大损失   |
| -------- | ---------- | ---------- |
| 期权买方 | 可能较大   | 限于权利金 |
| 期权卖方 | 限于权利金 | 可能较大   |

------

##### Case: Long and Short Call Option ｜ 案例：看涨期权多头与空头

On 2 January 2015, a trader bought 100 March 2015 call-option contracts on China Merchants Bank H shares traded on the Hong Kong Exchanges and Clearing. ｜ 2015年1月2日，某交易者买入100张香港交易所交易的招商银行H股看涨期权，该期权于2015年3月到期。

The main contract information was: ｜ 主要合约信息如下：

| Item                | Value               |
| ------------------- | ------------------- |
| Option type         | Call option         |
| Premium             | HKD 1.60 per share  |
| Exercise price      | HKD 19.50 per share |
| Current stock price | HKD 19.90 per share |
| Number of contracts | 100                 |
| Contract size       | 500 shares          |

| 项目         | 数值         |
| ------------ | ------------ |
| 期权类型     | 看涨期权     |
| 权利金       | 1.60港元/股  |
| 执行价格     | 19.50港元/股 |
| 当时股票价格 | 19.90港元/股 |
| 期权数量     | 100张        |
| 每张合约规模 | 500股        |

The total number of underlying shares was: ｜ 对应的标的股票总数量为：
$$
100\times500=50{,}000\text{股}
$$
The total premium paid by the buyer was: ｜ 期权买方支付的权利金总额为：
$$
1.60\times100\times500
=
80{,}000\text{港元}
$$
Assume that the stock price subsequently increased to HKD 50 per share. ｜ 假设招商银行H股价格随后上涨至每股50港元。

The option buyer could exercise the option by buying the shares at HKD 19.50 and then selling them in the market at HKD 50. ｜ 期权多头可以按照每股19.50港元行权买入股票，再按照每股50港元在市场上卖出。

The buyer’s net profit per share was: ｜ 买方每股净盈利为：
$$
50-19.50-1.60
=
28.90\text{港元}
$$
The total profit was: ｜ 总盈利为：
$$
28.90\times100\times500
=
1{,}445{,}000\text{港元}
$$
The return relative to the premium paid was: ｜ 相对于权利金支出的收益率为：
$$
\frac{28.90}{1.60}\times100\%
=
1806.25\%
$$
The option seller had the opposite result. ｜ 期权空头的结果与多头相反。

The seller had to purchase the stock in the market at HKD 50 and deliver it to the buyer at HKD 19.50. ｜ 卖方必须以每股50港元从市场买入股票，再按照每股19.50港元履约卖给期权买方。

The seller’s net result per share, including the premium received, was: ｜ 计入已收取权利金后，卖方每股净损益为：
$$
19.50+1.60-50
=
-28.90\text{港元}
$$
The total loss was: ｜ 总亏损为：
$$
-28.90\times100\times500
=
-1{,}445{,}000\text{港元}
$$
This case demonstrates the asymmetric risk-and-return structure of options. ｜ 该案例体现了期权买卖双方不对称的收益与风险结构。

------

#### III. Different Margin Requirements ｜ 三、买卖双方的保证金缴纳要求不同

The option buyer generally pays the full premium but does not deposit additional margin. ｜ 期权买方通常支付全部权利金，但不需要额外缴纳保证金。

The option seller receives the premium but must deposit margin because of the obligation to perform. ｜ 期权卖方收取权利金，但由于承担履约义务，需要按照规定缴纳保证金。

------

#### IV. Different Hedging Functions of Buying and Selling Options ｜ 四、买入期权与卖出期权的避险功能不同

Buying an option can limit adverse losses while preserving the possibility of benefiting from favourable price movements. ｜ 买入期权可以限制价格向不利方向变化时的损失，同时保留价格向有利方向变化时获得收益的可能性。

Selling an option mainly earns premium income but creates an obligation and may expose the seller to substantial price risk. ｜ 卖出期权主要用于获取权利金收入，但卖方需要承担履约义务和较大的价格风险。

------

#### V. Different Hedging Results Between Options and Futures ｜ 五、利用期权和期货避险的结果不同

Futures hedging generally locks in a price or substantially fixes the result of a future transaction. ｜ 利用期货套期保值通常会锁定价格，使未来交易结果基本固定。

Both adverse losses and favourable gains in the spot market are generally offset by the futures position. ｜ 现货市场的不利损失和有利收益，通常都会被期货头寸的反向盈亏抵销。

Option hedging is different. The buyer may exercise the option when prices move adversely and abandon the option when prices move favourably. ｜ 期权避险则不同。当价格向不利方向变化时，期权买方可以行权；当价格向有利方向变化时，可以放弃行权。

Therefore, an option can provide downside protection while retaining part or all of the favourable price movement, although the buyer must pay a premium. ｜ 因此，期权能够在提供风险保护的同时，保留部分或者全部有利价格变动带来的收益，但买方需要支付权利金。

------

#### VI. Nonlinear Profit-and-Loss Structure ｜ 六、独特的非线性损益结构

The profit or loss of a futures position changes approximately linearly with the price of the underlying asset. ｜ 期货头寸的盈亏通常随着标的资产价格变化呈近似线性关系。

The profit or loss of an option position is nonlinear because the buyer may choose whether to exercise. ｜ 由于期权买方可以选择是否行权，期权头寸的损益呈现非线性特征。

For a long call: ｜ 对于看涨期权多头：
$$
\text{Profit}
=
\max(S_T-K,0)-C
$$
For a long put: ｜ 对于看跌期权多头：
$$
\text{Profit}
=
\max(K-S_T,0)-P
$$
The use of the maximum function creates the option’s characteristic kinked payoff structure. ｜ 公式中的最大值函数形成了期权特有的折线形、非线性损益结构。

------

## Section 2: Option Prices and Influencing Factors ｜ 第二节 期权价格及影响因素

### Examination Topic: Intrinsic Value and Time Value of Options ｜ 考点：期权的内在价值和时间价值

#### I. Intrinsic Value of an Option ｜ 一、期权的内在价值

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

The intrinsic value of an option, also called its inherent value, is the benefit that the option buyer could obtain by exercising the option immediately, without considering transaction costs or the premium paid. ｜ 期权的内在价值也称内涵价值，是指在不考虑交易费用和期权费的情况下，期权买方立即执行期权合约可以获得的收益。

Intrinsic value cannot be negative. Its minimum value is zero. ｜ 期权内在价值不能为负数，其最小值为零。

------

##### (II) Calculation ｜ （二）计算

For a call option: ｜ 看涨期权的内在价值为：
$$
\text{Call intrinsic value}
=
\max(S-K,0)
$$
For a put option: ｜ 看跌期权的内在价值为：
$$
\text{Put intrinsic value}
=
\max(K-S,0)
$$
where: ｜ 其中：

- $S$: current price of the underlying asset ｜ $S$：标的资产即期价格
- $K$: exercise price ｜ $K$：期权执行价格

The basic idea is to compare the favourable exercise-price difference with zero and select the larger value. ｜ 其基本思路是：将对期权买方有利的行权价差与零进行比较，取其中较大者。

------

##### (III) In-the-Money, Out-of-the-Money and At-the-Money Options ｜ （三）实值期权、虚值期权和平值期权

###### In-the-Money Option ｜ 实值期权

An in-the-money option has intrinsic value greater than zero. ｜ 实值期权是指内在价值大于零的期权。

###### Out-of-the-Money Option ｜ 虚值期权

An out-of-the-money option has intrinsic value equal to zero, while the underlying asset price is not equal to the exercise price. ｜ 虚值期权是指内在价值等于零，而且标的资产价格不等于执行价格的期权。

###### At-the-Money Option ｜ 平值期权

An at-the-money option has intrinsic value equal to zero, and the underlying asset price equals the exercise price. ｜ 平值期权是指标的资产价格等于执行价格、内在价值为零的期权。

| Option status    | Call option | Put option |
| ---------------- | ----------- | ---------- |
| In the money     | $S>K$       | $S         |
| Out of the money | $S          | $S>K$      |
| At the money     | $S=K$       | $S=K$      |

| 期权状态 | 看涨期权           | 看跌期权           |
| -------- | ------------------ | ------------------ |
| 实值期权 | 标的价格＞执行价格 | 标的价格＜执行价格 |
| 虚值期权 | 标的价格＜执行价格 | 标的价格＞执行价格 |
| 平值期权 | 标的价格＝执行价格 | 标的价格＝执行价格 |

📌 **Call option: higher underlying price means greater intrinsic value.** ｜ 📌 **看涨期权：标的资产价格越高，内在价值越大。**

📌 **Put option: lower underlying price means greater intrinsic value.** ｜ 📌 **看跌期权：标的资产价格越低，内在价值越大。**

------

#### II. Time Value of an Option ｜ 二、期权的时间价值

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

The time value of an option, also called its extrinsic value, is the portion of the option premium that exceeds its intrinsic value. ｜ 期权的时间价值又称外涵价值，是指期权权利金超过内在价值的部分。

It reflects the possibility that fluctuations in the underlying asset price during the option’s remaining life may create additional benefits for the option holder. ｜ 时间价值体现了在期权剩余有效期内，标的资产价格波动可能为期权持有者带来额外收益的可能性。

Generally, the greater the volatility of the underlying asset, the greater the option’s time value. ｜ 通常，标的资产价格波动率越高，期权的时间价值越大。

------

##### (II) Calculation ｜ （二）计算

$$
\text{Time value}
=
\text{Option premium}
-
\text{Intrinsic value}
$$

If the intrinsic value is zero, the entire option premium consists of time value. ｜ 如果期权的内在价值等于零，则期权权利金全部属于时间价值。
$$
\text{Intrinsic value}=0
\Rightarrow
\text{Option premium}=\text{Time value}
$$

------

##### (III) Case: S&P 500 Call Options ｜ （三）案例：标准普尔500指数看涨期权

The source provides quotations for S&P 500 Index call options traded on 11 August 2017. ｜ 原材料提供了2017年8月11日交易的标准普尔500指数看涨期权报价。

At that time, the S&P 500 Index was: ｜ 当时标准普尔500指数为：
$$
S=2441.32\text{ points}
$$
For a call option: ｜ 对于看涨期权：
$$
\text{Intrinsic value}
=
\max(2441.32-K,0)
$$

| Exercise price | Option price | Intrinsic value | Time value |
| -------------- | ------------ | --------------- | ---------- |
| 2435           | 12.94        | 6.32            | 6.62       |
| 2440           | 9.36         | 1.32            | 8.04       |
| 2445           | 5.90         | 0               | 5.90       |
| 2450           | 3.20         | 0               | 3.20       |

| 执行价格 | 期权成交价 | 内在价值 | 时间价值 |
| -------- | ---------- | -------- | -------- |
| 2435     | 12.94      | 6.32     | 6.62     |
| 2440     | 9.36       | 1.32     | 8.04     |
| 2445     | 5.90       | 0        | 5.90     |
| 2450     | 3.20       | 0        | 3.20     |

For example, when the exercise price is 2,435 points: ｜ 例如，当执行价格为2435点时：
$$
\text{Intrinsic value}
=
2441.32-2435
=
6.32
$$
When the exercise price is 2,440 points: ｜ 当执行价格为2440点时：
$$
\text{Intrinsic value}
=
2441.32-2440
=
1.32
$$

> 原表将第二个期权的时间价值列为8.08点，但根据成交价9.36点和内在价值1.32点计算，结果应为8.04点。

The options with exercise prices of 2,445 and 2,450 points were out of the money. Their intrinsic values were zero, so their option prices consisted entirely of time value. ｜ 执行价格为2445点和2450点的看涨期权属于虚值期权，内在价值为零，因此其成交价格全部属于时间价值。

------

##### (IV) Time Value of Different Options ｜ （四）不同期权的时间价值

The time value of at-the-money and out-of-the-money options is always greater than or equal to zero. ｜ 平值期权和虚值期权的时间价值总是大于或者等于零。

The source states that the time value of an American option is always greater than or equal to zero. ｜ 原材料指出，美式期权的时间价值总是大于或者等于零。
$$
\text{American option time value}\geq0
$$
The source also states that the time value of certain in-the-money European options may be negative. ｜ 原材料还指出，部分实值欧式期权的时间价值可能小于零。

However, the supplied material is not fully consistent at this point: ｜ 但是，原材料在此处的表述并不完全一致：

- The general statement refers to an in-the-money European put option. ｜ 总述中写的是实值欧式看跌期权。
- The following explanation discusses in-the-money European call options on income-paying underlying assets. ｜ 后续解释讨论的则是支付收益标的资产的实值欧式看涨期权。

The source further states: ｜ 原材料进一步指出：

- An in-the-money European call option on an underlying asset paying relatively high income may have negative time value. ｜ 当标的资产支付较高收益时，实值欧式看涨期权的时间价值可能小于零。
- An in-the-money European call option on a non-income-paying or low-income-paying underlying asset does not have negative time value. ｜ 当标的资产不支付收益或者支付较低收益时，实值欧式看涨期权的时间价值不会小于零。

> 由于原文在“看涨期权”和“看跌期权”之间存在前后不一致，这里不自行统一，只保留原材料能够确认的表述。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🔄 **The four basic trading instructions are buy to open, sell to open, buy to close and sell to close.** ｜ 🔄 **期权的四种基本交易指令是买入开仓、卖出开仓、买入平仓和卖出平仓。**

💳 **The buyer pays the full premium and generally deposits no additional margin; the seller receives the premium but must provide margin.** ｜ 💳 **期权买方支付全部权利金后通常不再缴纳保证金，卖方收取权利金但需要缴纳保证金。**

⚖️ **The buyer has a right, while the seller assumes an obligation.** ｜ ⚖️ **期权买方拥有权利，期权卖方承担义务。**

📈 **The buyer’s maximum loss is generally limited to the premium, while the seller’s maximum income is generally limited to the premium.** ｜ 📈 **期权买方最大损失通常限于权利金，卖方最大收益通常也限于权利金。**

🛡️ **Option hedging limits adverse losses while retaining favourable price opportunities; futures hedging generally locks in both gains and losses.** ｜ 🛡️ **期权避险可以限制不利损失并保留有利收益机会，期货套保通常同时锁定收益和损失。**

📐 **Option payoff is nonlinear because the buyer may choose whether to exercise.** ｜ 📐 **由于买方可以选择是否行权，期权具有非线性损益结构。**

💎 **Intrinsic value is the immediate exercise benefit and cannot be negative.** ｜ 💎 **内在价值是立即行权能够获得的收益，最小为零。**

📊 **Call intrinsic value is $\max(S-K,0)$; put intrinsic value is $\max(K-S,0)$.** ｜ 📊 **看涨期权内在价值为$\max(S-K,0)$，看跌期权内在价值为$\max(K-S,0)$。**

⏳ **Time value equals the option premium minus intrinsic value.** ｜ ⏳ **时间价值等于权利金减去内在价值。**

🌊 **Greater underlying-asset volatility generally produces greater option time value.** ｜ 🌊 **标的资产价格波动率越高，期权时间价值通常越大。**

### Examination Topic: Basic Factors Affecting Option Prices ｜ 考点：影响期权价格的基本因素

#### I. Underlying Asset Price and Exercise Price ｜ 一、标的资产价格与期权执行价格

##### (I) Effect on Intrinsic Value ｜ （一）对内在价值的影响

The relationship between the underlying asset price $S$ and the exercise price $K$ directly determines the intrinsic value of an option. ｜ 标的资产价格 $S$ 与执行价格 $K$ 的相对关系，直接决定期权的内在价值。

For a call option: ｜ 对于看涨期权：
$$
\text{Intrinsic value}
=
\max(S-K,0)
$$
As the underlying asset price rises relative to the exercise price, the intrinsic value of the call option increases. ｜ 标的资产价格相对于执行价格越高，看涨期权的内在价值越大。

For a put option: ｜ 对于看跌期权：
$$
\text{Intrinsic value}
=
\max(K-S,0)
$$
As the underlying asset price falls relative to the exercise price, the intrinsic value of the put option increases. ｜ 标的资产价格相对于执行价格越低，看跌期权的内在价值越大。

------

##### (II) Effect on Time Value ｜ （二）对时间价值的影响

Generally, the greater the difference between the exercise price and the underlying asset price, the lower the option’s time value. ｜ 一般来说，执行价格与标的资产价格之间的相对差额越大，期权时间价值越小。

In other words, the relative price difference and time value generally move in opposite directions. ｜ 换言之，执行价格与标的资产价格的偏离程度，通常与时间价值呈反向关系。

When an option is deeply in the money or deeply out of the money, its time value tends toward zero. ｜ 当期权处于深度实值或者深度虚值状态时，其时间价值通常趋近于零。

Whether the option is American or European, its time value is generally greatest when it is at the money or near the money. ｜ 无论是美式期权还是欧式期权，当期权处于平值或者接近平值状态时，时间价值通常最大。
$$
\text{Deep ITM or deep OTM}
\Rightarrow
\text{Time value tends to }0
$$

------

#### II. Volatility of the Underlying Asset ｜ 二、标的资产价格波动率

The volatility of the underlying asset price is positively related to the option price. ｜ 标的资产价格波动率与期权价格呈正向关系。
$$
\text{Volatility}\uparrow
\Rightarrow
\text{Option price}\uparrow
$$
Greater volatility increases the possibility that the option will move into a favourable exercise position before expiry. ｜ 标的资产价格波动越大，期权在到期前进入有利行权状态的可能性越高。

Because the buyer’s downside is generally limited to the premium while favourable price movements may produce additional gains, greater volatility increases the value of both call and put options. ｜ 由于期权买方的最大损失通常限于权利金，而有利方向的价格变化可能带来额外收益，因此波动率上升通常会提高看涨期权和看跌期权的价值。

------

#### III. Remaining Life of the Option Contract ｜ 三、期权合约的有效期限

##### (I) American Options ｜ （一）美式期权

The longer the remaining life of an American option, the greater or at least no lower its value generally becomes. ｜ 美式期权的有效期限越长，其价值通常越高，至少不会因为期限延长而降低。

This applies to both American call options and American put options. ｜ 这一关系同时适用于美式看涨期权和美式看跌期权。

A longer validity period gives the buyer more time and more opportunities to exercise the option under favourable conditions. ｜ 有效期越长，买方拥有的行权时间和有利行权机会越多。

##### (II) European Options ｜ （二）欧式期权

An increase in the remaining life of a European option does not necessarily increase its value. ｜ 欧式期权的有效期限增加，并不必然导致期权价值上升。

This is because European options can only be exercised on the expiry date, and other factors such as dividends and interest rates may affect the value differently across maturities. ｜ 这是因为欧式期权只能在到期日行权，而且股息、利率等因素可能使不同期限期权的价值受到不同影响。

------

#### IV. Risk-Free Interest Rate ｜ 四、无风险利率

The effect of the risk-free interest rate on option prices must be analysed together with the prevailing economic environment. ｜ 无风险利率对期权价格的影响，需要结合当时的经济环境综合判断。

The analysis should consider: ｜ 分析时需要考虑：

- How a change in interest rates affects the underlying asset price ｜ 利率变化对标的资产价格的影响方向
- How the underlying price change affects the option’s intrinsic value ｜ 标的资产价格变化对期权内在价值的影响
- How interest rates affect the option’s time value ｜ 利率变化对期权时间价值的影响

The final effect on the option price is determined by the combined influence of these factors. ｜ 最终对期权价格的影响，是上述因素综合作用的结果。

> 原材料没有给出无风险利率分别对看涨期权和看跌期权价格影响的固定方向，因此这里不额外补充结论。

------

#### V. Income Paid by the Underlying Asset ｜ 五、标的资产收益

##### (I) Main Forms ｜ （一）主要形式

The effect of income paid by the underlying asset mainly refers to the effect of stock dividends on stock options and stock index options. ｜ 标的资产支付收益对期权价格的影响，主要是指股票股息对股票期权和股票价格指数期权价格的影响。

##### (II) Direction of Influence ｜ （二）影响方向

Income paid by the underlying asset generally has a negative effect on call-option prices. ｜ 标的资产支付收益通常会对看涨期权价格产生负向影响。
$$
\text{Underlying income}\uparrow
\Rightarrow
\text{Call price}\downarrow
$$
Income paid by the underlying asset generally has a positive effect on put-option prices. ｜ 标的资产支付收益通常会对看跌期权价格产生正向影响。
$$
\text{Underlying income}\uparrow
\Rightarrow
\text{Put price}\uparrow
$$
The reason is that dividend payments generally reduce the price of the underlying stock after the ex-dividend date. ｜ 这是因为股票支付股息后，除息通常会使股票价格下降。

A lower underlying price is unfavourable to call options but favourable to put options. ｜ 标的资产价格下降对看涨期权不利，而对看跌期权有利。

##### (III) Applicable Condition ｜ （三）适用条件

The above conclusion assumes that the exercise price is not adjusted when the stock pays dividends. ｜ 上述结论建立在股票分红时不调整期权执行价格的条件下。

------

#### Summary of Factors Affecting Option Prices ｜ 期权价格影响因素总结

| Factor increases                | Call-option value                | Put-option value                 |
| ------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | -------------------------------- |
| Underlying asset price          | Generally rises                  | Generally falls                  |
| Exercise price                  | Generally falls                  | Generally rises                  |
| Volatility                      | Rises                            | Rises                            |
| American-option validity period | Generally rises                  | Generally rises                  |
| Underlying income               | Falls                            | Rises                            |
| Risk-free interest rate         | Source gives no fixed conclusion | Source gives no fixed conclusion |

| 因素上升       | 看涨期权价值         | 看跌期权价值         |
| -------------- | -------------------- | -------------------- |
| 标的资产价格   | 通常上升             | 通常下降             |
| 执行价格       | 通常下降             | 通常上升             |
| 波动率         | 上升                 | 上升                 |
| 美式期权有效期 | 通常上升             | 通常上升             |
| 标的资产收益   | 下降                 | 上升                 |
| 无风险利率     | 原材料未给出固定结论 | 原材料未给出固定结论 |

------

## Section 3: Option Trading Strategies ｜ 第三节 期权交易策略

### Examination Topic: Basic Option Trading Strategies ｜ 考点：期权基本交易策略

#### I. Buying a Call Option ｜ 一、买进看涨期权

##### (I) Purpose and Basic Operations ｜ （一）目的和基本操作

###### Purpose ｜ 目的

One purpose of buying a call option is to avoid the potentially large loss that could arise from directly buying the underlying asset and then experiencing a price decline. ｜ 买进看涨期权的一个目的，是避免直接购买标的资产后，因标的资产价格下跌而遭受较大损失。

The option buyer pays a limited premium to obtain the opportunity to benefit from an increase in the underlying asset price. ｜ 期权买方通过支付有限的权利金，获得标的资产价格上涨时获取收益的机会。

###### Basic Operations ｜ 基本操作

If the underlying asset price rises above the exercise price, the buyer may exercise the option and acquire the underlying asset at the lower exercise price. ｜ 如果标的资产价格上涨至执行价格以上，买方可以行权，按照低于市场价格的执行价格获得标的资产。

The buyer may also sell the option before expiry. ｜ 买方也可以在期权到期前卖出期权平仓。

If the option premium has increased, selling the option may generate a premium-price profit. ｜ 如果期权价格上涨，卖出平仓可以获得权利金价差收益。

If the option price falls, selling before expiry may reduce the loss and avoid losing the entire premium. ｜ 如果期权价格下跌，提前卖出平仓可以减少损失，避免损失全部权利金。

------

##### (II) Profit-and-Loss Analysis ｜ （二）损益分析

Let: ｜ 设：

- $S_T$: underlying asset price at expiry ｜ $S_T$：到期时标的资产价格
- $X$: exercise price ｜ $X$：执行价格
- $C$: call-option premium ｜ $C$：看涨期权权利金

The profit of a long call at expiry is: ｜ 买进看涨期权的到期损益为：
$$
\Pi
=
\max(S_T-X,0)-C
$$
The break-even point is: ｜ 损益平衡点为：
$$
S_T=X+C
$$
If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T\leq X
$$
the buyer abandons the option and loses the entire premium: ｜ 买方放弃行权，损失全部权利金：
$$
\Pi=-C
$$
If: ｜ 如果：
$$
X<S_T<X+C
$$
the option is exercised, but the exercise gain is not sufficient to cover the premium, so the buyer still incurs a partial loss. ｜ 期权虽然可以行权，但行权收益不足以弥补权利金，因此买方仍然处于部分亏损状态。

If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T>X+C
$$
the buyer earns a net profit. ｜ 买方获得净盈利。

| Underlying price at expiry | Exercise decision | Net result              |
| -------------------------- | ----------------- | ----------------------- |
| $S_T\leq X$                | Abandon           | Maximum loss equals $C$ |
| $X                         | Exercise          | Partial loss            |
| $S_T=X+C$                  | Exercise          | Break-even              |
| $S_T>X+C$                  | Exercise          | Profit                  |

| 到期标的价格 | 行权决策 | 净损益         |
| ------------ | -------- | -------------- |
| $S_T\leq X$  | 放弃行权 | 最大损失为 $C$ |
| $X           | 行权     | 部分亏损       |
| $S_T=X+C$    | 行权     | 盈亏平衡       |
| $S_T>X+C$    | 行权     | 获得盈利       |

------

##### (III) Suitable Situations ｜ （三）适用情形

Buying a call option is generally suitable when: ｜ 买进看涨期权通常适用于以下情形：

- The market is bullish ｜ 市场处于牛市
- The trader expects prices to rise ｜ 交易者预期后市上涨
- The underlying asset price appears to have reached a bottom ｜ 标的资产价格可能已经见底
- Market volatility is expected to increase ｜ 市场波动率预计扩大
- Implied volatility is relatively low ｜ 隐含波动率较低

According to the source, low implied volatility means that the volatility reflected in the option price is lower than the theoretically estimated volatility. ｜ 根据原材料，隐含波动率低，是指期权价格所反映的波动率低于理论计算出的波动率。

------

##### (IV) Profit-and-Loss Calculations ｜ （四）损益计算

###### Closing Profit or Loss ｜ 平仓损益

$$
\text{Closing P/L}
=
\text{Premium received when selling}
-
\text{Premium paid when buying}
$$

###### Exercise Profit or Loss ｜ 行权损益

$$
\text{Exercise P/L}
=
\text{Selling price of underlying}
-
\text{Exercise price}
-
\text{Premium}
$$

###### Break-Even Point ｜ 损益平衡点

$$
\text{Break-even point}
=
\text{Exercise price}
+
\text{Premium}
$$

###### Position After Exercise ｜ 履约后头寸状态

After a call-option buyer exercises, the buyer obtains a long position in the underlying asset. ｜ 看涨期权买方行权后，获得标的资产多头头寸。

###### Maximum Loss ｜ 最大损失

$$
\text{Maximum loss}
=
\text{Entire premium}
$$

###### Margin Requirement ｜ 保证金要求

The buyer generally does not need to deposit additional margin after paying the premium. ｜ 期权买方支付权利金后，通常无须再缴纳保证金。

------

##### (V) Basic Uses ｜ （五）基本运用

Buying a call option may be used to: ｜ 买进看涨期权可以用于：

- Obtain a premium-price difference profit ｜ 获取权利金价差收益
- Pursue a greater leverage effect ｜ 追求更大的杠杆效应
- Limit the risk associated with selling or not holding the underlying asset ｜ 限制未持有或者未来需要买入标的资产时的价格风险
- Lock in the future purchase cost of the spot asset ｜ 锁定未来现货购买成本
- Hedge against an increase in the underlying asset price ｜ 对冲标的资产价格上涨风险

> 原材料中的“限制卖出标的资产的风险”表述较为简略，这里保留其避险含义，不进一步扩展。

------

#### II. Selling a Call Option ｜ 二、卖出看涨期权

##### (I) Purpose and Basic Operations ｜ （一）目的和基本操作

###### Purpose ｜ 目的

The primary purpose of selling a call option is to earn option-premium income. ｜ 卖出看涨期权的主要目的是获取权利金收入。

###### Basic Operations ｜ 基本操作

If the option buyer exercises, the seller must sell the underlying asset to the buyer at the exercise price. ｜ 如果期权买方行权，卖方必须按照执行价格向买方出售标的资产。

If the buyer abandons the option, the seller retains the entire option premium. ｜ 如果期权买方放弃行权，卖方可以获得全部权利金收入。

Before expiry, the seller may buy an identical call option to close the existing short position. ｜ 期权到期前，卖方还可以买入相同的看涨期权，将原有空头头寸对冲平仓。

If the option is bought back at a price lower than the original selling price, the seller earns a premium-price difference. ｜ 如果买入平仓时的权利金低于原卖出价格，卖方可以获得权利金价差收益。

------

##### (II) Profit-and-Loss Analysis ｜ （二）损益分析

Let: ｜ 设：

- $S_T$: underlying asset price at expiry ｜ $S_T$：到期时标的资产价格
- $X$: exercise price ｜ $X$：执行价格
- $C$: premium received ｜ $C$：卖方收取的权利金

The profit of a short call at expiry is: ｜ 卖出看涨期权的到期损益为：
$$
\Pi
=
C-\max(S_T-X,0)
$$
If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T\leq X
$$
the buyer abandons the option, and the seller earns the full premium: ｜ 买方放弃行权，卖方获得全部权利金：
$$
\Pi=C
$$
If: ｜ 如果：
$$
X<S_T<X+C
$$
the buyer exercises, but the seller still earns a partial net profit. ｜ 买方行权，但卖方的履约损失尚未超过权利金收入，因此仍有部分盈利。

If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T=X+C
$$
the seller breaks even. ｜ 卖方达到盈亏平衡。

If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T>X+C
$$
the seller incurs a net loss, and the loss increases as the underlying asset price rises. ｜ 卖方产生净亏损，而且标的资产价格越高，亏损越大。

------

##### (III) Suitable Situations ｜ （三）适用情形

The heading “suitable situations” appears in the source, but the corresponding content is missing from the supplied material. ｜ 原材料保留了“适用情形”标题，但没有显示其具体内容，因此此处不自行补充。

------

##### (IV) Profit-and-Loss Calculations ｜ （四）损益计算

###### Closing Profit or Loss ｜ 平仓损益

$$
\text{Closing P/L}
=
\text{Premium received when selling}
-
\text{Premium paid when buying back}
$$

###### Exercise Profit or Loss ｜ 履约损益

$$
\text{Exercise P/L}
=
\text{Exercise price}
-
\text{Purchase price of underlying}
+
\text{Premium}
$$

###### Break-Even Point ｜ 损益平衡点

$$
\text{Break-even point}
=
\text{Exercise price}
+
\text{Premium}
$$

###### Position After Exercise ｜ 履约后头寸

After an uncovered call seller performs the obligation, the resulting underlying position is economically a short position or requires delivery of the underlying asset. ｜ 无保护看涨期权卖方履约时，需要交付标的资产，其经济效果相当于承担标的资产空头风险。

###### Maximum Profit ｜ 最大收益

$$
\text{Maximum profit}
=
\text{Premium received}
$$

###### Margin Requirement ｜ 保证金要求

The call-option seller generally needs to deposit margin. ｜ 看涨期权卖方通常需要缴纳保证金。

If the call is covered by an existing holding of the underlying asset, the margin and risk treatment depend on the degree of protection. ｜ 如果卖出的看涨期权具有标的资产保护，即属于备兑看涨期权，则保证金和风险状况取决于具体保护安排。

------

##### (V) Basic Uses ｜ （五）基本运用

Selling a call option may be used to: ｜ 卖出看涨期权可以用于：

- Earn premium income ｜ 获取权利金收入
- Earn a premium-price difference by closing the position at a lower premium ｜ 通过低价买入平仓获取权利金价差收益
- Increase the income earned from an existing long position in the underlying asset ｜ 增加标的资产多头头寸的收益

------

##### (VI) Case: Covered Call Strategy ｜ （六）案例：备兑看涨期权

On 12 December 2014, a trader bought 50,000 China Merchants Bank H shares at HKD 17.12 per share. ｜ 2014年12月12日，某交易者以每股17.12港元的价格买入50000股招商银行H股。

The total market value of the stock position was: ｜ 股票总市值为：
$$
17.12\times50{,}000
=
856{,}000\text{港元}
$$
After holding the shares for 20 days, the stock price rose to HKD 19.90 per share on 2 January 2015. ｜ 持有20天后，2015年1月2日，该股票价格上涨至每股19.90港元。

The trader believed that the stock still had some potential to rise but was also concerned that the stock market might decline after the New Year. ｜ 交易者认为股票价格仍有进一步上涨的潜力，但同时担心新年后股票市场下跌。

The trader therefore sold 100 call-option contracts with: ｜ 因此，交易者卖出100张看涨期权，具体条件为：

- Exercise price: HKD 21 per share ｜ 执行价格：21港元/股
- Premium: HKD 0.52 per share ｜ 权利金：0.52港元/股

The source implies that each option contract represented 500 shares. ｜ 根据原材料，每张期权合约对应500股股票。
$$
100\times500
=
50{,}000\text{股}
$$
Therefore, the option position was fully covered by the 50,000 shares already held. ｜ 因此，交易者持有的50000股股票可以完全覆盖卖出的看涨期权，属于备兑看涨期权策略。

------

###### Result if the Stock Price Rises Above HKD 21 ｜ 股票价格上涨至21港元以上

If the option buyer exercises, the trader sells the shares at HKD 21 per share. ｜ 如果期权买方行权，交易者需要按照每股21港元出售股票。

Including the premium received, the effective selling price is: ｜ 加上权利金收入后，股票的实际有效卖出价格为：
$$
21+0.52
=
21.52\text{港元/股}
$$
The seller gives up any stock-price gain above HKD 21 but retains the premium income. ｜ 卖方放弃股票价格超过21港元部分的上涨收益，但可以保留权利金收入。

------

###### Actual Result: Stock and Option Prices Fall ｜ 实际结果：股票和期权价格下跌

In January, the underlying stock performed poorly. ｜ 1月份，标的股票表现不佳。

On 26 January, the stock price declined to HKD 18.54 per share, while the call-option price declined to HKD 0.01 per share. ｜ 到1月26日，股票价格下跌至每股18.54港元，该看涨期权价格也下跌至每股0.01港元。

The trader decided to close both the stock and option positions. ｜ 交易者决定同时平掉股票和期权头寸。

The short call position was closed by buying the call option back at HKD 0.01 per share. ｜ 交易者以每股0.01港元买入看涨期权，对原空头头寸进行平仓。

The option profit per share was: ｜ 每股期权收益为：
$$
0.52-0.01
=
0.51\text{港元}
$$
The total option profit was: ｜ 期权总收益为：
$$
0.51\times50{,}000
=
25{,}500\text{港元}
$$
The option premium income effectively increased the stock’s selling price by HKD 0.51 per share. ｜ 权利金价差收益相当于使股票的实际卖出价格提高了每股0.51港元。

The effective stock selling price was therefore: ｜ 因此，股票的有效卖出价格为：
$$
18.54+0.51
=
19.05\text{港元/股}
$$
Compared with simply selling the stock at HKD 18.54, the covered call reduced part of the stock-price decline. ｜ 与直接按照18.54港元卖出股票相比，备兑看涨期权获得的权利金收益弥补了部分股票下跌损失。

However, compared with the original stock purchase price of HKD 17.12, the total stock-and-option position still generated a profit: ｜ 不过，与最初17.12港元的股票买入价格相比，股票与期权组合仍然获得盈利：
$$
19.05-17.12
=
1.93\text{港元/股}
$$
Ignoring transaction costs, the total profit was: ｜ 不考虑交易成本，总盈利为：
$$
1.93\times50{,}000
=
96{,}500\text{港元}
$$

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🎯 **Option time value is generally greatest when the option is at or near the money.** ｜ 🎯 **期权处于平值或者接近平值状态时，时间价值通常最大。**

🌊 **Greater underlying-price volatility increases both call- and put-option prices.** ｜ 🌊 **标的资产价格波动率越高，看涨期权和看跌期权价格通常越高。**

⏳ **A longer remaining life generally increases the value of American options, but does not necessarily increase European-option value.** ｜ ⏳ **有效期延长通常会增加美式期权价值，但不必然增加欧式期权价值。**

💵 **Higher underlying income generally lowers call prices and raises put prices when exercise prices are not adjusted.** ｜ 💵 **在执行价格不调整时，标的资产收益增加通常使看涨期权价格下降、看跌期权价格上升。**

📈 **Buying a call has limited maximum loss and potentially large upside profit.** ｜ 📈 **买进看涨期权的最大损失有限，而上涨方向的潜在收益较大。**

⚖️ **The long-call break-even point equals the exercise price plus the premium.** ｜ ⚖️ **买进看涨期权的盈亏平衡点等于执行价格加权利金。**

💰 **Selling a call earns premium income, but the maximum profit is limited to the premium.** ｜ 💰 **卖出看涨期权可以获得权利金，但最大收益仅限于权利金。**

⚠️ **An uncovered short call may face increasingly large losses as the underlying asset price rises.** ｜ ⚠️ **无保护看涨期权空头会随着标的资产价格上涨而面临不断扩大的亏损。**

🛡️ **A covered call combines a long position in the underlying asset with a short call to increase income and partially cushion price declines.** ｜ 🛡️ **备兑看涨期权将标的资产多头与看涨期权空头结合，用于增加收入并部分缓冲价格下跌。**III. Buying a Put Option

#### 三、买进看跌期权

##### (I) Purpose and Basic Operations ｜ （一）目的和基本操作

###### Purpose ｜ 目的

Buying a put option gives the buyer the right to sell the underlying asset at the agreed exercise price. ｜ 买进看跌期权，是为了获得在未来按照约定执行价格卖出相关标的资产的权利。

The buyer generally expects the underlying asset price to fall or wishes to protect an existing long position against a price decline. ｜ 期权买方通常预期标的资产价格下跌，或者希望保护已经持有的标的资产多头头寸。

###### Basic Operations ｜ 基本操作

If the underlying asset price falls below the exercise price, the buyer may exercise the put option and sell the underlying asset at the higher exercise price. ｜ 如果标的资产价格下跌至执行价格以下，买方可以行权，按照较高的执行价格卖出标的资产。

If the underlying asset price rises, the buyer may abandon the option. ｜ 如果标的资产价格上涨，买方可以放弃行权。

The buyer may also sell the put option before expiry to close the position. ｜ 买方也可以在期权到期前卖出看跌期权平仓。

This can obtain a premium-price difference or reduce the loss of the premium. ｜ 这样既可能获得权利金价差收益，也可以减少权利金损失。

Buying a put can also avoid the risk of directly short-selling the underlying asset and then suffering a loss when its price rises. ｜ 买进看跌期权还可以避免直接卖空标的资产后，因价格上涨而遭受较大损失。

------

##### (II) Profit-and-Loss Analysis ｜ （二）损益分析

Let: ｜ 设：

- $S_T$: underlying asset price at expiry ｜ $S_T$：到期时标的资产价格
- $X$: exercise price ｜ $X$：执行价格
- $P$: put-option premium ｜ $P$：看跌期权权利金

The profit of a long put at expiry is: ｜ 买进看跌期权的到期损益为：
$$
\Pi
=
\max(X-S_T,0)-P
$$
The break-even point is: ｜ 损益平衡点为：
$$
S_T=X-P
$$
If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T\geq X
$$
the buyer abandons the option and loses the premium: ｜ 买方放弃行权，损失全部权利金：
$$
\Pi=-P
$$
If: ｜ 如果：
$$
X-P<S_T<X
$$
the buyer exercises the option, but the exercise gain does not fully cover the premium, so the position still incurs a partial loss. ｜ 买方虽然行权，但行权收益不足以弥补权利金，因此仍然处于部分亏损状态。

If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T=X-P
$$
the position breaks even. ｜ 该期权达到盈亏平衡。

If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T<X-P
$$
the buyer earns a net profit. ｜ 买方获得净盈利。

| Underlying price at expiry | Exercise decision | Net result              |
| -------------------------- | ----------------- | ----------------------- |
| $S_T\geq X$                | Abandon           | Maximum loss equals $P$ |
| $X-P                       | Exercise          | Partial loss            |
| $S_T=X-P$                  | Exercise          | Break-even              |
| $S_T                       | Exercise          | Net profit              |

| 到期标的价格 | 行权决策 | 净损益         |
| ------------ | -------- | -------------- |
| $S_T\geq X$  | 放弃行权 | 最大损失为 $P$ |
| $X-P         | 行权     | 部分亏损       |
| $S_T=X-P$    | 行权     | 盈亏平衡       |
| $S_T         | 行权     | 获得盈利       |

For a stock, the lowest possible underlying price is zero. Therefore, the maximum theoretical profit of a long put is: ｜ 对于股票而言，标的价格最低为零，因此买进看跌期权的最大理论收益为：
$$
X-P
$$

------

##### (III) Suitable Situations ｜ （三）适用情形

Buying a put option is generally suitable when: ｜ 买进看跌期权通常适用于以下情形：

- The market is bearish ｜ 市场处于熊市
- The trader expects the market price to decline ｜ 交易者预测后市下跌
- The underlying asset price appears to have reached a market top ｜ 标的资产价格可能已经见顶
- Market volatility is expected to increase ｜ 市场波动率预计扩大
- Implied volatility is relatively low ｜ 隐含波动率相对较低

> 原材料第一项受到OCR缺失。根据后续“预测后市下跌”等内容，其含义应为熊市或者看跌市场环境。

------

##### (IV) Profit-and-Loss Calculations ｜ （四）损益计算

###### Closing Profit or Loss ｜ 平仓损益

$$
\text{Closing P/L}
=
\text{Premium received when closing}
-
\text{Premium paid when opening}
$$

###### Exercise Profit or Loss ｜ 行权损益

$$
\text{Exercise P/L}
=
\text{Exercise price}
-
\text{Underlying asset price}
-
\text{Premium}
$$

###### Break-Even Point ｜ 损益平衡点

$$
\text{Break-even point}
=
\text{Exercise price}
-
\text{Premium}
$$

###### Maximum Loss ｜ 最大损失

$$
\text{Maximum loss}
=
\text{Entire premium}
$$

###### Position After Exercise ｜ 履约后头寸

After exercising a put option, the buyer sells the underlying asset and therefore obtains an economically short position. ｜ 看跌期权买方行权后卖出标的资产，履约后的经济头寸表现为空头。

###### Margin Requirement ｜ 保证金要求

The option buyer generally does not need to deposit margin after paying the premium. ｜ 期权买方支付权利金后，通常无须再缴纳保证金。

------

##### (V) Basic Uses ｜ （五）基本运用

Buying a put option may be used to: ｜ 买进看跌期权可以用于：

- Obtain a premium-price difference profit ｜ 获取权利金价差收益
- Pursue greater leverage ｜ 博取更大的杠杆效应
- Protect an existing long position in the underlying asset ｜ 保护标的资产多头头寸
- Lock in gains already obtained in the spot market ｜ 锁定现货市场已经取得的收益
- Hedge against a decline in the underlying asset price ｜ 对冲标的资产价格下跌风险

------

#### IV. Selling a Put Option ｜ 四、卖出看跌期权

##### (I) Purpose and Basic Operations ｜ （一）目的和基本操作

###### Purpose ｜ 目的

The primary purpose of selling a put option is to earn option-premium income. ｜ 卖出看跌期权的主要目的是赚取权利金收入。

###### Basic Operations ｜ 基本操作

If the underlying asset price remains above the exercise price, the buyer will generally abandon the option. ｜ 如果标的资产价格高于执行价格，期权买方通常会放弃行权。

The put seller then retains the entire premium. ｜ 此时期权卖方可以获得全部权利金收入。

If the underlying asset price falls below the exercise price, the buyer may exercise the option. ｜ 如果标的资产价格下跌至执行价格以下，买方可能选择行权。

The seller must then purchase the underlying asset from the buyer at the exercise price. ｜ 此时，卖方必须按照执行价格从买方处买入标的资产。

------

##### (II) Profit-and-Loss Analysis ｜ （二）损益分析

Let: ｜ 设：

- $S_T$: underlying asset price at expiry ｜ $S_T$：到期时标的资产价格
- $X$: exercise price ｜ $X$：执行价格
- $P$: premium received ｜ $P$：卖方收取的权利金

The profit of a short put at expiry is: ｜ 卖出看跌期权的到期损益为：
$$
\Pi
=
P-\max(X-S_T,0)
$$
If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T\geq X
$$
the buyer abandons the option, and the seller earns the entire premium: ｜ 买方放弃行权，卖方获得全部权利金：
$$
\Pi=P
$$
If: ｜ 如果：
$$
X-P<S_T<X
$$
the buyer exercises, but the seller’s exercise loss is smaller than the premium received, so the seller still earns a partial profit. ｜ 买方行权，但卖方履约损失小于权利金收入，因此卖方仍然有部分盈利。

If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T=X-P
$$
the seller breaks even. ｜ 卖方达到盈亏平衡。

If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T<X-P
$$
the seller incurs a net loss. ｜ 卖方产生净亏损。

| Underlying price at expiry | Buyer’s decision | Seller’s result           |
| -------------------------- | ---------------- | ------------------------- |
| $S_T\geq X$                | Abandon          | Maximum profit equals $P$ |
| $X-P                       | Exercise         | Partial profit            |
| $S_T=X-P$                  | Exercise         | Break-even                |
| $S_T                       | Exercise         | Net loss                  |

| 到期标的价格 | 买方决策 | 卖方结果       |
| ------------ | -------- | -------------- |
| $S_T\geq X$  | 放弃行权 | 最大收益为 $P$ |
| $X-P         | 行权     | 部分盈利       |
| $S_T=X-P$    | 行权     | 盈亏平衡       |
| $S_T         | 行权     | 产生亏损       |

For a stock, if the underlying price falls to zero, the maximum loss of an uncovered short put is: ｜ 对于股票而言，如果标的资产价格下跌至零，无保护看跌期权空头的最大亏损为：
$$
X-P
$$

------

##### (III) Suitable Situations ｜ （三）适用情形

The detailed content under “suitable situations” is illegible in the supplied material. ｜ 原材料中“适用情形”的具体内容无法辨认，因此不依据常识自行补写。

------

##### (IV) Profit-and-Loss Calculations ｜ （四）损益计算

###### Closing Profit or Loss ｜ 平仓损益

$$
\text{Closing P/L}
=
\text{Premium received when selling}
-
\text{Premium paid when buying back}
$$

###### Exercise Profit or Loss ｜ 履约损益

$$
\text{Exercise P/L}
=
\text{Selling price of underlying asset}
-
\text{Exercise price}
+
\text{Premium}
$$

This formula assumes that the seller later sells the underlying asset received through exercise. ｜ 该公式假设卖方履约买入标的资产后，再按照市场价格将其卖出。

###### Break-Even Point ｜ 损益平衡点

$$
\text{Break-even point}
=
\text{Exercise price}
-
\text{Premium}
$$

###### Maximum Profit ｜ 最大收益

$$
\text{Maximum profit}
=
\text{Premium received}
$$

###### Margin Requirement ｜ 保证金要求

The put-option seller generally needs to deposit margin. ｜ 看跌期权卖方通常需要缴纳保证金。

If the short put has corresponding protection, the margin requirement depends on the specific protective arrangement. ｜ 如果卖出的看跌期权具有相应保护，则保证金要求取决于具体保护方式。

------

##### (V) Basic Uses ｜ （五）基本运用

Selling a put option may be used to: ｜ 卖出看跌期权可以用于：

- Earn premium income ｜ 获取权利金收益
- Earn a premium-price difference ｜ 获取权利金价差收益
- Hedge a short position in the underlying asset ｜ 对冲标的资产空头头寸

------

### Comparison of the Four Basic Option Positions ｜ 四种基本期权头寸比较

| Position   | Market expectation             | Maximum profit          | Maximum loss            | Break-even point |
| ---------- | ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | ----------------------- | ---------------- |
| Long call  | Bullish                        | Potentially unlimited   | Premium $C$             | $X+C$            |
| Short call | Stable or not strongly bullish | Premium $C$             | Potentially unlimited   | $X+C$            |
| Long put   | Bearish                        | Up to $X-P$ for a stock | Premium $P$             | $X-P$            |
| Short put  | Stable or not strongly bearish | Premium $P$             | Up to $X-P$ for a stock | $X-P$            |

| 头寸         | 市场预期         | 最大收益               | 最大损失               | 盈亏平衡点 |
| ------------ | ---------------- | ---------------------- | ---------------------- | ---------- |
| 买进看涨期权 | 看涨             | 理论上可无限增加       | 权利金 $C$             | $X+C$      |
| 卖出看涨期权 | 稳定或不强烈看涨 | 权利金 $C$             | 理论上可能无限增加     | $X+C$      |
| 买进看跌期权 | 看跌             | 股票情形下最高为 $X-P$ | 权利金 $P$             | $X-P$      |
| 卖出看跌期权 | 稳定或不强烈看跌 | 权利金 $P$             | 股票情形下最高为 $X-P$ | $X-P$      |

------

### Examination Topic: Concepts and Types of Option Spread Combinations ｜ 考点：期权价差组合的概念及类型

#### I. Concept of an Option Spread ｜ 一、期权价差组合的概念

An option spread is a portfolio consisting of two or more positions in options of the same type, with the same expiry date but different exercise prices. ｜ 期权价差组合是指同时持有两个或者多个同种期权头寸，这些期权具有相同到期日，但执行价格不同。

The options must either all be call options or all be put options. ｜ 组合中的期权要么都是看涨期权，要么都是看跌期权。
$$
\text{Same option type}
+
\text{Same expiry}
+
\text{Different strikes}
$$

------

#### II. Types of Option Spreads ｜ 二、期权价差组合的类型

##### (I) Bull Spread ｜ （一）牛市价差组合

A bull spread is used when the investor expects the underlying asset price to rise moderately. ｜ 牛市价差组合通常用于投资者预期标的资产价格温和上涨的情形。

###### Bull Call Spread ｜ 看涨期权牛市价差

The portfolio consists of: ｜ 组合包括：

- One long call with a lower exercise price ｜ 买入一份较低执行价格的看涨期权
- One short call with the same expiry and a higher exercise price ｜ 卖出一份相同到期日、较高执行价格的看涨期权

Assume: ｜ 假设：
$$
X_1<X_2
$$
The position is: ｜ 头寸为：
$$
+\text{Call}(X_1)
-
\text{Call}(X_2)
$$
The long lower-strike call provides upside exposure, while the short higher-strike call reduces the initial premium cost but limits the maximum profit. ｜ 较低执行价格的看涨期权多头提供上涨收益，较高执行价格的看涨期权空头可以降低初始权利金成本，但也限制了最大收益。

###### Bull Put Spread ｜ 看跌期权牛市价差

The portfolio consists of: ｜ 组合包括：

- One long put with a lower exercise price ｜ 买入一份较低执行价格的看跌期权
- One short put with the same expiry and a higher exercise price ｜ 卖出一份相同到期日、较高执行价格的看跌期权

$$
+\text{Put}(X_1)
-
\text{Put}(X_2)
$$

where: ｜ 其中：
$$
X_1<X_2
$$

------

##### (II) Bear Spread ｜ （二）熊市价差组合

A bear spread is used when the investor expects the underlying asset price to decline moderately. ｜ 熊市价差组合通常用于投资者预期标的资产价格温和下跌的情形。

###### Bear Call Spread ｜ 看涨期权熊市价差

The portfolio consists of: ｜ 组合包括：

- One long call with a higher exercise price ｜ 买入一份较高执行价格的看涨期权
- One short call with the same expiry and a lower exercise price ｜ 卖出一份相同到期日、较低执行价格的看涨期权

$$
-\text{Call}(X_1)
+
\text{Call}(X_2)
$$

where: ｜ 其中：
$$
X_1<X_2
$$

###### Bear Put Spread ｜ 看跌期权熊市价差

The portfolio consists of: ｜ 组合包括：

- One long put with a higher exercise price ｜ 买入一份较高执行价格的看跌期权
- One short put with the same expiry and a lower exercise price ｜ 卖出一份相同到期日、较低执行价格的看跌期权

$$
-\text{Put}(X_1)
+
\text{Put}(X_2)
$$

where: ｜ 其中：
$$
X_1<X_2
$$

------

##### (III) Butterfly Spread ｜ （三）蝶式价差组合

Assume three exercise prices satisfy: ｜ 假设三个执行价格满足：
$$
X_1<X_2<X_3
$$
A standard butterfly spread usually uses equal spacing: ｜ 标准蝶式价差通常还满足：
$$
X_2-X_1=X_3-X_2
$$

###### Call Butterfly Spread ｜ 看涨期权蝶式价差

The portfolio consists of: ｜ 组合包括：

- One long call with exercise price $X_1$ ｜ 买入一份执行价格为 $X_1$ 的看涨期权
- Two short calls with exercise price $X_2$ ｜ 卖出两份执行价格为 $X_2$ 的看涨期权
- One long call with exercise price $X_3$ ｜ 买入一份执行价格为 $X_3$ 的看涨期权

$$
+\text{Call}(X_1)
-2\text{Call}(X_2)
+\text{Call}(X_3)
$$

###### Put Butterfly Spread ｜ 看跌期权蝶式价差

The portfolio consists of: ｜ 组合包括：

- One long put with exercise price $X_1$ ｜ 买入一份执行价格为 $X_1$ 的看跌期权
- Two short puts with exercise price $X_2$ ｜ 卖出两份执行价格为 $X_2$ 的看跌期权
- One long put with exercise price $X_3$ ｜ 买入一份执行价格为 $X_3$ 的看跌期权

$$
+\text{Put}(X_1)
-2\text{Put}(X_2)
+\text{Put}(X_3)
$$

A long butterfly spread generally benefits when the underlying asset price remains close to the middle exercise price at expiry. ｜ 蝶式价差组合通常在标的资产到期价格接近中间执行价格时获得较高收益。

------

### Examination Topic: Types of Straddle Combinations ｜ 考点：跨式期权组合的类型

#### I. Long Straddle ｜ 一、多头跨式期权组合

##### (I) Concept ｜ （一）概念

A long straddle consists of simultaneously buying equal quantities of a call option and a put option with: ｜ 多头跨式期权组合，是指同时买入数量相同的看涨期权和看跌期权，且两种期权具有：

- The same underlying asset ｜ 相同标的资产
- The same exercise price ｜ 相同执行价格
- The same expiry date ｜ 相同到期日

$$
+\text{Call}(X)
+
\text{Put}(X)
$$

A long straddle is suitable when the investor expects a large price movement but is uncertain about the direction. ｜ 当投资者预计标的资产价格将出现大幅波动，但无法判断上涨还是下跌时，可以使用多头跨式组合。

------

##### (II) Case ｜ （二）案例

On 8 August 2017, the market price of stock A was RMB 40 per share. ｜ 2017年8月8日，股票A的市场价格为每股40元。

The September call and put options both had an exercise price of RMB 40. ｜ 9月份到期的看涨期权和看跌期权的执行价格均为每股40元。

The premiums were: ｜ 两种期权的权利金分别为：
$$
C=8\text{元/股}
$$
The total premium paid was: ｜ 支付的权利金总额为：
$$
C+P=8+5=13\text{元/股}
$$
The long-straddle profit at expiry is: ｜ 多头跨式组合的到期损益为：
$$
\Pi
=
\max(S_T-40,0)
+
\max(40-S_T,0)
-13
$$
This can also be written as: ｜ 也可以写成：
$$
\Pi
=
|S_T-40|-13
$$

------

###### Lower Break-Even Point ｜ 下方盈亏平衡点

$$
40-13
=
27\text{元/股}
$$

###### Upper Break-Even Point ｜ 上方盈亏平衡点

$$
40+13
=
53\text{元/股}
$$

Therefore: ｜ 因此：

- If $S_T<27$, the position earns a profit from a substantial price decline. ｜ 如果 $S_T<27$，标的资产大幅下跌，组合获得盈利。
- If $27, the position incurs a loss. ｜ 如果 $27，组合产生亏损。
- If $S_T>53$, the position earns a profit from a substantial price rise. ｜ 如果 $S_T>53$，标的资产大幅上涨，组合获得盈利。
- The maximum loss occurs when $S_T=40$. ｜ 当 $S_T=40$ 时，组合损失最大。

$$
\text{Maximum loss}
=
13\text{元/股}
$$

| Item             | Result                     |
| ---------------- | -------------------------- |
| Exercise price   | RMB 40                     |
| Total premium    | RMB 13                     |
| Lower break-even | RMB 27                     |
| Upper break-even | RMB 53                     |
| Maximum loss     | RMB 13                     |
| Profit condition | Price below 27 or above 53 |

| 项目           | 结果                   |
| -------------- | ---------------------- |
| 执行价格       | 40元                   |
| 权利金总额     | 13元                   |
| 下方盈亏平衡点 | 27元                   |
| 上方盈亏平衡点 | 53元                   |
| 最大损失       | 13元                   |
| 盈利条件       | 价格低于27元或高于53元 |

------

#### II. Short Straddle ｜ 二、空头跨式期权组合

##### (I) Concept ｜ （一）概念

A short straddle consists of simultaneously selling equal quantities of a call option and a put option with the same underlying asset, exercise price and expiry date. ｜ 空头跨式期权组合，是指同时卖出数量相同、标的资产相同、执行价格相同、到期日相同的看涨期权和看跌期权。
$$
-\text{Call}(X)
-
\text{Put}(X)
$$
The supplied source is partially damaged at the end of the definition, but its meaning is clear from the case and payoff diagram. ｜ 原材料在定义末尾受到OCR损坏，但根据案例和损益图可以确认其基本含义。

A short straddle is suitable when the investor expects the underlying asset price to remain relatively stable. ｜ 如果投资者无法确定价格上涨或下跌，但判断标的资产价格会保持相对稳定，可以卖出跨式期权组合。

------

##### (II) Case ｜ （二）案例

Using the same data as the previous case: ｜ 沿用上一案例的数据：
$$
X=40,\qquad C=8,\qquad P=5
$$
The total premium received is: ｜ 收取的权利金总额为：
$$
C+P=13\text{元/股}
$$
The short-straddle profit is: ｜ 空头跨式组合的到期损益为：
$$
\Pi
=
13-|S_T-40|
$$
The break-even points remain: ｜ 盈亏平衡点仍为：
$$
27\text{元/股}
$$
and: ｜ 以及：
$$
53\text{元/股}
$$
The maximum profit occurs when the underlying price equals the exercise price: ｜ 当标的资产价格等于执行价格时，组合获得最大收益：
$$
S_T=40
$$
If the stock price rises above RMB 53, the short call produces an increasing loss. ｜ 如果股票价格上涨至53元以上，看涨期权空头会产生不断扩大的亏损。

If the stock price falls below RMB 27, the short put produces a loss. ｜ 如果股票价格下跌至27元以下，看跌期权空头会产生亏损。

For a stock, if the price falls to zero, the downside result is: ｜ 对于股票而言，如果股价下跌至零，下跌方向的损益为：
$$
13-40=-27\text{元/股}
$$
The upward loss is theoretically unlimited. ｜ 上涨方向的亏损理论上没有上限。

| Item                   | Result              |
| ---------------------- | ------------------- |
| Exercise price         | RMB 40              |
| Total premium received | RMB 13              |
| Lower break-even       | RMB 27              |
| Upper break-even       | RMB 53              |
| Maximum profit         | RMB 13              |
| Main expectation       | Stable market price |

| 项目           | 结果             |
| -------------- | ---------------- |
| 执行价格       | 40元             |
| 收取权利金总额 | 13元             |
| 下方盈亏平衡点 | 27元             |
| 上方盈亏平衡点 | 53元             |
| 最大收益       | 13元             |
| 主要市场预期   | 标的价格保持稳定 |

------

#### III. Long Strangle ｜ 三、多头宽跨式期权组合

##### (I) Concept ｜ （一）概念

A long strangle consists of simultaneously buying equal quantities of a call option and a put option on the same underlying asset with: ｜ 多头宽跨式期权组合，是指同时买入数量相同的看涨期权和看跌期权，且两种期权具有：

- Different exercise prices ｜ 不同执行价格
- The same expiry date ｜ 相同到期日

The most common structure is: ｜ 最常见的结构为：
$$
X_P<X_C
$$
where: ｜ 其中：

- $X_P$: exercise price of the put option ｜ $X_P$：看跌期权执行价格
- $X_C$: exercise price of the call option ｜ $X_C$：看涨期权执行价格

The investor buys: ｜ 投资者买入：

- A lower-strike put option ｜ 较低执行价格的看跌期权
- A higher-strike call option ｜ 较高执行价格的看涨期权

$$
+\text{Put}(X_P)
+
\text{Call}(X_C)
$$

Like a long straddle, a long strangle benefits from large price movements in either direction. ｜ 与多头跨式组合类似，多头宽跨式组合也适用于预期价格将大幅波动，但方向不确定的情形。

Because both options are often out of the money, the initial premium cost is generally lower than that of a comparable straddle, but a larger price movement is required to earn a profit. ｜ 由于两份期权通常都是虚值期权，其初始权利金成本通常低于跨式组合，但需要更大的价格波动才能盈利。

------

##### (II) Case ｜ （二）案例

On 8 August 2017, stock A was trading at RMB 40 per share. ｜ 2017年8月8日，股票A的市场价格为每股40元。

The investor bought: ｜ 投资者买入：

- A September put with an exercise price of RMB 35 and a premium of RMB 1 ｜ 一份9月份到期、执行价格为35元、权利金为1元的看跌期权
- A September call with an exercise price of RMB 40 and a premium of RMB 8 ｜ 一份9月份到期、执行价格为40元、权利金为8元的看涨期权

The total premium paid was: ｜ 支付的权利金总额为：
$$
1+8
=
9\text{元/股}
$$
The profit at expiry is: ｜ 到期损益为：
$$
\Pi
=
\max(35-S_T,0)
+
\max(S_T-40,0)
-9
$$

------

###### Lower Break-Even Point ｜ 下方盈亏平衡点

$$
35-9
=
26\text{元/股}
$$

###### Upper Break-Even Point ｜ 上方盈亏平衡点

$$
40+9
=
49\text{元/股}
$$

Therefore: ｜ 因此：

- If $S_T<26$, the position earns a profit from a substantial decline. ｜ 如果 $S_T<26$，标的资产大幅下跌，组合获得盈利。
- If $26, the position incurs a loss. ｜ 如果 $26，组合产生亏损。
- If $S_T>49$, the position earns a profit from a substantial rise. ｜ 如果 $S_T>49$，标的资产大幅上涨，组合获得盈利。

When the stock price lies between the two exercise prices, both options expire without intrinsic value. ｜ 当股票价格处于35元至40元之间时，两份期权都没有内在价值。

The maximum loss is therefore the total premium: ｜ 因此，最大损失为全部权利金：
$$
\text{Maximum loss}
=
9\text{元/股}
$$
For a stock, if the price falls to zero, the maximum downside profit is: ｜ 对于股票而言，如果股票价格下跌至零，下跌方向的最大盈利为：
$$
35-9
=
26\text{元/股}
$$
The upside profit is theoretically unlimited. ｜ 上涨方向的盈利理论上没有上限。

| Item                | Result                     |
| ------------------- | -------------------------- |
| Put exercise price  | RMB 35                     |
| Call exercise price | RMB 40                     |
| Total premium       | RMB 9                      |
| Lower break-even    | RMB 26                     |
| Upper break-even    | RMB 49                     |
| Maximum loss        | RMB 9                      |
| Profit condition    | Price below 26 or above 49 |

| 项目             | 结果                   |
| ---------------- | ---------------------- |
| 看跌期权执行价格 | 35元                   |
| 看涨期权执行价格 | 40元                   |
| 权利金总额       | 9元                    |
| 下方盈亏平衡点   | 26元                   |
| 上方盈亏平衡点   | 49元                   |
| 最大损失         | 9元                    |
| 盈利条件         | 价格低于26元或高于49元 |

------

#### Comparison of Long Straddle and Long Strangle ｜ 多头跨式与多头宽跨式比较

| Item                    | Long straddle                       | Long strangle                        |
| ----------------------- | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------ |
| Exercise prices         | Same                                | Different                            |
| Initial premium         | Generally higher                    | Generally lower                      |
| Required price movement | Relatively smaller                  | Relatively larger                    |
| Market expectation      | Large movement, uncertain direction | Larger movement, uncertain direction |
| Maximum loss            | Total premiums                      | Total premiums                       |

| 项目             | 多头跨式             | 多头宽跨式               |
| ---------------- | -------------------- | ------------------------ |
| 执行价格         | 相同                 | 不同                     |
| 初始权利金       | 通常较高             | 通常较低                 |
