| 盈利所需价格波动 | 相对较小             | 相对较大                 |
| 市场预期         | 大幅波动、方向不确定 | 更大幅度波动、方向不确定 |
| 最大损失         | 全部权利金           | 全部权利金               |

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

📉 **Buying a put gives the right to sell the underlying asset and benefits from a price decline.** ｜ 📉 **买进看跌期权获得按照执行价格卖出标的资产的权利，并从价格下跌中获益。**

⚖️ **The long-put break-even point equals the exercise price minus the premium.** ｜ ⚖️ **买进看跌期权的盈亏平衡点等于执行价格减去权利金。**

🛡️ **A long put can protect an existing long position and lock in spot-market gains.** ｜ 🛡️ **买进看跌期权可以保护标的资产多头，并锁定现货市场收益。**

💰 **A short put earns premium income, but may suffer losses if the underlying price falls substantially.** ｜ 💰 **卖出看跌期权可以获得权利金，但标的价格大幅下跌时可能产生较大亏损。**

📊 **An option spread combines options of the same type and expiry but with different exercise prices.** ｜ 📊 **期权价差组合由同种、相同到期日但不同执行价格的期权构成。**

🐂 **A bull spread is used for a moderately bullish expectation, while a bear spread is used for a moderately bearish expectation.** ｜ 🐂 **牛市价差适用于温和看涨，熊市价差适用于温和看跌。**

🦋 **A butterfly spread uses three exercise prices and generally benefits when the final price is near the middle exercise price.** ｜ 🦋 **蝶式价差组合使用三个执行价格，通常在到期价格接近中间执行价格时受益。**

↕️ **A long straddle buys a call and a put with the same strike and benefits from a large movement in either direction.** ｜ ↕️ **多头跨式同时买入相同执行价格的看涨和看跌期权，在价格向任一方向大幅波动时获利。**

➖ **A short straddle earns the greatest profit when the underlying price remains near the exercise price.** ｜ ➖ **空头跨式在标的价格接近执行价格时获得最大收益。**

↔️ **A long strangle uses different exercise prices, costs less than a comparable straddle, but requires a larger price movement to profit.** ｜ ↔️ **多头宽跨式使用不同执行价格，成本通常低于跨式组合，但需要更大的价格波动才能盈利。**



## Section 4: Exotic Options ｜ 第四节 奇异期权

#### I. Exotic Options and Their Classification ｜ 一、奇异期权及分类

Exotic options are non-standard options whose payoff structures, activation conditions, underlying assets or exercise arrangements differ from those of ordinary call and put options. ｜ 奇异期权是指到期收益结构、激活条件、标的资产或者行权安排不同于普通看涨期权和看跌期权的非标准化期权。

##### (I) Path-Dependent Options ｜ （一）路径依赖型期权

The payoff of a path-dependent option depends on the movement path of the underlying asset price during the life of the option, rather than only on the underlying asset price at expiry or exercise. ｜ 路径依赖型期权的收益取决于期权有效期内标的资产价格的变化路径，而不仅仅取决于到期日或者行权时的标的资产价格。

Examples listed in the source include: ｜ 原材料列举的路径依赖型期权包括：

- Asian options ｜ 亚式期权
- Lookback options ｜ 回望期权
- Ladder options ｜ 阶梯形期权
- Ratchet options ｜ 棘轮期权
- Shout options ｜ 呼叫期权

------

##### (II) Multi-Factor Options ｜ （二）多因子期权

The underlying object of a multi-factor option is not limited to one basic asset. ｜ 多因子期权的标的不再局限于单一基础资产。

Its underlying object may be another ordinary option or a portfolio composed of multiple assets. ｜ 其标的可以是普通期权，也可以是由多个基础资产构成的组合。

Examples include: ｜ 主要包括：

- Compound options ｜ 复合期权
- Rainbow options ｜ 彩虹期权
- Basket options ｜ 一篮子期权
- Exchange options ｜ 交换期权

------

##### (III) Time-Dependent Options ｜ （三）时间依赖型期权

A time-dependent option allows the option buyer to choose or determine certain contract characteristics at a specified time before expiry. ｜ 时间依赖型期权允许期权买方在到期前的某一时点，选择或者确定期权合约的某些特征。

The selected characteristics will influence the option’s final payoff. ｜ 这些选择将进一步决定期权到期时的收益。

Examples include: ｜ 主要包括：

- Chooser options ｜ 选择性期权
- Forward-start options ｜ 远期开始期权

------

##### (IV) Extremum-Dependent Options ｜ （四）极值依赖型期权

An extremum-dependent option is activated or terminated when the underlying asset price reaches a predetermined critical level. ｜ 当标的资产价格达到预先设定的临界水平时，极值依赖型期权可能被激活或者宣告失效。

The predetermined critical price is generally called the barrier level. ｜ 这一预先设定的临界价格通常称为障碍值。

Examples listed in the source include: ｜ 原材料列举的极值依赖型期权包括：

- Barrier options ｜ 障碍期权
- Customised-exercise options ｜ 自定义执行期权

------

##### (V) Payoff-Modified Options ｜ （五）支付修正型期权

A payoff-modified option is created by modifying the expiry payoff structure of an ordinary option. ｜ 支付修正型期权是在普通期权到期收益结构基础上进行修改而形成的期权。

The payoff of an ordinary call option is: ｜ 普通看涨期权的到期收益为：
$$
\max(0,S_T-K)
$$
The payoff of an ordinary put option is: ｜ 普通看跌期权的到期收益为：
$$
\max(0,K-S_T)
$$
where: ｜ 其中：

- $S_T$: price of the underlying asset at expiry ｜ $S_T$：到期时标的资产价格
- $K$: exercise price ｜ $K$：执行价格

Changing these payoff functions produces payoff-modified exotic options. ｜ 对上述支付函数进行修改，就可以形成支付修正型奇异期权。

Examples include: ｜ 主要包括：

- Binary or digital options ｜ 两值期权或者数值期权
- Power options ｜ 幂期权

> 原材料的分类表写作“指数期权”，但后文将其定义为收益是价差的“幂函数”，因此更可能是“幂期权”的OCR错误。该期权与以股票指数为标的的普通“股指期权”不是同一概念。

------

#### Classification Table of Exotic Options ｜ 奇异期权分类表

| Category                   | Main products                                      |
| -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| Path-dependent options     | Asian, lookback, ladder, ratchet and shout options |
| Multi-factor options       | Compound, rainbow, basket and exchange options     |
| Time-dependent options     | Chooser and forward-start options                  |
| Extremum-dependent options | Barrier and customised-exercise options            |
| Payoff-modified options    | Binary/digital and power options                   |

| 产品类别       | 主要产品                           |
| -------------- | ---------------------------------- |
| 路径依赖型期权 | 亚式、回望、阶梯形、棘轮、呼叫期权 |
| 多因子期权     | 复合、彩虹、一篮子、交换期权       |
| 时间依赖型期权 | 选择性期权、远期开始期权           |
| 极值依赖型期权 | 障碍期权、自定义执行期权           |
| 支付修正型期权 | 两值或数值期权、幂期权             |

------

#### II. Asian Options ｜ 二、亚式期权

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

An Asian option is a typical path-dependent option. ｜ 亚式期权是一种典型的路径依赖型期权。

It is also called an average-price option and developed from conventional stock options. ｜ 亚式期权又称平均价格期权，是在普通股票期权基础上发展形成的。

According to the source, it was first introduced in Tokyo, Japan, by Bankers Trust. ｜ 根据原材料，亚式期权最早由美国银行家信托公司在日本东京推出。

When determining the option payoff at expiry, an Asian option does not use only the current market price of the underlying asset. ｜ 亚式期权在确定到期收益时，并不只使用到期时标的资产的市场价格。

Instead, it uses the average price of the underlying asset over a specified period during the life of the option. ｜ 它使用期权有效期内某一段预定期间的标的资产平均价格。

This specified period is called the averaging period. ｜ 这一预定期间称为平均期。

------

##### (II) Classification by the Price Used in Calculation ｜ （二）按照计算基础价格分类

###### Average-Price Options ｜ 平均价格期权

In an average-price option, the average price of the underlying asset is used in place of its final spot price. ｜ 平均价格期权使用标的资产在平均期内的平均价格，代替期权到期时的即期价格。

Let: ｜ 设：
$$
S_{\text{ave}}
$$
denote the average price of the underlying asset calculated according to a predetermined arithmetic or geometric averaging method. ｜ 表示按照预定方法计算出的标的资产平均价格，该方法可以是算术平均或者几何平均。

The payoff of an average-price call option is: ｜ 平均价格看涨期权的到期收益为：
$$
\max(0,S_{\text{ave}}-K)
$$
The payoff of an average-price put option is: ｜ 平均价格看跌期权的到期收益为：
$$
\max(0,K-S_{\text{ave}})
$$
Compared with an ordinary option, the payoff depends on the average price rather than solely on the final price. ｜ 与普通期权相比，其到期收益取决于平均价格，而不是只取决于期末价格。

------

###### Average-Strike Options ｜ 平均执行价格期权

An average-strike option uses the average price of the underlying asset during the predetermined averaging period as the exercise price. ｜ 平均执行价格期权将预定平均期内标的资产价格的平均值作为执行价格。

Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
K=S_{\text{ave}}
$$
The option payoff is determined by the difference between the spot price at exercise and the calculated average exercise price. ｜ 其收益取决于行权时标的资产即期价格与平均执行价格之间的差额。

The payoff of an average-strike call option is: ｜ 平均执行价格看涨期权的到期收益为：
$$
\max(0,S_T-S_{\text{ave}})
$$
The payoff of an average-strike put option is: ｜ 平均执行价格看跌期权的到期收益为：
$$
\max(0,S_{\text{ave}}-S_T)
$$

| Type                  | Price averaged                  | Call payoff                  |
| --------------------- | ------------------------------- | ---------------------------- |
| Average-price option  | Underlying price used in payoff | $\max(0,S_{\text{ave}}-K)$   |
| Average-strike option | Exercise price                  | $\max(0,S_T-S_{\text{ave}})$ |

| 类型             | 平均值的用途               | 看涨期权收益                 |
| ---------------- | -------------------------- | ---------------------------- |
| 平均价格期权     | 用平均价格替代到期标的价格 | $\max(0,S_{\text{ave}}-K)$   |
| 平均执行价格期权 | 将平均价格作为执行价格     | $\max(0,S_T-S_{\text{ave}})$ |

------

#### III. Barrier Options ｜ 三、障碍期权

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

A barrier option is an extremum-dependent option. ｜ 障碍期权属于极值依赖型期权。

Its validity or payoff depends on whether the underlying asset price reaches a predetermined level during a specified period. ｜ 其有效性或者收益取决于标的资产价格在特定期间内是否达到预先设定的价格水平。

This predetermined price level is called the barrier. ｜ 这一预先设定的价格水平称为障碍值。

------

##### (II) Classification ｜ （二）分类

###### Knock-Out Options ｜ 敲出期权

A knock-out option becomes invalid if the underlying asset price reaches the barrier level. ｜ 如果标的资产价格触及障碍值，敲出期权立即失效。

If the underlying asset price does not touch the barrier during the specified period, the contract remains an ordinary option. ｜ 如果标的资产价格在规定期间内始终未触及障碍值，该期权继续按照普通期权处理。
$$
\text{Barrier reached}
\Rightarrow
\text{Option terminated}
$$

------

###### Knock-In Options ｜ 敲入期权

A knock-in option becomes effective only if the underlying asset price reaches the barrier level. ｜ 只有当标的资产价格触及障碍值时，敲入期权才被激活。

If the underlying price never reaches the barrier, the option expires without becoming effective. ｜ 如果在规定期间内始终没有触及障碍值，该期权不会被激活，并最终失效。
$$
\text{Barrier reached}
\Rightarrow
\text{Option activated}
$$
Because barrier options are either activated or terminated subject to additional conditions, they are generally cheaper than otherwise comparable ordinary options. ｜ 由于障碍期权附加了激活或者终止条件，其权利通常受到更多限制，因此价格一般低于其他条件相同的普通期权。

------

##### (III) Classification by Barrier Direction ｜ （三）按照障碍方向分类

According to the relationship between the barrier level and the initial underlying asset price, barrier options may be divided into: ｜ 根据障碍值与标的资产初始价格的相对关系，障碍期权可以分为：

- Up-and-out options ｜ 向上敲出期权
- Down-and-out options ｜ 向下敲出期权
- Up-and-in options ｜ 向上敲入期权
- Down-and-in options ｜ 向下敲入期权

| Type         | Barrier direction           | Result after barrier is reached |
| ------------ | --------------------------- | ------------------------------- |
| Up-and-out   | Barrier above initial price | Option terminates               |
| Down-and-out | Barrier below initial price | Option terminates               |
| Up-and-in    | Barrier above initial price | Option activates                |
| Down-and-in  | Barrier below initial price | Option activates                |

| 类型     | 障碍值方向         | 触及障碍值后的结果 |
| -------- | ------------------ | ------------------ |
| 向上敲出 | 障碍值高于初始价格 | 期权失效           |
| 向下敲出 | 障碍值低于初始价格 | 期权失效           |
| 向上敲入 | 障碍值高于初始价格 | 期权激活           |
| 向下敲入 | 障碍值低于初始价格 | 期权激活           |

------

#### IV. Binary Options ｜ 四、两值期权

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

A binary option is also called a binomial option or digital option. ｜ 两值期权又称二项式期权或者数值期权。

It is a payoff-modified option whose expiry payoff generally has only two possible results. ｜ 两值期权属于支付修正型期权，其到期支付通常只有两种可能结果。

For example, the option may pay a predetermined amount if a condition is satisfied and pay zero otherwise. ｜ 例如，在满足约定条件时支付固定金额，不满足条件时支付零。

------

##### (II) Classification ｜ （二）分类

###### Cash-or-Nothing Options ｜ 现金或无支付期权

A cash-or-nothing option pays a fixed amount of cash $Q$ if the exercise condition is satisfied and pays zero otherwise. ｜ 现金或无支付期权在满足行权条件时支付固定现金金额 $Q$，否则支付零。

For a cash-or-nothing call: ｜ 现金或无支付看涨期权的到期支付为：
$$
\text{Payoff}
=
\begin{cases}
Q, & S_T>K\\
0, & S_T\leq K
\end{cases}
$$
For a cash-or-nothing put: ｜ 现金或无支付看跌期权的到期支付为：
$$
\text{Payoff}
=
\begin{cases}
Q, & S_T<K\\
0, & S_T\geq K
\end{cases}
$$
The amount received does not increase further as the underlying asset moves deeper into the money. ｜ 只要满足支付条件，获得的现金金额就是固定的，不会随着实值程度增加而进一步增加。

------

###### Asset-or-Nothing Options ｜ 资产或无支付期权

An asset-or-nothing option delivers the underlying asset or pays an amount equal to its value if the exercise condition is satisfied. ｜ 资产或无支付期权在满足行权条件时，交付标的资产或者支付等于标的资产价值的金额。

Otherwise, it pays zero. ｜ 如果不满足条件，则支付零。

For an asset-or-nothing call: ｜ 资产或无支付看涨期权的到期支付为：
$$
\text{Payoff}
=
\begin{cases}
S_T, & S_T>K\\
0, & S_T\leq K
\end{cases}
$$
For an asset-or-nothing put: ｜ 资产或无支付看跌期权的到期支付为：
$$
\text{Payoff}
=
\begin{cases}
S_T, & S_T<K\\
0, & S_T\geq K
\end{cases}
$$

| Binary option    | Condition satisfied         | Condition not satisfied |
| ---------------- | --------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| Cash-or-nothing  | Pays fixed cash $Q$         | Pays 0                  |
| Asset-or-nothing | Pays underlying value $S_T$ | Pays 0                  |

| 两值期权类型     | 满足条件               | 不满足条件 |
| ---------------- | ---------------------- | ---------- |
| 现金或无支付期权 | 支付固定现金 $Q$       | 支付0      |
| 资产或无支付期权 | 支付标的资产价值 $S_T$ | 支付0      |

------

#### V. Other Exotic Options ｜ 五、其他奇异期权

##### (I) Lookback Options ｜ （一）回望期权

A lookback option does not necessarily determine its exercise price or settlement price in advance. ｜ 回望期权的执行价格或者标的资产结算价格并不一定在期权签订时预先确定。

At expiry, the contract looks back over the underlying asset’s price history and uses the most favourable observed price to determine the payoff. ｜ 期权到期时，需要回顾有效期内标的资产的历史价格，并选择对期权买方最有利的价格确定收益。

It therefore gives the option holder the benefit of using an optimal historical exercise or settlement price. ｜ 因此，回望期权使买方可以按照历史期间内最有利的价格进行结算或者行权。

------

##### (II) Ladder Options ｜ （二）阶梯形期权

A ladder option specifies a series of price levels in advance. ｜ 阶梯形期权事先规定一系列价格水平。

When the underlying asset price reaches the next specified level, the contract resets or adjusts the relevant exercise terms. ｜ 当标的资产价格达到下一个预定价格水平时，期权将重新确定或者调整相应的执行条件。

The price levels resemble the steps of a ladder, which gives the option its name. ｜ 由于这些价格水平呈现逐级变化的特征，因此被称为阶梯形期权。

------

##### (III) Ratchet Options ｜ （三）棘轮期权

A ratchet option, also called a strike-reset option in the source, specifies a series of possible exercise prices in advance. ｜ 棘轮期权在原材料中也称履约价期权，它会事先约定一系列可能的执行价格。

On certain predetermined future dates, the exercise price is adjusted according to the underlying asset price at that time. ｜ 在未来预先设定的日期，根据当时标的资产价格对执行价格进行调整。

The exercise price is therefore reset periodically rather than remaining fixed throughout the life of the option. ｜ 因此，棘轮期权的执行价格会定期重设，而不是在整个有效期内始终保持不变。

------

##### (IV) Shout Options ｜ （四）呼叫期权

A shout option is also called a call-to-stop or lock-in option. ｜ 呼叫期权又称叫停期权或者锁定期权。

It allows the option buyer to “shout” at a time considered favourable during the option’s life. ｜ 期权买方可以在有效期内其认为有利的时点进行一次“呼叫”或者“叫停”。

The shout resets a relevant contract value or exercise condition and may lock in a minimum profit. ｜ 呼叫后，合约中的相关价格或者执行条件被重新确定，从而锁定最低盈利。

The source describes the shout option as a special European-style option. ｜ 原材料将呼叫期权描述为一种特殊的欧式期权。

Although it is exercised at expiry, the buyer may lock in a favourable value at an earlier time. ｜ 虽然该期权在到期时行权，但买方可以在到期前锁定一个有利价格或者最低收益。

------

##### (V) Rainbow Options ｜ （五）彩虹期权

A rainbow option has two or more underlying assets. ｜ 彩虹期权以两个或者多个基础资产作为标的。

Its payoff depends on the difference between an exercise price and the highest or lowest value among the underlying assets. ｜ 其收益取决于多个基础资产中的最高价格或者最低价格与执行价格之间的差额。

It is therefore also called a maximum-value or minimum-value option. ｜ 因此，彩虹期权也称为极大值期权或者极小值期权。

------

##### (VI) Basket Options ｜ （六）一篮子期权

A basket option is based on a portfolio or “basket” of underlying assets. ｜ 一篮子期权以一组基础资产构成的资产组合为标的。

Its payoff is determined by the difference between: ｜ 其收益通常取决于以下两者之间的差额：

- The weighted average price of the basket of underlying assets ｜ 一篮子标的资产的加权平均价格
- The exercise price or a weighted average exercise price ｜ 执行价格或者执行价格的加权平均值

Unlike a rainbow option, a basket option focuses on the weighted combined performance of all underlying assets rather than only the best or worst asset. ｜ 与彩虹期权不同，一篮子期权关注全部标的资产的加权综合表现，而不是只选择表现最好或者最差的资产。

------

##### (VII) Dual-Currency Options ｜ （七）双币种期权

A dual-currency option has an underlying asset denominated in currency A but settles the option payoff in currency B. ｜ 双币种期权的标的资产以货币A计价，但最终使用货币B进行结算。

Therefore, its value may be affected by both the price of the underlying asset and the exchange rate between the two currencies. ｜ 因此，该期权的价值可能同时受到标的资产价格和两种货币之间汇率变化的影响。

------

##### (VIII) Compound Options ｜ （八）复合期权

A compound option is a multi-factor option whose underlying asset is another option. ｜ 复合期权属于多因子期权，其标的资产本身是另一份期权。

It is therefore commonly described as an “option on an option.” ｜ 因此，复合期权通常被称为“期权的期权”。

The buyer first obtains the right to purchase or sell another option at a specified future time. ｜ 买方首先获得在未来某一时点买入或者卖出另一份期权的权利。

------

##### (IX) Power Options ｜ （九）幂期权

According to the payoff description in the source, this product modifies the ordinary option payoff by raising the in-the-money price difference to a specified power. ｜ 根据原材料的收益描述，该期权是在普通期权的实值价差基础上进行幂次处理。

For a power call option, the payoff may take the form: ｜ 幂看涨期权的到期收益可以表示为：
$$
\left[\max(S_T-K,0)\right]^n
$$
For a power put option, the payoff may take the form: ｜ 幂看跌期权的到期收益可以表示为：
$$
\left[\max(K-S_T,0)\right]^n
$$
where $n$ is the specified power. ｜ 其中，$n$为预先设定的幂次。

When $n>1$, the payoff increases faster than that of an ordinary option as the option moves deeper into the money. ｜ 当 $n>1$ 时，随着期权实值程度提高，其收益增长速度快于普通期权。

Therefore, a power option magnifies the payoff of an ordinary option in the in-the-money region and is generally more expensive than an otherwise comparable ordinary option. ｜ 因此，幂期权会放大普通期权在实值状态下的收益，其价格通常也高于其他条件相同的普通期权。

> 原文将本项写作“指数期权”，但从“收益为价差的幂函数”这一表述判断，应为“幂期权”。若教材原图明确写作“指数期权”，则可能是教材使用了不同译名。

------

#### Comparison of Major Exotic Options ｜ 主要奇异期权比较

| Option               | Key feature                                      | Main pricing or payoff basis           |
| -------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------- |
| Asian option         | Uses an average price                            | Average underlying price               |
| Barrier option       | Activation or termination condition              | Whether a barrier is reached           |
| Binary option        | Two possible payment outcomes                    | Fixed cash, asset value or zero        |
| Lookback option      | Uses the most favourable historical price        | Historical maximum or minimum          |
| Ladder option        | Uses a series of price levels                    | Whether successive levels are reached  |
| Ratchet option       | Periodically resets the strike                   | Underlying prices on reset dates       |
| Shout option         | Allows early profit locking                      | Value locked when the holder shouts    |
| Rainbow option       | Multiple underlying assets                       | Best or worst asset                    |
| Basket option        | Multiple underlying assets                       | Weighted average basket value          |
| Dual-currency option | Different denomination and settlement currencies | Asset price and exchange rate          |
| Compound option      | Underlying asset is another option               | Value of the underlying option         |
| Power option         | Nonlinear magnified payoff                       | Power of the positive price difference |

| 期权类型   | 核心特征               | 主要收益依据           |
| ---------- | ---------------------- | ---------------------- |
| 亚式期权   | 使用平均价格           | 标的资产平均价格       |
| 障碍期权   | 附加激活或失效条件     | 是否触及障碍值         |
| 两值期权   | 只有两种支付结果       | 固定现金、资产价值或零 |
| 回望期权   | 使用最有利历史价格     | 历史最高价或最低价     |
| 阶梯形期权 | 预设多个价格层级       | 是否逐级达到价格水平   |
| 棘轮期权   | 定期重设执行价格       | 重设日的标的资产价格   |
| 呼叫期权   | 允许提前锁定最低盈利   | 呼叫时锁定的价值       |
| 彩虹期权   | 多个标的资产           | 最优或最差资产价格     |
| 一篮子期权 | 多个标的资产           | 资产组合加权平均值     |
| 双币种期权 | 计价货币与结算货币不同 | 标的价格和汇率         |
| 复合期权   | 标的是另一份期权       | 标的期权价值           |
| 幂期权     | 非线性放大收益         | 正价差的幂函数         |

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🛤️ **Path-dependent option payoffs depend on the price path during the option’s life, not only on the final price.** ｜ 🛤️ **路径依赖型期权的收益取决于有效期内的价格路径，而不只取决于到期价格。**

🔢 **Multi-factor options may use another option or multiple assets as their underlying objects.** ｜ 🔢 **多因子期权可以将另一份期权或者多个基础资产作为标的。**

⏰ **Time-dependent options allow the buyer to determine certain contract characteristics before expiry.** ｜ ⏰ **时间依赖型期权允许买方在到期前选择或者确定部分合约特征。**

🚧 **Barrier options are activated or terminated when the underlying price reaches a predetermined barrier.** ｜ 🚧 **障碍期权在标的资产价格触及预定障碍值时被激活或者失效。**

📉 **Knock-out means touching the barrier terminates the option; knock-in means touching the barrier activates it.** ｜ 📉 **敲出是触及障碍值后期权失效，敲入是触及障碍值后期权生效。**

📊 **Asian options use an average underlying price either as the payoff price or as the exercise price.** ｜ 📊 **亚式期权将标的资产平均价格用于计算到期收益或者确定执行价格。**

0️⃣ **Binary options provide one payment when a condition is met and zero otherwise.** ｜ 0️⃣ **两值期权在满足条件时支付约定金额，否则支付零。**

👁️ **Lookback options use the most favourable price observed during the option’s life.** ｜ 👁️ **回望期权根据有效期内最有利的历史价格确定收益。**

🌈 **Rainbow options depend on the best or worst performance among multiple assets, while basket options depend on their weighted average performance.** ｜ 🌈 **彩虹期权取决于多个资产中的最高或最低表现，一篮子期权取决于资产组合的加权平均表现。**

🪆 **A compound option is an option whose underlying asset is another option.** ｜ 🪆 **复合期权是以另一份期权为标的资产的期权。**

📈 **Power options raise the positive exercise-price difference to a specified power and magnify in-the-money payoffs.** ｜ 📈 **幂期权将正的行权价差进行幂次计算，从而放大实值状态下的收益。





# Chapter 10: Forward Contracts ｜ 第十章 远期合约

This chapter accounts for approximately 8% of the examination score. ｜ 本章分值占比约为8%。

## Chapter Structure ｜ 本章结构

- Section 1: Overview of Forward Contracts ｜ 第一节：远期合约概述
- Section 2: Commodity Forwards ｜ 第二节：商品远期
- Section 3: Forward Rate Agreements ｜ 第三节：远期利率协议
- Section 4: Foreign Exchange Forwards and Foreign Exchange Swaps ｜ 第四节：外汇远期与外汇掉期

------

## Section 1: Overview of Forward Contracts ｜ 第一节 远期合约概述

### Examination Topic: Concept and Trading Characteristics of Forward Contracts ｜ 考点：远期的概念及其交易特征

#### I. Concept of a Forward Contract ｜ 一、远期合约的概念

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

A forward contract is an agreement under which two parties agree to buy or sell a specified quantity of a commodity or financial asset at a contractually agreed price on a specified future date. ｜ 远期合约是指交易双方约定，在未来某一确定时间，按照合约规定的价格，买入或者卖出一定数量某种商品或者金融资产的合约。

Unlike an option, both parties to a forward contract generally have the right and obligation to perform the contract at maturity. ｜ 与期权不同，远期合约的买卖双方在到期时通常都具有履行合约的权利和义务。

------

##### (II) Main Terms Specified in a Forward Contract ｜ （二）远期合约明确的主要内容

A forward contract generally specifies: ｜ 远期合约通常需要明确以下内容：

- The specific type and quantity of the commodity or financial asset to be delivered ｜ 买卖双方需要交割的商品或者金融资产的具体种类和数量
- The delivery terms, including time and place ｜ 到期交割的时间、地点等物流条款
- The price and payment method to be used at delivery ｜ 交割时需要支付的价格以及支付方式

------

##### (III) Underlying Assets ｜ （三）远期合约的标的资产

The underlying asset of a forward contract may be: ｜ 远期合约的标的资产可以是：

- Commodities ｜ 商品
- Financial instruments ｜ 金融工具

Examples of financial underlying assets may include currencies, interest rates, bonds and stocks. ｜ 金融标的可以包括外汇、利率、债券和股票等。

------

##### (IV) Settlement Methods ｜ （四）结算方式

Forward contracts may use either physical delivery or cash settlement. ｜ 远期合约可以采用实物交割，也可以采用现金结算。

###### Physical Delivery ｜ 实物交割

The seller delivers the underlying asset, and the buyer pays the agreed delivery price. ｜ 卖方向买方交付标的资产，买方按照约定的交割价格支付款项。

###### Cash Settlement ｜ 现金结算

Some forward contracts are settled by paying the price difference rather than physically delivering the underlying asset. ｜ 部分远期合约不进行标的资产的实物交付，而是通过支付价格差额完成结算。

------

#### II. Characteristics of Forward Trading ｜ 二、远期交易的特征

##### (I) Over-the-Counter Trading ｜ （一）远期交易属于场外市场

Forward contracts are generally negotiated and traded in over-the-counter markets rather than on centralised exchanges. ｜ 远期合约通常在场外市场协商交易，而不是在集中化交易所挂牌交易。

The parties may negotiate contract terms according to their individual needs. ｜ 交易双方可以根据自身需求协商合约条款。

------

##### (II) Non-Standardised Contracts ｜ （二）远期合约通常是非标准化合约

The underlying asset, quantity, delivery date, delivery location, delivery price and settlement method may all be customised. ｜ 远期合约的标的资产、数量、交割日期、交割地点、交割价格和结算方式等通常可以定制。

This gives forward contracts greater flexibility than standardised futures contracts. ｜ 因此，与标准化期货合约相比，远期合约具有更高的灵活性。

------

##### (III) Relatively High Default Risk ｜ （三）违约风险相对较高

Whether a forward contract can be performed depends largely on the creditworthiness of the two parties. ｜ 远期合约能否顺利履行，在很大程度上取决于交易双方的信用状况。

Because ordinary over-the-counter forward transactions generally lack a central clearing institution or performance-guarantee mechanism, a party facing an unfavourable price movement may refuse or fail to perform. ｜ 由于普通场外远期交易通常没有中央结算机构或者履约担保机制，当价格变化对一方不利时，该方可能出现不愿履约或者无力履约的情况。

This creates counterparty credit risk. ｜ 这会形成交易对手信用风险。

------

##### (IV) Relatively Low Liquidity ｜ （四）远期合约的流动性较差

Forward contracts do not usually have a fixed centralised trading venue or a unified information-disclosure system. ｜ 远期合约通常没有固定的集中交易场所，也缺乏统一的信息披露制度。

Information transmission may therefore be slower and less transparent. ｜ 因此，远期市场的信息传递可能较慢，市场透明度也相对较低。

Before maturity, a party wishing to transfer the contract may find it difficult to locate a third party willing to accept exactly the same contract terms. ｜ 在合约到期前，如果一方希望转让合约，通常很难找到恰好愿意接受相同合约条款的第三方。

The transfer may also require the consent of the original counterparty. ｜ 而且，合约转让通常还需要征得原交易对手的同意。

------

##### (V) Positions Are Usually Closed Through Delivery ｜ （五）通常以交割方式了结持仓

Forward contracts are commonly held until maturity and settled by physical delivery or cash settlement. ｜ 远期合约通常持有至到期，并通过实物交割或者现金结算了结头寸。

Unlike futures contracts, forward positions are less commonly closed through an opposite transaction in an active secondary market. ｜ 与期货合约不同，远期合约较少通过活跃二级市场中的反向交易进行平仓。

------

##### (VI) No Daily Mark-to-Market Settlement ｜ （六）通常不实行逐日盯市制度

Forward contracts generally do not use the daily mark-to-market settlement system commonly used in futures markets. ｜ 远期合约通常不实行期货市场中的逐日盯市结算制度。

Profits and losses generally accumulate during the life of the contract and are settled at maturity or on the agreed settlement date. ｜ 合约存续期间产生的盈亏通常持续累积，并在到期日或者约定结算日统一结算。

Financial institutions such as banks often participate as dealers or market makers, provide credit intermediation and reduce market frictions. ｜ 银行等金融机构通常以交易商或者做市商身份参与远期市场，承担信用中介职能，从而减少市场摩擦。

------

#### Comparison Between Forward and Futures Contracts ｜ 远期合约与期货合约比较

| Item                 | Forward contract                           | Futures contract                           |
| -------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------ |
| Trading venue        | OTC market                                 | Organised exchange                         |
| Contract terms       | Customised and non-standardised            | Standardised                               |
| Counterparty risk    | Relatively high                            | Reduced by central clearing                |
| Liquidity            | Relatively low                             | Generally higher                           |
| Settlement system    | Usually no daily mark-to-market            | Daily mark-to-market                       |
| Position termination | Usually delivery or negotiated termination | Usually offsetting transaction or delivery |

| 项目         | 远期合约           | 期货合约           |
| ------------ | ------------------ | ------------------ |
| 交易场所     | 场外市场           | 集中化交易所       |
| 合约条款     | 可定制、非标准化   | 标准化             |
| 交易对手风险 | 相对较高           | 由中央结算降低     |
| 流动性       | 相对较低           | 通常较高           |
| 结算制度     | 通常不逐日盯市     | 逐日盯市           |
| 头寸了结     | 通常交割或协商解除 | 通常反向平仓或交割 |

------

### Examination Topic: Delivery Price, Value and Forward Price ｜ 考点：远期合约的交割价格、远期合约价值与远期价格

#### I. Delivery Price, Contract Value and Forward Price ｜ 一、远期合约的交割价格、价值与远期价格

##### (I) Delivery Price ｜ （一）远期交割价格

The delivery price is the price agreed by the parties in the forward contract for the future delivery of the underlying asset. ｜ 远期交割价格是买卖双方在远期合约中约定的、未来交付标的资产时采用的价格。

It is also called the contractual price or agreement price. ｜ 远期交割价格又称协议价格。

Once the contract is entered into, its delivery price is normally fixed. ｜ 远期合约签订后，交割价格通常保持不变。

------

##### (II) Value of a Forward Contract ｜ （二）远期合约价值

At the time a fairly priced forward contract is entered into, its initial value is generally zero. ｜ 在按照公平价格签订远期合约时，合约的初始价值通常为零。

After the contract is established, the market price of the underlying asset, interest rates or exchange rates may change. ｜ 合约签订后，标的资产市场价格、利率或者汇率等因素可能发生变化。

The value of the forward contract may then become positive for one party and negative for the other. ｜ 此时，远期合约对一方可能具有正价值，而对另一方则具有相应的负价值。
$$
\text{Value to long}
=
-\text{Value to short}
$$

------

##### (III) Forward Price ｜ （三）远期价格

The forward price is the delivery price that makes the value of a newly entered forward contract equal to zero at that time. ｜ 远期价格是指使新签订的远期合约在当时价值等于零的交割价格。

It represents the no-arbitrage expectation of the future price based on current market information. ｜ 远期价格体现了基于当前市场信息形成的未来价格无套利预期。

📌 **Delivery price belongs to an existing contract; forward price is the fair delivery price for a new contract at the current time.** ｜ 📌 **交割价格属于已经签订的具体合约，远期价格是当前新签合约应采用的公平交割价格。**

------

##### (IV) Relationship Between Forward Price and Spot Price ｜ （四）远期价格与即期价格的关系

Under no-arbitrage conditions, the forward price equals the spot price of the underlying asset plus its net cost of carry. ｜ 在无套利条件下，远期价格等于标的资产即期价格加上净持有成本。
$$
\text{Forward price}
=
\text{Spot price}
+
\text{Net cost of carry}
$$
For an asset that provides no income, the cost of carry mainly consists of financing costs. ｜ 对于不支付收益的资产，持有成本主要是资金占用成本。

If the difference between the actual forward price and the spot price is inconsistent with the cost of carry, an arbitrage opportunity may arise. ｜ 如果实际远期价格与即期价格之间的差额不等于合理持有成本，在不考虑交易费用和市场摩擦时，就可能存在套利机会。

------

##### (V) No-Arbitrage Pricing Case ｜ （五）无套利定价案例

Assume that a non-dividend-paying stock currently costs RMB 100 per unit and the market risk-free interest rate is 5% for the contract period. ｜ 假设一只不支付股息的股票当前价格为每手100元，合约期间的市场无风险利率为5%。

The no-arbitrage forward price should be: ｜ 无套利远期价格应为：
$$
F_0
=
100\times(1+5\%)
$$

------

###### Case 1: Actual Forward Price Above RMB 105 ｜ 情形一：实际远期价格高于105元

Assume that the actual forward price is RMB 110. ｜ 假设实际远期价格为110元。

The forward contract is overvalued. ｜ 此时期货式远期价格被高估。

The investor may: ｜ 投资者可以：

- Borrow RMB 100 at the risk-free interest rate ｜ 按照无风险利率借入100元
- Buy one unit of the stock in the spot market ｜ 在现货市场买入一手股票
- Sell one forward contract at RMB 110 ｜ 以110元卖出一份远期合约

At maturity: ｜ 到期时：

- Deliver the stock under the forward contract and receive RMB 110 ｜ 按照远期合约交付股票并获得110元
- Repay the loan principal and interest of RMB 105 ｜ 偿还借款本息105元

The risk-free arbitrage profit is: ｜ 无风险套利收益为：
$$
110-105
=
5\text{元}
$$
This is a cash-and-carry arbitrage. ｜ 这种操作属于正向套利或者持有成本套利。

------

###### Case 2: Actual Forward Price Below RMB 105 ｜ 情形二：实际远期价格低于105元

Assume that the actual forward price is RMB 102. ｜ 假设实际远期价格为102元。

The forward contract is undervalued. ｜ 此时远期价格被低估。

The investor may: ｜ 投资者可以：

- Short-sell one unit of the stock and receive RMB 100 ｜ 卖空一手股票并获得100元
- Invest the RMB 100 at the risk-free interest rate ｜ 将100元按照无风险利率投资
- Buy one forward contract at RMB 102 ｜ 以102元买入一份远期合约

At maturity: ｜ 到期时：

- The investment grows to RMB 105 ｜ 货币市场投资增长至105元
- Purchase the stock under the forward contract for RMB 102 ｜ 按照远期合约以102元买入股票
- Use the stock to close the previous short position ｜ 使用该股票归还原来的卖空头寸

The risk-free arbitrage profit is: ｜ 无风险套利收益为：
$$
105-102
=
3\text{元}
$$
This is a reverse cash-and-carry arbitrage. ｜ 这种操作属于反向套利。

Therefore, under no-arbitrage equilibrium, the reasonable forward price is RMB 105. 

------

#### II. Profit and Loss of a Forward Contract ｜ 二、远期合约的损益

Assume: ｜ 假设：

- $K$: delivery price specified in the forward contract ｜ $K$：远期合约约定的交割价格
- $T$: maturity date ｜ $T$：远期合约到期时间
- $S_T$: spot price of the underlying asset at maturity ｜ $S_T$：到期时标的资产的即期价格

At maturity, both parties are generally required to perform the contract regardless of the prevailing spot price. ｜ 远期合约到期时，无论标的资产的市场价格如何变化，买卖双方通常都必须履行合约。

------

##### (I) Long Forward Position ｜ （一）远期合约多头

The long pays the delivery price $K$ and receives the underlying asset. ｜ 多头按照交割价格 $K$ 支付款项，并取得标的资产。

If the long immediately sells the asset in the spot market at $S_T$, the profit per unit is: ｜ 如果多头立即按照即期价格 $S_T$ 卖出该资产，每单位标的资产的损益为：
$$
\Pi_{\text{long}}
=
S_T-K
$$
If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T>K
$$
the long earns a profit. ｜ 多头获得盈利。

If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T<K
$$
the long incurs a loss. ｜ 多头产生亏损。

------

##### (II) Short Forward Position ｜ （二）远期合约空头

The short delivers the underlying asset and receives the delivery price $K$. ｜ 空头向多头交付标的资产，并取得交割收入 $K$。

Relative to the spot-market price $S_T$, the short’s profit per unit is: ｜ 相对于到期时即期市场价格 $S_T$，空头每单位标的资产的损益为：
$$
\Pi_{\text{short}}
=
K-S_T
$$
If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T<K
$$
the short earns a profit. ｜ 空头获得盈利。

If: ｜ 如果：
$$
S_T>K
$$
the short incurs a loss. ｜ 空头产生亏损。

------

#### Forward Contract Profit-and-Loss Comparison ｜ 远期合约多空损益比较

| Position      | Profit at maturity | Profitable when | Loss-making when |
| ------------- | ------------------ | --------------- | ---------------- |
| Long forward  | $S_T-K$            | $S_T>K$         | $S_T             |
| Short forward | $K-S_T$            | $S_T            | $S_T>K$          |

| 头寸     | 到期损益 | 盈利条件 | 亏损条件 |
| -------- | -------- | -------- | -------- |
| 远期多头 | $S_T-K$  | $S_T>K$  | $S_T     |
| 远期空头 | $K-S_T$  | $S_T     | $S_T>K$  |

The profit of one party is exactly equal to the loss of the other party: ｜ 一方的盈利恰好等于另一方的亏损：
$$
\Pi_{\text{long}}
=
-\Pi_{\text{short}}
$$
The payoff structure of a forward contract is linear and symmetrical. ｜ 远期合约具有线性且对称的损益结构。

Unlike an option buyer, a forward-contract party cannot simply abandon an unfavourable contract at maturity. ｜ 与期权买方不同，远期合约交易者在合约到期且结果不利时，通常不能直接放弃履约。

> 原材料中的损益公式存在OCR漏字符，正确形式应为多头 $S_T-K$、空头 $K-S_T$。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

📄 **A forward contract requires the parties to buy or sell an asset at an agreed price on a specified future date.** ｜ 📄 **远期合约要求交易双方在未来约定日期，按照约定价格买卖标的资产。**

🏦 **Forward contracts are generally OTC, non-standardised and relatively flexible.** ｜ 🏦 **远期合约通常在场外市场交易，具有非标准化和灵活性较高的特点。**

⚠️ **The absence of central clearing creates relatively high counterparty credit risk.** ｜ ⚠️ **由于通常缺少中央结算担保，远期交易的交易对手信用风险相对较高。**

💧 **Forward contracts generally have lower liquidity and are usually held until delivery or settlement.** ｜ 💧 **远期合约流动性通常较低，通常持有至交割或者结算。**

📌 **The delivery price is fixed in an existing contract, while the forward price is the fair delivery price for a new contract.** ｜ 📌 **交割价格是已有合约约定的价格，远期价格是当前新签合约的公平交割价格。**

⚖️ **Under no-arbitrage conditions, the forward price equals the spot price plus the net cost of carry.** ｜ ⚖️ **在无套利条件下，远期价格等于即期价格加净持有成本。**

📈 **If the forward price is excessively high, buy the spot asset and sell the forward contract.** ｜ 📈 **远期价格过高时，可以买入现货并卖出远期合约。**

📉 **If the forward price is excessively low, short the spot asset and buy the forward contract.** ｜ 📉 **远期价格过低时，可以卖空现货并买入远期合约。**

🔄 **The long forward payoff is $S_T-K$, while the short forward payoff is $K-S_T$.** ｜ 🔄 **远期多头到期损益为 $S_T-K$，空头到期损益为 $K-S_T$。**

## Section 2: Commodity Forwards ｜ 第二节 商品远期

### Examination Topic: Overview of Commodity Forwards ｜ 考点：商品远期概述

#### I. Concept of Commodity Forwards ｜ 一、商品远期的概念

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

A commodity forward contract is a forward contract whose underlying asset is a specified commodity. ｜ 商品远期合约是以特定商品作为标的资产的远期合约。

In a commodity forward transaction, the parties agree in advance to: ｜ 在商品远期交易中，交易双方事先约定：

- Buy and sell a specified quantity of a commodity at an agreed forward delivery price on the delivery date; or ｜ 在交付日按照约定的远期交割价格，买卖约定数量的商品；
- Settle the contract in cash according to the forward settlement amount on the settlement date. ｜ 在结算日按照远期交易结算金额进行现金结算。

------

##### (II) Difference Between a Commodity Forward and an Ordinary Trade Contract ｜ （二）商品远期与普通贸易合同的区别

An ordinary commodity trade contract is normally signed directly between a buyer and a seller for the purchase and sale of goods. ｜ 普通商品贸易合同通常由商品买卖双方直接签订，用于完成具体商品交易。

A commodity forward transaction is more organised and regulated. ｜ 商品远期交易则具有更强的规范性和组织性。

It is generally conducted through: ｜ 商品远期通常通过以下渠道进行：

- Banks or other financial institutions ｜ 银行或者其他金融机构
- Organised trading platforms ｜ 有组织的交易平台

Although a commodity forward remains an over-the-counter transaction, its contract procedures, quotation methods and settlement arrangements are generally more formalised than those of an ordinary commercial contract. ｜ 虽然商品远期仍属于场外交易，但其合约程序、报价方式和结算安排通常比普通商品贸易合同更加规范。

------

##### (III) Settlement Methods ｜ （三）结算方式

Commodity forwards may be settled through: ｜ 商品远期可以采用以下两种结算方式：

###### Physical Delivery ｜ 实物交割

The seller delivers the agreed commodity, and the buyer pays the agreed forward delivery price. ｜ 卖方交付约定的商品，买方按照约定的远期交割价格付款。

###### Difference Settlement ｜ 差额结算

Under difference settlement, the commodity itself is not delivered. ｜ 采用差额结算时，交易双方不实际交付商品。

Instead, the difference between the forward settlement price and the agreed forward delivery price is calculated on the settlement date. ｜ 交易双方在结算日计算远期交易结算价格与约定远期交割价格之间的差额。

One party then pays the corresponding cash amount to the other party. ｜ 之后，由交易一方向另一方支付相应现金金额。

------

##### (IV) Calculation of the Settlement Amount ｜ （四）远期交易结算金额的计算

The settlement amount is: ｜ 远期交易结算金额为：
$$
\text{Settlement amount}
=
(\text{Forward settlement price}
-
\text{Forward delivery price})
\times
\text{Commodity quantity}
$$
where: ｜ 其中：

- **Forward delivery price** is the price agreed by the parties for buying or selling the commodity on the delivery date. ｜ **远期交割价格**是交易双方事先约定，在交付日买入或者卖出商品的价格。
- **Forward settlement price** is the specified commodity price used to calculate the amount payable on the settlement date. ｜ **远期交易结算价格**是交易双方约定，在结算日用于计算双方应收应付款项的商品价格。

------

##### (V) Direction of Settlement ｜ （五）结算方向

If the settlement amount is positive: ｜ 如果结算金额为正：
$$
\text{Settlement price}
>
\text{Delivery price}
$$
the forward seller pays the settlement amount to the forward buyer. ｜ 由远期卖方向远期买方支付结算金额。

If the settlement amount is negative: ｜ 如果结算金额为负：
$$
\text{Settlement price}
<
\text{Delivery price}
$$
the forward buyer pays the absolute value of the settlement amount to the forward seller. ｜ 由远期买方向远期卖方支付结算金额的绝对值。

If the settlement amount is zero, neither party needs to make a payment. ｜ 如果结算金额为零，双方无须进行差额支付。

| Settlement result | Payment direction |
| ----------------- | ----------------- |
| Positive          | Seller pays buyer |
| Negative          | Buyer pays seller |
| Zero              | No payment        |

| 结算结果 | 支付方向       |
| -------- | -------------- |
| 正数     | 卖方向买方支付 |
| 负数     | 买方向卖方支付 |
| 零       | 双方无须支付   |

The formula and settlement direction are provided in the source material. 

------

#### II. Origin and Development of Commodity Forwards ｜ 二、商品远期的产生与发展

Commodity forwards were among the earliest forms of forward contracts. ｜ 商品远期是最早出现的远期合约之一。

They were created by commodity producers and dealers to manage the risk of fluctuations in spot commodity prices. ｜ 商品远期最初由商品生产者和经营者创造，用于规避商品现货价格波动风险。

By the middle of the nineteenth century, forward supply and purchase contracts between producers and dealers had gradually developed into more complete forward contracts. ｜ 到19世纪中叶，商品生产者与经销商签订的远期供销合同，逐渐发展为较为完善的远期合约。

Contract terms, pricing methods and settlement arrangements became increasingly formalised. ｜ 其合约条款、计价方式和结算方式逐渐规范。

A commodity forward contract may specify: ｜ 商品远期合约通常规定：

- Commodity name ｜ 商品名称
- Quality ｜ 商品品质
- Specification ｜ 商品规格
- Delivery quantity ｜ 交割数量
- Delivery date ｜ 交割日期
- Delivery location ｜ 交割地点

Commodity forward contracts are generally non-standardised. ｜ 商品远期合约通常属于非标准化合约。

The source describes commodity forwards as essentially spot transactions with delivery of the physical commodity at maturity. ｜ 原材料将商品远期描述为本质上属于现货交易，并通常在到期时交收商品实物。

------

### Examination Topic: Trading Methods of Commodity Forwards ｜ 考点：商品远期的交易方式

Commodity forward trading methods include: ｜ 商品远期交易方式包括：

- Real-time trading ｜ 实时交易
- Order trading ｜ 挂单交易
- Inquiry-based real-time trading ｜ 询价实时交易
- Inquiry-based order trading ｜ 询价挂单交易

------

#### I. Real-Time Trading ｜ 一、实时交易

Real-time trading means that the client immediately conducts a commodity transaction according to the quotation provided by the bank. ｜ 实时交易是指客户按照银行提供的即时交易报价进行商品交易。

The bank may introduce or suspend real-time trading in a particular commodity according to market activity and liquidity. ｜ 银行可以根据市场活跃程度和流动性情况，适时推出或者停止某一商品品种的实时交易。

------

#### II. Order Trading ｜ 二、挂单交易

Order trading means that the client submits a trading instruction containing a specified price or condition. ｜ 挂单交易是指客户提交包含特定价格或者条件的挂单指令。

When the bank’s quotation satisfies the order condition, the transaction is executed at the specified order price. ｜ 当银行交易报价满足挂单条件时，交易按照挂单价格成交。
$$
\text{Bank quotation reaches order condition}
\Rightarrow
\text{Order executed}
$$

------

#### III. Inquiry-Based Real-Time Trading ｜ 三、询价实时交易

##### (I) Concept ｜ （一）概念

In inquiry-based real-time trading, the client sends a transaction inquiry to the bank. ｜ 在询价实时交易中，客户首先向银行发起交易询价。

The bank provides a quotation according to the client’s requirements. ｜ 银行根据客户的交易需求提供报价。

The client must confirm the quotation within the prescribed period for the transaction to be completed. ｜ 客户需要在规定时间内确认银行报价，交易才能成交。

If the client does not confirm the quotation within the prescribed period, the quotation expires and the client is deemed to have abandoned the transaction. ｜ 如果客户未在规定时间内确认，则该报价失效，视为客户放弃本次交易。

The client may then initiate a new inquiry. ｜ 之后，客户可以重新发起询价。

------

##### (II) Basic-Metal Forward Transactions ｜ （二）基本金属远期交易的分类

###### Price Inquiry Real-Time Trading ｜ 价格询价实时交易

The bank provides a specific transaction price according to the client’s requirements. ｜ 银行根据客户需求报出具体成交价格。

After the client confirms the price, the system generates one forward transaction. ｜ 客户确认成交价格后，系统生成一笔远期交易。

###### Spread Inquiry Real-Time Trading ｜ 价差询价实时交易

The bank provides a spread quotation according to the client’s requirements. ｜ 银行根据客户需求提供价差报价。

After the client confirms the spread, the bank uses the agreed spread to determine the near-end and far-end transaction prices. ｜ 客户确认成交价差后，银行根据成交价差确定近端和远端成交价格。

This normally generates two forward transactions with different maturities. ｜ 这种交易通常生成两笔不同期限的远期交易。

> 原材料中本项最后一句被截断，但可以确认其核心是根据成交价差确定近端和远端价格。

------

#### IV. Inquiry-Based Order Trading ｜ 四、询价挂单交易

##### (I) Concept ｜ （一）概念

Inquiry-based order trading means that the client submits a commodity transaction order to the bank through an inquiry process. ｜ 询价挂单交易是指客户通过询价方式向银行提交商品交易挂单。

The order specifies elements such as: ｜ 挂单通常包含：

- Price or spread ｜ 价格或者价差
- Trading quantity ｜ 交易数量
- Contract term ｜ 交易期限

The bank determines, according to market conditions, whether to accept the transaction and what quantity to execute. ｜ 银行根据市场情况，决定是否与客户成交以及实际成交数量。

------

##### (II) Basic-Metal Forward Transactions ｜ （二）基本金属远期交易的分类

###### Price Inquiry Order Trading ｜ 价格询价挂单交易

The bank considers the price, transaction quantity and term specified by the client and determines whether to execute the transaction. ｜ 银行根据客户询价挂单中的价格、交易量和期限等条件，决定是否成交以及成交数量。

###### Spread Inquiry Order Trading ｜ 价差询价挂单交易

The bank considers the spread, transaction quantity and term specified by the client. ｜ 银行根据客户询价挂单中的价差、交易量和期限等条件进行判断。

If the bank accepts the order, the near-end and far-end transaction prices are determined according to the agreed spread. ｜ 如果银行接受该挂单，则根据成交价差确定近端和远端成交价格。

The system then generates two forward transactions. ｜ 系统随后生成两笔远期交易。

------

#### Comparison of Commodity Forward Trading Methods ｜ 商品远期交易方式比较

| Trading method            | Price formation                      | Execution                                    |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------- |
| Real-time trading         | Bank’s current quotation             | Immediate execution                          |
| Order trading             | Client’s specified order price       | Executed when condition is met               |
| Inquiry real-time trading | Bank quotes after inquiry            | Client confirms within time limit            |
| Inquiry order trading     | Client submits customised conditions | Bank decides whether and how much to execute |

| 交易方式     | 价格形成方式     | 成交方式               |
| ------------ | ---------------- | ---------------------- |
| 实时交易     | 银行即时报价     | 客户即时成交           |
| 挂单交易     | 客户设定挂单价格 | 条件满足时成交         |
| 询价实时交易 | 银行根据询价报价 | 客户限时确认           |
| 询价挂单交易 | 客户提出定制条件 | 银行决定是否及成交多少 |

------

### Examination Topic: Applications of Commodity Forward Transactions ｜ 考点：商品远期交易的应用

#### I. Gold Forward Transactions ｜ 一、黄金远期交易

##### (I) Overview ｜ （一）概述

Gold is a special commodity with both commodity and financial characteristics. ｜ 黄金是一种特殊商品，既具有商品属性，也具有金融属性。

According to the source, the Shanghai Gold Exchange introduced interbank renminbi gold forward trading in 2013. ｜ 根据原材料，上海黄金交易所于2013年推出银行间人民币黄金远期交易。

Approved market participants conducted bilateral inquiry transactions through the foreign exchange trading system of the China Foreign Exchange Trade System. ｜ 经上海黄金交易所核准的市场参与者，通过中国外汇交易中心的外汇交易系统进行双边询价交易。

------

##### (II) Quotation Method ｜ （二）报价方式

Gold forwards are quoted using the outright forward price. ｜ 黄金远期采用远期全价报价。

The outright forward price is the agreed price at which the parties will buy or sell gold on the future value date. ｜ 远期全价是黄金交易双方约定，在未来起息日买卖黄金的完整价格。
$$
\text{Outright forward price}
=
\text{Spot price}
+
\text{Forward points}
$$
The spot price is the spot quotation given by the quoting party when the transaction is concluded. ｜ 即期价格是远期交易成交时，报价方给出的即期市场价格。

Forward points represent the difference between the forward price and the spot price. ｜ 远期点表示远期价格与即期价格之间的差额。

Forward points may be positive or negative. ｜ 远期点既可以为正数，也可以为负数。

According to the source, gold forward points are quoted in renminbi cents per gram. ｜ 根据原材料，黄金远期点以人民币分/克为单位。

------

##### (III) Quotation Case ｜ （三）报价案例

Assume that the quoting party provides: ｜ 假设报价方给出的报价为：
$$
\text{Spot price}
=
380.00/381.00
$$
Because 100 forward points equal RMB 1.000 per gram: ｜ 由于100个远期点相当于每克人民币1.000元：

###### Initiating Party Is the Seller ｜ 发起方为卖方

The initiating party sells gold, so the relevant spot bid price and forward-point bid are used. ｜ 发起方卖出黄金，因此使用即期买价和远期点买价。
$$
380.00+1.000
=
381.000
$$
The outright forward price is RMB 381.000 per gram. ｜ 远期全价为381.000元/克。

###### Initiating Party Is the Buyer ｜ 发起方为买方

The initiating party buys gold, so the relevant spot ask price and forward-point ask are used. ｜ 发起方买入黄金，因此使用即期卖价和远期点卖价。
$$
381.00+1.010
=
382.010
$$
The outright forward price is RMB 382.010 per gram. ｜ 远期全价为382.010元/克。

------

#### II. Forward Freight Agreements ｜ 二、远期运费协议

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

A Forward Freight Agreement, abbreviated as FFA, is a forward agreement concerning future freight rates. ｜ 远期运费协议简称FFA，是一种以未来运费或者租金为交易对象的远期协议。

The agreement specifies terms such as: ｜ 协议通常规定：

- Shipping route ｜ 航线
- Freight or charter price ｜ 运费或者租金价格
- Quantity or number of contract days ｜ 数量或者合约天数

At a specified future date, the parties settle the difference between: ｜ 在未来约定日期，交易双方根据以下两个价格之间的差额进行结算：

- The spot freight index published by the Baltic Exchange ｜ 波罗的海航运交易所发布的即期运费指数价格
- The freight or charter rate agreed in the contract ｜ 合约约定的运费或者租金价格

$$
\text{FFA settlement amount}
=
(\text{Actual freight rate}
-
\text{Contract rate})
\times
\text{Contract quantity}
$$

------

##### (II) Characteristics ｜ （二）特点

Unlike the physical shipping market, an FFA does not involve the actual transportation of goods. ｜ 与实际航运现货市场不同，FFA不涉及真实货物运输。

It is a paper freight-rate contract and a financial derivative based on freight or charter rates. ｜ 它属于“纸面运价”，是以运费或者租金为基础的金融衍生品。

FFAs generally use cash settlement. ｜ FFA通常通过现金差额进行结算。

------

##### (III) Hedging Case ｜ （三）套期保值案例

In June, an importing company signed a forward import contract for delivery in September. ｜ 某年6月，一家进口公司签订了9月份交付的远期进口贸易合同。

The goods were mainly to be transported by a Panamax vessel on the Atlantic-to-Far East route, referred to in the source as route 2A. ｜ 货物主要通过巴拿马型船在大西洋至远东航线运输，原材料将该航线称为2A航线。

The importer was concerned that freight rates might rise before September. ｜ 该进口商担心9月份之前运费上涨。

The current September charter rate was: ｜ 当时9月份日租金为：
$$
20{,}000\text{美元/天}
$$
The importer bought a September 2A FFA with a contract period of 60 days. ｜ 该进口商买入一份9月份2A航线的FFA，合约天数为60天。

Suppose that the September charter rate rose to: ｜ 假设9月份日租金上涨至：
$$
23{,}000\text{美元/天}
$$
The FFA profit should be: ｜ FFA市场盈利应为：
$$
(23{,}000-20{,}000)\times60
$$
The profit in the FFA market offsets the increase in the company’s actual shipping costs. ｜ FFA市场的盈利可以抵销企业实际运输成本的增加。

Therefore, the company achieves the objective of hedging freight-price risk. ｜ 因此，该企业实现了规避运费价格上涨风险的套期保值目的。

> 原材料将结果写为18000美元，但按照其给出的日租金差额3000美元和60天计算，正确结果应为180000美元。

------

##### (IV) Investment and Speculation Using FFAs ｜ （四）运用远期运费协议进行投资

Investors may also use FFAs for investment or speculation. ｜ 投资者也可以利用FFA进行投资或者投机交易。

Such investors may not have any physical shipping position. ｜ 这类投资者可能并没有实际航运或者货物运输头寸。

They simply predict changes in freight rates and attempt to earn a price-difference profit by buying at a low price and selling at a high price. ｜ 他们主要根据对未来运费变化的预测，通过低买高卖获取价差收益。

| Participant                     | Purpose                          |
| ------------------------------- | -------------------------------- |
| Cargo owner or shipping company | Hedge freight-rate risk          |
| Speculative investor            | Earn from freight-rate movements |

| 参与者         | 主要目的                 |
| -------------- | ------------------------ |
| 货主或航运企业 | 对冲运费价格风险         |
| 投机投资者     | 从运费变化中获取价差收益 |

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

📦 **A commodity forward uses a specified commodity as its underlying asset.** ｜ 📦 **商品远期以特定商品作为标的资产。**

📄 **Commodity forwards may be settled through physical delivery or cash difference settlement.** ｜ 📄 **商品远期可以采用实物交割或者现金差额结算。**

⚖️ **The settlement amount equals the settlement price minus the delivery price, multiplied by the commodity quantity.** ｜ ⚖️ **远期结算金额等于结算价格减交割价格，再乘以商品数量。**

💵 **A positive settlement amount is paid by the seller to the buyer; a negative amount is paid by the buyer to the seller.** ｜ 💵 **结算金额为正时由卖方向买方支付，为负时由买方向卖方支付。**

🏦 **Commodity forward trading methods include real-time trading, order trading, inquiry real-time trading and inquiry order trading.** ｜ 🏦 **商品远期交易方式包括实时交易、挂单交易、询价实时交易和询价挂单交易。**

🥇 **The outright gold forward price equals the spot price plus forward points.** ｜ 🥇 **黄金远期全价等于即期价格加远期点。**

🚢 **A Forward Freight Agreement settles the difference between the actual freight rate and the agreed contract rate.** ｜ 🚢 **远期运费协议按照实际运费与合约约定运费之间的差额进行结算。**

🛡️ **Cargo owners may buy FFAs to hedge against rising freight rates.** ｜ 🛡️ **货主可以通过买入FFA对冲运费上涨风险。**

📈 **Investors without physical shipping positions may also trade FFAs for speculative profit.** ｜ 📈 **没有实际航运头寸的投资者也可以利用FFA进行投机交易。**



## Section 3: Forward Rate Agreements ｜ 第三节 远期利率协议

### Examination Topic: Overview and Design of Forward Rate Agreements ｜ 考点：远期利率协议的概述和设计

#### I. Overview of Forward Rate Agreements ｜ 一、远期利率协议概述

A Forward Rate Agreement, abbreviated as FRA, is a financial contract under which two parties agree that, on a future date, they will settle the difference between interest calculated at a contractual rate and interest calculated at a reference rate on a specified notional principal. ｜ 远期利率协议简称FRA，是指交易双方约定在未来某一日期，以一定的名义本金为基础，对按照协议利率和参照利率分别计算的利息差额进行结算的金融合约。

The parties do not actually lend or exchange the notional principal. ｜ 交易双方并不实际借出或者交换名义本金。

Instead, they calculate the interest difference according to: ｜ 双方主要根据以下因素计算利息差额：

- The notional principal ｜ 名义本金
- The contractual interest rate ｜ 协议利率
- The market reference interest rate ｜ 市场参照利率
- The length of the agreement period ｜ 协议期限

The party owing the difference pays a settlement amount to the other party. ｜ 利息差额为负的一方向另一方支付结算金。

------

##### FRA Buyer ｜ FRA买方

The FRA buyer is economically equivalent to a notional borrower. ｜ FRA买方在经济意义上相当于名义借款人。

The buyer is concerned that future market interest rates may rise. ｜ 买方通常担心未来市场利率上涨。

If the reference rate at settlement is higher than the contractual rate, the buyer receives compensation. ｜ 如果结算时参照利率高于协议利率，买方获得利息差额补偿。
$$
\text{Reference rate}>\text{Contract rate}
\Rightarrow
\text{Buyer receives settlement}
$$

------

##### FRA Seller ｜ FRA卖方

The FRA seller is economically equivalent to a notional lender. ｜ FRA卖方在经济意义上相当于名义贷款人。

The seller is concerned that future market interest rates may decline. ｜ 卖方通常担心未来市场利率下降。

If the reference rate is lower than the contractual rate, the seller receives compensation. ｜ 如果参照利率低于协议利率，卖方获得利息差额补偿。
$$
\text{Reference rate}<\text{Contract rate}
\Rightarrow
\text{Seller receives settlement}
$$

------

#### II. Design of a Forward Rate Agreement ｜ 二、远期利率协议的设计

##### (I) Spot Rates and Forward Rates ｜ （一）即期利率与远期利率

###### Spot Rate ｜ 即期利率

A spot interest rate is the current market interest rate applicable from the present time to a specified future date. ｜ 即期利率是当前市场上，从现在开始到未来某一时点为止所适用的利率。

For example: ｜ 例如：

- A one-year spot rate applies from now to one year later. ｜ 一年期即期利率适用于从现在至一年后的期间。
- A two-year spot rate applies from now to two years later. ｜ 两年期即期利率适用于从现在至两年后的期间。

------

###### Forward Rate ｜ 远期利率

A forward interest rate is the interest rate applicable to a future period beginning at one future date and ending at a later future date. ｜ 远期利率是指从未来某一时点开始，到更远的未来时点结束这一期间所适用的利率。

Suppose: ｜ 假设：

- $t$: present time ｜ $t$：当前时点
- $T_1$: beginning of the future interest period ｜ $T_1$：未来计息期的开始时点
- $T_2$: end of the future interest period ｜ $T_2$：未来计息期的结束时点

where: ｜ 其中：
$$
T_2>T_1>t
$$
The forward rate determined at time $t$ for the period from $T_1$ to $T_2$ may be written as: ｜ 在时点 $t$ 确定的、适用于 $T_1$ 至 $T_2$ 期间的远期利率可以表示为：
$$
f(t;T_1,T_2)
$$

------

###### Calculation of a Forward Rate ｜ 远期利率的计算

Under annual compounding, assume: ｜ 在采用年度复利的情况下，假设：

- $r_1$: spot rate for maturity $T_1$ ｜ $r_1$：期限为 $T_1$ 的即期利率
- $r_2$: spot rate for maturity $T_2$ ｜ $r_2$：期限为 $T_2$ 的即期利率
- $f$: forward rate from $T_1$ to $T_2$ ｜ $f$：从 $T_1$ 至 $T_2$ 的远期利率

The no-arbitrage relationship is: ｜ 无套利关系为：
$$
(1+r_1)^{T_1}
(1+f)^{T_2-T_1}
=
(1+r_2)^{T_2}
$$
Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
f
=
\left[
\frac{(1+r_2)^{T_2}}
{(1+r_1)^{T_1}}
\right]^{\frac{1}{T_2-T_1}}
-1
$$

> 原材料中的公式受到OCR损坏，上式根据教材紧随其后的1年期和2年期利率案例整理。

------

###### Example ｜ 例题

Suppose: ｜ 假设：
$$
r_1=5\%
$$
for a one-year spot rate, and: ｜ 一年期即期利率为5%，同时：
$$
r_2=5.5\%
$$
for a two-year spot rate. ｜ 两年期即期利率为5.5%。

The one-year forward rate beginning one year later satisfies: ｜ 一年后开始、期限为一年的远期利率满足：
$$
(1+5\%)(1+f)
=
(1+5.5\%)^2
$$
Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
f
=
\frac{(1.055)^2}{1.05}-1
$$
The source rounds the result to: ｜ 原材料将结果取整为：
$$
f\approx6\%
$$
The meaning is that investing for two years at the two-year spot rate should produce the same result as investing for one year at the one-year spot rate and then reinvesting for another year at the implied forward rate. ｜ 其含义是：按照两年期即期利率投资两年，与先按照一年期即期利率投资一年，再按照隐含远期利率投资第二年，在无套利条件下应当取得相同结果。

------

##### (II) Basic Terms of a Forward Rate Agreement ｜ （二）远期利率协议的基本术语

###### Notional Principal ｜ 协议本金

The notional principal is the nominal amount used to calculate the interest difference. ｜ 协议本金是用于计算利息差额的名义本金额。

It is generally not actually exchanged between the parties. ｜ 交易双方通常不实际交换该本金。

------

###### Agreement Currency ｜ 协议货币

The agreement currency is the currency in which the notional principal and settlement amount are denominated. ｜ 协议货币是协议本金和结算金额所采用的货币币种。

------

###### Trade Date ｜ 交易日

The trade date is the date on which the parties enter into the FRA. ｜ 交易日是交易双方达成远期利率协议的日期。

------

###### Start Date ｜ 起算日

The start date is generally two business days after the trade date. ｜ 起算日通常为交易日后的第二个工作日。

It is the starting point used when measuring the deferral period. ｜ 起算日是计算递延期的起始时点。

------

###### Settlement Date ｜ 结算日

The settlement date is the date on which the notional borrowing period begins. ｜ 结算日是名义借贷开始的日期。

It is also the date on which the parties calculate and pay the interest-difference settlement amount. ｜ 交易双方通常在结算日计算并支付利息差额结算金。

------

###### Fixing Date ｜ 基准日

The fixing date, also called the determination date, is the date on which the applicable reference interest rate is determined. ｜ 基准日又称确定日，是确定参照利率的日期。

The fixing date is generally shortly before the settlement date. ｜ 基准日通常位于结算日之前。

------

###### Maturity Date ｜ 到期日

The maturity date is the date on which the notional borrowing period ends. ｜ 到期日是名义借贷期限结束的日期。

------

###### Agreement Period ｜ 协议期

The agreement period is the period from the settlement date to the maturity date. ｜ 协议期是从结算日至远期利率协议到期日之间的期间。
$$
\text{Agreement period}
=
\text{Maturity date}
-
\text{Settlement date}
$$

------

###### Deferral Period ｜ 递延期

The deferral period is the period from the start date to the settlement date. ｜ 递延期是从起算日至结算日之间的期间。
$$
\text{Deferral period}
=
\text{Settlement date}
-
\text{Start date}
$$

------

###### Contract Rate ｜ 协议利率

The contract rate is the interest rate agreed by the parties when entering into the FRA. ｜ 协议利率是交易双方在签订远期利率协议时约定的利率。

It is generally fixed throughout the contract. ｜ 协议利率通常在合约签订后保持不变。

------

###### Reference Rate ｜ 参照利率

The reference rate is the specified market interest rate used to calculate the settlement amount on the fixing date. ｜ 参照利率是双方约定在基准日确定，并用于计算结算金额的市场利率。

The textbook uses LIBOR and Shibor as examples. ｜ 教材使用LIBOR和Shibor作为参照利率示例。

------

###### Settlement Amount ｜ 结算金

The settlement amount is the amount exchanged between the parties based on the difference between the reference rate and the contract rate. ｜ 结算金是根据参照利率与协议利率之间的差额计算，并由交易双方进行交割的资金。

------

###### Per Annum ｜ 年利率

“PA” means “per annum,” indicating an annual interest rate. ｜ “PA”是“per annum”的缩写，表示年利率。

------

#### FRA Timeline ｜ FRA时间结构

$$
\text{Trade date}
\rightarrow
\text{Start date}
\rightarrow
\text{Fixing date}
\rightarrow
\text{Settlement date}
\rightarrow
\text{Maturity date}
$$

------

##### (III) FRA Quotation Using $M\times N$ ｜ （三）远期利率协议的表示方法

An FRA is commonly expressed as: ｜ 远期利率协议通常表示为：
$$
M\times N
$$
where: ｜ 其中：

- $M$ represents the number of months in the deferral period. ｜ $M$表示递延期的月数。
- $N$ represents the total number of months from the start date to the maturity date. ｜ $N$表示从起算日至协议到期日的总月数。

Therefore, the length of the agreement period is: ｜ 因此，协议期长度为：
$$
N-M
$$
months. ｜ 个月。

------

###### Example: $3\times6$ FRA ｜ 示例：$3\times6$ FRA

A $3\times6$ FRA means: ｜ 一份 $3\times6$ 的远期利率协议表示：

- The notional borrowing begins three months later. ｜ 名义借贷在3个月后开始。
- The agreement ends six months later. ｜ 协议在6个月后结束。
- The notional borrowing period is three months. ｜ 名义借贷期限为3个月。

$$
6-3=3\text{ months}
$$

------

###### Case: $1\times4$ FRA ｜ 案例：$1\times4$ FRA

On 1 June 2021, two financial institutions entered into a $1\times4$ FRA. ｜ 2021年6月1日，两家金融机构成交一份 $1\times4$ 的远期利率协议。

The contract terms were: ｜ 合约条件为：

| Item               | Content        |
| ------------------ | -------------- |
| Trade date         | 1 June 2021    |
| Notional principal | USD 10 million |
| Contract rate      | 0.2%           |
| FRA type           | $1\times4$     |
| Deferral period    | 1 month        |
| Agreement period   | 3 months       |

| 项目     | 内容         |
| -------- | ------------ |
| 交易日   | 2021年6月1日 |
| 名义本金 | 1000万美元   |
| 协议利率 | 0.2%         |
| FRA类型  | $1\times4$   |
| 递延期   | 1个月        |
| 协议期   | 3个月        |

According to the source: ｜ 根据原材料：

- Start date: 3 June 2021 ｜ 起算日：2021年6月3日
- Fixing date: 2 July 2021 ｜ 基准日：2021年7月2日
- Settlement date: 5 July 2021 ｜ 结算日：2021年7月5日
- Maturity date: 5 October 2021 ｜ 到期日：2021年10月5日

The settlement date was moved to 5 July because 3 and 4 July were non-business days. ｜ 由于7月3日和7月4日为非工作日，因此结算日顺延至7月5日。

The reference rate determined on 2 July was used to calculate the settlement amount. ｜ 交易双方根据7月2日确定的参照利率计算结算金额。

------

### Examination Topic: Applications of Forward Rate Agreements ｜ 考点：远期利率协议的应用

#### I. Interest Rate Risk Management ｜ 一、利率风险管理

##### (I) Function ｜ （一）作用

The principal function of an FRA is to manage the risk of future interest-rate changes. ｜ 远期利率协议的主要作用是管理未来利率变动风险。

A hedger can use an FRA to fix a future borrowing or investment rate in advance. ｜ 套期保值者可以通过签订FRA，提前固定未来的借款利率或者投资利率。

For a future borrower: ｜ 对于未来借款人：
$$
\text{Fear interest rates will rise}
\Rightarrow
\text{Buy an FRA}
$$
For a future lender or investor: ｜ 对于未来贷款人或者投资者：
$$
\text{Fear interest rates will fall}
\Rightarrow
\text{Sell an FRA}
$$

------

##### (II) Hedging Case ｜ （二）套期保值案例

On 1 July, financial institution A expected to borrow USD 100 million from bank B three months later. ｜ 7月1日，金融机构甲预计在3个月后向银行乙借入1亿美元。

The borrowing period would be three months, or 92 days. ｜ 借款期限为3个月，即92天。

The current annual interest rate was 0.45%. ｜ 当时市场年利率为0.45%。

Institution A expected that interest rates might rise, increasing its future financing cost. ｜ 金融机构甲预计未来利率可能上升，从而增加利息支出和筹资成本。

It therefore bought a $3\times6$ FRA with: ｜ 因此，金融机构甲买入一份 $3\times6$ 的远期利率协议：

- Notional principal: USD 100 million ｜ 名义本金：1亿美元
- Contract rate: 0.45% ｜ 协议利率：0.45%
- Agreement period: 92 days ｜ 协议期限：92天
- Reference rate: LIBOR ｜ 参照利率：LIBOR

Three months later, the three-month reference rate rose to 0.55%. ｜ 3个月后，3个月期参照利率上涨至0.55%。

Because the market rate exceeded the contract rate, institution A received a settlement payment from the FRA. ｜ 由于市场参照利率高于协议利率，金融机构甲通过FRA获得结算收益。

------

##### (III) Calculation of the FRA Settlement Amount ｜ （三）FRA结算金的计算

Let: ｜ 设：

- $R=0.55\%$: reference rate ｜ $R=0.55\%$：参照利率
- $K=0.45\%$: contract rate ｜ $K=0.45\%$：协议利率
- $L=100{,}000{,}000$: notional principal ｜ $L=100{,}000{,}000$：名义本金
- $D=92$: agreement days ｜ $D=92$：协议天数

Using a 360-day year, the settlement amount is: ｜ 按照一年360天计算，结算金额为：
$$
\text{Settlement amount}
=
\frac{
(R-K)\times L\times\frac{D}{360}
}{
1+R\times\frac{D}{360}
}
$$
Substituting the values: ｜ 代入数据：
$$
=
\frac{
(0.55\%-0.45\%)
\times100{,}000{,}000
\times\frac{92}{360}
}{
1+0.55\%\times\frac{92}{360}
}
$$
Institution A receives USD 25,519.69. ｜ 金融机构甲获得结算金25519.69美元。

The denominator discounts the interest difference from the loan maturity date back to the settlement date. ｜ 公式分母的作用，是将原本对应借款到期日的利息差额折算至结算日。

> 原材料公式中出现“0.459%”的OCR错误，但其案例条件、计算结果和最终固定利率均对应0.45%，因此这里按照0.45%整理。

------

##### (IV) Actual Loan Interest ｜ （四）实际贷款利息

Institution A then borrowed USD 100 million at the market rate of 0.55%. ｜ 金融机构甲随后按照市场利率0.55%借入1亿美元。

The loan interest was: ｜ 贷款利息为：
$$
100{,}000{,}000
\times0.55\%
\times\frac{92}{360}
$$

------

##### (V) Net Interest Cost After the FRA ｜ （五）FRA套保后的净利息成本

The FRA settlement income offsets the increase in loan interest. ｜ FRA结算收益抵销了市场利率上涨造成的额外贷款利息。
$$
\text{Net interest cost}
=
140{,}555.56-25{,}519.69
$$
The annualised effective borrowing rate is approximately: ｜ 实际年化借款利率约为：
$$
\frac{
115{,}035.87
}{
100{,}000{,}000
}
\times
\frac{360}{92}
$$
Therefore, although the market interest rate rose to 0.55%, the FRA effectively fixed institution A’s borrowing cost at approximately 0.45%. ｜ 因此，虽然市场利率上涨至0.55%，但FRA使金融机构甲的实际借款成本基本固定在0.45%。

------

#### II. Investment and Speculative Profit ｜ 二、投资获利

An investor may enter into an FRA even when the investor does not have an existing asset, liability or future borrowing requirement. ｜ 即使投资者并没有已经存在的资产、负债或者未来借款需求，也可以参与FRA交易。

The investor may voluntarily assume interest-rate risk in order to profit from an expected change in market interest rates. ｜ 投资者可以根据对未来市场利率变化的预测，主动承担利率风险，以获取投资收益。

If an investor expects interest rates to rise, the investor may buy an FRA. ｜ 如果投资者预期利率上涨，可以买入FRA。

If the reference rate subsequently exceeds the contract rate, the investor receives a settlement gain. ｜ 如果未来参照利率高于协议利率，投资者可以获得结算收益。

If an investor expects interest rates to fall, the investor may sell an FRA. ｜ 如果投资者预期利率下降，可以卖出FRA。

The difference is that: ｜ 二者的区别在于：

- A hedger uses the FRA to offset an existing or expected interest-rate exposure. ｜ 套期保值者利用FRA抵销已经存在或者预计将出现的利率风险。
- A speculator deliberately creates an interest-rate exposure to seek profit. ｜ 投资者或者投机者主动建立利率风险头寸以追求收益。

------

#### III. China’s Forward Rate Agreement Market ｜ 三、我国远期利率协议市场

According to the historical description in the source, financial forward transactions in China were mainly conducted in the interbank market. ｜ 根据教材中的历史表述，我国金融类远期交易主要在银行间市场进行。

On 8 October 2007, the People’s Bank of China issued regulatory provisions concerning FRA business, with business permitted from 1 November 2007. ｜ 2007年10月8日，中国人民银行发布远期利率协议业务相关管理规定，自2007年11月1日起可以开展相关业务。

The China Foreign Exchange Trade System introduced FRA quotations based on Shibor. ｜ 中国外汇交易中心推出了以上海银行间同业拆放利率Shibor为基准的FRA报价。

The source lists the following products: ｜ 原材料列举的主要品种包括：

- $1M\times4M$
- $2M\times5M$
- $3M\times6M$
- $4M\times7M$
- $5M\times8M$
- $6M\times9M$

The reference interest rate may be: ｜ 参照利率可以由交易双方约定为：

- A benchmark interbank market rate published by an authorised institution ｜ 经授权机构发布的银行间市场基准利率
- A benchmark interest rate published by the People’s Bank of China ｜ 中国人民银行公布的基准利率

The source identifies three-month Shibor as the principal reference rate at the time of writing. ｜ 教材将3个月期Shibor列为编写时的主要参照利率。

> 本部分属于教材中的历史市场制度描述，不代表当前银行间市场的最新产品、基准利率或监管规则。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

📄 **An FRA settles the interest difference between a contract rate and a future reference rate on a notional principal.** ｜ 📄 **远期利率协议按照名义本金，结算协议利率与未来参照利率之间的利息差额。**

💰 **The notional principal is used only for calculation and is generally not actually exchanged.** ｜ 💰 **名义本金通常只用于计算，交易双方并不实际交换本金。**

📈 **The FRA buyer is equivalent to a future borrower and benefits when market interest rates rise.** ｜ 📈 **FRA买方相当于未来借款人，在市场利率上涨时获益。**

📉 **The FRA seller is equivalent to a future lender and benefits when market interest rates fall.** ｜ 📉 **FRA卖方相当于未来贷款人，在市场利率下降时获益。**

🔢 **A $3\times6$ FRA begins three months later and covers a three-month interest period ending six months later.** ｜ 🔢 **$3\times6$ FRA在3个月后开始，协议期为3个月，并在6个月后结束。**

📅 **The principal FRA dates include the trade date, start date, fixing date, settlement date and maturity date.** ｜ 📅 **FRA主要日期包括交易日、起算日、基准日、结算日和到期日。**

🛡️ **A future borrower who fears rising rates can buy an FRA to fix borrowing costs.** ｜ 🛡️ **担心未来利率上涨的借款人，可以买入FRA固定融资成本。**

⚖️ **In the case, the FRA gain offset the higher market borrowing interest and fixed the effective rate near 0.45%.** ｜ ⚖️ **案例中，FRA收益抵销了市场借款利息的增加，使实际利率基本固定在0.45%。**

🎯 **Hedgers offset existing risk, while speculators voluntarily assume interest-rate risk to seek profit.** ｜ 🎯 **套期保值者利用FRA抵销既有风险，投机者则主动承担利率风险以追求收益。**

## Section 4: Foreign Exchange Forwards and Foreign Exchange Swaps ｜ 第四节 外汇远期与外汇掉期

### Examination Topic: Foreign Exchange Forward Transactions ｜ 考点：外汇远期交易

#### I. Definition of a Foreign Exchange Forward ｜ 一、外汇远期的概念

A foreign exchange forward transaction, also called a forward exchange transaction, is an agreement under which two parties exchange specified currencies at an agreed amount and exchange rate on a specified future date. ｜ 外汇远期交易又称期汇交易，是指交易双方约定币种、金额和汇率，在未来某一约定日期进行外汇交割的交易。

The future delivery date is not the spot value date. ｜ 其交割日不是即期起息日，而是未来约定日期。

Foreign exchange forwards may use: ｜ 外汇远期可以采用：

- **Physical delivery**, also called a deliverable forward ｜ **实物交割**，即有本金交割的外汇远期
- **Cash settlement**, also called a non-deliverable forward ｜ **现金差额结算**，即无本金交割的外汇远期。

------

#### II. Applications of Foreign Exchange Forwards ｜ 二、外汇远期的应用

##### (I) Importers and Exporters Hedge Exchange-Rate Risk ｜ （一）进出口商规避汇率风险

An importer that will need to purchase foreign currency in the future is exposed to the risk that the foreign currency may appreciate. ｜ 未来需要购买外汇的进口商，面临外币升值、本币支付成本增加的风险。

The importer may buy the foreign currency forward to lock in the future exchange rate. ｜ 进口商可以通过远期买入外币，提前锁定未来的换汇汇率。
$$
\text{Future foreign-currency payment}
+
\text{Fear foreign currency appreciation}
\Rightarrow
\text{Buy foreign currency forward}
$$
An exporter that expects to receive foreign currency in the future faces the opposite risk and may sell the foreign currency forward. ｜ 未来将收到外汇的出口商则面临外币贬值风险，可以远期卖出外币。

------

##### Importer Hedging Case ｜ 进口商套期保值案例

On 1 May, a mainland Chinese importer signed an automobile import contract with a US customer. ｜ 5月1日，某内地进口商与美国客户签订汽车进口合同。

The contract amount was: ｜ 合同金额为：
$$
10{,}000{,}000\text{美元}
$$
Payment was due three months later. ｜ 货款将在3个月后支付。

At the time of signing, the USD/CNY spot exchange rate was: ｜ 签约时美元兑人民币即期汇率为：
$$
1\text{美元}=6.4509\text{元人民币}
$$
The bank’s three-month forward quotation was: ｜ 银行3个月期美元兑人民币远期报价为：
$$
6.4621/6.5146
$$
Because the importer needed to **buy US dollars from the bank**, it used the bank’s ask rate: ｜ 由于进口商需要向银行**买入美元**，因此使用银行卖出美元的报价：
$$
6.5146
$$
The importer entered into a forward contract to buy USD 10 million three months later at that rate. ｜ 进口商与银行约定，3个月后按照6.5146的汇率买入1000万美元。

The locked renminbi payment was: ｜ 锁定的人民币支付金额为：
$$
10{,}000{,}000\times6.5146
=
65{,}146{,}000\text{元}
$$
Three months later, suppose the spot exchange rate rose to: ｜ 假设3个月后即期汇率上涨至：
$$
1\text{美元}=6.5513\text{元人民币}
$$
Without the forward hedge, the importer would have paid: ｜ 如果没有远期套期保值，进口商需要支付：
$$
10{,}000{,}000\times6.5513
=
65{,}513{,}000\text{元}
$$
The forward hedge reduced the payment by: ｜ 远期套保减少的人民币支出为：
$$
6551.3-6514.6
=
36.7\text{万元}
$$
Therefore, the importer avoided the increase in purchasing cost caused by the appreciation of the US dollar. ｜ 因此，进口商避免了美元升值造成的换汇成本增加。

> The purpose of hedging is to lock in the exchange rate rather than guarantee that the forward rate will be better than every possible future spot rate. ｜ 套期保值的目的在于锁定汇率，而不是保证远期汇率一定优于未来所有可能的即期汇率。

------

##### (II) Short-Term Investors and Foreign-Currency Debtors ｜ （二）短期投资者和外汇债务承担者

A short-term international investor may earn a higher interest rate by investing in another country, but the investment return may be reduced or eliminated by adverse exchange-rate movements. ｜ 短期国际投资者可能通过投资于高利率国家获取利差收益，但不利的汇率变化可能抵销利息收益，甚至造成损失。

The investor may sell the future foreign-currency proceeds forward to lock in the conversion rate. ｜ 投资者可以远期卖出未来取得的外币资金，锁定换回本币时的汇率。

A party with a future foreign-currency liability may buy the required foreign currency forward. ｜ 承担未来外币债务的企业，可以远期买入所需外币。

This prevents the amount of domestic currency required for repayment from increasing because of foreign-currency appreciation. ｜ 这样可以防止外币升值导致债务到期时需要支付更多本币。

------

#### III. Renminbi Foreign Exchange Forward Market ｜ 三、我国人民币外汇远期交易

According to the historical description in the textbook: ｜ 根据教材中的历史资料：

- In April 1997, the Bank of China introduced renminbi forward purchase and sale of foreign exchange. ｜ 1997年4月，中国银行推出人民币远期结售汇业务。
- On 10 August 2005, renminbi foreign exchange forward trading was introduced in China’s interbank foreign exchange market. ｜ 2005年8月10日，我国银行间外汇市场推出人民币外汇远期交易。
- The market adopted an inquiry-based trading model and introduced market makers. ｜ 市场采用询价交易模式，并引入做市商制度。
- On 3 May 2016, the China Foreign Exchange Trade System introduced standardised renminbi foreign exchange forward trading. ｜ 2016年5月3日，中国外汇交易中心在外汇交易系统中推出标准化人民币外汇远期交易。

The source lists standard terms including one day, one week, one month, three months, one year and longer maturities. ｜ 原材料列出的标准期限包括1天、1周、1个月、3个月、1年以及更长期限。

> These are historical market descriptions from the textbook and should not be treated as a complete statement of current trading rules or available products. ｜ 以上属于教材中的历史市场资料，不应直接视为当前完整的交易规则和产品范围。

------

### Examination Topic: Non-Deliverable Foreign Exchange Forwards ｜ 考点：无本金交割外汇远期交易

#### I. Meaning of a Non-Deliverable Forward ｜ 一、无本金交割外汇远期的含义

A Non-Deliverable Forward, abbreviated as NDF, is a type of foreign exchange forward in which one of the currencies is generally restricted or non-convertible. ｜ 无本金交割外汇远期简称NDF，是一种外汇远期交易，其中一方货币通常属于受限制或者不可自由兑换货币。

An NDF is generally: ｜ NDF通常具有以下特点：

- An over-the-counter derivative ｜ 属于场外金融衍生工具
- Traded in an offshore market ｜ 通常在离岸市场交易
- Intermediated by banks ｜ 通常由银行充当交易中介

The parties do not exchange the restricted currency principal. ｜ 交易双方不实际交割受限制货币的本金。

Instead, they calculate the difference between the agreed forward rate and the fixing-date reference rate and settle the difference in a freely convertible currency. ｜ 双方根据约定远期汇率与定价日参考汇率之间的差额计算盈亏，并使用可自由兑换货币进行差额结算。

The principal difference between an NDF and a traditional deliverable forward is therefore: ｜ NDF与传统有本金交割远期的主要区别是：
$$
\text{No exchange of currency principal}
$$
Early NDF markets were mainly associated with currencies subject to foreign-exchange controls. 

------

#### II. Renminbi NDF Case ｜ 二、人民币NDF案例

An importer wanted to hedge the exchange-rate risk associated with purchasing USD 10 million three months later. ｜ 某进口商为了规避3个月后购买1000万美元的汇率风险，与银行签订人民币NDF。

The agreed NDF rate was: ｜ 约定汇率为：
$$
K=6.4660
$$
Suppose the fixing rate three months later was: ｜ 假设3个月后定价汇率为：
$$
S=6.4810
$$
Because the US dollar had appreciated relative to the renminbi, the importer’s NDF position generated a profit. ｜ 由于美元相对于人民币升值，进口商的NDF头寸产生盈利。

The US-dollar settlement amount is calculated as: ｜ 以美元表示的结算金额为：
$$
\text{Settlement amount}
=
\frac{(S-K)\times N}{S}
$$
where: ｜ 其中：

- $S$: fixing-date USD/CNY rate ｜ $S$：定价日美元兑人民币汇率
- $K$: contractual NDF rate ｜ $K$：NDF协议汇率
- $N$: USD notional principal ｜ $N$：美元名义本金

Substituting the values: ｜ 代入数据：
$$
\text{Settlement amount}
=
\frac{
(6.4810-6.4660)
\times10{,}000{,}000
}{
6.4810
}
$$
The bank therefore pays the importer approximately USD 23,144.58. ｜ 因此，银行应当向进口商支付约23144.58美元。

Under a deliverable forward, the importer would instead pay RMB 64.66 million and receive the full USD 10 million principal. ｜ 如果采用有本金交割的外汇远期，进口商则需要支付人民币6466万元，并实际取得1000万美元本金。

Under the NDF, only the difference is settled. ｜ 采用NDF时，双方只支付差额，不交换1000万美元本金。

> The OCR formula shows USD 1 million, but the case states USD 10 million and the reported result of USD 23,144.58 is consistent with USD 10 million.  ｜ OCR公式中显示为100万美元，但案例本金为1000万美元，且23144.58美元的结果也是按照1000万美元计算得到的。

------

### Examination Topic: Synthetic Agreement for Forward Exchange ｜ 考点：远期外汇综合协议

#### I. Concept of a SAFE ｜ 一、远期外汇综合协议的概念

A Synthetic Agreement for Forward Exchange, abbreviated as SAFE, combines two forward foreign exchange transactions with different dates. ｜ 远期外汇综合协议简称SAFE，是由两个不同日期的远期外汇交易组合形成的协议。

The buyer agrees to: ｜ SAFE买方约定：

- Buy a specified notional amount of the original currency on the settlement date at the contractual settlement-date forward rate; ｜ 在结算日，按照合约约定的结算日远期汇率，用第二货币买入一定名义金额的原货币；
- Sell the same or a corresponding amount of the original currency on the maturity date at the contractual maturity-date forward rate. ｜ 在到期日，按照合约约定的到期日远期汇率，将原货币卖回给交易对手。

However, the parties do not actually exchange the currency principal on either date. ｜ 但是，交易双方在结算日和到期日均不实际交换外汇本金。

Instead, they settle the differences between: ｜ 双方根据以下汇率之间的差额进行现金结算：

- The contractual exchange rates ｜ 合约约定汇率
- The relevant market exchange rates on the fixing date ｜ 基准日的相关市场汇率

The notional reverse transaction for the maturity date is also settled on the settlement date. ｜ 原本应当在到期日进行的反向交易，也在结算日提前完成差额结算。

Therefore, the parties’ contractual rights and obligations effectively end on the settlement date rather than the stated maturity date. ｜ 因此，SAFE的实际结束时间是结算日，而不是名义到期日。

The amount of the original currency used in the two notional exchanges is generally the same. ｜ 前后两次名义兑换中的原货币金额通常相同。

| Party       | Settlement-date direction | Maturity-date direction |
| ----------- | ------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| SAFE buyer  | Buys original currency    | Sells original currency |
| SAFE seller | Sells original currency   | Buys original currency  |

| 交易方   | 结算日名义交易 | 到期日名义交易 |
| -------- | -------------- | -------------- |
| SAFE买方 | 买入原货币     | 卖出原货币     |
| SAFE卖方 | 卖出原货币     | 买入原货币     |

The supplied material provides the concept and transaction direction but does not provide a complete SAFE settlement formula or numerical example. 

------

### Examination Topic: Foreign Exchange Swap Transactions ｜ 考点：外汇掉期交易

#### I. Definition of a Foreign Exchange Swap ｜ 一、外汇掉期交易的定义

##### (I) Definition ｜ （一）定义

A foreign exchange swap, also called a currency swap in the source’s terminology, is an agreement involving two opposite exchanges of currencies on two different value dates. ｜ 外汇掉期是指交易双方约定，在前后两个不同的起息日，按照约定汇率进行方向相反的两次货币交换。

In the first exchange: ｜ 第一次货币交换中：

- One party exchanges currency A for currency B. ｜ 一方以货币A交换货币B。

In the second exchange: ｜ 第二次货币交换中：

- The parties reverse the transaction and exchange currency B back for currency A. ｜ 双方进行反向交易，以货币B交换货币A。

The two exchanges generally: ｜ 两次交易通常具有：

- The same currency pair ｜ 相同的货币对
- The same principal amount ｜ 相同的本金规模
- Opposite transaction directions ｜ 相反的交易方向
- Different value dates ｜ 不同的起息日

A foreign exchange swap is therefore essentially a combination of two foreign exchange transactions with different delivery dates. ｜ 因此，外汇掉期本质上是两笔交割期限不同、交易方向相反的外汇交易组合。

------

##### (II) Case ｜ （二）案例

Party A and bank B entered into a EUR/USD foreign exchange swap. ｜ 甲与银行乙进行欧元兑美元外汇掉期交易。

The spot exchange rate was: ｜ 即期汇率为：
$$
1\text{欧元}=1.1120\text{美元}
$$
The three-month forward rate was: ｜ 3个月远期汇率为：
$$
1\text{欧元}=1.1140\text{美元}
$$

###### Near-End Exchange ｜ 近端交易

At the spot date, party A sold USD 10 million to bank B and bought euros. ｜ 即期交易中，甲向银行乙卖出1000万美元并买入欧元。

The amount of euros received was: ｜ 甲收到的欧元为：
$$
\frac{10{,}000{,}000}{1.1120}
=
8{,}992{,}805.76\text{欧元}
$$

###### Far-End Exchange ｜ 远端交易

Three months later, bank B returned USD 10 million to party A. ｜ 3个月后，银行乙向甲支付1000万美元。

Party A paid euros at the forward rate of 1.1140: ｜ 甲按照1.1140的远期汇率支付欧元：
$$
\frac{10{,}000{,}000}{1.1140}
=
8{,}976{,}660.68\text{欧元}
$$
The difference between the two euro principal amounts was: ｜ 两次欧元本金金额的差额为：
$$
8{,}992{,}805.76-8{,}976{,}660.68
$$
This difference reflects the effect of the spot and forward exchange rates; it should not automatically be interpreted as the overall economic profit without considering funding and interest costs. ｜ 这一差额反映了即期汇率和远期汇率的差异；如果不考虑资金占用和利息成本，不能直接将其理解为完整的经济利润。

------

#### II. Types of Foreign Exchange Swaps ｜ 二、外汇掉期的类型

##### (I) Spot-Forward Swap ｜ （一）即期对远期掉期

In a spot-forward swap, a trader simultaneously: ｜ 即期对远期掉期中，交易者同时进行：

- Buys a currency spot and sells the same currency forward; or ｜ 买入即期外汇，同时卖出相同币种和金额的远期外汇；
- Sells a currency spot and buys the same currency forward. ｜ 卖出即期外汇，同时买入相同币种和金额的远期外汇。

The counterparty takes the opposite positions. ｜ 交易对手的交易方向与之相反。

------

##### (II) Forward-Forward Swap ｜ （二）远期对远期掉期

A forward-forward swap combines two forward transactions with: ｜ 远期对远期掉期包括两笔：

- The same currency and amount ｜ 币种和金额相同
- Opposite directions ｜ 交易方向相反
- Different delivery dates ｜ 交割日期不同
