For example, a trader may buy a three-month forward and sell a six-month forward. ｜ 例如，交易者可以买入3个月期远期外汇，同时卖出6个月期远期外汇。

The reverse structure is also possible. ｜ 也可以卖出较短期限远期，同时买入较长期限远期。

------

##### (III) Overnight Swap Transactions ｜ （三）隔夜掉期交易

###### O/N: Over-Night ｜ O/N：隔夜掉期

The near-end value date is today $T$, and the far-end value date is the next trading day $T+1$. ｜ 近端起息日为当天 $T$，远端起息日为下一交易日 $T+1$。

The trader may: ｜ 交易者可以：

- Buy the currency today and sell it for the next trading day; or ｜ 买入当天到期的外汇，卖出下一交易日到期的外汇；
- Sell the currency today and buy it for the next trading day. ｜ 卖出当天到期的外汇，买入下一交易日到期的外汇。

###### T/N: Tomorrow-Next ｜ T/N：明日次日掉期

The near-end value date is $T+1$, and the far-end value date is $T+2$. ｜ 近端起息日为 $T+1$，远端起息日为 $T+2$。

###### S/N: Spot-Next ｜ S/N：即期次日掉期

The near-end value date is the spot date $T+2$, and the far-end value date is $T+3$. ｜ 近端起息日为即期日 $T+2$，远端起息日为 $T+3$。

| Term | Full name      | Near-end value date | Far-end value date   |
| ---- | -------------- | ------------------- | -------------------- |
| O/N  | Over-Night     | $T$                 | $T+1$                |
| T/N  | Tomorrow-Next  | $T+1$               | $T+2$                |
| S/N  | Spot-Next      | $T+2$               | $T+3$                |
| 1W   | Spot–One Week  | $T+2$               | One week after spot  |
| 1M   | Spot–One Month | $T+2$               | One month after spot |
| 1Y   | Spot–One Year  | $T+2$               | One year after spot  |

| 期限 | 英文全称       | 近端起息日 | 远端起息日     |
| ---- | -------------- | ---------- | -------------- |
| O/N  | Over-Night     | $T$        | $T+1$          |
| T/N  | Tomorrow-Next  | $T+1$      | $T+2$          |
| S/N  | Spot-Next      | $T+2$      | $T+3$          |
| 1W   | Spot–One Week  | $T+2$      | 即期日后一周   |
| 1M   | Spot–One Month | $T+2$      | 即期日后一个月 |
| 1Y   | Spot–One Year  | $T+2$      | 即期日后一年   |

The OCR strings “07N” and “7/N” in the source correspond to O/N and T/N. 

------

#### III. Foreign Exchange Swap Quotations ｜ 三、外汇掉期的报价

A foreign exchange swap quotation combines: ｜ 外汇掉期报价由以下两部分构成：

- The spot exchange rate ｜ 即期汇率
- The near-end and far-end swap points ｜ 近端和远端掉期点

##### (I) Initiating Party Buys Near and Sells Far ｜ （一）发起方先买入、后卖出

The initiating party buys the base currency at the near end and sells it at the far end. ｜ 发起方在近端买入基准货币，在远端卖出基准货币。

According to the source’s quotation method: ｜ 根据原材料的报价方法：
$$
\text{Near outright rate}
=
\text{Spot ask}
+
\text{Near swap-point ask}
$$

------

##### (II) Initiating Party Sells Near and Buys Far ｜ （二）发起方先卖出、后买入

$$
\text{Near outright rate}
=
\text{Spot bid}
+
\text{Near swap-point bid}
$$

------

##### (III) Quotation Case ｜ （三）报价案例

For a $1M/2M$ USD/CNY swap, the bank quoted: ｜ 一笔 $1M/2M$ 美元兑人民币掉期交易中，银行报价为：
$$
\text{Spot rate}
=
6.4340/6.4343
$$
Here: ｜ 其中：
$$
1\text{ bp}=0.0001
$$

------

###### Buy Near, Sell Far ｜ 先买入、后卖出

Near-end outright rate: ｜ 近端掉期全价为：
$$
6.4343+0.004523
=
6.438823
$$
Far-end outright rate: ｜ 远端掉期全价为：
$$
6.4343+0.005515
=
6.439815
$$
Net swap points: ｜ 净掉期点为：
$$
55.15-45.23
=
9.92\text{ bp}
$$

------

###### Sell Near, Buy Far ｜ 先卖出、后买入

Near-end outright rate: ｜ 近端掉期全价为：
$$
6.4340+0.004001
=
6.438001
$$
Far-end outright rate: ｜ 远端掉期全价为：
$$
6.4340+0.006015
=
6.440015
$$
Net swap points: ｜ 净掉期点为：
$$
60.15-40.01
=
20.14\text{ bp}
$$
These calculations follow the quotation sides used in the textbook example. 

------

#### IV. Suitable Uses of Foreign Exchange Swaps ｜ 四、外汇掉期的适用情形

##### (I) Hedging Currency Depreciation Risk ｜ （一）对冲货币贬值风险

Suppose a trader currently needs to exchange currency A for currency B but will need to exchange currency B back into currency A at a future date. ｜ 假设交易者当前需要将货币A兑换为货币B，但未来又需要将货币B换回货币A。

The trader is exposed to the risk that currency B may depreciate during the holding period. ｜ 交易者面临货币B在持有期间贬值的风险。

A foreign exchange swap fixes both the initial exchange and the reverse exchange, thereby reducing this risk. ｜ 外汇掉期可以同时锁定近端兑换和远端反向兑换，从而降低货币B贬值风险。

------

##### (II) Adjusting the Maturity Structure of Funds ｜ （二）调整资金期限结构

The maturity structure of funds refers to the timing distribution of foreign-currency receipts and payments. ｜ 资金期限结构是指企业或者金融机构外汇收入与支出的期限分布。

When receipts and payments do not occur at matching dates, an institution may use an FX swap to: ｜ 当外汇收付时间不匹配时，可以通过外汇掉期：

- Convert spot foreign exchange into forward foreign exchange; ｜ 将即期外汇转换为远期外汇；
- Convert forward foreign exchange into spot foreign exchange; or ｜ 将远期外汇转换为即期外汇；
- Shorten or extend the original maturity. ｜ 缩短或者延长原有资金期限。

The objective is to align the timing of foreign-currency receipts and payments. ｜ 其目的是使外汇收入与支出的时间相互匹配。

------

##### Bank Sterling Example ｜ 银行英镑案例

Suppose a bank purchases GBP 1 million from a customer for delivery six months later. ｜ 假设银行从客户处买入一笔6个月后交割的100万英镑远期。

The bank is exposed to the risk that sterling may depreciate before delivery. ｜ 银行面临未来收到英镑时英镑贬值的风险。

One method is to sell a six-month sterling forward directly to another bank. ｜ 一种方法是直接向其他银行卖出6个月期远期英镑。

However, a one-directional forward transaction may increase the counterparty bank’s open-position risk. ｜ 但是，单方向远期交易可能增加交易对手银行的敞口风险，因此不一定容易找到交易对手。

An alternative is: ｜ 另一种操作是：

- Sell GBP 1 million spot in the interbank market; ｜ 在同业市场卖出100万英镑即期；
- Simultaneously enter into a swap that buys sterling at the near end and sells GBP 1 million six months forward. ｜ 同时进行近端买入、远端卖出100万英镑的掉期交易。

This structure both hedges sterling depreciation risk and adjusts the bank’s mismatched sterling maturity structure. ｜ 该结构既可以规避英镑贬值风险，也可以调整银行英镑资金的期限错配。

Because an FX swap contains two opposite transactions, it generally creates less directional position exposure for the counterparty than a one-sided forward trade. 

------

#### V. Foreign Exchange Swap Application Case ｜ 五、外汇掉期交易应用案例

A German trading company had the following US-dollar cash flows: ｜ 某德国贸易公司面临以下美元收支：

- Pay USD 800,000 one month later; ｜ 1个月后支付80万美元；
- Receive USD 1 million three months later. ｜ 3个月后收到100万美元。

The company wanted to manage the exchange-rate risk arising from the mismatch between the two cash-flow dates. ｜ 该公司希望管理两笔美元收支时间不匹配所产生的汇率风险。

The financial institution quoted: ｜ 金融机构报价为：
$$
\text{EUR/USD spot}
=
1.1888/1.1905
$$

------

##### (I) One-Month Transaction ｜ （一）1个月期交易

The company needed to buy USD 800,000 one month later. ｜ 公司1个月后需要买入80万美元。

According to the textbook calculation, the one-month outright rate was: ｜ 按照教材计算，1个月期远期全价为：
$$
1.1888+0.0182
=
1.2070
$$
The euros required to buy USD 800,000 were: ｜ 购买80万美元需要支付的欧元为：
$$
\frac{800{,}000}{1.2070}
$$

------

##### (II) Three-Month Reverse Transaction ｜ （二）3个月期反向交易

Three months later, the company would receive USD 1 million. ｜ 3个月后，公司将收到100万美元。

The source constructs the swap for USD 800,000, matching the earlier payment amount. ｜ 原材料只对其中80万美元构建掉期，与1个月后的支付金额相匹配。

According to the textbook, the three-month outright rate was: ｜ 按照教材计算，3个月期远端全价为：
$$
1.1888+0.0122
=
1.2010
$$
The euros obtained by selling USD 800,000 were: ｜ 卖出80万美元得到的欧元为：
$$
\frac{800{,}000}{1.2010}
$$
The difference between the two euro amounts was: ｜ 两次兑换欧元金额之差为：
$$
66.6112-66.28
=
0.3312\text{万欧元}
$$
Through the swap, the company fixed the exchange rates for the USD 800,000 payment and subsequent USD 800,000 receipt. ｜ 通过该外汇掉期，公司锁定了80万美元支出和后续80万美元收入所适用的汇率。

The remaining USD 200,000 of the expected USD 1 million receipt is not included in the textbook’s swap calculation. ｜ 预期收到的100万美元中，剩余20万美元没有纳入教材的掉期计算。

------

##### (III) Results Without the Swap ｜ （三）未进行掉期时的可能结果

If the future EUR/USD spot quotation were: ｜ 如果未来欧元兑美元即期报价为：
$$
1.1710/1.1780
$$
the source calculates the euros obtained from USD 800,000 as: ｜ 按照原材料，80万美元可以兑换：
$$
\frac{800{,}000}{1.1780}
\approx679{,}117\text{欧元}
$$
If the future quotation were: ｜ 如果未来报价为：
$$
1.2110/1.2190
$$
the euros obtained would be: ｜ 80万美元可以兑换：
$$
\frac{800{,}000}{1.2190}
\approx656{,}276\text{欧元}
$$
The source writes “65.628万美元,” but the correct unit in the context is “万欧元.”

原材料写作“65.628万美元”，但根据计算对象，单位应为“万欧元”。

> The textbook’s selection of bid and ask sides in the swap-rate calculation is not fully clear and may be internally inconsistent. The figures above preserve the calculation method shown in the source rather than replacing it with an independently reconstructed quotation.  ｜ 教材在该案例中对买价、卖价的选择并不完全清晰，可能存在内部不一致。以上保留教材显示的计算方式，没有自行替换为另一套报价结果。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

💱 **A foreign exchange forward locks in the currency amount and exchange rate for a future delivery date.** ｜ 💱 **外汇远期用于锁定未来交割的币种、金额和汇率。**

📈 **A future foreign-currency payer buys the currency forward to hedge against appreciation.** ｜ 📈 **未来需要支付外币的一方，可以远期买入外币，对冲外币升值风险。**

📉 **A future foreign-currency recipient sells the currency forward to hedge against depreciation.** ｜ 📉 **未来将收到外币的一方，可以远期卖出外币，对冲外币贬值风险。**

🚫 **An NDF does not exchange currency principal and settles only the exchange-rate difference.** ｜ 🚫 **NDF不交换货币本金，只结算约定汇率与参考汇率之间的差额。**

🔁 **A SAFE combines two notional forward transactions, with both differences settled on the settlement date.** ｜ 🔁 **SAFE由两笔名义远期交易组成，相关差额均在结算日完成结算。**

🔄 **An FX swap combines two currency transactions with opposite directions and different value dates.** ｜ 🔄 **外汇掉期由两笔方向相反、起息日不同的货币交易组成。**

🌙 **O/N, T/N and S/N represent overnight, tomorrow-next and spot-next swaps.** ｜ 🌙 **O/N、T/N和S/N分别表示隔夜、明日次日和即期次日掉期。**

📊 **FX swap outright rates are calculated by combining the spot exchange rate with near-end and far-end swap points.** ｜ 📊 **外汇掉期全价由即期汇率与近端、远端掉期点组合计算。**

🛡️ **FX swaps can hedge currency depreciation risk and adjust mismatched foreign-currency cash-flow maturities.** ｜ 🛡️ **外汇掉期可以规避货币贬值风险，并调整外汇收支期限错配。**

# Chapter 11: Swaps ｜ 第十一章 互换

## Section 3: Currency Swaps ｜ 第三节 货币互换

### Examination Topic: Overview of Currency Swaps ｜ 考点：货币互换概述

#### I. Definition ｜ 一、含义

A currency swap is a transaction in which two parties exchange agreed amounts of principal in two different currencies during a specified period and periodically exchange interest payments denominated in those currencies. ｜ 货币互换是指交易双方在约定期限内，交换约定数量的两种货币本金，并定期交换两种货币利息的交易。

A typical currency swap therefore includes: ｜ 典型货币互换通常包括：

- An initial exchange of principal ｜ 期初本金交换
- Periodic exchanges of interest ｜ 持有期内定期交换利息
- A reverse exchange of principal at maturity ｜ 到期时反向交换本金

Currency swaps can simultaneously change: ｜ 货币互换可以同时转换：

- The currency of an asset or liability ｜ 资产或负债的币种
- The interest-payment structure ｜ 利息支付结构
- The exchange-rate exposure ｜ 汇率风险敞口

------

#### II. Forms of Currency Swaps ｜ 二、货币互换的形式

##### (I) Forms of Interest Exchange ｜ （一）利息交换形式

###### Fixed-for-Fixed ｜ 固定利率换固定利率

Both parties pay interest at predetermined fixed rates, but the interest payments are denominated in different currencies. ｜ 双方均按照预先确定的固定利率支付利息，但支付的利息分别以不同货币计价。

For example: ｜ 例如：
$$
\text{Fixed USD interest}
\longleftrightarrow
\text{Fixed AUD interest}
$$

###### Fixed-for-Floating ｜ 固定利率换浮动利率

One party pays interest at a fixed rate, while the other pays interest at a floating rate in another currency. ｜ 一方按照固定利率支付利息，另一方按照另一种货币的浮动利率支付利息。
$$
\text{Fixed interest in currency A}
\longleftrightarrow
\text{Floating interest in currency B}
$$

> 原材料中的编号从“1”直接跳到“3”，说明中间内容可能缺失。这里仅整理原文能够确认的“固定换固定”和“固定换浮动”，不自行补入缺失类别。

------

##### (II) Forms of Principal Exchange ｜ （二）本金交换形式

According to the forms described in the source, principal exchange may occur in the following ways. ｜ 根据原材料，货币互换的本金可以采用以下交换方式。

###### Principal Exchanged at Both Inception and Maturity ｜ 期初和期末均交换本金

On the effective date, the parties exchange the two currency principals at an agreed exchange rate. ｜ 在协议生效日，双方按照约定汇率交换两种货币本金。

On the termination date, they reverse the exchange using the same exchange rate and the same principal amounts. ｜ 在协议终止日，双方再按照相同汇率和相同本金金额进行一次反向交换。
$$
\text{Initial exchange}
\rightarrow
\text{Periodic interest exchanges}
\rightarrow
\text{Reverse principal exchange}
$$

###### No Actual Principal Exchange ｜ 期初和期末均不实际交换本金

The principal amounts are used only as notional amounts for calculating interest payments. ｜ 本金仅作为计算利息现金流的名义金额，双方不实际交换本金。

###### Principal Exchanged Only Once ｜ 仅在期初或期末交换一次本金

The parties may exchange the two currency principals only on the effective date or only on the termination date. ｜ 交易双方也可以只在协议生效日或者只在协议终止日进行一次本金交换。

Other forms may be permitted under applicable market rules. ｜ 主管部门或者市场规则还可能规定其他本金交换形式。

------

#### III. Determining the Direction of a Currency Swap ｜ 三、货币互换交易方向的确定

##### (I) Renminbi–Foreign Currency Swap ｜ （一）人民币与外币货币互换

The direction is determined according to the exchange of foreign-currency principal. ｜ 交易方向按照外币本金的交换方向确定。

###### Buyer or Long Position ｜ 买方或互换多头

The party that: ｜ 买方是指：

- Receives foreign currency at inception ｜ 期初收入外币
- Returns or pays foreign currency at maturity ｜ 期末支付外币

During the life of the swap, the buyer: ｜ 互换存续期间，买方：

- Pays interest in the foreign currency ｜ 支付外币利息
- Receives interest in renminbi ｜ 收取人民币利息

###### Seller or Short Position ｜ 卖方或互换空头

The party that: ｜ 卖方是指：

- Pays foreign currency at inception ｜ 期初支付外币
- Receives foreign currency at maturity ｜ 期末收入外币

During the life of the swap, the seller: ｜ 互换存续期间，卖方：

- Receives foreign-currency interest ｜ 收取外币利息
- Pays renminbi interest ｜ 支付人民币利息

| Position | Initial principal         | Final principal           | Interest cash flows                             |
| -------- | ------------------------- | ------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- |
| Buyer    | Receives foreign currency | Pays foreign currency     | Pays foreign interest and receives RMB interest |
| Seller   | Pays foreign currency     | Receives foreign currency | Receives foreign interest and pays RMB interest |

| 头寸 | 期初本金 | 期末本金 | 利息现金流                   |
| ---- | -------- | -------- | ---------------------------- |
| 买方 | 收入外币 | 支付外币 | 支付外币利息、收取人民币利息 |
| 卖方 | 支付外币 | 收入外币 | 收取外币利息、支付人民币利息 |

------

##### (II) Swap Between Two Foreign Currencies ｜ （二）两个外币之间的货币互换

For a currency pair, the transaction direction is determined according to the base currency. ｜ 对于外币货币对，交易方向按照基准货币确定。

###### Buyer or Long Position ｜ 买方或互换多头

The buyer: ｜ 买方：

- Receives the base currency at inception ｜ 期初收入基准货币
- Pays the base currency at maturity ｜ 期末支付基准货币
- Pays interest in the base currency ｜ 支付基准货币利息
- Receives interest in the quote currency ｜ 收取计价货币利息

###### Seller or Short Position ｜ 卖方或互换空头

The seller: ｜ 卖方：

- Pays the base currency at inception ｜ 期初支付基准货币
- Receives the base currency at maturity ｜ 期末收入基准货币
- Receives interest in the base currency ｜ 收取基准货币利息
- Pays interest in the quote currency ｜ 支付计价货币利息

------

#### IV. Conditions for a Currency Swap ｜ 四、货币互换的条件

##### (I) Both Parties Have Borrowing Requirements ｜ （一）双方均有借款需求

A currency swap may be established when: ｜ 在双方都有借款需求时，通常需要满足：

- The parties need to borrow different currencies. ｜ 双方分别需要借入不同币种的资金。
- The borrowing amounts and maturities are substantially matched. ｜ 双方借款规模和期限基本匹配。
- The parties have comparative advantages in different currency markets or face different institutional borrowing barriers. ｜ 双方在不同货币借贷市场中具有比较优势，或者面临不同的制度性借款障碍。
- Each party needs funding in the market where it has a relative disadvantage or institutional restriction. ｜ 双方恰好需要在自己处于相对劣势或者存在制度障碍的市场融资。

Under this structure, each party first borrows in the market in which it has a comparative advantage and then obtains the required currency through the swap. ｜ 在这种结构下，双方先在各自具有比较优势的市场融资，再通过货币互换取得真正需要的币种。

The swap may: ｜ 货币互换可以：

- Produce credit arbitrage or institutional arbitrage benefits ｜ 实现信用套利或者制度套汇
- Reduce total financing costs ｜ 降低双方融资总成本
- Manage exchange-rate risk ｜ 管理汇率风险

------

##### (II) Parties Already Hold Assets or Liabilities ｜ （二）双方已经持有资产或负债

A currency swap may also be used when: ｜ 货币互换还可以用于以下情形：

- The parties hold assets or liabilities denominated in different currencies. ｜ 双方持有不同币种的资产或者负债。
- They are dissatisfied with the currency or cash-flow characteristics of those positions. ｜ 双方对现有资产或负债的币种及现金流性质不满意。
- They want to exchange their existing cash-flow structures. ｜ 双方希望交换现有现金流结构。
- The amounts and maturities of the assets or liabilities are substantially matched. ｜ 双方资产或负债的规模和期限基本匹配。

Through the swap, the parties can convert the form of their assets or liabilities and manage exchange-rate risk. ｜ 通过互换，双方可以转换资产或负债的币种及现金流形式，并管理汇率风险。

> 原材料中“不同币种资产或负债持有人”等文字存在OCR损坏。根据上下文，核心条件是双方现有资产或负债的币种、规模和期限能够相互匹配。

------

#### V. Purposes of Currency Swaps ｜ 五、货币互换的目的

##### (I) Reduce Financing Costs or Increase Asset Returns ｜ （一）降低融资成本或提高资产收益

Parties can borrow in markets where they have comparative advantages and use a swap to obtain the currency they actually require. ｜ 交易双方可以在各自具有比较优势的市场融资，再通过货币互换取得实际需要的货币。

This may lower total borrowing costs. ｜ 这种安排可能降低融资总成本。

##### (II) Manage Exchange-Rate and Interest-Rate Risks ｜ （二）管理汇率风险或同时管理利率风险

A currency swap can fix the exchange relationship between two currencies and change the interest-payment structure. ｜ 货币互换可以锁定两种货币之间的兑换关系，并改变利息支付形式。

It may therefore manage both exchange-rate and interest-rate exposures. ｜ 因此，货币互换可以同时管理汇率风险和利率风险。

##### (III) Avoid Institutional Foreign-Exchange Restrictions ｜ （三）规避外汇管制或制度性融资障碍

When an enterprise cannot directly obtain funding in a particular currency or faces high financing costs, it may borrow another currency and use a swap. ｜ 当企业难以直接获得某种货币融资，或者该币种融资成本较高时，可以先借入另一种货币，再通过互换取得所需货币。

##### (IV) Construct Structured Financial Products ｜ （四）构建结构化产品

Currency swaps may be combined with loans, bonds and other derivatives to create products with customised currency and interest cash flows. ｜ 货币互换可以与贷款、债券和其他衍生工具组合，构造具有特定币种及利息现金流的结构化产品。

------

### Examination Topic: Application of Currency Swaps ｜ 考点：货币互换的应用

#### I. Background of the Case ｜ 一、案例背景

A Chinese jewellery-processing company wanted to invest AUD 5 million to open stores in Australia. ｜ 国内某宝石加工企业准备投入500万澳元，在澳大利亚开设精品店。

Its business income mainly consisted of US dollars, euros and a small amount of Australian dollars. ｜ 该企业的收入主要由美元、欧元和少量澳元构成。

It therefore needed Australian-dollar funding but faced exchange-rate and financing-cost risks. ｜ 该企业需要澳元资金，但同时面临澳元汇率风险和较高融资成本。

Another Chinese equipment-manufacturing company needed USD 4.5 million to establish a factory in the United States. ｜ 另一家设备制造企业需要450万美元，在美国开设分厂。

Its products were mainly sold in Australia, so it had substantial Australian-dollar income. ｜ 该企业的产品主要销往澳大利亚，因此拥有较多澳元收入。

------

#### II. Borrowing Costs ｜ 二、双方借款成本

| Currency | Jewellery company | Equipment company | Cost advantage of jewellery company |
| -------- | ----------------- | ----------------- | ----------------------------------- |
| USD loan | 5.5%              | 7.6%              | 2.1%                                |
| AUD loan | 8.5%              | 9.8%              | 1.3%                                |

| 借款币种 | 宝石加工企业 | 设备制造企业 | 宝石企业成本优势 |
| -------- | ------------ | ------------ | ---------------- |
| 美元贷款 | 5.5%         | 7.6%         | 2.1%             |
| 澳元贷款 | 8.5%         | 9.8%         | 1.3%             |

The jewellery company had lower borrowing costs in both markets. ｜ 宝石加工企业在两个借款市场中的融资成本都较低。

However, its advantage was greater in the US-dollar market: ｜ 但是，它在美元市场的优势更大：
$$
7.6\%-5.5\%
=
2.1\%
$$
Its advantage in the Australian-dollar market was: ｜ 其在澳元市场的优势为：
$$
9.8\%-8.5\%
=
1.3\%
$$
Therefore, the total potential comparative-advantage benefit was: ｜ 双方可以通过互换分享的比较优势总额为：
$$
2.1\%-1.3\%
=
0.8\%
$$
Assuming no intermediary fee, the two companies could share this total cost saving. ｜ 假设中介机构不收取费用，双方可以共同分享0.8%的融资成本节约。

------

#### III. Financing and Principal Exchange ｜ 三、融资与本金交换

The spot AUD/USD exchange rate was approximately: ｜ 澳元兑美元即期汇率约为：
$$
1\text{澳元}=0.90\text{美元}
$$
Therefore: ｜ 因此：
$$
5{,}000{,}000\text{澳元}
\times0.90
=
4{,}500{,}000\text{美元}
$$
The two principal amounts were equivalent at the agreed exchange rate. ｜ 按照约定汇率，500万澳元与450万美元价值相等。

The parties borrowed in their comparative-advantage markets: ｜ 双方分别在具有比较优势的市场融资：

- The jewellery company borrowed USD 4.5 million at 5.5%. ｜ 宝石加工企业以5.5%的利率借入450万美元。
- The equipment company borrowed AUD 5 million at 9.8%. ｜ 设备制造企业以9.8%的利率借入500万澳元。

At the beginning of the swap: ｜ 货币互换期初：

- The jewellery company paid USD 4.5 million and received AUD 5 million. ｜ 宝石加工企业支付450万美元，获得500万澳元。
- The equipment company paid AUD 5 million and received USD 4.5 million. ｜ 设备制造企业支付500万澳元，获得450万美元。

The swap term was three years, with interest exchanged twice a year. ｜ 互换期限为3年，每年交换两次利息。

> 原材料有一处将美元借款利率写为5.59%，但前表及后续实际融资成本计算均使用5.5%，因此本案例按照5.5%整理。

------

#### IV. Interest Exchange ｜ 四、利息交换

Under the swap agreement: ｜ 根据互换协议：

- The jewellery company paid AUD interest at 8.5% to the equipment company. ｜ 宝石加工企业向设备制造企业支付8.5%的澳元利息。
- The equipment company paid USD interest at 5.9% to the jewellery company. ｜ 设备制造企业向宝石加工企业支付5.9%的美元利息。

The annual Australian-dollar interest paid by the jewellery company was: ｜ 宝石加工企业每年支付的澳元利息为：
$$
5{,}000{,}000\times8.5\%
=
425{,}000\text{澳元}
$$
Because interest was paid twice a year, each payment was: ｜ 每年付息两次，因此每次支付：
$$
\frac{425{,}000}{2}
=
212{,}500\text{澳元}
$$
The annual US-dollar interest paid by the equipment company was: ｜ 设备制造企业每年支付的美元利息为：
$$
4{,}500{,}000\times5.9\%
=
265{,}500\text{美元}
$$
Each semiannual payment was: ｜ 每半年支付：
$$
\frac{265{,}500}{2}
=
132{,}750\text{美元}
$$

------

#### V. Reverse Exchange of Principal at Maturity ｜ 五、到期反向交换本金

At the end of the three-year swap: ｜ 3年互换到期时：

- The jewellery company returned AUD 5 million and received USD 4.5 million. ｜ 宝石加工企业支付500万澳元，收回450万美元。
- The equipment company returned USD 4.5 million and received AUD 5 million. ｜ 设备制造企业支付450万美元，收回500万澳元。

Each company then used the returned currency principal to repay its original lender. ｜ 双方再使用收回的原借款币种本金偿还各自的贷款。

Because the reverse exchange used the same agreed principal amounts, the companies avoided uncertainty over the exchange rate required to repay the original loans. ｜ 由于期末按照相同本金金额进行反向交换，双方锁定了未来偿还原贷款本金所需的汇率关系。

------

#### VI. Effective Financing Costs ｜ 六、实际融资成本

##### (I) Jewellery Company ｜ （一）宝石加工企业

The jewellery company: ｜ 宝石加工企业：

- Paid 5.5% on its external US-dollar loan ｜ 向外部美元贷款人支付5.5%
- Received 5.9% US-dollar interest through the swap ｜ 通过互换收取5.9%的美元利息
- Paid 8.5% Australian-dollar interest through the swap ｜ 通过互换支付8.5%的澳元利息

Its effective Australian-dollar financing cost was: ｜ 其实际澳元融资成本为：
$$
5.5\%-5.9\%+8.5\%
=
8.1\%
$$
If it borrowed Australian dollars directly, it would pay 8.5%. ｜ 如果直接借入澳元，融资成本为8.5%。

Therefore, its saving was: ｜ 因此，节约成本为：
$$
8.5\%-8.1\%
=
0.4\%
$$

------

##### (II) Equipment Company ｜ （二）设备制造企业

The equipment company: ｜ 设备制造企业：

- Paid 9.8% on its external Australian-dollar loan ｜ 向外部澳元贷款人支付9.8%
- Received 8.5% Australian-dollar interest through the swap ｜ 通过互换收取8.5%的澳元利息
- Paid 5.9% US-dollar interest through the swap ｜ 通过互换支付5.9%的美元利息

Its effective US-dollar financing cost was: ｜ 其实际美元融资成本为：
$$
9.8\%-8.5\%+5.9\%
=
7.2\%
$$
If it borrowed US dollars directly, it would pay 7.6%. ｜ 如果直接借入美元，融资成本为7.6%。

Therefore, its saving was: ｜ 因此，节约成本为：
$$
7.6\%-7.2\%
=
0.4\%
$$

------

#### VII. Distribution of the Swap Benefit ｜ 七、互换收益的分配

The total available comparative-advantage benefit was: ｜ 可分配的比较优势总收益为：
$$
0.8\%
$$
The parties divided it equally: ｜ 双方平均分配：
$$
0.8\%\div2
=
0.4\%
$$

| Company                        | Direct borrowing cost | Cost after swap | Saving |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------- | --------------- | ------ |
| Jewellery company: AUD funding | 8.5%                  | 8.1%            | 0.4%   |
| Equipment company: USD funding | 7.6%                  | 7.2%            | 0.4%   |

| 企业                   | 直接借款成本 | 互换后成本 | 节约 |
| ---------------------- | ------------ | ---------- | ---- |
| 宝石加工企业：澳元融资 | 8.5%         | 8.1%       | 0.4% |
| 设备制造企业：美元融资 | 7.6%         | 7.2%       | 0.4% |

The swap achieved two objectives: ｜ 该货币互换实现了两个目标：

- Both companies reduced their financing costs. ｜ 两家企业均降低了融资成本。
- The jewellery company locked in the AUD/USD principal exchange and reduced its exposure to Australian-dollar exchange-rate fluctuations. ｜ 宝石加工企业锁定了澳元与美元的本金兑换关系，降低了澳元汇率波动风险。

------

### Examination Topic: Relationship and Differences Between Currency Swaps and FX Swaps ｜ 考点：货币互换与外汇掉期的联系与区别

#### I. Similarities ｜ 一、两者的相同之处

##### (I) Both Involve Two Currencies ｜ （一）均涉及两种货币

Both currency swaps and foreign exchange swaps involve exchanges between two different currencies. ｜ 货币互换与外汇掉期都涉及两种不同货币之间的交换。

##### (II) Both May Involve Initial and Final Principal Exchanges ｜ （二）均可能涉及前后两次本金交换

Traditional forms of both transactions may involve: ｜ 传统货币互换和外汇掉期都可能包括：

- An initial exchange of currency principal ｜ 协议开始时交换货币本金
- A reverse exchange at a later date ｜ 协议到期时反向交换本金

Under some market arrangements, both may also use cash settlement without actual delivery of principal. ｜ 在部分市场安排下，两者也可以不实际交换或者交割本金，而采用差额结算。

##### (III) Both Can Manage Exchange-Rate Risk ｜ （三）均可以管理汇率风险

Both instruments may lock in future currency exchanges and reduce uncertainty caused by exchange-rate movements. ｜ 两种工具都可以锁定未来货币兑换关系，降低汇率波动造成的不确定性。

------

#### II. Differences ｜ 二、两者的不同之处

##### (I) Different Methods of Principal Exchange ｜ （一）本金交换方式不同

###### Currency Swap ｜ 货币互换

When principal is exchanged at both inception and maturity: ｜ 如果货币互换在期初和期末均交换本金：

- The initial exchange generally uses the spot exchange rate. ｜ 期初通常按照即期汇率交换本金。
- The final reverse exchange generally uses the same exchange rate and the same principal amounts. ｜ 期末通常按照相同汇率和相同本金金额进行反向交换。
- Interest denominated in the two currencies is periodically exchanged during the term. ｜ 合约存续期间还会定期交换两种货币的利息。

###### Foreign Exchange Swap ｜ 外汇掉期

An FX swap uses different rates for its two currency transactions: ｜ 外汇掉期前后两笔货币交易通常使用不同汇率：

- The near-end outright rate ｜ 近端掉期全价
- The far-end outright rate ｜ 远端掉期全价

It is essentially a purchase and a reverse sale, or a sale and a reverse purchase, of the same currency principal at different value dates. ｜ 其本质是对同一货币本金在不同起息日进行一买一卖或者一卖一买。

| Item                | Currency swap                                   | FX swap                  |
| ------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------ |
| Initial rate        | Usually spot rate                               | Near-end outright rate   |
| Final rate          | Usually same rate as initial principal exchange | Far-end outright rate    |
| Principal direction | Reversed at maturity                            | Reversed at far-end date |

| 项目     | 货币互换                   | 外汇掉期         |
| -------- | -------------------------- | ---------------- |
| 期初汇率 | 通常使用即期汇率           | 使用近端掉期全价 |
| 期末汇率 | 通常按期初相同汇率反向交换 | 使用远端掉期全价 |
| 本金方向 | 到期反向交换               | 远端进行反向交易 |

------

##### (II) Different Starting Dates ｜ （二）协议开始时间不同

A currency swap generally begins with an exchange on or around the spot effective date. ｜ 货币互换一般从即期生效日附近开始。

An FX swap may begin: ｜ 外汇掉期的近端起息日可以是：

- On the spot date ｜ 即期日期
- Before the spot date, such as O/N or T/N ｜ 即期日前，例如O/N或T/N
- On a future forward date ｜ 未来某一远期日期

Therefore, an FX swap may be either spot-forward or forward-forward. ｜ 因此，外汇掉期既可以是即期对远期，也可以是远期对远期。

------

##### (III) Different Cash Flows During the Holding Period ｜ （三）持有期现金流不同

A currency swap involves periodic exchanges of interest between inception and maturity. ｜ 货币互换在协议开始和到期之间，通常涉及多期利息交换。
$$
\text{Currency swap}
=
\text{Principal exchanges}
+
\text{Periodic interest exchanges}
$$
An FX swap generally involves only two principal currency transactions. ｜ 外汇掉期通常只包括前后两次本金货币买卖。

It does not separately exchange periodic interest, so there are normally no intermediate cash flows. ｜ 外汇掉期不单独交换利息，因此近端与远端日期之间通常没有中间现金流。
$$
\text{FX swap}
=
\text{Near-end currency exchange}
+
\text{Far-end reverse exchange}
$$

------

##### (IV) Different Main Applications ｜ （四）主要应用情形不完全相同

Based on the applications described in the source: ｜ 根据原材料中的应用场景可以概括：

Currency swaps are commonly used to: ｜ 货币互换通常用于：

- Obtain medium- or long-term financing in another currency ｜ 获得其他币种的中长期融资
- Reduce financing costs through comparative advantages ｜ 利用比较优势降低融资成本
- Convert the currency and interest structure of assets or liabilities ｜ 转换资产或负债的币种及利息结构
- Manage longer-term exchange-rate and interest-rate risks ｜ 管理较长期的汇率和利率风险

FX swaps are commonly used to: ｜ 外汇掉期通常用于：

- Adjust short-term foreign-currency funding positions ｜ 调整短期外汇资金头寸
- Change the maturity of foreign-currency funds ｜ 调整外汇资金期限结构
- Manage mismatches between foreign-currency receipts and payments ｜ 管理外汇收支时间错配
- Temporarily exchange one currency for another and reverse the exchange later ｜ 临时将一种货币换成另一种货币，并在未来换回

------

#### Comprehensive Comparison ｜ 综合比较

| Item                    | Currency swap                                              | Foreign exchange swap                            |
| ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| Currencies involved     | Two currencies                                             | Two currencies                                   |
| Principal exchanges     | May occur at inception and maturity                        | Near-end and far-end opposite transactions       |
| Exchange rates          | Often same rate for initial and final principal exchange   | Different near-end and far-end outright rates    |
| Interest exchanges      | Periodic interest cash flows                               | No separate periodic interest exchanges          |
| Intermediate cash flows | Usually present                                            | Generally absent                                 |
| Typical term            | Often medium or long term                                  | Often short or medium term                       |
| Main use                | Financing-cost reduction and transformation of liabilities | Liquidity, maturity and short-term FX management |
| Risk managed            | Exchange-rate and possibly interest-rate risk              | Primarily exchange-rate and funding-timing risk  |

| 项目         | 货币互换                       | 外汇掉期                       |
| ------------ | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------ |
| 涉及货币     | 两种货币                       | 两种货币                       |
| 本金交换     | 可在期初和期末交换             | 近端与远端方向相反的交易       |
| 使用汇率     | 期初和期末本金通常使用相同汇率 | 近端和远端全价不同             |
| 利息交换     | 定期交换利息现金流             | 不单独交换利息                 |
| 中间现金流   | 通常存在                       | 通常不存在                     |
| 常见期限     | 通常为中长期                   | 通常为短期或中期               |
| 主要用途     | 降低融资成本、转换负债结构     | 管理流动性、期限和短期外汇头寸 |
| 主要风险管理 | 汇率风险及可能的利率风险       | 汇率风险和资金期限错配风险     |

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

💱 **A currency swap exchanges two currency principals and periodically exchanges interest payments in the two currencies.** ｜ 💱 **货币互换交换两种货币本金，并定期交换两种货币的利息。**

🔄 **Traditional currency swaps exchange principal at inception and reverse the exchange at maturity using the same agreed amounts and rate.** ｜ 🔄 **传统货币互换在期初交换本金，并在到期时按照相同金额和汇率反向交换本金。**

📊 **Currency swaps may use fixed-for-fixed or fixed-for-floating interest structures.** ｜ 📊 **货币互换可以采用固定换固定或者固定换浮动的利息结构。**

🏦 **When both parties need different currencies, each can borrow in its comparative-advantage market and obtain the required currency through a swap.** ｜ 🏦 **双方需要不同币种时，可以分别在具有比较优势的市场融资，再通过互换取得所需货币。**

⚖️ **In the case, the total comparative-advantage benefit was 0.8%, and each company reduced its financing cost by 0.4%.** ｜ ⚖️ **案例中的比较优势总收益为0.8%，两家企业分别降低融资成本0.4%。**

🛡️ **Currency swaps may simultaneously reduce financing costs and manage exchange-rate risk.** ｜ 🛡️ **货币互换可以同时降低融资成本并管理汇率风险。**

🔁 **Both currency swaps and FX swaps involve two currencies and can be used to hedge exchange-rate risk.** ｜ 🔁 **货币互换与外汇掉期都涉及两种货币，并可以用于管理汇率风险。**

💵 **Currency swaps involve periodic interest exchanges, while FX swaps generally contain only two opposite principal transactions.** ｜ 💵 **货币互换存在多期利息交换，外汇掉期通常只有前后两次方向相反的本金交易。**

📅 **Currency swaps usually transform medium- or long-term financing, while FX swaps are commonly used to adjust shorter-term liquidity and maturity structures.** ｜ 📅 **货币互换通常用于转换中长期融资结构，外汇掉期则常用于调整较短期的流动性和资金期限结构。**



# Chapter 13: Futures Market Regulation and Risk Control ｜ 第十三章 期货市场监管与风险控制

This chapter accounts for approximately 5% of the examination score. ｜ 本章分值占比约为5%。

## Chapter Structure ｜ 本章结构

- Section 1: Futures Market Risks and Their Causes ｜ 第一节：期货市场风险及其成因
- Section 2: China’s Futures Market Regulatory System ｜ 第二节：中国期货市场监管体系
- Section 3: Futures Market Risk-Control Mechanisms ｜ 第三节：期货市场风险控制

------

## Section 1: Futures Market Risks and Their Causes ｜ 第一节 期货市场风险及其成因

### Examination Topic: Types and Characteristics of Futures Market Risks ｜ 考点：期货市场风险的类型与特征

#### I. Two Definitions of Risk ｜ 一、风险的两种定义

##### (I) Risk as Uncertainty of Loss ｜ （一）风险表现为损失的不确定性

Under this definition, risk refers to the uncertainty concerning whether a loss will occur and how large the loss will be. ｜ 按照这种定义，风险是指损失是否发生以及损失程度具有不确定性。

The focus is on adverse consequences. ｜ 该定义主要强调风险可能带来的不利后果。

##### (II) Risk as Uncertainty ｜ （二）风险表现为不确定性

A broader definition treats risk as uncertainty itself. ｜ 更广义的定义将风险理解为不确定性本身。

The actual result may therefore be: ｜ 实际结果可能表现为：

- A loss ｜ 损失
- No material change ｜ 没有明显变化
- A gain ｜ 收益

This definition reflects the coexistence of risk and opportunity. ｜ 这种定义体现了风险与机会共存的特点。

------

#### II. Types of Futures Market Risks ｜ 二、期货市场风险的类型

##### (I) Classification by Controllability ｜ （一）按照风险是否可控分类

###### Uncontrollable Risks ｜ 不可控风险

Uncontrollable risks are risks that individual market participants generally cannot eliminate through their own actions. ｜ 不可控风险是指单个市场参与者通常无法通过自身行为完全消除的风险。

They include: ｜ 主要包括：

- Risks arising from changes in the macroeconomic environment ｜ 宏观经济环境变化风险
- Policy risks ｜ 政策性风险

Examples may include economic recessions, inflation, monetary-policy changes and major adjustments to industry regulation. ｜ 例如经济衰退、通货膨胀、货币政策变化以及行业监管政策重大调整等。

###### Controllable Risks ｜ 可控风险

Controllable risks are risks that may be reduced through appropriate internal controls, trading rules, position management and risk-management measures. ｜ 可控风险是指可以通过内部控制、交易制度、头寸管理和风险管理措施加以降低的风险。

------

##### (II) Classification by Stage of Futures Trading ｜ （二）按照期货交易环节分类

###### Agency Risk ｜ 代理风险

Agency risk arises when a client selects a futures company and establishes an agency relationship with it. ｜ 代理风险是指客户在选择期货公司并与其建立代理关系过程中产生的风险。

For example, the futures company may have: ｜ 例如，期货公司可能存在：

- Weak internal controls ｜ 内部控制薄弱
- Poor financial condition ｜ 财务状况较差
- Improper handling of client orders or funds ｜ 不当处理客户指令或者资金

###### Trading Risk ｜ 交易风险

Trading risk arises during the process of buying and selling futures contracts. ｜ 交易风险是指期货交易者在买卖期货合约过程中产生的风险。

It may result from: ｜ 可能来源于：

- Adverse price movements ｜ 不利价格变化
- Excessive positions ｜ 头寸规模过大
- Improper trading strategies ｜ 交易策略不当
- Insufficient margin ｜ 保证金不足

###### Delivery Risk ｜ 交割风险

Delivery risk arises when a client prepares for or carries out futures delivery. ｜ 交割风险是指客户在准备或者进行期货交割时产生的风险。

Examples include: ｜ 例如：

- Failure to prepare sufficient deliverable commodities ｜ 未准备足够的可交割商品
- Commodities failing to meet quality standards ｜ 商品不符合交割质量标准
- Failure to prepare sufficient delivery funds ｜ 未准备充足的交割资金
- Failure to complete delivery procedures on time ｜ 未按时完成交割手续

------

##### (III) Classification by Risk-Bearing Entity ｜ （三）按照风险产生的主体分类

Futures market risk may arise from the following entities: ｜ 期货市场风险可能由以下主体产生：

- Futures exchanges ｜ 期货交易所
- Futures companies ｜ 期货公司
- Futures traders ｜ 期货交易者
- Government authorities and regulatory departments ｜ 政府及监管部门

Each entity performs different functions and therefore faces different risk exposures. ｜ 不同主体承担的市场职能不同，面临的风险也不相同。

------

##### (IV) Classification by Source of Risk ｜ （四）按照风险来源分类

###### Market Risk ｜ 市场风险

Market risk is the risk that changes in market prices will change the value of futures positions. ｜ 市场风险是指市场价格变化导致所持期货合约价值发生变化的风险。

It is the most common and one of the most important risks in futures trading. ｜ 市场风险是期货交易中最常见、最需要重视的风险之一。

For a long position: ｜ 对于期货多头：
$$
\text{Futures price}\downarrow
\Rightarrow
\text{Loss}
$$
For a short position: ｜ 对于期货空头：
$$
\text{Futures price}\uparrow
\Rightarrow
\text{Loss}
$$

------

###### Credit Risk ｜ 信用风险

Credit risk is the risk of loss caused by a counterparty’s failure or refusal to perform its contractual obligations. ｜ 信用风险是指交易对手不履行或者无法履行合约义务而导致损失的风险。

In standardised futures markets, central clearing, margin requirements and daily settlement reduce direct counterparty credit risk. ｜ 在标准化期货市场中，中央结算、保证金制度和逐日结算可以降低直接交易对手信用风险。

However, credit risk may still exist among clients, futures companies, clearing institutions and other market participants. ｜ 但是，在客户、期货公司和结算机构等主体之间，仍然可能存在信用风险。

------

###### Liquidity Risk ｜ 流动性风险

Liquidity risk includes both market-liquidity risk and funding-liquidity risk. ｜ 流动性风险包括市场流动性风险和资金流动性风险。

\####### Market-Liquidity Risk ｜ \####### 流通量风险

Market-liquidity risk is the risk that a position cannot be established or closed promptly at a reasonable price. ｜ 流通量风险是指交易者无法及时按照合理价格建立或者平掉头寸的风险。

Market liquidity is commonly measured by breadth and depth. ｜ 期货市场流动性通常使用市场广度和市场深度衡量。

**Market breadth** refers to the market’s ability to satisfy trading demand at a given price level. ｜ **市场广度**是指市场在既定价格水平下满足投资者交易需求的能力。

**Market depth** refers to the market’s ability to absorb large-volume transactions without substantial price movements. ｜ **市场深度**是指市场承接大额交易需求而不引起价格剧烈变化的能力。

A highly liquid market generally has: ｜ 流动性较高的市场通常具有：

- More stable prices ｜ 价格更加稳定
- Lower transaction impact ｜ 大额交易对价格的冲击较小
- More reasonable price formation ｜ 价格形成更加合理

\####### Funding-Liquidity Risk ｜ \####### 资金量风险

Funding-liquidity risk arises when an investor does not have sufficient funds to satisfy margin requirements. ｜ 资金量风险是指投资者资金无法满足保证金要求而产生的风险。

If an investor cannot provide additional margin after receiving a margin call, the position may be forcibly liquidated. ｜ 当投资者收到追加保证金通知后不能及时补足资金，其持有的头寸可能被强制平仓。

------

###### Operational Risk ｜ 操作风险

Operational risk is the possibility of unexpected losses caused by defects in information systems, personnel procedures or internal controls. ｜ 操作风险是指因信息系统、人员操作或者内部控制缺陷而导致意外损失的可能性。

Examples include: ｜ 例如：

- System failures ｜ 交易系统故障
- Incorrect order entry ｜ 交易指令录入错误
- Inadequate authority management ｜ 权限管理不当
- Failure of risk-monitoring procedures ｜ 风险监控程序失效
- Internal fraud ｜ 内部舞弊

------

###### Legal Risk ｜ 法律风险

Legal risk arises when contracts, trading activities or risk-management arrangements are invalid, unenforceable or inconsistent with relevant laws and regulations. ｜ 法律风险是指合约、交易活动或者风险管理安排无效、无法执行，或者违反相关法律法规而产生的风险。

It may result from: ｜ 可能来源于：

- Unclear contractual terms ｜ 合约条款不清晰
- Lack of legal authority ｜ 交易主体缺少合法授权
- Changes in laws and regulations ｜ 法律法规发生变化
- Non-compliance with trading rules ｜ 违反市场交易规则

------

#### Summary of Futures Market Risk Types ｜ 期货市场风险类型总结

| Classification basis | Main risk types                                        |
| -------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |
| Controllability      | Uncontrollable risk and controllable risk              |
| Trading stage        | Agency risk, trading risk and delivery risk            |
| Risk-bearing entity  | Exchange, futures company, trader and regulator        |
| Risk source          | Market, credit, liquidity, operational and legal risks |

| 分类标准 | 主要风险类型                       |
| -------- | ---------------------------------- |
| 是否可控 | 不可控风险、可控风险               |
| 交易环节 | 代理风险、交易风险、交割风险       |
| 风险主体 | 交易所、期货公司、交易者、监管部门 |
| 风险来源 | 市场、信用、流动性、操作、法律风险 |

------

#### III. Characteristics of Futures Market Risks ｜ 三、期货市场风险的特征

##### (I) Objective Existence of Risk ｜ （一）风险存在的客观性

Risk objectively exists in futures markets and cannot be completely eliminated. ｜ 风险客观存在于期货市场中，不可能被彻底消除。

Price movements are affected by numerous uncertain factors, including economic conditions, supply and demand, policies and investor expectations. ｜ 期货价格受到经济环境、供求状况、政策和投资者预期等多种不确定因素影响。

Risk management can reduce or transfer risk, but it cannot remove all uncertainty. ｜ 风险管理可以降低或者转移风险，但不能消除全部不确定性。

------

##### (II) Coexistence of Risk and Opportunity ｜ （二）风险与机会的共生性

A futures price movement that causes a loss to one position may create a profit opportunity for the opposite position. ｜ 同一期货价格变化可能使一个方向的头寸产生损失，同时使相反方向的头寸获得收益。

For example: ｜ 例如：
$$
\text{Futures price rises}
\Rightarrow
\begin{cases}
\text{Long gains}\\
\text{Short loses}
\end{cases}
$$
Therefore, risk and return opportunities exist together. ｜ 因此，风险与收益机会通常同时存在。

------

##### (III) Amplification of Risk Factors ｜ （三）风险因素的放大性

Risk in futures trading may be amplified by its trading mechanisms. ｜ 期货交易风险可能受到交易制度和交易机制的放大。

###### Margin and Leverage ｜ 保证金与杠杆效应

Futures trading uses a margin system. ｜ 期货交易实行保证金制度。

An investor controls a relatively large contract value by depositing only a fraction of that value. ｜ 投资者只需要缴纳合约价值的一部分保证金，就可以控制较大规模的期货头寸。

Therefore, a small movement in the futures price may produce a much larger percentage change in the investor’s capital. ｜ 因此，期货价格的小幅变化可能造成投资者自有资金收益率或者损失率的大幅变化。
$$
\text{Leverage}
=
\frac{\text{Contract value}}{\text{Margin}}
$$

###### Two-Way Trading and Offset Mechanism ｜ 双向交易与对冲机制

Futures traders can establish either long or short positions and generally close contracts through offsetting transactions. ｜ 期货交易者既可以买入开仓，也可以卖出开仓，并可以通过反向交易对冲平仓。

Although this improves trading flexibility, it may also increase speculative motivation and encourage excessive speculation. ｜ 虽然这种机制提高了交易灵活性，但也可能强化投机动机，诱发过度投机。

###### T+0 Trading ｜ T+0交易制度

Futures markets generally permit positions to be opened and closed within the same trading day. ｜ 期货市场通常允许交易者在同一交易日内开仓和平仓。

This provides opportunities for frequent trading. ｜ 这种制度为频繁交易提供了条件。

Excessive trading may lead to: ｜ 过度频繁交易可能导致：

- Excessive trading volume ｜ 交易量过大
- Concentrated risk ｜ 风险过度集中
- Increased transaction costs ｜ 交易成本增加
- More impulsive decisions ｜ 冲动交易增加

------

##### (IV) Equality in Exposure to Loss ｜ （四）风险损失的均等性

Both hedgers and speculators may suffer losses in the futures market. ｜ 在期货市场中，套期保值者和投机者都可能遭受损失。

The actual degree of risk depends on factors such as: ｜ 实际风险程度取决于：

- The size and direction of the position ｜ 头寸的规模和方向
- Financial strength ｜ 交易者的经济实力
- Risk tolerance ｜ 风险承受能力
- Risk-management measures ｜ 风险管理措施

However, no participant is automatically exempt from the possibility of loss. ｜ 但是，任何市场参与者都不能天然免除遭受损失的可能性。

> 原材料将本项编号混入“风险放大原因”之中。根据内容逻辑，应与“客观性、共生性、放大性”并列，属于风险特征。

------

##### (V) Preventability of Risk ｜ （五）风险的可防范性

Although futures market risks cannot be completely eliminated, their occurrence and development have certain patterns. ｜ 虽然期货市场风险不能完全消除，但风险的产生和发展具有一定规律。

Market participants and regulators can use: ｜ 市场参与者和监管机构可以利用：

- Historical information ｜ 历史资料
- Statistical data ｜ 统计数据
- Risk indicators ｜ 风险指标
- Market-monitoring systems ｜ 市场监测系统

to identify warning signs and estimate possible consequences. ｜ 识别风险征兆，并预测风险可能产生的后果。

By improving regulatory systems and adopting effective control measures, risks may be: ｜ 通过完善监管制度和采取有效控制措施，可以实现：

- Avoided ｜ 规避风险
- Diversified ｜ 分散风险
- Transferred ｜ 转移风险
- Reduced ｜ 减弱风险

------

### Examination Topic: Causes and Management Process of Futures Market Risks ｜ 考点：期货市场风险的成因及管理流程

#### I. Causes of Futures Market Risks ｜ 一、期货市场风险的成因

##### (I) Price Volatility ｜ （一）价格波动

Futures prices are affected by supply and demand, macroeconomic conditions, policies, interest rates, exchange rates and market expectations. ｜ 期货价格受到供求关系、宏观经济、政策、利率、汇率和市场预期等多种因素影响。

Price volatility directly changes the value of open futures positions. ｜ 价格波动会直接改变未平仓期货头寸的价值。

The greater the price movement, the greater the potential profit or loss. ｜ 价格变动幅度越大，潜在盈利或者亏损通常越大。

------

##### (II) Leverage Effect ｜ （二）杠杆效应

The margin system allows investors to establish large positions with relatively little capital. ｜ 保证金制度使投资者能够使用较少资金建立较大规模的头寸。

Leverage amplifies both gains and losses. ｜ 杠杆同时放大盈利和亏损。

For example, if the initial margin ratio is 10%, the theoretical leverage is: ｜ 例如，当初始保证金比例为10%时，理论杠杆倍数为：
$$
\frac{1}{10\%}=10
$$
A 1% change in the contract value may therefore produce an approximately 10% change relative to the margin, ignoring other factors. ｜ 因此，在忽略其他因素时，合约价值变动1%，可能相当于保证金资金变动约10%。

------

##### (III) Irrational Speculation ｜ （三）非理性投机

Irrational speculation may include: ｜ 非理性投机可能表现为：

- Excessive position taking ｜ 头寸规模过大
- Frequent trading ｜ 交易过于频繁
- Following market rumours ｜ 盲目追随市场传闻
- Trading without stop-loss arrangements ｜ 缺乏止损安排
- Manipulative or herd behaviour ｜ 操纵行为或者羊群行为

Excessive speculation may increase market volatility and concentrate risks. ｜ 过度投机可能加剧市场波动并导致风险集中。

------

##### (IV) Imperfect Market Mechanisms ｜ （四）市场机制不健全

An incomplete market mechanism may involve: ｜ 市场机制不健全可能包括：

- Defective trading rules ｜ 交易规则存在缺陷
- Inadequate information disclosure ｜ 信息披露不足
- Weak clearing and settlement arrangements ｜ 结算制度不完善
- Insufficient supervision ｜ 监管不到位
- Inadequate investor-protection mechanisms ｜ 投资者保护机制不足

These weaknesses may allow risks to accumulate and spread. ｜ 这些制度缺陷可能导致风险积累和扩散。

------

#### II. Futures Market Risk-Management Process ｜ 二、期货市场风险管理流程

The basic risk-management process includes: ｜ 基本风险管理流程包括：
$$
\text{Risk identification}
\rightarrow
\text{Risk measurement}
\rightarrow
\text{Risk control}
$$

------

##### (I) Risk Identification ｜ （一）风险识别

Risk identification means identifying the types, sources and possible consequences of risks faced by an institution or trading activity. ｜ 风险识别是指发现机构或者交易活动所面临风险的种类、来源和可能后果。

###### Risk Listing Method ｜ 风险列举法

The risk-management department reviews the business process and lists the risks present in each stage. ｜ 风险管理部门按照业务流程，逐项列举各业务环节中可能存在的风险。

For example: ｜ 例如：

- Account-opening risk ｜ 开户风险
- Order-execution risk ｜ 指令执行风险
- Margin risk ｜ 保证金风险
- Settlement risk ｜ 结算风险
- Delivery risk ｜ 交割风险

###### Flowchart Analysis ｜ 流程图分析法

The risk-management department maps the complete business process and analyses each stage. ｜ 风险管理部门绘制完整业务流程，并逐项分析每个环节。

Potential risk points are identified by examining the connections among different stages. ｜ 通过分析各业务环节之间的联系，识别潜在风险点。

###### Decomposition Analysis ｜ 分解分析法

A complex system is divided into simpler components. ｜ 分解分析法是将复杂系统分解为多个较简单的组成部分。

Each component is analysed separately to identify possible risks and potential losses. ｜ 之后分别分析各个组成要素中可能存在的风险及潜在损失。

------

##### (II) Risk Measurement ｜ （二）风险测度

Risk measurement estimates the probability and severity of potential losses. ｜ 风险测度是对潜在损失发生的概率和严重程度进行估计。

###### Estimating the Probability of Risk ｜ 预测风险发生概率

Historical data and observations are used to identify patterns in loss events. ｜ 通过积累历史资料和进行观察，发现风险损失发生的规律。

###### Estimating the Severity of Risk ｜ 预测风险强度

Risk severity refers to the direct and indirect losses that may occur if the risk event materialises. ｜ 风险强度是指假设风险发生后，可能导致的直接损失和间接损失。

------

#### III. Value at Risk ｜ 三、在险价值

##### (I) Definition of VaR ｜ （一）VaR的定义

Value at Risk, abbreviated as VaR, measures the maximum possible loss of a financial asset or portfolio over a specified holding period under normal market conditions and at a specified confidence level. ｜ 在险价值简称VaR，是指在市场正常波动的情况下，在一定置信水平和特定持有期内，某一金融资产或者证券组合可能发生的最大损失。

The key elements of VaR are: ｜ VaR包含三个关键要素：

- Holding period ｜ 持有期
- Confidence level ｜ 置信水平
- Maximum loss amount ｜ 最大损失金额

------

##### (II) Mathematical Expression ｜ （二）数学表达

Let: ｜ 设：

- $L_{\Delta t}$: portfolio loss during holding period $\Delta t$ ｜ $L_{\Delta t}$：证券组合在持有期 $\Delta t$ 内的损失
- $C$: confidence level ｜ $C$：置信水平
- $\operatorname{VaR}$: value at risk ｜ $\operatorname{VaR}$：在险价值

Then: ｜ 则：
$$
P\left(L_{\Delta t}>\operatorname{VaR}\right)
=
1-C
$$
Equivalently: ｜ 等价地：
$$
P\left(L_{\Delta t}\leq\operatorname{VaR}\right)
=
C
$$
The source states that both VaR and loss are expressed as positive amounts. ｜ 原材料规定VaR和损失均以正数表示。

> 原文公式受到OCR损坏。整理后的含义是：实际损失超过VaR的概率等于 $1-C$。

------

##### (III) VaR Case ｜ （三）VaR案例

Suppose an investment company’s portfolio has a one-day VaR of RMB 5 million at a 95% confidence level. ｜ 假设某投资公司的证券组合，在95%置信水平下的一日VaR为500万元。

This means: ｜ 这意味着：
$$
P(\text{One-day loss}>5\text{ million})
=
5\%
$$
Equivalently: ｜ 等价地：
$$
P(\text{One-day loss}\leq5\text{ million})
=
95\%
$$
In ordinary language, there is 95% confidence that the company’s loss during the next trading day will not exceed RMB 5 million under normal market conditions. ｜ 通俗地说，在市场正常波动情况下，有95%的把握认为该公司下一个交易日的损失不会超过500万元。

A 5% tail probability is equivalent to approximately: ｜ 5%的尾部概率大致相当于：
$$
\frac{1}{5\%}=20
$$
Therefore, a loss exceeding RMB 5 million may occur on average about once every 20 trading days, provided the assumed probability distribution remains valid. ｜ 因此，在假设的概率分布持续成立时，超过500万元的损失平均大约每20个交易日可能出现一次。

However, this does **not** mean that such a loss will occur exactly once every 20 days. ｜ 但这并不意味着每20个交易日一定会准确发生一次超过500万元的损失。

VaR also does not indicate how large the loss may be after the VaR threshold has been exceeded. ｜ VaR也不能说明实际损失一旦超过VaR后，可能进一步扩大到什么程度。

------

##### (IV) Significance of the Confidence Level ｜ （四）置信水平的意义

A higher confidence level generally produces a larger VaR. ｜ 置信水平越高，计算得到的VaR通常越大。

For example: ｜ 例如：
$$
\operatorname{VaR}_{99\%}
>
\operatorname{VaR}_{95\%}
$$
under otherwise identical conditions. ｜ 在其他条件相同时，99%置信水平下的VaR通常高于95%置信水平下的VaR。

The choice of confidence level reflects the institution’s risk preference, risk tolerance and regulatory requirements. ｜ 置信水平的选择反映了机构的风险偏好、风险承受能力和监管要求。

------

##### (III) Risk Control ｜ （三）风险控制

Risk control refers to selecting and implementing measures to reduce the probability or severity of loss. ｜ 风险控制是指选择并实施措施，以降低风险发生概率或者损失程度。

###### Selection of Risk-Control Measures ｜ 风险控制措施的选择

The selection of risk-control measures is a cost-benefit decision. ｜ 风险控制措施的选择，本质上是成本与收益之间的权衡。

A control measure should reduce risk sufficiently without imposing unreasonable costs or preventing normal business operations. ｜ 风险控制措施应当在有效降低风险的同时，避免成本过高或者妨碍正常业务开展。

Possible measures include: ｜ 可能采取的措施包括：

- Position limits ｜ 持仓限额
- Stop-loss limits ｜ 止损限制
- Margin management ｜ 保证金管理
- Diversification ｜ 分散投资
- Hedging ｜ 套期保值
- Authority controls ｜ 交易权限控制
- Real-time risk monitoring ｜ 实时风险监控

###### Emergency Plans ｜ 应急方案

Institutions should establish practical emergency plans in advance. ｜ 机构应当提前制定切实可行的应急方案。

When a risk event occurs, the pre-arranged plan should be implemented promptly to minimise losses. ｜ 风险发生后，应当及时按照预设方案实施处置，将损失控制在最低限度。

An emergency plan may specify: ｜ 应急方案可以规定：

- Decision-making authority ｜ 决策权限
- Reporting procedures ｜ 风险报告程序
- Position-reduction measures ｜ 减仓措施
- Additional funding arrangements ｜ 补充资金安排
- System-recovery procedures ｜ 系统恢复程序
- Communication with clients and regulators ｜ 与客户及监管机构的沟通程序

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

⚠️ **Risk may be defined as uncertainty of loss or, more broadly, as uncertainty itself.** ｜ ⚠️ **风险既可以定义为损失的不确定性，也可以广义地定义为不确定性本身。**

📂 **Futures risks can be classified by controllability, trading stage, risk-bearing entity and source.** ｜ 📂 **期货风险可以按照是否可控、交易环节、风险主体和风险来源分类。**

📉 **Market risk arises when price changes alter the value of futures positions.** ｜ 📉 **市场风险是价格变化导致期货头寸价值变化的风险。**

💧 **Liquidity risk includes market-liquidity risk and the risk of insufficient funds for margin requirements.** ｜ 💧 **流动性风险包括市场流通量风险和无法满足保证金要求的资金量风险。**

🔍 **Market breadth measures the ability to satisfy trading demand at a given price; depth measures the ability to absorb large trades.** ｜ 🔍 **市场广度衡量既定价格下满足交易需求的能力，市场深度衡量承接大额交易的能力。**

🔧 **Operational risk arises from failures in systems, personnel procedures or internal controls.** ｜ 🔧 **操作风险来源于信息系统、人员操作或者内部控制缺陷。**

📈 **Margin leverage, two-way trading and T+0 trading can amplify futures-market risks.** ｜ 📈 **保证金杠杆、双向交易和T+0制度可能放大期货市场风险。**

🔄 **The risk-management process consists of risk identification, risk measurement and risk control.** ｜ 🔄 **风险管理流程包括风险识别、风险测度和风险控制。**

📊 **VaR estimates the maximum loss over a specified period at a specified confidence level under normal market conditions.** ｜ 📊 **VaR衡量在正常市场条件下，特定持有期和置信水平对应的最大可能损失。**

🧮 **A one-day 95% VaR of RMB 5 million means there is a 5% probability that the next day’s loss will exceed RMB 5 million.** ｜ 🧮 **一日95%置信水平VaR为500万元，意味着下一交易日损失超过500万元的概率为5%。**

🛡️ **Risk control requires selecting cost-effective measures and preparing practical emergency plans.** ｜ 🛡️ **风险控制需要选择具有成本效益的措施，并制定切实可行的应急方案。**



## Section 2: China’s Futures Market Regulatory System ｜ 第二节 中国期货市场监管体系

### Examination Topic: The “Five-in-One” Futures Market Regulatory System ｜ 考点：期货市场“五位一体”监管体系

#### I. Composition of the “Five-in-One” System ｜ 一、期货市场“五位一体”监管体系的构成

According to the textbook, China’s futures market regulatory system consists of five major participants: ｜ 根据教材，期货市场“五位一体”监管体系由以下五类主体构成：

##### (I) China Securities Regulatory Commission ｜ （一）中国证券监督管理委员会

The China Securities Regulatory Commission, abbreviated as the CSRC, is responsible for unified supervision and overall regulatory coordination. ｜ 中国证监会负责期货市场的统一监督管理和整体监管协调。

##### (II) Local CSRC Offices ｜ （二）地方证监局

Local CSRC offices supervise futures institutions and related market activities within their respective jurisdictions. ｜ 地方证监局负责对辖区内的期货经营机构和相关市场活动实施日常监管。

##### (III) Futures Exchanges ｜ （三）期货交易所

Futures exchanges organise trading, clearing and delivery and exercise self-regulation over their members. ｜ 期货交易所负责组织交易、结算和交割，并对会员实施自律管理。

##### (IV) China Futures Market Monitoring Center ｜ （四）中国期货市场监控中心

The source states that China Futures Margin Monitoring Center Co., Ltd. was renamed the China Futures Market Monitoring Center in April 2015. ｜ 教材指出，中国期货保证金监控中心有限责任公司于2015年4月更名为中国期货市场监控中心。

It performs functions such as margin monitoring, unified account opening and market-operation monitoring. ｜ 其主要履行保证金安全监控、统一开户和市场运行监测等职能。

##### (V) China Futures Association ｜ （五）中国期货业协会

The China Futures Association conducts industry self-regulation, member services and professional management. ｜ 中国期货业协会负责行业自律、会员服务和从业人员管理。

------

#### Regulatory Structure ｜ 监管结构

| Institution               | Main position in the system                    |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- |
| CSRC                      | Unified leadership, rulemaking and supervision |
| Local CSRC offices        | Jurisdictional and front-line supervision      |
| Futures exchanges         | Market organisation and self-regulation        |
| Monitoring Center         | Data monitoring and margin-safety monitoring   |
| China Futures Association | Industry self-regulation                       |

| 监管主体         | 主要定位                     |
| ---------------- | ---------------------------- |
| 中国证监会       | 统一领导、规则制定和监督检查 |
| 地方证监局       | 辖区日常监管和现场监管       |
| 期货交易所       | 市场组织和会员自律管理       |
| 期货市场监控中心 | 数据监测和保证金安全监控     |
| 中国期货业协会   | 行业自律管理                 |

------

### Examination Topic: China Securities Regulatory Commission ｜ 考点：中国证监会

#### I. General Regulatory Responsibilities ｜ 一、中国证监会的主要监管职责

##### (I) Formulating Rules and Exercising Approval Powers ｜ （一）制定监管规章并依法行使审批权

The CSRC formulates rules and regulations concerning futures-market supervision and exercises relevant approval powers in accordance with law. ｜ 中国证监会制定有关期货市场监督管理的规章和规则，并依法行使有关审批权。

##### (II) Supervising Futures Trading Activities ｜ （二）监督期货交易活动

It supervises activities relating to: ｜ 中国证监会监督管理以下期货活动：

- Contract or product listing ｜ 品种上市
- Trading ｜ 期货交易
- Clearing and settlement ｜ 结算
- Delivery ｜ 交割
- Other related activities ｜ 其他相关活动

##### (III) Supervising Market Participants ｜ （三）监督市场参与主体

The CSRC supervises the futures-related business activities of: ｜ 中国证监会对以下市场参与者的期货业务活动进行监督管理：

- Futures exchanges ｜ 期货交易所
- Futures companies and other futures institutions ｜ 期货公司及其他期货经营机构
- Non-futures-company clearing members ｜ 非期货公司结算会员
- Futures margin monitoring institutions ｜ 期货保证金安全存管监控机构
- Futures margin depository banks ｜ 期货保证金存管银行
- Delivery warehouses ｜ 交割仓库
- Other related market participants ｜ 其他相关参与者

##### (IV) Managing Futures Practitioners ｜ （四）管理期货从业人员

It formulates qualification standards and management measures for futures practitioners and supervises their implementation. ｜ 中国证监会制定期货从业人员资格标准和管理办法，并监督实施。

##### (V) Supervising Information Disclosure ｜ （五）监督信息公开

It supervises and examines the disclosure of futures-trading information. ｜ 中国证监会监督检查期货交易信息的公开情况。

##### (VI) Supervising the Industry Association ｜ （六）指导和监督期货业协会

It guides and supervises the activities of the China Futures Association. ｜ 中国证监会对中国期货业协会的活动进行指导和监督。

##### (VII) Investigating Violations ｜ （七）查处违法违规行为

It investigates and deals with conduct that violates futures-market laws and administrative regulations. ｜ 中国证监会查处违反期货市场监督管理法律和行政法规的行为。

##### (VIII) International Cooperation ｜ （八）开展国际交流与合作

It conducts international exchanges and cooperation relating to futures-market regulation. ｜ 中国证监会开展与期货市场监督管理有关的国际交流与合作。

##### (IX) Other Statutory Responsibilities ｜ （九）履行其他法定职责

It performs other responsibilities prescribed by laws and administrative regulations. ｜ 中国证监会履行法律和行政法规规定的其他职责。

------

#### II. Role in the “Five-in-One” Coordination Mechanism ｜ 二、在“五位一体”监管协调机制中的工作

The CSRC is responsible for rulemaking, unified leadership, overall coordination and supervision. ｜ 中国证监会负责监管协调机制的规则制定、统一领导、统筹协调和监督检查。

##### (I) Net Capital Regulation ｜ （一）净资本监管

The CSRC: ｜ 中国证监会：

- Establishes risk-regulatory indicator standards and net-capital rules for futures companies ｜ 制定期货公司风险监管指标标准、监管规则和净资本补足制度
- Supervises and guides implementation ｜ 督促并指导期货公司落实净资本监管要求
- Takes regulatory measures that must be implemented at the central regulatory level ｜ 采取依法必须由中国证监会实施的监管措施

##### (II) Margin-Safety Regulation ｜ （二）保证金安全监管

The CSRC: ｜ 中国证监会：

- Establishes margin-depository systems and regulatory guidelines ｜ 制定保证金存管制度和监管工作指引
- Leads and coordinates responses to margin-safety warnings ｜ 领导并协调保证金安全预警信息的处置

##### (III) Regulation of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Managers ｜ （三）董事、监事和高级管理人员监管

The CSRC: ｜ 中国证监会：

- Establishes the regulatory framework and management rules ｜ 研究制定董事、监事和高级管理人员监管制度框架及管理办法
- Approves the qualifications of specified senior positions ｜ 核准特定高级管理人员的任职资格

The source lists positions including: ｜ 教材列举的职位包括：

- Chairperson of the board ｜ 董事长
- Chairperson of the board of supervisors ｜ 监事会主席
- Independent directors ｜ 独立董事
- General manager and deputy general managers ｜ 总经理和副总经理
- Chief risk officer ｜ 首席风险官

It also: ｜ 此外，中国证监会还负责：

- Guides local offices in determining and pursuing the responsibility of non-compliant executives ｜ 指导地方证监局确认和追究违规高管责任
- Establishes regulatory databases and integrity records ｜ 建立监管数据库和诚信档案
- Organises professional training ｜ 组织相关人员培训
- Supervises the routine regulatory work of local offices ｜ 监督和指导地方证监局的日常监管工作

##### (IV) Risk Resolution for Futures Companies ｜ （四）期货公司风险处置

The CSRC: ｜ 中国证监会：

- Formulates laws, policies and risk-resolution arrangements ｜ 制定风险处置法规和政策
- Guides and coordinates risk-resolution work carried out by local CSRC offices ｜ 指导并协调地方证监局的风险处置工作

------

### Examination Topic: Local CSRC Offices ｜ 考点：地方证监局

#### I. Main Responsibilities ｜ 一、各地证监局的主要职责

##### (I) Jurisdictional Supervision ｜ （一）辖区监督管理

Under authorisation from the CSRC, local offices supervise securities- and futures-related activities within their jurisdictions. ｜ 地方证监局根据中国证监会授权，对辖区内证券、期货相关业务活动实施监督管理。

The supervised institutions may include: ｜ 监管对象包括：

- Listed companies ｜ 上市公司
- Securities and futures institutions ｜ 证券、期货经营机构
- Securities and futures investment-consulting institutions ｜ 证券期货投资咨询机构
- Law firms, accounting firms and asset-valuation institutions engaged in related business ｜ 从事证券期货业务的律师事务所、会计师事务所和资产评估机构

##### (II) Investigation of Violations ｜ （二）查处违法违规案件

Local CSRC offices investigate and handle violations occurring within their jurisdictions. ｜ 地方证监局查处辖区范围内的违法违规案件。

------

#### II. Role in the “Five-in-One” System ｜ 二、在“五位一体”监管体系中的工作

##### (I) Net Capital Regulation ｜ （一）净资本监管

Local CSRC offices: ｜ 地方证监局：

- Review futures companies’ risk-regulatory statements ｜ 审核期货公司风险监管报表
- Continuously monitor whether net capital and other indicators satisfy regulatory standards ｜ 持续监控净资本等风险监管指标是否符合标准
- Conduct on-site inspections ｜ 对期货公司实施现场检查
- Take timely regulatory measures ｜ 及时采取监管措施
- Report relevant matters to the CSRC ｜ 按规定向中国证监会报告有关情况

##### (II) Margin-Safety Regulation ｜ （二）保证金安全监管

Local offices are responsible for: ｜ 地方证监局负责：

- Routine supervision and on-site inspection of client margin ｜ 保证金日常监管和现场检查
- Verification and handling of margin-safety warning information ｜ 对保证金安全预警信息进行核实和处理

##### (III) Regulation of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Managers ｜ （三）董事、监事和高级管理人员监管

Local CSRC offices: ｜ 地方证监局：

- Supervise such personnel according to applicable rules and CSRC authorisation ｜ 根据相关管理办法和中国证监会授权实施监管
- Approve qualifications for certain directors, supervisors, financial officers and business-department heads ｜ 核准部分董事、监事、财务负责人和营业部负责人的任职资格
- Determine and pursue the responsibility of non-compliant executives ｜ 对违规高管进行责任确认和追究
- Maintain local regulatory databases and daily supervision files ｜ 管理辖区监管数据库和日常监管档案
- Record personnel qualifications, integrity and ongoing conduct ｜ 记录相关人员的任职资格、诚信状况和动态情况
- Supervise futures companies’ appointment and support of qualified chief risk officers ｜ 督导期货公司设置合格的首席风险官，并保障其充分履行职责

##### (IV) Risk Resolution ｜ （四）期货公司风险处置

Local offices: ｜ 地方证监局：

- Propose risk-resolution plans ｜ 提出风险处置方案
- Organise their implementation ｜ 组织实施风险处置方案
- Report progress to the CSRC ｜ 及时向中国证监会报告处置进展
- Maintain futures-market order and social stability within the jurisdiction ｜ 维护辖区期货市场秩序和社会稳定

------

### Examination Topic: China Futures Association ｜ 考点：中国期货业协会

#### I. Establishment ｜ 一、成立时间

According to the source, the China Futures Association was established in December 2000. ｜ 根据教材，中国期货业协会成立于2000年12月。

------

#### II. Basic Position ｜ 二、基本定位

##### (I) Industry Self-Regulation ｜ （一）行业自律管理

Under the premise of unified state supervision, the Association conducts self-regulation of the futures industry. ｜ 在国家对期货业实行集中统一监督管理的前提下，中国期货业协会对期货行业实施自律管理。

##### (II) Bridge Between Government and Industry ｜ （二）政府与行业之间的桥梁

It serves as a bridge between the government and the futures industry. ｜ 协会发挥政府与期货行业之间的桥梁和纽带作用。

It provides services to members and protects their lawful rights and interests. ｜ 协会为会员服务，并维护会员的合法权益。

##### (III) Protecting Market Order ｜ （三）维护市场秩序

It promotes openness, fairness and impartiality and protects legitimate competition and investor interests. ｜ 协会坚持期货市场公开、公平、公正，维护正当竞争秩序并保护投资者利益。

------

#### III. Main Responsibilities ｜ 三、中国期货业协会的职责

##### (I) Formulating Self-Regulatory Rules ｜ （一）制定自律规则

The Association: ｜ 协会：

- Educates and organises members and practitioners to comply with laws and policies ｜ 教育和组织会员及从业人员遵守法律法规和政策
- Formulates industry self-regulatory rules ｜ 制定行业自律性规则
- Establishes an industry integrity-evaluation system ｜ 建立期货业诚信评价制度
- Conducts integrity supervision ｜ 实施诚信监督

##### (II) Practitioner Qualification and Conduct Management ｜ （二）从业资格与执业行为管理

The Association is responsible for: ｜ 协会负责：

- Recognition, management and revocation of practitioner qualifications ｜ 期货从业资格的认定、管理和撤销
- Organisation of practitioner examinations ｜ 组织期货从业资格考试
- Organisation of qualification tests for senior management ｜ 组织期货公司高级管理人员资质测试
- Supervision and inspection of professional conduct ｜ 监督检查会员和从业人员的执业行为
- Investigation of complaints and reports ｜ 受理并调查投诉和举报
- Disciplinary action under self-regulatory rules ｜ 对违规会员和从业人员实施纪律惩戒

##### (III) Providing Regulatory Advice ｜ （三）提供监管意见

It reports the professional status of members and practitioners to the CSRC and provides opinions and recommendations for regulatory work. ｜ 协会向中国证监会反映和报告会员及从业人员执业状况，为监管工作提供意见和建议。

##### (IV) Industry Standards ｜ （四）行业行为与技术标准

It formulates industry conduct rules and business standards and participates in industry credit ratings and the development of relevant technical standards. ｜ 协会制定行业行为准则和业务规范，并参与行业资信评级及相关行业、技术标准的拟订。

##### (V) Complaint Handling and Dispute Mediation ｜ （五）投诉处理和纠纷调解

It accepts complaints relating to futures business and mediates disputes: ｜ 协会受理与期货业务有关的投诉，并调解：

- Between members ｜ 会员之间的纠纷
- Between members and clients ｜ 会员与客户之间的纠纷

##### (VI) Member Services ｜ （六）会员服务

It protects members’ lawful interests and reports their problems, recommendations and requests to the CSRC and other authorities. ｜ 协会依法维护会员合法权益，并向中国证监会及有关部门反映会员的问题、建议和要求。

##### (VII) Personnel Development and Continuing Education ｜ （七）人才发展和持续教育

It: ｜ 协会：

- Formulates and implements talent-development strategies ｜ 制定并实施人才发展战略
- Strengthens the professional workforce ｜ 加强人才队伍建设
- Provides continuing education and business training ｜ 开展持续教育和业务培训
- Improves professional skills and ethics ｜ 提高从业人员业务技能和职业道德水平

##### (VIII) Special Funds ｜ （八）专项基金

It may establish special funds to support talent development, investor education and other designated activities. ｜ 协会可以设立专项基金，为人才培养、投资者教育和其他特定事业提供资金支持。

##### (IX) Information Security ｜ （九）行业信息安全

It coordinates industry information-security work and promotes improvements in information technology and security protection. ｜ 协会协调行业信息安全保障工作，提高信息安全和信息技术水平。

##### (X) Information and Business Exchanges ｜ （十）信息收集与业务交流

It: ｜ 协会：

- Collects and organises futures-market information ｜ 收集和整理期货信息
- Promotes business exchanges among members ｜ 推动会员之间的业务交流
- Encourages improvements in corporate governance and internal controls ｜ 推动会员完善法人治理和内部控制
- Promotes business innovation ｜ 促进业务创新

##### (XI) Industry Research ｜ （十一）行业研究

It organises research on futures-industry development and participates in policy discussions. ｜ 协会组织会员研究期货业发展，并参与有关政策论证。

##### (XII) Publicity and Investor Education ｜ （十二）市场宣传和投资者教育

It communicates with news media and conducts futures-market publicity and investor education. ｜ 协会加强与新闻媒体的沟通，广泛开展市场宣传和投资者教育。

##### (XIII) Recognition and Industry Culture ｜ （十三）表彰与行业文化建设

It recognises members and individuals with outstanding contributions and organises competitions and cultural activities. ｜ 协会表彰有突出贡献的会员和个人，组织业务竞赛和文化活动，培育健康的行业文化。

##### (XIV) International Cooperation ｜ （十四）国际交流与合作

It represents the Chinese futures industry in international organisations and promotes qualification recognition and self-regulation of cross-border business. ｜ 协会开展国际交流与合作，代表中国期货业参加国际组织，并推动相关资质互认和涉外业务自律管理。

##### (XV) Other Responsibilities ｜ （十五）其他职责

It performs other responsibilities prescribed by law or assigned by the CSRC. ｜ 协会履行法律法规规定以及中国证监会赋予的其他职责。

------

#### IV. Role in the “Five-in-One” System ｜ 四、在“五位一体”体系中的工作

| Regulatory area   | Role of the China Futures Association                        |
| ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Net capital       | Trains practitioners and disciplines violators               |
| Margin safety     | Provides training and promotes investor use of the monitoring inquiry system |
| Senior management | Conducts qualification tests, training and self-regulation   |
| Risk resolution   | Imposes self-regulatory requirements on members and supports risk handling |

| 监管领域   | 中国期货业协会的工作                   |
| ---------- | -------------------------------------- |
| 净资本监管 | 培训从业人员，并依自律规则处罚违规人员 |
| 保证金安全 | 开展存管规定培训，宣传监控中心查询系统 |
| 高管监管   | 组织资质测试、业务培训和自律管理       |
| 风险处置   | 对会员提出自律要求，配合风险处置       |

------

### Examination Topic: Futures Exchanges ｜ 考点：期货交易所

#### I. Main Areas of Exchange Self-Regulation ｜ 一、期货交易所自律管理的内容

Futures exchanges generally conduct self-regulation in the following areas: ｜ 期货交易所通常在以下方面实施自律管理：

##### (I) Membership Qualification Review ｜ （一）审查会员资格

The exchange examines whether applicants and existing members satisfy membership requirements. ｜ 交易所审查申请人及现有会员是否符合会员资格条件。

##### (II) Supervision of Trading Rules ｜ （二）监督交易规则和程序执行

The exchange monitors compliance with trading procedures and market rules. ｜ 交易所监督交易规则和交易程序的执行情况。

##### (III) Client Order-Handling Standards ｜ （三）制定客户委托处理规范

It formulates standards governing how members process client orders. ｜ 交易所制定会员处理客户交易指令的规范。

##### (IV) Reporting and Record-Keeping ｜ （四）规定市场报告和交易记录制度

It requires members to maintain and submit prescribed trading records and reports. ｜ 交易所规定市场报告和交易记录制度。

##### (V) Market Inspection and Discipline ｜ （五）实施市场稽查和惩戒

It investigates abnormal trading and imposes disciplinary measures where necessary. ｜ 交易所对异常交易进行稽查，并对违规行为实施纪律处分。

##### (VI) Preventing False Markets ｜ （六）加强对制造假市场行为的监管

It supervises conduct that may create artificial prices, false trading activity or market manipulation. ｜ 交易所加强对制造虚假价格、虚假交易和操纵市场行为的监管。

------

#### II. Responsibilities of China’s Futures Exchanges ｜ 二、我国期货交易所的职责

##### (I) Providing Trading Facilities and Services ｜ （一）提供交易场所、设施和服务

The exchange provides the systems and facilities required for futures trading. ｜ 期货交易所提供期货交易所需的场所、设施和服务。

##### (II) Designing and Listing Contracts ｜ （二）设计合约并安排上市

It designs futures contracts and organises their listing. ｜ 交易所设计期货合约，并安排合约上市。

##### (III) Organising and Supervising Trading, Clearing and Delivery ｜ （三）组织并监督交易、结算和交割

It organises and supervises the complete market process. ｜ 交易所组织并监督交易、结算和交割活动。

##### (IV) Providing Centralised Performance Guarantees ｜ （四）提供集中履约担保

It provides a centralised guarantee for the performance of futures contracts. ｜ 交易所为期货交易提供集中履约担保。

##### (V) Supervising Members ｜ （五）监督管理会员

It supervises members in accordance with its articles of association and trading rules. ｜ 交易所按照章程和交易规则对会员实施监督管理。

##### (VI) Other Regulatory Responsibilities ｜ （六）履行其他规定职责

It performs other functions prescribed by the futures supervisory authority. ｜ 交易所履行国务院期货监督管理机构规定的其他职责。

------

#### III. Role in the “Five-in-One” System ｜ 三、在“五位一体”体系中的工作

##### (I) Net Capital Regulation ｜ （一）净资本监管

The exchange: ｜ 交易所：

- Provides trading and related data ｜ 提供期货交易等相关数据
- Participates in on-site inspections ｜ 参与定期或者不定期现场检查
- Cooperates with local offices and the CSRC in taking regulatory measures ｜ 配合证监局或者中国证监会采取监管措施

##### (II) Margin-Safety Regulation ｜ （二）保证金安全监管

The exchange: ｜ 交易所：

- Monitors futures companies’ trading activity ｜ 监控期货公司的交易情况
- Takes timely measures when abnormal activity is detected ｜ 发现异常动态时及时采取措施
- Reports relevant matters to local CSRC offices ｜ 向相关证监局通报
- Provides assistance for margin monitoring ｜ 为保证金安全存管和监控提供必要帮助

##### (III) Senior Management Regulation and Risk Resolution ｜ （三）高管自律管理和风险处置

The exchange conducts self-regulation of directors, supervisors and senior managers of member futures companies. ｜ 交易所依法对会员期货公司的董事、监事和高级管理人员实施自律管理。

It also participates in market-risk resolution and takes necessary measures under applicable rules. ｜ 交易所参与市场风险处置，并根据法规和风险处置需要及时采取相应措施。

------

### Examination Topic: China Futures Market Monitoring Center ｜ 考点：中国期货市场监控中心

#### I. Main Functions ｜ 一、期货市场监控中心的职能

##### (I) Unified Account Opening ｜ （一）期货市场统一开户

The Center manages or supports the unified account-opening system. ｜ 监控中心承担期货市场统一开户相关工作。

##### (II) Futures Margin Safety Monitoring ｜ （二）期货保证金安全监控

It monitors the safe deposit and movement of client margin. ｜ 监控中心对客户保证金的安全存管和变动进行监控。

##### (III) Market-Operation Monitoring ｜ （三）期货市场运行监测监控

It monitors overall futures-market operation and abnormal market activity. ｜ 监控中心监测期货市场整体运行情况和异常动态。

##### (IV) Monitoring Futures Institutions ｜ （四）期货经营机构监测监控

It monitors futures companies and other futures institutions. ｜ 监控中心对期货公司等经营机构实施监测。

##### (V) Trade Repository ｜ （五）期货及衍生品交易报告库

It builds and operates a trade repository for futures and derivatives. ｜ 监控中心建设并运营期货及衍生品交易报告库。

##### (VI) Investor Information Inquiry ｜ （六）为投资者提供交易结算信息查询

It provides investors with access to trading and settlement information. ｜ 监控中心为投资者提供交易和结算信息查询服务。

##### (VII) Macroeconomic and Industrial Research ｜ （七）宏观和产业分析研究

It conducts macroeconomic and industrial analysis. ｜ 监控中心开展宏观经济和产业分析研究。

##### (VIII) Investor Protection Fund Administration ｜ （八）代管期货投资者保障基金

It administers the futures investor protection fund on behalf of the relevant authorities. ｜ 监控中心代管期货投资者保障基金。

##### (IX) Information Services ｜ （九）提供监管信息服务

It provides information services to regulatory authorities and futures exchanges. ｜ 监控中心为监管机构和期货交易所等提供信息服务。

##### (X) Market Investigations ｜ （十）期货市场调查

It conducts futures-market investigations. ｜ 监控中心开展期货市场调查。

##### (XI) Assisting in Risk Resolution ｜ （十一）协助风险公司处置

It assists in dealing with futures companies experiencing significant risk. ｜ 监控中心协助对风险期货公司进行处置。

------

#### II. Role in the “Five-in-One” System ｜ 二、在“五位一体”体系中的工作

##### (I) Net Capital Regulation ｜ （一）净资本监管

The Center: ｜ 监控中心：

- Provides margin and related data ｜ 提供期货保证金等相关数据
- Monitors whether minimum settlement reserves in the closed margin system meet requirements ｜ 监控保证金封闭圈内最低结算准备金是否符合要求
- Reports non-compliance to local offices and the CSRC ｜ 发现不符合监管要求时，及时向证监局和中国证监会报告

##### (II) Margin-Safety Regulation ｜ （二）保证金安全监管

It: ｜ 监控中心：

- Assists with research on margin-depository systems ｜ 协助研究保证金存管制度
- Conducts daily operation of the margin-monitoring system ｜ 负责保证金监控系统日常运行
- Reports warning information to local CSRC offices ｜ 向证监局通报保证金安全预警信息

##### (III) Risk Resolution ｜ （三）风险处置

It monitors and warns of risks such as margin shortfalls and provides relevant data to regulatory authorities. ｜ 监控中心对保证金缺口等风险及时进行预警和监控，并根据监管部门要求提供风险公司的相关数据。

##### (IV) Information Sharing ｜ （四）信息共享

Participants in the regulatory system should establish information-query channels based on the principle of resource sharing. ｜ 监管协调机制各方应按照资源共享原则，建立并完善信息查询制度。

They should: ｜ 各方应当：

- Open appropriate data-query interfaces ｜ 根据监管需要开放查询端口
- Establish information-access permissions ｜ 按照规则设置查询权限
- Use existing systems to share regulatory data ｜ 充分利用现有系统共享监管信息

------

## Section 3: Futures Market Risk Control ｜ 第三节 期货市场风险控制

### Examination Topic: Risk Control by Futures Exchanges ｜ 考点：期货交易所的风险控制

#### I. Main Sources of Exchange Risk ｜ 一、交易所的主要风险源

##### (I) Monitoring and Enforcement Risk ｜ （一）监控执行风险

Monitoring and enforcement risk arises when exchange rules or risk-control systems are not properly implemented. ｜ 监控执行风险是指交易所的监管规则或者风险控制制度未能得到有效执行而产生的风险。

##### (II) Irrational Price-Fluctuation Risk ｜ （二）非理性价格波动风险

This may include: ｜ 非理性价格波动风险包括：

- Risk arising from concentrated positions held by large traders ｜ 大户持仓风险
- Funding-related risk ｜ 资金风险

The specific example following “funding risk” is illegible in the source. ｜ 原材料中“资金风险”之后的举例无法辨认，因此不自行补充。

------

#### II. Internal Risk-Monitoring Mechanisms ｜ 二、交易所内部风险监控机制

##### (I) Establishing and Enforcing Risk-Control Systems ｜ （一）正确建立并严格执行风险监控制度

###### Capital Adequacy Management ｜ 资本充足性管理

Appropriate capital-adequacy standards should be established to reduce credit risk. ｜ 交易所应加强资本充足性管理，制定适当标准，以降低信用风险。

###### Position Limits Based on Capital ｜ 根据资本确定持仓限额

A participant’s permitted position size should be linked to its financial capacity. ｜ 交易者或者会员的持仓限额应与其资本和风险承担能力相匹配。

###### Reasonable Margin Ratios ｜ 合理制定保证金比例

Margin ratios should be appropriately established and adjusted to reduce chain defaults. ｜ 交易所应合理制定并及时调整保证金率，避免出现连锁性合约违约风险。

###### Clearing, Settlement and Payment Management ｜ 加强清算、结算和支付系统管理

Strong clearing and payment systems improve liquidity and emergency-response capacity. ｜ 加强清算、结算和支付系统管理，可以提高市场流动性和风险应变能力。

###### Trading-System Management ｜ 加强交易系统管理

The exchange should strengthen system development, maintenance and inspection to prevent operational risk caused by technical failures. ｜ 交易所应加强交易系统的开发、维护和检修，防止系统故障引发操作风险。

###### Rational Contract and Trading-System Design ｜ 合理设计合约和交易制度

Contracts and trading mechanisms should be designed to maintain market liquidity and orderly operation. ｜ 交易合约和交易制度应合理设置，尽可能保持市场流动性和交易秩序。

------

##### (II) Dynamic Monitoring of the Entire Trading Process ｜ （二）建立交易全过程动态风险监控机制

The source lists several monitoring indicators. ｜ 教材列举了以下动态监控指标。

###### Market Funds Change Rate ｜ 市场资金总量变动率

Based on the damaged OCR formula, the indicator is intended to compare the current market’s total funds with the average level over the preceding $N$ days. ｜ 根据OCR公式，其基本含义是比较当日市场资金总量与前 $N$ 日平均资金水平。

It may be expressed as: ｜ 可以整理为：
$$
\text{Market funds change rate}
=
\frac{
\text{Current market funds}
-
\text{Average market funds over previous }N\text{ days}
}{
\text{Average market funds over previous }N\text{ days}
}
\times100\%
$$
The source states that $N$ is commonly set to 3. ｜ 原材料指出，$N$通常取3。

A movement beyond a predetermined threshold may signal substantial price volatility. ｜ 当变动超过特定临界值时，可能预示期货价格将发生较大波动。

###### Market Funds Concentration ｜ 市场资金集中度

$$
\text{Market funds concentration}
=
\frac{
\text{Funds of the top }n\text{ members}
}{
\text{Total market funds}
}
\times100\%
$$

A high ratio indicates that market funds are concentrated among a small number of members. ｜ 指标较高说明市场资金集中在少数会员手中。

###### Position Concentration for a Contract ｜ 某合约持仓集中度

$$
\text{Position concentration}
=
\frac{
\text{Positions held by the top }n\text{ members}
}{
\text{Total open interest in the contract}
}
\times100\%
$$

###### Change Rate of Total Open Interest ｜ 某合约持仓总量变动率

This indicator measures the change in a contract’s current open interest relative to its recent average. ｜ 该指标衡量某合约当日持仓量相对于近期平均持仓量的变化程度。

The exact OCR formula is damaged, but its intended form is a percentage change relative to the previous $N$-day average. ｜ 原文公式受到OCR损坏，其基本形式是相对于前 $N$ 日平均持仓量计算变动比例。

###### Member Position Change Rate ｜ 会员持仓总量变动率

This indicator monitors rapid changes in the positions held by a particular member. ｜ 该指标用于监测某一会员持仓量是否出现快速异常变化。

###### Spot–Futures Price Deviation and Futures Price Change ｜ 现货与期货价格偏离及期货价格变动

The source lists a spot–futures price deviation rate and a futures-price change rate, but the formulas are not sufficiently legible to reproduce reliably. ｜ 原材料列出了现价期价偏离率和期货价格变动率，但公式损坏严重，无法可靠还原，因此不自行补写。

------

##### (III) Establishing and Managing Risk Funds ｜ （三）建立并严格管理风险基金

The source identifies two major exchange-level risk funds: ｜ 教材列举了两类主要风险基金：

- Risk reserve fund ｜ 风险准备金
- Clearing guarantee fund ｜ 结算担保金

------

###### Risk Reserve Fund ｜ 风险准备金

The risk reserve fund is established by a futures exchange to: ｜ 风险准备金是期货交易所设立的资金，用于：

- Provide financial support for the normal operation of the futures market ｜ 为维护期货市场正常运转提供财务保障
- Cover losses caused by unforeseeable exchange risks ｜ 弥补交易所不可预见风险造成的损失

According to the textbook, its sources include: ｜ 根据教材，风险准备金的来源包括：

- 20% of the transaction-fee income collected from members, including preferential fee reductions, extracted from management expenses ｜ 按照向会员收取手续费收入，包括优惠减收部分的20%，从管理费用中提取
- Other income permitted by national financial policy ｜ 符合国家财政政策规定的其他收入

------

###### Clearing Guarantee Fund ｜ 结算担保金

A clearing guarantee fund is a common guarantee fund contributed by clearing members to address the default risk of clearing members. ｜ 结算担保金是由结算会员按照交易所规定缴纳、用于应对结算会员违约风险的共同担保资金。

An exchange using a tiered clearing-member system should collect such a fund from clearing members. ｜ 实行会员分级结算制度的交易所，应当向结算会员收取结算担保金。

It includes: ｜ 结算担保金包括：

\####### Basic Clearing Guarantee Fund ｜ \####### 基础结算担保金

The minimum amount a clearing member must contribute to participate in clearing and delivery. ｜ 基础结算担保金是结算会员参与交易所结算交割业务必须缴纳的最低金额。

\####### Variable Clearing Guarantee Fund ｜ \####### 变动结算担保金

The portion exceeding the basic amount, adjusted according to the clearing member’s business volume. ｜ 变动结算担保金是超过基础结算担保金的部分，并随结算会员业务量变化进行调整。

------

### Examination Topic: Risk Control by Futures Companies ｜ 考点：期货公司的风险控制

#### I. Main Risks Faced by Futures Companies ｜ 一、期货公司的主要风险

##### (I) Management Risk ｜ （一）管理风险
