Management risk arises from defective corporate governance, internal management or decision-making. ｜ 管理风险来源于公司治理、内部管理或者经营决策缺陷。

##### (II) Forecasting Risk ｜ （二）预测风险

Forecasting risk arises from incorrect market forecasts or risk assessments. ｜ 预测风险来源于对市场走势或者风险状况判断错误。

##### (III) Business Operational Risk ｜ （三）业务操作风险

Operational risk arises from errors or failures in account opening, order processing, settlement, information systems and other business processes. ｜ 业务操作风险来源于开户、指令处理、结算、信息系统等业务环节中的错误或者失效。

##### (IV) Client Credit Risk ｜ （四）客户信用风险

Client credit risk arises when a client fails to provide sufficient margin or perform delivery and settlement obligations. ｜ 客户信用风险是指客户不能及时补足保证金或者履行交割、结算义务所形成的风险。

------

#### II. Internal Risk-Control Mechanisms ｜ 二、期货公司内部风险控制机制

##### (I) Compliance with Net Capital Requirements ｜ （一）严格遵守净资本管理规定

Futures companies should ensure that net capital and other risk-regulatory indicators continuously meet applicable requirements. ｜ 期货公司应当确保净资本和其他风险监管指标持续符合监管标准。

##### (II) Chief Risk Officer System ｜ （二）设立首席风险官制度

A futures company should establish a chief risk officer position to supervise and inspect important systems affecting safe and stable operation. ｜ 期货公司应当设立首席风险官，对影响公司安全稳健经营的重要制度进行监督检查。

The source lists systems relating to: ｜ 教材列举的监督事项包括：

- Safe deposit of client margin ｜ 客户保证金安全存管
- Risk-regulatory indicators ｜ 风险监管指标
- Corporate governance and internal controls ｜ 公司治理和内部控制
- Brokerage and clearing business ｜ 经纪业务和结算业务
- Client risk management ｜ 客户风险管理
- Information security ｜ 信息安全
- Reporting of positions held by employees’ close relatives ｜ 员工近亲属持仓报告
- Other systems materially affecting client assets and trading safety ｜ 其他对客户资产安全和交易安全有重大影响的制度

The chief risk officer should also examine risks relating to intermediary-introduction business and comprehensive clearing business. ｜ 首席风险官还应关注中间介绍业务和全面结算业务中的风险。

------

##### (III) Reporting Major Risks ｜ （三）重大风险报告

Where serious violations or major risk exposures are discovered, they should be reported immediately to: ｜ 发现严重违法违规行为或者重大风险隐患时，应当立即报告：

- The local CSRC office where the company is domiciled ｜ 公司住所地中国证监会派出机构
- The company’s board of directors ｜ 公司董事会
- The company’s board of supervisors ｜ 公司监事会

The source lists matters including: ｜ 教材列举的重大事项包括：

- Misappropriation or occupation of client margin ｜ 占用、挪用客户保证金
- Company assets being withdrawn, occupied, frozen, seized or used as security ｜ 公司资产被抽逃、占用、挪用、查封、冻结或者用于担保
- Net capital being unable to continuously satisfy regulatory standards ｜ 净资本无法持续达到监管标准
- Major litigation or arbitration that may create substantial risk ｜ 可能造成重大风险的诉讼或者仲裁
- Shareholders interfering with normal operation ｜ 股东干预公司正常经营
- Other circumstances prescribed by the CSRC ｜ 中国证监会规定的其他情形

------

##### (IV) Controlling Client Credit Risk ｜ （四）控制客户信用风险

Futures companies should: ｜ 期货公司应当：

- Strictly implement account-opening procedures ｜ 严格执行开户程序
- Strictly implement order-acceptance procedures ｜ 严格执行委托程序
- Manage clients according to their credit status ｜ 根据客户资信状况实施风险管理
- Provide necessary training and strengthen clients’ risk awareness ｜ 对客户进行必要培训，加强客户风险意识
- Improve clients’ trading skills and reduce the possibility of substantial losses ｜ 提高客户交易技能，减少大额亏损的可能性

##### (V) Margin and Margin-Call Systems ｜ （五）严格执行保证金和追加保证金制度

Futures companies should continuously monitor client margin. ｜ 期货公司应当持续监控客户保证金状况。

When margin is insufficient, the company should issue a margin call and take measures in accordance with the agreement and trading rules. ｜ 保证金不足时，应当按照协议和交易规则通知客户追加保证金，并依法采取相应措施。

##### (VI) Strengthening Business Management ｜ （六）加强业务管理

One heading in the source is damaged as “严格经言管”; its precise original wording cannot be confirmed.

原材料中一项标题识别为“严格经言管”，无法确认完整原文，因此不自行还原。

Its location indicates that it concerns strict management of futures brokerage or operating activities. ｜ 根据其所在位置，可以判断该项与严格管理经纪或者经营业务有关。

##### (VII) Practitioner Management ｜ （七）加强从业人员管理

Futures companies should strengthen personnel management and improve professional competence and operational capacity. ｜ 期货公司应加强从业人员管理，提高业务运作能力和专业水平。

##### (VIII) Information-Technology Management ｜ （八）健全信息技术管理

They should establish sound information-technology systems and ensure technical support, cybersecurity and business continuity. ｜ 期货公司应健全信息技术管理，做好技术保障、系统安全和业务连续性管理。

------

#### III. Risk Control for Futures Intermediaries ｜ 三、期货居间人的风险控制

A futures company should establish an intermediary-management system. ｜ 期货公司应当制定居间人管理制度。

The system should include: ｜ 制度内容包括但不限于：

- The responsible department and its duties ｜ 居间人管理部门及其职责
- Codes of conduct ｜ 居间人行为准则
- Rights and obligations ｜ 权利和义务
- Training arrangements ｜ 培训管理
- Conditions for contract termination ｜ 解除合同条件
- Penalty provisions ｜ 罚则

According to the textbook: ｜ 根据教材：

- A special follow-up visit should be completed 24 hours after the client submits the account-opening application. ｜ 客户提交开户申请24小时后，期货公司应完成特别回访。
- A trading code may be applied for only after the client confirms the follow-up content. ｜ 客户确认回访内容后，方可申请交易编码。
- Intermediary files should be retained for at least 20 years from the first intermediary agreement. ｜ 居间人档案应当自首次签署居间合同之日起至少保存20年。

These are source-based regulatory details and are presented as textbook content. ｜ 以上属于教材记载的具体监管要求。

------

### Examination Topic: Risk Control by Futures Traders ｜ 考点：期货交易者的风险控制

#### I. Main Risks Faced by Clients ｜ 一、客户面临的主要风险

##### (I) Price-Fluctuation Risk ｜ （一）价格波动风险

Adverse price movements may cause futures positions to lose value. ｜ 期货价格向不利方向变化，可能使交易者头寸产生亏损。

##### (II) Agency Risk ｜ （二）代理风险

A client may suffer loss because of improper conduct or weak controls at the selected futures company. ｜ 客户可能因所选择期货公司的管理缺陷或者不当行为遭受损失。

##### (III) Trading Risk ｜ （三）交易风险

Trading risk may arise from incorrect directions, excessive positions, frequent trading or inadequate stop-loss arrangements. ｜ 交易风险可能来源于方向判断错误、头寸过大、频繁交易或者缺乏止损安排。

##### (IV) Delivery Risk ｜ （四）交割风险

A client may fail to satisfy delivery requirements concerning funds, commodities, quality or procedures. ｜ 客户可能无法满足交割所需的资金、商品、质量标准或者程序要求。

##### (V) Risks Caused by the Investor ｜ （五）投资者自身因素导致的风险

The source lists: ｜ 教材列举了：

- Weak price-forecasting ability ｜ 价格预测能力不足
- Full-position or excessively concentrated trading ｜ 满仓操作，承担过大风险

------

#### II. Risk-Prevention Measures for Investors ｜ 二、投资者的风险防范措施

##### (I) Understand the Basic Characteristics of Futures Trading ｜ （一）充分了解期货交易基本特点

Investors should understand: ｜ 投资者应当理解：

- Margin and leverage ｜ 保证金和杠杆制度
- Daily settlement ｜ 逐日结算
- Two-way trading ｜ 双向交易
- Margin calls and forced liquidation ｜ 追加保证金和强制平仓
- Delivery obligations ｜ 交割义务

##### (II) Select a Futures Company Carefully ｜ （二）慎重选择期货公司

Investors should assess the company’s legal status, financial condition, internal controls, service quality and information systems. ｜ 投资者应考察期货公司的合法资质、财务状况、内部控制、服务质量和信息系统。

##### (III) Establish an Appropriate Investment Strategy ｜ （三）制定正确的投资战略

Investors should limit risk to a level they can financially and psychologically bear. ｜ 投资者应当将风险控制在自身经济能力和心理承受能力范围内。

This may include: ｜ 具体措施包括：

- Position-size limits ｜ 控制仓位
- Stop-loss plans ｜ 设置止损
- Diversification ｜ 分散风险
- Avoiding excessive leverage ｜ 避免过度杠杆
- Matching strategies to investment objectives ｜ 使交易策略与投资目标相匹配

##### (IV) Regulate Personal Conduct ｜ （四）规范自身行为

Investors should strengthen risk awareness and psychological resilience. ｜ 投资者应增强风险意识和心理承受能力。

They should avoid emotional, impulsive and excessively speculative trading. ｜ 投资者应避免情绪化、冲动化和过度投机交易。

------

#### III. Internal Risk Control for Institutional Investors ｜ 三、机构投资者的内部风险控制机制

##### (I) Establishing a Risk-Management System ｜ （一）建立风险管理系统

Institutional investors should establish a comprehensive system covering identification, measurement, monitoring, reporting and control. ｜ 机构投资者应建立覆盖风险识别、测度、监测、报告和控制的风险管理系统。

##### (II) Establishing a Reasonable Risk-Management Process ｜ （二）制定合理的风险管理流程

A standard process should define responsibilities, approval procedures and escalation mechanisms. ｜ 风险管理流程应明确责任分工、审批程序和风险升级报告机制。

##### (III) Mutually Restrictive Front-, Middle- and Back-Office Controls ｜ （三）建立相互制约的业务操作机制

###### Front Office ｜ 前台

The front office is responsible for specific trading activities. ｜ 前台负责具体交易操作。

###### Back Office ｜ 后台

The back office is responsible for: ｜ 后台负责：

- Daily trade review ｜ 每日交易复核
- Reconciliation ｜ 对账
- Confirmation of buy and sell instructions ｜ 确认买卖委托
- Settlement and record keeping ｜ 结算和记录管理

###### Middle Office ｜ 中台

The middle office supervises and controls the activities of both the front and back offices. ｜ 中台负责监督并控制前台和后台的业务操作。

Its functions commonly include independent risk measurement and limit monitoring. ｜ 其核心作用是独立实施风险测度和限额监控。

##### (IV) Senior Management Oversight ｜ （四）加强高层管理人员监督

Senior management should strengthen oversight of internal risk controls and bear responsibility for major risk decisions. ｜ 高级管理人员应加强对内部风险控制的监督，并对重大风险决策承担责任。

##### (V) Mandatory Enforcement of Risk Controls ｜ （五）强制执行交易风险监控措施

Risk limits and control procedures should be mandatory rather than optional. ｜ 交易风险限额和监控措施应当强制执行，而不能由交易人员自行选择。

------

#### Core Summary ｜ 核心总结

🏛️ **The five-in-one system consists of the CSRC, local CSRC offices, futures exchanges, the Monitoring Center and the China Futures Association.** ｜ 🏛️ **“五位一体”体系由中国证监会、地方证监局、期货交易所、期货市场监控中心和期货业协会构成。**

📜 **The CSRC provides unified leadership, rulemaking, coordination and enforcement.** ｜ 📜 **中国证监会负责统一领导、规则制定、统筹协调和监督执法。**

📍 **Local CSRC offices conduct jurisdictional supervision, on-site inspection and front-line risk resolution.** ｜ 📍 **地方证监局负责辖区日常监管、现场检查和一线风险处置。**

🤝 **The China Futures Association conducts industry self-regulation, qualification management, training and dispute mediation.** ｜ 🤝 **中国期货业协会负责行业自律、资格管理、培训和纠纷调解。**

🏦 **Futures exchanges organise trading, clearing and delivery and provide centralised performance guarantees.** ｜ 🏦 **期货交易所组织交易、结算和交割，并提供集中履约担保。**

🔍 **The Monitoring Center performs unified account opening, margin-safety monitoring and market-operation monitoring.** ｜ 🔍 **期货市场监控中心负责统一开户、保证金安全监控和市场运行监测。**

📊 **Exchange risk monitoring focuses on changes and concentration in funds, positions and prices.** ｜ 📊 **交易所动态风险监控主要关注资金、持仓和价格的变动与集中程度。**

🛡️ **Exchange-level risk funds include the risk reserve fund and clearing guarantee fund.** ｜ 🛡️ **交易所风险基金主要包括风险准备金和结算担保金。**

⚠️ **Futures companies face management, forecasting, operational and client-credit risks.** ｜ ⚠️ **期货公司主要面临管理风险、预测风险、业务操作风险和客户信用风险。**

👮 **The chief risk officer independently supervises systems affecting client assets, trading safety and stable operation.** ｜ 👮 **首席风险官独立监督影响客户资产安全、交易安全和公司稳健经营的重要制度。**

📉 **Investors should control position size, avoid excessive leverage and establish stop-loss arrangements.** ｜ 📉 **投资者应控制仓位、避免过度杠杆并制定止损安排。**

🔄 **Institutional risk control should separate the front office, middle office and back office so that they supervise and restrict one another.** ｜ 🔄 **机构投资者应建立前台、中台和后台相互监督、相互制约的内部控制机制。**
